adhyāya 1
majjanamātaṅgagaṇḍacyutamadamadirāmodamattālijālam  |
snānaiḥ siddhāṅganānāṃ kucayugavigalatkuṅkumāsaṅgapiṅgam  || 1.1 ||
sāyaṃ prātarmunīnāṃ kuśakusumacayācchannatīrasthanīram  |
pāyādvo narmadāmbhaḥ karikarabhakarākrāntarahastaraṅgam  || 1.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
himavacchikhare ramye siddhagandharvasevite  |
yakṣavidyādharākīrṇe nānāgaṇasamanvite  || 1.3 ||
brahmaviṣṇusurāḥ sarve skandanandigaṇeśvarāḥ  |
candrādityau grahaiḥ sārdhaṃ nakṣatradhruvamaṇḍalam  || 1.4 ||
vāyuśca varuṇaścaiva kubero'tha yamastathā  |
indrādyā devatāssarve gandharvagaṇa eva ca  || 1.5 ||
brāhmyādyā mātaraśca ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
mūrtimantaśca tīrthāni caṇḍabhṛṅgimahābalāḥ  || 1.6 ||
dānavāsuradaityāśca piśācā bhūtarākṣasāḥ  |
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyaṃ maṇimāṇikyaśobhitam  || 1.7 ||
ratnavaidūryasopānavāpīkūpasahasrakam  |
padmanīlotpalopetaṃ nānāvṛkṣasamanvitam  || 1.8 ||
kāmyakāmaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ puṣpitaiḥ phalitairyutam  |
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ cakravākopakūjitam  || 1.9 ||
kokakokilasaṃghuṣṭaṃ nānāpakṣisamākulam  |
sthānaṃ sarve harasyāpuḥ siddhaiśca parisevitam  || 1.10 ||
tatrāsīnaṃ mahādevaṃ śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram  |
stuvantaḥ ke'pi deveśaṃ kecinnṛtyanti cāgrataḥ  || 1.11 ||
divyasiṃhāsanāsīnamumayā sahitaṃ haram  |
teṣāṃ madhye samutthāya skando vacanamabravīt || 1.12 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakarttāraṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 1.13 ||
brahmaviṣṇvindravaradaṃ bhaktānāṃ bhaktavatsalam  |
tryambakasitakaṇṭhāya jñātājñātasvarūpiṇe  || 1.14 ||
īśvarāyāvināśāya gajacarmāvaguṇṭhine  |
kapālamālābharaṇadvīpicarmadharāya ca  || 1.15 ||
bhasmoddhūlitadehāya namaste'stu pinākine  |
anantānantarūpāya kālāya parameṣṭhine  || 1.16 ||
sadyo vāmastathā ghorastatpuruṣāya te namaḥ  |
īśānāya pareśāya sadāśiva namo'stu te  || 1.17 ||
ṛgyajuḥ sāmarūpāya atharvāya namo'stu te  |
namaḥ kālāgnirūpāya sarvalokanivāsine  || 1.18 ||
namaḥ kāntārddhadehāya varṣiṣṭhāya ca te namaḥ  |
oṃ namaḥ śivāya rūpāya bhīmāya bhavarūpiṇe  || 1.19 ||
śivāya bhavarūpāya bhīmarūpakapardine  |
tvayā vyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 1.20 ||
jihvācāpalyabhāvena khedito'si mayā prabho  |
kṣamasva mama īśāna ajñānāñjñānato'pi vā  || 1.21 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te stavenānena suvrata  |
dadāmi te na sandeho varaṃ manasi kāṅkṣitam  || 1.22 ||

skanda uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātumamecchasi  |
uttare tu diśābhāge harmyā hemamayāḥ śubhāḥ  || 1.23 ||
saptabhaumāstu vistīrṇahemaprākāratoraṇāḥ  |
nānāmaṇisamuktādyā vajravaidūryamaṇḍitāḥ  || 1.24 ||
tatraiva madhurā vāṇī veṇuvīṇāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
prekṣaṇīyairnṛtyagītairdivyakāntimanoharaiḥ  || 1.25 ||
kasyaitāni gṛhāṇīti meroruttarataḥ śiva  |
tvatprasādāttu pṛcchāmi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 1.26 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
śṛṇu skanda suraśreṣṭha kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
bhṛgusthāne pure ye vai rāhusūrvyasamāgame  || 1.27 ||
kurukṣetre prabhāse ca mokṣe rudrapade tathā  |
kedārakṣetrake sthāne rudre kanakhale tathā  || 1.28 ||
bhairave lalitākṣetre śiva dhyātvā ca putraka  |
ye pṛtāḥ śivanadyāṃ tu śivadhyānaparāyaṇā  || 1.29 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhāṇi ramyāṇi svayaṃ karmaphalārjitaiḥ  |
saptabhaumairgṛhairdivyairvastrabhogāśca puṣkalāḥ  || 1.30 ||
dattāni ca mayaiteṣāṃ harmyabhogasukhāni ca  |

skanda uvāca - 
sahasraśatabhaumāśca vaidūryamāṇamaṇḍitāḥ  || 1.31 ||
kūṭaiḥ kapāṭakairnaddhasiddhadvārāṇyanekaśaḥ  |
nityotsavairnṛtyagītaiḥ kāmyairdivyairmanoharaiḥ  || 1.32 ||
asaṃkhyātā gṛhā ramyāḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhāḥ  |
kasyaitānīha harmyāṇi pūrvabhāge maheśvara  || 1.33 ||
dakṣiṇe paścime caiva nānābhogāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
surendrabhavane bhogāḥ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 1.34 ||
kena karmmavipākena śūbhenāpyaśubhena vā  |
kūṣmāṇḍavāsinaścakre ghore majjanti tāmase  || 1.35 ||
pūyaśoṇitakūpeṣu kṛmikīṭapataṅginaḥ  |
tiryagyonigatāḥ pāpaiḥ pīḍyamānāstu mānuṣāḥ  || 1.36 ||
dāridryaduḥkhitā dīnāḥ pacyamānā bubhukṣitāḥ  |
kena karmmavipākena śūbhāśubhagati prabho  || 1.37 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ kathayasva prasādataḥ  |

īśvara uvāca - 
ye mṛtā narmadātīre saṅgame tigmadārśite  || 1.38 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhāṇi ramyāṇi pūrvabhāge ca ṣaṇmukha  |
oṃkāradakṣiṇe bhāge pūrvato'marakaṇṭake  || 1.39 ||
narmadākoṭitīrthe ca ye mṛtāḥ skandamānuṣāḥ  |
harmye manorame ramye te vasanti narottamāḥ  || 1.40 ||
bhṛgāvagnau jale vāpi revākapilasaṅgame  |
dānaṃ dattaṃ tapastaptaṃ te vasanti gṛhairimaiḥ  || 1.41 ||
godāvaryyāṃ payasvinyāṃ tapatyāṃ caiva saṅgame  |
tryambake dhautapāpe ca himādrau vindhyaparvate  || 1.42 ||
maheśvaramaye sahye gokarṇe ca mahābale  |
hariścandrapure candre śrīśaile tripurāntake  || 1.43 ||
kṛṣṇāyāṃ sasamudrāyāmekādaśyāṃ mahānade  |
kārtike yonikuṇḍe ca ye mriyante ca putraka  || 1.44 ||
yāmye harmye hemamaye teṣāṃ śreṣṭhāssvayambhuvaḥ  |
nānā bhogāṃśca bhuñjanti yathā śakrastriviṣṭape  || 1.45 ||
sarasvatyāṃ tyajetprāṇān prabhāse śaśibhūṣaṇe  |
pāriyātre mahākāle prayāge ca mahāpathe  || 1.46 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhāṇi ramyāṇi nānābhogāśca puṣkalāḥ  |
maṇimāṇikyadīptābhāḥ saumye koṭitrayodaśa  || 1.47 ||
evaṃ tathaikāviṃśatyā bhūrve caivopaveśitāḥ  |
dakṣiṇe navakoṭyastu bhṛtā harmyā hiraṇmayāḥ  || 1.48 ||
gṛhā mauktikasaṃkāśāḥ koṭyaḥ ṣoḍaśa vāruṇe  |
tīrthayātrāviśeṣeṇa dānadharmaviśeṣataḥ  || 1.49 ||
bhuñjanti vividhān bhogānmayā tuṣṭena putraka  |
meroruttarato bhāge dakṣiṇe vāruṇe tathā  || 1.50 ||
krīḍanti ca manohārimandire devanirmite  |
devānāṃ vārddhakistatra kartā viśvasya karmaṇaḥ  || 1.51 ||
sarvaratnamayaṃ harmyaṃ bhuvanopari paśyasi  |
pūrvabhāge mahāsena nārmadaḥ phalamaśnute  || 1.52 ||
etatte kathitaṃ skanda paripṛṣṭaṃ tvayā ca yat |
devānāṃ ca hitārthāya tubhyaṃ sarvaṃ mayānagha  || 1.53 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 2

skanda uvāca - 
śrotukāmā ime sarve brahmaviṣṇusurottamāḥ  |
prabhāvamīdṛśaṃ yasya tīrthasyā'sya mahatphalam  || 2.1 ||
narmadāyāstathotpattiṃ saṅgamaṃ liṅgapūjanam  |
parvakāle ca devānāṃ parvate'marakaṇṭake  || 2.2 ||
ākhyānasahitaṃ deva kathayasva yathārthataḥ  |
tīrthayātrāphalaṃ samyagvaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca  || 2.3 ||
bhaktyā yuktaṃ ca yatkiṃcitkathayasva prasādataḥ  |

sūta uvāca - 
śrutvā'khyānamidaṃ puṇya purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 2.4 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajñaḥ saptakalpānuvartakaḥ  |
ājagāmā'thamārkaṇḍaṛṣibhiḥ sahitastadā  || 2.5 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā śreyān tvenaiva pūjitaḥ  |
sapādalakṣamadhikamṛṣīṇāṃ cogratejasām  || 2.6 ||
brahmarṣayo devarṣayastathā rājarṣayaḥ pare  |
etaiḥ parivṛtaḥ śrīmānmadhye'raṇyāśramaṃ prati  || 2.7 ||
tīrthayātrāphalaṃ prāpya narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
śrutvā mahāntaṃ mārkaṇḍaprabhāvaṃ lomaharṣaṇaḥ  || 2.8 ||
ājagāma tato draṣṭumṛṣiṃ kalmaṣanāśanam  |
narmadāpravāhe puṇye siddhagandharvasevite  || 2.9 ||
yakṣavidyādharākīrṇe kinnarairupaśobhite  |
nānādevagaṇākīrṇe nānāgaṇaniṣevite  || 2.10 ||
revāvataraṇaṃ śrutvā prabhāvaṃ puṇyasaṅgatam  |
nanṛturdevatāstatra siddhavidyādharā narāḥ  || 2.11 ||
kahlāraiḥ śatapatraiśca punnāgairnāgacampakaiḥ  |
āmrajambūkapitthaiśca dāḍimaiḥ panasaistathā  || 2.12 ||
nimbajambīranāraṅgaiḥ kadalīṣaṇḍamaṇḍitaiḥ  |
kumudairnāgavallyādyaiḥ śāleyaiśca tamālakaiḥ  || 2.13 ||
bījapūrakakharjūrairdrākṣāmadhurapāṭalaiḥ  |
bilvacandanapīlvādyaiḥ kadambakuṭajaistathā  || 2.14 ||
sarvakāmaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ phalitaṃ puṣpitaṃ bhṛśam  |
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ cakravākopaśobhitam  || 2.15 ||
kokilābarhiṇaśukairnānāpakṣivināditam  |
jātismarā pakṣiṇaśca vyājahrurmānuṣīṅgiram  || 2.16 ||
gandharvakinnarayutairyakṣavidyādharoragaiḥ  |
krīḍate mithunaṃ divyaṃ śanairgirivarottame  || 2.17 ||
divyagandharvanṛtyaiśca veṇuvīṇāsahasraśaḥ  |
divyodbhavaiḥ prekṣaṇīyaiḥ śobhitairgirigahvare  || 2.18 ||
gītadhvanininādena divaṃ bhūmiṃ vyanādayan  |
brahmarṣayo devarṣayastathā rājarṣayaḥ pare  || 2.19 ||
vedadhvanitayajñānāmagnihotraprakāśataḥ  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvāṇi cāturvarṇyā dvijottamāḥ  || 2.20 ||
pulastyaśca vasiṣṭhaśca pulahaśca kratustathā  |
bhṛguratrirmarīciśca bhāradvājo'tha kāśyapaḥ  || 2.21 ||
manuryamo'ṅgirāścaiva śātātapaparāśarau  |
āpastambo'tha śambaśca kāvyaḥ kātyāyano muniḥ  || 2.22 ||
gautamaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau dakṣaḥ kātyāyanistathā  |
jāmadagnyo yājñavalkya ṛṣyaśṛṅgo vibhāṇḍakaḥ  || 2.23 ||
gargaśaunakadālbhyā vyāsa uddālakaḥ śukaḥ  |
nāradaḥ parvataścaiva durvāsāścogratāpasaḥ  || 2.24 ||
śākalyo gālavaścaiva jābālirmudgalastathā  |
viśvāmitraḥ kauśikaśca ṛṣayo devasammatāḥ  || 2.25 ||
tathā dharmaśatānandavaiśampāyanavaiṣṇavāḥ  |
śākalāyanavārddhakyau juhutiścāvasustathā  || 2.26 ||
bālakhilyā mahātmāno bhūmimaṇḍalavāsinaḥ  |
brahmadaṇḍaṃ samāruhā devalokaṃ vrajanti ye  || 2.27 ||
ete cānye'vrajaṃstatra ṛṣayo dhārmikāḥ pare  |
jvalantastejasā sarve nirdhūmā iva pāvakāḥ  || 2.28 ||
māsopavāsina kecitkecitpakṣopavāsinaḥ  |
trirātrakāḥ sāntapanā nirāhārāstathā pare  || 2.29 ||
kecitpuṣpaphalāhārāḥ śāntā vātāśanistathā  |
kecidgomayabhakṣyāśca jalāhārāstathāpare  || 2.30 ||
sāgnihotrāśca vidvāṃso mokṣavedārthacintakāḥ  |
itihāsapurāṇādi śrutismṛtiviśāradāḥ  || 2.31 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo mārkaṇḍeyaṃ priyayā tapasvinaḥ  |
etaiśca saṃtiṣṭhanṛkṣairiva niśākaraḥ  || 2.32 ||
tīrthayātrāphalaṃ śrutvā dharmaputro yudhiṣṭhiraḥ  |
abhijñairbrāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ draupadyā priyayā saha  || 2.33 ||
vidvadbhirvedavidbhiśca brahmiṣṭhairbrahmacintakaiḥ  |
narmadātīramāyāte mārkaṇḍeyāśramaṃ prati  || 2.34 ||
pradakṣiṇatriḥ praṇamya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ  |
upaviṣṭastadā tatra bhrātṛbhissaha dharmajaḥ  || 2.35 ||
upaviṣṭaṃ nṛpaṃ dṛṣṭvā mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devaṃ dharmaputraṃ yudhiṣṭhiram  || 2.36 ||
kuśalaṃ nṛpaśārdūla bhrātṛbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha  |
prahasya so'bravīdvākyaṃ mārkaṇḍaṃ munisattamam  || 2.37 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam  |
kṛtakṛtyo'bhavaṃ tvadya tvatpādāmbujadarśanāt || 2.38 ||
adya me'ntarmalaṃ naṣṭaṃ pāpocchedananirmmalam  |
dṛṣṭvā tapovanaṃ sarvaṃ jvalantaṃ sūryavarcasam  || 2.39 ||
nirmuktaḥ kilbiṣādasmātsaptajanmāntarādvibho  |
rantvā cottaradigbhāge devabhūmiṃ manoharām  || 2.40 ||
gaṅgāṃ tripathagāṃ devīṃ yamunāṃ ca sarasvatīm  |
gaṅgādvāraṃ himasthānaṃ kubjāgraṃ brahmayonikam  || 2.41 ||
ugraṃ kanakhalañcaiva kedāraṃ bhairavaṃ tathā  |
naimiṣaṃ ca gayātīrthaṃ kurukṣetraṃ ca puṣkaram  || 2.42 ||
vārāṇasīṃ prayāgaṃ ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  |
kālañjaraṃ cāpyatīrthaṃ prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 2.43 ||
puṇyānyetāni cānyāni tyaktvā kṣetrāṇi suvrata  |
sevyate kena kārvyeṇa narmadaiva mahānadī  || 2.44 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajña trikālajña trivedika  |
śrotukāmā ime sarve kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 2.45 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābāho brāhmaṇairbhrātṛbhiḥ saha  |
tatte'haṃ kathayiṣyāmi purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 2.46 ||
svāyambhuve'tha munaya ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
devānāṃ saṅgame tatra kailāse ca śivālaye  || 2.47 ||
kārtikyāṃ devadeveśaṃ haraṃ draṣṭuṃ samāyayuḥ  |
prākpṛṣṭaṃ kārttikeyena dharmādharmanidarśanam  || 2.48 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  |
viṣṇubrahmādidevānāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ yakṣarakṣasām  || 2.49 ||
aṣṭalakṣāṇi sārddhāni śivaloke ca gīyate  |
tadarddhaṃ vaiṣṇave loke cārddhārddhaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pure  || 2.50 ||
caturaśītisahasrāṇi martyaloke ca gīyate  |
ārādhitaḥ śivaḥ pūrvaṃ brahmādyaiśca surāsuraiḥ  || 2.51 ||
tatastuṣṭaḥ sureśāno varaṃ dātuṃ yathepsitam  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa devānāṃ varado bhava  || 2.52 ||
saptadvīpā vasumatī vasudhā karmmabhāginī  |
navamadvīpaparyyantaṃ jambūdvīpaṃ pracakṣate  || 2.53 ||
vāpī kūpasahasrāṇi navārṇaḥ sarito nahi  |
na sṛtāścāpagāḥ śarvapitṛghorāvatāriṇīḥ  || 2.54 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝn pātuṃ hi sāmpratam  |
tāriṇī sarvalokānāmavatāraya narmadām  || 2.55 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
duṣprāpyaṃ tridaśaiḥ sarvairayācyaṃ yācyate surāḥ  |
vegaṃ śaknoti kaḥ soḍhuṃ tattoyabhramaṇasya ca  || 2.56 ||
toyaṃ bhūtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ nipatiṣyajjavena tu  |
yācadhvamanyāṃ saritaṃ sukhasādhyāntaraṅgiṇīm  || 2.57 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
kā'nyā tārayituṃ śaktā puṇyā soḍhuṃ saridvarā  |
muktvā trinayaneśānīṃ narmadāṃ śokatāriṇīm  || 2.58 ||
devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā samāhūtā saridvarā  |
āgatā ca tato devī sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā  || 2.59 ||
pratyakṣā makarārūḍhā devadevasya cājñayā  |
deva me dehi vaktavyaṃ kartavyā tu kṛpā mayi  || 2.60 ||
niśamya tadvacasaumyaṃ narmadāyāstrilocanaḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ martyaloke'dya gamyatām  || 2.61 ||
lokānāṃ ca hitārthāya martyānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |

narmadovāca - 
tavājñāṃ ca kariṣye'haṃ nirādhārā kathaṃ vibho  || 2.62 ||
ko māṃ dhartuṃ ca śaknoti mahī yāsyati viplavam  |
ādideśa tataḥ sarvān parvatān parameśvaraḥ  || 2.63 ||
ādiśyaitāṃśca tān sarvāṃstato devaḥ svayaṃ śivaḥ  |

parvatā ūcu̱ḥ
na śaknumo vayaṃ sarve dhartuṃ vegaṃ ca nārmadam  || 2.64 ||
bhedamāpnoti vai pṛthvī śatadhā yānti bhūbhṛtaḥ  |
ṛkṣavānabravīddevaṃ dehyājñā tridaśeśvara  || 2.65 ||
dhārayāmi ca satyena tvatprasādādumāpate  |
tato'vatāritā devī jambūdvīpe narādhipa  || 2.66 ||
dārayitvā mahīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
tato devagaṇairuktā maryādāṃ vaha suvrate  || 2.67 ||
ekaviṃśatsahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ  |
sabhittvā saptapātālān rasātalatalaṃ yayau  || 2.68 ||
devalokaṃ jagāmā'tha pralaye samupasthite  |
prathamā kathitā rājannavatārasya kalpanā  || 2.69 ||
svārociṣe dvitīye tu ādikalpe yuge'nagha  |
na sāgarā na sarito na tīrthāni na saṅgamaḥ  || 2.70 ||
tretāyuge'vatīrṇā tu purā bhāgīrathī sarit |
jahnunā culukenaiva hṛdi madhye vyavasthitā  || 2.71 ||
sā kathaṃ sevyate rājastyaktvā caiva tu kalpagām  |
ajñānatamasāchannā viṣṇumāyāvimohitāḥ  || 2.72 ||
tyaktvā vai narmadāṃ rājan sevante'nyāṃ nadīṃ surāḥ  |
yāvṛśaṃ sūryadevānāṃ maṇiratnaprabhāsu ca  || 2.73 ||
antaraṃ tādṛśaṃ rājannarmadā'nyāpagāsu ca  |
śrutvākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ śaṅkarasya prabhāvataḥ  || 2.74 ||
sevyate saptagā tatra purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathā śrutam  || 2.75 ||
kṛtakṛtyaḥ sarvakāmān brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
prāpnoṣi paramaṃ sthānaṃ vaṃśakṣayavivarjitam  || 2.76 ||
śrutvākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ pavitramaghanāśanam  |
ye śṛṇvanti sadā nityaṃ gohatyā ca praṇaśyati  || 2.77 ||

iti śrīrevākhaṇḍe ādikalpakathanaṃ dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 3

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
revā'vataraṇaṃ puṇyaṃ saṅgamaṃ liṅgamarcitam  |
tīrthayātrāpramāṇaśca yugamanvantārāṇi ca  || 3.1 ||
parvatasyatu māhātmyaṃ tatra nadyāṃ yathocitam  |
parvakālastu devānāṃ tīrthe tīrthe viśeṣataḥ  || 3.2 ||
pattanasya tu māhātmyaṃ tatra vāsaṃ yathocitam  |
bhṛgostu kīrtanaṃ puṇyaṃ brahmahatyāvimocanam  || 3.3 ||
kenā'vatīrya kāryeṇa jambūdvīpe saridvarā  |
śivalokaṃ gatā sā tu tanme vada mahāmune  || 3.4 ||
uktānuktāśca yatkiṃcitkathayasva prasādataḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrūyatāṃ rājarājendra kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  || 3.5 ||
hiraṇyakaśipuścāsīdyuge cādye mahā'suraḥ  |
śivaprasādasampanno hyalaṃ mātṛbalotkaṭaḥ  || 3.6 ||
nirjitā devatāstena palāyanaparāyaṇāḥ  |
jagmuḥ śaraṇamudvignā bhayārtā bhayavihvalaḥ  || 3.7 ||
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ devaṃ sasārārṇavatāraṇam  |
śaraṇaśca prapannāḥ smo mādhavaṃ madhusūdana  || 3.8 ||
agnihotrāśca vedāśca na devānāṃ ca yājanam  |
na svādhyāyo na yajñāśca na hutaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam  || 3.9 ||
vyāvitaṃ dānavairduṣṭairdharmakarma ca nāśitam  |
etasminnantare rājan viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā  || 3.10 ||
daityāśca nihatāssarve nṛsiṃhavapuṣā tu te  |
punaḥpravartate dharmo devabrāhmaṇayājanam  || 3.11 ||
kṣayitā dānavāssarve pāpakarmmaratā'dhamāḥ  |
gatā tenaiva kāryeṇa narmadā lokadhāriṇī  || 3.12 ||
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
avatāraṃ punarmartye devī tridaśapūjitā  || 3.13 ||
kariṣyati naraśreṣṭhalokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
dharmakarmarataṃ jñātvā martyaṃ suragaṇārcitaḥ  || 3.14 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
āsītpurā cakravartī somavaṃśe purūravāḥ  |
śaśāsa pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ yathāśakrastriviṣṭapam  || 3.15 ||
ekadā sa nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ sabhāmadhye purūravāḥ  |
papraccha brāhmaṇān vṛddhān vṛddhasevī dhṛtavrataḥ  || 3.16 ||
yajñādibhirvinā kena mānavāḥ pāpamohitāḥ  |
svargaṃ prāpurupāyena tanme vada yathātatham  || 3.17 ||

brāhmaṇā ūcu̱ḥ
āste svarge mahārāja narmadā lokapāvanī  |
avatārayatāṃ svargāllokānāṃ pāpahāriṇīm  || 3.18 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dvijānāṃ vidhṛtāmanām  |
ārādhayāmāsa devamayutaṃ sāgrameva ca  || 3.19 ||
kandamūlaphalaiḥ śākairjalāhāraistathāpi saḥ  |
śivabhaktiparo nityaṃ viśuddhenāntarātmanā  || 3.20 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo varaṃ varaya putraka  |
dadāmi te na sandeho yatheṣṭaṃ manasepsitam  || 3.21 ||

purūravā uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  |
hitāya sarvalokānāmavatāraya narmadām  || 3.22 ||
navakhaṇḍāssaptadvīpāstvāpagāssaritaḥ tathā  |
nimagnaṃ narake ghore jagatsarvaṃ mayā śrutam  || 3.23 ||
lakṣayojanaparyyantaṃ jambūdvīpaṃ nirāśrayam  |
na devāstṛptimāyānti na mātṛpitṛmānuṣāḥ  || 3.24 ||
etacchrutvā mahādevo naradevasya bhāṣitam  |

hara uvāca - 
uvāca durlabhaṃ devairayācyaṃ yācyate nṛpa  || 3.25 ||
varamanyaṃ prayacchāmi varjayitvā tu narmadām  |

purūravā uvāca - 
nānyaṃ varaṃ mahādeva prāṇatyāge'pi prārthaye  || 3.26 ||
jñātvā tu niścayaṃ rājñastapasogreṇa sādhanam  |
ājñāpitā mekalā sā'vatara tvaṃ sureśvari  || 3.27 ||
purūravāvipākena martyalokahitaṃ kuru  |
ājñāpitāgatā sā ca śivasyāgre vyavasthitā  || 3.28 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā mamādeśo'dya dīyatām  |

hara uvāca - 
svargātprayāhi reve tvaṃ martyalokaṃ mamājñayā  || 3.29 ||
purūravastapaḥ satyaṃ kuru kalyāṇi sāmpratam  |

narmadovāca - 
kathamīśa nirādhārā svargādyāsyāmyahaṃ dharām  || 3.30 ||
tatastadvacanaṃ śrutvā devadeva umāpatiḥ  |
āvāhayāmāsa sarvānaṣṭau kulanagottamān  || 3.31 ||
uvāca parvatān devaḥ kaḥ sariddhāraṇe kṣamaḥ  |

parvatā ūcu̱ḥ
śatadhā bhedamāyānti jalāghātānmahādrayaḥ  || 3.32 ||
abhāṣata tato vindhyo dhartumutsahate nadīm  |
mama putraḥ sureśāgrya tvatprasādānna saṃśayaḥ  || 3.33 ||
paryaṅka iti vikhyātaḥ kandarpadṛḍhavikramaḥ  |
jyeṣṭhaḥ sarvagaṇairyukto mānyaḥ sarvamahībhṛtām  || 3.34 ||
devairapi ca durjñeyaḥ śivāyāścarcane rataḥ  |
santyeva parvatāssarve yadyapīha maheśvara  || 3.35 ||
tathāpi dhāraṇe śaktaḥ sa eveha na saṃśayaḥ  |
aśaktā vikalā vṛddhāssarva eva mahīdharāḥ  || 3.36 ||
anujñāṃ dehi paryaṅkaḥ kṣamastāṃ dhartumāpagām  |
anujñātaśca devena paryaṅkaḥ sa nagottamaḥ  || 3.37 ||
uvāca dhārayiṣye'haṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara  |
tataḥ pracalitā devī mūrdhni paryaṅkabhūbhṛta  || 3.38 ||
jalaughavegabhramaṇātsaśailavanakānanā  |
plāvitā vasudhā sarvā akālakalitaṃ jagat || 3.39 ||
stutā devagaṇaiḥ sarvaistadā mekalakanyakā  |
maryādāṃ vaha kalyāṇi lokānāṃ hitakāriṇī  || 3.40 ||
tvayā vyāptamidaṃ sarva trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
tataḥ saṃvṛtarūpeṇa śivājñātaśca mekalā  || 3.41 ||
pramāṇato yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇyekaviṃśati  |
rasātalaṃ sā viviśe tarpayitvā pitāmahān  || 3.42 ||
spṛśa māṃ tvaṃ svahastena ityuktaḥ sa purūravāḥ  |
pītvā ca salilaṃ datte pitṛbhyaśca tilodakam  || 3.43 ||
agaman paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatsurairapi durlabham  |
pavitraṃ paritassarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 3.44 ||
sevyate tena kāryyeṇa narmadā saptakalpagā  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannākhyānaṃ ca śivoditam  || 3.45 ||
vaivasvatamidānīṃ tu dvāparānte samudyate  |
tvaṃ rājā bhrātṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ satyasaṃdho dṛḍhavrataḥ  || 3.46 ||
tretāyāḥ prathame pāde gaṅgā bhāgīrathī smṛtā  |
adahatkapilaścāsya pitṝṇāmayutāni ṣaṭ || 3.47 ||
mohitāmāyayā viṣṇorgatā saptarasātalam  |
eva mandākinī nāma tvanyā gaṅgā saridvarā  || 3.48 ||
mekalātoyasampūrṇāssāgarāssaptayantritāḥ  |
tṛtīye ca tathā pāde avatīrṇā sarasvatī  || 3.49 ||
sthāneśvaragatā sā tu punargaṅgā samāgame  |
gaṅgākanakhale puṇyā gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  || 3.50 ||
ūrdhvatīrthe prayāge ca vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
prācī sarasvatī yatra kurukṣetre ca puṇyadā  || 3.51 ||
praṇaṣṭe dvāvaśāditye pralaye samupasthite  |
saptakalpakṣaye vṛtte na mṛtā tena narmadā  || 3.52 ||
saritaśca kṣayaṃ yānti gaṅgādyāśca sahasraśaḥ  |
narmadā tiṣṭhate devī saptakalpānugāminī  || 3.53 ||
brāhmī sarasvatī mūrtivaiṣṇavī tripathā smṛtā  |
narmadā śāṅkarī mūrtirnadyastisrastridevatāḥ  || 3.54 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva sarayūśca sarasvatī  |
śatabhāgā candrabhāgā ramyā sindhurmahānadī  || 3.55 ||
irāvatī ca kapilā narmadā savitastikā  |
daṇḍakī gaṇḍakī caiva ghargharā ca mahānadī  || 3.56 ||
śoṇo mahānadaścaiva vedikā ca taraṅgiṇī  |
brahmavāhā viṣṇuvāhā sāraṅgā gautamī tathā  || 3.57 ||
viśvavāhā dhenumatī apārā aparā tathā  |
vetravatī ca kumudā mahātāpī payoṣṇikā  || 3.58 ||
veṇā ca dugdhikā śiprā'jahāsā'bhramatī tathā  |
kṛṣṇā bhīmarathī caiva tuṅgabhadrā mahānadī  || 3.59 ||
godāvarīti vikhyātā gaṅgā sā dakṣiṇā smṛtā  |
nadyaścaiva tathā cānyāssarvatīrthāni sāgarāḥ  || 3.60 ||
sarvāstāḥ pralayaṃ yānti varjayitvā tu kalpagām  |
gaṅgā kiṃ varṇyate devī hareṇa śirasā dhṛtā  || 3.61 ||
gaurī vārddhaśarīrasthā śivasya parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
narmadā varṇyate devī saptakalpānugāminī  || 3.62 ||
te deśāḥ parvatāḥ puṇyāste grāmāste'pi cāśramāḥ  |
yatra yātā saricchreṣṭhā narmadā saptakalpagā  || 3.63 ||
tribhiḥ sārasvataṃ puṇyaṃ saptāhena tu yāmunam  |
sadyaḥ punāti gāṅgeyaṃ darśanādeva narmadā  || 3.64 ||
revātaṭeṣu ye vṛkṣāḥ patitāḥ kālaparyaye  |
narmadātoyasaṃspṛṣṭāste'pi yānti parāṅgatim  || 3.65 ||
revāyā yatra kutrāpi saṅgame bharatarṣabha  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛpūjanam  || 3.66 ||
devatārādhanaṃ dīkṣā nyāso dehavisarjanam  |
yatkiṃcitkriyate martyaistadanantaphalaṃ smṛtam  || 3.67 ||
gosahasraśataṃ dhenu mahādānāni kṛtsnaśaḥ  |
vaiśākhyāmathavā mādhyāṃ kārttikyāṃ grahaṇāyane  || 3.68 ||
viṣuve saṃkrame bhānorvyatīpāte ca vaidhṛtau  |
darśe dinakṣaye vṛddhau manvādiṣu yugādiṣu  || 3.69 ||
kalpādau ca yugādau ca mātāpitroḥ kṣaye'hani  |
oṃkāre vā bhṛgau kṣetre viśeṣādupasaṅgame  || 3.70 ||
revāyāṃ snānadānāni japahomārcanādikam  |
yaḥ kuryānmanujaśreṣṭhaḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 3.71 ||
brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpairmucyate nā'tra saṃśaya  |
kulānāṃ śatamāgāmi samatītaṃ tathā śatam  || 3.72 ||
uddharedātmanā sārddhaṃ revātīrthāvagāhanāt |
grāmārddhaṃ grāmamekaṃ vā yo dadyānnarmadātaṭe  || 3.73 ||
sa labhedvipulāṃ lakṣmīmatyantaphalamaśnute  |
narmadātīrasambhūtāṃ mṛdaṃ mūrdhni bibharti yaḥ  || 3.74 ||
bibharti mūrtimarkasyatamonāśāya kevalam  |
yatra yatra naraḥ snāyātnarmadāyā yudhiṣṭhira  || 3.75 ||
prāpnuyādaśvamedhasya phalametacchivoditam  |
narmadātoyapānasya snānasya prekṣaṇasya ca  || 3.76 ||
api cāndrāyaṇaśata tulyaṃ bhavati vā na vā  |
narmadā kīrtayedyastu prātarutthāya mānavaḥ  || 3.77 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  |
saṅgamena samāyuktā narmadāliṅgasaṅgatā  |
hayamedhaphalaṃ tatra snātvā śivapuraṃ vrajet || 3.78 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe revādvitīyā'vatārakathanaṃ nāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 4

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
hiraṇyatejasā pūrva rājñā vai narmadā kathaṃ  |
martye'vatāritā devī tatsarvaṃ vaktumarhasi  || 4.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
hiraṇyatejā rājarṣiḥ somavaṃśe mahīpate  |
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ prajāpatisamo'bhavat || 4.2 ||
ekachatrāṃ śaśāsorvīṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
khyātā candrapurī tasya śakrasyevāmarāvatī  || 4.3 ||
nirābādhāḥ prajāstatra bhayadāridryavarjitāḥ  |
cirāyuṣo narāstatra samālakṣaṃ tu jīvinaḥ  || 4.4 ||
svayaṃ kāmadughā dhenurdharaṇī sasyaśālinī  |
kauśeyapaṭṭavastrāśca vṛkṣe vṛkṣe samudbhavā  || 4.5 ||
etasminnantare prāpte hyamāvāsyāṃ ravigrahe  |
satkāro'trāsti devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 4.6 ||
vāpīkūpasaro divyaṃ jambūdvīpaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
sravantī nimnagā kācittasmin dvīpe na vidyate  || 4.7 ||
gavāṃ śatasahasrāṇi hemaratnaṃ maṇīṃstathā  |
kośaṃ hayānasaṃkhyātānmattāṃśca varadantinaḥ  || 4.8 ||
graste ca rāhuṇā sūrye brāhmaṇān pradadau nṛpaḥ  |
puro dharmasahasrāṇi hemaratnamaṇīṃstathā  || 4.9 ||
atarpayatpitṝṃścāpi havyakavyena bhārata  |
kaśmalaṃ jalapānaṃ cāpaśyatpitṛjugupsitam  || 4.10 ||
kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭhatālvoṣṭhān kaśmalāṃśca mṛdambhasā  |
nagnānmalinavastrāṃśca śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 4.11 ||
hiraṇyatejā rājarṣiḥ paśyati sma tadā pitṝn  |

hiraṇyatejā uvāca - 
ke yūyaṃ vikṛtākārāḥ pretabhūtā bubhukṣitāḥ  || 4.12 ||
majjante narake ghore raurave lomaharṣaṇe  |
kathayadhvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ karmmaṇā kena pāvitāḥ  || 4.13 ||

pitara ūcu̱ḥ
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha ca  |
sariddhīnamidaṃ dvīpaṃ dharmakarmavināśitam  || 4.14 ||
na devāstṛptimāyānti pitaro vā kathañcana  |
pitṝnmocayituṃ śaktā durārādhyā surairapi  || 4.15 ||
āgamiṣyati dvīpe'smin revā muktirbhaviṣyati  |
etatte kathita rājan yatheṣṭaṃ kartumarhasi  || 4.16 ||

hiraṇyatejā uvāca - 
pitṝṇāṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ yathā yānti parāṅgatim  |
mayā rājyena kiṃ kāryaṃ jīvitaśca nirarthakam  || 4.17 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājā hyudayaṃ siddhiparvatam  |
kandamūlaphalāhāraḥ śivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  || 4.18 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu ugre tapasi saṃsthita  |
rājñastu paramāṃ bhaktiṃ jñātvā vai tatra tryambakaḥ  || 4.19 ||
vṛṣārūḍhastrinetraśca devadevo maheśvara  |
pratyakṣa tasya rājñaśca tadātmānamadarśayat || 4.20 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ devadevasya bhārata  |
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  || 4.21 ||
stotraṃ cakāra devasya śambhoramitatejasaḥ  |
namaste'stu sureśāna śūlapāṇe namo'stu te  || 4.22 ||
pṛthivyāpaśca tejaśca vāyurākāśameva ca  |
śabdaṃ sparśaśca rūpaśca raso gandhaśca pañcamaḥ  || 4.23 ||
buddhirmanastvahaṅkāraḥ prakṛtiśca gaṇāstrayaḥ  |
sarvākṣaḥ sarvago devasakalo niṣkalo'vyaya  || 4.24 ||
jihvācāpalyabhāvena kleśito'si mayā prabho  |
brahmaviṣṇvādi devaiśca tavādyanta na labhyate  || 4.25 ||
kathaṃ tu mānuṣā pāpāḥ stotuṃ śaktā umādhavam  |
hiraṇyatejasaḥ stotraṃ śrutvā devo jagatpatiḥ  || 4.26 ||
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi rocate  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā śūlapāṇiṃ maheśvaram  || 4.27 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  |
saptakalpavahāṃ devīṃ narmadāmavatāraya  || 4.28 ||
pitṝṇāṃ majjatāṃ ghore narake dāruṇe bhṛśam  |
mucyante te yathā pretāstṛptā yānti parāṅgātim  || 4.29 ||
evaṃ varamahaṃ manye tvatprasādādumāpate  |

īśvara uvāca - 
ayācyaṃ yācitaṃ tāta brahmaviṣṇusurāsuraiḥ  || 4.30 ||
nāvatārayituṃ śakyā tathānyairalpajīvibhiḥ  |
anyaṃ yācasva bhadraṃ te varaṃ dāsye'dhunā tava  || 4.31 ||

rājovāca - 
tato rājā mahābhāgaḥ provāca śaśibhūṣaṇam  |
tvayi tuṣṭe mahādeve lokanāthe jagadgurau  || 4.32 ||
sādhyāsādhyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ triṣu lokeṣu kiṃcana  |
janmāntarasahasreṇa vara nānya vṛṇomyaham  || 4.33 ||
tyaktvā caiva saricchreṣṭhāṃ narmadāṃ saptakalpagām  |
dīyatāṃ mama deveśa bhṛtyabhṛtyo'smi śādhi mām  || 4.34 ||
hiraṇyatejaso jñātvā niścayaṃ mānasaṃ tadā  |
āhūtā ca tato devī narmadā lokapāvanī  || 4.35 ||
makarāsanamārūḍhā divyābharaṇabhūṣitā  |
śyāmavarṇā mahātejā śivasyāgre vyavasthitā  || 4.36 ||
umāmaheśvarau natvā pādagrahaṇapūrvakam  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devīṃ kimarthaṃ deva saṃsmṛtā  || 4.37 ||
ādeśaṃ deva me dehi tvadādeśe sthitā hyaham  |

īśvara uvāca - 
hiraṇyatejā nṛpatistyaktvā rājya ca narmade  || 4.38 ||
caturdaśaṃ tapastepe ghorarūpaṃ sudāruṇam  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu ugre tapasi saṃsthitaḥ  || 4.39 ||
kandamūlaphalāhāraḥ śivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  |
jambūdvīpe'vatāraṃ tvaṃ kuruṣva dharaṇītale  || 4.40 ||
kṣipraṃ yāhi varārohe saṃsārārṇavatāriṇi  |
pretarūpān pitṝn sarvānnarakasthān samuddhara  || 4.41 ||
mucyante yena cāghaughāddhūtapāpā yathepsitam  |

narmadovācā̱
maheśvaraṃ śūlapāṇimevamastviti cābravīt || 4.42 ||
nirādhārā kathaṃ deva jambūdvīpe samāśraye  |
āhūtāste tatastatra parvatāśca kulākulāḥ  || 4.43 ||
kṣaṇamātraṃ tu tiṣṭhadhvaṃ yena yāti saridvarā  |
ūcuśca parvatāstaṃ ca aśaktā dhāraṇe vayam  || 4.44 ||

narmadovācā̱
aśaktān dhāraṇe cainān prapaśyāmi jagatpate  |
mama toyaughapātena kallolapravaṇāvṛtāḥ  || 4.45 ||
śatadhā bhedamāyānti girayo vajradāritāḥ  |
teṣāṃ madhye samutthāya prābravīdudayācalaḥ  || 4.46 ||
śivaprasādasampanno hyahaṃ dhārayituṃ kṣamaḥ  |
tataḥ pracalitā devī dattvā pādaṃ nagopari  || 4.47 ||
gaganātpracyutā devī papāta dharaṇītale  |
vāyuvegā punarbhūtvā vāruṇīṃ diśamāśritā  || 4.48 ||
akālapīḍite loke jambūdvīpe carācare  |
hāhākāro mahānāsīttriṣu lokeṣu bhārata  || 4.49 ||
śrutvā ca kalkalāśabdaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ lomaharṣaṇam  |
pātālādutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ jvalitaṃ dīptatejasam  || 4.50 ||
huṅkāreṇaiva tadbhadre narmadāmabravīdvaca  |
sarvapāpahare devi maryādāṃ vaha suvrate  || 4.51 ||
tvaddhāraṇārthamīśena nisṛṣṭāḥ parvatāstrayaḥ  |
vindhyaścaturthakastatra śreṣṭhaḥ sarvamahībhṛtām  || 4.52 ||
meruśca himavāṃstatra kailāsaśca tṛtīyakaḥ  |
tadā bhittvā triśūlena kīlitā vasudhātale  || 4.53 ||
dvātriṃśattu sahasrāṇi pravāha pūrvapaścime  |
sahasrārddhaśca vistāraṃ dakṣiṇottaramānataḥ  || 4.54 ||
nṛtyanti devatāssarvāḥ śaṅkhavāditranisvanaiḥ  |
siddhā vidyādharā yakṣā gandharvā kinnarā narāḥ  || 4.55 ||
ādityā vasavo rudrā viśvedevā marudgaṇāḥ  |
stuvanti parayā bhaktyā narmadāṃ saptakalpagām  || 4.56 ||
hiraṇyatejā rājarṣirnarmadāṃ cedamabravīt |
anugrahaḥ kṛto devi pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇāya me  || 4.57 ||
uvāca kalpagā rājaṃstvatto'nyaḥ ko nu vai bhuvi  |
prabhāva īdṛśo yasya rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  || 4.58 ||
tapastaptvā maheśasya mamārthaṃ tvaṃ yato'nagha  |
mātṛkāḥ paitṛkā ye ye sāntaḥ puraparigrahāḥ  || 4.59 ||
mama prabhāvātnṛpate umāmāheśvaraṃ puram  |
namaskṛtya suraśreṣṭhāṃ putro'haṃ te varānane  || 4.60 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā ca vidhivatpitṝndevāṃśca tarpayan  |
śrāddhaṃ piṇḍapradānaṃ ca tatra sarvamakalpayat || 4.61 ||
narakāduddhṛtāssarve devayānapathe sthitāḥ  |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgāśca pakṣiṇaścāṇḍajāśca ye  || 4.62 ||
bhūtagrāmaḥ samagraśca reje vidyādhare pure  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannādisargāvakalpanam  || 4.63 ||
kīrtitāni mayā revāvatārāṇi viśāmpate  |
āgatā yena mārgeṇa lokānugrahakāmyayā  || 4.64 ||
eṣo'vatāraḥ prathama ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
dvitīyastu tathā skandadakṣasāvarṇike'ntare  || 4.65 ||
tṛtīyaḥ purūravasā tathā vaiṣṇavake'ntare  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathā dṛṣṭaṃ purātanam  || 4.66 ||
snānāvagāhanātpānātsmaraṇātkīrtanādapi  |
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoramaghaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt || 4.67 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kailāso himavānmeruḥ kena kāryyeṇa suvrata  |
narmadātīramāsādya nikṣiptāḥ parvatāstrayaḥ  || 4.68 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
unmārgagāminīṃ revāṃ vindhyo vai dhartumakṣamaḥ  |
āpagā toyapūreṇa plāvitaṃ jagatītalam  || 4.69 ||
kṛtvā vrajantīṃ dṛṣṭvā vai śaṅkaro lokaśaṅkaraḥ  |
bhittvā bhūmi triśūlena kīlitāḥ parvatāstrayaḥ  || 4.70 ||
atrāntare ca vaidūryyagirau vai paryakalpata  |
etatsarvaṃ samāsena śaṃsitaṃ ca mayānagha  || 4.71 ||
kathayiṣye tathaivānyadyadyadicchasi bhārata  || 4.72 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 5

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
rājñā'vatāritā revā kathaṃ vai purukutsunā  |
tatsarva śrotumicchāmi tvatto brahmavidāṃ vara  || 5.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāṃ purā skandasya kīrtitām  |
tatte'haṃ kathayiṣyāmi śivenoktaṃ śrutaṃ mayā  || 5.2 ||
kṣatriyasya kulotpannaḥ purukutsurmahāyaśāḥ  |
tena varṣasahasraṃ vai pūrvamārādhito haraḥ  || 5.3 ||
tapasā ca bhavenoktaṃ kastvayā prārthito varaḥ  |
dadāmi te varaṃ hyetadyena tvaṃ sukhamedhase  || 5.4 ||
evamuktastadā rājā prārthaye'haṃ tavājñayā  |
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva kāryaṃ tatkathayāmyaham  || 5.5 ||
āste nadī mahābhāgā narmadā nāma viśrutā  |
avatārayatāṃ śambho kumārīṃ divyarūpiṇīm  || 5.6 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śambhurvismayamāgataḥ  |
suciraṃ kliśyamānena kṣatriyeṇa mahātmanā  || 5.7 ||
aprārthyaṃ prārthitaṃ skanda duḥsādhyaṃ tridaśairapi  |
uktāśayaḥ sa rājarṣiḥ sādhyaṃ kiṃcinniṣevaya  || 5.8 ||
mama vākyaṃ tu tacchrutvā kṣatriyastu sa vihvalaḥ  |
apatatsa mahābhāgo mūrchito dharaṇītale  || 5.9 ||
āśvāsito mayā tatra svastho bhava viśāmpate  |
martyaloke saricchreṣṭhāṃ narmadāmavatāraye  || 5.10 ||
tasyāstvāgamanaṃ śrutvā trastā devāḥ savāsavāḥ  |
kaḥ śaknuyādimaṃ bhāraṃ soḍhuṃ vegaśca nārmadam  || 5.11 ||
dharaṇī bhārabhūtā ca naṣṭā bhraṣṭāśca parvatāḥ  |
aśaktāstoyavarṣasya dhāraṇe saritastathā  || 5.12 ||
asti siddho mahāpuṇyaḥ paryaṅko nāma parvataḥ  |
sa saheta mahābhāraṃ voḍhuṃ vegaśca nārmadam  || 5.13 ||
sarve devā'gatāstatra prārthituṃ taṃ nagottamam  |
pṛṣṭāstu parvatendreṇa kena kāryyeṇa cāgatā  || 5.14 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
ikṣvākuvaṃśasambhūtaḥ purukutsurnarādhipaḥ  |
tenāvatāritā puṇyā nadī nāmā tu narmadā  || 5.15 ||
tejaḥ śabdaṃ ca bhāraṃ ca tasyāḥ pātaṃ rayaṃ tathā  |
asamarthā nagā voḍhuṃ tvāmṛte girisattama  || 5.16 ||

paryaṅka uvāca - 
āgacchatu mahādevī dhārayāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
āgacchantīṃ mahāpuṇyāṃ dhārayāmi ca sāmpratam  || 5.17 ||
calitā narmadā śīghraṃ vegena samupasthitā  |
kṛtvā tasyottamāṅgeṣu paryaṅkasyaiva bhūbhṛtaḥ  || 5.18 ||
rājñā tu sahitā devī tridaśairapi saṃyutā  |
vainyena tu purā ceṣṭamaśvamedhena bhūbhujā  || 5.19 ||
makhasthāne ca vainyasya veṇastambo mahānabhūt |
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayaṃ prāptāssarve devāssavāsavāḥ  || 5.20 ||
veṇostasyaiva mūlāttu nirgatā sā mahānadī  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā devāsthitāḥ sarve'tra saṃsthitāḥ  || 5.21 ||
jaya śabdaṃ prakurvantastridaśā brahmavādinaḥ  |
gaṇagandharvayakṣāśca marutaśca tathā'śvinau  || 5.22 ||
piśācā rākṣasā nāgā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
sarve pādyena cārdhyeṇa bhītāste kiṅkarā iva  || 5.23 ||
pādyopasaṃgrahaṃ kṛtvā narmadāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  |
adyaḥ naḥ saphalaṃ janma tapo'dya saphalaśca naḥ  || 5.24 ||
kṛtārthā devatāḥ sarvā devi tvaddarśanādiha  |
tameva puruṣaṃ manye yenaiṣā hyavatāritā  || 5.25 ||
vadanti devatāssarvā martyalokaḥ paraṃ gataḥ  |
ṛṣibhirdaivataiḥ proktā spṛśa hastena devatāḥ  || 5.26 ||
yena pūtatvamāyānti narmade naṣṭakilviṣāḥ  |
narmadā tu vadatyevaṃ na spṛśāmi surān gaṇān  || 5.27 ||
kumārī hyahamadyā'pi mama bhartā na vidyate  |
aho kaṣṭaṃ tu devāśca cintayā vihvalīkṛtāḥ  || 5.28 ||
tvatulyo rūpasampannaḥ kutaḥ prāpyo varottamaḥ  |
yena tvaṃ darśitā loke sa te bhartā bhaviṣyati  || 5.29 ||
brahmaṇastu purā śāpātsamudraḥ purukutsukaḥ  |
gato martye mahābāhurikṣvākukulanandanaḥ  || 5.30 ||
purukutsurvaraste'stu kṣatriyo devasannibhaḥ  |
udvāhitā tadā tena kṣatriyeṇa tu narmadā  || 5.31 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devānnarmadā saptakalpagā  |
devatvamīdṛśa yasya prajā dharme vyavasthitāḥ  || 5.32 ||
kimanyattasya vācyaṃ syātpurukutsormahātmanaḥ  |
svayambhuvo yathā putro mānasaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 5.33 ||
purukutsustathā cā'yaṃ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā purukutsuśca narmadām  || 5.34 ||
narmade devakanyā tvaṃ prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  |
yāntu me pitaraḥ svargaṃ mama cā'stu mahadyaśaḥ  || 5.35 ||

narmadovāca - 
eva bhavatu rājendra mattastvaṃ yadyadicchasi  |
tatsarvaṃ bhavataścādya matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 5.36 ||
evamuktvā yayau devī vāruṇīṃ ca diśāṃ tadā  |
niṣkrāntā parvatāttasmātparyaṅkātsaritāṃ varā  || 5.37 ||
yāti vegāttathā devī dhanurmuktaḥ śaro yathā  |
vidārya medinīṃ sarvā parvatāṃ chikharāṇi ca  || 5.38 ||
cūrṇayitvā ca tān sarvān vajrāśanihatāniva  |
yatra yatra gatā vindhye tatra tatrāvagāhyate  || 5.39 ||
tatra gaṅgāsahasrasya phala syāttīrthavarjite  |
tadā stutā mahādevī narmadā lokapāvanī  || 5.40 ||
ṛṣibhirvaidikaiḥ sarvaiḥ sukhasantānakārikā  |
vedā dharmasya mūlāni smṛtipuṣpaphalāni ca  || 5.41 ||
bhakṣayanti dvijāḥ puṇyā agnihotraparāyaṇāḥ  |
sevante narmadā te'pi svargasopānapaddhatim  || 5.42 ||
yatra yatra gatā skanda tatra tatraiva durlabhā  |
darśane narmadāyāstu agniṣṭomaphala labhet || 5.43 ||
ye tu saṃsnānti sevante narmadāmapyakāmataḥ  |
phalaṃ bahu suvarṇasya te'pi prāpsyanti mānavāḥ  || 5.44 ||
yatra yatra gatā puṇyā śivasyāyatane śubhe  |
gaṅgāsnānasya lakṣeṇa tatra snāne sama bhavet || 5.45 ||
agnihotreṇa yatpuṇya pitṛśrāddhena yatphalam  |
sarvaṃ sampadyate tasya narmadāyā jalena tu  || 5.46 ||
yatkiṃcidvaidikaṃ karmma brāhmaṇāḥ kurvate sadā  |
narmadāyāstaṭe śūddhirnātra kāryyā vicāraṇā  || 5.47 ||
narmadāsaṅgamo yatra śivakṣetraṃ vinirdiśet |
gaṅgāyā lakṣamātreṇa tatsthānan tu viśiṣyate  || 5.48 ||
kīrtanaṃ narmadāyāstu dānaṃ tīrthasya saṅgame  |
na tena sadṛśaṃ rājan satyametadbravīmyaham  || 5.49 ||
narmadāṃ prātarutthāya ye smaranti vicakṣaṇāḥ  |
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpamaihikaśca vinaśyati  || 5.50 ||
narmadāṃ mānavaḥ kaścittatra yatrāvagāhate  |
śatajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇātnaśyati dhruvam  || 5.51 ||
narmadāyāstaṭe caiva prāṇāṃstyajati mānavaḥ  |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  || 5.52 ||
ye'pyadharmaparādhīnā narmadāyāṃ kathañcana  |
te'pi rudratvamāyānti satyametadbravīmyaham  || 5.53 ||
prāyaścittāni dīyante yatra revā na vidyate  |
revātoye tu saṃprāpte prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate  || 5.54 ||
narmadāṃ ca mahāpuṇyāṃ saptakalpādhivāsinīm  |
satataṃ yaḥ smaredbhaktyā sa śivaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 5.55 ||
tatra vaiṇyeśvaraṃ devaṃ veṇumūle vyavasthitam  |
svargadaṃ mokṣadaṃ viddhilokānāṃ pāpanāśanam  || 5.56 ||
vindhyasyaiva tathā cāṣṭau mānasāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  |
paryaṅkaḥ prathamo jñeyaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvamahībhṛtām  || 5.57 ||
asitāṅgo varāṅgaśca tathā'nye cāpi parvatāḥ  |
pāvako jātavedāśca kadambaḥ suranāyakaḥ || 5.58 ||
arcitāstairmahābhāgairdharmakarmaparāyaṇaiḥ  |
udgamātnirgamaṃ yāvadvāruṇīṃ diśamāsthitāḥ  || 5.59 ||
sārddhamekaśataṃ tasyāḥ pravāhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  |
krośārddhasya tribhāgaśca vistāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 5.60 ||
śivasya vartate cātra brahmaviṣṇuṃ viḍaujasām  |
koṭibhistridaśānāntukā'nyā dhartuṃ praśakyate  || 5.61 ||
ātmānamātmanā sā tu babhāra parameśvarī  |
tridaśānāṃ hitārthāya mānavānāṃ ca bhārata  || 5.62 ||
lakṣayojanaparyyantaṃ jambūdvīpaṃ pracakṣate  |
dviguṇaḥ pariveṣastu lavaṇodastu bhārata  || 5.63 ||
sahasrayojanaṃ viddhikanyādvīpañca bhārata  |
tasyāḥ pañcaśatī gamyā tvagamyā ca tathā'parā  || 5.64 ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ cātra puṇyapāpavibhāgayoḥ  |
prathamaṃ mānuṣo bhūtvā devatvamupapadyate  || 5.65 ||
kṣīṇe puṇye punaryāti mānuṣatvaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
karmmabhūmimimāṃ viddhi viditaṃ tava bhārata  || 5.66 ||
dharmeṇa karmmaṇā svargaḥ syādeva sukṛte kṛte  |
nadyāḥ pravāhe bhūbhāge kalpagāyā vyavasthitiḥ  || 5.67 ||
lokānāṃ tāraṇārthāya avatīrṇā saridvarā  |
atra svargaśca mokṣaśca narmadātīramāśritau  || 5.68 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathā vṛṣṭaṃ surottamaiḥ  || 5.69 ||
iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāyāścaturthāvatāre pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 6

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
mune mekalakanyāyā avatāraḥ śruto mayā  |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi tīrthāni ca yathākramam  || 6.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
vatsagulmātsamārabhya yāvadvai bhṛgukacchakaḥ  || 6.2 ||
atrāntare'pi tīrthānāṃ saṃkhyā kena tu śakyate  |
tathāpi te pravakṣyāmi yathā vṛṣṭaṃ viśāmpate  || 6.3 ||
narmadā saumyabhāge tu anantapuramavyayam  |
anantasiddhiliṅgaṃ ca sarvapāpaharaṃ param  || 6.4 ||
tīrthaṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ nāma kauberaṃ dhanadaṃ tathā  |
maṇibhadraṃ tathā yajñaṃ tattīrthasya prabhāvataḥ  || 6.5 ||
mokṣadaṃ kāmadaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvalokavyavasthitam  |
ṛṣīṇāmāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubham  || 6.6 ||
sāvarṇiḥ kauśikaścaiva muniścaivāghamarṣaṇaḥ  |
śākalyaśca kuśākarṇiḥ śarabhaṅgo'gnirbhagaḥ  || 6.7 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyāttapastaptvā divaṅgatāḥ  || 6.8 ||
vālmīkirṛṣivaryo vai naiṣādīṃ yonimāśritaḥ  |
prabhāvāttasya tīrthasya brahmatejo vapurdharaḥ  || 6.9 ||
ikṣvākuḥ kuvalayāśvo dilīpo nahuṣastathā  |
veṇuryayātī rājā ca ajapālasma haihayaḥ  || 6.10 ||
ete cānye ca rājāno hyanantapuravāsinaḥ  |
atrāntare maheśānaṃ mekalātīramāśritam  || 6.11 ||
tamabhyarcya vidhānena sarve te tridivaṅgatāḥ  |
akhilairanyatīrthaiśca cirakālaphalapradaiḥ  || 6.12 ||
atrāste svargadaḥ śrīmān svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ  |
tīrthaṃ saptaṛṣirnāma saptasārasvataṃ nṛpa  || 6.13 ||
amartyasambhavaṃ liṅgaṃ tathaivāraṇyakeśvaram  |
aghaughanāśanaṃ tīrthaṃ tathānyatkalmaṣāpaham  || 6.14 ||
pañcabrahmamayaṃ tīrthaṃ sahasraśirasaṃ haram  |
vārāhaṃ vāmanaṃ tīrthaṃ yamatīrthaṃ mahotkaṭam  || 6.15 ||
saurabhaṅgaṃ sahasraṃ tu aśvamedhaṃ mahāmakham  |
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ sāvitryāṃ cāturvedaṃ ca pāvanam  || 6.16 ||
etatsarva vijānīhi lokānāṃ pāvanaṃ param  |
tathā'nantapuraṃ yāvatparyaṅkātpaścime śubham  || 6.17 ||
atrāntare mṛtā ye ca dānadharmavivarjitāḥ  |
te'pi svarge mahīyante yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 6.18 ||
tato dvīpeśvaraṃ nāma vyāsatīrthamanuttamam  |
tatra snātvā tu labhate hayamedhaphalaṃ naraḥ  || 6.19 ||
kāmikaṃ tatparaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
pradakṣiṇāṃ ca yaḥ kuryādvyāsatīrthasya bhārata  || 6.20 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasundharā  |
naro vā yadi vā nārī śivaloke mahīyate  || 6.21 ||
dvīpeśvare tatassnātvā vṛṣabhaṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
kāñcanena vimānena rudraloke mahīyate  || 6.22 ||
kārtikasya caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe jitendriyaḥ  |
snāpayedyaḥ śivaṃ tatra hyupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  || 6.23 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudraloke mahīyate  |
vyāsatīrthaṃ tu vai gatvā sarve brahmādayaḥ surāḥ  || 6.24 ||
stuvanti bhāvitātmānaṃ ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
ye cānye siddhagandharvakinnaroragarākṣasāḥ  || 6.25 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya modante gatakalmaṣāḥ  |
stuvanti vividhaiḥ stotrairmāṅgalyastuti saṃskṛtaiḥ  || 6.26 ||
yasyāsti śaktirasaśaktiriha pravīraḥ prordhvī karotu yadi vā'vanatiṃ karotu  |
yaḥ pūjitaḥ suravaraḥ śaśiśekhareṇa nā'rādhitaḥ sapadi bhūtapati surairvā  || 6.27 ||
kiṃ vā na vṛṣṭaṃ hi pitāmahena na vā surairvā madhusūdanena  |
kṣīrodamanthodbhavakālakūṭaṃ kaṇṭhe vṛtaṃ kena haraṃ vihāya  || 6.28 ||
ekena dagdhaṃ tripuraṃ śareṇa kāmo lalāṭākṣinirīkṣaṇena  |
bhinno'ndhakaḥ śūlavareṇa yena kastena sārddhaṃ kurute virodham  || 6.29 ||
jalaughakallolataraṅgabhaṅgā gaṅgā dhṛtā yena jaṭāgrabhāge
pādāmbujāṅguṣṭhanipīḍanena papāta laṅkādhipatirvisaṃjñaḥ  || 6.30 ||
surāsurāṇāmiha yatsamakṣaṃ vidhvaṃsito dakṣamakhaḥ kṣaṇena
praṇamya dakṣaḥ kṣayakārakasya lebhe varaṃ tārakamārakasya  || 6.31 ||
sarvasya pūjyaṃ hi varottamāṅgaṃ sampūjyate liṅgavaraṃ harasya
anena paryāptamatīva mūḍhaḥ prāptuṃ padaṃ yacchati yatkaroti  || 6.32 ||
brahmātra vṛddho hariratra liṅgaṃ surā'surāścaiva samarcayanti
tathāpi nūnaṃ suvicārayānti ko vā'dhiko vā'sti samo hareṇa  || 6.33 ||
tenaiva cakrāṅkasaroruhāṅkau liṅgāṅkitaṃ yasya jagadbhagāṅkam
haste prabaddhe navakaṅkaṇe vai paśyanti mūḍhāḥ khalu darpaṇena  || 6.34 ||
puṇyaṃ pavitraṃ stavanaṃ vyāsenoktaṃ mahātmanā  |
kṛtaṃ devādhināthasya sarvakalmaṣanāśanam  || 6.35 ||
pratipūjya yathā nyāyamarghapādyairanukramāt |
uvāca madhuraṃ vākyaṃ prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  || 6.36 ||
deyaṃ tasya phalaṃ deva yaḥ pumān chraddhayā'rcati  |
iti stutvā maheśānaṃ ṛṣayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 6.37 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle prayātāste yathātatham  |
anye ca ṛṣayo devā dvaipāyanamathābruvan  || 6.38 ||
āśramaste mahābhāga vāyasairākulīkṛtaḥ  |
yathā na vāyasāḥ ke'pi praviśanti tapovanam  || 6.39 ||
mriyante te sadā sarve mūrdhā teṣāṃ viśīryate  |
vyāsena śaptāste sarve vāyasā mahato bhayāt || 6.40 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe mahārāja praviśanti na karhicit |
ṛṣayo vacanaṃ śrutvā vyāsasya tu mahātmanaḥ  || 6.41 ||
narmadā dakṣiṇe kūle sarvameva kṛtaṃ kṣaṇāt |
tiṣṭhanti munayaḥ sarve devasyārādhane ratāḥ  || 6.42 ||
ārādhya vidhinā devamastuvan parameśvaram  |
yasyāsti śaktirityādi vyāsastutiparāyaṇāḥ  || 6.43 ||
stotraṃ tadetattridaśeśvarasya vyāsena bhaktyā kṛtamīśvarasya  |
prātaḥ paṭhedyaḥ smarati prayatnātkāle sa jāto'nucaro harasya  || 6.44 ||
vyāsāṣṭakamidaṃ puṇyaṃ yaḥ paṭhecchivasannidhau  |
vyāsastasya bhavetprīto narmadā ca prasīdati  || 6.45 ||
evaṃ stuto mahādevo vyāsena tu mahātmanā  |
śivo vyāsasya santuṣṭa idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 6.46 ||
kiṃ karomi sthito vipra varaṃ brūhi yathepsitam  |
varaṃ dadāmi te prīto yena tvaṃ sukhamedhase  || 6.47 ||

vyāsa uvāca - 
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tiṣṭha tvaṃ cottare śiva  |
āśramāste bhaviṣyanti sarve puṇyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 6.48 ||
evamuktvā tato vyāsaṃ tatraivāntara dhīyata  |
vyāso'pi cātra vatsyāmi dakṣiṇaṃ narmade vraja  || 6.49 ||
tataśca daṇḍakāṣṭhena cālayāmāsa narmadām  |
narmadā cābravīdvākyaṃ pūrvamārgeṇa sā gatā  || 6.50 ||
tato vyāsastu saṃkruddho yugāntāgnirivābhavat |
vyāsastu kupito rājan santīrṇo dvijasattamaḥ  || 6.51 ||
huṃkāritā ca sā devī nāgatā tatra narmadā  |
nāhaṃ kopavata vipra pralayaṃ yāmi karhicit || 6.52 ||
saptakalpakṣaye vṛtte na mṛtā'smīti narmadā  |
tato dvijastu saṃjāto dakṣiṇena tato gatā  || 6.53 ||
na vāritā ca sā'nena vyāsabhītā mahānadī  |
uttare munayaḥ sarve kūle tiṣṭhantyanekaśaḥ  || 6.54 ||
tataste hṛṣṭamanaso munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
vyāsena cārjitaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā'nyairmunipuṅgavaiḥ  || 6.55 ||
pitaro dvādaśābdāni tārpitāste narādhipa  || 6.56 ||

iti revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātye ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 7

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
anyattīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
aśvamedhasamudbhūtā nadī yatra varāṅganā  || 7.1 ||
snātvā ca saṅgame yatra hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
vyādhibhirmucyate yatra nārī vā yadi vānaraḥ  || 7.2 ||
śrāddhaṃ tatra prakurvantaḥ pitṝṇāṃ prītivardhanam  |
tripuryāṃ pūrvabhāge tu diśi yāmyāṃ śivaḥ sthitaḥ  || 7.3 ||
anugrahārthaṃ lokānāṃ śaivakṣetraṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
pañcāśītiḥ kurukṣetre paścāśītirivātra tu  || 7.4 ||
rāhusūryasamāyoge samānaṃ kīrtitaṃ suraiḥ  |
śrāddhe trīṇi ca lakṣāṇi purāṇe kīrtitāni vai  || 7.5 ||
tīrthānāṃ tāni atraiva tiṣṭhanti nṛpasattama  |
yatra tiṣṭhati deveśo mādhavo madhusūdanaḥ  || 7.6 ||
utpalāvartanāmā ca sahasramastako hariḥ  |
haraśca dvividhāve tau tṛtīyā caiva narmadā  || 7.7 ||
tasyāḥ kiṃ varṇyate rājan devairapi savāsavaiḥ  |
tatra snātvā mahārāja sambhavo na punarbhavet || 7.8 ||
pāpayoniṃ ca na viśedyamalokaṃ na paśyati  |
arcanāddevadevasya gāṇapatyamavāpyate  || 7.9 ||
utpanno hi hariryatra śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
upāsyate suraiḥ sarvairbrahmaśakrapurogamaiḥ  || 7.10 ||
krośamātraṃ harikṣetraṃ kathaṃ śocanti mānavāḥ  |
atrāntare mṛtā ye ca kṛmikīṭapataṅgamāḥ  || 7.11 ||
te'pi yānti harerlokaṃ kiṃ punarvaiṣṇavā nṛpa  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo'pi syātprāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  || 7.12 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vaidyādhare pure  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍadānena bhārata  || 7.13 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti tṛptā yānti parā gatim  |
ekādaśyāṃ nirāhāro gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet || 7.14 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpamālāṃ prabodhayet |
dvādaśyāṃ pañcagavyaṃ tu haviṣyānnena pāraṇam  || 7.15 ||
bhaktyā tu bhojayedviprān sahasraṃ ca sadakṣiṇām  |
oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya  ||
imaṃ mantraṃ japan yastu śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 7.16 ||
na tasya punarādhānaṃ janma caiva yudhiṣṭhira  |
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoraṃ tūlarāśimivānalaḥ  || 7.17 ||
tatkṣaṇāddahate kṛtsnamedhāṃsīvahutāśanaḥ  |
kalpakalpānugau devau nārāyaṇamaheśvarau  || 7.18 ||
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāsthāya revātīre vyavasthitau  |
arcitau ca tau deveśau surāsuragaṇaistathā  || 7.19 ||
siddhavidyādharairyakṣairgandharvaiḥ kinnarairnaraiḥ  |
sthāṇuḥ puṇyajalāvarte hyavatāra purākṛtaḥ  || 7.20 ||
anugrahāya lokānāṃ devānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
nanāda sumahānādaṃ ghorarūpaṃ bhayānakam  || 7.21 ||
pinākinā ca śūlena bhittvā caiva rasātalam  |
samānītā ca sāvitrī svargasopānapaddhatiḥ  || 7.22 ||
mānavāḥ kṣīṇapāpāśca tathā yānti parāṃ gatim  |
ajñānatama sā dhvastā nāvarohānti ye janāḥ  || 7.23 ||
ātmānaṃ nāvamanyante pāpopahatacetasaḥ  |
kalpagāṃ ye na sevante teṣāṃ janma nirarthakam  || 7.24 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatiratraiva liṅgānāṃ tu svayambhuvām  |
pūjane saṃsthitā rājan chivasya ca mahātmanaḥ  || 7.25 ||
sthāneśvaraṃ mahādevaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ tathā param  |
sapteśvaraṃ ca kalpeśaṃ hiraṇyaṃ jātavedasam  || 7.26 ||
prājāpatyaṃ siddhanāthaṃ śaśāṅkanayanaṃ tathā  |
anukeśaṃ tathā skandamāśvinaṃ taijasaṃ tathā  || 7.27 ||
brahmeśvaraṃ cāgnigarbhaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaṃ ca umāpatim  |
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ ca khaṭvāṅgaṃ mahākālaṃ ghaṭeśvaram  || 7.28 ||
trilocanaṃ tryambakaṃ ca devadevaṃ maheśvaram  |
etānyanyāni caiveha siddhaliṅgāni bhārata  || 7.29 ||
anaṅgaṃ kāmadevaṃ ca ratiprītisamanvitam  |
siddhamanvantaraṃ caiva yatrānaṅgatrayodaśī  || 7.30 ||
rambhātṛtīya tatraiva tathā kṛṣṇāṣṭamī nṛpa  |
vidyādharī ca tatraiva urvaśī ca tilottamā  || 7.31 ||
ahalyā menakā caiva tathā'nyāśca varāṅganāḥ  |
dākṣāyaṇī cānumatī campakā saṃbharāyaṇī  || 7.32 ||
etāścā'nyāśca tatraiva bahvyaḥ siddhā viśāmpate  |
keśavasya purī ramyā puṇyā pāpaharā nṛpa  || 7.33 ||
surāsurāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ dānavānāṃ ca bhārata  |
svargamārgapradā devī tathā hariharātmikā  || 7.34 ||
etatte kīrtitaṃ rājan yathā vṛṣṭaṃ purātanam  |
snānāvagāhanātpānācchravaṇātkīrtanādapi  || 7.35 ||
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoramaghaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt |
tatastasminmahābhāga kapilātīrthamuttamam  || 7.36 ||
revāyā uttare kūle sarvapāpaharaṃ param  |
tatra snātvā naro rājannārī vāpi jitendriyā  || 7.37 ||
tarpayitvā pitṝn devānmucyate ca ṛṇatrayāt |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu labhate paramāṅgatim  || 7.38 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vyāsatīrthamahimavarṇano nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 8

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
tripurī nāma vikhyātaṃ revāyā uttaretaṭe  || 8.1 ||
sapādalakṣatīrthāni yatra tiṣṭhanti bhārata  |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tatra liṅgānāṃ tu svayambhuvām  || 8.2 ||
tripuraḥ pātito rājan devadevena śūlinā  |
stotavyaṃ kiṃ paraṃ tatra surāsuraniṣevitam  || 8.3 ||
lokānugrahakaṃ devaṃ svayaṃ viddhi maheśvaram  |
gokarṇaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ bhogadaṃ mokṣadāyakam  || 8.4 ||
kīrtanāddevadevasya snapanenā'rcanena ca  |
toyena narmadāyāstu brahmahatyā praṇaśyati  || 8.5 ||
gandhadhūpapradīpaiśca tathā vibhavavistaraiḥ  |
api varṣasahasreṇa puṇyasaṃkhyā na jñāyate  || 8.6 ||
yadā tadā śive dānaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
dhanyāste mānavā rājan snātvā paśyanti ye haram  || 8.7 ||
kailāse sa vasenmartyastripuryāṃ yo vasennṛpa  |
akāmātkāmato vā'pi prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  || 8.8 ||
haṃsayuktavimānena kiṅkiṇīravaśālinā  |
chatreṇa dhriyamāṇena sauvarṇena virājatā  || 8.9 ||
jhallarītūryavādyena nṛtyagītotsavenaca  |
surāsurairvīkṣyamāṇaḥ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 8.10 ||
bhuṅkte tu vipulān bhogān yāvadicchanmaheśvare  |
evamevā'pi cānyāni yāni kāmyāni mānuṣaiḥ  || 8.11 ||
tripurāristu deveśo yatra tiṣṭhati bhārata  |
trayastriṃśatprasiddhānāṃ devānāṃ koṭibhiḥ śivaḥ  || 8.12 ||
tripuryāṃ nivasedyasmācchivakṣetramataḥ smṛtam  |
krośadvayapramāṇaṃ tu śivakṣetra prakīrtitam  || 8.13 ||
atrāntare mṛtā ye ca te prayānti śubhāṃ gatim  |
brahmaṇā tu purā ceṣṭaṃ brahmayajñaṃ makhottamam  || 8.14 ||
śakreṇa devarājena kṛtaṃ kratuśataṃ purā  |
gokarṇaṃ ca mahādevaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam  || 8.15 ||
vaṭeśvaraṃ tathā cānyaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ sureśvaram  |
īśvaraṃ caiva kāmeśamaśvibhyāmarcitaṃ haram  || 8.16 ||
anaṅgaṃ vāmadevaṃ ca kapoteśvarameva ca  |
sarveśvaraṃ somanāthaṃ ṛṇamocanameva ca  || 8.17 ||
kapālamocanaṃ devaṃ tathānyamaghanāśanam  |
indreśvaraṃ ca brahmeśaṃ śivaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ bhavam  || 8.18 ||
viśvadevaṃ siddhanāthamamaraṃ cāndrameva ca  |
siddhaṃ vidyādharaṃ yajñamatulaṃ vāsavaṃ tathā  || 8.19 ||
īśānamagnigarbhaṃ ca kuberamatulaṃ tathā  |
gāyatraṃ caiva sāvitraṃ rohiṇītīrthameva ca  || 8.20 ||
dākṣāyaṇī caiva satī dakṣayajñaharā smṛtā  |
ratnāvalī sūryapatnī sūryabhāmā ca vāruṇī  || 8.21 ||
viṣṇurmarīcirmaitreyaṛṣyaśṛṅgo vibhāṇḍajaḥ  |
tapasvī śaunako gargo durvāsā ugratāpasaḥ  || 8.22 ||
pañcāyutāni siddhāni tripuryāṃ nṛpasattama  |
api varṣasahasrāṇi na stotuṃ śakyate purī  || 8.23 ||
tripurīkṣetramāhātmyaṃ śakreṇāpi narādhipa  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi kṣatriyāṇāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 8.24 ||
dīkṣāyajñavidhānena nākapṛṣṭhamupāsate  |
itihāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi ādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 8.25 ||
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte kāpile kālasaṃjñake  |
manurnāma purā rājā cakravartī mahāyaśāḥ  || 8.26 ||
ayodhyāṃ sa purīṃ lebhe sūryavaṃśo mahīpatiḥ  |
samārādhya mahādevaṃ śaṅkaraṃ madhusūdanam  || 8.27 ||
avadhyā dānavairyā tu nirmitā viśvakarmaṇā  |
vāpīkūpasahasreṇa parikhā'ṭṭālakena vā  || 8.28 ||
dhanadasya purī yadvadalakā nāma viśrutā  |
ayodhyā śobhanā rājan chakrasyevāmarāvatī  || 8.29 ||
dhanadhānyasamākīrṇā sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitā  |
brahmaghoṣaninādena bhūmiṃ divamivākarot || 8.30 ||
sāgnihotraiśca vidvadbhirbrāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
caturvarṇāśca dharmaśca prākṛtā itare janāḥ  || 8.31 ||
sapādalakṣavarṣāṇi prajā sarvā ca jīvati  |
na kārmaṇyajarārogā durbhikṣaṃ na tu mṛtyubhīḥ  || 8.32 ||
svayaṃ kāmadughā dhenuḥ pṛthivī sasyaśālinī  |
anyāyena ca bhūteṣu dattā hartā na vidyate  || 8.33 ||
navakhaṇḍāṃ saptadvīpāṃ saṃśailavanakānanām  |
śaśāsa medinīṃ sarvāṃ yathā śakrastriviṣṭapam  || 8.34 ||
ekapatnīgṛhaṃ yadvadgṛhasthasya virājate  |
yajñadānasahasreṇa tarpitāssarvadevatāḥ  || 8.35 ||
yaṃ yaṃ prārthayate kāmaṃ taṃ tamāpa na saṃśayaḥ  |
eko doṣaḥ paraṃ tatra nadī naivā'tra vidyate  || 8.36 ||
rāhusomasamāyoge devakhātaṃ samāyayau  |
snānaṃ kartuṃ samādhāya brāhmaṇairvaidapāragaiḥ  || 8.37 ||
vimānānāṃ sahasreṇa gatvā viśaṃ nṛpottamaḥ  |
śaṅkhatūryaninādena veṇuvīṇāsvanena ca  || 8.38 ||
sāntaḥ puraparīvāro vīkṣyamāṇo'ṅganāgaṇaiḥ  |
nirvartya parvakāle tu dānahomavidhikriyām  || 8.39 ||
viveśa nagarīṃ puṇyāmayodhyāṃ devanirmitām  |
paurṇamāsyāṃ gate yāme svasthe caiva nṛpottame  || 8.40 ||
śrūyate kiṅkiṇīśabda ākāśe vyomacāriṇām  |
gītavāditrayuktānāṃ sahasraṃ paṅktiḥ vājinām  || 8.41 ||
manoharāṇāṃ yānānāṃ dṛṣṭvā caiva divaukasām  |
jagāma vismayaṃ rājā bhavanopari śobhitaḥ  || 8.42 ||
kasyaitāni vimānāni mamaiva bhavanopari  |
śayane śayane caiva kāmabhogavivārjitaḥ  || 8.43 ||
śokopahatacittasya cintayā vyākulīkṛtaḥ  |
kimidaṃ sāhasaṃ loke vimānānāṃ nabhopari  || 8.44 ||
evaṃ cintayatastasya sā niṣkrāntā niśā nṛpa  |
udite ca tathā sūrye dharmakarma samāpya vai  || 8.45 ||
vaśiṣṭhaṃ prāha rājarṣirabhivādya namaskṛtam  |
upaviṣṭaṃ yathā nyāyamāsane devanirmite  || 8.46 ||
itihāsapurāṇādi śrāvayan vidhipūrvakam  |
manunā ca purā pṛṣṭo vaśiṣṭho munipuṅgavaḥ  || 8.47 ||
kasyaitāni vimānāni mamopari mahāmune  |
kena karmmavipākena dānena niyamena ca  || 8.48 ||
saṃśayo me mahābhāga trikālajña nivedaya  |
kasmin deśe kṛto yajñaḥ svargakāmaphalapradaḥ  || 8.49 ||
yena yāmya kṣayāṃllokānnityāntaka vivārjitān  |
kṣatravaṃśasamutpanno yastu vai śikṣate kṣitim  || 8.50 ||
mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ vaṃśaṃ yajñamiṣṭvā divaṃ nayet |
sa jāto yena bhūlokaḥ sarvathāvadyavarjitaḥ  || 8.51 ||
anye putratvamāpannāste tu kleśāya kevalam  |
evamukto vaśiṣṭhastu manunā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha  |
prasannastvabravīttaṃ tu vaśiṣṭho munisattamaḥ  || 8.52 ||
śṛṇuṣva tvaṃ mahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
brahmaṇā gaditaṃ pūrvaṃ kaśyapasya mahāmuneḥ  || 8.53 ||
dakṣasyātrerguroścaiva prajāpatirakalpayat |
vedaśrutapurāṇoktaṃ tatra kṛtsnaṃ mayā śrutam  || 8.54 ||
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  |
purāṇavedabāhyaṃ tu karmma yatkriyate nṛpa  || 8.55 ||
na tatsantaḥ praśaṃsanti dharmahāniśca jāyate  |
narmadātīramāśritya tripurī nāma viśrutā  || 8.56 ||
yairiṣṭaṃ tatra yajñaistu dānahomabalikriyā  |
teṣāṃ rājan vimānāni sthitānyupari veśmanaḥ  || 8.57 ||
na tāṃ laṅghayituṃ śaktāḥ sarve devāssavāsavāḥ  |
atikrāmanti te svargaṃ śivena saha modante  || 8.58 ||
viṣādaṃ tyaja rājendra gahanā karmmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  |
anyakṣetre sahasraṃ tu dattaṃ taptaṃ hutaṃ tathā  || 8.59 ||
ekaṃ tu kalpagā tīre tulyaṃ bhavati vā na vā  |
śivena śrāvitaṃ cāsītpurāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 8.60 ||
jambūdvīpe mahārāja ekā devī tu narmadā  |
pāpakarmadurācārānnayate sma divaukasam  || 8.61 ||
te'pi yānti na sandehaḥ kalpagātoyadarśanāt |
dharmadhvajāśca ye martyāstriṣu lokeṣu viśrutāḥ  || 8.62 ||
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ pāpopahatacetasām  |
yānarūpāvarārohā trailokye sacarācare  || 8.63 ||
ekavaktrastu tatpuṇyaṃ na guṇān stotumarhati  |
tyaktvā caiva mahābhāga brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān  || 8.64 ||
saṃkhyāṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti tapaso dānakarmmaṇām  |
na gaṅgāyamunā cāpi nadī caiva sarasvatī  || 8.65 ||
irāvatī vitastā ca vipāśā kapilā tathā  |
śoṇaśca ghargharaścaiva sāraṅgā badarī tathā  || 8.66 ||
puṇyā mahānadī tāpī gaṇḍakī ca payoṣṇikā  |
tuṅgabhadrā mahāpuṇyā bhīmarathyā mahānadī  || 8.67 ||
tīrthāni sāgarāṇāṃ hi jambūdvīpe vasanti hi  |
devakhātataḍāgeṣu garteṣu ca saritsu ca  || 8.68 ||
kiṃ phalaṃ labhate martya iṣṭvā yajñairnṛpottama  |
anyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ puṇyakṣetre vinaśyati  || 8.69 ||
puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vajralepo bhaviṣyati  |
ayaṃ yathā tathā dharmaḥ samaṃ bhārata vartate  || 8.70 ||
tasmātpāpaṃ na kurvīta cañcale jīvite sati  |
śrutvākhyānamidaṃ rājā narmadākīrtanaṃ śubham  || 8.71 ||
ādideśa tato'mātyān bhṛtyāṃścaiva sahasraśaḥ  |
rājopaskaramādāya yūyaṃ gacchatamāciram  || 8.72 ||
dhenūnāṃ pañcalakṣāṇi savatsānāṃ ca bhārata  |
śyāmakarṇahayānāṃ ca lakṣamekaṃ śitatviṣām  || 8.73 ||
ayutaṃ ca karīndrāṇāṃ ghaṇṭābharaṇasaṃyujām  || 8.74 ||
hiraṇyakoṭīḥ pañcāśatsarvāsṛṅmaṇayastathā  |
sumuhūrte sunakṣatre candre caikādaśe śubhe  || 8.75 ||
nānādeśanṛpaiḥ sārddhaṃ gītavāditramaṅgalaiḥ  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ stūyamāno muhurmuhuḥ  || 8.76 ||
brāhmaṇairvedavidbhiśca prāyācca tripurīṃ nṛpaḥ  |
saptakalpavahāṃ puṇyāṃ surāsuranamaskṛtām  || 8.77 ||
ājanmarūḍhaistu pāpaiḥ sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  |
vimuktaḥ pṛthivīpālaḥ kalpagā toyadarśanāt || 8.78 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni āsamudrāntagocare  |
mekalātoyasaṃsparśātpavitrāṇīha tānyapi  || 8.79 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā yathā nyāyaṃ pitṝn devāṃśca tarpayan  |
arcayitvā maheśānaṃ gandhapuṣpavilepanaiḥ  || 8.80 ||
daśayojanaparyyantaṃ yajñarūpaṃ ca maṇḍapam  |
akārayanmahābāhuḥ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 8.81 ||
hemarūpyamayaṃ sarvaṃ yaccānyadbhojyabhājanam  |
agastyo gautamo gargo viṣṇuḥ śātātapastathā  || 8.82 ||
atriścaiva vaśiṣṭhaśca pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  |
bhṛguragnirmarīciśca kaśyapo'tha manustathā  || 8.83 ||
durvāsā yājñavalkyaśca bharadvājo'tha bhallukaḥ  |
viśvāmitro jamadagnī ṛṣyaśṛṅgo vibhāṇḍakaḥ  || 8.84 ||
dakṣaḥ parāśaro vyāsaḥ kāṣāyaṇabṛhaspatī  |
ete cānye ca bahava ṛṣayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 8.85 ||
caturvidyāvedavido yajñakarmmaviśāradāḥ  |
tīrthaṃ vai puṣkaraṃ yadvadbrahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam  || 8.86 ||
tatra prāvartayadyajñaṃ hayamedhamanuttamam  |
āhūtā devatāḥ sarvā devendraḥ pākaśāsanaḥ  || 8.87 ||
tarpitā arghapādyaiśca madhuparkaiśca viṣṭaraiḥ  |
tato nivartito yajño yathokto vedakarmmaṇā  || 8.88 ||
tarpitā brāhmaṇāssarve yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ  |
hārakeyūrakaṭakaiḥ kaṇṭhābharaṇabhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 8.89 ||
kecitkuṅjaramārūḍhāstathā ca hayasaṃsthitāḥ  |
divyayānasamārūḍhā divyamālāvalambinaḥ  || 8.90 ||
vayāṃsi pakṣiṇo yatra tathā'nye vanacāriṇaḥ  |
yajñocchiṣṭeṣu lulitā jātāḥ sarve hiraṇmayāḥ  || 8.91 ||
yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ prārthayate taṃ taṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  |
ghoṣaṇā kriyatāṃ rāṣṭre daṇḍahastaistu kiṅkaraiḥ  || 8.92 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāśca tṛptā yānti parāṃ gatim  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānā varaṃ dattvā divaṃ yayuḥ  || 8.93 ||
atra yajñastapodānaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  |
evaṃ nivartito yajño rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  || 8.94 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā manurityāha kalpagām  |
cāndrāyaṇasahasrasya somayāgaśatasya ca  || 8.95 ||
tvattoyapānamātreṇa samaṃ bhavati vā na vā  |
lokānāṃ tāraṇārthāya avatīrṇā mahānadī  || 8.96 ||
tvayā vyāptaṃ jagatkṛtsnaṃ lokāścaiva carācarāḥ  |
snānāvagāhanātpānātsmaraṇātkīrtanādapi  || 8.97 ||
anekabhāvikaṃ pāpaṃ tūlarāśimivānalaḥ  |
dahatyevaṃ hi toyaṃ te nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā  || 8.98 ||
svargasopānabhūtā'si pitṝṇāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
divaṃ naya varārohe bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  || 8.99 ||
yāḥ kāścitsarito loke tīrthāni vividhāni ca  |
teṣāṃ tvaṃ jananī devi pitṝṇāṃ tāriṇī parā  || 8.100 ||
tvayā vinā tu yattīrthaṃ dharmakarmaśubhodayam  |
sūryeṇaiva vihīnaṃ hi nirālokaṃ jagadyathā  || 8.101 ||
sūryācandramasorbhāvaḥ sāmānyaḥ sarvajantuṣu  |
samaṃ varṣati parjanyaḥ sasyeṣu ca tṛṇeṣu ca  || 8.102 ||
tathā tvaṃ sarvalokānāṃ mātā caiva garīyasī  |
api varṣasahasreṇa guṇān kīrtayituṃ śubhe  || 8.103 ||
brahmā bṛhaspatiścaiva na śakto'pi varānane  |
stotraṃ śrutvā mahābhāgā manoramitatejasaḥ  || 8.104 ||
pratyuvāca varārohā makarāsanasaṃsthitā  |
sarvābharaṇaśobhāḍhyā candrakāntinibhānanā  || 8.105 ||
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga tuṣṭā'smi manasīpsitam  |
namaskṛtya mahādevīṃ rājā vacanamabravīt || 8.106 ||
yadi tuṣṭā varārohe varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  |
tīrthabhūtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ kuruṣva varavarṇini  || 8.107 ||
ayodhyāviṣaye deśe sravantyaḥ sambhavantviti  |
nānāvidhāstu sarito gaṅgādyāstu surālaye  || 8.108 ||
yathā ca patitāssarvāstathā tvaṃ kalpage kuru  || 8.109 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe tripurīvarṇano nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 9

narmadovāca - 
tretāyāṃ prathame pāde tava vaṃśe nṛpottama  |
bhagīratha iti khyātaḥ sa gaṅgā mānayiṣyati  || 9.1 ||
sagarāḥ ṣaṣṭisāhasrā alābujasamudbhavāḥ  |
kapilasya tu mārgeṇa haye pātālagāmini  || 9.2 ||
piturājñā'vasāne tu vidārya dharaṇīṃ tataḥ  |
hayaṃ tu vāsudevena sāgarāste vidurbudhāḥ  || 9.3 ||
praviṣṭāḥ sāgarāste tu rasātalatalaṃ gatāḥ  |
tānahaṃ pūrayiṣyāmi ātmatoyena suvrata  || 9.4 ||
evaṃ vṛto varastāvaccheṣaṃ vṛṇu nareśvara  |
pāde dvitīye tretāyāḥ kālindī ca sarasvatī  || 9.5 ||
sarayūrgaṇḍakī nāma mahābhāgā viniḥsṛtāḥ  |
bhagīratha iti khyātastava vaṃśe bhaviṣyati  || 9.6 ||
bhāgīrathī ca vikhyātā bhaviṣyati saridvarā  |
gaṅgā ca jāhnavī caiva samabhāgā prakīrtitā  || 9.7 ||
khyātiṃ yāsyanti tāssarvāḥ kanyādvīpe na saṃśayaḥ  |
āgacchantī tu sā gaṅgā jahnunā ca maharṣiṇā  || 9.8 ||
hastenākṛṣya sā pītā yathānyatprākṛtaṃ jalam  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu udare ca visarpitā  || 9.9 ||
viṣaṇṇavadano rājā tadāsītsa bhagīrathaḥ  |
niṣkalaṃ me tapastaptaṃ śivasevā ca niṣkalā  || 9.10 ||
mayā tu sevitaṃ hyetajjagatsarvaṃ carācaram  |
kiṃ karomīti niścitya maheśaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 9.11 ||
śivājñā sayuto bhūtvā lokānāṃ hitakāmayā  |
āgataśca punarmohāttāṃ gaṅgāṃ sa mahāyaśāḥ  || 9.12 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājanṛṣiḥ krodhasamanvitaḥ  |
gaṅgāyā mokṣaṇaṃ kartuṃ yo māmārādhayiṣyati  || 9.13 ||
gaṅgāyā mokṣaṇaṃ tatra kartavya nā'tra saṃśayaḥ  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jahnoramitatejasaḥ  || 9.14 ||
tamevārāthayāmāsa tapasogreṇa bhārata  |
tatastuṣṭastu bhagavānmumocottaravāhinīm  || 9.15 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti loke'smiñjāhnavīti prakīrtitā  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃstretāyāṃ yadbhaviṣyati  || 9.16 ||
tatraivā'nyaṃ pravakṣyāmi markaṭītīrthamuttamam  |
yatra snātvā mahārāja kāmato'kāmato'pi vā  || 9.17 ||
cāndrāyaṇaśatasyoktaṃ yatpuṇyaṃ tadavāpnuyāt |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
saṃkṣepācchrutametaddhi vistareṇa tapodhana  || 9.18 ||
kathaṃ tu markaṭītīrthaṃ tanme kathaya suvrata  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
āsīttretāyuge rājā satyaseno mahātapāḥ  || 9.19 ||
rājñī tasya priyā cāsīnnāmnā śṛṅgāravallarī  |
jātismarā tu subhagā kevalaṃ markaṭānanā  || 9.20 ||
kadācitsa mahīpālo mṛgayā priyayā saha  |
jagāma narmadātīraṃ nānādrumalatāyutam  || 9.21 ||
sthāpayitvā tu tāṃ devīṃ vanāntaramagāttataḥ  |
krīḍamānā ca sā tatra vaṃśagulme svakaṃ śiraḥ  || 9.22 ||
vṛṣṭvā vismayamāpannā pārśvasthaṃ kaṃcidabravīt |
gṛhītvaitacchiraḥ śīghraṃ narmadāyā jale kṣipa  || 9.23 ||
nikṣiptamātre śirasi rājñī candrānanā'bhavat |
etasminnantare rājā prāptastatra priyā'ntikam  || 9.24 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ tasyā mukhaṃ pūrṇaśaśiprabham  |
pṛcchati sma priyāṃ rājā vismayāviṣṭacetanaḥ  || 9.25 ||
kathayāmāsa vṛttāntaṃ pūrvajanmasamudbhavam  |
atrāhaṃ markaṭīvāsaṃ tīre vai nārmade śūbhe  || 9.26 ||
kadācitkrīḍamānā'haṃ vaṃśaṃ bhittvā hyakāmataḥ  |
tataḥ kālavaśājjīrṇaṃ śarīraṃ patitaṃ jale  || 9.27 ||
śirastatraiva saṃlagnaṃ kapivaktrā'smi tena vai  |
idānīṃ narmadātoye nikṣipte śirasi priya  || 9.28 ||
mukhaṃ me tīrthamāhātmyāccandrabimbasamaprabham  |
śrutvā sa tīrthamāhātmyaṃ vismayotphullalocanaḥ  || 9.29 ||
purohitaṃ samāhūya snātuṃ tatra pracakrame  |
snātvā tatra vidhānena hemakoṭiśataṃ dadau  || 9.30 ||
tadā prabhṛti rājendra markaṭītīrthamucyate  |
pūrvabhāgasthitaṃ tasya bhṛgutīrthamanuttamam  || 9.31 ||
tatra snātvā tu kārtikyāṃ nara pāpātpramucyate  |
svargado mokṣadaścaiva devastu narakeśvaraḥ  || 9.32 ||
devasya cāgre vaṃśo vai sammukho yatra dṛśyate  |
pūrvabhāge sthitaṃ tatra tasmin vaṃśe tu nirmale  || 9.33 ||
trilocana iti khyātaṃ tathaiva bhrukuṭisthitam  |
tṛtīyaṃ locanaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhṛgustu munisattama  || 9.34 ||
praṇamya daṇḍavadbhūmau stotuṃ samupacakrame  |


bhṛguruvāca - 
praṇamāmi jane saṃsthaṃ bhūteśaṃ bhūtidaṃ haram  || 9.35 ||
bhāvyaṃ bhargaṃ paśupatiṃ bhuvaneśvarameva ca  |
doṣamātravihīnaṃ ca nityavijñānavigraham  || 9.36 ||
paradravyāpaharaṇātparadāraniṣevaṇāt |
parābhavātparābhūtaṃ rakṣa māṃ kalmaṣātprabho  || 9.37 ||
ātmābhimānamuditaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgurake tathā  |
kupathābhimukhaṃ dīnaṃ trāhi māṃ parameśvara  || 9.38 ||
dīnadvijavarasyārthe prajñāne parito bhava  |
vṛṣṭvā sadā śaṅkarastvaṃ mūḍhaṃ māṃ kiṃ vilambase  || 9.39 ||
tṛṣṇāṃ hara harātyarthaṃ lakṣmīṃ me dehi niścalām  || 9.40 ||
nityaṃ chinatti mohaṃ pāpaṃ hanti tāraṇaṃ vidadhāti  |
tava tīrthamātragamanaṃ tadapi na saṃcitaṃ maheśāna  || 9.41 ||
bhūtimūlavibhūḍhasya vibhāgaṃ tannirarthakam  |
karuṇāhṛdayaṃ nāma stotrametadbhṛgūditam  || 9.42 ||
yaḥ paṭhetprātarutthāya sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  |
stotreṇānena santuṣṭaḥ śivaḥ provāca taṃ bhṛgum  || 9.43 ||
sarvaṃ dāsyāmi te vipravaraṃ yanmanasīpsitam  |
siddhiṃ caiva punaḥ ślāghyāṃ yatsurairapi durlabhām  || 9.44 ||

bhṛguruvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātumihecchasi  |
mama nāmnā'sya tīrthasya khyātirbhavati bhūtale  || 9.45 ||
avatāraya cātmānaṃ bhṛgukṣetre maheśvara  |

śaṅkara uvāca - 
evaṃ bhavatu viprendra tava nāmnā bhaviṣyati  || 9.46 ||
kṣetraṃ pāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ devānāmapi durlabham  |
pitṛputravisaṃvādaḥ krodhājjātaḥ kathañcana  || 9.47 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyātsa tu śāntiṃ gamiṣyati  |
tataḥ prabhṛti ye devā brahmādyāḥ kinnarā narāḥ  || 9.48 ||
upāsate bhṛgukṣetraṃ yatra tuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  |
darśanātsparśanāttasya mucyate brahmahatyayā  || 9.49 ||
snānaṃ yaḥ kurute tatra mucyate sa ṛṇatrayāt |
avatāraḥ kṛto rājan yuge tatra svayambhuvā  || 9.50 ||
tatra kṣetre naraśreṣṭha aṣṭau rudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
bhṛguścaiva tathā śūlī vedaścandramukhastathā  || 9.51 ||
aṭṭahāsastathā kālaḥ karālī cāṣṭamastathā  |
aṣṭau rudrāssamutpannāstasmin kṣetre yudhiṣṭhira  || 9.52 ||
tena ramyaṃ ca dhanyaṃ ca bhṛgukṣetramudāhṛtam  |
ayane viṣuve caiva saṃkrāntau grahaṇeṣu ca  || 9.53 ||
vyatīpāte dinacchede chāyāyāṃ tu gajasya ca  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tathā homaṃ tarpaṇaṃ devatārcanam  || 9.54 ||
sarvaṃ tadakṣayaṃ rājaṃstasmin kṣetre na saṃśayaḥ  |
snātasya ca bhṛgukṣetre ekarātroṣitasya ca  || 9.55 ||
yatpuṇyaṃ jāyate puṃso na tatkratuśatairapi  |
darśe bhādrapade māse śuklapakṣe viśeṣataḥ  || 9.56 ||
naraḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā bhṛgutīrthasya saṃyataḥ  |
tatkṣaṇādvirajo bhūtvā śivaloke mahīyate  || 9.57 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyānmucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  |
markaṭyāḥ paścime bhāge hyarkatīrthamudāhṛtam  || 9.58 ||
tatra nityaṃ sthito bhānuḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ  |
naraḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 9.59 ||
māhātmyaṃ tasya devasya śṛṇu rājan samāsataḥ  |
purā gandharvarājastu mohano nāma nāmataḥ  || 9.60 ||
brahmaṇastu sabhāṃ prāptastadārādhanatatparaḥ  |
tatra durvāsasaṃ dṛṣṭvā rūpeṇānena garvitaḥ  || 9.61 ||
ajñānenā'vamānena sa jahāsa muniṃ nṛpa  |
smerānanaṃ samālokya taṃ śaśāpa munistadā  || 9.62 ||
citrakuṣṭhī durācāro bhava tvaṃ rūpagarvitaḥ  |
sa tu śāpabhayātprāha muniṃ gandharvarāṭtataḥ  || 9.63 ||
śāpāntaṃ kuru me vipra bāliśasya prasādataḥ  |

durvāsā uvāca - 
gaccha gandharvarāja tvaṃ tripuryāṃ narmadātaṭam  || 9.64 ||
yasminnāste svayaṃ devaḥ samagrabhayanāśanaḥ  |
bhāsate bhāskaraṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ cottare taṭe  || 9.65 ||
tatra snānānmahārāja śāpāntaste bhaviṣyati  |
sa jagāma punarnatvā tato vai narmadātaṭam  || 9.66 ||
tatra snātvā vidhānena pūjayāmāsa bhāskaram  |
trirātryārādhito bhānuḥ prātaḥ provāca taṃ nṛpa  || 9.67 ||
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |

gandharva uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātumihecchasi  || 9.68 ||
citrakuṣṭhaṃ vinaśyeta tvatprasādena me prabho  |
evamastviti taṃ prāha gandharvādhipatiṃ tadā  || 9.69 ||
śāpānmukto jagāmātha svapuraṃ prati bhārata  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan chivena parikīrtitam  || 9.70 ||
sāvitryārādhanaṃ tatra putrārthaṃ kila bhārata  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā samabhyarcya ca bhāskaram  || 9.71 ||
putravān vyādhimuktaśca jāyate nā'tra saṃśayaḥ  |
koṭīśvaraṃ tu tatraiva viddhidakṣiṇabhāgataḥ  || 9.72 ||
tamabhyarcya vidhānena koṭiliṅgārcanātphalam  |
naraḥ prāpnoti rājendra satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyaham  || 9.73 ||
koṭitīrthe naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  |
tatra yaḥ santyajetprāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pivā  || 9.74 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  || 9.75 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'rkatīrthamāhātmye navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 10

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dakṣiṇe tasya tīrthasya kalpagātīramāśritam  |
somenārādhitaṃ tīrthaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 10.1 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti mṛtāste na punarbhavāḥ  |
dakṣiṇe tasya devasya sthitaḥ śakreśvaraḥ śivaḥ  || 10.2 ||
śakreṇārādhitaḥ pūrvaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhaye  |
anyattīrthaṃ pravakṣyāmi brahmakuṇḍamiti smṛtam  || 10.3 ||
yatrāste bhagavān viṣṇū revā cottaravāhinī  |
tatra snātvā mahārāja vaiṣṇavaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 10.4 ||
darśe caiva vyatīpāte tilatoyapradānataḥ  |
śrāddhasya karaṇāttatra pitṝṇāṃ tṛptirakṣayā  || 10.5 ||
udīcī narmadā yatra pratīcī yatra jāhnavī  |
kṣetraṃ gaccha nṛpaśreṣṭha prācī yatra sarasvatī  || 10.6 ||
brahmakuṇḍottare bhāge viddhi devaṃ sanātanam  |
ambarīṣamiti khyātaṃ mādhavaṃ madhusūdanam  || 10.7 ||
ekādaśyāṃ samabhyarcya snātvā yastu narādhipa  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  || 10.8 ||
tasyaiva paścime bhāge haṃsatīrthaṃ narādhipa  |
tatra snātvā purā rājan haṃsā vai tridivaṅgatāḥ  || 10.9 ||
tatrāpi kurute śrāddhaṃ dānaṃ caiva narādhipa  |
haṃsatīrthaprabhāveṇa tiryagyonau na jāyate  || 10.10 ||
paścime tasya bhāge tu liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  |
mahākālamiti khyātaṃ yanmayārādhitaṃ purā  || 10.11 ||
tamabhyarcya vidhānena śivalokamavāpnuyāt |
athānyatsaṃpravakṣyāmi tīrtharājamanuttamam  || 10.12 ||
mātṛtīrthamiti khyātaṃ liṅgaṃ ca mātṛkeśvaram  |
tatra snātasya rājendra hayamedhaphalaṃ bhavet || 10.13 ||
tatpravāhamatikramya yadvā revottaraṃ mahat |
saptaviṃśodbhavaḥ śivastatra lokeṣu gīyate  || 10.14 ||
tatra snātvā pitṛbhyaśca toyapiṇḍapradānataḥ  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā śivaloke mahīyate  || 10.15 ||
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ kiṃcidvāpi yudhiṣṭhira  |
tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate ityāha bhagavān chivaḥ  || 10.16 ||
tata pratīcyāṃ liṅgan tu brahmeśvaramiti śrutam  |
brahmaṇā sādhitaṃ sadyaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 10.17 ||
darśanāttasya devasya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
aṅgāre vā caturdaśyāṃ tadabhyarcya vidhānataḥ  || 10.18 ||
śivabhaktiparo martyaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
anyattīrthaṃ pravakṣyāmi svargadvāramanuttamam  || 10.19 ||
tatra snāto naravyāghra svargaloke mahīyate  |
tatra paścimabhāge tu liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ param  || 10.20 ||
tatra siddheśvaraṃ caiva tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 10.21 ||
pauṣe māsi sitāṣṭamyāṃ tamabhyarcya vidhānataḥ  |
dattvā tu kapilāṃ dhenuṃ svargaloke mahīyate  || 10.22 ||
tasmāduttarato viddhi saṅgamaṃ lokaviśrutam  |
gaṅgāyamunayornityaṃ revāyāśca narādhipa  || 10.23 ||
tatra snātasya rājendra aśvamedhaphalaṃ bhavet |
śrāddhaṃ tatra prakurvīta pitṝṇāṃ prītivarddhanam  || 10.24 ||

rājovāca - 
gaṅgā ca yamunā cātra samāyāte kathaṃ mune  |
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ prabrūhi munipuṅgava  || 10.25 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
mataṅgo nāma rājarṣirāsīddharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 10.26 ||
śivabhakto mahāyogī tripuryāṃ vedavittamaḥ  |
ṣaṇmāsabhojī dharmātmā hatvā karivaraṃ svayam  || 10.27 ||
ṣaṣṭhe māse tu saṃprāpte yathāvadvidhipūrvakam  |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya śeṣaṃ bhuṅkte narādhipa  || 10.28 ||
evaṃ tapasi tapte tu kālena mahatā tataḥ  |
saptarṣayaḥ samāyātāstena mārgeṇa bhārata  || 10.29 ||
sa tān dṛṣṭvā namaskṛtya arghapādyairapūjayat |
kuśāsanopaviṣṭāṃstu provāca munisattamaḥ  || 10.30 ||
dhanyo'smi pitṛmedhe yatsaṃprāptā me bhavādṛśāḥ  |
tattasya vacanaṃ śrutvā mataṅgasya mahāmune  || 10.31 ||
ṛṣayaścintayāmāsuranyo'nyaṃ vai tadā nṛpa  |
māṃsena pitṛmedho'sya kathaṃ tyājyo bhavediti  || 10.32 ||
cintāviṣṭānmunīn dṛṣṭvā vaśiṣṭhaḥ prāha taṃ munim  |
gaṅgāyamunayoryoge snānaṃ kṛtvā mahāmune  || 10.33 ||
bhokṣāmahe vayaṃ sarve nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā mataṅgaḥ prāha tān hasan  || 10.34 ||
gaṅgāyamunayoryoge snānaṃ cātra bhaviṣyati  |
ityuktvā dhyānamāsthāya sa gaṅgāṃ yamunāṃ tathā  || 10.35 ||
samāhvayatmuniśreṣṭhaḥ samāyāte tu tatkṣaṇāt |
snānaṃ kurudhvaṃ munayo gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame  || 10.36 ||
te dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ karmma munestasya mahātmanaḥ  |
vismayāviṣṭahṛdayāḥ praśaśaṃsuśca taṃ munim  || 10.37 ||
tatastu munayaḥ sarve snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya mataṅgasya yayurdivam  || 10.38 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva praviṣṭe sapta kalpagāṃ  |
itthaṃ sa saṅgamo jātaḥ sarvapāpaharaḥ paraḥ  || 10.39 ||
amāsomasamāyoge snānaṃ yaḥ kurute naraḥ  |
sarvadharmasusampannaḥ śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ  || 10.40 ||
uddhṛtya pūrvajān saptacaivāparāṃstathā  |
divyadehasamāpanno divyābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  || 10.41 ||
divyayāna samārūḍhaḥ stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
tatkṣaṇādvirajo bhūtvā svargaloke mahīyate  || 10.42 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyānmucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  |
nairantaryeṇa ṣaṇmāsājjitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  || 10.43 ||
snānaṃ yaḥ kurute tatra pūjayecca maheśvaram  |
pathikaḥ kāraṇādvāpi mlecchadeśe'pi vā kvacit || 10.44 ||
yatra tatra mṛtaḥ so'pi prāpnuyācchivasannidhim  |
snānaṃ dāna tapo homaḥ saṃyoge caiva parvaṇi  || 10.45 ||
yadatra kriyate tasya puṇyasaṃkhyā na vidyate  |
etatte me kṛtaṃ rājaṃstripurīkṣetravarṇanam  || 10.46 ||
śravaṇaṃ yasya rājendra daivatairapi durlabham  |
etacchrutvā mahārāja narmadākīrtanaṃ śubham  || 10.47 ||
namaskṛtya varārohāṃ kāmikaṃ yānamāsthitaḥ  |
viveśa nagarīṃ puṇyāmayodhyāṃ devanirmitām  || 10.48 ||
mudā paramayā yukto yathā pūrvaṃ tathaiva saḥ  |
etatte kīrtitaṃ rājan yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 10.49 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ skandasya tu mahātmanaḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanātcaiva śivaloke mahīyate  || 10.50 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mātaṅgākhyānaṃ nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 11
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
yogatīrthamiti khyātaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 11.1 ||
yogī snātvā vidhānena yogānte tridivaṃ gataḥ  |
tatra snātvā naro rājaṃstiryagyonau na gacchati  || 11.2 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ dhruvo yatra prakāśate  || 11.3 ||
dhruvatīrthe naraḥ snātvā sarvakāmaphalodaye  |
dhruveśvaraṃ mahādevaṃ bhaktyā yastu prapūjayet || 11.4 ||
daśāyutāni rājā vai pure vaidyādhare śubhe  |
nākṣatraṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu narmadātīramāśritam  || 11.5 ||
yatra ṛkṣeśvaro devaḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ  |
saptaviṃśatisaṃsiddhiṃ nakṣatrāṇi gatāni vai  || 11.6 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyāddivi dīvyantidevatāḥ  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 11.7 ||
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ vārāhaṃ nāma viśrutam  || 11.8 ||
mahāśūkararūpeṇa dhātrī yatra samuddhṛtā  |
ekādaśyāṃ naraḥ snātvā kṛtvā caiva yathoditam  || 11.9 ||
upavāsaparo bhūtvā dvādaśyāṃ tu yudhiṣṭhira  |
vaiṣṇavastu śucirbhūtvā vārāhaṃ ca samarcayet || 11.10 ||
puṣpopahāradhūpaiśca gandhadīpavilepanaiḥ  |
varṣalakṣaṃ tu sāgraṃ vai loke krīḍati vaiṣṇave  || 11.11 ||
brahmacārī jitakrodho viṣṇudharmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
bhaktyā bhojayate yastu viprān vaiṣṇavakāṃstathā  || 11.12 ||
lekhayitvā viṣṇudharmān vastrālaṅkārabhūṣitān  |
nivedayedbrāhmaṇāya śrotriyāya viśeṣataḥ  || 11.13 ||
purāṇaṃ narmadākhyānaṃ śrāvayecca samāhitaḥ  |
varadāśca trayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 11.14 ||
bhavanti tasya satyaṃ vai nā'tra kāryyā vicāraṇā  |
yāvadakṣarasaṃkhyānaṃ yāvatpatrasamuccayaḥ  || 11.15 ||
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  |
vidyādānātparaṃ dānaṃ nānya lokeṣu gīyate  || 11.16 ||
adīpā ca yathā rātriranādityaṃ yathā nabhaḥ  |
vidyāhīnaṃ tathā sarvamandhe tamasi majjati  || 11.17 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ vidyādānasya yatphalam  |
sarvadānaphalaṃ tasya vidyādānaprabhāvataḥ  || 11.18 ||
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ cāndrāyaṇamiti smṛtam  |
śaśāṅke rohiṇīyukte paurṇamāsyāṃ mahotsave  || 11.19 ||
śaśāṅkabhūṣaṇaṃ devaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam  |
arcayitvā vidhānena svargaloke mahīyate  || 11.20 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu kurute rāhusūryasamāgame  |
tilodakaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  || 11.21 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti pāpopahatacetasaḥ  |
sarvaṃ pākhaṇḍayuktaṃ ca āvṛtaṃ kalinā tathā  || 11.22 ||
purāṇavedadharmāśca dānaṃ yajñastapastathā  |
ācchāditamidaṃ puṇyaṃ hetukaiḥ pāpakarmabhiḥ  || 11.23 ||
nagnairmalinadīnaiśca kalau loke digambaraiḥ  |
tasmāddharmaparairnityamupāsyā narmadā nadī  || 11.24 ||
saumye tu dvādaśādityaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyavivardhanam  |
tatra snātvā naro rājannarcayitvā tu bhāskaram  || 11.25 ||
saṃkrāntau viṣuve caiva sūryaloke mahīyate  |
ekasmin bhojite vipre lakṣaṃ bhavati bhojitam  || 11.26 ||
tilānnaṃ ca hiraṇyaṃ ca yathā śaktyā dadāti yaḥ  |
śuklapakṣasya māghasya śubhā ṣaṣṭhī ca saptamī  || 11.27 ||
tasyāṃ dānaprabhāveṇa hyupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi sūryaloke mahīyate  || 11.28 ||
tīrthamāpsarasaṃ nāma yāmyāṃ diśi samāśritam  |
campakā sīmapā nāmā keśinī bhāminī tathā  || 11.29 ||
kaumudī suprabhā caiva utpalā ca mahodayā  |
niṣādayoniṃ saṃprāptāḥ pūrvajanmani bhārata  || 11.30 ||
etā āpsarasaṃ devaṃ gaurī caiva sureśvarī  |
māghe māsi tṛtīyāyāṃ nirāhārāśca nirjalāḥ  || 11.31 ||
udakaiḥ snāpayitvā tu bilvapatrairapūjayan  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi sāvadhānāstu nārmadaiḥ  || 11.32 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhāstā apsarobhiḥ supūjitāḥ  |
sarvālaṅkāraśobhāḍhyā nānāvasanabhūṣitāḥ  || 11.33 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyātsaṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  |
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi puṇyatīrthamanuttamam  || 11.34 ||
yatra snātasya vidhivatkanyādānaphalaṃ bhavet |
śaṅkaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ tu uttarasyāṃ diśi smṛtam  || 11.35 ||
arcayitvā tu taṃ devaṃ darśe caiva narādhipa  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate  || 11.36 ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi saṅgamaṃ lokaviśrutam  |
dattātreyā nadī yatra saṅgatā saha revayā  || 11.37 ||
saumyabhāge varārohāsurāsuranamaskṛtā  |
tatra snātvā ca dattvā ca arcayitvā tu keśavam  || 11.38 ||
pāpiṣṭhā ye durācārā dharmakarmabahiṣkṛtāḥ  |
prabhāvāttasya tīrthasya te'pi yānti hareḥ puram  || 11.39 ||
medhātithiḥ karaḥ skandaḥ sāvarṇiḥ kauśiko manuḥ  |
kāśyapo gālavaścaiva maitreyastapasāṃ nidhiḥ  || 11.40 ||
ete cānye'pi bahavo ṛṣayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ  |
tīrthasyā'sya prabhāveṇa śaṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  || 11.41 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe tīrthamahimavarṇano nāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 12

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
atha śṛṇuṣva rājendra dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāśritam  |
śaivaṃ gāñjālabhedaṃ ca saṃgamaṃ surapūjitam  || 12.1 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
yaḥ kuryātpitṛdevānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ piṇḍapātanam  || 12.2 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvaccandrārkatārakam  |
mahotsave ca kaumudyāṃ kārttikyāṃ caiva parvaṇi  || 12.3 ||
gāñjāleśvaraliṅgaṃ ca arcayetsiddhidāyakam  |
harikeśaścakravartī kanyāpurapatiḥ purā  || 12.4 ||
tīrthasyā'sya prabhāveṇa mukto bhūdvatsagovadhāt |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
mamāsti saṃśayastāta sumahāṃllomaharṣaṇaḥ  || 12.5 ||
kathaṃ rājani gohatyā kathaṃ muktaśca govadhāt |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāmitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 12.6 ||
somavaṃśe nṛpaścāsītsatyadharmavrate sthitaḥ  |
devānīka iti khyāto harikeśastadātmajaḥ  || 12.7 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaścakravartī mahābalaḥ  |
anekāśca makhāstena rājanniṣṭā mahātmanā  || 12.8 ||
khyātaṃ kanyāpuraṃ tasya dhanadasyālakā yathā  |
cirāyuṣaḥ prajāḥ sarvā dhanadhānyasamanvitāḥ  || 12.9 ||
tuṅgabhadreti vikhyātā śrīśaile tripurāntike  |
uktā pātālagaṅgeti mallikārjunadarśanāt || 12.10 ||
pūrvabhāge tatastasya śrīdhāmaṃ nāma kheṭakam  |
nānā munisamākīrṇaṃ devakoṭisamāvṛtam  || 12.11 ||
lalitodvāhitā tatra śivena paramātmanā  |
purā tvasyāyuṣā nāma prajāpatirakalpayat || 12.12 ||
suprabhā nāma tasyāstu vikhyātamabhavattathā  |
devyāḥ pūrvāvatāro'yaṃ kathitaste viśāmpate  || 12.13 ||
dvitīye himavatputrī pārvatī ca umā tathā  |
tṛtīye dakṣaduhitā nāmnā gaurīti viśrutā  || 12.14 ||
puṇyatīrthe ca kṣetre'smin harikeśaḥ pratāpavān  |
suprabhā nikaṭe nāma suprabho rājasattama  || 12.15 ||
śaśāsa medinīṃ rājā sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
lakṣamekaṃ tu dogdhrīṇāṃ rāhusūryasamāgame  || 12.16 ||
niṣkāṇāṃ tu sahasraṃ vai pratigaṅgāmakalpayat |
sarvābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāmekāṃ gāṃ copaveśayat || 12.17 ||
brāhmaṇāṃśca samāhūya vedavidyābahuśrutān  |
pañcabhirdivasaiḥ pūrvaṃ rāhusūryasamāgame  || 12.18 ||
prayāge tatra yoge vā ityuktaṃ vedapāragaiḥ  |
daivopakṛtayogena pūrvakarmmakṛtena ca  || 12.19 ||
āgneyī hūyate ceṣṭī rājñā'nalasamāgame  |
agnāvāhavanīye'tra raudrairmantraiḥ sutejasaiḥ  || 12.20 ||
pātālādutthito vahṇiryugāntāgnisamaprabhaḥ  |
dagdhaṃ gomaṇḍalaṃ kṛtsnamayutaṃ brahmacāriṇām  || 12.21 ||
bhasmībhūtaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ maṇḍapaṃ purameva hi  |
harikeśo viṣaṇṇātmā praveṣṭuṃ vai hutāśanam  || 12.22 ||
āsanādutthito rājā sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  |
amātyaiḥ saṃvṛtastāvatsamayajñairbalottaraiḥ  || 12.23 ||
hāhākāro mahānāsīttriṣu lokeṣu bhārata  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā brāhmaṇān vedapāragān  || 12.24 ||
brāhmaṇasyaiva hatyāyā gavāṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ  |
api varṣasahasreṇa niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate  || 12.25 ||
dhenūnāṃ caiva vatsānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yathāgatiḥ  |
sā gatirme bhavennityaṃ satyametadbravīmyaham  || 12.26 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
gaccha tvaṃ mahābhāga kalpagrāmaṃ purottamam  |
agastirbhagavān yatra kaśyapo bhṛgureva ca  || 12.27 ||
bhāradvājo'trigargau ca gautamo manureva ca  |
yājñavalkyo vaśiṣṭhaśca ṛṣayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 12.28 ||
tatra gatvā mahārāja prāyaścittaṃ pragṛhyatām  |
maharṣīṇāṃ matenaiva vedaśāstrārthadarśinām  || 12.29 ||
evamukto yayau rājā pādacārī dvijaiḥ saha  |
viveśa nagarīṃ puṇyāṃ brahmalokasamāṃ nṛpaḥ  || 12.30 ||
maharṣīṃstatra tān dṛṣṭvā so'bhivādya praṇamya ca  |
niścayaṃ cirakālaṃ tu teṣāmevāgrata sthitaḥ  || 12.31 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
svāgataṃ te nṛpaśreṣṭha phalaṃ karmasahasraśaḥ  |
kimāgamanakāryaṃ ca satyametadvadasva naḥ  || 12.32 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā ṛṣīṇāmūrddhvaretasām  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vṛttāntaṃ svaṃ nyavedayat || 12.33 ||
daivādvipadyate kāryaṃ saṃsiddhamapi bho dvijāḥ  |
daśā'yutaṃ gavāṃ dagdhaṃ dvijānāmayutaṃ tathā  || 12.34 ||
anugrahaṃ bahu manye prāyaścittamathoditam  |


brāhmaṇā ūcu̱ḥ
daśalakṣāṇi gāyatryāstīrthe tīrthe japaṃ kuruṃ  || 12.35 ||
ayutaṃ tu gavāṃ dattvā sahiraṇyaṃ nṛpottama  |
koṭihomaṃ tu kurvīta sahasraśatadakṣiṇam  || 12.36 ||
prayāgaṃ ca mahātīrthaṃ gacchedvārāṇasīṃ śubhām  |
kedāraṃ ca tatheśānaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  || 12.37 ||
pitṛtīrthaṃ gayāṃ caiva naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  |
