māyāpurīṃ harikṣetraṃ gaṅgādvāraṃ mahāphalam  || 12.38 ||
tīrtheṣveteṣu cānyeṣu yāvaddvādaśavatsarān  || 12.39 ||
anena kramayogena śuddhiste nātra saṃśaya  |
muktiste bhavitā rājan brāhmaṇānāṃ prabhāvataḥ  || 12.40 ||
dattaṃ te śrutivākyena prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaiḥ  |
pādapūjā dvijendrāṇāṃ kartavyā ca vidhānataḥ  || 12.41 ||
kurukṣetraṃ jagāmātha kartuṃ somasavaṃ nṛpaḥ  |
vicintyaivaṃ svāghanyāsaṃ sarasvatyāṃ samāśrayat || 12.42 ||
japati sma śivastotraṃ haraṃ viṣṇuṃ sarasvatīm  |
kurukṣetraṃ gamiṣyāmi kurukṣetre vasāmyaham  || 12.43 ||
kurukṣetrasya nāmnā'pi naraḥ pāpātpramucyate  |
akhilaśabdamahauṣadhaprakṣālitasakalābhūtakalaṅkā  |
munibhirupāsitatīrthā sarasvatīha haratu me duritam  || 12.44 ||
rājñastadvacanaṃ śrutvā prāha pāpaharā nadī  |
viṣādaṃ tyaja rājendra śṛṇu me vacanaṃ param  || 12.45 ||
upadeśaṃ pradāsyāmi sthātuṃ tava nṛpottama  |
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoraṃ smaraṇādeva naśyati  || 12.46 ||
brahmahatyā sahasraṃ tu gohatyālakṣameva ca  |
na ca mocayituṃ śaktā gaṅgā caiva saridvarā  || 12.47 ||
carācare'sya loke'smin kartrī tiṣṭhati kalpagā  |
dīpādityādibhirvīkṣya yathāndhatva praṇaśyati  || 12.48 ||
tathā nāśayate pāpaṃ kalpagā saritāṃ varā  |
dānairdagdhā purā cāhaṃ kṣatriyāṇāmanekadhā  || 12.49 ||
grastā'haṃ tena pāpena nṛpadakṣiṇayā tataḥ  |
śuklataḥ kṛṣṇatāṃ cāhaṃ prāptā bho pṛthivīpate  || 12.50 ||
dvādaśā'bde caturviṃśe snānaṃ kartuṃ samāgamam  |
graste vai rāhuṇā sūrye koṭitīrthe narādhipaḥ  || 12.51 ||
pratyakṣaṃ me śarīraṃ tvaṃ paśya tejaḥ samujjvalam  |
snātvā tu śivamabhyarcya bahu svarṇaṃ makhottamam  || 12.52 ||
kuru tatra nṛpaśreṣṭha tena te niṣkṛtirbhavet |
ye mṛtā brāhmaṇā gāvasteṣāmasthipravāhanam  || 12.53 ||
narmadodakasamparkāddivi devatvamāpyate  |
tilodakapradānena teṣāṃ muktiḥ parā bhavet || 12.54 ||
śrutvaitatkathitaṃ vākyaṃ sarasvatyā nṛpaśca tām  |
namaskṛtya samutthāya kanyāpuramagāttataḥ  || 12.55 ||
sāntaḥ puraparīvāro mudā paramayā nṛpaḥ  |
tenājñaptāstato bhṛtyāḥ sarvasambhārasabhṛtāḥ  || 12.56 ||
yajñopaskaramādāya mekalā yatra gacchata  |
asthi bhasma yathānyāyaṃ nītaṃ karmakaraistataḥ  || 12.57 ||
prāvāhayatkalpagāyāṃ mantrayuktena vāriṇā  |
asthyādi pūjayitvā ca yathā vidhamanuttamam  || 12.58 ||
devāṃśca brāhmaṇāṃstatra tarpayitvā kṛtāñjaliḥ  |
pravāho nirgatastatra narmadāyāṃ samāviśat || 12.59 ||
sa gāñjāleti vikhyāto narmadā saṅgamo nṛpa  |
gāñjālasiddhaliṅgaṃ ca sūryakoṭisamaprabham  || 12.60 ||
divyayānasamārūḍhā dagdhāḥ kālāgninā tu ye  |
āśīrvādaparāssarve harikeśaṃ pratuṣṭuvuḥ  || 12.61 ||
tvatprasādānmahābhāga divi devatvamāgatāḥ  |
devadundubhinirghoṣairveṇuvīṇāravaistathā  || 12.62 ||
divyayānasamārūḍhā gatāste vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  |
harikeśo nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ parayā ca mudāyutaḥ  || 12.63 ||
saptakalpavahāṃ devīṃ narmadā lokapāvanīm  |
namaskṛtya saricchreṣṭhāṃ stutiṃ cakre samāhitaḥ  || 12.64 ||
namaste'stu saricchreṣṭhe saptakalpanivāsini  |
yatra tatra naraḥ snātvā mukto bhavati kalmaṣāt || 12.65 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  |
janmāntarasahasreṇa na tvāṃ stambhayate balī  || 12.66 ||
jahnunā hi purā pītā karatoyena jāhnavī  |
tvayā ca pūritaṃ sarvaṃ viśvaṃ caiva carācaram  || 12.67 ||
tvatprasādātmahādevi muktiścāpi bhavārṇavāt |
pratyakṣā kalpagaitacca stotraṃ śrutvā nṛpoditam  || 12.68 ||

narmadovāca - 
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā harikeśo'bravīdidam  || 12.69 ||
yadi me varadādevi pūtaṃ māṃ parikalpaya  |
snānāvagāhanātpānātsmaraṇātkīrtanādapi  || 12.70 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sadya eva praṇaśyatu  |

revovāca - 
evamastu nṛpaśreṣṭha matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 12.71 ||
evamuktvā tato devī tatraivāntara dhīyata  |
harikeśaścakravartī sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  || 12.72 ||
kāmikaṃ yānamāruhya sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  |
viveśa nagaraṃ puṇyaṃ yathāśakro'marāvatīm  || 12.73 ||
kālāntare tataḥ prāpte rājyaṃ kṛtvā surālaye  |
sāntaḥ puraparīvāro bhogān bhuṅkte sma puṣkalān  || 12.74 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājanmahābhāga viśāmpate  |
dvāparaṃ vaṃśamaikṣvākaṃ brāhmaṃ vaivasvataṃ tathā  || 12.75 ||
kāpilaṃ puṣkaraṃ ceti saptakalpān vidurbudhāḥ  |
kāpilaṃ prathamaṃ viddhi prājāpatyaṃ dvitīyakam  || 12.76 ||
brāhmaṃ raucaṃ ca sāvitraṃ bārhaspatyaṃ prabhāsakam  |
mahendramagnikalpaṃ vaijayantaṃ mārutaṃ tathā  || 12.77 ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ brahmarūpaṃ ca jyotiṣaṃ ca caturdaśam  |
ete kalpāstu saṃkhyātā na mṛtā yeṣu narmadā  || 12.78 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ mahatāṃ kīrtivarddhanam  || 12.79 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādvāpi mucyate sarvakilviṣāt || 12.80 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye gāñjālatīrthavarṇano nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 13

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam  |
diśi yāmyāṃ samākhyātaṃ nāmnā vai bālukeśvaram  || 13.1 ||
puṇyaṃ prakīrtitaṃ tatra liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  |
tatra snātvā yathānyāyaṃ hemabhāraphalaṃ labhet || 13.2 ||
śaṅkukarṇa iti khyāto yakṣaḥ paramasiddhidaḥ  |
uttarāyaṇamāsādya kanyā revāsamāgame  || 13.3 ||
saṃjagāma nṛpaśreṣṭha śivasaṃsaktamānasaḥ  |
maṇimāṇikyaratnāni brāhmaṇārthamakalpayat || 13.4 ||
tāvannṛpastu taṃ sthānātsthāṇuṃ caiva maheśvaram  |
cālayāmāsa yakṣastu tataḥ kruddho maheśvaraḥ  || 13.5 ||
dadāha hastau yakṣasya vismayāviṣṭacetasaḥ  |
ākāśavacasā proktaṃ viṣādaṃ tyaja putraka  || 13.6 ||
surasaṅghāstrayastriṃśatnainaṃ cālayituṃ kṣamāḥ  |
surāsuragurūn devaṃ sthāṇubhūtaṃ svayambhuvam  || 13.7 ||
kiṃ punardānavā yakṣamānuṣāścālpacetasaḥ  |
kṣamayitvā tu deveśaṃ śaṅkukarṇo maheśvaram  || 13.8 ||
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyaṃ jvalantaṃ dīptatejasam  |
ajñānātkṛtametattu kṣantavyaṃ mayi putrake  || 13.9 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devaṃ varaṃ dehi maheśvara  |
prasādya cābravīdyakṣastava bhṛtyaṃ vaśaṃ gatam  || 13.10 ||
gaṇatvaṃ gaṇamadhye tu dehi he surasattama  |
yakṣatīrthamiti khyātaṃ liṅgaṃ vai bālukeśvaram  || 13.11 ||
kartumarhasi deveśa tīrthe'smin sacarācaraiḥ  |
ayane cottare hyatra snānadānamanuttamam  || 13.12 ||
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye yadi tuṣṭo'si śaṅkara  |

śaṅkara uvāca - 
sarvakābhaphalāvāptirmatprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 13.13 ||
evamuktvā maheśānastatraivāntara dhīyata  |
śaṅkukarṇo mahātejāstīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 13.14 ||
divyayānaṃ samāruhya yayau māheśvaraṃ puram  |
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 13.15 ||
diśi yāmyāṃ samākhyātaṃ nāmnā pūrṇamanoratham  |
puṇyakīrtanaliṅgaṃ tu brahmahatyāpraṇāśanam  || 13.16 ||
tatra snātvā mahārāja gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |
harivarmā purā cāsīdvirāṭnagarādhipaḥ  || 13.17 ||
sarvadharmaguṇopeto yajñayājī mahāyaśāḥ  |
iṣṭastatra mahāyajño yena vai kalpagātaṭe  || 13.18 ||
golakṣaṃ tatra dattaṃ ca hemabhārapariṣkṛtam  |
devāśca tārpitāstatra brahmāviṣṇurbṛhaspatiḥ  || 13.19 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ pūjitā bhaktyā nānāratnavibhūṣitāḥ  |
praśaśaṃsuśca nṛpatiṃ surāḥ pūrṇamanorathāḥ  || 13.20 ||
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthāttīrthamanuttamam  |
revāmatsyāsamāyoge surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 13.21 ||
nābhāgasya ca saṃvādamāpastambasya cānagha  |
manvantare cākṣuṣe vai saṃprāpte dvāpare yuge  || 13.22 ||
nāmnā matsyeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ jalamadhye vyavasthitam  |
pūjyate nāgakanyābhirna taṃ paśyanti mānuṣāḥ  || 13.23 ||
mahātejo maṇimayaṃ candrabimbasamaprabham  |
smaraṇādyasya devasya brahmahatyā praṇaśyati  || 13.24 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
sādhubhiḥ saha saṃvāsātke guṇāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  |
kāḥ kathāḥ kāni puṇyāni saṅgame sāptakalpage  || 13.25 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
atraivodāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
nābhāgasya ca saṃvādamāpastamba taponidheḥ  || 13.26 ||
maharṣiścātmavān pūrvamāpastambo dvijottamaḥ  |
upavāsakṛtārambho babhūva bhagavāṃstathā  || 13.27 ||
nityaṃ krodhaṃ ca kāmañca lobhaṃ mohaṃ visṛjya ca  |
revāmatsyāsamāyoge viveśa salilāśaye  || 13.28 ||
sa matsyaiḥ salilāvarte saritaścānugaistadā  |
tatrānyotpatitairjālaiḥ samānīto mahāyaśāḥ  || 13.29 ||
tasmāduttārayāmāsuḥ salilādbrahmanandanam  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tapasā dīptaṃ kaivartā bhayavihvalāḥ  || 13.30 ||
śirobhiḥ praṇipatyoccairidaṃ vacanamabruvan  |
ajñānātkriyamāṇānāmasmākaṃ kṣantumarhasi  || 13.31 ||
kiṃ vā kiṃ ca priyaṃ te'dya tadājñāpaya suvrata  |
sa munistanmahaddṛṣṭvā matsyānāṃ kadanaṃ kṛtam  || 13.32 ||
kṛpayā parayāviṣṭo dāśān provāca duḥkhitaḥ  |
duḥkhitānīha bhūtāni yo na bhūtaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ  || 13.33 ||
kevalātma sukhecchāto'vennṛśaṃsataro'sti kaḥ  |
aho'svastheṣvakāruṇyaṃ svaārthe caiva balirvṛthā  || 13.34 ||
jñānināmapi cedyastu kevalātmahite rataḥ  |
jñānino hi yathā svārthamāśritya dhyānamāśritāḥ  || 13.35 ||
duḥkhārtānīha bhūtāni prayānti śaraṇaṃ tataḥ  |
yo'bhivāñchati bhoktuṃ vai sukhānyekān tato janaḥ  || 13.36 ||
pāpātparataraṃ taṃ hi pravadanti mumukṣavaḥ  |
ko nu me syādupāyo hi yenāhaṃ duḥkhitātmanām  || 13.37 ||
antaḥpraviśya bhūtānāṃ bhaveyaṃ sarvaduḥkhabhuk |
yanmamāsti śubhaṃ kiṃcittaddīnānupagacchatu  || 13.38 ||
yatkṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ taiśca tadaśeṣamupaitu mām  |
dṛṣṭvā tān kṛpaṇān vyaṅgānanaṅgān rogiṇastathā  || 13.39 ||
dayā na jāyate yasya sa rakṣa iti me matiḥ  |
prāṇasaṃśayamāpannān prāṇino bhayavihvalān  || 13.40 ||
yo na rakṣati śasto'pi sa tatpāpaṃ samaśnute  |
āhūtānāṃ nayārtānāṃ sukhaṃ yadupajāyate  || 13.41 ||
tasya svargāpavargau ca kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
tasmāccaitānahaṃ dīnāṃ chaktuṃ mīnān suduḥkhitān  || 13.42 ||
yādṛṅmātraṃ na paśyāmi kiṃ punastridaśālayam  |
niśamyaitanmunervākyaṃ dāśāste jātasaṃbhramāḥ  || 13.43 ||
yathārthaṃ tu tathā sarvaṃ nābhāgāya nyavedayan  |
nābhāgo'pi tataḥ śrutvā taṃ draṣṭuṃ brahmanandanam  || 13.44 ||
tvaritaḥ prayayau tatra sāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ  |
sa samyakpūjayitvā tu devakalpaṃ nṛpastataḥ  || 13.45 ||
provāca bhagavan vidvan kiṃ karomi tavājñayā  |

āpastamba uvāca - 
śrameṇa mahatāviṣṭāḥ kaivartā duḥkhajīvinaḥ  || 13.46 ||
mama mūlyaṃ prayaccheti yadyogyaṃ manyase nṛpa  |

nābhāga uvāca - 
sahasrāṇi śataṃ mūlyaṃ niṣādebhyo dadāmyaham  || 13.47 ||
niṣkrayārthaṃ hi bhagavannītaṃ te brahmanandana  |

āpastamba uvāca - 
nāhaṃ śatasahasreṇa niyamyaḥ pārthiva tvayā  || 13.48 ||
sadṛśaṃ dīyatāṃ mūlyamamātyaiḥ saha cintaya  |

nābhāga uvāca - 
koṭiṃ pradīyatāṃ mūlyaṃ niṣādebhyo dvijottama  || 13.49 ||
yadyetadapi yogyaṃ no tato bhūyaḥ pradīyatām  |

āpastamba uvāca - 
rājannārhā vayaṃ koṭimadhikaṃ cāpi pārthiva  || 13.50 ||
sadṛśaṃ dīyatāṃ mūlyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha saṃvada  |

rājovāca - 
arddhaṃ rājyaṃ samastaṃ vā niṣādebhyaḥ pradīyate  || 13.51 ||
etanmūlyamahaṃ manye kiṃ vālaṃ manyase dvija  |

āpastamba uvāca - 
arddhaṃ rājyaṃ samastaṃ vā nāhamarhāmi vai nṛpa  || 13.52 ||
sadṛśaṃ dīyatāṃ mahyaṃ ṛṣibhiḥ saha cintaya  |
maharṣestadvacaḥ śrutvā nābhāgastu vivādayan  || 13.53 ||
cintayāmāsa dharmātmā sāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ  |
tataḥ kaścidṛṣistatra lomaśastu mahātapāḥ  || 13.54 ||
nābhāgamabravītmābhaiḥ toṣayiṣyāmi taṃ munim  |

rājovāca - 
brūhi mūlyaṃ mahābhāga me jñātikulabāndhavān  || 13.55 ||
nirdagdhavānṛṣiḥ kruddhastrailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
kiṃ punarmānavaṃ dīnamatyalpaṃ viṣayātmakam  || 13.56 ||

lomaśa uvāca - 
tvaṃ samartho mahārāja jagatpūjyā dvijottamāḥ  |
gāvaśca divyāstasmādvai mūlyamasmai pradīyatām  || 13.57 ||
tataḥ śrutvā tu vacanaṃ sa sāmātyapurohitaḥ  |
harṣeṇa mahatāviṣṭaḥ provācedaṃ vaco munim  || 13.58 ||
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha bhagavan kṛtameva na saṃśayaḥ  |
etadyogyataṃ mūlyaṃ bhavato munisattama  || 13.59 ||

āpastamba uvāca - 
uttiṣṭhāmyeva saṃprītyā samyakkrīto'smi pārthiva  |
gobhyo mūlyaṃ na paśyāmi pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam  || 13.60 ||
gāvaḥ pradakṣiṇī kāryyā vandanīyā hi nityaśaḥ  |
maṅgalāyatana divyāssṛṣṭāstvetāḥ svayambhuvā  || 13.61 ||
apyāgārāṇi viprāṇāṃ devatāyatanāni ca  |
yadgomayena śuddhyanti kiṃ brūmo hyadhikaṃ tataḥ  || 13.62 ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpistathaiva ca  |
gavāṃ pañca pavitrāṇi punanti sakalaṃ jagat || 13.63 ||
gāvo me cāgrato nityaṃ gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhata eva ca  |
gāvo me hṛdaye caiva gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham  || 13.64 ||
eva yaḥ paṭhate nityaṃ trisandhyaṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ svargalokaṃ sa gacchati  || 13.65 ||
agre grāsaparo bhāvaḥ kartavyo bhaktito'nvaham  |
akṛtvā svayamāhāraṃ kurvannāpnoti durgatim  || 13.66 ||
tenāgnayo hutāssamyakpitaraścāpi tārpitāḥ  |
devāśca pūjitāstena yo dadāti gavāhnikam  || 13.67 ||
mantraḥ
saurabheyī jagatpūjyā nitya viṣṇupade sthitā  |
sarvadevamayī grāsaṃ mayā dattaṃ pratīkṣatām  || 13.68 ||
rakṣaṇādbrahmaputrāṇāṃ gavāṃ kaṇḍūyanāttathā  |
kṣīṇārtarakṣaṇāccaiva tataḥ svarge mahīyate  || 13.69 ||
ādirhi gāvo yajñasya madhyaṃ cāntyaṃ prakīrtitāḥ  |
kṣaranti tāstu sakalaṃ kṣīrājyamamṛtaṃ tathā  || 13.70 ||
tasmādgāvaḥ pradātavyāḥ pūjanīyā hi nityaśaḥ  |
svargasya saṅgamāyaitāssopānaṃ hi vinirmitāḥ  || 13.71 ||
etacchrutvā niṣādāste gavāṃ māhātmyamuttamam  |
praṇipatya mahābhāgamāpastambamathābruvan  || 13.72 ||

niṣādā ūcu̱ḥ
sambhāṣā darśanasparśavarṇanaṃ kīrtanaṃ tathā  |
pāvanāni samastāni sādhūnāmiti naḥ śrutam  || 13.73 ||
sambhāṣā darśanaṃ caivamihāsmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ dvija  |
kuruṣvānugrahaṃ tasmāttvāṃ vayaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 13.74 ||

āpastamba uvāca - 
etāṃ gāṃ pratigṛhṇantu tataste muktakilbiṣāḥ  |
niṣādāśca gatāḥ svargaṃ saha matsyairjalodbhavaiḥ  || 13.75 ||
prāṇināṃ prītimutpādya ninditenā'pi karmaṇā  |
narakaṃ yadi paśyāmi vatsyāmi svargameva vā  || 13.76 ||
yanmayā sukṛtaṃ kiṃcinmanovākkāyakarmabhiḥ  |
kṛta tenāpi duḥkhārtāḥ sarve yāntu śubhāṃ gatim  || 13.77 ||
tatastasya prasādena maharṣerbhāvitātmanaḥ  |
niṣādāstena vākyena saha matsyā divaṃ gatāḥ  || 13.78 ||
tān dṛṣṭvā atha gatān svargaṃ samatsyānmatsyajīvinaḥ  |
sāmātyabhṛtyo nṛpatirvismayādidamabravīt || 13.79 ||
sevyāḥ śreyo'rthibhiḥ santaḥ tīrthaṃ puṇyaṃ jalottamam  |
kṣaṇopāsanamapyatra na teṣāṃ niṣkalaṃ bhavet || 13.80 ||
sadbhissaha samāsīta sadbhiḥ kurvīta satkathām  |
āpastambo munistatra lomaśaśca mahātapā  || 13.81 ||
padaistu vividhairiṣṭairbodhayāmāsa tu nṛpam  |
tataḥ saṃdhārayāmāsa dharmabuddhiṃ sudurlabhām  || 13.82 ||
tatheti kṛtvā coktvā ca nṛpaṃ taṃ praśaśaṃsatuḥ  |
aho dhanyo'si rājendra yatte dharmaparāmatiḥ  || 13.83 ||
dharmaḥ sudurlabhaḥ puṃsāṃ viśeṣeṇa mahīkṣitām  |
yadi rājyamadāviṣṭaḥ svadharmaṃ na parityajet || 13.84 ||
tato jagati kastasmātpunarabhyadhiko bhavet |
dhruvaṃ dharmaśca rājyaṃ vai mohaścaiva sadā dhruvaḥ  || 13.85 ||
mahādhruvaśca narako rājyaṃ nindanti te budhāḥ  |
rājyaṃ hi manyate mūḍho naro viṣayalolupaḥ  || 13.86 ||
manīṣiṇastu paśyanti sadaiva narakopamam  |
tasmācchokaśca mohaśca na kartavyo madastvayā  || 13.87 ||
yadīcchasi mahārāja śāśvatīṃ gatimātmanaḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ityuktvā mahātmānau jagmatuḥ svaṃ svamāśramam  || 13.88 ||
nābhāgo'pi varaṃ labdhvā prahṛṣṭastvaviśatpuram  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan ye gaṇāḥ satsamāgame  || 13.89 ||
māhātmyaṃ vai gavāṃ tadvatkiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi  |
revāmatsyāsamāyoge gavāṃ māhātmyamuttamam  || 13.90 ||
tatra snātvā mahārāja matsyeśvaramathā'rcaya  |
āpastambo mahābhāgo niṣādā matsyajīvinaḥ  || 13.91 ||
matsyaiḥ saha gatāḥ svargaṃ tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
divyakāntidharāḥ sarve loke krīḍanti vaiṣṇave  || 13.92 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe matsyeśvaratīrthavarṇanonāma trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 14

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthaṃ pāpavimokṣaṇam  |
matsyāyāḥ śubhatāpyāśca saṅgame surasevitam  || 14.1 ||
devo matsyeśvaro nāma nāgakanyābhirarcitaḥ  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 14.2 ||
pañcavaktrāstrinetrāśca matsyatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  |
kalahaṃsa iti khyāto devarṣirdhyānatatparaḥ  || 14.3 ||
tasyāśramaṃ paraṃ ramyaṃ brahmarṣi viniṣevitam  |
śākamūlaphalāhāro japadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  || 14.4 ||
so'tiṣṭhadayutaṃ sārddhamekapādena bhārata  |
śivadhyānaparo bhūtvā sarvabhūtahite rataḥ  || 14.5 ||
tatastu dhyānayogena tasya devaḥ śatakratuḥ  |
cakampe tapasā cāsau devarājo bhaviṣyati  || 14.6 ||
hariṣyati na sandeho purīṃ caivāmarāvatīm  |
brāhmaṇaśca sureśānaḥ kubjo vāmanarūpadhṛk || 14.7 ||
jagāma vṛddharūpeṇa kalahaṃsāśramaṃ prati  |
uvāca vacanaṃ śakro brāhmaṇaṃ tapasi sthitam  || 14.8 ||
kimarthaṃ tapase śīghraṃ kāmametadbravīhi me  |
ārādhayasi kaṃ devaṃ satyametattapodhana  || 14.9 ||
saṃhṛtya tapasā yogaṃ prahasannabravīdvacaḥ  |
jānāmi tvāṃ mahābhāga śakrastvaṃ tridaśeśvaraḥ  || 14.10 ||
na kāmaye'hamindratvaṃ rājyaṃ kuru yathepsitam  |
ārādhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ nānyaṃ devaṃ kathañcana  || 14.11 ||
etacchrutvā vacastasya maharṣerabravīdvṛṣaḥ  |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yathā drakṣyasi śaṅkaram  || 14.12 ||

kalahaṃsa uvāca - 
vinā'haṃ tryambakaṃ yāce nānyāddevādaho varam  |
vijayī bhava śakra tvaṃ nānyaṃ vṛṇe varaṃ tvaham  || 14.13 ||
evamukto yayau śakraḥ sarvakāmasamanvitaḥ  |
jñātvā tasya parāṃ bhaktiṃ devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 14.14 ||
adarśayadathātmānaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ trilocanam  |
dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ maheśasya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  || 14.15 ||
kalahaṃso muniśreṣṭhaḥ stotuṃ samupacakrame  |
namo'stu te mahādeva nīlakaṇṭhatrilocana  || 14.16 ||
namaḥ śivāya śāntāya śūlahasta namo'stu te  |
namaḥ śivāya śambhavāyā'nāthāya namo namaḥ  || 14.17 ||
tryambakāya mahādevetyādi nāmādibhiḥstuta oṃ namo devāya śambhavāya bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ  |
somarudradhvāntasūryāya namo rudrāgnaye namaḥ  || 14.18 ||
namaḥ śambho pañcavaktra mahāśiva namo'stu te  |
svayambhūvana pātāla nīlakaṇṭha namo'stu te  || 14.19 ||
brahmaśarvasureśānahariharāya namo namaḥ  |
jñānaśaktikriyāśakticarācara namo'stu te  || 14.20 ||
hāṭakāya namastubhyamumānātha namo'stu te  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśāya sarvajñāya namo namaḥ  || 14.21 ||
sadyojātastathā ghorastatpuruṣāya namo namaḥ  |
tvayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 14.22 ||
ādimadhyāntarūpāya kalikāla namo'stu te  |
umākāntārdhadehāya śrīkaṇṭhoragabhūṣaṇa  || 14.23 ||
anantagaṇarūpāya nāgayajñopavītine  |
śabdaḥ sparśaśca gandhaśca raso rūpaṃ ca pañcamam  || 14.24 ||
buddhirmanastvahaṅkāro hyaṣṭamūrte namo'stu te  |
sūryo'ryamā bhagastvaṣṭā pūṣārkaḥ savitā raviḥ  || 14.25 ||
gabhastimāṃśca tvaṃ kālo mṛtyurdhātā prakāśakaḥ  |
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca  || 14.26 ||
somo bṛhaspatiḥ śukro budho'ṅgāraka eva ca  |
indro vivasvān dīptāṃśuḥ śuciḥ śaurirjaneśvaraḥ  || 14.27 ||
kalāste viṣṇubrahmādyā vedo vaiśravaṇo yamaḥ  |
kalākāṣṭhāmuhūrtāśca pakṣamāsartavastathā  || 14.28 ||
saṃvatsarastvamevāsi kālacakro vibhāvasuḥ  |
puruṣaḥ śāśvato yogo vyaktāvyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 14.29 ||
lokādhyakṣaḥ surādhyakṣo viśvakarmā tamonudaḥ  |
varuṇaḥ śītabhāraśca jīmūto jīvano'rihā  || 14.30 ||
bhūto yajño bhūtapatirlokapālairniṣevitaḥ  |
manaḥ suparṇo bhūtādiḥ sadāśiva namo'stu te  || 14.31 ||
jihvācāpalyabhāvena khedito'si mayā prabho  |
brahmaviṣṇvādibhirdevairyasyānto naiva labhyate  || 14.32 ||
bhavasāgaramagnānāṃ kaḥ stotuṃ śaktimān bhavet |
ajñānāñjñānato vā'pi śūlapāṇe kṣamasva tat || 14.33 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devaḥ kalahaṃsasya bhārata  |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahāprājñaprītaḥ stotreṇa te'nagha  || 14.34 ||

kalahaṃsa uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātumihecchasi  |
kalahaṃseśvaraṃ nāma tīrthaliṅgaṃ sureśvara  || 14.35 ||
akṣayā devadevā'tra homadānabalikriyāḥ  |
atrākṣayaṃ kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ tvatprasādātmaheśvara  || 14.36 ||
śivājñā vartate te'tra naraḥ svargamavāpnuyāt |
utkrāntiṃ kurute yastu avaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  || 14.37 ||
stavenānena ya stautiyastvā stoṣyati śaṅkara  |
brahmahā vā surāpo vā steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ  || 14.38 ||
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa sarve yāntu śivālayam  |
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye sarvakāmasamṛddhaye  || 14.39 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ trailokye sacarācare  |
matprasādānna sandeha sarvametadbhaviṣyati  || 14.40 ||
evamuktvā yayau devaḥ kailāsanilayaṃ nṛpa  |
kalahaṃso'tha munibhirbrahmiṣṭhaiḥ sajitendriyaḥ  || 14.41 ||
varṣāyutāni sāgrāṇi bhuṅkte bhogāñchivālaye  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 14.42 ||
veṇuvīṇāninādena sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  |
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi tathaiva śivasannidhau  || 14.43 ||
dakṣiṇottaradigbhāgapañcakrośapramāṇataḥ  |
snānāttatra divaṃ yāti mriyate vibudho bhavet || 14.44 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan kalahaṃsasya kīrtanam  |
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 14.45 ||
kalahaṃsasya cākhyānānna sīdanti kalau janāḥ  |
putradārāvṛtānāgha māyāmohasamanvitāḥ  |
ākhyānasahitaṃ devaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā  || 14.46 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śivamahimavarṇano nāma caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 15

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
bhūyaścecchāmyahaṃ śrotuṃ narmadākīrtanaṃ śubham  |
ākhyānasahitaṃ deva viditaṃ vada sāmpratam  || 15.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājendra tīrthaṃ vai śivena kathitaṃ purā  |
kāmadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva yajñadānaprabhāvataḥ  || 15.2 ||
revāyā uttare kūle kapilāsaṃgamātparam  |
vaidūryātpaścime bhāge vikhyātaṃ narmadāpuram  || 15.3 ||
ardhakoṭistu tīrthānāṃ mahādevena bhārata  |
śatamaṣṭottara tatra liṅgānāṃ parikīrtitam  || 15.4 ||
yatra vai vasurudraiśca brahmādyaiśca surāsuraiḥ  |
siddhagandharvayakṣaiśca vidyādharamahoragaiḥ  || 15.5 ||
vedadhvani ninādena vyāptamasti carācaram  |
yatrāgnirhuta homena tridivaṃ paryapūrayat || 15.6 ||
devarṣayo mahārāja tathā brahmarṣayaḥ pare  |
āsan rājarṣayastatra tāpasā vyavasāyinaḥ  || 15.7 ||
brāhmaṇāśca vasanti sma manasā brahmacintanāḥ  |
āśramaṃ jamadagneśca ṛṣikoṭisamāvṛtam  || 15.8 ||
kaśyapo gālavo gargo bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  |
atriścaiva vaśiṣṭhaśca pulastya pulahaḥ kratuḥ  || 15.9 ||
bhṛguścaiva marīciśca bhāradvājo mahātapāḥ  |
śākalya ṛṣyaśṛṅgaśca manurviṣṇuryamo'ṅgirāḥ  || 15.10 ||
vāśiṣṭhadakṣau saṃvartaḥ śātātapaparāśarau  |
āpastambaḥ śuko vyāsāḥ kātyāyanabṛhaspatī  || 15.11 ||
hārītaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau yājñavalkyo'tha gautamaḥ  |
agastyaḥ pāvakākhyaśca durvāsā ugratāpasaḥ  || 15.12 ||
śatānandastathā jahnurvaiśampāyanajaiminī  |
lomaśaśca vihaṅgaśca śaunako harireva ca  || 15.13 ||
ete cānye'pi munayo bahavaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ  |
jvalantastapasā tatra sūryatejaḥ samaprabhāḥ  || 15.14 ||
caturvidyāvedavidaḥ śrutismṛtiviśāradāḥ  |
saṃvatsarakṛtāhārā apāhārakarāstathā  || 15.15 ||
māsopavāsinaścānye tathā pakṣopavāsinaḥ  |
śākāhārānirāhārāḥ tathā'nye mārutāśanāḥ  || 15.16 ||
kandamūlaphalāhārā mahātmānastathā pare  |
itihāsapurāṇādi nānāśāstravicintakāḥ  || 15.17 ||
mokṣopāyadhṛtātmāno maunikāśca na maunikāḥ  |
ekapādārddhapādāśca bhūmau kāraṇabhojinaḥ  || 15.18 ||
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi narmadāpuravāsinām  |
jamadagnistu nivasanmuniḥ śivaparāyaṇaḥ  || 15.19 ||
narmadāsaṅgame snātvā tatrābhyarcyamaheśvaram  |
vividhairgandhapuṣpaiśca tathā'gurumanoharaiḥ  || 15.20 ||
dakṣiṇāmūrtimāśritya japannāsīttathā muneḥ  |
māsaṃ ca japatastasya devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 15.21 ||
siddheśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ siddhā yatra surāsurāḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devo brāhmaṇaṃ prati bhārata  || 15.22 ||
tuṣṭo'haṃ tava bhaktyā tu rudrajāpyena toṣitaḥ  |

jamadagniruvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  || 15.23 ||
homārthaṃ caiva dhenuṃ me dadasva parameśvara  |
dharmakarmaśubhārtheṣu śivapūjāsu tarpaṇe  || 15.24 ||
pitṛkārye devakārye gāvaḥ puṇyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
etasmātkāraṇādīśa homadhenuṃ prayaccha me  || 15.25 ||
dattā caiva mahābhāga sarvakāmasamṛddhaye  |
evamuktvā dadau rājaṃstatraivāntaradhīyata  || 15.26 ||
yān yān prārthayate kāmāṃstāṃstān prāpnotyasau tataḥ  |
ṛṣīṇāṃ ca sahasrāṇi kāmairbhojayate dvijaḥ  || 15.27 ||
hiraṇmayaiḥ svarṇapātrairnānābhakṣyairyathecchayā  |
kīrtirbrahmarṣibhirmānyaiḥ sarvaiḥ suragaṇaistathā  || 15.28 ||
jamadagnermunīndrasya āśrame copavṛṃhitā  |
etāvati gate kāle kārtavīryo nṛpottamaḥ  || 15.29 ||
māhiṣmatīṃ parityajya svargasopānamuttamam  |
ekasmin divase rājā mṛgayāṃ ca samāśritaḥ  || 15.30 ||
vindhyaṃ girimanuprāpto revātīraṃ samāśritaḥ  |
hatvā paśusahasrāṇi jamadagnyāśramaṃ gataḥ  || 15.31 ||
dehyetāṃ māṃ nijāyogyāṃ gāṃ jagāda muniṃ prati  |
kārtavīryavacaḥ śrutvā jamadagnirbhayākulaḥ  |
cintayāmāsa suciraṃ kimetaditi vismitaḥ  || 15.32 ||
dharmo hyadharmarūpeṇa karmabhiḥ puṇyasaṃcitaiḥ  |
adharmo dharmaphalado gahanā karmaṇo gatiḥ  || 15.33 ||
iti cintayatastasye na kiṃcidbruvato muneḥ  |
apahṛtya balāddhenuṃ ghātayitvā ca taṃ munim  || 15.34 ||
sthānānniragamattasmātkārtavīryo nṛpādhamaḥ  |
kaśādibhistāḍitā tu homadhenurmuhurmuhuḥ  || 15.35 ||
śāpaṃ dattvā tatastasya punardhenurdivaṅgatā  |
kariṣyate kulāntaṃ ca raiṇukeyo nṛpādhama  || 15.36 ||
hāhākāro mahānāsīlokānāṃ ca yudhiṣṭhira  |
durācārastataḥ ko'yaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kopakṛt || 15.37 ||
atha śrutvā mahādevo bhārgavaḥ pitṛsūtakam  |
sahasaivāgataḥ kopātsamidho'gniriva jvalan  || 15.38 ||
nihataṃ pitaraṃ dṛṣṭvā kopāddviguṇavikramaḥ  |
jagāma sahasotthāya purīṃ māhiṣmatīṃ prati  || 15.39 ||
kārtavīryārjunaṃ dṛṣṭvā kruddhaḥ provāca bhārgavaḥ  |
tiṣṭha tiṣṭha kva gantāsi nihatya pitaraṃ mama  || 15.40 ||
ityuktvā'dāya paraśuṃ ciccheda ca bhujāvanam  |
śirasaiva samaṃ kopātkṣatriyāntakaro muniḥ  || 15.41 ||
hate tasminmahāvīrye kārtavīrye durātmani  |
devadundubhayoneduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ca  || 15.42 ||
tatkopādakarotsarvāṃ niḥkṣatrāṃ pṛthivīṃ tadā  |
pratijñāṃ saphalīkṛtya sa jagāma tamāśramam  || 15.43 ||
mātaraṃ ca namaskṛtya tataścānyānmunīśvarān  |
devaṃ paraśurāmeśaṃ saṃsthāpya vidhipūrvakam  || 15.44 ||
viśokairaṇḍikā caiva tṛtīyā pāvanī tathā  |
pituścakāra tatraiva sahasaivottarāṃ kriyām  || 15.45 ||
devadroṇīti vikhyātā tatrāste kapilā śilā  |
tatra piṇḍapradānena divaṃ gacchanti pūrvajāḥ  || 15.46 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannarmadāpuramuttamam  |
śravaṇātkīrtanāttasya divi devatvamāpyate  || 15.47 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kārtavīryākhyāne pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 16

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi saṅgamaṃ lokaviśrutam  |
bṛhatyā narmadāyāstu surāsuraniṣevitam  || 16.1 ||
nānādrumalatākīrṇaṃ vindhyaparvatasevitam  |
campakairbakulairyuktamaśokaiḥ stabakairapi  || 16.2 ||
punnāgaiḥ kiṅkirātaiśca sugandhairnāgakeśaraiḥ  |
mallikotpalajātībhiḥ pāṭalaiḥ pārijātakaiḥ  || 16.3 ||
āmrajambūkapitthaiśca śrīphalaiḥ panasaistathā  |
kharjūrairbadarībhiśca dāḍimairbījapūrakaiḥ  || 16.4 ||
aruṣkaraiḥ kṣīravṛkṣairnāraṅgairupaśobhitaiḥ  |
bharaṇāyātmavargasya saṃvidhāya vanecaraḥ  || 16.5 ||
mukhagaṇḍūṣasalilaṃ śivāya sa samāharat |
ajñānabhaktigarbhastu snāpayitvā phalaṃ dadau  || 16.6 ||
vāsare vāsare caiva niṣādo dharmatatparaḥ  |
saumyāyane ca cakṣurvai dattvā saumyaṃ tathātmanaḥ  || 16.7 ||
evaṃ sampūjayāmāsa tryambakaṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan kalpagā netrasaṅgamam  || 16.8 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
bhagavan chrotumicchāmi mahākautūhalaṃ hi me  |
saṃjāto muniśārdūla sa kathaṃ netrasaṅgamaḥ  || 16.9 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ekasmin vāsare prāpte vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  |
sa puṣpabhāramādāya praviveśa śivālaye  || 16.10 ||
tṛtīyamīkṣaṇaṃ tatra devasya ca na paśyati  |
tadā tu cintayāmāsa vismayāviṣṭa cetanaḥ  || 16.11 ||
kenāpahṛtametasya netraṃ devasya pāpmanā  |
ityuktvā ca svakaṃ netraṃ tīkṣṇabāṇena bhārata  || 16.12 ||
lalāṭe devadevasya utkṛtya saṃnyaveśayat |
na kampo na ca kārpaṇyaṃ nā'nyathā tasya mānasam  || 16.13 ||
tatastuṣṭaḥ sureśāno niṣādaṃ prati bhārata  |
prahasanna bravīddevo varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 16.14 ||
śivaprasādasampannā buddhiranyā pracakrame  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrutvā tu vacanaṃ śambhoḥ sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya saḥ  || 16.15 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  |
niṣādāsta ime sarve mṛgapakṣigaṇaiḥ saha  || 16.16 ||
saputradārapaśavo ye cānye pāpayonayaḥ  |
tvatprasādānmaheśāna śivalokaṃ prayāntu te  || 16.17 ||
etadvaramahaṃ manye bhūtānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
etacchrutvā vacastasya niṣādādhipateḥ śivaḥ  || 16.18 ||
uvāca sarvakāmāptiṃ matprasādāttvamāpsyasi  |
evamuktvā śivo rājaṃstatraivāntaradhīyata  || 16.19 ||
divyayānasamārūḍho niṣādo'nucaraiḥ saha  |
dhṛtasvarṇātapatraṃ tu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 16.20 ||
divyābharaṇaśobhāḍhyaḥ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  |
divyavastraparīdhāno divyagandhānulepanaḥ  || 16.21 ||
surāsuraiḥ stūyamāno veṇuvīṇāpurassaraḥ  |
prāyācchivapuraṃ kṣipraṃ tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 16.22 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan chivanetrahradāśritam  |
anekaśatasaṃkhyātāḥ pāpayoniratā narāḥ  || 16.23 ||
narmadāśivasaṃyoge saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  |
avaśa svavaśo vā'pi yastu prāṇān parityajet || 16.24 ||
vasedvarṣasahasrāṇi umāmāheśvare pure  |
śravaṇātkīrtanāccāpi mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 16.25 ||
dhanyāste puruṣā rājan ye mriyante śivāyane  |
na teṣāṃ garbhabhūtiśca janma caiva yudhiṣṭhira  || 16.26 ||
putradāraparigrastā mohajālasamāvṛtāḥ  |
kalpagāṃ tu na paśyanti pāpopahatacetasaḥ  || 16.27 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye hradākhyānaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 17

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
sahasrayajñaṃ vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 17.1 ||
tatra snātvā ca japtvā ca pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  || 17.2 ||
kathayāmi tavākhyānamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
tārābalirnāma nāgaḥ kambalāśvatarau tathā  || 17.3 ||
śvetoragāstathā cānye bhujaṅgāśca tathā'pare  |
nāgeśvaraṃ te'rcayanti nānāpuṣpavilepanaiḥ  || 17.4 ||
nāgapūgāḥ samāsādya pūjayanti maheśvaram  |
gargo'ghamarṣaṇaścaiva cyavanaḥ śaunako'ṅgirāḥ  || 17.5 ||
ete cānye'pi bahavo brahmavidyāṅgapāragāḥ  |
nāgānāṃ cāśramaṃ hartuṃ tatra sarve hyupasthitāḥ  || 17.6 ||
tatastaiḥ kupitairnāgairdaṣṭāścaiva dvijottamāḥ  |
viṣāghrātānmunīn dṛṣṭvā kupitaścāghamarṣaṇaḥ  || 17.7 ||
vāhanaṃ vāsudevasya pakṣirājaṃ samāhvayat |
tatkṣaṇādāgataḥ pakṣī garuḍaḥ krodhamūrchitaḥ  || 17.8 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā brāhmaṇaṃ cedamabravīt |
bhujaṅgān bhakṣayiṣyāmi brāhmaṇā nirviṣāstataḥ  || 17.9 ||
brahmaśāpabhayārtena garuḍena viṣolbaṇāḥ  |
bhakṣitāḥ pannagāḥ sarve mocitā munayo viṣāt || 17.10 ||
pakṣīndre ca gate sthāne bhakṣayitvā bhujaṅgamān  |
avaśiṣṭāstu ye nāgā viviśuste rasātalam  || 17.11 ||
āplave ca susaṃprāpte pravāho nārmadaḥ kṣaṇāt |
asthīni srāvayāmāsa tato nāgā divaṃ yayuḥ  || 17.12 ||
sarpayoniṃ parityajya loke krīḍanti śāmbhave  |
nāgeśvarasya revāyāḥ samparkātpāpamokṣaṇam  || 17.13 ||
tatra nāgahradaścāste triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  |
tatra snāto divaṃ yāti yo nāgeśvaramarcayet || 17.14 ||
ayaṃ te kathito rājannitihāsaḥ purātanaḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādvā'pi mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 17.15 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe nāgeśvarākhyānaṃ nāma saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 18

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthaṃ ca janakottamam  |
revāyā uttare kūle sarvasiddhipradāyakam  || 18.1 ||
aśvamedhaṃ tu tatraiva śatamedhaṃ tathāparam  |
sahasramedhaṃ vijñeyaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 18.2 ||
lakṣamedhaṃ tathā cānyaṃ śivena parikīrtitam  |
janako nāma rājarṣiryatreṣṭvā tridivaṃ yayau  || 18.3 ||
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte tretāyāṃ ca narādhipa  |
purodhasaṃ yājñavalkyaṃ brahmarṣiṃ brahmavittamam  || 18.4 ||
kaśyapasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ brahmarṣigaṇasevitam  |
siddhagandharvagītāḍhyaṃ vedadhvaninināditam  || 18.5 ||
yuktaṃ kāmaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ puṣpitaiḥ phalitaiḥ śubham  |
dhanadasya purī yadvadalakā devanirmitā  || 18.6 ||
tadvacca śobhitaṃ nānāmuni vṛnda niṣevitam  |
kāmadhenūstathā divyāḥ śatasaṃkhyāḥ payasvinīḥ  || 18.7 ||
yājñikānṛtvijaścaiva kratuṃ kartuṃ yathāvidhi  |
yajñopaskarasaṃbhāraṃ sarvamādāya gacchati  || 18.8 ||
ādideśa tato martyān bhṛtyāṃścaiva sahasraśaḥ  |
bhakṣabhojyādi saṃbhārasaṃkhyāṃ kartuṃ na śakyate  || 18.9 ||
evaṃ pravartitastatra lakṣamedhaḥ kratūttamaḥ  |
jagṛhuryajñabhāgaṃ ca śakrādyāḥ surasattamāḥ  || 18.10 ||
nivartitastato yajño harirbrahmā śatakratuḥ  |
nānavidhaistathā ratnairvāsoyugmaiśca tārpitāḥ  || 18.11 ||
svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ samārūḍhā jagmurdevāstriviṣṭapam  |
narmadāvabhṛthaṃ snātvā putradāropaśobhitaḥ  || 18.12 ||
haraṃ hariṃ cā'rcayitvā varadānaprabhāvataḥ  |
divyaṃ yānaṃ samārūḍho yathā śakro'maraiḥ saha  || 18.13 ||
dhṛtasvarṇātapatrastu bījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
gīyamāno'psarobhiśca nānālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 18.14 ||
dṛṣṭo janakarājā'taḥ dharmarājaḥ samutthitaḥ  |
yānasyā'gre pādacārī arghapādyādisaṃyutaḥ  || 18.15 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vacanaṃ cedamabravīt |
tapasā dhyānayogena dānadevārcanairapi  || 18.16 ||
śivarevāprasādena jitā lokāstvayākhilāḥ  |
uvāca janako rājā dharmarājaṃ yaśasvinam  || 18.17 ||
tathaiva tava mūrtiśca yathā bhānuḥ prabhākaraḥ  |
brahmāviṣṇustathā śambhuḥ sākṣī sakalakarmaṇām  || 18.18 ||
evaṃ janakasaṃvāde dharmādharmavicāraṇāt |
etasminnantare prāptaḥ kiṅkiṇījālamaṇḍitam  || 18.19 ||
veṇuvīṇāninādāḍhyamapsarogaṇasevitam  |
vimānaṃ divyamārūḍho devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ  || 18.20 ||
nāradaḥ parvataścaiva tathānye munisattamāḥ  |
dharmarājapuraṃ prāptāḥ śrutvā janakamāgatam  || 18.21 ||
adhyāsustamakrudhyantamāsanāni mahānti ca  |
prasamīkṣya yathārhantu pūjitāśca pṛthakpṛthak || 18.22 ||
teṣāṃ madhye mahārāja nārado dharmamabravīt |
ke deśāḥ parvatāḥ puṇyāḥ kā nadyaścāśramāśca ke  || 18.23 ||
kāni tīrthāni loke'smin yatra dattaṃ hutaṃ tapaḥ  |
na kṣīyate manuṣyāṇāṃ tanme kathaya tattvataḥ  || 18.24 ||
jānāmi tvāṃ mahārāja sūryaputro bravīhi me  |
svarūpamadya sarveṣāṃ yathā vadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 18.25 ||

dharma uvāca - 
śrūyatāṃ muniśārdūlaśivaloke yathā śrutam  |
kalmāṣapādo vikhyāto mathurāyāṃ narādhipaḥ  || 18.26 ||
nābhāgo nāma rājarṣirayodhyādhipatistathā  |
yānaṃ copari rājarṣernābhāgasya mahātmanaḥ  || 18.27 ||
adhaḥ kalmāṣapādasya dṛśyate sarvadaivataiḥ  |
śivaloke vivādo'bhūttayostatra mahātmanoḥ  || 18.28 ||

kalmāṣapāda uvāca - 
puṣkare daśayajñāśca mayā ceṣṭā vidhānataḥ  |
gaṅgāyāṃ naimiṣāraṇye prabhāse śaśibhūṣaṇe  || 18.29 ||
gaṅgāyamunayoryoge vārāṇasyāṃ tathaiva ca  |
iṣṭaṃ yajñaśataṃ sāgraṃ mayā tatra maheśvara  || 18.30 ||
adhobhāge vimāno me nābhāgasya mamopari  |
kalpagāṃ varjayitvā tu tīrthe tīrthe makhottamāḥ  || 18.31 ||
kṛtā mayā mahādeva vimānaṃ me tathā'pyadhaḥ  |

īśvara uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  || 18.32 ||
lakṣamedhaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ revāyā uttare taṭe  |
lakṣamedheśvaraṃ nāmnā liṅgaṃ tatra paraṃ smṛtam  || 18.33 ||
cakāra tatra nābhāgo yajñamekaṃ yathoditam  |
tattīrthayajñamāhātmyādyānamasyopari sthitam  || 18.34 ||
asaṃkhyeṣvapi tīrtheṣu tvayeṣṭaṃ rājasattama  |
vihāya kalpagāṃ tena tava yānamadhaḥ sthitam  || 18.35 ||

dharma uvāca - 
na śaṅkarātparo devo na revāyāḥ parā nadī  |
na satyātparo dharmo kāruṇyaṃ sarvajantuṣu  || 18.36 ||
mayā śrutaṃ sūryeloke sūryeṇāpi maheśvarāt |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ purātanam  || 18.37 ||
vāsīyo narmadātīre śivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  |
na tasya vai yamaḥ śāstā yamalokaṃ na paśyati  || 18.38 ||
brahmā viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ śāstā satyameva yathoditam  |
dharmarājñā samagraṃ tu nāradādyāmaharṣayaḥ  || 18.39 ||
dharmākhyānamidaṃ śrutvā mudāparamayāyutāḥ  |
svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ samāruhya śakrādyāstridivaṃ yayuḥ  || 18.40 ||
dharmākhyānamidaṃ puṇyamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
kathitaṃ tava yatnena videhādyā narādhipāḥ  || 18.41 ||
dānayajñaprabhāveṇa tridivaṃ varamāyayuḥ  || 18.42 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe janakayajño nāmāṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 19

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
saptasārasvataṃ tīrtha śaṃsa me'tra mahāmune  |
utpattiṃ cāsya tīrthasya kathayasva yathārthataḥ  || 19.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
saptasārasvato nāma gandharvaḥ śivagāyanaḥ  |
vīṇāveṇuprekṣaṇīya yantragītaviśāradaḥ  || 19.2 ||
maṇibhadrā subhadrā ca hemagarbhā tathā parā  |
ābhirvarāpsarobhiśca cikrīḍe prativāsaram  || 19.3 ||
madirā naṣṭacaitanyaḥ kāmārtaḥ kāmamohitaḥ  |
vihāya śāṅkarīṃ pūjāṃ bhakṣyabhojyarato'bhavat || 19.4 ||
kiyatyapi gate kāle yayau draṣṭumumāpatim  |
gītanṛtyaiśca tuṣṭāva kailāse taṃ nagottame  || 19.5 ||
gandharvaṃ taṃ samālokya cirakāle samāgatam  |
śaśāpa nandī kopena śivabhaktiparāṅmukham  || 19.6 ||
cāṇḍālayoniṃ gaccha tvaṃ pāpasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
kṣutkṣāmastvaṃ nirāhāro martyaloke cariṣyasi  || 19.7 ||
prasādya nandinaṃ so'tha gandharvo vākyamabravīt |
śāpasyāntaṃ mahābhāga dātuṃ me tvamihārhasi  || 19.8 ||

nandyuvāca - 
narmadāyāṃ vyatīpāte snātvā'bhyarcya maheśvaram  |
antaṃ śāpasya saṃprāpya punastvaṃ cāgamiṣyasi  || 19.9 ||
evaṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā cāṇḍālīṃ yonimāśritaḥ  |
jātismaratvaṃ saṃprāpya saśailavanakānanām  || 19.10 ||
babhrāma dharaṇīṃ sarvāṃ tīrthayātrāprasaṅgataḥ  |
atha cakrāṅgayogena narmadā tīramāgataḥ  || 19.11 ||
śaṅkarasthaṇḍile yāgaṃ tatra gatvā cakāra saḥ  |
puṣpopahārairvividhairvīṇāvādyairmanoharaiḥ  || 19.12 ||
gandharvabhaktiṃ vijñāya pratyakṣo'bhūtmaheśvaraḥ  |
sthaṇḍilādutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ mahāpāvanamambhasi  || 19.13 ||
uvāca taṃ mahādevo gītavāditratoṣitaḥ  |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  || 19.14 ||
kalpagātoyasaṃsparśātprāpnoṣi paramāṃ gatim  |

gandharva uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo maheśāna varaṃ dātumihecchasi  || 19.15 ||
saptasārasvataṃ tīrthaṃ liṅgaṃ sārasvataṃ tathā  |
khyātiṃ yātu mahādeva tvatprasādātmahītale  || 19.16 ||
tiryagyonigatāḥ pāpāścāṇḍālāśca narādhamāḥ  |
sarve te tridivaṃ yāntu tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 19.17 ||
evamastviti taṃ coktvā maheśo'ntaradhīyata  |
divyayāna samārūḍhaḥ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 19.18 ||
śivalokamavāpyaivaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tathaiva saḥ  |
saptasārasvate snātvā arcayitvā vṛṣadhvajam  || 19.19 ||
kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya svargaloke mahīyate  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 19.20 ||
śivājñā vartate rājaṃstatra yastridivaṃ jayet |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kaḥ sraṣṭā cāsya jagato hartā dhartā ca bho mune  || 19.21 ||
yathārthametadācakṣva vistareṇa tapodhana  |
tvatto'haṃ śrotumicchāmi sarvametanmudāyutaḥ  || 19.22 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
yathā śrutaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  |
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu rājan yathārthataḥ  || 19.23 ||
skandena tu purā pṛṣṭo haraḥ kalpāntakārakaḥ  |
carācaramidaṃ sarvaṃ kathaṃ gacchati saṃlayam  || 19.24 ||
kutra gacchantyamī devāḥ kathaṃ caiṣāṃ sthitirbhavet |
kvaca gacchati vai brahmā kutra gacchati keśavaḥ  || 19.25 ||
vedāstu kutra gacchanti saṣaḍaṅgapadakramāḥ  |
agnayaḥ parvatāḥ sarve samudradvīpasaṃyutāḥ  || 19.26 ||
siddhāḥ sarve sanakṣatrāḥ sūryādyāśca tathā grahāḥ  |
pātālabhuvanādīni devalokāśca śāśvatāḥ  || 19.27 ||
kalpānte ca suraśreṣṭha kva līyante ca devatāḥ  |

hara uvāca - 
kathayāmi paraṃ guhyaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakam  || 19.28 ||
pralaye sarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  |
śivaliṅge vilīyante naṣṭe jagati śāśvate  || 19.29 ||
śūnyaṃ caitajjagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
ātiṣṭhati yadā vahṇirvisphuran sarvatomukhaḥ  || 19.30 ||
tato bṛhaspatirgāyan vedatrayasamanvitam  |
tadaivātibalo devaḥ kūrmarūpo mahādyutiḥ  || 19.31 ||
narakāṃścādahatsarvāstataḥ prajvalito mahān  |
sarvaṃ dagdhaṃ tu pātālaṃ nāgānāṃ bhuvanāni ca  || 19.32 ||
kālarūpeṇa dagdhāni bhuvanāni caturdaśa  |
jvālāmālākulībhūte vāṅmaye sacarācare  || 19.33 ||
naṣṭe triviṣṭape sarve saśailavanakānane  |
māyāmaye tu sarve'smiṃstraiguṇye pralayaṃ gate  || 19.34 ||
vinaśyati tato brahmā sahitaḥ sarvadaivataiḥ  |
nāśayitvā ca bhūtāni brahmāṇḍaiḥ saha daivataiḥ  || 19.35 ||
sthitā tu narmadā caikā sutīrthā surapūjitā  |
narmadāyā'vatāro'yaṃ martyaloke vyavasthitaḥ  || 19.36 ||
gatamindrasahasraṃ tu yāvadvai daśapañca ca  |
atītaṃ brahmaṇaḥ ṣaṭkaṃ saptamo'yaṃ prajāpatiḥ  || 19.37 ||
tadevamagnimadhyasthaṃ tena sarvamacetanam  |
avyakte sarvabhūtānāmīśe jāgarti jāgrati  || 19.38 ||
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlitam  |
sa viṣṇuḥ sṛṣṭikartā ca hartā ca jagataḥ prabhuḥ  || 19.39 ||
ekībhūteṣu bhūteṣu vyapāsyan sarvatejasām  |
punaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ prakurute devadevaḥ sadāśivaḥ  || 19.40 ||
brahmā bhūtvā'sṛjallokaṃ viṣṇurbhūtvā hyapālayat |
rudraḥ kālāgnirūpeṇa haratyante sa eva hi  || 19.41 ||
tanmayā kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi  |
iḍā nāma kalā hyeṣā śambhorvai saptakalpagā  || 19.42 ||
akṣayā tena loke  |॰
aṣṭau yugasahasrāṇi ahorātraṃ prajāpateḥ  || 19.43 ||
anenaiva tu mānena śataṃ brahmā sa jīvati  |
pitāmahaśatenaiva viṣṇormānaṃ vidhīyate  || 19.44 ||
nimeṣārddhaṃ ca śambhostu sahasrāṇi caturdaśa  |
etāvati vinaśyanti hyasaṃkhyātāḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 19.45 ||
tatra dvādaśasāhasraṃ daivataṃ yugamucyate  |
tadekasaptatiyugaṃ manvantaramihocyate  || 19.46 ||
etaccaturdaśaguṇaṃ kalpamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  |
harārkacandramanavaḥ śakrasyāyuḥ prakīrtitam  || 19.47 ||
lokapālādayo devāḥ sādhyāścaiva marudgaṇāḥ  |
aṣṭāviṃśatiparyanta yugānāṃ santi te'pi ca  || 19.48 ||
etatte kalpagā kālamānaṃ nigaditaṃ mayā  |
ye mṛtāḥ kalpagātīre dānayajñatapaḥ sthitāḥ  || 19.49 ||
durgamaṃ yamalokaṃ ca na paśyanti kadācana  |
tapasā dhyānayogena brahmārcanaparāyaṇāḥ  || 19.50 ||
narmadātīramāsādya ye'tra prāsādakārakāḥ  |
dāruṇaṃ narakaṃ ghoraṃ nāśrayante yamālayam  || 19.51 ||
narmadāṃ tu niṣevante vārddhakye tu subuddhayaḥ  |
etadeva paraṃ dhyānaṃ śeṣamanyannirarthakam  || 19.52 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe tīre aśokavanikāsu ca  |
aśokajananaṃ nāma tīrtha tatra vyavasthitam  || 19.53 ||
aśokeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
tatra golakṣadānena yatphalaṃ tatra gacchataḥ  || 19.54 ||
saptasārasvataṃ liṅgaṃ tathā liṅgaṃ surārcitam  |
sāptarṣaṃ nāma liṅgaṃ ca liṅgaṃ yogeśvaraṃ tathā  || 19.55 ||
candrakāntaṃ tathā liṅgaṃ varuṇeśvarameva ca  |
abhyarcya parayā bhaktyā yamalokaṃ na paśyati  || 19.56 ||
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  |
pitṝn samuddharatyāśughorātpūrvaṃ paraṃ śatam  || 19.57 ||
tatra dattaṃ hutaṃ yacca tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
purā devagaṇāḥ sarve tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 19.58 ||
saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ prāpya divi devatvamāyayuḥ  |
saṃsārasāgare rājan sañcaranti na dāruṇe  || 19.59 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
liṅgāni kīrtitānīha tathā tīrthāni yāni ca  |
nānākhyānasametāni prasādātkathayasva me  || 19.60 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga itihāsaṃ purātanam  |
nāgeśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ sthitaṃ nāgahrade śubhe  || 19.61 ||
siddhidaṃ sarvalokānāṃ nāgakanyābhirarcitam  |
brahmatejovapuḥ śrīmānnāmnā caivāghamarṣaṇaḥ  || 19.62 ||
dvādaśādityasaṃkāśo dīpyamāna ivānalaḥ  |
āpastambo'tha maitreyaḥ saṃvartaścātrireva ca  || 19.63 ||
ete cānye'pi bahava ṛṣayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ  |
ayutāni munīndrāṇāmaṣṭau tatrāśrame nṛpa  || 19.64 ||
kandamūlaphalāhārāḥ śākāhārāstathā'pare  |
jalāhārāstathaivā'nye kecidgomayabhakṣiṇaḥ  || 19.65 ||
cāndrāyaṇaparāścā'nye śrutismṛtiviśāradāḥ  |
mokṣopāyaṃ vicinvanti brahmiṣṭhā brahmavittamā  || 19.66 ||
aghamarṣā'śramapadaṃ brahmalokasamaṃnṛpa  |
dharmastu vaiśyarūpeṇa jijñāsārthaṃ samāgamat |
saptasārasvate tīrthe so'rcitvā vṛṣabhadhvajam  || 19.67 ||
gandhapuṣpaistathā dīpairupahārairmanoramaiḥ  |
varteranṛṣayastatra śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tathā  || 19.68 ||
bhikṣā ca bhikṣave dattā kaupīnaṃ mṛgacarma ca  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca namaskṛtya tān devarṣigaṇānmunīn  || 19.69 ||
māsopavāsanirato nāśikāraṇyamāgamat |
putradārasnuṣāstasya nityaṃ tadgātagamānasāḥ  || 19.70 ||
māse māse tvatikrānte ravisaṃkramaṇe nṛpa  |
ṛtavaḥ ṣaḍatikrāntā nirāhāraṃ tapasyata  || 19.71 ||
na bhogo na ca kārpaṇyaṃ na māno na ca matsaraḥ  |
na kāmakrodhalobhāśca nirjitāstena bhārata  || 19.72 ||
vedārthe brāhmaṇārthe ca mama vittasya cārjanam  |
viṣṇudharmaparo nityaṃ viṣṇvārāthana tatparaḥ  || 19.73 ||
uttare cāyane māse saptasārasvate tathā  |
snātvā devaṃ samabhyarcya liṅgarūpaṃ janārdanam  || 19.74 ||
yathā vibhavayogena bhikṣāṃ dattvā yathā purā  |

hara uvāca - 
varaṃ brūhi mahābhāga siddhastvaṃ dharmato yataḥ  || 19.75 ||
mahārāja mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |
idaṃ vimānamāruhya divyabhogasukhaṃ kuru  || 19.76 ||
putradārasnuṣopeto viṣṇulokamito vraja  |

śārṅga uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ yathepsitam  || 19.77 ||
varaṃ daśasahasrāṇi lokaṃ yāntu dvijottamāḥ  |
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye harernānyaṃ kadācana  || 19.78 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
vaiśyaḥ pāpo durācāro mahatāṃ lāghavaṃ yataḥ  |
sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo gururucyate  || 19.79 ||
trīn varṇān yājayitvā ca tvaṃ yadā divamānayeḥ  |
na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ cāsīdevaṃ śrutipurāṇayoḥ  || 19.80 ||
brāhmaṇasyāpamānena dharmo vai jāyate kvacit |
tapasā dhyānadharmābhyāṃ na yāsyasi divamune  || 19.81 ||
api varṣasahasreṇa durgamaste surālayaḥ  |
vraja tvaṃ vaiśyadharmeṇa svagṛhaṃ prati sāmpratam  || 19.82 ||
sa evamukto vipraistān sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
uvāca vacanaṃ vaiśyo vimuktaṃ munipuṅgavam  || 19.83 ||
ajñānāñjñānato vā'pi kopaṃ kartuṃ na yujyate  |
ajñātaṃ tvāṃ na gṛhṇīyācchraddhāhīnaṃ ca devatā  || 19.84 ||
sakrodhena tapastaptaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati niṣphalam  |
daivatairapi durjñeyā gahanā karmaṇo gatiḥ  || 19.85 ||
satyena dhriyate dharmastataḥ svargaḥ prajāyate  |
svakarmanirataṃ caivamuddharantaṃ tathā dvijam  || 19.86 ||
vaiśyaṃ brāhmaṇamityāhurbrāhmaṇaṃ vaiśyameva ca  |
niṣṭhuraṃ nirghṛṇaṃ krūraṃ kṛtaghnaṃ dīrghakopinam  || 19.87 ||
dvijaṃ vācālarūpaṃ tu dūrataḥ parivarjayet |

brāhmaṇā ūcu̱ḥ
ko bhavān vaiśyarūpeṇa brahmā śakro janārdanaḥ  || 19.88 ||
sāṅgopāṅgāstathā vedāstvayi dharmaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |

śārṅga uvāca - 
dharmo'haṃ vaiśyarūpeṇa jijñāsā'rthamihāgataḥ  || 19.89 ||
idaṃ vimānamāruhya gamyatāṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  |
ete vimānamāruhya samprāptāstridaśālayam  || 19.90 ||
saptasārasvataṃ natvā tīrthaṃ devaṃ haraṃ harim  |
vaiśyaḥ putrādibhiryukto prāpa lokaṃ tu vaiṣṇavam  || 19.91 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannarmadātīrthamuttamam  || 19.92 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe sṛṣṭisaṃhārasārasvatatīrthakathanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 20

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śāṇḍilyākalpagāyogaḥ sarvapāpaharaḥ paraḥ  |
śāṇḍilyeśvaraliṅgaṃ ca sarvapāpaharaṃ param  || 20.1 ||
snātamātro narastasminnarcayitvā maheśvaram  |
karmabhūmiṃ na labhate harasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 20.2 ||
tilodakapradānena haviṣā piṇḍapātanāt |
tṛpyanti pitarastasya yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 20.3 ||
rāhusomasamāyoge kurukṣetre mahāphalam  |
tatraiva janma rājarṣeḥ kārtavīryanṛpasya ca  || 20.4 ||
snātvābhyarcya mahādevaṃ gandhapuṣpādyupaskaraiḥ  |
sūryācandramasau yāvadumāmāheśvare pure  || 20.5 ||
bhuṅkte sa vividhān bhogāṃstāvadvai śivasannidhau  |
tatra śāṇḍilyakauṇḍinyau māṇḍavyo munimattamaḥ  || 20.6 ||
kauśikaśca mahātejāḥ kaśyapo bhṛgureva ca  |
ete cānye'pi bahavo japadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ  || 20.7 ||
munīnāṃ ṣaṣṭisāhasraṃ tapasyugre vyavasthitam  |
tasyāśramapadaṃ ramyaṃ śāṇḍilyākalpagāyuji  || 20.8 ||
śāṇḍilyapuramityeva brahmarṣi viniṣevitam  |
dvādaśādityatīrthaṃ ca narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 20.9 ||
devadāru tathā tīrthamanyaddevavanaṃ tathā  |
daśalakṣāṇi tīrthāni tatra tiṣṭhanti saṃgame  || 20.10 ||
dvādaśārkamidaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
anyāni yāni liṅgāni kathitāni tavā'nagha  || 20.11 ||
mūṣakaśca tathā tīrthe mārjāreṇaiva bhakṣitaḥ  |
niśāyāṃ mūṣakaḥ kaścidaṭamāna itastataḥ  || 20.12 ||
mārjareṇa tataḥ so'pi bhakṣitaśca dhṛtaḥ purā  |
tato meghāgame kāle pravāhastatra nirgataḥ  || 20.13 ||
asthipravahaṇaṃ tasya nimagnaṃ tatra saṅgame  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa yakṣarājo'bhavatnṛpa  || 20.14 ||
bṛhadvṛndaḥ samākhyāto yakṣāyutasamāvṛtaḥ  |
haṃsayuktavimānena yakṣaloke mahīyate  || 20.15 ||
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi śāṇḍilyānarmadāśritam  |
nāmnā cāpsarasaṃ liṅgaṃ muktāścāpsaraso yataḥ  || 20.16 ||
jñānarūpamayaṃ devaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ prakīrtitam  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kasmādapsarasaḥ śaptāḥ kathaṃ yātāśca saṅgamam  || 20.17 ||
kathaṃ ca mokṣaṇaṃ śāpādviditaṃ kuru sāmpratam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan yathā nyāyaṃ kathayāmi tavā'nagha  || 20.18 ||
madhūtsavaṃ śivasyāgre vihāyendrotsavaṃ gatāḥ  |
urvaśī ca tathā rambhā ahalyā ca tilottamā  || 20.19 ||
ghṛtācī menakā caiva citrarekhā ca śālinī  |
etāścāpsaraso bahvyaḥ sarvā vai deva nirmitāḥ  || 20.20 ||
vasante tā viśanti sma śakralokaṃ suraiḥ saha  |
madirānandapānena mohitāḥ kāmapīḍitāḥ  || 20.21 ||
samatīte vasante tu umāmāheśvaraṃ puram  |
kailāsanilayaṃ devaṃ samārādhayituṃ gatāḥ  || 20.22 ||
śāpasya bhayabhītāstā devedevasya suvratāḥ  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatyātha mantragītena tuṣṭuvuḥ  || 20.23 ||
tāścaivāpsaraso gaurī jñātvā tatra parāṅmukhī  |
gauryāḥ paramacittajñāḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ  || 20.24 ||
anaṅgakusumā cānyā rūpayauvanaśālinī  |
dhanapālī tathā cānyā vyomarekhā tathāparā  || 20.25 ||
cāmaragrāhiṇī cānyā gāndharvī ca tathā'parā  |
hemadaṇḍā pratīhārā ūcurapsarasaḥ prati  || 20.26 ||
umāmāheśvaraṃ nāma bhavatyo na samāgatāḥ  |
aparādho'yamevādya bhavatīnāṃ suduḥsahaḥ  || 20.27 ||
anenaivāparādhena ajā martye bhaviṣyatha  |
divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ sāgramevaṃ bhavata duḥkhitāḥ  || 20.28 ||

apsarasa ūcu̱ḥ
śāpāntaṃ no varārohe dātumarhasi pārvati  |
iti tāsāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā devī vacanamabravīt || 20.29 ||
śāṇḍilyākalpagāyoge narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti śāṇḍilyeśvarapūjanāt || 20.30 ||
gaurītīrthaṃ tu tatraiva jñātuṃ yogyaṃ hi kalpagām  |
uttīrṇā tena mārgeṇa lagnā skandasya pṛṣṭhataḥ  || 20.31 ||
tatra snātvā śivaṃ natvā kailāsaṃ cā gamiṣyatha  |
śāpabhraṣṭāstu tāssarvāḥ kṣmāṃ gatvā atiduḥkhitāḥ  || 20.32 ||
śāpāvasāne samprāpte kalpagātīramāśritāḥ  |
tatra snātvā tu śāṇḍilyaṃ pūjayitvā vidhānata  || 20.33 ||
ajārūpadharāssarvāstasminnapsarasaḥ kṣaṇāt |
divyayānasamārūḍhāssarvālaṃkārabhūṣitāḥ  || 20.34 ||
gaurītīrthaṃ tathaivāpuryathāpūrvaṃ tathaiva tu  |
ajātīrthamiti khyātaṃ devaścājeśvarastathā  || 20.35 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ caiva liṅgamāpsarasaṃ tathā  |
tatra snātvā vyatīpāte nārī vā yadi vā naraḥ  || 20.36 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vidyādhare pure  || 20.37 ||
kanakeśvaramanyattu kanakāmokṣadaṃ śubham  |
jvareśvaraṃ tatra liṅgaṃ jvaro yatra na vidyate  || 20.38 ||
jvaritā ṛṣayo yatra babhūvurjvaravarjitāḥ  |
pañcabrahmeśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ pāpavimocanam  || 20.39 ||
asuro malaketuśca śakrasyaiva bhayena tu  |
pañcabrahmātmakairmantraiḥ sthaṇḍilasthaṃ tu śaṅkaram  || 20.40 ||
nānāvidhaiḥ puṣpadhūpaiḥ sampūjya svapuraṃ yayau  |
aparāhṇe'tha saṃsmāra sa pāpo liṅgamaiśakam  || 20.41 ||
namaskṛtvā'tha nirmālyaṃ yāvaduddhartumicchati  |
tāvattatsthāṇubhūtaṃ vai liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahāsuraḥ  || 20.42 ||
viṣasādamahābāhuḥ kimetaditi vismitaḥ  |
ākāśavācācoktaṃ vai viṣādaṃ tyajaputraka  || 20.43 ||
pañcabrahmeśvaraṃ nāma liṅgametatmahāsura  |
ākāśavacanaṃ śrutvā namaskṛtya divaṃ yayau  || 20.44 ||
pañcabrahmeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ liṅgaṃ puṣpeśvaraṃ tathā  |
tṛtīyaṃ tu tathā viddhi liṅgaṃ vai sthaṇḍileśvaram  || 20.45 ||
nitye naimittike kārye grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
śraddhayā saṅgame snātvā liṅgatritayapūjanāt || 20.46 ||
gacchanti pitaraḥ svargaṃ havirdānādibhiryathā  |
manvantaraśataṃ yāvanmodate brahmaṇaḥ pure  || 20.47 ||
narmadāyāpyabhāge tu gopyaliṅgaṃ vyavasthitam  |
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ saptarātreṇa naśyati  || 20.48 ||
tvaṣṭā pūṣeti vikhyātau balinau brahmarākṣasau  |
vajreṇa svena śakreṇa hatau tau sindhucāriṇau  || 20.49 ||
buddhvā brahmādibhirdevairbrahmahā tveṣa vāsavaḥ  |
indrāṇyā caiva saṃtyaktaḥ sarvaiḥ suragaṇaistathā  || 20.50 ||
brahmahatyā samāyuktaḥ samprāpto vai himālayam  |
candrahīnā yathā rātriranādityaṃ yathā nabhaḥ  || 20.51 ||
iyamābhāti vai tadvacchakrahīnā'marāvatī  |
tato devagaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ preṣito havyavāhanaḥ  || 20.52 ||
śakrasyānveṣaṇārthāya kṛśāno gamyatāṃ tvayā  |
jale dṛṣṭvā nivartyā'tha devatāssaṃnyavedayat || 20.53 ||
tatassaṃpreṣito devaiḥ sarvavyāpī prabhañjanaḥ  |
sa taṃ praveśayāmāsa provāca tridaśeśvaram  || 20.54 ||
devānāṃ śāsanādeva samāgacchaṃ ca tvatkṛte  |
tamānīya puraṃ divyaṃ brahmaviṣṇupurasmarāḥ  || 20.55 ||
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve śakreṇa sahitā gatāḥ  |
īśāno bhagavān yatra kailāse parvatottame  || 20.56 ||
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
praviṣṭāṃstu yathānyāyaṃ papraccha bhagavān haraḥ  || 20.57 ||
kimāgamanakāryaṃ vaḥ kuto vo bhayamāgatam  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
eṣa tvāṃ prati saṃpraṣṭuṃ devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ  || 20.58 ||
mokṣaṇaṃ brahmahatyāyāḥ syādyathā vai tathā kuru  |

īśvara uvāca - 
vārāṇasyāṃ kratuṃ ceṣṭvā hayamedhaṃ yathā vidhi  || 20.59 ||
tīrthayātrākrameṇaiva brahmahatyā praṇaśyati  |
namaskṛtya tato devaṃ surāḥ kāśīpuraṃ yayuḥ  || 20.60 ||
yajñopaskaramādāya tatra ceṣṭo makhottamaḥ  |
pauṣkaraṃ naimiṣāraṇyaṃ kurukṣetraṃ tathā punaḥ  || 20.61 ||
kedāraṃ bhairavaṃ tīrthaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  |
oghatīrthaṃ prayāgaṃ ca prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 20.62 ||
babhramuḥ sarvatīrthāni pṛthivyāṃ yāni kāni ca  |
kṛṣṇamasya śarīrārddhamarddhaṃ gauraṃ nirīkṣya ca  || 20.63 ||
vismayaṃ paramaṃ jagmuḥ sarve devagaṇāstathā  |
vārāṇasīṃ purīṃ gatvā surā vijñāpayanti tam  || 20.64 ||
antarikṣe purī khyātā vārāṇasyasamā tathā  |
pañcakrośāntare tasyā brahmahatyā na sarpati  || 20.65 ||
purāṇe śrūyate deva devarāja śatakrato  |
yāvattiṣṭhati gaṅgāyāṃ vārāṇasyāṃ samāgame  || 20.66 ||
na tāvadbrahmahatyā tu nirgame punarāviśet |

kāśyuvāca - 
nāhaṃ samarthā haraṇe muktvā kācittu kalpagām  || 20.67 ||
brahmahatyā yathā naśyedupadeśaṃ dadāmi te  |
śāṇḍilyā narmadāyoge yāmye bhāge tu nārmade  || 20.68 ||
tatra snātvā mahārāja trīṇi liṅgāni cārcayet |
pañcabrahmeśvaraṃ devaṃ puṣpeśvaramathāparam  || 20.69 ||
tṛtīyaṃ tu tathā śakraliṅgaṃ caiva sthaṇḍileśvaram  |
śivena kīrtitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  || 20.70 ||
kāśīpuryā vacaḥ śrutvā devadevaḥ śatakratuḥ  |
taṃ deśaṃ samanuprāpya sarvaṃ snānādikaṃ vyadhāt || 20.71 ||
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehaśca sūryasaṃkramaṇaṃ yayau  |
śarīrāttasya nirgatya brahmahatyā ha vāsavam  || 20.72 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyādekahatyā tavaiva kā  |
brahmahatyāsahasraṃ hi tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  || 20.73 ||
tīrthe'sminna viśeddhatyā yojanāni caturdaśa  |
etasminnantare śakraṃ pratyakṣaṃ prāha kalpagā  || 20.74 ||
śivamastu mahārāja svagṛhaṃ yāhi sāmpratam  |
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā namaskṛtya tu narmadām  || 20.75 ||
divyaṃ yānaṃ samārūḍho mudā paramayā yutaḥ  |
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇaṃ siddhagandharvasevitam  || 20.76 ||
tatrārūḍhaḥ surapatiryathāpūrvaṃ tathaiva ca  |
dhṛtadivyātapatrastu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 20.77 ||
stūyamānaḥ suragaṇaiḥ praviveśāmarāvatīm  |
śivena kathitaṃ hyetatpārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  || 20.78 ||
mayā tu kathitaṃ rājaṃstava brahmarṣipūrvakam  || 20.79 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe brahmahatyāchedano nāma viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 21

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
bhūyaścecchāmyahaṃ śrotuṃ narmadātīrthakīrtanam  |
tṛptiṃ naivādhigacchāmi śṛṇvannapi mahāmune  || 21.1 ||
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi revākubjāsamāgamam  |
ākhyānasahitaṃ brahman kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 21.2 ||
kubjikā karmaṇā kena triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  |
etannastvaṃ paraṃ brahman saṃśayaṃ chettumarhasi  || 21.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  |
kathitāmīśvareṇaiva pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya me  || 21.4 ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ brahmamukhyānāṃ gaṇānāṃ cānucāriṇām  |
nānāṛkṣāṇi sūryasya jagadudyotakāriṇaḥ  || 21.5 ||
śaśisūryopagamane tārā bhānti nabhastale  |
nānyā payasvinī śaktā saṃsārārṇavatāraṇe  || 21.6 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ muktvā caiva tu kalpagām  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 21.7 ||
cāndrāyaṇasahasraṃ ca brahmakūrcāyutaṃ tathā  |
narmadātoyapānena tulyaṃ bhavati vā na vā  || 21.8 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ prītirakṣayā  |
gāyanti pitaro gāthā tathaiva ca pitāmahāḥ  || 21.9 ||
mātāmahādyāḥ satataṃ sarva eva parasparam  |
api syātsvakule'smākaṃ putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  || 21.10 ||
havistilayutaṃ dadyādyo revāsalilārcitam  |
varṣalakṣaṃ tathā tena tṛptā yāmaḥ parāṃ gatim  || 21.11 ||
yajñakriyā kṛtā tena samagrā bhūridakṣiṇā  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃ śivenokta yathā purā  || 21.12 ||
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
mā gayāṃ gaccha kaunteya mā gaṅgāṃ mā sarasvatīm  || 21.13 ||
tatra gaccha nṛpaśreṣṭha yatra kubjā sanarmadā  |
vārāṇasīṃ prayāgaṃ ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  || 21.14 ||
kedāraṃ kanakhalaṃ ca prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  |
hariścandrapuraṃ cāndraṃ śrīśailaṃ tripurāntakam  || 21.15 ||
māhendra malayaṃ caiva gokarṇaṃ ca mahābalam  |
kālañjaraṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ tryambakaṃ dhūtakilbiṣam  || 21.16 ||
rudrakoṭiṃ himasthānaṃ bhairavaṃ ca mahāpatham  |
tīrthānyetāni cānyāni kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 21.17 ||
nānātīrthasahasrāṇi purāṇe kīrtitāni ca  |
brahmaviṣṇuśivoktaṃ hi tatpramāṇaṃ hi siddhaye  || 21.18 ||
some prasiddhā'māvāsyā revākubjāsamāgame  |
eraṇḍyāṃ caṇḍavegāyāṃ revayā saha saṃgame  || 21.19 ||
rājan darśe yadā somaḥ saṃviśedravimaṇḍale  |
vyatīpāte ca saṃkrāntau vaidhṛtau viṣuve tathā  || 21.20 ||
dakṣottarāyaṇe caiva ṣaḍaśītimukhe tathā  |
darśe syādviṃśatiguṇaṃ vyatīpāte samāṃ śataḥ  || 21.21 ||
saṃkrame vaidhṛtau rājaṃ chatārdhaṃ parikīrtitam  |
amāsomasamāyoge rāhusomasamāgame  || 21.22 ||
kurukṣetrācchataguṇaṃ puṇyamāha śivaḥ svayam  |
bilvāmrakaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ brahmahatyāvyapohanam  || 21.23 ||
darśanātsparśanāttasya śivaloke mahīyate  |
kubjeśvaraṃ tathaivānyatna tatpaśyanti mānavāḥ  || 21.24 ||
narmadākubjikāmadhye nāgakanyābhirarcyate  |
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 21.25 ||
kālāgnirudrasaṃkāśaṃ bhittvā pātālasaṅgamam  |
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyaṃ pradīptajvalanaprabham  || 21.26 ||
utthitaṃ kalpagāmadhye devā'vāhanakāmyayā  |
oṃkārasya yavaikena hīnaṃ liṅgaṃ nṛpottama  || 21.27 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni loke'smin sacarācare  |
hīnān yasmādyavārddhena satyametacchivoditam  || 21.28 ||
tatra yastyajati prāṇānamāsomasamāgame  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 21.29 ||
dhṛtadivyātapatrastu sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  |
triṃśacchatasahasrāṇi vasecchivapure śubhe  || 21.30 ||
tyajeddhi tatra yaḥ prāṇānna vaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vidyādhare pure  || 21.31 ||
rantidevaścakravartī śakratulyo mahīpatiḥ  |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  || 21.32 ||
kaṇvāśramapade ramye surāsuraniṣevite  |
ākhyānasahitaṃ rājaṃ chṛṇu veda yathākramam  || 21.33 ||
tīrthasaṃkhyāpramāṇaṃ ca itihāsaṃ purātanam  |
haranti ke ca kārttikyāṃ kailāse parvatottame  || 21.34 ||
brahmādayo gatāstatra viṣṇuśakrapurogamāḥ  |
umayā sahito rudro gaṇakoṭisamanvitaḥ  || 21.35 ||
nandiskandamahākālasurāsuraniṣevitaḥ  |
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāstīrthānāṃ koṭayastathā  || 21.36 ||
teṣāṃ madhye samutthāya skando vacanamabravīt |
kasyaitāni vimānāni dīvyante divi śaṅkara  || 21.37 ||
sāptabhaumā gṛhā ramyā hemaprākāratoraṇāḥ  |
gītanṛtyaninādena kāminīnāṃ manoharāḥ  || 21.38 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
śṛṇu skanda mahābhāga kathyamānaṃ yathoditam  |
tīrthadānaprabhāveṇa tīrthajyeṣṭhakrameṇa tu  || 21.39 ||
ye mṛtā narmadātīre parvate'marakaṇṭake  |
māheśvarāditīrtheṣu bhṛgukacchāvasānataḥ  || 21.40 ||
imāni yāni tīrthāni liṅgamūrtidharāṇi ca  |
saritaścāpi gaṅgādyāstāsu dānaprabhāvataḥ  || 21.41 ||
etatte kathitaṃ skanda harmyāssanti samuddhṛtāḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
evaṃ purā mahārāja kailāse parvatottame  || 21.42 ||
brahmā papraccha tīrthānāṃ śaṅkaraṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
devakhātāni sarvāṇi saritaḥ sāgarāstathā  || 21.43 ||
nadyaḥ sarvāśca bhūpṛṣṭhe gaṅgādyā girisambhavāḥ  |
uttamāḥ katiciddeva kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 21.44 ||

hara uvāca - 
kathayāmi paraṃ tīrthaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam  |
kautukena tvayā pṛṣṭā nadīnāmuttamā nadī  || 21.45 ||
sarvāsāṃ saritāṃ madhya uttamā saptakalpagā  |
sarveṣāṃ jananī ceha nadī trailokyasundarī  || 21.46 ||

brahmovāca - 
surasindhuṃ parityajya narmadā varṇyate katham  |
viruddhaṃ sarvalokānāṃ devānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 21.47 ||
brahmaṇastu vacaḥ śrutvā agastyo vākyamabravīt |
madhye pade niyuktaśca vaktumeva na yujyate  || 21.48 ||
dharmāḥ sarve vedamūlā brāhmaṇā vedasambhavāḥ  |
vedahīnā na sidhyanti yajñadānavidhikriyāḥ  || 21.49 ||
svayambhūrbhagavāṃ chambhuḥ śambhuśca bhagavān hariḥ  |
vedāśca bhagavāṃ chambhuḥ kalpagā śambhusambhavā  || 21.50 ||
avyaktāvyaktarūpeṇa jagataḥ kāraṇecchayā  |
saṃhārasṛṣṭirūpeṇa pralayotpattikāriṇī  || 21.51 ||
bhagīrathaneminītā hariṇā'trāvatāritā  |
gaṅgā sā jahnunā pītā muninā culukena ca  || 21.52 ||
kruddhaḥ pītvā munistāṃ tu sthito dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu sā tu tasyodare sthitā  || 21.53 ||
vismayo devatānāṃ hi samāpede kutūhalam  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvā gatvā tasyāśramaṃ prati  || 21.54 ||
śāpaśaṅkākulātmāno muniṃ vijñāpayanti te  |
mahānadīṃ mune muñca pavitrāṃ pāpanāśinīm  || 21.55 ||
martye vahatu sā nityaṃ lokasantāpahāriṇī  |
devatā ṛṣiproktena muktā tena mahānadī  || 21.56 ||
muktā tu jahnunā tatra tena sā jāhnavī smṛtā  |
sā tu kiṃ varṇyate brahman pītāyā jahnunā purā  || 21.57 ||
mayā culukamātreṇa śoṣitāḥ saptasāgarāḥ  |
hariśca bhagavān śambhuśśambhuśca bhagavāniti  || 21.58 ||
vindhyaḥ saptaśailarājo narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
tenoddhatā devamārgā vartamānāni vāritāḥ  || 21.59 ||
yajñaparvataparyaṅkāvubhau vindhyastau purā  |
mayā nivāritau buddhyā devamārga pravṛttaye  || 21.60 ||
evaṃ vigarhito brahmā saṃvādena parasparam  |
tatastuṣṭāḥ surāḥ sarve hyagastyaṃ prati bhārata  || 21.61 ||
devadundubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ca  |
iti te kathito rājannitihāsaḥ purātanaḥ  || 21.62 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kubjāmāhātmya ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 22

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kathaṃ nivartito yajño rantidevasya dhīmataḥ  |
kaṇvāśramapade ramye revākubjāsamāgame  || 22.1 ||
nikhilaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva śivoditam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇvantu munayaḥ sarve śrotukāmāssamāgamam  || 22.2 ||
mayā khyātamidaṃ puṇyamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
śaṇḍāmarko'tha rājarṣirmānaso brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ  || 22.3 ||
sūryatejaḥ samaprakhyastejasā prajvalanniva  |
brahmiṣṭhaḥ satyavādī ca vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  || 22.4 ||
brahmacārī jitakrodhaḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ  |
ṣaḍaśītisahasrāṇi munīnāṃ dīptatejasām  || 22.5 ||
tapastatraiva tapyante mokṣopāyavicintakāḥ  |
kandamūlaphalāhārā jalāhārāstathā pare  || 22.6 ||
māsopavāsinaścānye tathā pakṣopavāsinaḥ  |
caranti ke sāntapanaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tathaiva ca  || 22.7 ||
cāndrāyaṇaparāścānye brahmakūrcāstathāpare  |
śaṇḍāmarkāśritāḥ sarve sarveṣāṃ gurureva ca  || 22.8 ||
vedadhvanitanirghoṣairdivaṃ bhūmiṃ vyanādayan  |
nirdhūmo jvalanastatra hūyamāno hutāśanaḥ  || 22.9 ||
kāmakrodhavinirmuktairbrahmacārijitendriyaiḥ  |
śaṇḍāmarkāśramaṃ divyaṃ yuktaṃ brahmasamadvijaiḥ  || 22.10 ||
tathākāmaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ puṣpitaistvatiśobhitam  |
sarvāśramaguruścāsīdrantidevo mahīpatiḥ  || 22.11 ||
śāsitā dharaṇī tena saśailavanakānanā  |
na narāḥ śokamātsaryarogadāridryaduḥkhitāḥ  || 22.12 ||
cirāyuṣaḥ prajāḥ sarvā dhanadhānyasamākulāḥ  |
svayaṃ kāmadughā gāvaḥ pṛthivī sasyaśālinī  || 22.13 ||
kauśeyaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vimalaṃ citam  |
parjanyaḥ kāmavarṣī ca kāle kāle ṛtāvṛtau  || 22.14 ||
nānā purāṇi ramyāṇi hemaratnānvitāni ca  |
nānā cāmaramālā ca hemaratnāvagaṇṭhitā  || 22.15 ||
kiṅkiṇījālasaṃchannā maṇimālāvilambitā  |
budbudairardhacandraiśca pārijātakadambakaiḥ  || 22.16 ||
gopuraiśca mahādivyairnānānakanināditā  |
nāṭyaharmyaiśca saṅgītairvicitrakusumāvṛtaiḥ  || 22.17 ||
bhūmirhemamayī caiva cārcitā yatra saṃcitaiḥ  |
prāsādaśca mahāratnairdevadevasya śūlinaḥ  || 22.18 ||
merumandarakailāse yādṛśaśca virājate  |
nānāratnaśilābhiśca anekaśikharotkaraiḥ  || 22.19 ||
candraśālāgavākṣaiśca ratnamālāvibhūṣitaiḥ  |
śatārdhasāptabhaumaiśca tathaivāyutabhūmikaiḥ  || 22.20 ||
candrānanapatākābhiryajñaiścāpi virājitā  |
evaṃ vidhā nṛpaśreṣṭha sarvaśobhā samanvitā  || 22.21 ||
rantidevasya rājarṣerayodhyānagarī śubhā  |
itthaṃ śaśāsa dharaṇīṃ yathā śakrastriviṣṭapam  || 22.22 ||
nṛpo yāgasahasraiśca bhūmipṛṣṭhaṃ dadāha saḥ  |
purodhasaṃ vaśiṣṭhaṃ ca papraccha munisattamam  || 22.23 ||
kasmiṃstīrthe tu nirvighnā yajñasiddhirmahāmune  |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā rājarṣermunisattamaḥ  || 22.24 ||
uvāca rantidevaṃ ca vaśiṣṭho brahmavittamaḥ  |
durvāsāḥ kaśyapo gargo nāradaḥ parvataḥ kratuḥ  || 22.25 ||
agniśca śaunakaścaiva bṛhaspatirathāṅgirāḥ  |
bhṛguratristathā vatsyaḥ pulastyaḥ pulahastathā  || 22.26 ||
kāśyapo gālavaścaiva ṛṣyaśṛṅgo vibhāṇḍakaḥ  |
ahaṃ ca munayaścaite munayo brahmavarcasaḥ  || 22.27 ||
sarveṣāṃ matamevaṃ vai mama caiva narādhipa  |
anyatīrthātparaṃ tīrthaṃ purāṇe parikīrtitam  || 22.28 ||
koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ kalpagā yatra vartate  |
evameveti taṃ brūyātsatyametattvayoditam  || 22.29 ||
tataścājñāpayāmāsa bhṛtyāmātyapurodhasaḥ  |
yajñopaskarasambhāraḥ śīghrameva vidhīyatām  || 22.30 ||
ādideśa tato dūtānnānādeśeṣu satvarān  |
ghoṣaṇā kriyatāṃ rāṣṭre samāgacchantu bhūmipāḥ  || 22.31 ||
āgatāste tataḥ sarve rantidevasya śāsanāt |
yathā vibhavaśobhāḍhyāḥ śakratulyā mahībhṛtaḥ  || 22.32 ||
gāvaśca daśalakṣāṇi savatsāśca payasvinīḥ  |
hemabhārairbhūṣitāśca kāmadhenupayasvinīḥ  || 22.33 ||
lakṣamekaṃ hayānāṃ tu ayutaṃ dantināṃ tathā  |
maṇimāṇikyaratnānāṃ tatra saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 22.34 ||
anekāni sahasrāṇi karabhānāṃ ca bhārata  |
divyayānaṃ samāruhya sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  || 22.35 ||
nānātūryairgītavādyairmekalātīramāgataḥ  |
tatra maṇḍapakuṇḍāni yajñayūpā hiraṇmayāḥ  || 22.36 ||
nānābhakṣāṇi bhojyāni pakvāni vividhāni ca  |
nānābharaṇaratnaiśca brāhmaṇāḥ samalaṃkṛtāḥ  || 22.37 ||
yajñadīkṣāṃ ca jagrāha patnyā saha narādhipaḥ  |
tataḥ pravartito yajño revātīre suśobhane  || 22.38 ||
tatra jvalati nirdhūmaḥ pratyakṣo havyavāhanaḥ  |
brahmaśakrādayo devā lokapālā marudgaṇāḥ  || 22.39 ||
viśvedevāśca sādhyāśca vasavaścandrabhāskarau  |
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāḥ sarvatīrthāni cāpagā  || 22.40 ||
mātaraḥ siddhagandharvāḥ sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ  |
umayā sahito rudro viṣṇuścaiva sureśvaraḥ  || 22.41 ||
sarveṣā yajñabhāgāṃśca pṛthakpṛthagakalpayat |
etasminnantare rājan vedadhvani niveditam  || 22.42 ||
vismitā hyabhavan sarve śrutvā smṛtibhayānakam  |
dānavā balavanto'dya te krauñcapuravāsinaḥ  || 22.43 ||
mahābāhuḥ subāhuśca daityakoṭisamāvṛtāḥ  |
āgatā narmadā tīre sabhṛtyabalavāhanāḥ  || 22.44 ||
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāśritya yajñavighnaṃ vicakrire  |
brahmādyā munayaḥ sarve bhayatrastāśca kampire  || 22.45 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā devatulyaṃ purodhasam  |
mayā vidhirvismṛtaśca mantrādīnāṃ mahābhayāt || 22.46 ||
uvāca rantidevaṃ ca yajñadhvaṃsaṃ nivāraya  |
brahmiṣṭhastadvacaḥ śrutvā rantidevo mahīpatiḥ  || 22.47 ||
yadi me vidyate satyaṃ śivabhaktiparā matiḥ  |
daityarākṣasaduṣṭāśca ye cānye vighnakārakāḥ  || 22.48 ||
sarve te vilayaṃ yānti tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  |
paśyantu mama sāmarthyaṃ devā vigatakalmaṣāḥ  || 22.49 ||
evamuktvā tato rājan kuśāgreṇa tu nārmadaḥ  |
pravāho duṣṭarakṣārthaṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ prakalpitaḥ  || 22.50 ||
viṣṇuścaiva smṛtastatra śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
uttare devayānasya pravāhaḥ parikalpitaḥ  || 22.51 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā tu ghoreṇa devamantreṇa suvrataḥ  |
juhāvāhavanīye tu kuṇḍe bilvāmravetasam  || 22.52 ||
tasmātsamutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
nānto nādirna madhyaṃ ca tasya liṅgasya bhārata  || 22.53 ||
tataḥ surāsurāḥ sarve cakruḥ stotramidaṃ puraḥ  |
oṃ namo bhuvaneśāya ādideva namo'stu te  || 22.54 ||
carācarakhyāpakāya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāriṇe  |
stotraṃ śrutvā mahādevaḥ śāntarūpo jagatpatiḥ  || 22.55 ||
babhūva paramaprītaścarācaragurustadā  |
taddṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ karma rantidevasya dhīmataḥ  || 22.56 ||
āgatya dānavādhīśo rantidevamapūjayat |
ādeśo dīyatāṃ mahyaṃ kiṃ karomīti cābravīt || 22.57 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā prahasanniva bhārata  |
ātithyakāle samprāpte yajñabhāgaṃ vadāmi te  || 22.58 ||
devānāṃ yajñabhāṃgaṃ ca dānavānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
parikalpya yathānyāyaṃ mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 22.59 ||
svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ samāruhya dānavāste ca sarvaśaḥ  |
liṅgaṃ bilvāmrakaṃ nāma juṣṭaṃ daivairjagatpateḥ  || 22.60 ||
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ rantidevaṃ pratuṣṭuvuḥ  |
tataḥ pramuditā devā rantidevamapūjayan  || 22.61 ||
umayā saha deveśaṃ gaṇakoṭisamanvitam  |
brahmā viṣṇuśca devāśca varaṃ dattvā divaṃ yayuḥ  || 22.62 ||
gateṣu teṣu deveṣu rantidevaḥ puraṃ yayau  |
kadācidvṛtrahā tatra brahmahatyāsamāvṛtaḥ  || 22.63 ||
revākubjāsamāyoge sarvaṃ snānādikaṃ vyadhāt |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehastu sūryasaṃkāśatejasaḥ  || 22.64 ||
śarīrāttasya nirgatya brahmahatyāha vāsavam  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyādekahatyā tavaiva kā  || 22.65 ||
brahmahatyāsahasraṃ hi tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  |
tīrthe'smin vinaśedyāvadyojanāni caturdaśa  || 22.66 ||
etasminnantare śakraṃ pratyakṣaṃ prāha kalpagā  |
śāntiste'stu mahābhāga yatheṣṭaṃ gaccha sāmpratam  || 22.67 ||
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā tāṃ namaskṛtya kalpagām  |
divyayānaṃ samāruhya mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 22.68 ||
apsarogaṇasaṃyuktaṃ divyagandharvanāditam  |
tatrārūḍhaḥ surapatiryathā pūrvaṃ tathaiva saḥ  || 22.69 ||
dhṛtadivyātapatrastu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
stūyamānaḥ suragaṇaiḥ praviveśāmarāvatīm  || 22.70 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  |
mayā ca kathitaṃ rājaṃstava brahmarṣipūrvakam  || 22.71 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe bilvāmrakotpattivarṇano nāma dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 23

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
harikeśa iti khyātaḥ śālagrāme dvijottamaḥ  |
śiloñchavṛttirdharmātmā satyavrataparāyaṇaḥ  || 23.1 ||
brāhmaṇī suvratā tasya dharmapatnī yaśasvinī  |
pativratā mahābhāgā patiśuśrūṣaṇe ratā  || 23.2 ||
kāle ṛtumatī sā tu ṛtugāmī ca sa dvijaḥ  |
tasya putraśataṃ jajñe kapilāpuramāśritam  || 23.3 ||
śiloñchavṛttiyogena prasthamekamupārjayat |
kṣutkṣāmāḥ te ca śiśavo rudanti karuṇaṃ tataḥ  || 23.4 ||
śiśūn bubhukṣitān dṛṣṭvā mātā śokārtivihvalā  |
garhayantī suduḥkhārtā brāhmaṇī patimabravīt || 23.5 ||
vṛddhau ca mātāpitarau sādhvī bhāryā sutaḥ śiśuḥ  |
bharaṇīyāḥ prayatnena eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 23.6 ||
bharaṇaṃ poṣyavargasya putrāṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
etacchrutvā tu vacanaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ śokavihvalaḥ  || 23.7 ||
harikeśo'bravīdvākyaṃ brāhmaṇīṃ prati bhārata  |
na mayā saṃcitaṃ dhānyaṃ na vittaṃ gṛhamedhinā  || 23.8 ||
grāme grāme bhikṣayitvā savibhāgaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
dadāmi paramaṃ dhānyaṃ nānyāṃ vṛttiṃ tu kāraye  || 23.9 ||

brāhmaṇyuvāca - 
bālahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ bāle vṛddhe kṣudhārdite  |
tasmātpratigrahaṃ kṛtvā bhartavyā mama putrakāḥ  || 23.10 ||
putreṇa lokaṃ jayati putreṇa sukhamedhate  |
putreṇa svargamāpnoti pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  || 23.11 ||
ambarīṣasya nṛpaterayodhyādhipateḥ kila  |
vartamāne mahāyajñe kurukṣetre dvijottamāḥ  || 23.12 ||
gatāśca brāhmaṇāstatra pratigrahajighṛkṣayā  |
gāḥ kāṃcanaṃ dhanaṃ prāpya śālagrāmanivāsinaḥ  || 23.13 ||
nānābharaṇaśobhāḍhyā āgatāḥ samalaṃkṛtāḥ  |
yāhi yatra dvijāḥ sarve śālagrāmanivāsinaḥ  || 23.14 ||
sutārthaṃ harikeśo'tha rakṣite kurujāṅgale  |
ambarīṣasya nṛpatervartamāne mahāmakhe  || 23.15 ||
gṛhītvā brāhmaṇīṃ putrān kurukṣetraṃ jagāma ha  |
praviṣṭaścādhvare tasmin yatra te santi ṛtvijaḥ  || 23.16 ||
brahmaghoṣasvareṇaiva sa dṛṣṭvā divi devatāḥ  |
yamo'ṅgirāmunirviṣṇurvaśiṣṭho dakṣa eva ca  || 23.17 ||
bṛhadviṣṇuḥ prasannātmā śātātapaparāśarau  |
āpastambośanovyāsāḥ kātyāyanabṛhaspatī  || 23.18 ||
hārītaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau yājñavalkyo'tha gautamaḥ  |
durvāsāḥ kāśyapo gargo bhāradvājo'trireva ca  || 23.19 ||
nāradaḥ parvataścaiva pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  |
vibhāṇḍako bhṛguścaiva śākaṭo bādarāyaṇaḥ  || 23.20 ||
bālakhilyā brahmaputrā brahmatejo vapurdharāḥ  |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi brahmadaṇḍaṃ samāruhan  || 23.21 ||
ambarīṣo mahārāja dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇapuṅgavam  |
brahmarṣīṃstānnamaskṛtya arghapādyairapūjayat || 23.22 ||
kimarthamāgato viprasabhāryaḥ sahaputrakaiḥ  |
anugrahamimaṃ manye yanmāṃ vadasi suvrata  || 23.23 ||
ātithyakāle samprāpto yācayasva yathocitam  |

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
vittaṃ varṣaśataṃ yāvadekaikāya sutāya me  || 23.24 ||
tvaṃ dehi jīvanāyāśa homadhenuṃ tathottamām  |
ayutaṃ tu gavāṃ bhūpa hemabhārapariṣkṛtam  || 23.25 ||
koṭimekāṃ hiraṇyasya vastrabhūṣaṇamuttamam  |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā dvijasya pṛthivīpatiḥ  || 23.26 ||
śraddhayā parayā yuktaḥ sarvaṃ dattvā yathoditam  |
śālagrāmapadaṃ yānaiḥ śīghraṃ prāveśayattataḥ  || 23.27 ||
kratumiṣṭvā sa rājarṣirmumude devavacciram  |
nānāvidhān sa bhuktvā'tha bhogān patnīsutaiḥ saha  || 23.28 ||
kālāntare tataḥ prāpte ṛṣirmṛtyuvaśaṃ gataḥ  |
marudeśe nirudake brahmarakṣastvamāgataḥ  || 23.29 ||
rājapratigrahādduṣṭātpunarjanma na vidyate  |
brāhmaṇyaṃ yaḥ parityajya dravyalobhena mohitaḥ  || 23.30 ||
viṣayāmiṣalubdhastu kuryādrāja pratigraham  |
narake raurave ghore tasyeha patanaṃ dhruvam  || 23.31 ||
vṛkṣādāvāgninā dagdhāḥ prarohanti vanāgame  |
rājapratigrahāddagdhā na prarohanti karhicit || 23.32 ||
śocanti pūrva janmāni anyajanmakṛtāni ca  |
bhāryāputrakṛtenaiva gato'haṃ narakārṇavam  || 23.33 ||
evamuktvā kurukṣetraṃ putradārādibhiḥ saha  |
tatra dvādaśavarṣāṇi uṣitvā sububhukṣitaḥ  || 23.34 ||
ucchiṣṭaṃ kaśmalaṃ bhuṅkte rākṣasīṃ yonimāśritaḥ  |
vārāṇasīṃ prayāgaṃ tu puṣkaraṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā  || 23.35 ||
gaṅgāsāgarasaṃ bhedaṃ kṣetraṃ kanakhalaṃ tathā  |
kedāraṃ ca mahāpuṇyaṃ prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 23.36 ||
aṭitvā sarvatīrthāni pāpayonirato nṛpa  |
cintayāmāsa dehaṃ me na nivṛttaṃ kathaṃcana  || 23.37 ||
tasmātpāpaviśaddhyarthaṃ praviśāmi hutāśanam  |
bhāryā tasya saputrā vai bhartāraṃ suvratā'bravīt || 23.38 ||
kiṃcidvijñāpayāmi tvāṃ yadi māṃ manyase vibho  |
kṣaṇamātreṇa duḥkhena sādhayāmi sukhaṃ bahu  || 23.39 ||
brāhmaṇasya hi dharmo'yaṃ sarvastadvahṇisādhanena  |
tatsamāhṛtya dārūṇi pradīpya ca hutāśanam  || 23.40 ||
ahaṃ viśāmyavidhavā bhartāraṃ prathamaṃ drutam  |
na paśyāmi patantaṃ vai jvalane dāruṇe bhṛśam  || 23.41 ||
uktā sā'kāśavāṇyā ca mā te mṛtyubhayaṃ śubhe  |
śrūyatāṃ mama vākyaṃ hi yathā dharmo na hīyate  || 23.42 ||
kubjārevāsamāyoge brahmarakṣovimokṣaṇam  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yāti bilvāmrakamarcanāt || 23.43 ||
labhate brahmalokaṃ ca rakṣoyoneśca mokṣaṇam  |

brahmarākṣasa uvāca - 
kā'si tvaṃ ca varārohe kasya cāsi yaśasvini  || 23.44 ||
anugrahārthaṃ bhūtānāṃ pūrvajanma kṛtaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |

ākāśavāṇyuvāca - 
dhanyā'haṃ sarvabhūtānāmavyaktāvyaktarūpiṇī  || 23.45 ||
evamustvā tu sā devī tatraivāntaradhīyata  |
putradārānvito natvā harikeśaḥ sureśvaram  || 23.46 ||
samprāpta sumudāyuktaḥ kubjārevāsamāgamam  |
tatra snātvā yathānyāyamarcayitvā maheśvaram  || 23.47 ||
hutāśanaṃ praviviśuḥ smṛtvā devaṃ hāriṃ haram  |
svīyaṃ gṛhamivākleśāḥ kāmakrodhavivarjitāḥ  || 23.48 ||
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehāstu brahmatejovapurdharāḥ  |
divyayānaṃ samāruhya brahmalokamavāpnuyuḥ  || 23.49 ||
putradārasamāyukto harikeśo nṛpottama  |
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyāddivi dīvyati devavat || 23.50 ||
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tatra liṅgānāṃ puṇyasaṅgame  |
mārkaṇḍeśvaramityekaṃ madhureśvarameva ca  || 23.51 ||
śūlapāṇiṃ tathaivānyamagastyeśvarameva ca  |
etānyanyāni caiveha siddhaliṅgāni santi vai  || 23.52 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathā nyāyaṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrata  |
kubjārevāsamāyogaṃ surāsuraniṣevitam  || 23.53 ||
prātaryaḥ kīrtayedbhaktyā narmadāṃ saptakalpagām  |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa śaivaṃ labhate puram  || 23.54 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kubjāmāhātmye trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 24

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
samutpattiṃ ca kubjāyā yasyāṃ vimalamānasāḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmuktā gandharvo ragarākṣasāḥ  || 24.1 ||
etadvai śrotumicchāmi vistareṇa mahāmune  |
kathyatāṃ muniśārdūla samākhyānaṃ purātanam  || 24.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  |
citrāṅgadaḥ śakrasuto gandharvaḥ kāmamohitaḥ  || 24.3 ||
samālambya kumārīṇāṃ daśalakṣāṇi bhārata  |
reme yathecchayā so'pi paścānmuktaśca kilviṣāt || 24.4 ||
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathāvṛtta purātanam  |
suvarṇo nāma gandharvastasya bhāryā yaśasvinī  || 24.5 ||
hemagarbheti vikhyātā śakrasyaiva yathā śacī  |
sutā tasyāḥ sukāmeti viditānaṅgamohinī  || 24.6 ||
rūpayauvanasampannā ūḍhā vai dhanadena sā  |
gauryārādhanayogena tasyāḥ putro'pyajāyata  || 24.7 ||
ketumāla iti khyāto rājā vaidyādhare pure  |
śaśirekhā priyā tasya bhārvyā vai rūpaśālinī  || 24.8 ||
dve kanye janayāmāsa ratiḥ prītirmanorame  |
dadau sa kāmadevāya pāṇigrahaṇapūrvakam  || 24.9 ||
kanyānāṃ daśalakṣāṇi bhūṣaṇairbhūṣitāni ca  |
tāstu yānasamārūḍhāḥ kāmarūpā manoramāḥ  || 24.10 ||
vicitravastrābharaṇā gauryārādhana tatparā  |
tāvanna bhujyate tābhistāmbūlaṃ bhojanādikam  || 24.11 ||
yāvanna kāryamasmābhiriti saṅkalpamādadhuḥ  |
nityaṃ tairyatrikaṃ tāstu cakruḥ sarvā samāhitā  || 24.12 ||
vasante samanuprāpte draṣṭuṃ prekṣaṇakaṃ purā  |
puṣpakeṇa vimānena jagmustā indramandiram  || 24.13 ||
krīḍayitvā yathā nyāyaṃ madirāmadavihvalāḥ  |
adṛṣṭvaiva bhavānīṃ tāścakre tāmbūlabhakṣaṇam  || 24.14 ||
citrāṅgadasya cāpāraṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā varāṅganāḥ  |
mohitā nā'bhijānanti madironmattamānasāḥ  || 24.15 ||
taiścāpi kāmitāḥ sarvā gandharvyo muditānanāḥ  |
nānāvidhaistathā kāryaiścikrīḍurmanmathāddivi  || 24.16 ||
atīte vatsare tāstu gandharvapuramāgatāḥ  |
gaurīṃ draṣṭuṃ samājagmurumā māheśvaraṃ puram  || 24.17 ||
namaskṛtya tato devīmidaṃ vacanamabruvan  |
rakṣasvāsmākamīśāni cyutānāṃ niyamātsvakāt || 24.18 ||
tataścukopa devī tān pāpakarmaratān prati  |
vratādikaṃ hatā ye ye paradārāvidūṣakāḥ  || 24.19 ||
apatrapā durācārāḥ sarvalokabahiṣkṛtāḥ  |
tadadharmasya pāpasyāniṣkṛtirdharaṇītale  || 24.20 ||
kumārīṃ kāmayedyastu kāmalobhena mohitaḥ  |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ  || 24.21 ||
etasminnantare rājaṃstataścaitrāṅgadaḥ stuvan  |
praṇamya śirasā devīmidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 24.22 ||
śāpasya sa bhayādbhūri vilalāpa narādhipa  |
akāryaṃ kṛtamasmābhirgandharvatanayānvitaiḥ  || 24.23 ||
prāyaścittaṃ kimasyādya pāpasya gada niṣkṛtim  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devī gandharvaṃ kāmamohitam  || 24.24 ||
dharmakriyāviluptasya svargalokacyutasya ca  |
kumārīdūṣaṇe pāpaṃ mahatāṃ lomaharṣaṇam  || 24.25 ||
kumārīdūṣaṇe pāpe niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate  |
mama gandharvakanyāśca madhupānairviḍambitāḥ  || 24.26 ||
yūyaṃ tvanena pāpena gandharvāḥ kāmamohitāḥ  |
kubjāvāmanahīnāṅgā bhaviṣyatha sahasraśaḥ  || 24.27 ||
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu martyaloke cariṣyatha  |
kanyāvikṛtadoṣeṇa kubjāmarkaṭakānanāḥ  || 24.28 ||
svakarmakṛtadoṣeṇa pāpaṃ bhuñjantu bhūtale  |
eva śaptāstu te devyā sarve martya samāgaman  || 24.29 ||
śocantaḥ svāni karmāṇi pūrvajanmakṛtāni ca  |
babhramuḥ sarvatīrthāni loke caiva carācare  || 24.30 ||
viṣaṇṇavadanāssarve pāpenā'nena kārṣitāḥ  |
śāpasyā'ntaṃ na vindanti sarvatīrthān bhramantyapi  || 24.31 ||
ekaviṃśatilakṣāṇi gandharvāṇi tathā'nagha  |
na devā na ca tīrthāni pāpasyā'sya viśuddhaye  || 24.32 ||
nānyadvā yujyate karma muktvā caiva hutāśanam  |
karmaṇāṃ doṣadāhena muñcāmaḥ pāpakarmaṇaḥ  || 24.33 ||
datto'smākaṃ mahāśāpo pāpamārganivartakaḥ  |
etasminnantare prāptaḥ sakuberaḥ purādhipaḥ  || 24.34 ||
samprāpto naimiṣāraṇye yatra citrāṅgadādayaḥ  |
tān dṛṣṭvā so'bravīddevaḥ kṣaṇamātraṃ pratīkṣata  || 24.35 ||
umāmāheśvare yāvadvacmi gatvā yathoditam  |
evamuktvā yayau devaḥ kailāsaṃ parvatottamam  || 24.36 ||
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca namaskṛtya umayā sahitaṃ haram  |
jaya devi sureśāni saṃsārārṇavatāriṇi  || 24.37 ||
tvayā sṛṣṭamidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
vyāpinī śaktirūpeṇa sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmapi  || 24.38 ||
ityevamādibhirvākyairdevīṃ tuṣṭāva vāsavaḥ  |
stotraiḥ stutā mahādevī śakraṃ vacanamabravīt || 24.39 ||
ārtiḥ kā te samutpannā yathārthaṃ kathayasva me  |
uvāca vacanaṃ śakraḥ pārvatīṃ prati bhārata  || 24.40 ||
varadā yadi me devi varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  |
citrāṅgadādipāpasya mokṣaṇaṃ kriyatāṃ śubhe  || 24.41 ||

devyuvāca - 
śivājñā vartate loke pāpasyāsya viśuddhaye  |
tasmātpāpaviśuddhyarthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 24.42 ||
devadevaṃ mahādevaṃ yācasva parameśvaram  |
evaṃ devyā vacaḥ śrutvā śakraḥ provāca śaṅkaram  || 24.43 ||
niṣkṛtistvasya pāpasya kriyatāṃ vacanātmama  |

śaṅkara uvāca - 
yajñapākāśramaṃ gatvā mekalātīramāśritam  || 24.44 ||
yajñaparvatamāsādya vindhyasyaiva sutottamam  |
tatra yajñeśvaraṃ devaṃ liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  || 24.45 ||
bilvāmrakaṃ tathā cānyatkalpagā tīramāśritam  |
puṇyasthāne tu tatrāsya śāpasyānto bhaviṣyati  || 24.46 ||
ekaviṃśatilakṣāṇi tatra muktāni saṅgame  |
gandharvāṇāṃ mahārāja kubjabhāvamupeyuṣām  || 24.47 ||
mahānadī nṛpaśreṣṭha kubjā tena prakīrtitā  |
divyayānasamārūḍhāḥ stūyamānā marudgaṇaiḥ  || 24.48 ||
mudā paramayā yuktā gāndharvaṃ lokamāpnuyuḥ  |
bilvāmrakaṃ pūjayitvā gandharvā gatakilbiṣāḥ  || 24.49 ||
yathā pūrvaṃ tathedānī tīrthasyā'sya prabhāvataḥ  |
harṣeṇa mahatā yuktaḥ śakro'pi tridivālayam  || 24.50 ||
jagāma tridaśaiḥ sārdhaṃ paripūrṇamanorathaḥ  |
ṣaḍasya cottare bhāge ṣaḍasya dakṣiṇe tathā  || 24.51 ||
evaṃ te kathito rājan revākubjāsamāgamaḥ  |
aneke yasya māhātmyātsaṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  || 24.52 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādvā'pi mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 24.53 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kubjāmāhātmye citrāṅgadaśāpamocano nāma caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 25

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthānāṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ svargasopānasannibham  || 25.1 ||
sapādakoṭitīrthāni pure māheśvare nṛpa  |
raudraṃ vāruṇamāsādya krośamātrapramāṇataḥ  || 25.2 ||
atrāntare mahārāja śivakṣetramudāhṛtam  |
prāṇatyāgaṃ ca yaḥ kuryācchivaloke ca modate  || 25.3 ||
tiryagyonigatāḥ pāpāḥ kīṭapakṣimṛgādayaḥ  |
te'pi yānti śivasthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 25.4 ||
tilodakapradānena mātṛkāḥ pitṛkāstathā  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 25.5 ||
tatreṣṭā brahmaṇā pūrvamasaṃkhyeyāṃ makhottamāḥ  |
śakraśca devarājratvaṃ tatreṣṭvā samavāptavān  || 25.6 ||
kārtavīryeṇa tatraiva kṛtaṃ yajñaśataṃ purā  |
ayodhyāyāṃ purā rājan yajñadānaparāyaṇaḥ  || 25.7 ||
ādityasya sutaścāsītsūryavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ  |
jitvā'surāṃstathā daityān rakṣogaṇasamanvitān  || 25.8 ||
manurnāmnā cakravartī śakrādīśādguṇottamaḥ  |
purottame na sarpanti mṛtyurogajarāstathā  || 25.9 ||
śatārddhasāptabhaumaiśca gṛhairhemamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
vāpīkūpataḍāgānāṃ dīrghikānāṃ śatairyutā  || 25.10 ||
suśobhitā prekṣaṇīyairnānārūpairvilāsibhiḥ  |
veṇuvīṇādhvaniyutā nānāvādyaiḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 25.11 ||
alakeva kuberasya śakrasyevāmarāvatī  |
purī virājate tadvadayodhyā devanirmitā  || 25.12 ||
lakṣāṇi dve ca sārdhāṇi prajā jīvanti tatra ca  |
tatraiva vaṃśe rājarṣīrabhūtparamadhārmikaḥ  || 25.13 ||
nānāmakhasahasrairyo dadāha pṛthivīmimām  |
na taddeśo na tattīrthaṃ na tadrāṣṭaṃ na cāśramaḥ  || 25.14 ||
neṣṭaṃ yatra mahāyajñaiḥ sālaṅkāyanabhūbhṛtā  |
sasyamālādhṛtā pṛthvī dhanadhānyasamanvitā  || 25.15 ||
svayaṃ kāmadughā gāvaḥ paṭṭavastramahīruhāḥ  |
yajñaiḥ sarvairvivāhaiśca vedairmāṅgalyamaṅgalaiḥ  || 25.16 ||
evaṃ tu satataṃ dhātrī kālena mahatā tataḥ  |
anāvṛṣṭirabhūdrāṣṭre purā dvādaśavārṣikī  || 25.17 ||
mṛtā jānapadāḥ sarve dvipadāśca catuṣpadāḥ  |
tṛṇagulmalatāvalyo bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  || 25.18 ||
hāhākāromahānāsīddevāsuranṛṇāṃ tathā  |
sālaṅkāyanarājarṣiḥ cintayāmāsa bhārata  || 25.19 ||
janmaprabhṛti me pāpaṃ kiṃcideva na vidyate  |
pūjayāmi hariṃ devaṃ saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam  || 25.20 ||
brāhmaṇāṃśca munīṃścaiva tarpayāmāsa cecchayā  |
bṛhaspatisamaṃ buddhyā vaśiṣṭhaṃ brahmavādinam  || 25.21 ||
papraccha nṛpatirbhaktyā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
anāvṛṣṭirabhūdvipra kathaṃ dvādaśavārṣikī  || 25.22 ||
nā'parādho mayā kaścitkṛto brāhmaṇadevayoḥ  |
