saṃśayo me mahāstatra vedānugrahatatpara  || 25.23 ||

vaśiṣṭha uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābāho anāvṛṣṭeśca kāraṇam  |
purā sāṃvatsare yajñe vṛddhāśca munayo'vadan  || 25.24 ||
teṣāṃ ca vacanaṃ śrutvā kartavyaṃ janasaṃsadi  |
tāvadeva muniścātriḥ pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  || 25.25 ||
bhṛguragnirmarīciśca kaśyapo'tha vibhāṇḍakaḥ  |
jamadagniśca māṇḍavyo yamo viṣṇustathāṅgirāḥ  || 25.26 ||
bṛhaspatistathā dakṣaḥ śātātapaparāśarau  |
uśanā gautamaścaiva vyāsaḥ kātyāyanastathā  || 25.27 ||
viśvāmitro'tha śāṇḍilyaḥ kakṣaḥ kātyāyanistathā  |
hārītaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau yājñavalkyo'tha gālavaḥ  || 25.28 ||
ātreyaḥ śaunako gargo jahnuruddālakastathā  |
kauśiko bhārgavo'gastyo durvāsāścyavanastathā  || 25.29 ||
ete cānye'pi bahavo ṛṣayaḥ sūryavarcasaḥ  || 25.30 ||
dharmāṇāmupadeṣṭāro vedaśāstrānuyāyinaḥ  |
anāvṛṣṭiṃ tu vai jñātvā pratyayodhyāṃ pratasthire  || 25.31 ||
āgatān sa munīn dṛṣṭvā rājā paramadhārmikaḥ  |
udatiṣṭhanmahābhāga divi śakra iva'paraḥ  || 25.32 ||
tānabhyarcya yathā nyāyamarghapādyādibhiḥ svayam  |
upaviṣṭān yathā nyāyamāsane śubhadarśane  || 25.33 ||
sa kāraṇamanāvṛṣṭeḥ tān papraccha mahātmanaḥ  |
abruvanmunayo vākyaṃ sālaṅkāyanabhūpatim  || 25.34 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajñaṃ saptakalpāntavāsinam  |
mārkaṇḍeyaṃ mahātmānaṃ sarveṣāṃ gurumarcaya  || 25.35 ||
tasyāśramapadaṃ gatvā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha cintaya  |
yaṃ yaṃ dharmaṃ sa vadati taṃ taṃ kartumihārhasi  || 25.36 ||
evamukto dvijaiḥ sarvaiḥ sālaṅkāyanabhūpatiḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ sarvānmunīndrāṃ chaṃsitavratān  || 25.37 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye prasādaṃ munisattamāḥ  |
ādideśa tato rājā bhrātarau dvārapālakau  || 25.38 ||
sudhanvavīradhanvānau pratīhārau mahābalau  |
rathe hayāni yujyantāṃ brāhmaṇārohaṇaṃ prati  || 25.39 ||
brahmaśarmā devaśarmā mantriṇau tattvadarśinau  |
papraccha gamanārthāya kartavyaṃ ca yathā'diśat || 25.40 ||

mantriṇāvūcatu̱ḥ
samayajño mahābāho tvameva vihitaṃ kuru  |
rājā tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dharmaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 25.41 ||
uvāca ślakṣṇāyā vācā harṣagadgadamānasaḥ  |
gantavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ yatra kalpāntago muniḥ  || 25.42 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhārata  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ samprāptaḥ kalpagātaṭam  || 25.43 ||
dharmāraṇyaṃ samāsādya mārkaṇḍaṃ munibhiḥ saha  |
sa samīpe samāsīnaṃ praṇamya praṇatikṣamam  || 25.44 ||
āpṛṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ tena tadā vai brahmavādinā  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā mārkaṇḍaṃ jñānacakṣuṣam  || 25.45 ||
adya me kuśalaṃ brahmaṃstvatpādāmbujadarśanāt |
kiṃtu māṃ bādhate nityaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ caiva tattvavit || 25.46 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
apamārgeṇa prajānāṃ deva brāhmaṇahiṃsayā  |
varṇāśramavilopena adharmo dharmabādhakaḥ  || 25.47 ||
śambhurna pūjyate yatra rudrabhāgo na dīyate  |
deśe tasminanāvṛṣṭirdurbhikṣaṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam  || 25.48 ||
vinaśyanti prajā rāṣṭre alpāyurnṛpatirbhavet |
abrahmaṇyā brāhmaṇāśca śūdrā vai brahmavādinaḥ  || 25.49 ||
śivajapaṃ yajñasūtraṃ śūdro dhārayate yadā  |
aliṅgino liṅginaśca avratā vratadhāriṇaḥ  || 25.50 ||
svapāpaṃ kṛtavantaśca tān svarāṣṭre pravāsinaḥ  |
savyāṅge brahmasūtraṃ ca vahṇisūtraṃ ca kārayet || 25.51 ||
gardabhārohaṇaṃ tasya kārayellokagārhitam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannanāvṛṣṭeśca kāraṇam  || 25.52 ||
anāvṛṣṭyā sasyahānistatkṣayānmriyate prajā  |
prajākṣayādvedahānistaddhānau yajñasaṃkṣayaḥ  || 25.53 ||
tatkṣayāddharmahāniśca taddhānau varṇasaṅkaraḥ  |
tatsaṅkarātkarmalopaḥ patanaṃ narake dhruvam  || 25.54 ||
gaṅgāsāgarasambhede cāṇḍālāḥ saptasāmpratam  |
kaṇadhūmaṃ pibantyāśu ūrdhvapādā hyadhaḥ śirāḥ  || 25.55 ||
cakampire surāḥ sarve siddhagandharvakinnarāḥ  |
devarājaḥ suraiḥ sārdhamāsanāccalito nṛpa  || 25.56 ||
tapasastu prabhāvo'yamapi dṛṣṭo narādhipa  |
naimiṣe ca mahāraṇye surāsuranamaskṛte  || 25.57 ||
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ sarve tāpasāḥ śūdrajanmanaḥ  |
brahmakarma samāsādya sthitā dharmaparāyaṇāḥ  || 25.58 ||
adharmacāriṇāṃ puṃsāṃ rājā pāpena lipyate  |
ayajñayājakasthāne anāvṛṣṭirbhavetsadā  || 25.59 ||
apūjanāttathā nityaṃ devedevasya śūlinaḥ  |
na svargo nāpavargaśca na bhogāścāpi puṣkalāḥ  || 25.60 ||
brahmaviṣṇusurendrādyā arcayanti maheśvaram  |
kiṃ punarmānuṣāḥ pāpā rājānaḥ pāpajīvinaḥ  || 25.61 ||
nārcayanti maheśaṃ ye te narāḥ pāpabhāginaḥ  |
na ca svargasya mokṣasya phalaṃ bhogamavāpnuyuḥ  || 25.62 ||
tasmānnṛpa tvaṃ śreyāṃsi brāhmaṇaiḥ saha cintaya  |
narmadātīramāsādya rudrayajñaṃ samārabha  || 25.63 ||
teṣāṃ śirāṃsi home'smin pātaya tvaṃ yathāvidhi  |
samarcaya sureśānaṃ tataḥ śāntirbhaviṣyati  || 25.64 ||
kāmavarṣī ca parjanyaḥ punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  |
mucyatāṃ pāpadoṣeṇa rājyaṃ svargamavāpsyasi  || 25.65 ||
tavaitatkathitaṃ rājan yathādṛṣṭaṃ mayā'nagha  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rājā paramadhārmikaḥ  || 25.66 ||
namaskṛtya muniśreṣṭhamṛṣibhiḥ saha bhārata  |
anugrahamimaṃ manye tvatprasādādyathoditam  || 25.67 ||
ādideśa pratīhārān yajñasambhārasiddhaye  |
gatvā'yodhyāṃ purīṃ ramyāmādeśaṃ makhasambhavam  || 25.68 ||
yajñopaskaramādāya sarvairāgamya tāmiti  |
rājñīnāṃ ca kumārāṇāṃ sahasraṃ sāṣṭakaṃ tathā  || 25.69 ||
sarvāṃścaiva mahīpālānnānādeśasamudbhavān  |
saptarātrābhyantarato yathā yajñaḥ pravartate  || 25.70 ||
namaskṛtya gatau tau tu pratīhārau puraṃ prati  |
kathayāmāsatustatra yathoddiṣṭaṃ nṛpeṇa tu  || 25.71 ||
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu rājñīnāṃ divyavāsasām  |
kumārā ye ca rājāno ye cānye gṛhakarmiṇaḥ  || 25.72 ||
asūtākṣatayonīnāṃ lakṣamekaṃ tu yoṣitām  |
rambhorvaśīsamānānāṃ rūpeṇāpratimatviṣām  || 25.73 ||
mahoraskandhagātrāṇāṃ vāhānāmayutāni ṣaṭ |
suvarṇāratnapūrṇānāṃ mudrāṇāmayutaṃ tathā  || 25.74 ||
savasānāṃ ca dhenūnāṃ triṃśallakṣāṇi yantritaḥ  |
pāṇḍurāṇāṃ hayānāṃ tu ayutāni daśaiva tu  || 25.75 ||
ghaṇṭābharaṇaśobhānāṃ dantināmayutaṃ tathā  |
yajñopaskaramādāya sarvasambhārasambhṛtam  || 25.76 ||
prasthitau narmadāṃ rājñe sanniveśayatāṃ puraḥ  |
praṇamya cābravīdrājā saptakalpāntavāsinam  || 25.77 ||
ādeśo dīyatāṃ mahyaṃ makhaṃ yatra pravartate  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
vaidūryasya ca vāruṇyāṃ yajñayūpāṃśca maṇḍapān  || 25.78 ||
anyāṃśca yajñasambhārān sarvāṃstatraiva kāraya  |
vaśiṣṭhaṃ vāmadevaṃ ca bhṛgumaṅgirasaṃ tathā  || 25.79 ||
pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ caiva bhāradvājaṃ ca kaśyapam  |
yājñavalkyaṃ muniṃ caiva muniṃ durvāsasaṃ tathā  || 25.80 ||
vibhāṇḍakaṃ parvataṃ ca viśvāmitraṃ ca nāradam  |
śaunakaṃ caiva gargaṃ ca saṃvartaṃ ca parāśaram  || 25.81 ||
āpastambośano vyāsān sakātyāyanagautamam  |
hārītaṃ śaṅkhalikhitau ṛṣyaśṛṅgaṃ ca somapam  || 25.82 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi vālakhilyānmunīṃstathā  |
sarvadevanamaskāryo yajñaparvatasaṃjñitaḥ  || 25.83 ||
hiraṇmayā mahāstambhā yādṛśairupaśobhitāḥ  |
bahudhā yatra śobhante tathokte yajñamaṇḍape  || 25.84 ||
kuṇḍasthalīḥ sruvāssarvāḥ kṛtvā hemamayā nṛpaḥ  |
nānāvidhairbhakṣyabhojyai rasaiśca vividhaistathā  || 25.85 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāṃ śailāṃstīrtharājaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
lokapālānmahābāhurasurān daityadānavān  || 25.86 ||
candrādityau grahaiḥ sārdhaṃ nakṣatradhruvamaṇḍalam  |
brahmādyāṃśca surāṃstatra maruto devatāstathā  || 25.87 ||
viṣṇuṃ caiva sureśānaṃ yajñe tatpuruṣaṃ svayam  |
āvāhayanmahādevaṃ gaṇakoṭisamanvitam  || 25.88 ||
āvāhitastataścāgnirbrāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
nirdhūmaḥ prajvalaṃścaiva sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ  || 25.89 ||
vedadhvanitanirghoṣairdivaṃ bhūmiṃ ca nādayan  |
kaṇadhūmakṛtāhārāṃścāṇḍālān saptavānaya  || 25.90 ||
tānānaya tathā śūdrānnaimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  |

vaśiṣṭha uvāca - 
anugrahamimaṃ manye yanmāṃ vadasi pārthiva  || 25.91 ||
kuśāgreṇa tato rājā teṣāṃ mūrdhno nyapātayat |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānuṣaṃ homaṃ sāmiṣaṃ pretarūpiṇam  || 25.92 ||
praṇaṣṭatoyā revā tu vihāya tridivaṃ gatā  |
homāvasāne samprāpte snānārthaṃ narmadāṃ yayuḥ  || 25.93 ||
śuṣkatoyāṃ tato'paśyannarmadāṃ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
vismayaṃ paramaṃ prāptaḥ prāha durvāsasaṃ nṛpaḥ  || 25.94 ||
cukopa rājā vipreṣu pāpakarmā durāsadaḥ  |
parjanyārthaṃ vṛṣṭikāmaiḥ kṛto yajño nirarthakaḥ  || 25.95 ||
payaḥ purātanaṃ naṣṭaṃ na jātaṃ varṣaṇaṃ kvacit |
rājñastu vacanaṃ śrutvā durvāsāścā'bravītnṛpam  || 25.96 ||
munīṃśca sarvāṃstatrasthān dharmatattvaviśāradān  |
udakaṃ sarvalokānāmīpsitaṃ ca na saṃśayaḥ  || 25.97 ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ tapo homo vedamantrā vaśe sthitāḥ  |
dakṣiṇā yajñarakṣā ca yajamānavaśe nṛpa  || 25.98 ||
yajñopaskaraṇaṃ kiṃcidyaccānyadvedasammitam  |
tatsarvaṃ yajamānena vedamūlaṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 25.99 ||
vitoyā narmadā jātā parjanyo naiva varṣati  |
tatsarvaṃ kṛtamevaṃ tu śrutireṣā sanātanī  || 25.100 ||
yā yayau tāṃ pratīkṣasva narmadāmāpagottamām  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā kṣamasvetyabravītnṛpaḥ  || 25.101 ||
vaśiṣṭho vāmadevaśca vadatyevaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
kāśīpuryāṃ prayāge vā gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame  || 25.102 ||
tatraiva vartate yajñaḥ satyameva tapodhanāḥ  |
kecidāhuḥ kurukṣetraṃ sthāne yatra sarasvatī  || 25.103 ||
samuddiṣṭāni tīrthāni munibhistu pṛthakpṛthak |
abravītsahasā rājan durvāsā raudratāpasaḥ  || 25.104 ||
nārado'pi muniśreṣṭhaḥ tāpaso gatakilbiṣaḥ  |
sarasvatyāṃ mahārāja tatra toyaṃ na vidyate  || 25.105 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sālaṅkāyanabhūpatiḥ  |
abravīcca tato vākyaṃ sarvānṛṣigaṇān prati  || 25.106 ||
kṣaṇamekaṃ pratīkṣadhvaṃ yāvadvahati kalpagā  |
ityuktvā sa nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ tatastuṣṭāva kalpagām  || 25.107 ||
namaste'stu sureśāni namaste śaṅkarātmaje  |
iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva umā gaṅgā sarasvatī  || 25.108 ||
gāyatrī vedamātā ca sāvitrī ca sarasvatī  |
brāhmī ca vaiṣṇavī gaurī lokamātā yaśasvinī  || 25.109 ||
samuddiṣṭāni tīrthāni pṛthivyāṃ yāni kāni ca  |
tvayā'vṛtāni sarvāṇi jagacca sacarācaram  || 25.110 ||
na tatpaśyāmi tvadvārā'vṛtaṃ yanna pradṛśyate  |
tvattoyasnānamātreṇa tṛptā yānti parāṃ gatim  || 25.111 ||
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devī rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  |
makarāsanamārūḍhā pratyakṣā saptakalpagā  || 25.112 ||
prāha brūhi varaṃ rājan yatte manasi vartate  |

rājovāca - 
pūrvān sapta parān sapta pravāhānakṣayān kuru  || 25.113 ||
varametamahaṃ manye saptakalpāntavāsini  |

narmadovāca - 
datto varo mayā hyeṣa satyaṃ tava narādhipa  || 25.114 ||
evamuktvā saricchreṣṭhā jalaughena pariplutā  |
pravāhairvistṛtaistatra vahantī sā vyavasthitā  || 25.115 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ karma sālaṅkāyanabhūpateḥ  |
tuṣṭuvurmunayaḥ sarve satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ  || 25.116 ||
snānāvagāhanaṃ pānaṃ cakruste pitṛtarpaṇam  |
tato nirvatito yajño vipraiḥ sarvasvadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 25.117 ||
yo yatkāmayate kāmaṃ tattasmai pratipāditam  |
vastrālaṅkāradānaiśca divyayānaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  || 25.118 ||
ṛtvijaḥ pūjitāḥ sarve sālaṅkāyanabhūbhṛtā  |
yugapatpūjitāḥ sarve brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 25.119 ||
tataḥ śivālayaṃ gatvā liṅgaṃ tridaśapūjitam  |
nāmnā māheśvarākhyātaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 25.120 ||
umayā sahitaṃ śambhuṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam  |
oṃ maheśvarāya devāya śambhavāya namo namaḥ  || 25.121 ||
ityādinā tu mantreṇa samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā sthitastatraiva pārthivaḥ  || 25.122 ||
tato vinirgatā devī padamūlena śūlinaḥ  |
pravāho narmadā bhede nārmadaḥ surapūjitaḥ  || 25.123 ||
īśvarādyāstathā devāḥ sarve tuṣṭāstu bhārata  |
varaṃ yācasva bhūpāla yatheṣṭaṃ manasepsitam  || 25.124 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devān rājā paramadhārmikaḥ  |
yadi me varadā yūyaṃ kāmadāśca prasādataḥ  || 25.125 ||
idaṃ sthānaṃ tu na tyājyamīśvarādyaiḥ surairapi  |
yāntu rāṣṭre prajāvṛddhimanāvṛṣṭyā prapīḍitāḥ  || 25.126 ||
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye pāpā yāntu triviṣṭapam  |
agniścāhavanīyo'tra svayaṃ tiṣṭhati sarvadā  || 25.127 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
yattvayā bhāṣitaṃ rājaṃstatsarvaṃ tu bhavediti  |
evamuktvā yayuḥ sarve hyantardhānaṃ ca khecarāḥ  || 25.128 ||
punaḥ pravardhitaṃ rāṣṭraṃ kāmavarṣī ca vāsavaḥ  |
yajñaṃ nivartayitvā tu divyāmātyaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  || 25.129 ||
mahīpālasahasraistu sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  |
viveśa nagarīṃ ramyāmayodhyāṃ devanirmitām  || 25.130 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannumāmāheśvaraṃ prati  |
tiryagyonigatāḥ pāpā mṛgapakṣisarīsṛpāḥ  || 25.131 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi śivalokamavāpnuyāt || 25.132 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 26

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi maṇḍapeśvaramuttamam  |
snātamātro narastatra na viśedyonisaṅkaṭam  || 26.1 ||
daśalakṣāṇi tīrthāni tasmiṃstiṣṭhanti bhārata  |
maṇḍapeśvaratīrthasya kūrmavṛddhivadarcanam  || 26.2 ||
surāsuragaṇairiṣṭaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
anekabhāvikaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  || 26.3 ||
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  |
tṛpyanti pitaraḥ sarve yāvattiṣṭhati kalpagā  || 26.4 ||
tatra yastyajati prāṇānavaśasvavaśo'pi vā  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vaidyādhare pure  || 26.5 ||
mānasau brahmaṇaḥ putrau bādarāyaṇa śākaṭau  |
agastyasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ maharṣigaṇasevitam  || 26.6 ||
kandamūlaphalaiḥ śākaiḥ śivabhaktiparāyaṇaiḥ  |
ekadā'vasare prāpte ajāpālo nṛpātmajaḥ  || 26.7 ||
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān cakratulyaparākramaḥ  |
aṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ vyāghrānajāḥ kṛtvā rarakṣa ca  || 26.8 ||
sapādalakṣaṃ jīvanti prajāstasminmahīpatau  |
dhanāḍhyā dhanadasyeva praśaśāsa purīṃ tathā  || 26.9 ||
sa kadācitmahābhāgaḥ sasainyo mṛgayāṃ gataḥ  |
mahīpālasahasreṇa mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 26.10 ||
so'paśyatparvatasyāgre merutulye mahīpatiḥ  |
puṣpārāmasahasrāṇi harmyāṇi vividhāni ca  || 26.11 ||
tatraiva śatasāhasraṃ yatīnāmūrdhvaretasām  |
bhaktyā'bhyarcya vidhānena deveśaṃ ca munīṃstathā  || 26.12 ||
praṇamyovāca madhuraṃ bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  |
pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇārthāya śrāddhakālatvasiddhaye  || 26.13 ||
mahānayaṃ bhavadbhiśca prasāda kriyatāṃ mayi  |
abhyarcya tānmunīn sarvān praṇipatya sthitastataḥ  || 26.14 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā ajāpālasya bhūbhṛtaḥ  |
ṛṣīṇāṃ tāpasau vṛddhau bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  || 26.15 ||
ūcatustau tu vacanamajāpālaṃ narādhipam  |
na rājagrāhakāḥ sarve munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 26.16 ||
rājapratigraho ghoro raudraḥ pāpo bhayāvahaḥ  |
narake yātanāṃ ghorāṃ kaḥ soḍhuṃ śaktimān bhavet || 26.17 ||
svasti te'stu nṛpaśreṣṭha panthānaḥ santu te śivāḥ  |
evamuktvā tu cakruste munayo vākyasaṃyamam  || 26.18 ||
brahmaśāpabhayādbhīto nṛpastūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha  |
gajānāṃ daśasāhasraṃ ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 26.19 ||
prādādviprāya saṃkalpya maṭhaṃ hemamayaṃ śubham  |
kālañjaragiriṃ rājā triścakāra pradakṣiṇām  || 26.20 ||
namaskṛtvā maheśānaṃ bhaktiyuktaḥ suhṛdvṛtaḥ  |
jagāma svapuraṃ rājā yathā śakro'marāvatīm  || 26.21 ||
gate tasminmahīpāle ṛṣayaḥ kāmamohitāḥ  |
kecidgajasamārūḍhā nānāratnasamanvitāḥ  || 26.22 ||
kecidaśvasamārūḍhā vījyamānāśca cāmaraiḥ  |
labhante vividhān bhogāṃste brahmarṣitapodhanāḥ  || 26.23 ||
devasvabhakṣakāḥ sarve strīlobhavaśavartinaḥ  |
sukhaṃ ca paramaṃ prāptā devadravyena rājasāḥ  || 26.24 ||
kālāntare tataḥ prāpte sarve mṛtyuvaśaṃ gatāḥ  |
varjayitvā tu viprau dvau bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  || 26.25 ||
śvayoniṃ samanuprāptāḥ sarve te munipuṅgavāḥ  |
amedhyabhakṣakāḥ pāpā devanirmālyabhakṣaṇāt || 26.26 ||
teṣāṃ samparkabhāvena śvavaktrau dvāvupasthitau  |
śocantaḥ svāni karmāṇi vyāharantaḥ svakāṃ giram  || 26.27 ||
papracchatuḥ śvayonīṃstau bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  |
karmaṇā kena yūyaṃ vai śvayoniṃ samupāgatāḥ  || 26.28 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
devadravye gurudravye dravye caṇḍīśvarasya ca  |
trividhaṃ pātakaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ dānabhakṣaṇalaṅghanāt || 26.29 ||
tasmātsamparkadoṣeṇa sārameyatvamāgatāḥ  |
babhramuḥ sarvatīrthāni divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ tathā  || 26.30 ||
naimiṣāraṇyamāsādya yathā yogaṃ vyavasthitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  || 26.31 ||
jagmaturbrahmalokaṃ tau brahmaputrau yaśasvinau  |
abhivādya yathānyāyaṃ brahmāṇaṃ jagatāṃ patim  || 26.32 ||
ūcatuśca svavṛttāntaṃ pitaraṃ tattvadarśinam  |
atyantau muniśārdūlau dṛṣṭvā tau vikṛtānanau  || 26.33 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ śrīmān brahmālokapitāmahaḥ  |
devadravyāpahāreṇa duṣkṛtaṃ svargagarhitam  || 26.34 ||
surāsuragaṇairyattu laṅghituṃ naiva śakyate  |
kiṃ punarmānuṣaiḥ kṣudrairdevadravyopajīvikaiḥ  || 26.35 ||
teṣāṃ tu niyataṃ ghore narake patanaṃ smṛtam  |
niṣkṛtirnarmadā teṣāṃ vihitā lokapāvanī  || 26.36 ||
snātvā tu kalpagā toye'bhyarcayitvā vṛṣadhvajam  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ satyametatmayoditam  || 26.37 ||
pitāmahavacaḥ śrutvā bādarāyaṇaśākaṭau  |
ṛṣibhiḥ saha tatraiva narmadātīramāśritau  || 26.38 ||
sarinnānyā'sti loke'sminpāpasyāsya viśuddhaye  |
tataste munayaḥ sarve smarantaḥ pūrvaduṣkṛtam  || 26.39 ||
ṣaṇmāsā'bhyantare rājan chivadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ  |
niṣkalmaṣāḥ babhūvuste tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 26.40 ||
balyādyā munayaḥ sarve śatakratupurogamāḥ  |
dadṛśuste kratuvaraṃ śivenaiva yathoditam  || 26.41 ||
gṛhītvā'tha munīn sarve brahmādyāśca surāsurāḥ  |
suprabhāstāṃstu devatvaṃ maṇḍapeśvaradarśanāt || 26.42 ||
tena liṅgaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ loke'sminmaṇḍapeśvaram  |
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte tretāyāṃ tu nṛpottama  || 26.43 ||
kṣatriyāṇāṃ sahasrāṇi tatra siddhāni bhārata  |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ samāsena mayā'nagha  || 26.44 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādrājan hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 26.45 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
amareśvarapūrveṇa paryaṅkātpaścime tathā  || 26.46 ||
tīrthasaṃkhyāṃ krameṇaiva kathayasva tapodhana  || 26.47 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga pūrvabhāge vyavasthitam  || 26.48 ||
śvetakiṃśukanāmānaṃ tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
narāḥ sukhena rūpeṇa yatra snātā divaṃ gatāḥ  || 26.49 ||
śvetakiṃśukanāmāsti liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  |
tāṭakeśvaradevaśca tatra svargaphalapradaḥ  || 26.50 ||
anyattu varṇanāmeti tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
tryambakastu mahādevo yatraloke varapradaḥ  || 26.51 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyādgaṇḍeśastridivaṃ gataḥ  |
gaṇḍakeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu liṅgaṃ śukleśvaraṃ tathā  || 26.52 ||
narmadādantivanikāsaṅgamo lokaviśrutaḥ  |
tatra liṅgeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam  || 26.53 ||
bālakeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu tathā'nyatpūrṇakeśvaram  |
revāyā uttare kūle narmadāpuramuttamam  || 26.54 ||
tīrthaṃ kapiśilā nāma sarvānarthavidūṣaṇam  |
liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ nāma tathā'nyannāḍakeśvaram  || 26.55 ||
atrāntare nṛpaśreṣṭha daśalakṣāṇi nāmataḥ  |
tīrthāni daśalakṣāṇi kīrtitāni yathākramam  || 26.56 ||
tato gacchennṛpaśreṣṭha vaidūryātpaścimāṃ diśam  |
śaśabhīnarmadāyogaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 26.57 ||
bhuktidaṃ muktida caiva liṅgaṃ vai śaśabheśvaram  |
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ gardabhīyonimokṣaṇam  || 26.58 ||
maṇḍaleśvaranāmeha tīrthaṃ liṅgaṃ narādhipa  |
yatra māṇḍalikāḥ siddhā ajāpālo manustathā  || 26.59 ||
tatra ceṣṭvā tu manujaḥ sambhavenna punarbhave  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  || 26.60 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvaccandradivākarau  |
tatra pradīyate dānaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 26.61 ||
kāntārakaṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthavaraṃ śubham  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 26.62 ||
sapādalakṣamadhikaṃ tīrthānāṃ maṇḍaleśvare  |
kīrtite tava rājendra yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam  || 26.63 ||
tretāyāṃ raghuvaṃśe tu kumārau rāmalakṣaṇau  |
maithilyā saha rājendra uttīrṇo yatra kalpagām  || 26.64 ||
jagmatuḥ piturājñāṃ vai kurvantau viṣṇurūpiṇau  |
snātvā tīrthavare tatra bhaktyā'bhyarcya maheśvaram  || 26.65 ||
rājatīrthaṃ tu tadgopyaṃ liṅgaṃ vai lakṣaṇeśvaram  |
sīteśvaraṃ tathā liṅgaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 26.66 ||
tatra snātvā'rcayitvā tu śūlapāṇiṃ maheśvaram  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt || 26.67 ||
tatogacchetnṛpaśreṣṭha puṇyatīrthaṃ śivālayam  |
māhiṣmatīṃ purīṃ ramyāṃ tāṃ dṛṣṭvā na cyutaḥ kvacit || 26.68 ||
yatra kālāgnirudro'sti prajvalanniva hetubhiḥ  |
trayastriṃśattu tiṣṭhantu liṅgānāṃ koṭayastathā  || 26.69 ||
tataḥ koṭīśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ koṭitīrthe narādhipa  |
yajñakoṭiphalaṃ tatra tasya liṅgasya pūjanāt || 26.70 ||
tatra dattasya dānasya koṭisaṃkhyā tu vidyate  |
daśāśvamedhatīrthaṃ tu bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 26.71 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ gatiruttamā  |
snātamātro narastatra sūryateja samaprabhaḥ  || 26.72 ||
purāṇi pañcasāmānyācchambhunā kīrtitāni vai  |
prabhāsaśca kurukṣetraṃ tathā māyāpurī śubhā  || 26.73 ||
avantī ca mahākālaṃ tathā māheśvaraṃ puram  |
eteṣu ca samagreṣu viddhi liṅgānyanukramāt || 26.74 ||
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ veṣṭamakṣayādapi cākṣayam  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti ya  || 26.75 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
kīrtayetprātarutthāya puṇyānyetāni yo naraḥ  || 26.76 ||
na sa pāpena lipyeta yamalokaṃ na paśyati  |
tīrthaṃ pipīlikā nāma gatā yatra pipīlikāḥ  || 26.77 ||
śivalokaṃ mahābhāga sarvalokottamottamam  |
bandhyārevāsamāyogaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 26.78 ||
saṅgame yatra rājendra liṅgaṃ vai munakeśvaram  |
yoginastattu paśyanti na tatpaśyanti mānuṣāḥ  || 26.79 ||
vikhyātaṃ tattu nagaraṃ narmadādakṣiṇe tate  |
ayuta yatra liṅgānāṃ tīrthānāṃ ca narādhipa  || 26.80 ||
liṅgaṃ caṇḍīśvaraṃ nāma tathaivoḍugaṇeśvaram  |
vakeśvaraṃ tatra viddhi vakā yatra divaṃ gatāḥ  || 26.81 ||
tīrthaṃ gaṅgāvahaṃ nāma liṅgaṃ vai sarvasiddhidam  |
aṅgāreśamiti jñeyaṃ vimalaṃ tatra bhārata  || 26.82 ||
somatīrthamiti jñeyaṃ śuklatīrthamataḥ param  |
tīrthānāṃ nirasaṃ nāma dhruvatīrthaṃ narādhipa  || 26.83 ||
anekāni sahasrāṇi tīrthānāṃ caiva bhārata  |
tīrthaṃ pipīlikā nāma bhuktimuktipradāyakam  || 26.84 ||
krośamātraṃ tu vijñeyaṃ pūrvapaścimatastathā  |
tīrthānāmayutaṃ sārddhaṃ ṛṣidevaniṣevitam  || 26.85 ||
tatra dattaṃ hutaṃ caiva tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
tatra yaḥ santyajetprāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  || 26.86 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukta umāmāheśvare pure  |
modate sarvakāmaistu yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 26.87 ||
yasmādeva śivaṃ tasmādarcayecchāntamānasaḥ  |
maitraḥ kāruṇiko nityaṃ prāpnoti paramaṃ padam  || 26.88 ||
kṣaṇamātreṇa yatpuṇyaṃ tataḥ kuryānnarādhipa  |
na tadvarṣaśatenā'pi na tu yajñaśatairapi  || 26.89 ||
śakyaṃ sādhayituṃ rājaṃstathā tīrthaśatairapi  |
sarvaprāṇiṣu kāruṇyaṃ dīnānātheṣu bhāvayan  || 26.90 ||
maitrī ca muditā rājan puṇyaśīleṣu sarvadā  |
puṇyavatsukhamāpekṣyaṃ sarvaprāṇiṣu yatnataḥ  || 26.91 ||
akṣetre tu kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ samaṃ bhavati bhārata  |
narmadāsaṅgamo yatra tatra saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 26.92 ||
anyadeśe kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ puṇyakṣetre vinaśyati  |
puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vajralepo bhaviṣyati  || 26.93 ||
uttīrṇo narmadāṃ yatra kārttikeyo mahābalaḥ  |
liṅgaṃ tatra ca vijñeyaṃ siddhidaṃ kārttikeśvaram  || 26.94 ||
candreśvaraṃ tathā liṅgaṃ liṅgaṃ caiva śikhīśvaram  |
śaktīśvaraṃ tathā cānyatsarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 26.95 ||
teṣāñcaiva liṅgānāmarcanaṃ bhaktibhāvataḥ  |
brahmahatyādikātpāpānmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 26.96 ||
śivalokamavāpnoti pitṝṇāṃ svargatistathā  || 26.97 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
bhagavān chrotumicchāmi māhiṣmatyāstu paścime  || 26.98 ||
sannidhau ca tileśasya sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
rāsabhīnarmadābhedaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 26.99 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga itihāsaṃ purātanam  |
narmadāgardabhībhedaṃ tiryagyonivimokṣaṇam  || 26.100 ||
yasmiṃstīrthe mahārāja satejāharikeśayoḥ  |
apārādrāsabhatvācca muktiḥ kalmaṣanāśane  || 26.101 ||
havirdhānastu rājarṣirāsītkalpo yudhiṣṭhira  |
ātreyasya sutaścāsīdbrahmarṣirbrahmavittamaḥ  || 26.102 ||
pāvakasya sutāyā tu pāṇigrahaṇadharmataḥ  |
havirdhānāya sā dattā sutejā nāma nāmataḥ  || 26.103 ||
kuśavalkaparīdhānā kandamūlaphalāśinī  |
rūpayauvanasampannā pārvatīva manoharā  || 26.104 ||
havirdhānastu rājarṣirṛtuṃ buddhvā gatastu tām  |
āgato'sau yuvā tatra sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 26.105 ||
maharṣiṃ tvāgataṃ jñātvā sutejā kāmituṃ gatā  |
sahasā'laṃkṛtāṃ tāṃ tu karaṃ jagrāha sa dvijaḥ  || 26.106 ||
tena sā dharṣitā tatra yatheṣṭaṃ kāmapīḍitā  |
agnihotrasya śālāyāṃ dāmpatyaṃ kāmasaṃyutam  || 26.107 ||
dadṛśe sa mahātmā vai dharmādharmaviśāradaḥ  |
āsīdviṣaṇṇavadano dṛṣṭvā tāṃ pāvakātmajām  || 26.108 ||
avadhyo brāhmaṇo duṣṭaḥ pāpātmā pāpakarmakṛt |
iyaṃ ca patnī duṣṭā me na vadhyā strīsvabhāvataḥ  || 26.109 ||
tamuvāca vicāryaivaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dārakarṣakam  |
mātaraṃ gurupatnīṃ ca svasāraṃ duhitaraṃ tathā  || 26.110 ||
gatvā tu praviśedagniṃ tataḥ śuddhyeta mānavaḥ  |
gardabhastvaṃ bhavedvipra gardabhī ca tathā vidhiḥ  || 26.111 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu amedhyaṃ bhakṣayiṣyathaḥ  |
sutejāharikeśau tu havirdhānaḥ śaśāpa tau  || 26.112 ||
pīḍitau karmaṇā tena gatau dvau badrikāśramam  |
himasthānaṃ ca kedāraṃ bhairavaṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā  || 26.113 ||
sūryākṣaṃ ca gayātīrthaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  |
vārāṇasīṃ prayāgaṃ ca oghatīrthaṃ ca puṣkaram  || 26.114 ||
yogīśvaraṃ rudrakoṭiṃ mahendraṃ brahmasambhavam  |
prabhāsaṃ ca kurukṣetraṃ tīrthaṃ saumyeśvaraṃ tathā  || 26.115 ||
anekāni ca tīrthāni pṛthivyāṃ yāni kāni ca  |
sārddhaṃ tayā tapasvinyā havirdhānasya śāpataḥ  || 26.116 ||
anekaduḥkhasampanno harikeśo bhramanmahīm  |
kharayoniniyuktastu paradārābhikarṣakaḥ  || 26.117 ||
kālena bhūyasā tatra harikeśastayā saha  |
tenaiva khararūpeṇa agastyaṃ ca mahāmunim  || 26.118 ||
namaskṛtya muniṃ tatra sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
gurutalpagapāpasya paradārābhigaminaḥ  || 26.119 ||
prāyaścittaṃ vidhānena dīyatāṃ me dvijottama  |
mocaya tvamimāṃ yoniṃ brahmalokapadasthitaḥ  || 26.120 ||
havirdhānāntikaṃ yāhi tato'gastya uvāca tam  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā munīnāmūrdhvaretasām  |
harikeśo'bravīdvākyaṃ sutejā saha saṅgataḥ  || 26.121 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye brāhmaṇānāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
tato gatau tu taṃ rājan havirdhānasya cāśramam  || 26.122 ||
namaskṛtya muniśreṣṭhaṃ harikeśo'bravīdvacaḥ  |
gurutalpagapāpo'haṃ kṣamasva mayi putrake  || 26.123 ||
śāpāntaṃ ca varaṃ manye dātumarhasi suvrata  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā harikeśasya durmateḥ  || 26.124 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ viprastaṃ vai gardabharūpiṇam  |
svakarmaṇā tanuṃ tvaṃ hi gārdabhīṃ prāptavānasi  || 26.125 ||
janmāntarakṛtaiścaiva karmabhiḥ karmakāribhiḥ  |
śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vāpi prāpyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 26.126 ||
karmaṇāṃ ca vipāko'yamapi devaiḥ savāsavaiḥ  |
jñātuṃ na śakyate vipra gāhanā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  || 26.127 ||
doṣo na vidyate caiva tadbrāhmaṇa kathaṃcana  |
kiṃtu janmāntare yena karmaṇā tatkṛtaṃ tava  || 26.128 ||
tasmādviśa hutāśaṃ tvamanayā mekalātaṭe  |
śaṅkarādvaramāsādya tāvatprāpsyasi sadgatim  || 26.129 ||
evamukto yayau rājan harikeśastayā saha  |
harikeśaḥ sutejā ca narmadātīrasannidhau  || 26.130 ||
dārūṇi ca samāhṛtya praviṣṭau ca hutāśanam  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehau tu snātvā spṛṣṭvā hyubhāvapi  || 26.131 ||
kāmikaṃ yānamārūḍhau sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitau  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyādyathā lakṣmījanārdanau  || 26.132 ||
bhuñjantau vividhān bhogān gatau māheśvaraṃ purum  |
tenā'sau saṅgamaḥ puṇyastiryagyonivimokṣaṇaḥ  || 26.133 ||
harikeśeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ sutejānirmitaṃ tathā  |
havirdhāneśvaraṃ nāma caturtho'gastyanirmitam  || 26.134 ||
catvāri puṇyaliṅgāni kāmamokṣapradāni tu  |
tilodakapradānena tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 26.135 ||
mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ caiva narakāduddharetpitṝn  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 26.136 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannākhyānaṃ ca purātanam  || 26.137 ||
iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe gardabhītīrthavarṇano nāma ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 27

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
gaurīkhaṇḍaṃ tato gacchetsarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
tatra snānena labhate sarvatīrthaphalaṃ naraḥ  || 27.1 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ tṛptirakṣayā  |
jāyate ca nṛpaśreṣṭha nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 27.2 ||
gaurīkhaṇḍeśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ pāpaharaṃ param  |
tatrajñeyaṃ maṇimayaṃ jalamadhye vyavasthitam  || 27.3 ||
na tatpaśyanti manujāḥ sarvairdevaistu pūjitam  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
gaurīkhaṇḍeśvaraṃ nāma tasmiṃstīrthe kathaṃ mune  || 27.4 ||
kathyatāṃ ca yathā nyāyaṃ viditaṃ yattu sāmpratam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
purādevagaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ kumāraḥ śaṅkarātmaja  || 27.5 ||
saināpatye niyuktaśca tārakasya vadhaṃ prati  |
kāmitāstena tatraiva sarvāstāḥ surayoṣitaḥ  || 27.6 ||
upālabdhāstataḥ sarvā umāmāheśvare pure  |
upālabdhaṃ tu taṃ śrutvā viṣaṇṇā caiva pārvatī  || 27.7 ||
kāmitaṃ yatra yatraiva tatra tatreśvareśvarī  || 27.8 ||
dṛṣṭvā'tha lajjitaḥ so'pi pakṣiṇā'sau samāyayau  |
devaiḥ parivṛtaḥ śrīmānmayūrastho mahābalaḥ  || 27.9 ||
pṛṣṭhato'nugatā mātā rudatīva sureśvarī  |
uttīrṇā kalpagāṃ devī pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 27.10 ||
gaurīkhaṇḍaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu tena tat |
liṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tatra kumāreśvarasaṃjñitam  || 27.11 ||
mayūreśvaraliṅgaṃ tu bhuktimuktiphalapradam  |
yasya devasya māhātmyātmayūrāstridivaṅgatāḥ  || 27.12 ||
arcanāttasya devasya tiryagyonirna jāyate  |
tato gacchetmahārāja karamardāsamāgamam  || 27.13 ||
tatra snāto mahārāja sa bhave na punarbhavet |
karamardeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ pūjayettatra bhārata  || 27.14 ||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇāttatra svargaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kathayasva mahābhāga karamardāsamudbhavam  || 27.15 ||
bhāviṣyabhūtatattvajñastrikālajñastrivedavit |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kathayāmi yathādṛṣṭaṃ śṛṇu caikamanā nṛpa  || 27.16 ||
maitreyasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ ṛṣibhistu niṣevitam  |
munīnāṃ tu sahasrāṇi kandāmūlaphalāśinām  || 27.17 ||
nivasanti yadā tatra tapaḥ kartuṃ nirantaram  |
kasmiṃścidanyakāle tu tasminmunivarāśrame  || 27.18 ||
rājā kuśadhvajo nāma ekacchatrādhipo nṛpa  |
āgamatkalātīraṃ rāhusūryasamāgame  || 27.19 ||
avatīrṇānmunīnsarvān yathārhaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhakālo'dya prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  || 27.20 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
gavāṃ daśāyutānyekaprasūtānāṃ payomucām  |
savatsānāṃ suvarṇānāṃ ghaṇṭābharaṇaśobhinām  || 27.21 ||
yadi śaknoṣi dātuṃ tvaṃ homārthe pitṛdevayoḥ  |
tatra pravartatāṃ śrāddhaṃ satyametattavoditam  || 27.22 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā munīnāmūrdhvaretasām  |
kuśadhvajo'bravīdvākyaṃ brāhmaṇāṃstān yathārthataḥ  || 27.23 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye yathoktaṃ brahmacāribhiḥ  |
dadāmyahaṃ na sandeha ihaiva munipuṅgavāḥ  || 27.24 ||
bhojayitvā tataḥ śrāddhe brāhmaṇāṃstānnṛpottamaḥ  |
sakuśaṃ jalamādāya tebhyo dattā tu gāstadā  || 27.25 ||
dattvā dāna mudāyuktaḥ sa jagāma svakaṃ puram  |
sthitāstu brāhmaṇāstatra homakāryārthasiddhaye  || 27.26 ||
ekasmin vāsare prāptā rākṣasā ghorarūpiṇaḥ  |
bubhukṣitā mahādaṃṣṭrā vikṛtāsyā bhayānakāḥ  || 27.27 ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ tadā gā vai bhakṣituṃ samupāgatān  |
dṛṣṭvā tān vikṛtākārāṃstīvranādaparāyaṇān  || 27.28 ||
praṇaṣṭāstu tataḥ sthānānnarmadājalamāviśan  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyalokasthāḥ sarvāstāḥ kāmadhenavaḥ  || 27.29 ||
tataste kṣudhitāssarve brāhmaṇān bhakṣituṃ gatāḥ  |
hariṃ smaranti te sarve brāhmaṇāḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 27.30 ||
revājalaṃ praviṣṭā vai rākṣasaiḥ paripīḍitāḥ  |
viṣṇoḥ prasvedajastatra pravāho narmadāṃ gataḥ  || 27.31 ||
gopadaṃ dṛśyate tatra sarvāmaranamaskṛtam  |
karamardeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ viṣṇucakrādviniḥsṛtam  || 27.32 ||
pratiṣṭhitaṃ ca tatraiva viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā  |
gāvaśca brāhmaṇāścaiva satyametadbravīmi te  || 27.33 ||
brahmalokaṃ gatāḥ sarve tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
karamardeśvaraṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
kīrtitaṃ karmaṇā tena mahyāmamitatejasā  || 27.34 ||
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 27.35 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe karamardeśvarīkīrtanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 28

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
māndhātā rājaśārdūlastriṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  |
etadicchāmyahaṃ śrotuṃ caritaṃ tasya dhīmataḥ  || 28.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi  |
ikṣvākuvaṃśasambhūto yuvanāśvo mahīpatiḥ  || 28.2 ||
so'yajatpṛthivīpālaḥ kratubhirbhūridakṣiṇaiḥ  |
anapatyastu rājarṣiḥ sa mahātmā dṛḍhavrataḥ  || 28.3 ||
mantriṣvādhāya tadrājyaṃ vananiṣṭho mahīpatiḥ  |
śāstradṛṣṭena vidhinā saṃyamyātmānamātmanā  || 28.4 ||
phalamūlānubhakṣaśca sa cacāra mahattapaḥ  |
śuṣkakaṇṭhaḥ pipāsārtaḥ pānīyārthe bhṛśaḥ nṛpaḥ  || 28.5 ||
sampraviśyāśramasyāntaḥ pānīyaṃ so'bhyayācata  |
tasya śuṣkeṇa kaṇṭhena krośatastu tadā bhṛśam  || 28.6 ||
nā'śrauṣīdvacanaṃ tatra cātakasyaiva vāsavaḥ  |
bhagavāṃstu tadā kaścidṛṣistasya mahīpateḥ  || 28.7 ||
putrīyamagrataḥ kṛtvā mantraiścāpyabhimantritam  |
rātrau ca kalaśaṃ tatra jalapūrṇaṃ pipāsitaḥ  || 28.8 ||
abhyadravatsa vegena pītvāpastatra cāsvapat |
sa pītvā śītalaṃ toyaṃ pipāsārto mahīpatiḥ  || 28.9 ||
agnirnivartitastasya sukhī caivā'bhavattadā  |
tataste'cāpyabudhyanta munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 28.10 ||
kasyedaṃ karma kupitāḥ papracchustaṃ nṛpaṃ tadā  |
yuvanāśvo mametyevaṃ satyaṃ samabhipadyate  || 28.11 ||
yuvanāśvamidaṃ prāha bhagavān bhārgavastadā  |
sutārthaṃ sthāpitaṃ hyetattapasā caiva sambhṛtam  || 28.12 ||
mayā karma kṛtaṃ caitattapa āsthāya dāruṇam  |
putrārthaṃ tava rājendra yena te balavān bhavet || 28.13 ||
mahābalo mahāvīryastapobalasamanvitaḥ  |
sutaḥ śakrasamo'tyarthaṃ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 28.14 ||
vidhinā mantrayuktena mayaitadupapāditam  |
abhakṣaṇaṃ tvayā rājan yuktaṃ na kṛtamatra vai  || 28.15 ||
nūnaṃ daivakṛtaṃ tvadya yattattvaṃ kṛtavānasi  |
pipāsunā ca yatpītaṃ vidhimantrapuraskṛtam  || 28.16 ||
jalaṃ tvayā mahārāja tena tvaṃ vīryavānasi  |
anvahaṃ karma kṛtvā'pi mahāntaṃ sukhamāpsyasi  || 28.17 ||
vidhāsyāmo vayaṃ cātra putreṣṭiṃ paramāṃ tadā  |
vīryeṇa śakratulyaṃ tvaṃ putraṃ vai janayiṣyasi  || 28.18 ||
tato varṣaśate pūrṇe tasya rājño mahātmanaḥ  |
vāmapārśvaṃ vinirbhidya sutaḥ sūrya ivāparaḥ  || 28.19 ||
niścakrāma mahātejā na ca taṃ mṛtyurāviśat |
yuvanāśvasya nṛpatestadadbhutamivābhavat || 28.20 ||
taṃ draṣṭuṃ samupāgataḥ śakraṃ pṛcchanti taṃ devāḥ  |
sutaḥ kiṃ dhāsyatītyayam  .... .... || 28.21 ||
eṣa māṃ dhāsyatītyevaṃ śakraḥ provāca tān surān  |
pradeśinīṃ ca tasyāsye tataḥ śakraḥ samādadhau  || 28.22 ||
sa tāṃ bālastato hṛṣṭaḥ papau tasya pradeśinīm  |
māndhāteti ca nāmāsya śakraścakre yathārthavat || 28.23 ||
avāpya sa śiśustatra śakradattāṃ pradeśinīm  |
avardhatmahīpālaḥ kiṃtu ṣoḍaśikāḥ samāḥ  || 28.24 ||
āyurvedādiśāstrāṇi divyaśāstrāṇi sarvaśaḥ  |
upatasthurmahārājaṃ dhyānamātreṇa taṃ tadā  || 28.25 ||
dhanurājagavaṃ nāma śarāśśṛṅgodbhavāśca ye  |
abhedyaṃ kavacaṃ caiva sadyastamupatasthire  || 28.26 ||
so'bhiṣikto maghavatā devaiḥ sārddhaṃ ca bhārata  |
dharmeṇa cākramallokān sarvān viṣṇuriva kramaiḥ  || 28.27 ||
tasyā'pratihataṃ cakraṃ pracacāra mahātmanaḥ  |
śatāni caiva rājānaḥ svayamevopatasthire  || 28.28 ||
tasyaivamabhavatpūrvaṃ vasudhā vasudhāpate  |
teneṣṭaṃ vividhairyajñairbahubhiścāptadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 28.29 ||
hṛṣṭamanā mahātejāḥ svadharmaṃ prāpya puṣkalam  |
śakrasyārdhāsanaṃ dhīmān labdhavānamitadyutiḥ  || 28.30 ||
āpālitā ca pṛthivī tena dharmeṇa dhīmatā  |
nirjitā śāsanādeva saratnākarapattanā  || 28.31 ||
tatkṛtānāṃ mahārāja kratūnāṃ dakṣiṇāvatām  |
caturantā mahī vyāptā nāsītkiṃcidanāvṛtam  || 28.32 ||
daśalakṣasahasrāṇi rājyaṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ  |
tena dvādaśavārṣikyāmanāvṛṣṭyāṃ mahātmanā  || 28.33 ||
vṛṣṭiṃ ca sasyavṛddhyarthaṃ miṣatā vajrapāṇinā  |
tena somakulotpanno gandharvādhipatirmahān  || 28.34 ||
gatvā samānayanmeghaṃ pramathyābhihitaḥ śaraiḥ  |
prajāścaturvidhāstena dhṛtāstatra mahātmanā  || 28.35 ||
tenāptāstapasā lokāḥ sthāpitāḥ svena tejasā  |
tasyaiva devavasatisthānamādityatejasaḥ  || 28.36 ||
yasya puṇyatame deśe dṛśyate'marakaṇṭakaḥ  |
iṣṭvā tatra kratuśatamoṅkārasyaiva cāgrataḥ  || 28.37 ||
rājñā ca parvate tasmin stotrametadudāhṛtam  |
namaste kālameghāya kālātmaka namo'stu te  || 28.38 ||
kālādhipaṃ namaste'stu kālarūpaḥ pravartase  |
kālātmā kālarūpeṇa viśvātmā viśvarūpadhṛk || 28.39 ||
viśveśvara namaste'stu kālatyāge pravartakaḥ  |
bhavāya bhavanāśāya bhavodbhava namo'stu te  || 28.40 ||
oṃ namo mahādevāya śambhavāya bhavāya ca  |
ajapāya ajātāya ajāyatanamīḍhuṣe  || 28.41 ||
prabhavāya śivatarāya akṣamāya namo namaḥ  |
tryambakāya trimūrtāya trilokeśāya te namaḥ  || 28.42 ||
akālāya ajarāya amarāya namo namaḥ  |
oṃkāramādidevaṃ ca ye vai dhyāyanti nityaśaḥ  || 28.43 ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  |
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devaḥ oṃkāraḥ kālarūpadhṛk || 28.44 ||
pratyuvāca mahīpālaṃ devadeva umāpatiḥ  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te stotreṇānena suvrata  || 28.45 ||
tuṣṭo'smīti na sandeho yatheṣṭaṃ taddadāmyaham  |

māndhātovāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  || 28.46 ||
vaidūryo nāma śailendro māndhātākhyānamarhati  |
devasthānamidaṃ deva tvatprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 28.47 ||
atra dānaṃ tapaḥ pūjā tathā prāṇavisarjanam  |
ye kurvanti narāsteṣāṃ śivaloke nivāsitā  || 28.48 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā māndhātuḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devo māndhātāraṃ mahīpatim  || 28.49 ||
sarvametatnṛpaśreṣṭha matprasādādbhaviṣyati  |
evamastviti taṃ coktvā varaṃ labdhvā mahīpati  || 28.50 ||
jagāma svāṃ purīṃ śīghraṃ yathā śakro'marāvatīm  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ māndhātuścaritaṃ mahat || 28.51 ||
yo mamāgre mahīpāla dṛṣṭo'drirvai tvayānagha  |
tadā prabhṛti māndhātā vaidūryo gīyate giriḥ  || 28.52 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyātmāndhātṛpramukhā nṛpāḥ  |
sarvakāmasamudyuktā loke krīḍanti vaiṣṇave  || 28.53 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādvā'pi hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 28.54 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe māndhāturupākhyāne'ṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāya


adhyāya 29

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kṛte yuge'tha samprāpte balirnāma mahāsuraḥ  |
tasya putro mahāvīryaḥ sahasrabhujaviśrutaḥ  || 29.1 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tena cārādhito haraḥ  |
tuṣṭena tena samproktaḥ prārthayasva varaṃ vara  || 29.2 ||
yatkiṃcidvai varaṃ proktaṃ taddāsyāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
bāṇāsuro vadatyevaṃ yadi tuṣṭo'si me prabho  || 29.3 ||
puraṃ bhavatu me divyamajeyaṃ sarvadaivataiḥ  |
tvāmeva varjayitvā tu duṣprāpyaṃ sarvadaivataiḥ  || 29.4 ||
mayi tiṣṭhati yattiṣṭhetmayi gacchati gacchatu  |
kāmikaṃ bhavanaṃ deva pura bhavatu me tadā  || 29.5 ||
ukto bāṇāsurastena baliputro mahāyaśāḥ  |
viṣṇunābhihitaścāsau kiṃ tvayā prārthito haraḥ  || 29.6 ||

bāṇa uvāca - 
mahyaṃ dattaṃ maheśena puraṃ puravarottamam  |
ajeyaṃ sarvadevānāmasurāṇāṃ ca durlabham  || 29.7 ||
yadi dattaṃ maheśena puraṃ tubhyaṃ yathepsitam  |
mayā'pi te pradattaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ tādṛśaṃ puram  || 29.8 ||
viṣṇunā'pi puraṃ dattaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca manoramam  |
ekībhūtau tu tau devāyūcatustau baleḥ sutam  || 29.9 ||
gaccha bāṇāsura kṣipraṃ yatrāste kamalāsanaḥ  |
gatastatra baleḥ putro yatrātiṣṭhatpitāmahaḥ  || 29.10 ||
pariṣvajya svahastena pṛṣṭaścaiva svayambhuvā  |
bahuvarṣasahasraṃ tu tapo ghoraṃ tvayā kṛtam  || 29.11 ||
varastu kastvayā prāptastapasārādhya śaṅkaram  |
brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā pratyuvāca mahāsuraḥ  || 29.12 ||
mayā tu prārthito rudro dattastena prasādataḥ  |
kāmarūpaṃ puraṃ prāptaṃ manoramyaṃ manoramam  || 29.13 ||
tādṛśaṃ tu puraṃ dattaṃ dvitīyaṃ viṣṇunā punaḥ  |
bāṇāsuravacaḥ śrutvā pratyuvāca mahāsuram  || 29.14 ||
tvayā tu prārthito rudro dattaṃ tena mahātmanā  |
mayā'pi te puraṃ dattaṃ tenāsau tripuraḥ smṛtaḥ  |
evaṃ prāptavaro rājanmahābalaparākramaḥ  || 29.15 ||
sahasrabhujavistīrṇastvavadhyaḥ sarvadaivataiḥ  |
purāṇi dānavānāṃ tu amarāṇāṃ tu yāni ca  || 29.16 ||
yakṣavidyādharāṇāṃ tu gandharvāṇāṃ ca rakṣasām  |
bhagnāni tāni sarvāṇi sthaṇḍilāni hṛtāni ca  || 29.17 ||
bhagnāmarāvatī tena purā śakrasya bhārata  |
tripuraṃ hyabhavatsarvaṃ kailāsaḥ kevalaṃ pṛthak || 29.18 ||
udvignamānasā devā harapārśvamupāyayuḥ  |
varo dattastvayā tasmai brahmaṇā viṣṇunāpi ca  || 29.19 ||
tena sārdhaṃ tu saṃgrāme śaktirnāstīti kasyacit |
yastasya puratastiṣṭhettamasau bhasmatāṃ nayet || 29.20 ||
evaṃ śrutvā śivo vākyaṃ paraṃ kautūhalaṃ tataḥ  |
saṃpreṣitāstadā devā brahmaviṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 29.21 ||
sametya gamyatāṃ devāstriṃśatkoṭyo mahābalāḥ  |
vināśayata yatnena tripuraṃ purasaṃsthitam  || 29.22 ||
tato gatāḥ surāḥ sarve baddhavairāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
vāṇāsurapuraṃ yatra sutīkṣṇaniśitāyudhāḥ  || 29.23 ||
suyuddhabalasampannāḥ sarve te balaśālinaḥ  |
mārgaṃ puraṃ ca deśaṃ ca chādayanto ghanā iva  || 29.24 ||
saṃkṣiptenaiva kālena manasā cintitena ca  |
sarve te tripuraṃ prāptā dhanuḥ kṣiptāḥ śarā iva  || 29.25 ||
draṣṭuṃ daśa diśastena preṣitā ye ca kiṅkarāḥ  |
ūcurbāṇāsuraṃ te tu niścintastvaṃ kathaṃ prabho  || 29.26 ||
uktaṃ bāṇāsureṇā'pi varo'dya saphalo mama  |
samīhitaphalaṃ prāptaṃ kuto gacchanti te surāḥ  || 29.27 ||
tena te kṣaṇamātreṇa sarve devā jitāstadā  |
hṛtāni ca tato'strāṇi pātra bhojanaṃ yathā  || 29.28 ||
indrasyāpi hṛtaṃ vajraṃ cakraṃ vai keśavasya tu  |
jalaṃ pitāmahasyāpi pāśaṃ ca varuṇasya ca  || 29.29 ||
kubaresya gadāṃ caiva marutaścāṅkuśaṃ tathā  |
yamasyāpahṛto daṇḍaḥ śaktirvaiśvānarasya ca  || 29.30 ||
kāmarūpaṃ puraṃ tasya haradattaṃ prasādataḥ  |
na śakyate suraiḥ sarvairbrahmaviṣṇupurogamaiḥ  || 29.31 ||
bādhituṃ daityarājasya samantānmilitairapi  |
surā bāṇāsureṇaiva tato yuddhe parājitāḥ  || 29.32 ||
bhagnāstūtsāharahitā harapārśvamupāgatāḥ  |
śivenoktāstu te sarve tatra gatvā tu kiṃ kṛtam  || 29.33 ||
saṃgrāmaḥ kīdṛśastena bhavadbhiḥ saha nirmitaḥ  |
tataḥ kiṃ kathyate deva na śaktāstasya karmaṇi  || 29.34 ||
na tena saha saṃgrāme sammukhaṃ kenacitkṛtam  |
devatāvacanaṃ śrutvā kruddhaḥ provāca śaṅkaraḥ  || 29.35 ||
tripuraṃ ca mahāduṣṭāmimaṃ vyāpādayāmyaham  |
athavā cāpamākṛṣya hyasuraṃ pradahāmyaham  || 29.36 ||
yena jīvannaro yastu surāṇāṃ kiṅkaro bhavet |
pativratā prasādenā tripuraṃ ca surāsuraiḥ  || 29.37 ||
na śakyaṃ dharṣituṃ tasmānnāradaṃ preṣayāmyaham  |
nāradaḥ preṣitastatra kṣobhaya tvaṃ pativratāḥ  || 29.38 ||
evamuktastu devarṣirbāṇāsurapuraṃ yayau  |
tvaritaṃ puramadhye tu yatra bāṇāsuro nṛpaḥ  || 29.39 ||
taṃ tu deva ṛṣiṃ dṛṣṭvā hyasuro vākyamabravīt |
namaskṛtya ca sāṣṭāṅgamarghapādyaiḥ prapūjya ca  || 29.40 ||
kuto'trāgamanaṃ te'dya kiṃ vā kāryaṃ mahāmune  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā muniḥ provāca taṃ tadā  || 29.41 ||
kuśalaṃ te baleḥ putrasādaraṃ tu punaḥ punaḥ  |
bāṇāsuro'bravīdvākyaṃ kuśalaṃ tava darśanāt || 29.42 ||
devarṣirupaviṣṭastu divyāsanasuśobhitaḥ  |
rājñī cābhyarcayattatra sa tasyai nāradastadā  || 29.43 ||
purāṇavedabāhyāni vṛttānyādeśayanmuniḥ  |
nārīṇāṃ calitaṃ cittaṃ kṛtvā devamunistadā  || 29.44 ||
āgato nāradaḥ śrīmān kailāsaṃ parvatottamam  |
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ vṛttāntaṃ saṃnyavedayat || 29.45 ||
ghātaya tripuraṃ deva sapuraṃ surakaṇṭakam  |
nirgatastu harastasmātkailāsanilayātprabhuḥ  || 29.46 ||
svakīyenaiva mārgeṇa yatra'sau tripura'suraḥ  |
devī caṇḍeśvaro nandī mahākālo maheśvaraḥ  || 29.47 ||
vṛṣo bhṛṅgiriṭiścaiva vighneśaḥ skanda eva ca  |
puṣpadanto mahāvīro ghaṇṭākarṇo mahodaraḥ  || 29.48 ||
gomukho hastikarṇaśca sthūlajaṅgho vṛkodaraḥ  |
gaṇāḥ pañcadaśa tvete haratulyaparākramāḥ  || 29.49 ||
asti siddho mahākṣetraṃ śrīśailo nāmaparvataḥ  |
tatra sthitvā mahādevo hantavyastripuraḥ priye  || 29.50 ||
sthānaṃ māheśvaraṃ cakre vyāpī tatra pinākadhṛk |
ekapādena brahmāṇḍaṃ pātālaṃ cāpareṇa ca  || 29.51 ||
himavantaṃ dhanuḥ kṛtvā guṇaṃ kṛtvā tu vāsukim  |
śaraṃ vaiśvānaraṃ kṛtvā tasyā'graṃ kālameva tu  || 29.52 ||
rathaṃ bhūmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā vedān kṛtvā hayāṃstathā  |
raśmīṃstakṣakakarkoṭau brahmāṇaṃ sārathiṃ svayam  || 29.53 ||
cakrarakṣaṃ vāsudevamaghoraṃ mantrasaptakam  |
tathā pāśupataṃ caiva mantrarājaṃ tathaiva ca  || 29.54 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu sthānaṃ kṛtvā sthito'bhavat |
tiṣṭhato mama tatraiva kālena mahatā priye  || 29.55 ||
lakṣyālakṣye tanū kṛtvā gaṇeśo vighnamācarat |
vāmapādanakhāgreṇa calitaḥ sthānakādaham  || 29.56 ||
tato mayā hato nā'sau rakṣituṃ tripuraṃ katham  |
pravṛtto'si gaṇādhīśa jagadvidhvaṃsakārakam  || 29.57 ||
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā vighneśo vākyamabravīt |
ahaṃ na vighnaye tvāṃ cetkathamanye'rcayanti mām  || 29.58 ||
apūjite mayi vibho yaḥ kāryaṃ kartumicchati  |
tasmai vighnaṃ pradāsyāmi surāsuragaṇeṣvapi  || 29.59 ||
evamastviti taṃ prāha śaṅkaraḥ tadanantaram  |
vadhyaṃ caiva gato lakṣyaṃ saṃśaktastripuraṃ prati  || 29.60 ||
tāpaso'yaṃ durācāro daityaḥ parapuraṃ jayaḥ  |
vināśāyā'sya duṣṭasya kaṃcāhaṃ preṣaye śaram  || 29.61 ||
tripurasya vadhārthāya kṣipraṃ pāśupataṃ mahat |
astramanyadvidhāsyāmītyuktvā devo haraḥ puram  || 29.62 ||
aghorāstreṇa taddagdhaṃ trikhaṇḍaṃ jarjarīkṛtam  |
pātitaṃ tu jale tatra tato māṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 29.63 ||
bhavāni dānavaiḥ sārddhaṃ teṣāṃ patnyaḥ sutekṣaṇāḥ  |
āpatanti rudantyastāḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 29.64 ||
sarvāsāṃ nirdayo vahṇiḥ sa dadāha patīṃstathā  |
śrīśaile patitaṃ caikamanyaccāmarakaṇṭake  || 29.65 ||
gaṅgāsāgarasambhede tṛtīyaṃ ca tathā priye  |
putrapautrakalatrāṇi maṇihemapurāṇi ca  || 29.66 ||
vināśaṃ yānti tān yatra liṅgamekaṃ na naśyati  |
liṅgānāṃ navakoṭīnāṃ yadyekamapi dahyate  || 29.67 ||
prāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyāmi hutāśe'smiṃstadā dhruvam  |
dagdhaṃ tu tripuraṃ kṛtsnamaghorāstreṇa dāruṇam  || 29.68 ||
pātitaṃ narmadā madhye jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
tadbhittvā saptapātālaṃ rasātalatalaṃ yayau  || 29.69 ||
tena jāleśvaraṃ tīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
arcanāttasya devasya mucyate brahmahatyayā  || 29.70 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi vasecchivapure sukhī  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 29.71 ||
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti śivo yāvacca kalpagā  || 29.72 ||
sārddhakoṭiśca liṅgānāṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  |
sārddhakoṭiśca patitā liṅgānāṃ puravardhane  || 29.73 ||
daśārdhakoṭiḥ patitā śrīśaile tripurāntike  |
tisraḥ koṭyārdhakoṭī ca patitāmarakaṇṭake  || 29.74 ||
etāni bāṇaliṅgāni bhuktimuktipradāni tu  |
tripuraghnamaghorāstraṃ jvalatkālāgnirudravat || 29.75 ||
kalpagāṃ varjayitvā tu kānyā dhārayituṃ kṣamā  |
etādṛśaṃ tu patitaṃ tadastraṃ kalpagājale  || 29.76 ||
jāleśvaraṃ tu kathitaṃ tripuraghnamidaṃ tava  |
etattīrthaṃ na jānanti lokāśca sacarācarāḥ  || 29.77 ||
dahantaṃ tripuraṃ dṛṣṭvā devā vismayamāgatāḥ  |
brahmādyā devatā yena saṃgrāmeṣu parājitāḥ  || 29.78 ||
śareṇaikena tadvīryaṃ kṛtaṃ bhasmaikapuñjavat |
bāṇāsuraḥ pure dagdhe bhītaḥ stotramidaṃ jagau  || 29.79 ||
oṃ namo'nādideveśa vighneśvara maheśvara  |
sarvajñājñānahṛjjñānapradānaika namo'stu te  || 29.80 ||
anantaguṇaratnāya pareśāya namo'stu te  |
parātpara parātīta utpattisthānakāraka  || 29.81 ||
sarvārthasādhanopāya viśveśvara namo'stu te  |
nirañjana nirādhāra svabhāva nirupadrava  || 29.82 ||
prasannaparameśāna yogeśvara namo'stu te  |
asuraghna piśācaghna bhūtavetālanāśana  || 29.83 ||
bhūtanātha jagannātha sarvādhāra namo'stu te  |
sṛṣṭisaṃhāra nirvāṇasaptapātālasaṃśraya  || 29.84 ||
śrīkaṇṭha nīlakaṇṭheśa mahākaṇṭha namo'stu te  |
tryambakāya triśūlāya trilokāya ca te namaḥ  || 29.85 ||
kapāline kapālaiśca baddhāṅga śaśiśekhara  |
umākāntārdhadehāya surāsuranamaskṛta  || 29.86 ||
yatra tvaṃ rūpasaṃsthaṃ calamacalatanuṃ vyāpakaṃ lakṣyahīnaṃ tejo'bhyantarmarālaṃ ghanamaghanamajaṃ sphāṭikaṃ sphāṭikābham  |
raktaṃ nīlaṃ ca pītaṃ sitamasitamanekālparūpaṃ prayuktaṃ madhyāntādivyapetaṃ sphuṭatanurahitaṃ liṅgarūpaṃ namāmi  || 29.87 ||
madhyāhne lakṣyayogena hṛdayakamale dhāraṇīśe na haṃse nākāśe vāyutattve'naladharaṇijale vidyate naiva śakyam  |
no nāde naiva vindau na karaṇanilaye nādimadhyāvasāne sthāneṣveṣu prabuddho na ca niyamayituṃ yaṃ sadādyaṃ namāmi  || 29.88 ||
jihvācāpalyabhāvena varṇitaṃ me mahāprabho  |
kṣantavyaṃ tatsureśāna kastvāṃ varṇayituṃ kṣamaḥ  || 29.89 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrutvā stutiṃ ca bāṇasya tuṣṭo'sau bhagavān haraḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ śambhurasuraṃ prati bhārata  || 29.90 ||
sevāparādhajo hyeṣa kṣāntaste daityanāyaka  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  || 29.91 ||
śivasya vacanaṃ śrutvā bāṇo daityapatistadā  |
praṇamya cābravīdvākyaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 29.92 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  |
anenaiva śarīreṇa sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  || 29.93 ||
tava lokaṃ gamiṣyāmi yatra janma na vidyate  |
atrotpattivipattibhyāṃ nirviṇṇo'surayoniṣu  || 29.94 ||
tribhirdevaiḥ puraṃ dattaṃ bhinnaṃ tatpuraṃ tvayā  |
kathaṃ tatpatitaṃ bhūmau gahanā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  || 29.95 ||
dātābaliḥ prārthayitā ca viṣṇurdānaṃ mahī vājimakhasya kālaḥ  |
āsītphalaṃ bandhanameva tasya namo'stu tasyai bhavitavyatāyai  || 29.96 ||
svargo durgaḥ surāḥ sainyaṃ gajāścairāvatādayaḥ  |
śastraṃ vajramavāpyāste yatra devo bṛhaspatiḥ  || 29.97 ||
nirjito meghanādena daśānanasutena ca  |
sarvamātmavaśaṃ nītaṃ daivaṃ hi balavattaram  || 29.98 ||
bāṇāsuravacaḥ śrutvā devadevo varapradaḥ  |
mama bhaktiprasādena madantikamavāpsyasi  || 29.99 ||
tato bāṇāsuraḥ śrīmān devadevaprasādataḥ  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ  || 29.100 ||
prāyācchivapuraṃ yatra devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
narmadā sā'bravīdvākyaṃ śambhuṃ prati viśāmpate  || 29.101 ||
bindumātraṃ na me dagdhamaghorāstreṇa śaṅkara  |
dadāha tripuraṃ kṛtsnaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  || 29.102 ||
śāntaṃ ca mama toyena rasātalatalaṃ yayau  |

īśvara uvāca - 
puraṃ tu nikhilaṃ dagdhamaghorāstraṃ sudussaham  || 29.103 ||
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyaṃ madhyadeśe tavāmbhasaḥ  |
agamatsaumyarūpatvaṃ prabhāvāttava narmade  || 29.104 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailā gaṅgādyāśca sahasraśaḥ  |
gopyaṃ tatkṣaṇamātreṇa bhasmapuñjo yathā jalam  || 29.105 ||
soḍhuṃ kā'nyā saricchreṣṭhā tvāṃ vinā bhuvi kalpage  |
evamuktvā yayau devaḥ surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ  || 29.106 ||
etatte kathitaṃ bhūpa ākhyānaṃ śrutisammitam  |
jāleśvarasya tīrthasya samāsena yudhiṣṭhira  || 29.107 ||
sevyate tena kāryeṇa narmadā saptakalpagā  |
śravaṇātkīrtanāttasya rudrasyānucaro bhavet || 29.108 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'mareśvaramāhātye ekonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 30

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ṛṣīṇāmayutaṃ rājannaimiṣāraṇyavāsinām  |
gargaśca śaunako dakṣaḥ sarveṣāṃ dharmataḥ pitā  || 30.1 ||
teṣāṃ nivasatāṃ tatra āścaryamabhavatnṛpa  |
hiraṇyā nāma nagarī dṛṣṭā vā yadi vā śrutā  || 30.2 ||
hiraṇyavegā nāmnī tu nadīpuṣpaphaladrumā  |
dhanadasya purī ramyā dhanāḍhyā ca yathā sthitā  || 30.3 ||
sāptabhaumairgṛhairramyairhemaprākāratoraṇaiḥ  |
veṇuvīṇāninādaughairgandharvairnāditāyutaiḥ  || 30.4 ||
nāgarāḥ pūjitāḥ sarve'trādhiṣṭhitadhanānvitāḥ  |
agnihotraiśca vidvadbhirbrāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 30.5 ||
evaṃ vidhā purī ramyā sarvotsavasamanvitā  |
akāle mriyate yastu sa rājñā gṛhyate punaḥ  || 30.6 ||
mudā paramayā yukto vitteśo dhanado yathā  |
prajāpatiṃ saṃpapraccha brahmaṇo mānasaṃ sutam  || 30.7 ||
praṇipatya namaskṛtya dakṣaṃ taṃ munipuṅgavam  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi munīnāṃ brahmavarcasām  || 30.8 ||
sautrāmaṇiḥ somasaṃsthā kasmin deśe ca siddhyati  |
ācakṣva muniśārdūla yataḥ tvaṃ sarvadharmavit || 30.9 ||
hiraṇyabāhuṃ provāca dakṣo vaivasvataṃ tadā  |
tīrthaṃ hi puṣkaraṃ nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 30.10 ||
tasminniṣṭaṃ hutaṃ rājan sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet |
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena nā'tra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 30.11 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rājā provāca taṃ munim  |
niṣpādyatāṃ ca yajño me bhagavan puṣkare śubhe  || 30.12 ||
mānasaṃ brahmaṇaḥ putraṃ sarvavedaviśāradam  |
tvāmṛte dīkṣituṃ kasya śaktirasminmahītale  || 30.13 ||
evamastviti taṃ prāha dakṣo rājarṣisattamam  |
tato jagāma rājarṣiḥ puṣkaraṃ sarvasambhṛtaḥ  || 30.14 ||
gavāṃ ca daśalakṣāṇi sārddhaṃ lakṣaṃ tu vājinām  |
dvipañcāśatsahasrāṇi gajendrāṇāṃ rathāyutam  || 30.15 ||
maṇimāṇikyaratnāni vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca  |
teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyeta kuberasya dhanaṃ yathā  || 30.16 ||
tato bhakṣāṇi bhojyāni pānāni vividhāni ca  |
evaṃ pravartito yajñaḥ pavitre śreṣṭhapuṣkare  || 30.17 ||
āhūtaśca tato brahmā śakraścāpi sureśvaraḥ  |
anyadevaiśca kiṃ kāryaṃ tābhyāṃ sarvaṃ prapūjitam  || 30.18 ||
na rudro yajñabhāgārho vāsudevaḥ tvayājakaḥ  |
na cādityo na varuṇo na devā na ca candramāḥ  || 30.19 ||
vedamūlo yato yajño rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  |
vedanirghoṣaśabdena yajñadhūmena bhārata  || 30.20 ||
rodasyanantaraṃ rājan sarvameva prapūritam  |
etasminnantare yajñacchidrānveṣaṇatatparāḥ  || 30.21 ||
samprāptā rājaśārdūla balena balavattarāḥ  |
parājayaṃ ca devānāmasurāṇāṃ jayaṃ tathā  || 30.22 ||
kartuṃ prasthāpitāḥ sarve hyasurā devakaṇṭakāḥ  |
tebhyo niketunā proktaṃ daityānāmīśvareṇa hi  || 30.23 ||
na rudro'sti na viṣṇurvā brahmāste sa tu pūjakaḥ  |
na kartavyaṃ bhayaṃ teṣāṃ brāhmaṇā jñānadurbalāḥ  || 30.24 ||
gacchantu dānavā daityā bhūtavetālarākṣasāḥ  |
pibantu somaṃ yajñāṅgaṃ bhakṣayantu tathā dvijān  || 30.25 ||
vidhvaṃsitastato yajño brāhmaṇāścaiva bhakṣitāḥ  |
agnirvināśito yajñayūpaśca yajñamaṇḍapaḥ  || 30.26 ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ dharṣitāḥ patnyo nagnarūpaistathā balāt |
kumārā ṛṣayaścaiva bhayārtāḥ prāṇapīḍitāḥ  || 30.27 ||
praṇaṣṭaśca tato brahmā śakro devagaṇaiḥ saha  |
evaṃ yajñe ca vidhvaste cakravartī nṛpottamaḥ  || 30.28 ||
hiraṇyabāhuḥ kupito brāhmaṇān prati bhārata  |
pāpiṣṭhāśca durācārā gatāste bhikṣukā dvijāḥ  || 30.29 ||
svasthānaṃ ca gatā daityā gṛhītvā yajñasambhṛtim  |
ekākī hayamāruhya saha patnyā vrajāmyaham  || 30.30 ||
na pauruṣasya kālo'yaṃ kopasya ca kathaṃcana  |
śambhurna devatā yatra śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  || 30.31 ||
kathaṃ siddhyati yajñau'sau na sūryo naiva candramāḥ  |
lolupā brāhmaṇāḥ pāpāḥ tvaśaktā yajñarakṣaṇe  || 30.32 ||
yadi me vidyate satyaṃ bhavantu brahmarākṣasāḥ  |
sakaṇṭake nirudake pradeśe naṣṭacetanāḥ  || 30.33 ||
dakṣādyairbrāhmaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ so'bhiśapto mahīpatiḥ  |
arakṣitā tvaṃ yajñasya kṣatriyāṇāṃ tathā'dhamaḥ  || 30.34 ||
kharo dvādaśavarṣāṇi bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ  |
śāpādbabhūvuranyo'nyaṃ te kharabrahmarākṣasāḥ  || 30.35 ||
etatte kathitaṃ vṛttaṃ na haro na hariḥ prabhuḥ  |
na yajño na ca taddānaṃ na tapo'dhyayanaṃ na ca  || 30.36 ||
vedoktaṃ karma na brāhmyaṃ na dharmaṃ na triviṣṭapam  |
na vrataṃ vaṣaṭkāraḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ  || 30.37 ||
etasminnantare rājan devarṣirnāradastadā  |
ājagāma kratuṃ draṣṭuṃ puṣkaraṃ prati bhārata  || 30.38 ||
tato viplāvite yajñe daityairduṣkṛtikāribhiḥ  |
hiraṇyabāhuśca paraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brahmapāragam  || 30.39 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā devarṣirnāradaṃ tadā  |
nāśito brāhmaṇairyajñaḥ kṣudrairme munipuṅgava  || 30.40 ||
yajñadharmavidhau śakto ghātayāmyadhvarāntakam  |
śakto'smi trijagajjetuṃ kiṃ punardaitya dānavān  || 30.41 ||
mayā cāpi kṛto yajño hariśaṅkaravarjanāt |
brahmaśāpavaśādbhīto gārdabhaṃ yonimāśritaḥ  || 30.42 ||
kenopāyena devarṣe trikālajña trivedavit |
svargalokaṃ gamiṣyāmi ṛtvigbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha  || 30.43 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye yanmāṃ prāpto'si nārada  |

nārada uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  || 30.44 ||
revācarukasambhedaṃ pañcaliṅgāni bhūmipa  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 30.45 ||
tatra gaccha naraśreṣṭha brāhmaṇairbrahmarākṣasaiḥ  |
na yajño na tapodānaṃ śivadhyānaparo bhava  || 30.46 ||
sadyaḥ pramucyate pāpādbrāhmaṇaiḥ śāpadūṣitaiḥ  |
yatrāsurastu nihato bhairavaṃ rūpamāśritaḥ  || 30.47 ||
pāpapraṇāśanaṃ liṅgaṃ ṛṇamocanameva ca  |
catuṣkeśvaramaparaṃ tathā siddheśvaraṃ param  || 30.48 ||
pañcamaṃ vāruṇaṃ liṅgaṃ siddhaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhitam  |
evaṃ tu nāradaḥ prāha bhagavantaṃ nṛpottamam  || 30.49 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
bhagavan kīdṛśaṃ rūpaṃ yadā nṛtyati bhairavaḥ  |
etadācakṣva me sarvaṃ prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ prabho  || 30.50 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
gauryāpṛṣṭaḥ purā rājan kautukena sureśvaraḥ  |
nṛtyarūpaṃ samākhyāhi kimanyaiḥ kathitairmama  || 30.51 ||
śāntarūpaṃ tatastyaktvā kṛtaṃ rūpaṃ sudāruṇam  |
sthitaścaikena pādena prapīḍya vasudhātalam  || 30.52 ||
dvitīyena ca pādena brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaram  |
khyātaṃ dāruvanaṃ nāma pañcaliṅgasamanvitam  || 30.53 ||
nihatya cāsuraṃ tatra punarnṛtyaṃ mayā priye  |
tasmin dāruvane caṇḍi rudraṃ bhuvanadāruṇam  || 30.54 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  |
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  || 30.55 ||
gaccha gaccha nṛpa sthānaṃ narmadācārusaṅgamam  |
tatra te snātamātrasya śāpasyānto bhaviṣyati  || 30.56 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājan devarṣirnāradastadā  |
hiraṇyabāhurnṛpatiḥ sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  || 30.57 ||
dakṣaśaunakagargādyaiḥ śāpabhraṣṭaiḥ samanvitaḥ  |
ājagāma tataḥ śīghraṃ narmadācarusaṅgamam  || 30.58 ||
tatra snātvā sa rājarṣirdattvā caiva tilodakam  |
pañcaliṅgāni cābhyarcya tasmin dāruvane tadā  || 30.59 ||
siddheśvaraṃ caruliṅgaṃ ṛṇamocanameva ca  |
pāpapraṇāśanaṃ cānyaccaṇḍikeśvaramuttamam  || 30.60 ||
pūjayitvā yathā nyāyaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam  |
stotraistuṣṭāva vividhaiḥ śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ  || 30.61 ||
tvayā vinā tapodānaṃ na yajñaṃ na ca yājanam  |
na svargaṃ na ca mokṣaṃ ca kāmayeya maheśvara  || 30.62 ||
na haro na hariryatra sarva tanniṣkalaṃ bhavet |
tatastuṣṭaḥ sureśāno varaṃ vṛṇvityuvāca tam  || 30.63 ||
hiraṇyabāhū rājarṣiḥ prasādya śivamabravīt |
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  || 30.64 ||
tadāsyāḥ kharayonermāṃ mahādevavimocaya  |
tyajanti cātra ye prāṇān pāpā api narādhamāḥ  || 30.65 ||
te'pi yānti tava sthānaṃ satyametadvaco mama  |
nirvighnā yajñasiddhiśca śreyo dānaṃ tapastathā  || 30.66 ||
evamastviti taṃ proktvā śivastvantaradhīyata  |
śāpānmuktaḥ sa dharmātmā divyakāntivapurdharaḥ  || 30.67 ||
kāmikaṃ yānamāruhya sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  |
dhriyamāṇātapatraśca stūyamānaśca māgadhaiḥ  || 30.68 ||
mudā paramayā yukto hiraṇyapuramāviśat |
anyaṃ yājakamanveṣṭuṃ yajñārthaṃ brahmavādinam  || 30.69 ||
etasminnantare prāpte nāradaḥ puramabhyagāt || 30.70 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe revācarusaṅgamavarṇano nāma triṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 31

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
hiraṇyabāhuḥ śāpānte nagarīṃ prāpya nirvṛtaḥ  |
dakṣādayaḥ kathaṃ muktāstasmācchāpācca kathyatām  || 31.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābāho kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
hiraṇyabāhunā śaptā yāvanmuktā dvijottamāḥ  || 31.2 ||
babhramuḥ sarvatīrthāni āsamudrānta gocare  |
teṣāṃ brahmādayaḥ śāpaṃ na nivartayituṃ kṣamāḥ  || 31.3 ||
vārāṇasīṃ mahāpuṇyāṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamam  |
himavantaṃ ca kedāramaurvatīrthaṃ ca bhārata  || 31.4 ||
gaṅgā ca naimiṣāraṇyaṃ bhairavaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  |
māyāpurīṃ tathā ramyāmugraṃ kanakhalaṃ tathā  || 31.5 ||
raudraṃ caiva tatheśānaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
gaṅgādvāraṃ himasthānaṃ prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 31.6 ||
rudrakoṭisamāyogaṃ gaṅgābhedaṃ sarasvatīm  |
sthāneśvaraṃ tathā puṇyaṃ kurukṣetraṃ tathaiva ca  || 31.7 ||
kurukṣetraṃ gamiṣyāmi kurukṣetre vasāmyaham  |
kurukṣetrasya nāmnāpi naraḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 31.8 ||
bhrameṇaivaṃ viṣaṇṇāste śāpasyānta na lebhire  |
ninditāni ca tīrthāni pāpakarmarataistataḥ  || 31.9 ||
ākāśavacanaṃ śrutvā mahātīrthāni nindata  |
haraṃ hariṃ ca yo dveṣṭi nābhinandati yaḥ surān  || 31.10 ||
sa yāti yatra yatraiva duḥkhaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ  |
etasminnantare prāpto nārado devapūjitaḥ  || 31.11 ||
dakṣaśaunakagargādīn sarvāṃstānmunisattamān  |
brahmarakṣastanūn dṛṣṭvā nārado vākyamabravīt || 31.12 ||
bhavantaḥ karmaṇā kena sañjātā brahmarākṣasāḥ  |
tasyātha vacanaṃ śrutvā nāradasya mahāmuneḥ  || 31.13 ||
so'bhivādya namaskṛtya dakṣo vacanamabravīt |
yathotsavaṃ kṛtaṃ karma brahmalokahitāya yat || 31.14 ||
tadviparyāsamāpannaṃ gahanā karmaṇo gatiḥ  |
karmopadiśa me kiṃcidyena muñcāpi duṣkṛtam  || 31.15 ||
yatra yatra ca gacchāmo jalamannaṃ na vidyate  |
anāvṛṣṭirabhūdghorā tatra tatraiva nārada  || 31.16 ||

nārada uvāca - 
prasādyatāṃ muniśreṣṭhāḥ śāpasyāntaṃ kariṣyati  |
hiraṇyabāhurdharmātmā hiraṇyapuramāsthitaḥ  || 31.17 ||
yajñaṃ kartuṃ samāyāto revācarukasaṅgame  |
pañcaliṅgāni cābhyarcya śāpāntaṃ ca kariṣyati  || 31.18 ||
evamuktvā sa devarṣirbrāhmaṇaiḥ śāpakarṣitaiḥ  |
ājagāma tato divyāṃ hiraṇyanagarīṃ śubhām  || 31.19 ||
hiraṇyabāhurnṛpatirvaśiṣṭhaśca mahāmuniḥ  |
vilokya tānmunīn sarvān sa devarṣipurogamān  || 31.20 ||
abhivādya yathā nyāyamarghapādyairapūjayat |
nāradastu tato vākyaṃ rājānamidamabravīt || 31.21 ||
kuśalaṃ te nṛpaśreṣṭha sukhaṃ tiṣṭhasi suvrata  |
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 31.22 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā nāradaṃ ślakṣṇayā girā  |
adya me kuśalaṃ brahmaṃstavapādābjadarśanāt || 31.23 ||
kiṃ kartavyaṃ mayā te'dya brahmanme'nugrahaṃ kuru  |

nārada uvāca - 
kuru brahmamunīnāṃ tvaṃ śāpāntaṃ rājadarśanāt || 31.24 ||
nipātaya mahāduṣṭān daityān duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ  |
bandhanasthā hi moktavyā brāhmaṇāstu viśeṣataḥ  || 31.25 ||

rājovāca - 
vedaṃ paṭhanti ye'jñānādvadantyarthaṃ na tasya ca  |
pramāṇaṃ bhūtalokasya tadvatkarma ca kurvataḥ  || 31.26 ||
brahman vedena vidhivatte tu svargaṃ prayānti vai  |
ahaṃkaravimūḍhāśca mithyadānena yājakāḥ  || 31.27 ||
patanti narake viṣṇuśaṅkaradveṣaṇe ratāḥ  |
agastyaṃ ca vaśiṣṭhaṃ ca kṛtvā tau yājakau mune  || 31.28 ||
kratumiṣṭvā vidhānena revācarukasaṅgame  |
mocayiṣyāmyahaṃ śāpātpañcaliṅgārcanāddvija  || 31.29 ||
vedamantrahutaṃ tatra svayaṃ viṣṇurgrahīṣyati  |
rudraḥ kālāgnirūpeṇa grāhakaśca bhaviṣyati  || 31.30 ||
evaṃ yajñe tu sampūrṇe tuṣṭe nārāyaṇe śive  |
dvijānāṃ mokṣaṇaṃ tatra bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ  || 31.31 ||
na mune devatā yatra śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
ihaloke pare caiva gatistasya na vidyate  || 31.32 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājā sarvasaṃbhārasaṃbhṛtaḥ  |
yajñopaskaramādāya brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 31.33 ||
revācarukasaṃbhede yajñamiṣṭvā vidhānataḥ  |
vihāya pāpaṃ śaptāṃstānmocayāmāsa sa dvijān  || 31.34 ||
pañcaliṅgasamāyoge tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
brahmayānasamārūḍho vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 31.35 ||
dhriyamāṇātapatrastu stūyamānaśca vandibhiḥ  |
prāyācchivapuraṃ divyamumāmāheśvaraṃ nṛpa  || 31.36 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan purāṇākhyānamuttamam  |
śrutvākhyānāmidaṃ puṇyaṃ pañcaliṅgasamāgamam  || 31.37 ||
yamalokaṃ na paśyedvai pāpayoniṃ na gacchati  |
hayamedhaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate  || 31.38 ||
pāpagrasto vimūḍhātmā viṣṇumāyāvimohitaḥ  |
kathaṃ prayāti tattīrthaṃ kālagrahavaśīkṛtaḥ  || 31.39 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 31.40 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pañcaliṅgamāhātmye ekatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||

adhyāya 32

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
guhyātiguhyarūpāṇi pavitrāṇi yudhiṣṭhira  |
śrāddhakāryasya siddhāni tīrthānīha nibodha me  || 32.1 ||
atiguhyasya puṇyasya sarvato'marakaṇṭake  |
tamārabhya giriśreṣṭhaṃ sarvaṃ puṇyataraṃ smṛtam  || 32.2 ||
yāvatsā narmadā madhye puṇyasrotā mahānadī  |
nāsti tasmātparaṃ puṇyaṃ triṣu lokeṣu bhārata  || 32.3 ||
tasyottaravibhāge'sti nāmato yajñaparvataḥ  |
kaniṣṭho vindhyaputrastu bhrātā paryaṅkabhūbhṛtaḥ  || 32.4 ||
svayaṃbhuvā purā tasminniṣṭaḥ sautrāmaṇirmakhaḥ  |
tatraiveṣṭaṃ maghavatā hayamedhena bhārata  || 32.5 ||
dadhīcinā'tha devaiśca tatraiveṣṭaṃ mahāmakhaiḥ  |
niśkrāntā parvatāttasmāccaturnāmā mahānadī  || 32.6 ||
patitā narmadāyāṃ tu saṅgamo lokaviśrutaḥ  |
tasyāstīre tu ye darbhāḥ pītavarṇāḥ kṣitiṃ gatāḥ  || 32.7 ||
te śrāddhakaraṇe bhūpa pitṝṇāṃ mokṣadāyakāḥ  |
sa yāvatsaṅgamo nadyāḥ sa yāvadyajñaparvataḥ  || 32.8 ||
etasminnantare rājan śrāddhaṃ yaḥ parikalpayet |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti snānaṃ kuryātpradakṣiṇam  || 32.9 ||
siddheśvaraṃ nāmaliṅgaṃ catuṣkeśvarameva ca  |
saṃkhyā na bhūyo lokeṣu khyātamātraṃ mayā'nagha  || 32.10 ||
saṅgame vidyate devo na taṃ paśyanti mānavāḥ  |
pūjyate nāgakanyābhiḥ sadevāsurasattamaiḥ  || 32.11 ||
kathayāmi tavākhyānamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
ṛṣiḥ suparṇo yatrāsīdbrahmayonirjitendriyaḥ  || 32.12 ||
puruhūtā tasya bhāryā dharmapatnī pativratā  |
naimiṣāraṇyavāsasya kandamūlaphalāśinaḥ  || 32.13 ||
kṛṣṇājinaparīdhānavalkalādikavāsasaḥ  |
trikālavahṇihotāro vedādhyayanatatparāḥ  || 32.14 ||
vedasmṛtipurāṇoktamokṣopāyavicintakāḥ  |
ṛṣayo daśalakṣāṇi naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  || 32.15 ||
bālakhilyāśca tatraiva brahmaṇo mānasāḥ sutāḥ  |
brahmadaṇḍaṃ samāruhya devalokaṃ prayānti te  || 32.16 ||
māsopavāsinastatra jalāhārāstathāpare  |
tiṣṭhanti bahavaḥ kecidekapādena cāpare  || 32.17 ||
tasmiṃstapovane ramye siddhagandharvasevite  |
prasādya cābravīdvākyaṃ puruhūtā nijaṃ patim  || 32.18 ||
ṛtukāle tu parvaṇi māṃbhajasva mahāmune  |
jāyate me yathā putraḥ sarvasantānapāvanaḥ  || 32.19 ||
putreṇa lokān jayate tṛpyanti pitṛdevatāḥ  |
aputrasya gatirnāsti tasmātputramajījanaḥ  || 32.20 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
amāvāsyādya gotre'sminmaithunaṃ varjitaṃ priye  |
akartavyamidaṃ bhadre pitṝṇāṃ vārjitaṃ dhruvam  || 32.21 ||
pitarastasya tanmāṃsaṃ bhuñjate ṛtugāminaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu taptaṃ me duṣkaraṃ tapaḥ  || 32.22 ||
snātvā tatkṣaṇamātreṇa cāṇḍālastridivaṅgataḥ  |
dhikdhikdhigīritaṃ sarvaṃ na vedo na ca yājanam  || 32.23 ||
prahasya cābravīdvākyaṃ niṣādī taṃ muniṃ prati  |
viṣādaṃ tyaja viprarṣe satyametadbravīmyaham  || 32.24 ||
ahaṃkāravimūḍhātmā tvatsamo nāsti tāpasaḥ  |
na snānaṃ na japo homo na svādhyāyaḥ śivārcanam  || 32.25 ||
niṣkalaṃ kleśamāpanno vāyubhakṣo nirāśrayaḥ  |
tvattapo dhyānayogaśca svargaprāptiśca tatkatham  || 32.26 ||

jābāliruvāca - 
kā'si tvaṃ ca varārohe niṣādī rūpamāśritā  |
umā sarasvatī gaṅgā māṃ jijñāsitumāgatā  || 32.27 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye dharmaṃ brūhi śucismite  |
niṣādo naimiṣāraṇye mamāsītmunipuṃgava  || 32.28 ||
kāmārtayā mayā darśe bhartā putrāya yācitaḥ  |
akāle yācamānā'haṃ tena śaptā mahātmanā  || 32.29 ||
sa ca bhartā mayā śaptastato'nyo'nyaṃ samāgate  |
asya tīrthasya mahātmyādāvāṃ muktau tu kilbiṣāt || 32.30 ||
tasmāttvaṃ hi muniśreṣṭha śivārādhanatatparaḥ  |
madarthaṃ kuru karmāṇi homajāpyādikaṃ muhuḥ  || 32.31 ||
yāsyase tvaṃ same deśe satyametadbravīmyaham  |
ityuktvā sā yayau svargaṃ nijabhartrā samanvitā  || 32.32 ||
tyaktvā svakalpaṃ jābāliḥ śivārādhanatatparaḥ  |
acireṇaiva kālena brahmalokamupāgataḥ  || 32.33 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe brāhmaṇasya bhāryayā saha svargārohaṇaṃ nāma dvātriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 33

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
paścime nīlagaṅgāyā narmadottarakūlataḥ  |
vyatīpāteśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  || 33.1 ||
somanāthaṃ svayaṃ viddhi somamūrtiṃ jagatpatim  |
sāvitryā ca tapastaptaṃ tatra saptarṣibhistathā  || 33.2 ||
sāvitrīkuṇḍamityetadvikhyātaṃ narmadātaṭaḥ  |
narasya snānamātrasya kanyādānaphalaṃ bhavet || 33.3 ||
tilodakapradānena cānnadānena bhārata  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti sāvitrīlokamāśritāḥ  || 33.4 ||
dakṣasya duhitā cāsītkumudā nāma viśrutā  |
somāya dattvā tāṃ dakṣo mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 33.5 ||
tasyā sa janayāmāsa himāṃśuḥ putramadbhutam  |
jñātvā'mṛtamayaṃ taṃ tu surā yajñaphalārthinaḥ  || 33.6 ||
himāṃśuṃ somaputraṃ ca te yayuśca mudānvitāḥ  |
anyā somasya paramā kalā yā ṣoḍaśī smṛtā  || 33.7 ||
adhyāste sā candramasaṃ pitṛṇāṃ tāraṇāya ca  |
vanaspatau gate some gavāṃ kṣīre haviḥṣu ca  || 33.8 ||
somapāne mahāyajñe darśe diviṣadāṃ tadā  |
pītaṃ śrutvā'surāḥ sarve himāṃśuṃ pātumudyatāḥ  || 33.9 ||
asurāṇāṃ bhayādvindhyaṃ himāṃśurgirimāśrayat |
śrutvā te giridurgasthamasurāḥ svabhayāttadā  || 33.10 ||
graste te rāhuṇā sūrye himāṃśuṃ prati bhārata  |
kāyātikāyapramukhāḥ samprāptā dānavāstataḥ  || 33.11 ||
rāhostu bhrātaraḥ sarve saiṃhikeyā mahābalāḥ  |
samāhṛtya himāṃśustaiḥ sudhārthe cirajīvibhiḥ  || 33.12 ||
bhūtale pātitaḥ pāpaiḥ krandanti sma surāstadā  |
uvāca sa patan bhūmau himāṃśuḥ somanandana  || 33.13 ||
pātu māṃ deva īśāno dānavānāṃ bhayaṃkaraḥ  |
etasminnantare bhūpa himāṃśo rakṣaṇāya vai  || 33.14 ||
pātālādutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
tena bāṇena raudreṇa huṅkāreṇa mahāsurāḥ  || 33.15 ||
bhasmīkṛtāstu tatraiva devadevena śūlinā  |
tatastamabravīddevo bhayamāsurajaṃ tyaja  || 33.16 ||
śivabhaktiparo bhūtvā nirbhayastiṣṭha putraka  |
atha somaḥ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ gatvā brahmaniketanam  || 33.17 ||
vṛttāntaṃ kathayāmāsa tatastadvacanāttu tāḥ  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvā jagmuḥ somapurogamāḥ  || 33.18 ||
oṃkāraḥ kalpagātīre yatra tiṣṭhati bhārata  |
dṛṣṭvā tu tryambakaṃ devaṃ ṣaṣṭisaṃhārakārakam  || 33.19 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devo brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
śrīmānamṛtarūpo'yaṃ himāṃśuḥ somanandanaḥ  || 33.20 ||
rakṣaṇīyastvayā nātha devadānavaśaṅkayā  |
saptaviṃśatiyogānāṃ gatiḥ proktā tu candramāḥ  || 33.21 ||
tvaṃ gatiḥ sarvalokānāṃ tasmāttvaṃ trātumarhasi  |
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śiva ūce yathocitam  || 33.22 ||
pūjayitvā tu deveśaṃ somanāthaṃ jagatpatim  |
pātāleśvaranāmānaṃ gatāḥ sarve triviṣṭapam  || 33.23 ||
tadā prabhṛti tatsthānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
sāvitrīkuṇḍatoyena snānaṃ kṛtvā tamarcayet || 33.24 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ satyametacchivoditam  |
prabhāse yatphalaṃ rājan dṛṣṭaṃ ca śaśibhūṣaṇe  || 33.25 ||
tatphalaṃ nṛpaśārdūla bhavedeva na saṃśayaḥ  |
kālātikramaṇaṃ kuryāttatra yo bhaikṣamāśritaḥ  || 33.26 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ satyametadbravīmyaham  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  || 33.27 ||
patitānuddharejjatūnnarakānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya somaloke mahīyate  || 33.28 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pātāleśvaramāhātmyaṃ nāma trayastriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 34

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kathayāmi tavākhyānamindradyumnasya bhārata  |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān sūryavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ  || 34.1 ||
bubhuje sa mahīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
cakravartī nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ satyasaṃdho dṛḍhavrataḥ  || 34.2 ||
sa śaśāsa mahīṃ sarvāṃ yathā śakro'marāvatīm  |
yajñahomasahasraistu dadāha vasudhāmimām  || 34.3 ||
yajñotsavavivāhaistu vedamaṅgalamaṅgalaiḥ  |
cāturvarṇyaṃ svadharmasthaṃ prākṛtā itare janāḥ  || 34.4 ||
kāmaṃ kāmadughā dhenurdharaṇī sasyaśālinī  |
papraccha sa tu rājarṣirvaśiṣṭhaṃ brahmavittamam  || 34.5 ||
śakraścāṅgirasaṃ yadvadayodhyādhipatistathā  |
somasūryānvayasyaikaṃ tāraṇaṃ ca purodhasam  || 34.6 ||
hayamedhaṃ mahāyajñaṃ kasmiṃstīrthe yajāmyaham  |
kathaya tvaṃ prasādena yathā dṛṣṭaṃ yathā śrutam  || 34.7 ||

vaśiṣṭha uvāca - 
brahmarṣayo vedavido yathā te cābruvannṛpa  |
yajño nivartitavyastu ṛtvigbhirbrāhmaṇaistava  || 34.8 ||
marīciḥ kaśyapaścāpi aṅgirā gautamastathā  |
durvāsāścyavano dhūmraḥ kaṇvaścaiva mahāmuniḥ  || 34.9 ||
ete cānye ca nṛpate munīndrāḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
papraccha tānmahārāja indradyumno mahīpatiḥ  || 34.10 ||
kasmiṃstīrthe kṛto yajñaḥ kāmabhogaphalapradaḥ  |
etatsarvaṃ yathārthaṃ me vadantu munipuṃgavāḥ  || 34.11 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
kecidvārāṇasīṃ bhūpa prayāgaṃ ca tathā pare  || 34.12 ||
anye vai naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ puṣkaraṃ ca tathāpare  |
kubjāmrakaṃ tathā cānye gaṅgādvāramathāpare  || 34.13 ||
māyāpurīṃ tathā cānye sarvadevanamaskṛtām  |
vadantyanye himasthānaṃ bilvakaṃ nīlaparvatam  || 34.14 ||
kuśāvartaṃ tathaivā'nye tathā rudramahālayam  |
īśānaṃ caiva kedāraṃ sarvatīrthamayaṃ śubham  || 34.15 ||
aurvatīrthaṃ vadantyanye tathā badarikāśramam  |
kālañjaraṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ devadāruvanaṃ tathā  || 34.16 ||
hemakūṭaṃ virūpākṣamanye caṇḍīśvaraṃ tathā  |
bhūteśvaraṃ bhasmagātraṃ prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 34.17 ||
tīrthānyetāni cānyāni kathitāni tavānagha  |
matāntaraṃ munīnāṃ ca vividhaṃ parikīrtitam  || 34.18 ||
durvāsāścābravīdvākyaṃ prahasanniva taṃ nṛpam  |
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ  || 34.19 ||
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇāṃ caiva janmataḥ  |
mārkaṇḍeye vidyamāne saptakalpānuvartini  || 34.20 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajñe trikālajñe trayīvidi  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānapramāṇajñe tapodhane  || 34.21 ||
kasyāsti dharmakathane sāmarthyaṃ dharmaniścaye  |
dharmāraṇye mahārāja gamyatāṃ kalpagātaṭe  || 34.22 ||
mārkaṇḍeyo vakṣyate yattatra yajñaḥ pravartyatām  |

indradyumna uvāca - 
yathā vadasi devarṣe tathā kāryaṃ mayānagha  || 34.23 ||
ityuktvā munibhiḥ sārddhaṃ gantuṃ mārkaṇḍamāśramam  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā pratīhāraṃ suvarcasam  || 34.24 ||
mantriṇaṃ devagarbhaṃ ca bṛhaspatisamaṃ nṛpa  |
yajñopaskarasambhāraṃ sarvamādāya satvaram  || 34.25 ||
āgacchantu bhavanto'tra mamādeśaparāyaṇāḥ  |
sāgnibhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ yāsyāmaḥ kalpagātaṭam  || 34.26 ||
śīghraṃ sampādyatāṃ sarvaṃ yathā yajñaḥ pravartyatām  |
sarveṣāmeva viprāṇāṃ yānameṣāṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 34.27 ||
śatāni cāṣṭau rājñīnāṃ yānamāsāṃ prakalpaya  |
navakhaṇḍakṣitīśānāṃ samudradvīpavāsinām  || 34.28 ||
nānānṛpasahasrāṇāṃ ghoṣaṇā kriyatāṃ muhuḥ  |
candradvīpaṃ ca setuṃ ca tāmrapātraṃ śilāṣṭakam  || 34.29 ||
bhogadvīpaṃ ca saumyaṃ ca gāndharvavāruṇaṃ tathā  |
kumārikākhyaṃ navamaṃ navabhedaṃ pravartitam  || 34.30 ||
jambūśākakuśakrauñcaśālmaliplakṣapuṣkarāḥ  |
saptadvīpāḥ samākhyātāḥ samudrāśca nibodha me  || 34.31 ||
kṣārasarpirdadhikṣīramadirekṣujalātmakāḥ  |
samudrāḥ parikhākārāḥ pṛthivīmānakīrtitāḥ  || 34.32 ||
eteṣāṃ ghoṣaṇāṃ kāryāṃ mamādeśānuvartinām  |
gavāṃ ca trīṇi lakṣāṇi savatsānāṃ payomucām  || 34.33 ||
aśvānāṃ śyāmakarṇānāṃ sapādaṃ lakṣameva ca  |
dantināmayutaṃ caiva ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 34.34 ||
sahasrāṇi ca catvāri yānānāṃ kāmacāriṇām  |
lakṣaṃ tu karabhāṇāṃ vai maṇimāṇikyameva ca  || 34.35 ||
agniśaucāni vastrāṇi brāhmaṇārthe tathaiva ca  |
nānābhakṣāṇi bhojyāni pānāni vividhāni ca  || 34.36 ||
lakṣaṃ karmakarāṇāṃ tu tiladarbhādikaṃ tathā  |
devagarbhaṃ samādiśya mantriṇaṃ vadatāṃ varaḥ  || 34.37 ||
tatrasthairbrāhmaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ divyaṃ yānaṃ samāśritaḥ  |
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  || 34.38 ||
dharmāraṇyaṃ tataḥ prāpto yatrāste saptakalpagaḥ  |
oṃkāraṃ kalpagāṃ dṛṣṭvā vaidūryaṃ parvataṃ tathā  || 34.39 ||
mukto'smi kilbiṣādadya saptajanmakṛtādapi  |
evamuktvopaviṣṭo'sau sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  || 34.40 ||
yathārthaṃ pūjayāmāsa tamṛṣiṃ sa nṛpottamaḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ nṛpatiṃ prāha mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ  || 34.41 ||
kuśalaṃ nṛpaśārdūla ciraṃ dṛṣṭo'si suvrata  |
kimāgamanakāryaṃ te brāhmaṇairmunipuṃgavaiḥ  || 34.42 ||

indradyumna uvāca - 
yajñaṃ kartuṃ samāyātaḥ kasmiṃstīrthe dvijottama  |
śivena kathitaṃ yatte purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 34.43 ||
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi yajñakṣetraphalaṃ mahat || 34.44 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni āsamudrāntagocare  || 34.45 ||
snānaṃ kartuṃ samāyānti narmadāyāṃ narādhipā  |
uttare yāni liṅgāni yāni tīrthāni dakṣiṇe  || 34.46 ||
līyante koṭitīrtheṣu koṭitīrthaṃ tataḥ smṛtam  |
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  || 34.47 ||
brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāṃ śakrasyāpi prakīrtitam  |
tanmayā kathitaṃ rājan yathoddiṣṭaṃ tu śambhunā  || 34.48 ||
oṃkārasannidhau revākoṭitīrthaṃ prakīrtitam  |
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tapastaptaṃ suduṣkaram  || 34.49 ||
tasyānto naiva vidyate maheśvaravaco yathā  |
rāhusomasamāyoge kurukṣetraṃ praśasyate  || 34.50 ||
sarvadā sarvakāryeṣu narmadā puṇyadāyinī  |
trayodaśaguṇaṃ viddhi tīrthamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 34.51 ||
śatakoṭiguṇaṃ tattu koṭitīrthaṃ pracakṣate  || 34.52 ||
candrasūryoparāge tu viśeṣeṇa narādhipa  |
yaja tvaṃ koṭitīrtheṣu yadi svargamabhīpsasi  || 34.53 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rājā paramadhārmikaḥ  |
pādau jagrāha tatraiva muneramitatejasaḥ  || 34.54 ||
anugrahamimaṃ manye yattvayā kathitaṃ mama  |
etasminnantare prāpte yajñayūpānupaskarān  || 34.55 ||
nānādeśān kṣatriyāṃśca gāścāśvāṃśca gajāṃstathā  |
tatkṣaṇāddevagarbhaśca pratīhāraḥ samārpayat || 34.56 ||
triṃśadyojanaparyantaṃ yajñayūpāṃśca maṇḍapam  |
cakāra svapramāṇāni kuṇḍāni vividhāni ca  || 34.57 ||
vedadhvanitanirghoṣā divaṃ bhūmiṃ samaspṛśan  |
nirdhūmaścābhavadvahṇiḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ  || 34.58 ||
brahmāṇaṃ ca tathā viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ caiva samāhvayat |
rudrā ekādaśa tatra tathādityāśca dvādaśa  || 34.59 ||
viśvedevāstathāsādhyā marutaśca tathā vasuḥ  |
lokapālāstathā cāṣṭau samudrāḥ saritastathā  || 34.60 ||
vanaspatayastathā śailāstīrthāni vividhāni ca  |
dikpālā bhūtapālāśca siddhagandharvakinnarāḥ  || 34.61 ||
pitaraḥ somapāḥ sarve bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ  |
rākṣasā guhyakā bhūtā uragāśca yathā tathā  || 34.62 ||
sadmavātāstathākāśavāsinaśca tathotsave  |
ghṛtakṣīravahā nadyo dadhipāyasakardamāḥ  || 34.63 ||
babhūva nṛpatestasya tasmin yajñe mahotsave  |
bhakṣabhojyaiśca vividhaiḥ kāmyayānadibhistathā  || 34.64 ||
tṛptā devāśca munayo bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
evaṃ nivartito yajño brāhmaṇairbhūridakṣiṇaiḥ  || 34.65 ||
surāsuraistathā divyairindradyumnaḥ stutastadā  |
yajñabhāgāṃśca devānāṃ pṛthakpṛthagakalpayat || 34.66 ||
tathā bhāgo hi rudrāṇāṃ yathā sṛṣṭasvayambhuvā  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
rudrādityavasūtāṃ ca viśvedevahavirbhujām  || 34.67 ||
pramāṇaṃ nāma gotrāṃśca kathayasva mahāmune  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
atriścāṅgirasaḥ sarpirmarutaśca bṛhaspatiḥ  || 34.68 ||
dhruvo dhūmraśca ketuśca dharo dhātā harastathā  |
ekādaśa smṛtā rudrāḥ sarve kāmaphalapradāḥ  || 34.69 ||
aryamā varuṇaścendra pūṣā caiva gabhastimān  |
mitraścaiva samākhyāto jaghanyo jalakṛttathā  || 34.70 ||
vivasvāṃścaiva parjanyo dhātā vai dvādaśa smṛtāḥ  |
kaśyapasyāśrame jātāstejonidhaya utthitā  || 34.71 ||
māsāvaraprayogeṇa saṃcaranti parecchayā  |
ahorātramime sarve svarlokaṃ ca samantataḥ  || 34.72 ||
vasūnaṣṭau mahārāja kathyamānāṃ śṛṇuṣva vai  |
dhruvo dharaśca somaśca sāvitro hyanilo'nala  || 34.73 ||
pratyūṣaścaiva kalpaśca aṣṭau te vasavaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
viśvāyāśca tathā putrā viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 34.74 ||
kraturdakṣastathā satyaḥ kālaḥ kāmo ghnanistathā  |
purūravomārdravasau rocamānaśca te daśa  || 34.75 ||
muhūrtāyāstathā putrāḥ sādhyā devāḥ prakīrtitā  |
ekahīnāstu pañcāśatmarutaścaiva kīrtitāḥ  || 34.76 ||
eṣāṃ nāmāni sarveṣāṃ khyātānyeva yudhiṣṭhira  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ tvayā'nagha  || 34.77 ||
tato dhruvaṃ visṛjyātha munīṃśca brahmanandanān  |
oṃkāraṃ ca tato jñātvā rājā pūjāṃ cakāra ha  || 34.78 ||
maṇimāṇikyaratnaiśca liṅgasyābharaṇaṃ kṛtam  |
gandhadhūpaiśca vividhaiḥ karpūrāgarucandanaiḥ  || 34.79 ||
dhvajachatravitānaiśca vyajanairdivyacāmaraiḥ  |
pūjayitvā vidhānena stotraṃ me tadudāhṛtam  || 34.80 ||
oṃkāraṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ nityaṃ dhyāyanti yoginaḥ  |
kāmado mokṣadaścaiva oṃkārāya namo namaḥ  || 34.81 ||
brahmaviṣṇvindravarada sarvadeva namo'stu te  |
kadrudāya pracetase sahasrākṣāyamīḍhuṣe  || 34.82 ||
yā te rudra śivā tanūraghorā pāpakāśinī  |
sarvānanaśirogrīva sarvabhūtaśivāya ca  || 34.83 ||
sarvavyāpī ca bhagavāṃstasmai sarvagate namaḥ  |
sarvataḥ pāṇipādāntaḥ sarvato'kṣiśiromukhaḥ  || 34.84 ||
sarvataḥ śrutimāṃlloke sarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati  || 34.85 ||
jihvācāpalyabhāvena khedito'si maheśvara  |
kṛtaṃ stotramidaṃ devasyendradyumnena bhārata  || 34.86 ||
liṅgamadhye paraṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
yaddṛṣṭaṃ taduvācainaṃ rājānaṃ prati bhārata  || 34.87 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  || 34.88 ||

indradyumna uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  || 34.89 ||
atratvamumayā sārdhaṃ devadroṇyāṃ samarcitaḥ  |
āvāsaṃ kuru deveśa sarvadā yajñaparvate  || 34.90 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vāpi prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
tīrthe'smin devadeveśa śivalokaṃ prayātu saḥ  || 34.91 ||
sahasrayājī dharmajño dānayajñādhipastathā  |
andhāśca vāmanāścaiva tiryagyonigatānarāḥ  || 34.92 ||
maraṇādubhayoḥ sāmyaṃ bhavedatra maheśvara  |
parvataṃ veṣṭayedyastu sūtreṇaikena parvaṇi  || 34.93 ||
pṛthivī veṣṭitā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
sarvatīrthaphalāvāptiḥ śivaloke prayātyasau  || 34.94 ||

oṃkāra uvāca - 
sarvametatnṛpaśreṣṭha kāmaḥ sampadyatāṃ tava  || 34.95 ||
evamuktvā tato devaḥ tatraivāntaradhīyata  |
surāsurairathādityaiḥ sādhyaiḥ sārddhaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
svakīyaṃ yānamāruhya kailāsanilayaṃ yayau  || 34.96 ||
rājā'pi śrāvayāmāsa bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
mama yajñaprabhāveṇa sarve santu nirāmayāḥ  || 34.97 ||
sarve tu tṛptimāyāntu mama yajñaprabhāvataḥ  |
indradyumnastu brahmāṇaṃ tatastuṣṭāva bhārata  || 34.98 ||
tuṣṭaḥ provāca dhātā taṃ varaṃ vṛṇu viśāmpate  |

rājovāca - 
tuṣṭo'si yadi me deva varaṃ dātumihecchasi  || 34.99 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle liṅgamūrtidharo bhava  |
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye yadi tuṣṭo'si me prabho  || 34.100 ||
prāṇatyāge kṛte tatra brahmalokaṃ prayātu vai  |
evaṃ bhavatu rājendra brahmā provāca satvaram  || 34.101 ||
evamuktvā yayau brahmā rudralokaṃ suraiḥ saha  |
viṣṇuṃ caiva tato rājā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca yudhiṣṭhira  || 34.102 ||
keśavaṃ mādhavaṃ viṣṇuṃ govindaṃ madhusūdanam  |
padmanābhaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ śrīdharaṃ ca trivikramam  || 34.103 ||
dāmodaraṃ vāsudevaṃ hariṃ ca praṇamāmyaham  |
śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgavanamālāvibhūṣaṇam  || 34.104 ||
lokanāthaṃ jagannāthaṃ śrīnāthaṃ sarvavittamam  |
śrīkāntaṃ śrīdharaṃ śrīśaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ namāmyaham  || 34.105 ||
acyutānantayajñeśa yajñādhipa namo'stu te  |
ṛksāmātharvarūpāya yajñarūpāya te namaḥ  || 34.106 ||
nṛsiṃhamatsyavārāhakūrmarūpāya te namaḥ  |
tīrthayānasamārūḍha garuḍadhvaja te namaḥ  || 34.107 ||
sahasraśirasaṃ devaṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ param  |
vedyaṃ puruṣamadhyakṣamādyaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhum  || 34.108 ||
praṇato'smi sadā devaṃ daityāntakaraṇaṃ harim  |
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ bhūgarbhaṃ yajñagarbhāmṛtodbhavam  || 34.109 ||
śrīgarbhajñānagarbhāya vāsudeva namo'stu te  |
tvayā sṛṣṭaṃ jagatsarvaṃ carācaramidaṃ prabho  || 34.110 ||
sraṣṭā pālayitā tvaṃ vai hartā tvaṃ ca yuge yuge  |
viśvataścakṣuravyakto vijñeyo viśvato mukhaḥ  || 34.111 ||
viśvātmā viśvato devo vāsudevo namo'stu te  |
tvamādityaśca vāyuśca tvamagnistvaṃ ca candramāḥ  || 34.112 ||
tvaṃ dhātā devadeveśa tvamindrastvaṃ prajāpatiḥ  |
tvatprasādātsuraśreṣṭha yajñasiddhirmamābhavat || 34.113 ||
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  || 34.114 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ satyaṃ varaṃ vṛṇu viśāmpate  |
dadāmi te na saṃdeho yajñasiddhimavāpsyasi  || 34.115 ||

rājovāca - 
oṃkārasaumyabhāge tu liṅgarūpī janārdanaḥ  |
vaidūryaśikharasyāgre bhava tvaṃ lokapāvanaḥ  || 34.116 ||
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye yadi dātumihecchasi  |
yāsyanti vaiṣṇavaṃ sthānamihābhyarcya vidhānataḥ  || 34.117 ||
tiryagyoniṃ na gacchanti yamalokaṃ tathā narāḥ  |
prāṇatyāge kṛte tatra narā gacchanti te padam  || 34.118 ||
pitṝṇāmannadānena pitaro vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  |
prasādātte ca gacchantu satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyaham  || 34.119 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ viṣṇurindradyumnaṃ viśāmpate  || 34.120 ||
avatāraṃ kariṣyāmi ihaiva nṛpasattama  |
sarvameva nṛpaśreṣṭha matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 34.121 ||
evamuktvā yayau devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
surāsuraiḥ stūyamānastridivaṃ prati bhārata  || 34.122 ||
eṣa te kathito rājannindradyumna mahādhvaraḥ  |
tenāsau parvataḥ puṇyaḥ sarvalokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 34.123 ||
siddheśvaraṃ ca brahmāṇaṃ viddhi nārāyaṇaṃ harim  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya viṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 34.124 ||
tatastuṣṭāva tīrthāni rājā satyavrate sthitaḥ  |
pitṛṇāṃ tāraṇārthāya tīrthebhyaśca namo namaḥ  || 34.125 ||

tīrthānyūcu̱ḥ
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |
tīrthānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā indradyumna uvāca ha  || 34.126 ||
oṃkārasannidhau tīrthe sthātavyaṃ madanugrahāt |
evamastviti rājānamuktvā cakruḥ saritstutim  || 34.127 ||
kalpagāṃ tvāṃ namaskurmo harasya paramāṃ kalām  |
atīva sarvalokānāṃ bhuvi khyātāṃ saridvarām  || 34.128 ||
nāsmatprabhāvataḥ pūtā kiṃtu pūtā svabhāvataḥ  |
yadvatsūryaprabhā puṇyā vahṇeścāpi prabhā yathā  || 34.129 ||
himāṃśoścaiva rājendra tathaiveyaṃ mahānadī  |
ityuktvā taṃ ca rājānaṃ tīrthānyantardadhustadā  || 34.130 ||
tatastuṣṭāva gaṅgāṃ vā arghaṃ dattvā nṛpottamaḥ  |
gaṅgā bhāgīrathī devī tathā bhogavatī śubhā  || 34.131 ||
jāhnavī mokṣadā bhadrā tāriṇī pāpanāśinī  |
svarge mandākinī caiva devadevanamaskṛtā  || 34.132 ||
gāyatrī vedamātā tvamumā kātyāyanī tathā  |
kimanyadapi te devi hareṇa śirasā dhṛtā  || 34.133 ||
kasyāsti śaktiḥ stotuṃ tvāmṛte candrārdhaśekharāt |

gaṅgovāca - 
tuṣṭā'smi te mahārāja varaṃ yācasva suvrata  || 34.134 ||
gaṅgāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā rājā tāṃ pratyuvāca ha  |

rājovāca - 
yadi tuṣṭā'si deveśi varaṃ dātumihecchasi  || 34.135 ||
ihaiva kriyatāṃ vāso vara eṣa pradīyatām  || 34.136 ||

gaṅgovāca - 
evaṃ bhavatu rājendra bhāgenaiva vahāmyaham  || 34.137 ||
tatastuṣṭāva gaṅgā tu kalpagāṃ saritāṃ varām  |
