śreṣṭhā tvaṃ kalpage devi namaskārye cirāyuṣi  || 34.138 ||
tvattoyasya prabhāveṇa pāvitryamabhavacca me  |
kalpānte tu kṣayaṃ yānti saritaḥ sāgarādayaḥ  || 34.139 ||
tīrthāni caiva sarvāṇi tvamevātraiva tiṣṭhasi  |
pūjyā tridaśavandyā ca subhage ciragaminī  || 34.140 ||
gaurīsamā jaṭāgre vai haramūrtirbhaviṣyasi  |
evamuktvā tato gaṅgā namaskṛtya ca mekalām  || 34.141 ||
divyayānasamārūḍhā stūyamānāpsarogaṇaiḥ  |

sarasvatyuvāca - 
duhitā'haṃ tava hyeṣā dharmataḥ paripālitā  || 34.142 ||
saptakalpavahe dhanye tvacchuśrūṣāparāyaṇā  |
āgatāhaṃ mahābhāge tvatpādābjadidṛkṣayā  || 34.143 ||
snānaṃ kartuṃ tathā devi caturviṃśativatsaraiḥ  |
kiṃ na vetsi mahābhāge māṃ dhareśvari vāhini  || 34.144 ||
kariṇīrūpamāsthāya koṭitīrthe samāgatā  |
sapādakoṭitīrthāni candrasūrye same grahe  || 34.145 ||
snānārthaṃ kalpage devi ādiṣṭā viṣṇunā svayam  |
dvāparasya caturthāṃśe kurupāṇḍavasenayoḥ  || 34.146 ||
yuddhārthaṃ samitau puñjaṃ sarvatīrthamaye śubhe  |
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ tapyate'tha tapastathā  || 34.147 ||
sarvaṃ śataguṇaṃ taddhi tvattoye kalpage punaḥ  |
tadeva koṭiguṇitaṃ rāhugraste divākare  || 34.148 ||
evamuktvā mahārāja kalpagāṃ ca sarasvatī  |
indradyumnaṃ ca rājānaṃ svasthānaṃ cābhyagātpunaḥ  || 34.149 ||
indradyumnastato rājā svayaṃ tuṣṭāva bhārata  |
tvattoyasya prabhāveṇa pitṛdevāśca tārpitāḥ  || 34.150 ||
pavitraṃ ca tvayā devi trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
tvaṃ mātā sarvabhūtānāṃ nṛṇāṃ saṃsāratāriṇī  || 34.151 ||
mekalāsi mahādevi kalpagā narmadā tathā  |
jalapūrṇeti vikhyātā vindhyaparvatabhūṣaṇam  || 34.152 ||
api varṣasahasreṇa kaḥ stotuṃ ca kṣamaḥ śubhe  |
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devī nṛpasyāsya tadā'bravīt || 34.153 ||
kṣatriyāṇāṃ sahasraistu kṛto yajño'tra pārthiva  |
sadṛśaste mahārāja na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 34.154 ||
varaṃ dadāmi te rājan yena siddhimavāpsyasi  |
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā kalpagāyā yudhiṣṭhira  || 34.155 ||
prahasannabravīdrājā brāhmaṇaiḥ sahitastadā  |
śivabhaktiparo nityaṃ saptakalpavahe śubhe  || 34.156 ||
yadi tuṣṭā'si me devi varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  |
pravāhān kuru sapta tvaṃ dakṣiṇottarakūlayoḥ  || 34.157 ||
sarvataḥ sarvage devi yadi māṃ bahu manyase  |
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye yadi tuṣṭā'si suvrate  || 34.158 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā indradyumnasya bhārata  |
narmadā cābravīdvākyamindradyumnaṃ narādhipa  || 34.159 ||
sarvametatprabhāveṇa matprasādādbhaviṣyati  |
atrāntare ca yaddānaṃ dīyate nā'tra saṃśayaḥ  || 34.160 ||
tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate satyametadbravīmi te  |
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirityāha bhagavānnṛpa  || 34.161 ||
antyajāḥ śvapacā vāpi mṛgāścaiva sarīsṛpāḥ  |
sarve te tridivaṃ yānti nīlagaṅgāsamāgame  || 34.162 ||
yajñamiṣṭvā nṛpaśreṣṭha indradyumno viśāmpate  |
narmadāṃ ca namaskṛtya oṃkāraṃ caiva bhārata  || 34.163 ||
svakīyaṃ yānamāruhya satyadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
vṛto nṛpa sahasreṇa stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 34.164 ||
viveśa nagarīṃ puṇyāmayodhyāṃ devanirmitām  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 34.165 ||
tataḥ kālena mahatā rājyaṃ kṛtvā mahābalaḥ  |
ayutaṃ sāgrameveha sa rājā tridivaṃ gataḥ  || 34.166 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |
yamalokaṃ na paśyecca pāpayoniṃ na gacchati  || 34.167 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe indradyumnayajñanīlagaṅgāvatāro nāma catustriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 35

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
vaidūryaparvataṃ ramyaṃ surasiddhaniṣevitam  |
māndhātā kena kāryeṇa gato vai saptakalpagām  || 35.1 ||
tathā pāśupatāstreṇa hatvā vai tripuraṃ haraḥ  |
pātālaṃ bhedayitvā tu rasātalatalaṃ yayau  || 35.2 ||
jāleśvaraṃ tathā liṅgamutthitaṃ kena hetunā  |
nīlagaṅgā kathaṃ tatra kalpagā bhedamāgatā  || 35.3 ||
bāṇāsuraḥ śive bhaktaḥ śivalabdhavaraḥ śuciḥ  |
liṅgāni tāni teneha pūjitāni mahāmune  || 35.4 ||
saṃkhyayā navakoṭyastu tāni kṣiptāni tatra vai  |
etatsarvaṃ samāsena kathayasva mahāmune  || 35.5 ||
yathāśrutaṃ purākalpe purāṇe skandakīrtite  |
brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  || 35.6 ||
tathā tu kathyatāṃ tāta yathoddiṣṭaṃ śivena tu  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābāho kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  || 35.7 ||
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
devānāṃ saṅgamaṃ yatra kailāse parvatottame  || 35.8 ||
kārttikyāṃ ca surāḥ sarve haraṃ draṣṭumupāgatāḥ  |
tatra devagaṇān sarvān skando vacanamabravīt || 35.9 ||
mayā śrutaṃ ca dṛṣṭaṃ ca śivena kathitaṃ purā  |
aṣṭalakṣāṇi sārdhāni granthe'smin kīrtitāni ca  || 35.10 ||
śivaloke śivenaiva nibaddhāni yathā yathā  |
tadardhaṃ vaiṣṇave loke tadarddhaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pure  || 35.11 ||
sapādalakṣaṃ sūryasya loke caiva vyavasthitam  || 35.12 ||
caturbhiradhikāśītisahasrāṇi tathā kramam  |
khyātāni martyaloke ca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 35.13 ||
sūtaḥ saṃgrahakartā ca saṃhitāstatra eva ca  |
kalpaskando mahāskandaḥ purāṇaṃ saptadhā viduḥ  || 35.14 ||
sargaśca pratisargaśca vaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca  |
vaṃśānucaritaṃ ceti purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam  || 35.15 ||
aśaktā mānuṣā loke alpajīvena jīvikāḥ  |
yuddhihīnā durācārā bhaviṣyanti kalau yuge  || 35.16 ||
na svādhyāyo vaṣaṭkāro na tapo na ca yājanam  |
strīkāmā lolupā viprāḥ kāmyakṛtyāstu yācakāḥ  || 35.17 ||
pratigrahaparā nityaṃ kuṭumbabharaṇārthinaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ naiva budhyante strīṇāṃ snehavaśaṃgatā  || 35.18 ||
kukarmāṇi kariṣyanti dharmiṣṭhāstāpasāstathā  |
kalau yuge tathā prāpte kāle kaulā digambarāḥ  || 35.19 ||
ekavarṇāḥ prajāḥ sarvā rājā mleccho bhaviṣyati  |
hīne yuge tathā prāpte bauddhasthe caiva keśave  || 35.20 ||
alpāyuṣaścaiva martyā alpavīryaparākramāḥ  |
nānādeśopadravāśca bhaviṣyanti mahāmune  || 35.21 ||
vedān vai prārpayiṣyanti dvijāścāṇḍālavaṃśinaḥ  |
vedāveśaṃ kariṣyanti vedavikrayaṇaṃ tathā  || 35.22 ||
rājadvāre gamiṣyanti dhanaprāptisamīhayā  |
vikrayaṃ cāgnihotrāṇāṃ kanyānāṃ vikrayaṃ tathā  || 35.23 ||
kariṣyanti dvijāḥ sarve śrotriyā vratavarjitāḥ  |
pūrṇe tu dvādaśe varṣe narāḥ palitayauvanāḥ  || 35.24 ||
daśa dvādaśavarṣā tu nārī garbhadharā bhavet |
nārī na puruṣaṃ manyetna mātāpitarau sutaḥ  || 35.25 ||
nasnuṣā manyate śvaśrūṃ duhitā mātaraṃ tathā  |
etaduddeśataḥ proktaṃ prakṛtaṃ śṛṇu bhārata  || 35.26 ||
yajñaṃ kartuṃ mahārāja māndhātā nṛpasattamaḥ  |
vaidūryaparvataṃ prāptaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇāya ca  || 35.27 ||
devatānāṃ hitārthaṃ vai jaghāna tripuraṃ haraḥ  |
jvalatpāśupatāstreṇa taddagdhamabhavatpurā  || 35.28 ||
pātālāttena cānītaṃ liṅgaṃ jāleśvaraṃ tathā  |
kāmadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva sadādevanamaskṛtam  || 35.29 ||
lokānāṃ tāraṇārthāya nīlagaṅgā samāgatā  |
megharājasamudbhūtā sarvapāpaharā parā  || 35.30 ||
tasyāṃ śrāddhaṃ prakurvīta pitṝṇāmakṣayecchayā  |
bāṇāsurasya liṅgāni koṭitīrthe śivālaye  || 35.31 ||
patitāni jale caiva narmadāyā narādhipa  |
pūjitāni pavitrāṇi bhuktimuktipradāni tu  || 35.32 ||
bāṇaliṅgāni rājendra khyātāni bhuvanatraye  |
na pratiṣṭhā na saṃskāraḥ teṣāmāvāhanādi ca  || 35.33 ||
evameva prapūjyāni śivarūpāṇi bhārata  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ tvayā'nagha  || 35.34 ||
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāmanyāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm  |
aghaghnī yā pratītā me kīrtayiṣyāmi te'nagha  || 35.35 ||
astīha parvataḥ śrīmān vaidūryo nāma nāmataḥ  |
tatra puṇyāni tīrthāni tiṣṭhantyāyatanāni ca  || 35.36 ||
kāmaṃ kāmaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ puṣpitaiḥ phalitaistathā  |
divyapakṣininādaiśca vānīravanarājibhiḥ  || 35.37 ||
śālaistālaiśca saralaistamālairupaśobhitā  |
nadī pāpaharā puṇyā narmadā tatra bhārata  || 35.38 ||
trailokye yāni tīrthāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca  |
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailādevāśca sapitāmahāḥ  || 35.39 ||
narmadāyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha siddhagandharvacāraṇāḥ  |
snātumāyānti te sarve sarvasiddhistu bhārata  || 35.40 ||
kiṃ tatra parvate rājan yatra viśvāvasurmuniḥ  |
na yakṣādhipatiryatra kubero naravāhanaḥ  || 35.41 ||
vaidūryaśikhare puṇye tatra sākṣādvivasvataḥ  |
nityaṃ puṣpaphalā yatra pādapā haritacchadāḥ  || 35.42 ||
bahvāścaryaṃ tathā tatra dṛśyate parvatottame  |
puṇye svargopame divye nityaṃ devarṣisevite  || 35.43 ||
hlādinī puṇyatīrthānāṃ rājarṣe tatra vai sarit |
vaivasvatāntare prāpte rājannādikṛte yuge  || 35.44 ||
oṃkārasyāvatāreṇa savaidūryamayo giriḥ  |
tretāyuge maṇimayo dvāpare haimarūpakaḥ  || 35.45 ||
kalau pāpaharaḥ prokto yathā devastathā giriḥ  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ purātanam  || 35.46 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vaidūryaparvatavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 36

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
vasudānastu rājarṣiścakravartī purā'bhavat |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān chakratulyaparākramaḥ  || 36.1 ||
bubhuje pṛthivī sarvāṃ grāmamekamivātha saḥ  |
īje yāgasahasreṇa hayamedhakṛtena ca  || 36.2 ||
sapādalakṣamadhikaṃ sarvā jīvanti ca prajāḥ  |
na tatra dīno duḥkhī vā daridro vā'pi kaścana  || 36.3 ||
svayaṃ kāmadughā dhenuḥ pṛthivī sasyaśālinī  |
eva pālayatastasya pṛthivīṃ pṛthivīpate  || 36.4 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle daityāstridaśakaṇṭakāḥ  |
ketumālī suketuśca sumukho dundubhistathā  || 36.5 ||
dharmavighnaṃ ca kartāraḥ samudbhūtāḥ surālaye  |
ke'pi no śaknuvantīha yajñaṃ kartuṃ bhayāttadā  || 36.6 ||
vasudāno'bravīdvākyaṃ vaśiṣṭhaṃ brahmavittamam  |
kasmiṃstīrthe hi nirvighno hayamedho vidhīyate  || 36.7 ||
yajñavidyā vidhānena nirdiśya mama kathyatām  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vasudānasya dhīmataḥ  || 36.8 ||
vaśiṣṭhaścābravīdvākyaṃ rājānaṃ prati bhārata  |
na śakyate mukhaḥ kartuṃ hayamedho viśeṣataḥ  || 36.9 ||
amareśvarasya mūrtirvai dakṣiṇāśāmukhe sthitā  |
dānavairnirjitāstatra sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 36.10 ||
yajñabhāgaṃ grahītuṃ na śaknuvanti kadācana  |
etasmātkāraṇādrājan satyametattavoditam  || 36.11 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vaśiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ  |
cukopa vasudānaśca pratyāha ca purodhasam  || 36.12 ||
hatvaitān rākṣasān pāpānadya vai devakaṇṭakān  |
devānāṃ ca prasādena kariṣye yāgamuttamam  || 36.13 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājā sarvasambhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  |
brāhmaṇaiḥ sahito vidvānṛtvigbhirvedapāragaiḥ  || 36.14 ||
gavāṃ ca pañcalakṣāṇi savatsānāṃ payomucām  |
sapādalakṣamaśvānāṃ dantināmayutaṃ tathā  || 36.15 ||
maṇimāṇikyaratnāni hemarūpyavasūni ca  |
vividhaṃ bhakṣyabhojyaṃ ca ājyaṃ vrīhitilāṃstathā  || 36.16 ||
maṇḍapān yajñayūpāṃśca samādāya nṛpottamaḥ  |
evaṃ pravartite yajñe nirvighnena tu bhārata  || 36.17 ||
āvabhṛtyodakaiḥ klinnā vasudhā hemanirmitā  |
babhūvurlulitā ye tu kardame mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ  || 36.18 ||
te hemavarṇakāḥ sarve babhūvustatra bhārata  |
yugapatpūjitā yatra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 36.19 ||
homācca pṛthakkṣīrājyapravāhāstatra nirgatāḥ  |
gavāṃ mūtrapravāhaśca tatrābhūtnṛpasattama  || 36.20 ||
snānodakaṃ ca devānāmṛṣibhirvedapāragaiḥ  |
yatkṛtaṃ tatpravāhaśca tatrāsītnṛpasattama  || 36.21 ||
pravāheṣu ca sarveṣu militeṣu tadā nṛpa  |
āpagā kapilā nāma dṛṣṭā brahmarṣidaivataiḥ  || 36.22 ||
narmadāsaṅgamastatra rudrāvartaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
tatastu brāhmaṇāḥ sarve dakṣiṇābhiḥ prapūjitāḥ || 36.23 ||
gajāśvarathamārūḍhā nānābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ  |
tuṣṭā devagaṇāḥ sarve ūcustaṃ pārthivaṃ tadā  || 36.24 ||
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yajñenānena suvrata  |

vasudāna uvāca - 
revākapilasambhede snātvā'bhyarcya maheśvaram  || 36.25 ||
vimānaistridivaṃ yāntu ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye dīyatāṃ madanugrahāt || 36.26 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
kāmaṃ kāmaṃ phaliṣyanti cintitāste manorathāḥ  |
varaṃ dattvā ca bho rājan prītā vai devatāstataḥ  || 36.27 ||
svaṃ svaṃ vimānamāruhya muditāstridivaṃ yayuḥ  |
vasudāno'pi rājarṣirbrāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 36.28 ||
mudā paramayā yukto hyayodhyāmāyayau tadā  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  || 36.29 ||
bhogāṃśca puṣkalān bhuktvā gato māheśvaraṃ puram  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ revākapilasaṅgamam  || 36.30 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 36.31 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kapilāvatāro nāma ṣaṭtriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 37

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
arundhatīprasūtāyā dākṣāyaṇyāssuto'bhavat || 37.1 ||
viśravāṃ nāma rājā'bhūcchakratulyaparākramaḥ  |
mathurādhipatiḥ śrīmāṃścakravartī mahābalaḥ  || 37.2 ||
nirjitā'maradaityāśca sa rājā maghavā tviva  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhāśca brāhmaṇāścetare janāḥ  || 37.3 ||
evaṃ śāsayataḥ pṛthvīṃ dhanadhānyasamākulām  |
na durbhikṣaṃ na dāridryaṃ kvacidastiṃ mahītale  || 37.4 ||
etasminnantare prāpte prasthito'marakaṇṭakam  |
snātvā ca koṭitīrthe vai samabhyarcya maheśvaram  || 37.5 ||
dakṣiṇāṃ mūrtimāśritya tasya devasya bhārata  |
samādhistho bhavedyāvatsa rājā dharmavatsalaḥ  || 37.6 ||
tāvatpaśyati madhyāhne pratīcyādityamaṇḍalāt |
dvau sūryau pūrvadigbhāge gatau dvau dākṣiṇāṃ diśam  || 37.7 ||
dvau pātālaṃ tathā corddhvaṃ dvau sūryau caṇḍarūpiṇau  |
kālaḥ saṃvartako vahniḥ prajajvāla tadā nṛpa  || 37.8 ||
raudrāśca dāruṇā vātā vavuḥ pralayakāriṇaḥ  |
digdāho bhūmikampaśca utkāpātāśca dāruṇāḥ  || 37.9 ||
patitāssiddhagandharvā devalokātsakinnarāḥ  |
vidyādharāstathā yakṣāḥ karmacchedādiva vyutāḥ  || 37.10 ||
vidyādharyo rudantyaśca tathaivāpsarasastathā  |
prajajvāla mahāvahṇiḥ saśailavanakānanām  || 37.11 ||
bhasmīkṛtya dharāṃ sarvāṃ revākūlamivāgamat |
tṛṣārto dāhasantaptastadābhūtnṛpasattamaḥ  || 37.12 ||
samudyataḥ payaḥ pātuṃ yāvatpaśyati mekalām  |
tāvattanmadhyamārge tu vāri kiṃcinna vidyate  || 37.13 ||
pravāho reṇubhūtaśca dṛśyate na tu narmadā  |
saritaḥ sāgarān chailān babhrāma sa sarāṃsi ca  || 37.14 ||
jalaṃ pātuṃ na kutrāpi labdhavān sa nṛpottamaḥ  |
apaśyatnarmadāṃ devīṃ vindhyaṃ girivarottamam  || 37.15 ||
praviṣṭo narmadāmadhye nṛpaścintākulo'bhavat |
jalaṃ vinādya maraṇaṃ niyataṃ me na saṃśaya  || 37.16 ||
antarhitaṃ jagatkṛtsnaṃ na ca kiṃcijjalaṃ vinā  |
cintayitvā nṛpaśreṣṭha oṃkāraṃ ca samāśrayat || 37.17 ||
parvatārohaṇaṃ yāvattṛṣārtaḥ kartumicchati  |
tāvatpaśyati rājāsau tatra vai kalpaśākhinam  || 37.18 ||
phalitaṃ puṣpitaṃ ramyaṃ śākhābhirupaśobhitam  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā śayito rājā chāyāṃ prāpya suśītalām  || 37.19 ||
śarīramamṛtaṃ bhūtaṃ jīvitāśābhavattadā  |
apaśyatpuruṣaṃ tatra śayānaṃ dīptatejasam  || 37.20 ||
taducchvāsena lokā vai kampitāścorddhvaśastadā  |
pātālāni tathā sapta niḥśvāsena yudhiṣṭhira  || 37.21 ||
kumbhāḥ pārśve ca catvārastasya tiṣṭhanti sodakāḥ  |
tān dṛṣṭvā cintayāmāsa tṛṣārto nṛpasattamaḥ  || 37.22 ||
bodhaye sukhasuptaṃ no nādattaṃ ca pibāmyaham  |
kiṃ karomītī saṃcintya taṃ praṇamya sthito nṛpaḥ  || 37.23 ||
mānuṣīṃ tanumāśritya sthito'yaṃ puruṣottamaḥ  |
svayaṃ prajāpatirdevo hartā kartā jagatprabhuḥ  || 37.24 ||
tato'paśyaddvitīyaṃ ca puruṣaṃ dīptatejasam  |
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ trinetraṃ ca śūlapāṇipinākinam  || 37.25 ||
bhasmojjvalitagātraṃ ca jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇam  |
kapālamālābharaṇaṃ taḍijjyotiḥsamaprabham  || 37.26 ||
daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ puruṣaṃ piṅgākṣaṃ śaśiśekharam  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ sarveṣāṃ lomaharṣaṇam  || 37.27 ||
rājā tuṣṭāva deveśaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
jaya deva mahādeva ādideva namo'stu te  || 37.28 ||
tṛṣā māṃ bādhate deva śūlapāṇe śṛṇuṣva tat |
prayaccha śītalaṃ toyaṃ yena jīvāmi śaṅkara  || 37.29 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prahasannabravīcchivaḥ  |
jalpatastasya devasya cāsye dṛṣṭāḥ samantataḥ  || 37.30 ||
surā brahmarṣayo lokāḥ sapta caiva carācarāḥ  |
pātālāni ca bhūtāni gandharvoragarākṣasāḥ  || 37.31 ||
tato dadarśa nārīṃ ca samudvignāṃ samāgatām  |
rajo guṇṭhita sarvāṅgīṃ rudhireṇa pariplutām  || 37.32 ||
anantadīptatejaskāṃ mārutoddhūtamūrdhajām  |
bālaṃ nidhāya vāmorau kanyāṃ caiva stanāntare  || 37.33 ||
brahmasūtratridaṇḍābhyāṃ kuṇḍalābhyāṃ alaṃkṛtām  |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sa tato rājā provāca vacanaṃ tadā  || 37.34 ||
jaya devi mahādevi lokānugrahakāriṇi  |
tṛṣayāplutadeho'haṃ jalaṃ pāyaya māmiha  || 37.35 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā taṃ prāha parameśvarī  |
stanyaṃ piba mahīpa tvaṃ yadi tvāṃ vādhate tṛṣā  || 37.36 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā cintayāmāsa pārthivaḥ  |
stanaṃ pāsyāmi deveśi nārī vacanamabravīt || 37.37 ||
pratijñātaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ sutejaste nṛpottama  |
agre nyasya stanaṃ tvāṃ vai paścāddāsyāmi bālakam  || 37.38 ||
tasmātpiba mahābhāga stanyaṃ me nāsti pātakam  |
tata provāca nṛpatistāṃ nārīṃ kāmarūpiṇīm  || 37.39 ||
akāryametatsarveṣāṃ mādṛśānāṃ kutaḥ kathā  |
anyo'nyaṃ jalpatāṃ teṣāṃ sā tatrāntaradhīyata  || 37.40 ||
kva gatā'si mahādevītyuktvā vismayamāgataḥ  |
svapnalabdhā yathā vārtā niṣkalā pratibhāsate  || 37.41 ||
indrajālamayaṃ yadvanmṛgatṛṣṇājalaṃ yathā  |
evaṃ cintayatastasya punaragre vyavasthitā  || 37.42 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvaiva tato rājā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
kāsi tvaṃ hi varārohe satyametadbravīhi me  || 37.43 ||
umā kātyāyanī gaṅgā vedamātā sarasvatī  |
brahmāṇī ca mahālakṣmīḥ kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 37.44 ||
samānaḥ puruṣaḥ ko'yaṃ kumbhāścatvāra eva ke  |
vṛṣārūḍhastrinetraśca ko'yaṃ śūlapinākadhṛk || 37.45 ||
utsaṅge nihitaḥ ko'yaṃ bālakaḥ kā ca kanyakā  |
mūrkho'haṃ nābhijānāmi jananaṃ maraṇaṃ yathā  || 37.46 ||

stryuvāca - 
pṛthivīṃ viddhi māṃ rājan saptadvīpairalaṃkṛtām  |
saptapātālalokaiśca saśailavanakānanām  || 37.47 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāmamodaranivāsinaḥ  |
vyāptaṃ mayā jagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 37.48 ||
paryaṅkaśāyinaṃ devaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  |
jagadyoniṃ hariṃ viddhi saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam  || 37.49 ||
trinetraṃ śūlapāṇiṃ ca viddhi devaṃ maheśvaram  |
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ mahādevaṃ lokanāthaṃ jagatpatim  || 37.50 ||
utsaṅgasthaṃ śiśuṃ viddhi brahmāṇaṃ sṛṣṭikārakam  |
tribhirvyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraiḥ  || 37.51 ||
jagatsṛṣṭikaro brahmā svayaṃ pālayitā hariḥ  |
saṃhārakārako rudrastribhirvyāptaṃ jagattataḥ  || 37.52 ||
kalaśāṃścaturo viddhi samudrān parvatasthitān  |
stanamadhye gatāṃ kanyāṃ narmadāṃ viddhipāvanīm  || 37.53 ||
etacchrutvā vaco rājā vismayaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  |
tataḥ kālena mahatā punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  || 37.54 ||
sarvalokahitārthāya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
kalpe kalpe mayā dṛṣṭametatsarvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 37.55 ||
nyagrodhaḥ kalpavṛkṣaśca oṃkāraḥ saptakalpagā  |
ahaṃ vaidūryaśailaśca paraliṅgāni bhārata  || 37.56 ||
akṣayaṃ vā'vyayaṃ caitatsarvaṃ jānīhi tattvataḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ tvayā'nagha  || 37.57 ||
bhaktastvaṃ śravaṇe śraddhā purāṇānāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 37.58 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kalpāntadarśanaṃ nāma saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 38

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
īdṛśaṃ yasya māhātmyaṃ tīrthasya ca paraṃtapa  |
tṛptiṃ naivādhigacchāmi śṛṇvan vedavidāṃ vara  || 38.1 ||
samarthaiḥ sarvakāryeṣu brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
bhūyastīrthasya māhātmyaṃ nivedaya mahāmate  || 38.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
asti tīrthaṃ mahārāja liṅgamanyaṃ yameśvaram  |
darśanātpūjanādasya yamalokaṃ na paśyati  || 38.3 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe tīre kapilāyāśca paścime  |
viṣṇostatra purī ramyā śakrasyevāmarāvatī  || 38.4 ||
kalpakoṭiyugaṃ cāpi tasyāṃ tiṣṭhati mādhavaḥ  |
purā devāsure yuddhe dānavairdevakaṇṭakaiḥ  || 38.5 ||
nirjitā devatāḥ sarvāstadāsīcca dharārditā  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvā kṣīrodarṇavaśāyinam  || 38.6 ||
tuṣṭuvuḥ praṇipatyainaṃ stutyaṃ cāntaḥsthamavyayam  |
jaya deva jagannātha daityāntaka janārdana  || 38.7 ||
vārāharūpamāsthāya proddhṛtā vasudhā tvayā  |
uddhṛtā matsyarūpeṇa vedāḥ sāgaramadhyataḥ  || 38.8 ||
kūrmaśeṣādirūpaiśca devānāmārtināśanam  |
balirvāmanarūpeṇa tvayā baddho'surāriṇā  || 38.9 ||
hiraṇyakaśipurdaityaḥ sarvalokabhayaṃkaraḥ  |
tvayā nṛsiṃharūpeṇa nihato devakaṇṭakaḥ  || 38.10 ||
vedamūla jagannātha rakṣa naḥ śaraṇāgatān  |
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devo brahmāṇaṃ vākyamabravīt || 38.11 ||
kimarthaṃ ca tvayā brahman kṣīrode'haṃ prabodhita  || 38.12 ||

brahmovāca - 
tvayā vinā jagannātha kṣoṇī channā surāribhiḥ  || 38.13 ||
sūryeṇa ca vihīnaṃ hi nirālokaṃ jagadyathā  || 38.14 ||

viṣṇuruvāca - 
brahman bhayaṃ tyajāśūtvaṃ dānavo'yaṃ durāsadaḥ  |
acireṇaiva kālena ghātayāmi ca dānavam  || 38.15 ||
evamuktvā suraiḥ sārddhamājagāma janārdanaḥ  |
sudarśanena cakreṇa dānavānāṃ śirāṃsi ca  || 38.16 ||
cakarta bhagavān viṣṇuḥ sarvalokahitāya vai  |
bhayārtā dānavāḥ sarve palāyanaparāyaṇāḥ  || 38.17 ||
viṣṇostrāsāttu te sarve rasātalatalaṃ yayuḥ  |
punaḥ pravartito yajño dvijadevatapasvinām  || 38.18 ||
tasyāṃ puryāṃ mahābhāga devo'bhūddaityasūdanaḥ  |
tatra yastyajati prāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  || 38.19 ||
vaiṣṇavena vimānena viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
kapilā paścimāyāṃ tu nīlagaṅgāsamāgatā  || 38.20 ||
aṣṭāyutāni tīrthāni viṣṇunā preritāni vai  |
cakratīrthaṃ vāmanaṃ ca koṭitīrthaṃ yugaṃdharam  || 38.21 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājan koṭitīrthaphalaṃ labhet |
sudarśanaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ daityasūdanameva ca  || 38.22 ||
viṣṇvāvartaṃ śivāvartaṃ lakṣmyāvartaṃ tathaiva ca  |
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 38.23 ||
viṣṇostu prīṇanaṃ kṛtvā anantaphalamaśnute  |
krośamātrapramāṇaṃ hi viṣṇukṣetraṃ prakīrtitam  || 38.24 ||
na viśedbrahmahatyā ca satyametacchivoditam  |
māsopavāsaṃ yaḥ kuryādagnihotraṃ tathaiva ca  || 38.25 ||
pativratā tvaṃ satyaṃ ca svādhyāyaṃ yajñakarma ca  |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parākaṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ca tilodakam  || 38.26 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti loke krīḍanti vaiṣṇave  |
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ tathā  || 38.27 ||
atikṛcchraṃ parṇakṛcchraṃ tathā'nyadvaiṣṇavaṃ vratam  |
ekādaśyāṃ na bhuṅkte yaḥ pakṣayorubhayorapi  || 38.28 ||
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ yatrāste bhagavān hariḥ  |
anyakṣetrātkoṭiguṇaṃ phalaṃ vai keśavo'bravīt || 38.29 ||
tatra japtaṃ tapastaptaṃ sarvaṃ bhavatu cākṣayam  |
śravaṇe dvādaśī puṇyā rohiṇyāmaṣṭamī śubhā  || 38.30 ||
tatropoṣya mahārāja viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
cāṇḍālaḥ śvapaco vā'pi tiryagyonigato'pi vā  || 38.31 ||
atra yastyajati prāṇān viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  |
kārttike caiva vaiśākha upavāsaṃ karoti yaḥ  || 38.32 ||
daśakoṭyupavāsānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
śaṅkareṇa purā gītaṃ purāṇe skandakīrtite  || 38.33 ||
māsopavāsino dṛṣṭvā tatraiva ca pativratāḥ  |
dharmarājaḥ svayaṃ gatvā arghaṃ tebhyo nivedayet || 38.34 ||
ṣaṣṭiyānasahasraistu āgato bhagavān prabhuḥ  |
sahasrayojanaṃ yāvadāyāti bhagavān yamaḥ  || 38.35 ||
śuklavastraparīdhāno dharmatejo vapurdharaḥ  |
sthitvā yānasya caivāgre bhaktyā pādau ca vandate  || 38.36 ||
pradṛśya vaiṣṇavaṃ lokaṃ tato gatvā nivartate  |
pratyāgateṣu yāneṣu apṛcchan svecchayā yamam  || 38.37 ||
brahmaṇo mānasāḥ putrāḥ sapta caiva maharṣayaḥ  |
kasmācca kāraṇāddharma pādacārī gataḥ svayam  || 38.38 ||
kathayasva mahābhāga paraṃ vismayamāgatāḥ  |
prahasya cābravīddharmaḥ śṛṇvantu munisattamāḥ  || 38.39 ||
pativratānāṃ dīptiṃ ca dīptiṃ māsopavāsinām  |
aśaktā draṣṭumetāsāṃ vimānān dūrataḥ sthitān  || 38.40 ||
tābhyaḥ pratyāgatāḥ sarve mama bhṛtyā bhayaṃkarāḥ  |
etasmātkāraṇādviprāḥ pādacārī gato hyaham  || 38.41 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
māhātmyāccāmarāvatyāṃ hareramitatejasaḥ  |
vāsaśca yajanaṃ tatra kīrtitaṃ kalināśanam  || 38.42 ||
athānyaḥ paramo devo brahmeśa iti viśrutaḥ  |
revāyā uttare kūle saṃsthitaḥ pāpanāśanaḥ  || 38.43 ||
arcanāttasya devasya naśyetaino yudhiṣṭhira  |
pitarastṛptimāyānti brahmalokamavāpnuyuḥ  || 38.44 ||
laṅkeśvarāddakṣiṇataḥ siddhaliṅgaṃ prakīrtitam  |
arcanātsparśanāttasya gāṇapatyamavāpyate  || 38.45 ||
viśveśvaraṃ mahāliṅgaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubham  |
śuklāṣṭamyāṃ ca vaiśākhe liṅgāyutaphalaṃ labhet || 38.46 ||
arcanāttasya liṅgasya śivasyānucaro bhavet |
tato gacchenmahārāja revāyā uttare taṭe  || 38.47 ||
pāpapraṇāśanaṃ caiva liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  |
arcanāttarpaṇātsnānādbrahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 38.48 ||
ṛṇānāṃ mocanaṃ caiva liṅgaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
anekabhāvikaṃ caiva ṛṇaṃ tasya praṇaśyati  || 38.49 ||
tilodakapradānena ṛṇamocanadarśanāt |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvaccandrārkatārakam  || 38.50 ||
athānyatparamaṃ liṅgaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
dāruvane siddhaliṅgaṃ kathaṃ paśyanti kaśmalāḥ  || 38.51 ||
narmadācarusambhede snānaṃ kṛtvā yathoditam  |
snāpayitvodakeneśaṃ bilvapatraiśca pūjanāt || 38.52 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirumāmāheśvarātpurāt || 38.53 ||
catuṣkeśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgamāmalakyāḥ pramāṇataḥ  |
arcitaṃ vai surairdaityairna taṃ paśyanti mānavāḥ  || 38.54 ||
karotyatra tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadāhūta saṃplavam  || 38.55 ||
śākalyasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ yojanāyutavistaram  |
yajñaparvatamāsādya niḥsṛtā carukā nadī  || 38.56 ||
śakreṇeṣṭaṃ purā tatra bṛhaspatipurodhasā  |
etatpuṇyatamaṃ loke gīyate sarvadaivataiḥ  || 38.57 ||
candrasūryoparāge tu yatphalaṃ kurujāṅgale  |
revācarukasaṃyoge tatphalaṃ śivakīrtitam  || 38.58 ||
tatrākhyātaṃ dāruvanaṃ bhuvi sarvairniṣevitam  |
munīnāṃ ṣaṣṭisāhasraistretātapasi saṃsthitaiḥ  || 38.59 ||
kandamūlaphalāhārairagnihotraparāyaṇaiḥ  |
etattīrthaprabhāveṇa gatāste brahmaṇaḥ purīm  || 38.60 ||
nihatya cāndhakaṃ tatra nanarta bhagavāñchiva  |
gaṇānāṃ nāyakāḥ sarve nānāvādyānavādayan  || 38.61 ||
tatra nṛtyati deveśaḥ sarvā nṛtyanti devatāḥ  |
tena dāruvanaṃ khyātaṃ liṅgaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  || 38.62 ||
arcanāttasya devasya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
vimaleśvaraṃ tatra liṅgaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubham  || 38.63 ||
snānāttatra divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
nirmalīkṛtadehāstu kṛtāstatra surāsurāḥ  || 38.64 ||
pinākinā mahārāja nītāḥ sarve triviṣṭapam  |
tilodakaiḥ piṇḍadānaiḥ pitṝn prīṇāti yo naraḥ  || 38.65 ||
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
candrasūryagrahe prāpte dānasaṃkhyā na vidyate  || 38.66 ||
śivamabhyarcya tatraiva gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
vimaleśvaraṃ tatra liṅgaṃ svayaṃ viddhi maheśvaram  || 38.67 ||
vyāghreśvaraṃ yatra liṅgaṃ vyāghrī yatra divaṃ gatā  |
tilodakapradānena haviṣā piṇḍadānataḥ  || 38.68 ||
pitṝnuddharate putraḥ śatapūrvāṃstathā parān  |
vasetsa vāruṇe loke yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 38.69 ||
arcanāttasya devasya kīrtanācchravaṇādapi  |
indralokamavāpnoti satyametanmayoditam  || 38.70 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe cakrasvāmivarṇano nāmāṣṭatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 39

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
liṅgaṃ vyāghreśvaraṃ nāma vighnakartṛsamāgame  |
kathitaṃ yanmahābhāga sarvapāpavimokṣaṇam  || 39.1 ||
prabhāvaṃ vedituṃ tasya punaricchāmyahaṃ mune  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  || 39.2 ||
babhrurnāma purā cāsīdrājā paramadhārmikaḥ  |
puṇḍravarddhanavāsī ca satyadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 39.3 ||
pituḥ kṣayāhe samprāpte śrāddhakāle susaṃyataḥ  |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ karma pitrarthaṃ sa cakāra ha  || 39.4 ||
yajñakāle nivṛtte'tha buddhiṃ prāpya tu bhārata  |
cintayāmāsa rājāsau kiṃkaromītī cābravīt || 39.5 ||
ṛṣayo nirapekṣāśāḥ kandamūlaphalāśinaḥ  |
viprātithiṃ na paśyāmi kaṃ bhojayitumutsahe  || 39.6 ||
saṃcintya viprabhojyārthaṃ pākamapsu vyanikṣipat |
ṛgvedaśca tato vākyaṃ kopātprāha tatastadā  || 39.7 ||
idānīṃ dvāparasyānte vartate pāpasaṃbhavaḥ  |
vaivasvate'ntare prāpte brāhmaṇā labdhakāmukāḥ  || 39.8 ||
te bhuñjan kāmukāḥ sarve rājadravyaṃ sakilbiṣam  |
deśe kadācitpuṇyāhe bhāradvājāśramaṃgataḥ  || 39.9 ||
so'bhivādya namaskṛtya muniṃ tatra vyavasthitaḥ  |
bhāradvājo'bravīdvākyaṃ tasmai dattvāsanaṃ mahat || 39.10 ||
svāgataṃ te mahārāja kuśalaṃ saha bhāryayā  |
ciraṃ dṛṣṭo'si dharmajña ekākī tvaṃ kathaṃcana  || 39.11 ||

rājovāca - 
adya me kuśalaṃ brahmaṃstvatpādāmbujadarśanāt |
maharṣe śrāddhakālo'yāṃ pitṝṇāṃ mama vartate  || 39.12 ||
bhojaye'haṃ yathā vipraṃ tathā tvaṃ kartumarhasi  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā bhāradvājo'bravīdvacaḥ  || 39.13 ||
maharṣīṇāṃ sahasrāṇi tuṣṭānāmūrdhvaretasām  |
haṃsalomaśamukhyānāṃ yadi bhojayituṃ kṣamaḥ  || 39.14 ||
paścātpravartate śrāddhaṃ satyametadbravīmyaham  |
dadyā hemamayaṃ pīṭhaṃ dadyā dhānyaṃ tathā vasu  || 39.15 ||
madhudhenuṃ tathā divyāṃ payodhenuṃ tathaiva ca  |
agniśaucasya kaupīnaṃ vastrasya tu viśāmpate  || 39.16 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā bhāradvājasya bhūpatiḥ  |
evamastviti taṃ coktvā sarvaṃ patnyai nyavedayat || 39.17 ||
mahokṣaṃ kalpayitvā pitṛkāryaṃ nivartaye  |
śrautasmārtādikaṃ karma āmiṣaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam  || 39.18 ||
ityādismṛtivākyāni paryālocya punaḥ punaḥ  |
mahokṣaṃ ca tato hatvā tarpayāmāsa tān pitṝn  || 39.19 ||
anaḍvāhaṃ paśuṃ jñātvā bhāradvājādayastataḥ  |
kupitāḥ karmaṇā tena rājñīṃ rājānameva ca  || 39.20 ||
kopasaṃbhārasaṃtaptā garhayanto'bruvaṃstadā  |
surāmāṃsādimādhvīkaṃ kṛtvā gomāṃsabhakṣaṇam  || 39.21 ||
taptakṛcchraṃ caredvipro mauñjībandhena śūddhyati  |
abhakṣabhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā karīṣāgnau viśeddvijaḥ  || 39.22 ||
ghātitāni tvayā cāṣṭau sahasrāṇi tapasvinām  |
havyaṃ devā na gṛhṇanti kathaṃ ca pitaraḥ punaḥ  || 39.23 ||
tvayā vai kratavaśceṣṭāste gatā vai śvayoniṣu  |
vyarthamāsa tapaste'dya mūlanāśastvayā kṛtaḥ  || 39.24 ||

babhruruvāca - 
vedoktaṃ yaḥ parityajya dharmamanyaṃ samācaret |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi śvayonau jāyate dhruvam  || 39.25 ||
vedārthanindakā ye vai brāhmaṇā jñānadurbalāḥ  |
iha janmani śūdrāste mṛtāḥ śvāno bhavanti te  || 39.26 ||
gomedho hayamedhaśca naramedhastathā'paraḥ  |
kṣatriyāstvatha jīvanti pūjyante devamānuṣaiḥ  || 39.27 ||
rājñastadvacanaṃ śrutvā bhāradvājo'śapatnṛpam  |
yasmānmahokṣamāṃsena tārpitā brāhmaṇāstvayā  || 39.28 ||
bhavatu anena pāpena vyāghro vyāghrī tava priyā  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu vasudhāṃ vicariṣyathaḥ  || 39.29 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā muneramitatejasaḥ  |
sudharmā cābravīdvākyaṃ rājapatnī yaśasvinī  || 39.30 ||
vedoktakarmakartā tu yataḥ śaptaḥ patirmama  |
pativratā ca nirdoṣā dharmiṣṭhā'haṃ tathaiva ca  || 39.31 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi tena pāpena duḥkhitāḥ  |
bhaviṣyatha mahāghorā yūyaṃ vai brahmarākṣasāḥ  || 39.32 ||
nirjale marudeśe ca apare kaṇṭakāvṛte  |
kṣudhārtāśca tṛṣārtāśca kākagṛdhrairanudrutāḥ  || 39.33 ||
bhāradvājaśca rājñī ca śaptvā tatra parasparam  |
bhāradvājaśca sārddhaṃ taiḥ kṛtvā puṇyaṃ tato nṛpa  || 39.34 ||
brahmalokaṃ jagāmātha brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhārata  |
so'bhivādya yathā nyāyaṃ brahmāṇaṃ surasattamam  || 39.35 ||
sarvaṃ nyavedayattatrābhakṣabhojyaṃ ca yatkṛtam  |

brahmovāca - 
lokāpacaritaṃ karma [] kāryaṃ devavarjitam  || 39.36 || [ṅapin Original]
uktāni ca niṣiddhāni dharmādharmavicāraṇe  |
lokoktaṃ vihitaṃ karma trayī mārgaṃ vicāritam  || 39.37 ||
kartavyaṃ tu prayatnena svargamokṣapradāyakam  |
bodhitā brāhmaṇāstena brahmaṇā nṛpatistadā  || 39.38 ||
śāpāntaḥ kathitasteṣāṃ tenaiva parameṣṭhinā  |
śāpasyānto mayā dṛṣṭo bhavatāṃ pāpakarmiṇām  || 39.39 ||
puṇyakṣaye ca śaptāstu samprāpte tu yugāntare  |
narmadātīravāsī yo mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ  || 39.40 ||
sa dāsyatyupadeśaṃ vo yena mokṣamavāpsyatha  |
tataḥ kālena mahatā mārkaṇḍeyaṃ muniṃ gatāḥ  || 39.41 ||
daivayogena te sarve kalpagā tīravāsinam  |
abhivādya yathā nyāyaṃ tamṛṣiṃ praṇipatya ca  || 39.42 ||
sthitāḥ svakarma tatraiva vyāharantaḥ parasparam  |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃścintayāmāsa mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ  || 39.43 ||
mithunaṃ vyāghrarūpeṇa vyāharanmānuṣīṃ giram  |
kena karmavipākena brāhmaṇā brahmarākṣasāḥ  || 39.44 ||
evaṃ saṃcintya mārkaṇḍo bhāradvājamathābravīt |
dampatī vyāghrarūpeṇa brāhmaṇā brahmarākṣasāḥ  || 39.45 ||
yūyaṃ ca vikṛtākārāḥ kathayadhvaṃ kathaṃ ca tat |

bhāradvāja uvāca - 
śṛṇu brahman samāsena kathyamānaṃ vaco mama  || 39.46 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajña trikālajña trivedavit |
babhrurnāmā cakravartī puṇḍravardhanasaṃsthitaḥ  || 39.47 ||
sudharmā nāma tasyaiva dharmapatnī pativratā  |
ekadā śrāddhakāle tu nātithiṃ prāptavān dvijam  || 39.48 ||
saṃcintyātha tadātmānamāgato hyāśramaṃ mama  |
pādau jagrāha śirasā tato vacanamabravīt || 39.49 ||
pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhakālo me prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  |
tato mayopadiṣṭo'sau munīṃstvaṃ bhojayādhunā  || 39.50 ||
mahokṣaṃ śātayitvā tu bhojitāste tapodhanāḥ  |
śapto mayā ca śīghraṃ tvaṃ vyāghrayoniṃ gamiṣyasi  || 39.51 ||
nirbhartsya māṃ ca tatpatnī śaśāpa brahmarākṣasāḥ  |
yūyaṃ bhavata vai sarve marudeśe kṣudhārditāḥ  || 39.52 ||
brahmāṇaṃ ca tataḥ prāptā vivādena parasparam  |
brahmaṇā preṣitāste'mī tvatsakāśamihāgatāḥ  || 39.53 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kāraṇaṃ vikṛte mahat |
nānyatsthānaṃ prapaśyāmo yatrāsmākaṃ sthitirbhavet || 39.54 ||
na divā naiva rātriśca na sūryo na ca candramāḥ  |
na grahā naiva ṛkṣāṇi ṛṣayo yatra maṇḍalam  || 39.55 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailā bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
āsīdidaṃ tamo bhūtamādi madhyāntavārjitam  || 39.56 ||
mugdhā vayaṃ na jānīmo na cājñāyata kiṃcana  |
nyagrodhaṃ narmadāmadhye meghābhaṃ kālarūpiṇam  || 39.57 ||
tvāṃ vai sarvadvijaśreṣṭhaṃ nānya paśyāmi kiṃcana  |
tvaṃ pitā no guruścaiva harirdhātā svayaṃ śivaḥ  || 39.58 ||
akāmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ svayametadupārjitam  |
tvatprasādātmuniśreṣṭha muñcāmo ghorakilbiṣāt || 39.59 ||
nābhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma kalpakoṭiśatairapi  |
avaśyameva bhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśabham  || 39.60 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śākalyasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ brahmarṣigaṇasevitam  |
vaidūryātpaścime bhāge yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 39.61 ||
tatra gatvā ca yūyaṃ vai tapaścograṃ kariṣyatha  |
japadhyānaparā nityaṃ kandamūlaphalāśinaḥ  || 39.62 ||
evaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi saṃtiṣṭhadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
yāvadvai vedamānena punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  || 39.63 ||
tamo bhūtamidaṃ sarvaṃ na prājñāyata kiṃcana  |
sarvaṃ cakāra bhagavānmahākālavapurdharaḥ  || 39.64 ||
prāṇināṃ karmaṇā hyetajjagatkālavaśaṅgatam  |
vāyuragnirjalaṃ pṛthvī bhūtagrāmaścaturvidhaḥ  || 39.65 ||
kālasya bhojanaṃ sarvaṃ bhasmapuñjamivābhavat |
nāścaryamatra kartavyaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati  || 39.66 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sarve te pāpayonayaḥ  |
namaskṛtya muniśreṣṭhaṃ mārkaṇḍaṃ kalpavāsinam  || 39.67 ||
rājā ca rājapatnī ca ṛṣayo vimaleśvaram  |
jagmuḥ kālena mahatā punaḥsṛṣṭiḥ pravartitā  || 39.68 ||
svayaṃ prajāpatirbrahmā sasarja vividhāḥ prajāḥ  |
kalāḥ kāṣṭhā muhūrtāśca jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam  || 39.69 ||
saritaḥ sāgarān gulmān parvatāṃ ca latāstathā  |
āpo'gniṃ vai tathā vāyuṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  || 39.70 ||
sūryaṃ ca dakṣiṇe netre so'sṛjatprāṇinastathā  |
vāmanetre himāṃśuṃ ca sa tathā paryakalpayat || 39.71 ||
etadṛkṣavaraiḥ sārddhaṃ pavitraṃ dhruvamaṇḍalam  |
vivasvataḥ prabhāṃ caiva sarvalokaprakāśinīm  || 39.72 ||
sṛṣṭā cāndramasī jyotsnā divā rātrī tathaiva ca  |
apareṣu ca dvīpeṣu na sūryo naiva candramāḥ  || 39.73 ||
raudrī prabhā'sti tatraivaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ pravartitā  |
pūrṇe tu dvādaśe varṣe śāpānte samupasthite  || 39.74 ||
arcayitvā maheśānaṃ revāvyāghrasamāgame  |
bhāradvājādayaḥ sarve brahmalokamavāpnuyuḥ  || 39.75 ||
kāmikaṃ yānamāruhya rājā muniyutastadā  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa umāmāheśvare pure  || 39.76 ||
vyāghreśvaro vyāghrakartṛsaṅgame tridaśārcitaḥ  |
anyalliṅgaṃ na paśyāmi divyaṃ vai vimaleśvarāt || 39.77 ||
arcanāttasya liṅgasya darśanātsparśanāttathā  |
brahmahatyā praṇaśyeta pāpeṣvanyeṣu kā kathā  || 39.78 ||
tīrtheṣu śrāddhakaraṇātpitṝṇāmakṣayā gatiḥ  |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi kalpakoṭiśatāni ca  || 39.79 ||
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa loke krīḍanti vaiṣṇave  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam  || 39.80 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 39.81 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vimaleśvaravarṇano nāmaikonacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 40

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
manasā cintitaṃ yacca tadeva samupasthitam  |
lakṣaṇaṃ sūtrayāgasya parvatasya tu veṣṭanam  || 40.1 ||
kasmin kāle prakartavyaṃ vidhānāṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
tvameva vetsi sarvaṃ vai viditaṃ kuru sāmpratam  || 40.2 ||
yena devāḥ sagandharvā mānuṣāḥ pāpayonayaḥ  |
tatra yāgaprabhāveṇa divi devatvamāgatāḥ  || 40.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrūyatāṃ rājaśārdūla sūtrayāgasya lakṣaṇam  |
candrasūryoparāge tu ṣaḍaśītimukhe tathā  || 40.4 ||
yugādau viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  |
kārttikyāṃ ca tathā māghyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ caiva bhārata  || 40.5 ||
sūryoparāgatulyāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ hi kapilasya tu  || 40.6 ||
vaiśākhamāsasya tu yā tṛtīyā navamyathorjasya ca śuklapakṣe  |
māghe tvamā caiva nabhasyakṛṣṇā trayodaśī ceti yugādayastathā  || 40.7 ||
parvasveteṣu kaunteya sūtrayāgaṃ tu kārayet |
anantaphalasaṃyuktaṃ śivena kathitaṃ purā  || 40.8 ||
sūtreṇa veṣṭayedyastu parvataṃ śivasannibham  |
umayā sahitaṃ devaṃ gaṇeśena tathaiva ca  || 40.9 ||
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  |
putriṇī bhartṛsaṃyuktā nārī tadveṣṭinī bhavet || 40.10 ||
kauśeyaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ ca kārpāsaṃ ca mahīpate  |
navatantuṃ ca yaḥ kuryāddaśa dvādaśatantukam  || 40.11 ||
aṣṭādaśa mahārāja caturviṃśati vā kramāt |
kṣālayetkoṭitīrthe tu gandhadhūpena vāsayet || 40.12 ||
badhnīyātpuṣpamālāṃ ca dīpamālāṃ ca bodhayet |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā oṃkāre vidhipūrvakam  || 40.13 ||
śivadhyānaparo bhūtvā nirāhāro niśāṃ nayet |
prabhāte cotsavaṃ kuryādoṃkārābhyarcanaṃ tathā  || 40.14 ||
nyagrodhe bandhayetsūtraṃ samādhistho nareśvara  |
koṭitīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthamayaṃ śubham  || 40.15 ||
ṛṇamokṣaṃ pāpanāśaṃ narakeśvaramuttamam  |
gandharveśvaraliṅgaṃ tu gacchetparamaśobhanam  || 40.16 ||
adṛśyaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ nāgakanyābhirarcyate  |
tatra snātvā naro rājan gāndharvaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 40.17 ||
tato gacchennṛpaśreṣṭha aṅgāreśvaramuttamam  |
darśanāttasya liṅgasya gāṇapatyamavāpyate  || 40.18 ||
aṅgāreṇa tapastaptaṃ divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ tathā  |
grahatvamagamatso'pi tīrthasya'sya prabhāvataḥ  || 40.19 ||
brahmāvartaṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthamayaṃ śubham  |
tatra snātvā naro rājan chivalokamavāpnuyāt || 40.20 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tapastepe suduṣkaram  || 40.21 ||
brahmā caiva purākalpe lokānugrahakārakaḥ  |
liṅgaṃ madhyeśvaraṃ nāma jalamadhye vyavasthitam  || 40.22 ||
pūjyate nāgakanyābhirna tatpaśyanti mānavāḥ  |
dārukeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 40.23 ||
arcanāttasya devasya naro vidyādharo bhavet |
bhṛguliṅgaṃ tato gacchedbhairavo yatra saṃsthitaḥ  || 40.24 ||
bhṛguṃ gatvā naraśreṣṭha mucyate brahmahatyayā  |
jāleśvaraṃ tato gacchetparvatīrthottamottamam  || 40.25 ||
snānamātro narastatra sa bhave na punarbhavet |
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāmakṣayā gatiḥ  || 40.26 ||
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsthāya bāṇasya ca puratrayam  |
pātitaṃ jalamadhye tu narmadāyā hareṇa vai  || 40.27 ||
jvalatpāśupatāstreṇa tatraiva tadanantaram  |
tadastrasaṅgamācchīghraṃ pātālāccotthitaṃ tataḥ  || 40.28 ||
liṅgaṃ jāleśvaraṃ nāma brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  |
koṭitīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnātvā ca vidhipūrvakam  || 40.29 ||
valakṣaṃ bandhayetsūtramoṃkārasya tu bhārata  |
oṃkāraṃ ca samabhyarcya dīpamālāṃ ca bodhayet || 40.30 ||
saphalaḥ sūtrayāgastu tvatprasādānmaheśvara  |
yatīṃśca bhojayettatra dadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām  || 40.31 ||
sārddhaṃ ca bandhubhṛtyaiśca pāraṇaṃ kriyate nṛpa  |
yaḥ śārīreṇa kaṣṭena paryaṭecchivaparvatam  || 40.32 ||
pade pade yajñaphala tasya syācchaṅkaro'bravīt |
purā devagaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ siddhagandharvakinnaraiḥ  || 40.33 ||
vidyādharaistathā yakṣairasurairdaityadānavaiḥ  |
candrādityagrahaiścaiva nakṣatradhruvamaṇḍalaiḥ  || 40.34 ||
viśvedevaiśca sādhyaiśca marudbhirvasubhistathā  |
devarājena cendrāṇyā sāvitryā caiva bhārata  || 40.35 ||
arundhatyā sarasvatyā gāyatryā sa ca parvataḥ  |
sūtreṇa veṣṭito bhaktyā phalaṃ prāptaṃ śivoditam  || 40.36 ||
ahalyā menakā rambhā ghṛtācī corvaśī tathā  |
tilottamā tathā cānyā saritaḥ sāgarāśca vai  || 40.37 ||
veṣṭanātparvatasyāsya digdevatvamavāptavān  |
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca yāvattiṣṭhati medinī  || 40.38 ||
sūtrayāgaprabhāveṇa tāvacchivapure vaset |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan sūtrayāgasya yatphalam  || 40.39 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya rudrasyānucaro bhavet || 40.40 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe sūtrayāgavarṇano nāma catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 41

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kīrtanaṃ koṭitīrthasya mānasaṃkhyāpramāṇataḥ  |
tīrthāni cottare yāmye prākāre saṃsthitāni ca  || 41.1 ||
kathayasva prasādena tāni me munisattama  |
teṣāṃ tu darśanādeva pāpaṃ duṣkṛtakarmiṇām  || 41.2 ||
praṇaśyettatkṣaṇādeva tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
uttare yāni tīrthāni yāni tīrthāni dakṣiṇe  || 41.3 ||
līyante koṭitīrthe tu sākṣācchivapade nṛpa  |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni āsamudrāntagocare  || 41.4 ||
tāni sākṣātkoṭitīrthe līyante parame pade  |
kapilā'marayormadhye narmadoṃkāramadhyataḥ  || 41.5 ||
atrāntare nṛpaśreṣṭha koṭitīrthaṃ vyavasthitam  |
daśāyutāni tīrthānāṃ pātālatalavāsinām  || 41.6 ||
kapilānarmadāmadhye śivena sthāpitāni ca  |
atrāntare mahārāja rudrāvartajalena tu  || 41.7 ||
snātvā vidhānataste vai gacchanti śivamandiram  |
ye vasantyuttare kūle rudraloke vasanti te  || 41.8 ||
vasanti vāmabhāge vai te lokaṃ yānti vaiṣṇavam  |
ye pūrvapaścime bhāge vasantyamarakaṇṭake  || 41.9 ||
rudrasya brahmaṇo lokaṃ te prayānti ca vaiṣṇavam  |
jale caikādaśāgnau tu patane ṣoḍaśaiva tu  || 41.10 ||
sahasrāṇi tathāśītirmaraṇādgogṛhe tathā  |
anaśane brahmalokaḥ sārddhaṃ lakṣamudāhṛtaḥ  || 41.11 ||
kapilānarmadātoye jalāgnī sādhayanti ye  |
caturyugasahasrāṇi rudraloke vasanti te  || 41.12 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi mṛgapakṣisarīsṛpāḥ  |
tiryagyonigatāḥ pāpā mriyante ye yudhiṣṭhira  || 41.13 ||
rājānaste prajāyante śubhe vaidyādhare pure  |
rāhusomasamāyoge koṭitīrthe narādhipa  || 41.14 ||
puṇyaṃ yatkīrtitaṃ puṃsāṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
gayāyāṃ ca kurukṣetre puṣkare'marakaṇṭake  || 41.15 ||
tulyaṃ phalamavāpnoti rāhugraste divākare  |
prācī sarasvatī yatra sthāṇuḥ sārasvataṃ jalam  || 41.16 ||
sārddhaṃ dvādaśa lakṣaṃ tu dattaṃ tatra pravartate  |
tattulyaṃ tu bhavetsarvaṃ koṭitīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 41.17 ||
nyāyopārjitavittasya rāhugraste divākare  |
nānāvidhaṃ tu puṇyaṃ ca mataṃ vai parikīrtitam  || 41.18 ||
brahmaviṣṇuśivoktaṃ ca pramāṇa sarvasammatam  |
alpasattvā narā rājan kāle māyāvimohitāḥ  || 41.19 ||
oṃkāraṃ ye na paśyanti tathā vai saptakalpagām  |
gochāgayormahārāja hemarūpye yadantaram  || 41.20 ||
śūdrabrāhmaṇayoryadvadyathā vaddadhitakrayoḥ  |
amareśvaratīrthaṃ hi jñeyaṃ tīrthāntaraiḥ saha  || 41.21 ||
dīyate koṭitīrthe yadguñjāmātraṃ hiraṇyakam  |
tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate yāvadāhūta saṃplavam  || 41.22 ||
caturhastapramāṇaṃ tu koṭitīrthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
hastamātraṃ tathā cānye vitastiṃ ca tathā'pare  || 41.23 ||
caturaṅgulamayaṃ ke'pi kecidaṅgulamānataḥ  |
arddhāṅgulaṃ yavamātraṃ brahmasūtrapramāṇataḥ  || 41.24 ||
caturviṃśe dvādaśābde kurukṣetrātsarasvatī  |
koṭitīrthe tathā snātuṃ rāhugraste divākare  || 41.25 ||
kariṇīrūpamāsthāya āyātsvapuṇyakakṣayāt |
snānaṃ kṛtvā punaryāti kurukṣetraṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 41.26 ||
kāverīsaṅgamaṃ yāvadārabhyo dadhisaṅgamam  |
atrāntare mahārāja tīrthakoṭyo daśa smṛtāḥ  || 41.27 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ samārabhya nīlagaṅgāvasānataḥ  |
aṣṭalakṣāṇi tīrthānāṃ brahmasūtrapramāṇataḥ  || 41.28 ||
kāveryāḥ pūrvabhāge ca yāvatparyaṅkaparvataḥ  |
daśa lakṣāṇi tīrthānāṃ saṃkhyā ca kathitā tava  || 41.29 ||
narmadāyāṃ samāsādya jamadagnermahāśramam  |
śrīkaṇṭhaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ ca liṅgaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  || 41.30 ||
kanyātīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ kapileśvaramuttamam  |
kapilāvartasaṃjñaṃ tu tīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ param  || 41.31 ||
tatra pūṣā ca sūryastu śivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tapastepe suduṣkaram  || 41.32 ||
tatastuṣṭaḥ sureśānastamuvāca tadā nṛpa  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  || 41.33 ||

pūṣovāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  |
sūryapiṅgalasaṃjñaṃ tu liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  || 41.34 ||
ākāśe pratapadraśmisahasrāṃśu samaprabham  |
māse māse'nyamitrastu saṃkramaścānyarāśiṣu  || 41.35 ||
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye jagaddyotanakārakam  |

īśvara uvāca - 
sarvasampatsamṛddhiste matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 41.36 ||
kṛte yuge mahārāja mayaitaddṛṣṭameva ca  |
caturdaśyāṃ tathā'ṣṭamyāṃ piṅgaleśvarapūjanāt || 41.37 ||
koṭiyajñaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate  |
revātīreṣu ye devāḥ krośamātraṃ svayaṃbhuvaḥ  || 41.38 ||
sarve te siddhidā jñeyāḥ kāmabhogaphalapradāḥ  |
kecitkūṣmāṇḍamātrā vai kecidvai puṣpamātrakāḥ  || 41.39 ||
eraṇḍaphalamātrāśca vajramauktikamānataḥ  |
kṛte maṇimayāḥ proktāstretāyāṃ tu hiraṇmayāḥ  || 41.40 ||
dvāpare raupyatāmrāśca kalau cāśmamayāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
brāhmaṃ kṛtayugaṃ prāhustretāṃ vai kṣatriyaṃ tathā  || 41.41 ||
dvāparaṃ vaiśyamityevaṃ kaliṃ śūdraṃ tathaiva ca  |
nāpuṇyāliṅgamāsādya mriyate'marakaṇṭake  || 41.42 ||
ko na mucyeta kaunteya saptajanmajakilbiṣāt |
yādṛśo'yaṃ giriḥ puṇyaḥ sarvato'marakaṇṭakaḥ  || 41.43 ||
tādṛśaṃ nānupaśyāmi triṣu lokeṣu bhārata  |
parvatasya samantāttu tīrthakoṭirvyavasthitā  || 41.44 ||
svargasopānamāsādya prātyakṣaṃ śivadarśanam  |
supuṇyaṃ koṭitīrthaṃ vai tathā cāmarakaṇṭakam  || 41.45 ||
svargadaṃ mokṣadaṃ viddhi sarvasiddhipradāyakam  |
vaidūryaparvate siddho māndhātā ca kṛte yuge  || 41.46 ||
kathito nṛpaśārdūla tathānyatkathayāmi te  |
kāverīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ sarvalokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 41.47 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
kāveryāṃ bhūtajābhaume vyatīpāto yadā bhavet || 41.48 ||
sahasraguṇitaṃ puṇyaṃ bhavettasyāstu saṅgame  |
śastreṇa nihatā ye vai tilamiśrāmbudānataḥ  || 41.49 ||
te caikoddiṣṭaśrāddhena svargalokamavāpnuyuḥ  |
cāṇḍālādguhakātsarpādvidyuto brāhmaṇādapi  || 41.50 ||
daṃṣṭribhyaśca paśubhyaśca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmiṇām  |
sarve te nṛpaśārdūla kāverīsaṅgame śubhe  || 41.51 ||
śrāddhasya karaṇātsatyaṃ tṛptā yānti parāṃ gatim  |
kubereṇa tapastaptaṃ divyaṃ varṣasahasrakam  || 41.52 ||
ārādhitaḥ pūjitaśca lokanātha umāpatiḥ  |
śivaprasādasampanno lokapālatvamāptavān  || 41.53 ||
tatra yastyajati prāṇāṃstasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi devarājyamavāpnuyāt || 41.54 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vaidyādhare pure  || 41.55 ||
revākāverikaistoyaistilairāraṇyakaistathā  |
pitarastarpitāstatra tṛptā yānti parāṃ gatim  || 41.56 ||
nānāmukhasahasraistu kāverī prathitā bhuvi  |
carācaraṃ yathā vyāptaṃ vāyunā sūryaraśmibhiḥ  || 41.57 ||
tathā toyena kāveryā vyāptaṃ ca vasudhātalam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle vārāhe vindhyaparvate  || 41.58 ||
pratyakṣā sarvadevānāṃ payoṣṇī nirgatā yataḥ  |
somasya duhitā cāsīthimagarbhenduśītalā  || 41.59 ||
hareṇārādhitā pūrvamumāmūrtiḥ payasvinī  |
lokānāṃ tāraṇārthāya somagaṅgeti gīyate  || 41.60 ||
viniṣkrāntā śarīrācca vārāhasya yaśasvinaḥ  |
tatra snātvā naro rājan bhave vai na punarbhavet || 41.61 ||
tilodakapradānena pitarastasya bhārata  |
daśāyutasahasrāṇi loke krīḍanti śāṅkare  || 41.62 ||
kārttikyāṃ yatphalaṃ tasya vārāhe vindhyaparvate  |
saṃkhyā na śakyate snānādatra varṣaśatairapi  || 41.63 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ purāṇe skandakīrtite  |
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 41.64 ||
ye cārcayanti vārāhaṃ na te prākṛtamānuṣāḥ  |
prāṇatyāge kṛte tatra śivalokamavāpnuyuḥ  || 41.65 ||
rāhusomasamāyoge vārāhe vindhyaparvate  |
kurukṣetrasamaṃ puṇyaṃ purā vai śaṅkaro'bravīt || 41.66 ||
girerārabhya vārāhātpayoṣṇyāḥ saṅgamāvadhi  |
atrāntare nṛpaśreṣṭha tīrthakoṭirudāhṛtā  || 41.67 ||
payoṣṇīsaṅgame tatra somāvartaḥ sa ucyate  |
sa deśaḥ sarvataḥ puṇyaḥ satyametattavoditam  || 41.68 ||
payoṣṇīsaṅgame pāpahare liṅgasya darśanāt |
tatra snānasya dānasya saṃkhyā kartuṃ na śakyate  || 41.69 ||
tāpīpayoṣṇīsaṃbhedaścandrasūryagrahe nṛpa  |
kurukṣetrācchataguṇaḥ śivena parikīrtitaḥ  || 41.70 ||
caturbhujo hariryatra śrīpatiḥ puruṣottamaḥ  |
viṣṇukṣetraṃ tu vijñeyaṃ krośamātraṃ tu tannṛpa  || 41.71 ||
āśvinasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  |
amāvāsyā sinīvālī parvāṇyetānyanukramāt || 41.72 ||
caturdaśyāṃ caturyoge pīyūṣaṃ vahate nṛpa  |
pitarastṛptimāyānti dine tasminna saṃśayaḥ  || 41.73 ||
suryagrahe kurukṣetre yatphalaṃ parikīrtitam  |
tāpīpayoṣṇīsamparke tatphalaṃ parikīrtitam  || 41.74 ||
dīpotsarge tu kaumudyāṃ phalaṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
puruṣaścakravartī syāddīpaṃ tatra cakāra yaḥ  || 41.75 ||
kārttikyāmāśvine māsi pakṣayorubhayorapi  |
māsamekaṃ na bhuñjīta tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 41.76 ||
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca himavāṃśca mahodadhiḥ  |
tāvatkālaṃ vasetsvarge viṣṇuloke ca bhārata  || 41.77 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe bhittvā pātālāttu samutthitam  |
kāverī kuṇḍamityevaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 41.78 ||
snātamātro narastatra gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
kuṇḍeśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ surasiddhaniṣevitam  || 41.79 ||
pramādātkurute yastu pūtaliṅgasya pūjanam  |
na tatpuṇyasya saṃkhyāsti yāvaccandrārkatārakam  || 41.80 ||
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  |
asaṃkhyakālikā tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ nāsti saṃśayaḥ  || 41.81 ||
kāveryāstu prabhāveṇa narmadāsaṅgamātpunaḥ  |
yajñāvarto'bhavaddeśaḥ satyametacchivoditam  || 41.82 ||
svayambhuvāni liṅgāni svargamokṣapradāni tu  |
yatra tatra naraḥ snātvā kāveryā nṛpasattama  || 41.83 ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
tyaktvā prāṇāṃstu kauveryāṃ kauberaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 41.84 ||
kaubereśvaraliṅgaṃ tu sarvasiddhipradāyakam  |
kāverīnarmadāmadhye na tatpaśyanti mānavāḥ  || 41.85 ||
pūjyate suradaityaistu nāgakanyābhirarcyate  |
arcanāttasya devasya dvādaśādityasannibhaḥ  || 41.86 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
kāverīnarmadābhede bāṇaliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  || 41.87 ||
kubereṇa purā dṛṣṭaṃ saṅgameśvaranāmataḥ  |
arcanāttasya devasya lokapure vaset || 41.88 ||
gaṅgāyamunasaṃbhede yatphalaṃ parikīrtitam  |
bhaume tu bhūtajāyoge vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  || 41.89 ||
rāhusomasamāyoge tadevāṣṭaguṇaṃ smṛtam  |
aśītiśca guṇāḥ proktā gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame  || 41.90 ||
kāverīnarmadāyoge bāṇā aṣṭaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
gaṅgāṣaṣṭisahasraistu kṣetrapālaiḥ prapūjyate  || 41.91 ||
tadardhairanyatīrthāni rakṣante nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
amareśvarayāmye tu liṅgaṃ caiva jaleśvaram  || 41.92 ||
darśanāttasya liṅgasya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
lakṣaiśca rakṣitā devairnarmadā saptakalpagā  || 41.93 ||
dhanvibhiḥ ṣaṣṭipuruṣaiḥ sahasraiśca yudhiṣṭhira  |
oṃkāraṃ śatasāhasryā parvato liṅgameva ca  || 41.94 ||
anyadeśe kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ puṇyakṣetre vinaśyati  |
puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vajralepo bhaviṣyati  || 41.95 ||
kṣaṇamātreṇa duḥkhena atyantasukhamaśnute  || 41.96 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kāverīmāhātmyaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||

adhyāya 42

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
revā bhittvā caṇḍavegā mahābhāgā viniḥsṛtā  |
tatra snātvā naravyāghra brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 42.1 ||
tilodakaṃ tatra tīrthe haviṣā piṇḍadānataḥ  |
pitṝn samuddharedghorātnarakānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 42.2 ||
pāpamṛtyumṛtā ye tu caṇḍakarmakṛto narāḥ  |
mucyante tena pāpena caṇḍavegāsamāgame  || 42.3 ||
caṇḍeśvaraṃ tatra liṅgaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubham  |
darśanātpūjanādasya puṣpagandhādidānataḥ  || 42.4 ||
brahmahatyā sahasrairhi tatkṣaṇādeva mucyate  |
candrasenaḥ purā cāsīddurātmā pāpakarmakṛt || 42.5 ||
paradārarataścauro brahmaghno gurutalpagaḥ  |
raghuvaṃśī ca pāpātmā ṛṣipatnīṃ ca brāhmaṇīm  || 42.6 ||
anyāśca yā vai subhagāḥ patiṃ hatvā samāharat |
naimiṣāraṇyavāsī ca śāṇḍilyo brahmavittamaḥ  || 42.7 ||
saudāminī tasya bhāryā dharmapatnī yaśasvinī  |
rūpayauvanasampannā candrakāntisamaprabhā  || 42.8 ||
kāminī valkaladharā pīnonnatapayodharā  |
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi tasyāstasya purātanam  || 42.9 ||
hayārūḍhaścandraseno naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  |
śāṇḍilyasyāśramaṃ prāpto vane sa mṛgayanmṛgān  || 42.10 ||
daṣṭā saudāminī tena śāṇḍilyasya priyā tadā  |
uvāca vacanaṃ tāṃ vai tvaṃ me rājñī bhavediti  || 42.11 ||
valkalājinadhārī ca kuśakāśapavitrakaḥ  |
kandamūlaphalāśī ca brāhmaṇaśca patistava  || 42.12 ||
kiṃ kariṣyasi tattena mama bhogāṃśca puṣkalān  |
sarvadā matprasādena bhuṅkṣva tvaṃ varavarṇini  || 42.13 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā nṛpateḥ pāpakarmaṇaḥ  |
āha saudāminī vākyaṃ candrasenaṃ nṛpādhamam  || 42.14 ||
yācasva me patiṃ rājaṃstasyāhaṃ vaśavārtinī  |
śāṇḍilyamabravīdrājā tato'mūṃ varayāmi te  || 42.15 ||
kanyāmimāṃ ca patnī te cedadya tvaṃ dadasva me  |
maulye dadāmi te dravyamasyāḥ śatasahasraśaḥ  || 42.16 ||

śāṇḍilya uvāca - 
sarvāḥ striyaḥ kaitavabaddhamūlāstāsāṃ priyo nāsti manuṣyaloke  |
yatheṣṭaceṣṭo bhava bhūmipāla kiṃ māṃ dvijaṃ pṛcchasi durbalaṃ ca  || 42.17 ||
kāmāndhastu tato rājā kare jagrāha tāṃ tadā  |
sā nirīkṣyā hataṃ pāpaṃ jvalantī tīvrakopataḥ  |
rajasvalā'haṃ māṃ spṛṣṭvā cāṇḍālastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 42.18 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśīṃ nagnāṃ bhayārtāṃ prāṇavallabhām  |
cāṇḍālastatkṣaṇājjātaḥ sarvabhūtabhayāvahaḥ  || 42.19 ||
hāhākāraṃ tataścakruḥ sarve devāḥ samānuṣāḥ  |
aśvamāruhya rājā'sāvayodhyāmāviśatpurīm  || 42.20 ||
dṛṣṭvā cāṇḍālarūpaṃ taṃ brāhmaṇāḥ puravāsinaḥ  |
rājaputrā mahīpālasyāntaḥpuranivāsinaḥ  || 42.21 ||
babhūvurbhayabhītāśca garhayanto mahīpatim  |
vaśiṣṭhaṃ śaraṇaṃ prāptaḥ śocitvā cātmanastanum  || 42.22 ||
rājā viṣaṇṇavadana uvāca svapurohitam  |
jagāmāhaṃ samuddeśaṃ naimiṣāraṇyavāsinam  || 42.23 ||
śāṇḍilyaṃ ca namaskṛtya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
abravaṃ dehi bhāryāṃ svāṃ vittena bahulena me  || 42.24 ||
śāṇḍilyasya tu patnyā vai tayā śapto'hamantike  |
śrutvā tasya caritraṃ ca pāpasya bho narādhipa  || 42.25 ||
vaśiṣṭho'pyabravīdvākyaṃ candrasenaṃ narādhipam  |
śāṇḍilyaṃ gaccha rājendra tāpasaṃ ṛtugāminam  || 42.26 ||
saudāminīmṛṣerbhāryāṃ jvalantīmiva tejasā  |
evamastviti coktvā taṃ namaskṛtya purodhasam  || 42.27 ||
sa jagāma tamuddeśaṃ naimiṣāraṇyavāsinam  |
śāṇḍilyaṃ tu namaskṛtya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 42.28 ||
abravīttaṃ muniśreṣṭhaṃ bhayatrasto narādhipaḥ  |
kṣamasva me muniśreṣṭha tvadbhāryāṃ prati kāminaḥ  || 42.29 ||
tvaṃ mātā me pitā cāsi raghuvaṃśaṃ samuddhara  |
mayā tvapakṛtaṃ te'dya tasya prāptaṃ phalaṃ hi vai  || 42.30 ||
uvāca brāhmaṇaḥ prīto bhāryā caiva pativratā  |
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ pitā rājan chiṣyo'haṃ tasya dhīmataḥ  || 42.31 ||
bhāryārthamiha saṃprāpto naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  |
tvaṃ tu gaccha nṛpaśreṣṭha caṇḍavegāsamāgamam  || 42.32 ||
caṇḍeśvaraṃ tamabhyarcya tatra snātvā nṛpottama  |
avāpsyasi paraṃ sthānaṃ muktaścāsmācca kilbiṣāt || 42.33 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śāṇḍilyasya mahātmanaḥ  |
śāṇḍilyaṃ ca namaskṛtya tathā saudāminīṃ nṛpaḥ  || 42.34 ||
svasti vo'stu gamiṣyāmi caṇḍavegāsamāgamam  |
evamuktvā gatastatra samāgatya kṣamāpatiḥ  || 42.35 ||
caṇḍeśvaraṃ samabhyarcya tatra snātvā vidhānataḥ  |
divyayānasamārūḍhaḥ stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 42.36 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktastīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
kṣaṇācchivapuraṃ prāptaścandraseno mahīpatiḥ  || 42.37 ||
svārociṣe'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
bhūtānāṃ ca sahasrāṇi saṃsiddhiṃ tatra cānvayuḥ  || 42.38 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃścaṇḍavegāsamāgamam  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādvāpi bhrūṇahatyā praṇaśyati  || 42.39 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe caṇḍavegāmāhātmyavarṇano nāma dvicatvāriṃśo'dhyāya


adhyāya 43

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
eraṇḍīnarmadāyogaṃ tato gacchedyudhiṣṭhira  |
tṛtīyā caṇḍavegā ca svargasopānapaddhatiḥ  || 43.1 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  || 43.2 ||
anekakālikā tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāmupajāyate  |
amāsomasamāyoge rāhugraste divākare  || 43.3 ||
eraṇḍīsaṅgamasthāne puṇyasaṃkhyā na vidyate  |
eraṇḍīśvaraliṅgaṃ tu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 43.4 ||
kuṅkumena samālipya gandhadhūpaiḥ prapūjayet |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu modate śivasannidhau  || 43.5 ||
darśanāttasya liṅgasya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
bhadrarudreśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ paramasiddhidam  || 43.6 ||
bhadrarudrau purākalpe gandharvau bhrātarau tathā  |
tamabhyarcya vidhānena gatau vaidyādharaṃ puram  || 43.7 ||
gāyanti pitaro gāthāṃ tathaiva ca pitāmahāḥ  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍapātena bhārata  || 43.8 ||
vasetmanvantarāṇīha rudraloke caturdaśa  |
astreṇa tu hatā ye vai daivātpāpamṛtā narāḥ  || 43.9 ||
caturdaśyāṃ tu śrāddhena vṛṣotsargeṇa bhārata  |
vīralokamavāpyaiva tatra krīḍanti mānavāḥ  || 43.10 ||
tatra yastyajati prāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa sa bhave na punarbhavet || 43.11 ||
caturyugasahasrāṇi rājā vaidyādhare pure  |
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathādṛṣṭaṃ purā'nagha  || 43.12 ||
cākṣuṣe cāntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
nimirnāma purā rājā cakravartī viśāmpate  || 43.13 ||
pakṣiyonau ca saṃprāptaḥ kopādvai brāhmaṇasya ca  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyāddarśasomasamāgame  || 43.14 ||
snātvā saṃtyajya tāṃ yoniṃ rājyaṃ kṛtvā divaṃ gataḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kathaṃ sa rājā saṃprāpto mānuṣīṃ ca tanuṃ punaḥ  || 43.15 ||
etadāścaryabhūtaṃ me kathayasva mahāmate  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sukanyāṃ nāma kanyāṃ vai yayāce cyavano nṛpam  || 43.16 ||
rājñī candravatī nāma nimerāsītpativratā  |
sā putrīṃ janayāmāsa prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasīm  || 43.17 ||
ekadā tu nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ sukhāsīno yadṛcchayā  |
āgataṃ cyavanaṃ dṛṣṭvā arghapādyairapūjayat || 43.18 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma tvatpādāmbujadarśanāt |
anugrahamimaṃ manye bhojanaṃ kartumarhasi  || 43.19 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā provāca cyavano muniḥ  |
kanyāṃ dadāsi cetmahyaṃ bhāryārthaṃ dharmatatparām  || 43.20 ||
tadā bhokṣye mahīpāla no cetpāpamavāpsyasi  |

rājovāca - 
ekā me duhitā brahman rājñīṃ yācasva varṇinīm  || 43.21 ||
kanyādāne na śakto'haṃ satyametattavoditam  |
tataḥ śrutvā vaco rājñīṃ munirvacanamabravīt || 43.22 ||
kanyāṃ dehi ca me rājñi gṛhiṇaḥ putrakāraṇāt |
prahasya cābravīdrājñī na te yogyā dvijottama  || 43.23 ||
anyadācakṣva brahmarṣe taddadāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
tataḥ kopācchaśāpaināṃ pakṣiyoniṃ tu gacchasi  || 43.24 ||
śāpaṃ śrutvā tato rājñī śaśāpātha mahāmunim  |
yadi me vidyate satyaṃ bhartṛbhaktiśca niścalā  || 43.25 ||
śaptā niraparādhāhaṃ netrahīno bhaviṣyasi  |
parasparaṃ ca tau śaptau narmadātīramāgatau  || 43.26 ||
cacāra cyavanaścogrameraṇḍīsaṅgame tapaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu valmīkena tu pūritaḥ  || 43.27 ||
girervai pūrvabhāge tu madhyāraṇyamiti smṛtam  |
kadambavṛkṣamāsādya vane tasmiṃśca dampatī  || 43.28 ||
puṣpite phalite ramye saṃjātau pakṣirūpiṇau  |
anye'pi pakṣiṇastatra samudbhūtāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 43.29 ||
jātismarā vyāharanto mānuṣīṃ giramuttamām  |
sāraṅgāḥ pakṣiṇaste tu sarvadā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ  || 43.30 ||
jyeṣṭhe māse tu saṃprāpte dāvāgniradahadvanam  |
praṇaṣṭāścāṇḍajāḥ sarve jvālāmālākulīkṛtāḥ  || 43.31 ||
koṭare tu samālambya putraiḥ saha yathā sukham  |
ekadā garbhiṇī jātā pakṣiṇī tatra bhārata  || 43.32 ||
bhartāraṃ pakṣiṇī prāha kiṃ tvaṃ tiṣṭhasi nirbhayam  |
gatāśca pakṣiṇaḥ sarve kiṃ tvaṃ saṃhārayiṣyasi  || 43.33 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā khecaro vākyamabravīt |
bhāryāṃ tyaktvā sutāṃścaiva yāmyekākī kathaṃ priye  || 43.34 ||
iti loke na vede ca dṛṣṭaṃ kenāpi kutracit |
pāṇigraheṇa yā bhāryā suśīlā dharmacāriṇī  || 43.35 ||
tyaktvā gacchati tāṃ mohādbhrūṇaghnaḥ sa tu kīrtitaḥ  |

pakṣiṇyuvāca - 
ātmānaṃ rakṣa jīveśa mūlabhūtaṃ kulasya tu  || 43.36 ||
bhūyo'nyāste bhaviṣyanti bhāryāḥ śatasahasraśaḥ  |
jīvamāne tu yā patyāvanyaṃ kāmayate varam  || 43.37 ||
sā pāpiṣṭhā durācārā viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ  |
vidhavā bhartṛhīnā yā'nugatā na svakaṃ patim  || 43.38 ||
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ yāvatsā pāpiṣṭhā prakīrtitā  |
patyau jīvati yā nārī mriyate bharturagrataḥ  || 43.39 ||
bhartṛdattodakaśrāddhaiḥ sā yāti paramāṃ gatim  |
prasādādyasya labhyeta putrālaṅkārakīrtayaḥ  || 43.40 ||
ko'nyaḥ priyatarastasmādiha loke paratra ca  |
svayaṃ prāptastu dāvāgniḥ śīghraṃ gaccha kathaṃ sutān  || 43.41 ||
tyaktvā gacchāmi jīveśa saṃhāro vartate'dhunā  |
hastau pādau na vidyete pāvako naiva śāmyati  || 43.42 ||
aśaktā nīyamāne tu pakṣiṇaścāṇḍajīvinaḥ  |
sutāṃstyaktvā tu yā mātā bhayārtā yāti garhitā  || 43.43 ||
sā saptajanmaparyantaṃ sarpiṇī jāyate dhruvam  |
evamuktvā tu tatraiva putrāṇāṃ ca parasparam  || 43.44 ||
sā saṃgṛhya prayatnena yugmaṃ yugmaṃ dampatī  |
saṃsthāpyairaṇḍikāyoge bhartā bhāryāmuvāca ha  || 43.45 ||
daṃṣṭribhyaśca prayatnena rakṣaṇīyāḥ sutā mama  |
gacchāmi ca sutānanyānānetuṃ sāṃprataṃ priye  || 43.46 ||
evamuktvā gataḥ pakṣī kadambāśritamandiram  |
so'paśyattadā tatra mandiraṃ vahnisaṃkulam  || 43.47 ||
snehātpraviṣṭaḥ putrārthī jvālāmālāvṛtaṃ gṛham  |
taṃ dadāha tadā vahṇiḥ sasutaṃ sa mahīruham  || 43.48 ||
dagdhastu vahṇinā tena vahnipuñja ivābhavat |
bhasmībhūte vane tasmin prāvaṭkālaḥ samāgataḥ  || 43.49 ||
eraṇḍyantarjale tatra sarvaṃ tatplāvitaṃ nṛpa  |
amāsomasamāyoge pakṣiṇastasya cāsthi vai  || 43.50 ||
eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame prāptaṃ daivādvai nṛpasattama  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehastu divyayānaṃ samāśritaḥ  || 43.51 ||
dhriyamāṇātapatrastu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
divyavastraparīdhāno divyālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 43.52 ||
uparivyāharan bhāryāṃ sa jagāmātmanaḥ purīm  |
āgaccha subhage śīghraṃ bhāryā tvaṃ me bhaviṣyasi  || 43.53 ||
siddhavidyādharairyakṣaiḥ sādhuvādena pūjitaḥ  |
puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papātoccairdevarājopakalpitā  || 43.54 ||
etasminnantare rājan patnīṃ bhūyo'pi cābravīt |
kanyārthaṃ tvaṃ suśaptāsi cyavanena mahātmanā  || 43.55 ||
muñcātmānamavāpya tvaṃ bhavanaṃ dharmacāriṇām  |
smāritākāśavacasā nṛpeṇaivaṃ puraṃ gatā  || 43.56 ||
sutān pragṛhya cāgatya bhartāramidamabravīt |
yā gatirmama bhartuḥ syātsā me nityaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 43.57 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ bhartā śīghraṃ viśa hutāśanam  |
amāsomasamāyoge eraṇḍītīrthasaṅgame  || 43.58 ||
tatra yatpatitaṃ cāsthi pāpāttvāṃ tārayiṣyati  |
evamastviti taṃ coktvā pakṣiṇī satvaraṃ tadā  || 43.59 ||
āhṛtya tṛṇakāṣṭhāni sampradīpya hutāśanam  |
tato yānaṃ samārūḍhā bhartuḥ sā ca sutaiḥ saha  || 43.60 ||
umāmaheśvaraṃ yadvacchrīpatiṃ ca yathā ramā  |
tadvaccāvāpa bhartāraṃ tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 43.61 ||
evaṃ yānaṃ samāruhya sabhāryastu tadā nṛpaḥ  |
candraseno devaseno yajñasenastathāparaḥ  || 43.62 ||
tribhiḥ putraiḥ parivṛto dharmavṛttiparāyaṇaḥ  |
viveśa nagarīṃ ramyāmayodhyāṃ devanirmitām  || 43.63 ||
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  |
rājyaṃ kṛtvā varṣalakṣaṃ tataḥ prāptaḥ śivālayam  || 43.64 ||
eraṇḍīśvaramabhyarcya eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame nṛpa  |
pañcāśītisahasrāṇi kṣatriyāṇāṃ mahātmanām  || 43.65 ||
gatāni tatra rājendra satyaṃ vai śāmbhavaṃ puram  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannākhyānaṃ vai purātanam  || 43.66 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādvā'pi gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 43.67 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe eraṇḍīsaṅgamamahimānuvarṇano nāma tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 44

yudhiṣṭhirauvāca - 
sukanyārthe muniḥ śaptaścandravatyā mahāmune  |
valmīkācca kathaṃ muktaścyavanaḥ kāmamohitaḥ  || 44.1 ||
paralokaṃ kathaṃ prāpto brāhmaṇo brahmavittamaḥ  |
kathayasva mahābāho paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 44.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu tvaṃ rājaśārdūla kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
nimiḥ putraṃ ca rājye vai sūryasenaṃ niveśya ca  || 44.3 ||
ādideśātha matimāñjyeṣṭhā vai bhaginī tathā  |
cyavanāya pradātavyā brāhmaṇāya na saṃśayaḥ  || 44.4 ||
eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame ceṣṭvā hayamedhaṃ makhottamam  |
sarvayajñakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya pradāsyasi  || 44.5 ||
uktvaivaṃ sūryasenaṃ tu sa gataḥ śivamandire  |
evamastviti taṃ coktvā sūryasenaḥ pratāpavān  || 44.6 ||
evaṃ saṃcintya manasā sarvasaṃbhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  |
sa jagāma suraiḥ sārddhaṃ kanyāmādāya bhūpatiḥ  || 44.7 ||
maṇḍapāṃścaiva yūpāṃśca pañcayojanavistarān  |
sa cakāra tato rājā piturājñāmanusmaran  || 44.8 ||
yajñastena samārabdhaḥ samāptavaradakṣiṇaḥ  |
ramamāṇā sukanyā ca kanyābhirnṛpatisvasā  || 44.9 ||
śrutvā śabdaṃ ca valmīke kartuṃ krīḍāṃ samāyayau  |
tatrāpaśyatmānuṣaṃ sā cakṣurhīnamadhomukham  || 44.10 ||
dṛṣṭvā sā kaṇṭakenaiva vivyādha ca gṛhaṃ gatā  |
hāhākāro mahānāsītkimetaditi bhārata  || 44.11 ||
durmanāḥ sūryasenastu brāhmaṇaiḥ saha satvaram  |
ājuhāva tato devāvaśvinau pākaśāsanam  || 44.12 ||
yajñaṃ nivartayāmāsa yathāvadvidhipūrvakam  |
nivartya ca tato yajñaṃ rājā paramadhārmikaḥ  || 44.13 ||
devānabhyarcayāmāsa cyavanāya mahātmane  |
divyaṃ cakṣurdadadhvaṃ hi vapuḥ kāntaṃ navaṃ vayaḥ  || 44.14 ||
evamabhyarthitairdevairdattaṃ cakṣuryudhiṣṭhira  |
rūpayau vanasaṃpannaṃ kāmadevasamaṃ vapuḥ  || 44.15 ||
tataḥ prasādayitvā'sau cyavanaṃ vākyamabravīt |
tatkṣamasva mahābhāga yatkṛtaṃ te sukanyayā  || 44.16 ||
gṛhāṇa pāṇimasyāstvaṃ mune kopaṃ parityaja  |
evamabhyarthito rājñā muniromityuvāca ha  || 44.17 ||
tataścāvabhṛthe yugmaṃ caṇḍavegāsamāgame  |
saṃsthāpya vidhivadrājā tasmai dattvā kratoḥ phalam  || 44.18 ||
cakāra pāṇigrahaṇaṃ sukanyāyā manoharam  |
divyarūpadharau tau tu lakṣmīnārāyaṇāviva  || 44.19 ||
saṃjātau dampatī tatra harṣeṇotphullalocanau  |
dattvā kanyāṃ munestatra sūryasenaḥ puraṃ yayau  || 44.20 ||
bubhuje vividhān bhogān chakratulyaparākramaḥ  |
sūryasenaṃ sukanyāṃ ca cyavanaṃ śakramaśvinau  || 44.21 ||
bhojanānte smaredyastu cakṣustasya na hīyate  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃścaṇḍairaṇḍakasaṅgamam  || 44.22 ||
tatra snāto divaṃ yāti na punargarbhamāviśet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣamutsṛjet || 44.23 ||
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathā dṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam  |
babhrāma sarvatīrthāni durvāsāścogratāpasaḥ  || 44.24 ||
pitṛtīrthaṃ samāgamya pitṝṇāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
tatra snātvārcayitvā ca śūlapāṇiṃ pitāmaham  || 44.25 ||
jalāñjaliṃ kuśatilāṃ pitṛpiṇḍamavāsṛjat |
dattvā piṇḍaṃ munīn prāha paraṃ vismayamāgataḥ  || 44.26 ||
kare gṛhṇanti pitaraḥ piṇḍānīha mayā śrutam  |
tadadya bho na paśyāmi tīrthayātrā nirarthikā  || 44.27 ||
tamugratāpasaṃ jñātvā procuste munayastadā  |
kare gṛhṇanti pitaraḥ piṇḍaṃ darśe prakalpitam  || 44.28 ||
satyametanmuniśreṣṭha nānyathā vedabhāṣitam  |
taddarśaṃ ca pratīkṣa tvaṃ tīrthāntaradidṛkṣayā  || 44.29 ||
cukopa vai tadā vipra ṛṣiścaivābravīdvacaḥ  |
atra no pātaye piṇḍaṃ snānaṃ dānaṃ karomi na  || 44.30 ||
durvāsāstu tataḥ prāha eraṇḍaṃ munipuṅgavam  |
kare kamaṇḍaluṃ kṛtvā jalapūrṇaṃ mahāmunim  || 44.31 ||
śarīraṃ kliśyase kasmāttavātra niṣphalaṃ tapaḥ  |
oṃkāraṃ kalpagāṃ gaccha gṛhītvaikaṃ kamaṇḍalum  || 44.32 ||
ekaḥ pitāmahaḥ pūjyo gayāyāṃ prabhuravyayaḥ  |
uktvaivamṛṣibhiḥ sārddhaṃ giriṃ tvamarakaṇṭakam  || 44.33 ||
ājagāma mahātejā gatvā tatra ca bhārata  |
oṃkārasyārcanaṃ kṛtvā stotrametadudāharat || 44.34 ||
namaḥ kālāya devāya tridevāya trimūrtaye  |
avyaktāvyaktarūpāya anantānantagāmine  || 44.35 ||
ṛgyajuḥ sāmarūpāya sarvajñāya namo'stu te  |
bhavodbhava jagannātha umākānta namo'stu te  || 44.36 ||
asuraghnāya devāya tripuraghnāya te namaḥ  |
sadyojātastathā ghoraḥ puruṣeśāya te namaḥ  || 44.37 ||
jayakālāgnirudrāya saṃvartāya namo namaḥ  |
hṛṣīkeśvararudrāya puruṣeśāya te namaḥ  || 44.38 ||
namaḥ śambhavāya mayobhavāya śaṅkarāya namo'stu te  |
brahmaviṣṇvindravarada trinetrāya namo'stu te  || 44.39 ||
śrīkaṇṭhanīlakaṇṭhāya namaḥ somārdhadhāriṇe  |
kapālamāline tubhyaṃ namaḥ khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇe  || 44.40 ||
namaḥ triśūlahastāya nāgābharaṇabhūṣiṇe  |
namaḥ pinākine tubhyaṃ mahānātha namo'stu te  || 44.41 ||
śarvāya sarvarūpāya carācara namo'stu te  |
jihvācāpalyabhāvena khedito'si mayā prabho  || 44.42 ||
kṣamasva me sureśāna iha loke paratra ca  |
tvatsamo nāsti deveśa kaścidanya umāpate  || 44.43 ||
śrutvā stotramidaṃ divyaṃ śiva oṃkārarūpadhṛk |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga iti vākyamuvāca ha  || 44.44 ||

durvāsā uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
pitṛtīrthasamaṃ tīrthametadastviti dehi me  || 44.45 ||

oṃkāra uvāca - 
etatsarvaṃ bhavatvadya matprasādāttapodhana  |
asādhyamapi sādhyaṃ te trailokye sacarācare  || 44.46 ||
evaṃ labdhvā varaṃ vipro girervai pūrvabhāgataḥ  |
uvāsa munibhiḥ sārddhaṃ durvāsā ugra tāpasaḥ  || 44.47 ||
atrāntare nṛpaśreṣṭha brahmāṇaṃ prāha nāradaḥ  |
pitṛtīrthaṃ gayā naṣṭā durvāsasaḥ kopatastataḥ  || 44.48 ||
āste sa narmadātīre oṃkāre'marakaṇṭake  |
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā brahmā lokānukampayā  || 44.49 ||
haṃsayānaṃ samārūḍho devaiḥ saha nṛpottama  |
ājagāmāśramaṃ tatra durvāsā yatra saṃsthitaḥ  || 44.50 ||
dṛṣṭvā pitāmahaṃ devameraṇḍo munipuṃgavaḥ  |
kamaṇḍaluṃ samādāya pādamūle tu brahmaṇaḥ  || 44.51 ||
vinikṣipya yathā nyāyamarghaṃ dattvā ca tasthivān  |
kamaṇḍalujalodbhūtaḥ pravāho narmadāṃ gataḥ  || 44.52 ||
tataḥ saṃpūjya vidhivaddurvāsāstaṃ pitāmaham  |

brahmovāca - 
udbhavedyadi te tīrthamamāsomasamāgame  || 44.53 ||
idānīṃ pitṛtīrthaṃ tu janairnehopadṛśyate  |
anivartyastu śāpaste tatpūrṇaṃ kuru sāṃpratam  || 44.54 ||

durvāsā uvāca - 
mayā nivartitaḥ śāpo vacanātte pitāmaha  |
pitṝṇāṃ darśanaṃ tatra gayāpitṛvisarjinī  || 44.55 ||
bhaviṣyati prasādātte tasmiṃstīrthe pitāmaha  |
evamastviti taṃ coktvā divaṃ brahmā yayau nṛpa  || 44.56 ||
namaskṛtya maheśānaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
harṣeṇa mahatāviṣṭaḥ pūjyamāno dvijottamaiḥ  || 44.57 ||
durvāsāstu muniśreṣṭhastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
tena puṇyatamaṃ loke tatrairaṇḍī samāgatā  || 44.58 ||
eraṇḍīśvaraliṅgaṃ tu surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
puṇyakarmānupaśyedamāsomasamāgame  || 44.59 ||
dṛṣṭvā tatparamaṃ liṅgaṃ yamalokaṃ na paśyati  |
etattu kathitaṃ rājanmayā tvāṃ prati bhārata  || 44.60 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya gacchetmāheśvaraṃ puram  || 44.61 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe durvāsaścaritre eraṇḍītīrthavarṇano nāma catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


adhyāya 45

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchetmahābhāga revāśalyāviśalyayoḥ  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yāti phalaṃ yajñeśvarājñayā  || 45.1 ||
tatra yajñeśvarāṃ liṅgaṃ dhūpeśvaramanuttamam  |
siddhidaṃ mokṣadaṃ viddhi na te paśyanti mānavāḥ  || 45.2 ||
tilodakapradānena cānnadānena bhārata  |
pitarastṛptimāyānti yāvaccandrārkatārakam  || 45.3 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu some vai vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  |
dānaṃ yatkriyate tatra tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 45.4 ||
bharatena kṛtastatra hayamedhaḥ purā yathā  |
tatte'haṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu kaunteya sāmpratam  || 45.5 ||
bharato nāma rājāsītsūryavaṃśe viśāmpate  |
praśaśāsa mahārāja kṛtsnaṃ vai sa mahītalam  || 45.6 ||
yāvattṛṇaṃ vijānīyā yāvatkīrtiśca bhāskaraḥ  |
tāvadvai bharatakṣetraṃ saśailavanakānanam  || 45.7 ||
ekadā sa nṛpaśreṣṭho yajñakarmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
bhṛgordakṣiṇabhāge tu kuṇḍamaṇḍapamaṇḍitām  || 45.8 ||
daśayojanavistīrṇāṃ yajñabhūmiṃ cakāra ha  |
gavāṃ hi daśalakṣāṇi savatsānāṃ payomucām  || 45.9 ||
lakṣamekaṃ hayānāṃ ca dantināmayutaṃ tathā  |
maṇimāṇikyaratnāni vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca  || 45.10 ||
yajñopaskaramādāya sarvasaṃbhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  |
vedadhvanininādena divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca saṃspṛśan  || 45.11 ||
homena devatāstṛptāḥ saptalokanivāsinaḥ  |
evaṃ pravartite yajñe rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  || 45.12 ||
yajñavidhvaṃsanārthaṃ tu rākṣasā raudrarūpiṇaḥ  |
mālyavāṃśca sumālī ca sukeśī śaṅkhadūṣaṇau  || 45.13 ||
rākṣasānā sahasrāṇi samāyātāstu satvaram  |
bhagnāni yajñavastūni triṣu lokeṣu dāruṇaiḥ  || 45.14 ||
praṇaṣṭā devatāḥ sarvā ṛtvijaśca nipātitāḥ  |
evaṃ vināśite yajñe rakṣobhiśca tato'nagha  || 45.15 ||
kopājjajvāla rājāpi hutāśana ivāhutaḥ  |
jaghāna rākṣasān sarvān girīn vajradharo yathā  || 45.16 ||
praṇaṣṭān bhayamītāṃśca patitān dharaṇītale  |
rākṣasairnihatān dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇānṛtvijastathā  || 45.17 ||
śokāviṣṭastataḥ prāha bharato devamantriṇam  |
gurustvaṃ sarvadevānāṃ trikālajñastrivedavit || 45.18 ||
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ mamārthe devakaṇṭakaiḥ  |
prāyaścittaṃ mayā kāryaṃ kiṃ tvaṃ brūhi bṛhaspate  || 45.19 ||

bṛhaspatiruvāca - 
vidyāṃ saṃjīvanīṃ te'haṃ dadāmi nṛpasattama  |
jīvitā brāhmaṇā devāḥ śaśaṃsurdevamantriṇam  || 45.20 ||
tato nivartito yajñaḥ samagravaradakṣiṇaḥ  |
yūpamūlasamudbhūtā śalyā caiva viśalyakā  || 45.21 ||
praviveśa mahārāja narmadāṃ lokapāvanīm  |
tato devāḥ samāruhya svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ divaṃ yayuḥ  || 45.22 ||
bharato'pi dvijaiḥ sārddhaṃ praviveśa purīṃ tataḥ  |
tena śalyā viśalyā ca vikhyātā bhuvanatraye  || 45.23 ||
bharateśvaraliṅgaṃ ca brahmayonyāṃ samāsthitam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājanyathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam  || 45.24 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya na viśedyonisaṅkaṭe  || 45.25 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śalyāviśalyāmāhātmyānuvarṇano nāma pañcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 46
bhṛguṃ patanti ye śūrāḥ kāṃ gatiṃ prāpnuvanti te  |
śrotumicchāmyahaṃ tve tatkathayasva mahāmune  || 46.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
anāśakena bho rājan bhṛgugograhasaṅgaraiḥ  |
prāṇāṃstyajanti ye śūrā gatiṃ teṣāṃ nibodha me  || 46.2 ||
pṛthakpṛthaṅnivāsāṃśca teṣāṃ karmmāṇi bhārata  |
caturviṃśatikoṭyastu saptaviṃśatireva ca  || 46.3 ||
umayā tu purā jñaptā madhyamottamakanyakāḥ  |
anena vidhinā ye tu prāṇāṃstyakṣyanti mānavāḥ  || 46.4 ||
tāṃśca bhuṅgdhvaṃ mayā dattā yuṣmākaṃ suprasādataḥ  |
amareśe pramṛtāśca bhraṃśituṃ ye patanti te  || 46.5 ||
bhṛgūn dṛṣṭvā nṛpaśreṣṭha mucyante brahmahatyayā  |
caturaśītibhṛgavo jambūdvīpe prakīrtitāḥ  || 46.6 ||
tathā'nye saptanirdiṣṭāḥ svargasopānamuttamam  |
bhairavastu bhṛguśreṣṭho jñeyastvamarakaṇṭake  || 46.7 ||
śūdrāśca kṣatriyā vaiśyā antyajāścādhamāstathā  |
ete tyajanti prāṇān vai varjayitvā dvijaṃ nṛpa  || 46.8 ||
patito brāhmaṇastatra brahmahā cātmahā bhavet || 46.9 ||
dvāviṃśatisahasrāṇi rāhusomasamāgame  |
varṣāṇāṃ jāyate rājan rājā vaidyādhare pure  || 46.10 ||
graste tu rāhuṇā sūrye dviguṇaṃ phalamaśnute  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi jalapūrānalāhataḥ  || 46.11 ||
mriyate yo bhṛguṃ prāpya sa vidyādhararāḍbhavet |
bhṛgurbhairavarūpeṇa vindhyakailāsasannibhaḥ  || 46.12 ||
garhayanti bhṛguṃ ye tu te liṅgabrahmabhedinaḥ  |
bhairavaḥ kṣamate teṣāṃ neti skandena kīrtitam  || 46.13 ||
manyāsācca cyuto vipro mātṛhā pitṛhā tathā  |
svasṛgaḥ svasnuṣāgaśca tathā svajñātigastathā  || 46.14 ||
eteṣāṃ patanaṃ śastaṃ karīṣāgnau prasādhanam  |
mucyate tena pāpena śivalokaṃ sagacchati  || 46.15 ||
hariścandrapure candre śrīśaile tripurāntike  |
traiyambake dhautapāpe vārāhe vindhyaparvate  || 46.16 ||
kāveryāstu tathā kuṇḍe patanātsvargamāpnuyāt |
bhṛgordakṣiṇabhāge tu liṅgaṃ vai capaleśvaram  || 46.17 ||
kṣetrasaṃrakṣaṇāyeha vikhyātaṃ pāpanāśanam  |
dhanuḥ ṣaṣṭyāṃ tataḥ kṣetraṃ vijñeyaṃ cāpaleśvaram  || 46.18 ||
ārohati giriṃ yastu tamadṛṣṭvā tu mānavaḥ  |
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ sarvaṃ sa gṛhṇāti na saṃśayaḥ  || 46.19 ||
ālekhya ca paṭe sūryaṃ patākādaṇḍamaṇḍitam  |
valayaṃ ca kare kṛtvā vījyamānastu cāmaraiḥ  || 46.20 ||
vīrastu patituṃ gacchedārohedbhṛguparvatam  |
pade pade yajñaphalaṃ tasya syācchaṅkaro'bravīt || 46.21 ||
pratīkṣante sarvakāle'psarasaḥ kāmamohitāḥ  |
divyaṃ yānaṃ samārūḍhā divyā bharaṇabhūṣitāḥ  || 46.22 ||
vīrastu patitastatra svaṃ ca tyaktvā kalevaram  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehastu śakratulyaparākramaḥ  || 46.23 ||
kāmadaṃ yānamāruhya vivādena parasparam  |
gacchecchivapuraṃ sārddhamapsarobhiḥ samanvitaḥ  || 46.24 ||
klībasya sattvahīnasya uttīrṇasya bhṛgoḥ punaḥ  |
pade pade brahmahatyā bhavettasya na saśayaḥ  || 46.25 ||
na cirāyurbhavetmartyaḥ kasmātmṛtyorbibhetyasau  |
na ko'pi rakṣituṃ śaktaḥ kālamṛtyuvaśaṃ gatam  || 46.26 ||
sa pāpiṣṭho durācāraścāṇḍālo lokagarhitaḥ  |
sanyāsādikamāruhya cyavate yastu mānavaḥ  || 46.27 ||
sanyāsātpracyutaṃ vipraṃ dṛṣṭvā snānārkavīkṣaṇam  |
kuryātsarvaprayatena sparśāccāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 46.28 ||
ṛtānṛtaṃ na vaktavyaṃ tena sārddhaṃ kadācana  |
sthātavyaṃ caiva maunena no cetpāpamavāpnuyāt || 46.29 ||
niścite maraṇe prāpte kathaṃ mṛtyurupekṣate  |
jarāmṛtyuśca rogāśca saṃsārodadhi samplave  || 46.30 ||
evaṃ jñātvā nṛpaśreṣṭha hyārohedbhṛguparvatam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan bhṛgormāhātmyamuttamam  || 46.31 ||
na brūyādduṣṭabuddhīnāṃ kalau pākhaṇḍakarmmaṇām  |
digambaraśvetapaṭabauddhādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 46.32 ||
asaṃbhāṣyā durācārāḥ purāṇasmṛtinindakāḥ  |
na taiḥ saha prakartavyaḥ saṃvādo hi kadācana  || 46.33 ||
pratyekaṃ sarvadevānāṃ svayamāha vṛṣadhvajaḥ  |
na manyante tu ye mūḍhāstīrtharājaṃ mayoditam  || 46.34 ||
prayānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ bhṛgorye'vataranti te  || 46.35 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe bhṛguparvatamahimā'nuvarṇano nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 47

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
oṃkārakīrtanaṃ vipradānaṃ yajñastapastathā  |
sambhavaṃ pañcavaktrāṇāṃ liṅgānāṃ saṃbhavaṃ tathā  || 47.1 ||
yugasaṃkhyāṃ kalāṃ caiva caritaṃ ca mahāmune  |
kathayasva prasādena yathoddiṣṭaṃ tu śaṃbhunā  || 47.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrūyatāṃ rājarājendra purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  |
dvātriṃśatisahasrāṇi lakṣāṇyaṣṭādaśaiva ca  || 47.3 ||
eṣā kṛtayuge saṃkhyā sandhyāsandhyāṃśamānataḥ  |
lakṣyāṇyaṣṭau tathā cāṣṭau sahasrāṇi yudhiṣṭhira  || 47.4 ||
dvāpare bhānamicchanti sandhyāsandhyāṃśamānata  |
sahasrāṇi ca catvāri tathā lakṣacatuṣṭayam  || 47.5 ||
mānaṃ kaliyugasyaitatsandhyāsandhyāṃśamānataḥ  |
alpakṣīrapradā gāvo hyalpasasyā ca medinī  || 47.6 ||
alpodakāstathā meghāḥ svalpavidyāstathā dvijāḥ  |
pūrṇe tu ṣoḍaśe varṣe narāḥ palitayauvanāḥ  || 47.7 ||
daśame dvādaśe varṣe nārī garbhadharā bhavet |
śūdrā dharmaparā nityaṃ kalau kāle digambarāḥ  || 47.8 ||
ekavarṇāḥ prajāḥ sarvā rājā mleccho bhaviṣyati  |
kalau yuge tathā prāpte kalirūpe ca mādhave  || 47.9 ||
nāgnihotraṃ na vedāśca na dharmo na ca yājanam  |
na satyaṃ na tapo dānaṃ na sattvaṃ na ca devatāḥ  || 47.10 ||
vedavikrayiṇo viprā antyajānāṃ gṛhe gṛhe  |
vedādeśaṃ kariṣyanti vedaviplavakārakāḥ  || 47.11 ||
kanyāvikrayiṇaḥ pāpāstathā kanyopajīvinaḥ  |
sahasrāṃśo na dharmasya kalā caikā pravartitā  || 47.12 ||
yatra siddhastatra tīrthaṃ jale snāsyanti mānavāḥ  |
śūdrā patnī dvijānāṃ tu bhaviṣyati gṛhe gṛhe  || 47.13 ||
adharottarabhāvena bhaviṣyanti kalau narāḥ  |
bauddhāḥ kṣapaṇakāḥ pāpā nagnā malinakaśmalāḥ  || 47.14 ||
viḍambayanti bālānāṃ mohitāḥ pāpakarmaṇām  |
na guruṃ manyate śiṣyaḥ putraśca pitaraṃ tathā  || 47.15 ||
svavaṃśadravyahartāraḥ pravrajyāveṣadhāriṇaḥ  |
liṅgopajīvinaḥ pāpāstathā bhasmopajīvinaḥ  || 47.16 ||
vaivasvate'ntare prāpte kalau sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan kalau yadyadbhaviṣyati  || 47.17 ||
oṃkārasyaiva cotpattiṃ vidhānaṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
kathayāmi samāsena yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ tvayā'nagha  || 47.18 ||
kīrtanādasya devasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt |
oṃ mityekākṣara rājan vyāharan samanusmaran  || 47.19 ||
yaḥ prayāti tyajan dehaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  |
vedamātā ca gāyatrī oṃkāraprabhavā tathā  || 47.20 ||
oṃmityekākṣare tattve brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
oṃkāro vedamūlaṃ tu śrutiśākhaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 47.21 ||
phalaṃ caiva tu puṣpaṃ ca parṇāni smṛtirāgamaḥ  |
yathādau sarvavidyānāmoṃkāraḥ paripaṭhyate  || 47.22 ||
tathādau sarvadevānāmādidevo maheśvaraḥ  |
sandhyātrayaṃ trikālādi oṃkāre parikīrtitam  || 47.23 ||
agnitrayaṃ trayo lokāstrivargaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
aṣṭaṣaṣṭiṃ ca tīrthānāṃ brahmaṇe śivakīrtitam  || 47.24 ||
ekena ca śataṃ pūrṇaṃ rudrāṇāṃ parikīrtitam  |
kedāre śatamekaṃ oṃkāraikottaraṃ śatam  || 47.25 ||
pañcabrahma pañcavaktramoṃkāraṃ liṅgamuttamam  |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni liṅgāni āsamudrāntagocare  || 47.26 ||
na teṣāṃ pañcavaktrāṇi tyaktvoṃkāraṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 47.27 ||
dānavairnirjitā devā narmadātīramāśritāḥ  |
avadrutāḥ kaṅkolaistu kālikeyaiśca kālakaiḥ  || 47.28 ||
te devā brahmasahitā īśvaraṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  |
bṛhaspatistataḥ prāha brahmāṇaṃ prati bhārata  || 47.29 ||
iṣṭiṃ kuru mahāraudrīṃ dānavānāṃ kṣayaṃkarīm  |
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā tadā taṃ devamantriṇam  || 47.30 ||
mamaiva vismṛtā mantrā dānavānāṃ bhayena ca  |
etasminnantare bhittvā pātālāni ca sapta ca  || 47.31 ||
oṃkārapūrvakaṃ rājan bhūrbhuvaḥsvaśca kīrtayan  |
parvatādutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  || 47.32 ||
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyaṃ jvālāmālāsamāśritam  |
ādimadhyāntahīnaṃ ca na dṛṣṭaṃ paramaṃ kvacit || 47.33 ||
caturvargaiścaturvedairvedāṅganigamaiḥ svayam  |
uvāca vacanaṃ śambhurbrahmāṇaṃ lokabhāvanam  || 47.34 ||
saumyāṃ caiva tu bho brahmaṃllokānāṃ śāntikāriṇīm  |
mayā samārpitā vedā iṣṭiṃ kuru yathepsayā  || 47.35 ||
tato brahmā cakāreṣṭiṃ raudrīṃ daityakṣayaṃkarīm  |
iṣṭiṃ caiva tata saumyāṃ lokānāṃ śāntikāriṇīm  || 47.36 ||
tato'surā mahārāja dṛṣṭvā ceṣṭiṃ bhayaṃkarīm  |
brahmaśāpabhayodvignā gatāste tu diśo daśa  || 47.37 ||
oṃkārasya prabhāveṇa sarve devāstu nirbhayāḥ  |
tato'bhyarcya sureśānaṃ devāste tridivaṃ yayuḥ  || 47.38 ||
kalpāntagaṃ mahāliṅgaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
kāmadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva oṃkāraṃ viddhi pārthiva  || 47.39 ||
tasmiṃlliṅge tu līyante kalpānte sarvadevatāḥ  |
amaraṃ brahma vetyāhurhariṃ siddheśvaraṃ tathā  || 47.40 ||
piṅgaleśvaramādityaṃ somaṃ pitrīśvaraṃ tathā  |
yatra siddhāstrayo vedāḥ saṣaḍaṅgapadakramāḥ  || 47.41 ||
tena siddheśvaraṃ viddhi sarvasiddhipradāyakam  |
kathitaṃ parvatasyāgre liṅgakoṭisamanvitam  || 47.42 ||
arcanāttasya liṅgasya viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
kalpe kalpe mahārāja kṣīyante sarvadevatāḥ  || 47.43 ||
muktvā tu pañcaliṅgāni mārkaṇḍaṃ nārmadaṃ nṛpa  |
avimuktaṃ ca kedāramoṃkāramamareśvaram  || 47.44 ||
tathaiva ca mahākālamevaṃ liṅgaṃ tu bhārata  |
puṇyāni pañcaliṅgāni prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet || 47.45 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate  |
ekā kālī mahākāle vasedvai vyāpinī sadā  || 47.46 ||
sapādakoṭistīrthānāṃ mahākāle vasetnṛpa  |
śivalokena kāverī śivakṣetre sthitā nṛpa  || 47.47 ||
catuḥ krośābhyantarato brahmahatyā na sarpati  |
āgneyaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ ca tasyāstīre samāśritam  || 47.48 ||
śivakhyātamiti khyātaṃ tīrthe tu kurunandana  |
snātamātro narastatra sa bhave na punarbhavet || 47.49 ||
kīṭapakṣipataṅgādi tiryagyonigatā narāḥ  |
mucyante tatra pāpena śivasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 47.50 ||
tatra yaḥ kurute śrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca tilodakam  |
yugakoṭisahasraṃ tu pitaraste na tarpitāḥ  || 47.51 ||
sarveṣāmeva liṅgānāṃ divyaṃ vā'tra prakīrtitam  |
tatra snāto divaṃ yāti na viśedyonisaṅkaṭe  || 47.52 ||
koṭiyajñaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate  |
aṣṭakoṭistu tīrthānāṃ kedāre kathitā nṛpa  || 47.53 ||
darśanādarcanāttasya sparśātmokṣaphalaṃ nṛṇām  |
kedārasyodake pīte punarjanma na vidyate  || 47.54 ||
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā payaḥ pānaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
tasyodare bhavelliṅgaṃ ṣaṇmāsādbrahmacāriṇaḥ  || 47.55 ||
kedāradarśanādeva śivaloke mahīyate  |
vārāṇasī mahāpuṇyā triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  || 47.56 ||
antarikṣe purī sā tu mṛtyulokasya bāhyataḥ  |
pañcakrośāntare yāvadbrahmahatyā na sarpati  || 47.57 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatikoṭyastu liṅgānāṃ tatra bhārata  |
gaṅgāvaruṇayormadhye snānaṃ kṛtvā yathoditam  || 47.58 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto devavanmodate divi  |
tatra yastyajati prāṇān chivaṃ dhyātvā tu mānavaḥ  || 47.59 ||
sahasrakulamuddhṛtya śivalokaṃ sa gacchati  |
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 47.60 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṛṇāṃ prītirakṣayā  |
tatra yastyajati prāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  || 47.61 ||
trinetraḥ śūlapāṇistu śivasyānucaro bhavet |
avimuktasya liṅgasya sparśanātmuktirāpyate  || 47.62 ||
kalātrayaṃ tu tatrāste kāśīpuryāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
gaṅgāsāgarasaṃbhede catasrastu kalāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 47.63 ||
gaṅgāsahasravaktreṇa praviṣṭā yatra sāgaram  |
snānāvagāhanātpānātpiṇḍadānācca tarpaṇāt || 47.64 ||
gacchecchivapuraṃ tatra pitṛbhiḥ saha mānavaḥ  |
aparaṃ kālarudraṃ tu saptapātālavāsinam  || 47.65 ||
hāṭakaṃ viddhi taṃ devaṃ na tu paśyanti mānavāḥ  |
pūjyate suradaityaiśca surasiddhaniṣevitam  || 47.66 ||
gaṅgeśvaradvitīyaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ sāgareśvaram  |
caturthaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ tu catasraśca kalā iti  || 47.67 ||
kalāpañcātmakaṃ rudramāsamudrāntagocare  |
varjayitvā maheśānamoṃkāraṃ kāmarūpiṇam  || 47.68 ||
pañcabrahmapañcavaktraṃ navaśaktisamanvitam  |
oṃkāraṃ kalpagātīre śivena kathitaṃ purā  || 47.69 ||
tena puṇyātmake loke lokatritayapūjitam  |
śaṅkhakundendusaṃkāśaṃ sadyovaktraṃ tu paścimam  || 47.70 ||
ṛgvedo nirgato yasmādbrahmā tatrādhidevatā  |
uttaraṃ vāmadevaṃ tu pītābhaṃ sumanoharam  || 47.71 ||
yajurvedodbhavaṃ viddhi viṣṇustatrādhidevatā  |
aghora meghavarṇābhaṃ yāmyāṃ ca diśi cāsthitam  || 47.72 ||
sāmavedodbhavaṃ viddhi sūryakālāgnidaivatam  |
