pūrve tatpuruṣaṃ jñeyaṃ kuṅkumāruṇasannibham  || 47.73 ||
atharvaṃ nirgataṃ turyamāpastatrādhidevatāḥ  |
īśānastava vaktraṃ tu pañcavarṇaṃ mahātanum  || 47.74 ||
śrutisiddhāntasaṅgītaṃ somaṃ tatrādhidevatā  |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ sadāśivaṃ nāma nirbhāgaṃ ca nirāmayam  || 47.75 ||
nirlakṣaṃ lakṣahīnaṃ tu jñātvā mokṣe na saṃśayaḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājannoṃkārasya tu varṇanam  || 47.76 ||
sahasrāsyasya no śaktirekavaktrasya kā kathā  |
snāpayitvodakenaiva bilvapatreṇa pūjayet || 47.77 ||
caturvarṣasahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate  |
oṃkāradakṣiṇāmūrtau prāṇatyāga karoti yaḥ  || 47.78 ||
varṣakoṭisahasrāṇi vasetmāheśvare pure  |
prāsādaṃ ca maṭhaṃ cāpi sudhayeṣṭakasaṃyutam  || 47.79 ||
citramālekhyamūle ca patākādhvajaśobhitam  |
vitānāṃ kiṅkiṇīyuktaṃ netraṃ vaṃśodbhavaṃ śubham  || 47.80 ||
pañcavarṇakaśobhāḍhyamoṃkārasya tu kārayet |
pañcāmṛtaiḥ snāpayitvā candanāgurukuṃkamaiḥ  || 47.81 ||
samāveṣṭya parīdhānairnānāvastraiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  |
hemamauktikaratnaiśca saghṛtaṃ gugguluṃ dahet || 47.82 ||
ghaṇṭāṃ ca dīpakaṃ caiva vidhūmārārtikaṃ ca yat |
mṛdaṅgān paṭahāṃścaiva veṇuṃ vīṇāṃ ca gītakam  || 47.83 ||
kāhalī śaṅkhavādyāni kāṃsyatālādyameva ca  |
vyajanaṃ geḍukaṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ dhvajadaṇḍakam  || 47.84 ||
hema cānnadharādīni gṛhāṃśca grāmapattanam  |
yadvā tadvā nṛpaśreṣṭha oṃkārāya nivedayet || 47.85 ||
tasya dānaphalasyeha saṃkhyā kartuṃ na śakyate  |
siddheśvarauṃkārayostu candrasūryagrahagrahe  || 47.86 ||
dhvajamālākulaṃ kuryāttasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
yāvatī taṃ tu saṃkhyā'sti vāyunoddhūyate punaḥ  || 47.87 ||
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi śivaloke vasennṛpa  |
yugakoṭisahasrāṇi yugakoṭiśatāni ca  || 47.88 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā brahmaviṣṇuśivālaye  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājanoṃkārotpattilakṣaṇam  || 47.89 ||
brahmaṇā tu kṛtaṃ tasya stotraṃ tvaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  || 47.90 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe oṃkāramahimānuvarṇano nāma saptacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 48

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
brahmaṇe kathito mantra oṃkāreṇa tato'nagha  |
brahmāpi tadvacaḥ śrutvā stotrametadudāharat || 48.1 ||
oṃ vyomasaṃkhyāyite vyomaharāya sarvavyāpine  |
anantāya anāthāya amṛtāya dhruvāya ca  || 48.2 ||
śambhavāya śāśvatāya yogapīṭhasaṃsthitāya  |
nityaṃ yogayogine vyomaharāya  || 48.3 ||
oṃ namaḥ śivāya sarvaprabhavāya śivāya īśānāya  || 48.4 ||
mūrdhāya tatpuruṣāya vaktrāya aghorāya hṛdayāya vāsudevāya guhyāya sadyojātāya mūrtaye oṃkārāya namo namaḥ  || 48.5 ||
kalātīto'vyayo buddho vajradehopamardanaḥ  || 48.6
adhyakṣaśca vidhuḥ śāstā pinākī tridaśādhipaḥ  |
agnī rudro hutāśaśca piṅgalaḥ pāvano hara  || 48.7 ||
jvalano dahano vasturbhasmāntaśca kṣamāntakaḥ  |
apamṛtyuharo dhātā vidhātā kartṛsaṃjñakaḥ  || 48.8 ||
kālo dharmapatiḥ śāstā viyoktā navamaḥ priyaḥ  |
nirmitto vāruṇo hantā krūradṛṣṭirbhayāvahaḥ  || 48.9 ||
ūrdhvadṛṣṭirvirūpākṣo daṃṣṭrāvān dhūmralocanaḥ  |
vālo hyatibalaścaiva pāśahasto mahābalaḥ  || 48.10 ||
śveto virūpo rudraśca dīrghabāhurjaḍāntakaḥ  |
śīghro laghurvāyuvego bhīmaśca vaḍavāmukhaḥ  || 48.11 ||
pañcaśīrṣā kapardī ca sūkṣmastīkṣṇaḥ kṣapāntakaḥ  |
nidhīśo raudravān dhanvī saumyadehaḥ pramardanaḥ  || 48.12 ||
anantapālako dhāraḥ pātāleśo vṛṣadhvajaḥ  |
sadhūmraḥ śāśvataḥ śarvaḥ sarvapiṅgaḥ karālavān  || 48.13 ||
viṣṇurīśo mahātmā ca sukho mṛtyuvivārjitaḥ  |
śambhurvibhurgaṇādhyakṣastryakṣaścaiva divaspatiḥ  || 48.14 ||
saṃvādaśca vivādaśca prabhaviṣṇurvivardhanaḥ  |
śatamekottaraṃ yāvadrudrāṇāṃ saṃkhyayā smṛtam  || 48.15 ||
śatamekottaraṃ sarvamoṃkāre ca pratiṣṭhitam  |
stotraṃ kṛtvā tathā brahmā devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 48.16 ||
bhūmau praṇamya sāṣṭāṅgaṃ kṛtvā caiva pradakṣiṇam  |
manasā saṃsmaran devaṃ tasthau lokapitāmahaḥ  || 48.17 ||
stotraṃ śrutvā bhagavato brahmaṇo lomaharṣaṇam  |
devadevo'bravīdvākyaṃ brahmāṇaṃ prati bhārata  || 48.18 ||
stotreṇānena divyena tuṣṭo'haṃ te varaṃ vṛṇu  |
dadāmi te na sandeho duṣprāpyaṃ tridaśairapi  || 48.19 ||

brahmovāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
pañcavaktreṣu yajanaṃ brahma nāma bhavatviha  || 48.20 ||

hara uvāca - 
evaṃ bhavatu vai brahman satyametattavoditam  |
brahmaṇo dharmapūjā vai tadā prabhṛti bhārata  || 48.21 ||

brahmovāca - 
paṭhiṣyanti stavaṃ raudramoṃkārasya tavāgrataḥ  |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ sadā tadgatamānasāḥ  || 48.22 ||
sarvakāmamavāpsyanti ceha loke paratra ca  |
yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 48.23 ||
śatamekottaraṃ nityaṃ paṭhitvā ca divaṃ vrajet |
evamuktvā tadā brahmā namaskṛtya maheśvaram  || 48.24 ||
divyayānasamārūḍho brahmalokaṃ mudā yayau  |
caturyugasahasreṇa brahmaṇo'haḥ prakīrtitam  || 48.25 ||
anenaiva tu mānena śataṃ brahmā hi jīvati  |
pitāmahaśataṃ yāvadviṣṇormānaṃ vidhīyate  || 48.26 ||
oṃkāranimiṣārdhena sahasrāṇi caturdaśa  |
vinaśyanti paraṃ viṣṇorasaṃkhyātāḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 48.27 ||
evaṃ brahmagatiṃ jñātvā śivamantaḥ sadārcayet |
śivājñā vartate liṅge tasmālliṅgaṃ sadārcayet || 48.28 ||
dveṣṭi liṅgaṃ tu yo mohātsarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ śivasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 48.29 ||
bauddhakṣapaṇapākhaṇḍā mithyātattvavicakṣaṇāḥ  |
naṣṭāstu nāśitā ye vai śivārādhanavārjitāḥ  || 48.30 ||
janmajanmāntarābhyāsātte prayānti rasātalam  |
purāṇeṣu tathā yuddhvā śivoktaṃ dharmamācaret || 48.31 ||
saduṣṭaḥ pāpabuddhistu yo'nyaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 48.32 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya śivaloke mahīyate  || 48.33 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pañcabrahmātmakastavo nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 49

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchetmahābhāga revākapilasaṅgamam  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 49.1 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
dināni navasārdhāni rāhugraste niśākare  || 49.2 ||
vṛddhiṃ yāti mahārāja puṇyavṛddhyā na saṃśayaḥ  |
graste tu rāhuṇā sūrye dināni ca daśaiva tu  || 49.3 ||
vardhate kapilābhedastadvadeva viśāmpate  |
revāyāḥ kapilāyoge vārāṇasyāḥ samāgame  || 49.4 ||
samānaṃ phalāṃ uddiṣṭaṃ tilodakenāpi vidyate  |
vārāṇasīsamā revā kapilāyāśca saṅgame  || 49.5 ||
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte brahmalabdhavarodbhavāḥ  |
rudrāvartaṃ brahmāvartaṃ sūryāvartaṃ tathā'param  || 49.6 ||
kapilānarmadāyoge jñeyametattrayaṃ punaḥ  |
narmadābhedanaṃ yatra caturhastapramāṇataḥ  || 49.7 ||
revākapilayo rājaṃstatrāvartadvayaṃ smṛtam  |
pippalāvāhinī tatra saptapātālavāsinī  || 49.8 ||
tatraiva kapilāvartaṃ pippalāvartameva ca  |
kāmayanti hi tīrthaṃ ca pitarastṛptidāyakam  || 49.9 ||
tasmātputraḥ prayatnena pitṛbhyaśca yathāvidhi  |
jalāñjaliṃ tilairmiśraṃ dadyātpiṇḍaṃ ca yatnataḥ  || 49.10 ||
pitṝn samuddharedghorācchrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tu mānavaḥ  |
revākapilayoryoge śuciḥ snātvā tu mānavaḥ  || 49.11 ||
yaḥ paśyedamaraṃ tatra phalaṃ tasyāśvamedhikam  |
candrasūryoparāge tu parvakāle viśeṣataḥ  || 49.12 ||
gandhaṃ dhūpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ dīpamālāṃ ca kārayet |
tilataṇḍulamiśrairyaḥ kuryālliṅgasya cārcanam  || 49.13 ||
kuṅkumena samālipya raktavastraiḥ praveṣṭayet |
puṣpamālārcanaṃ kṛtvā hemaratnādibhistathā  || 49.14 ||
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca himavāṃśca mahodadhiḥ  |
tāvadyugasahastrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate  || 49.15 ||
kauśeyaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ ca kārpāsaṃ raktatāntavam  |
vaijayantīvitānaṃ ca kalaśopari vardhayet || 49.16 ||
pañcaratnasamāyuktaṃ kiṅkiṇīravasaṃyutam  |
tattantusaṃkhyayā yāvanmuhūrtamiha bhārata  || 49.17 ||
tāvatkālaṃ vasetsvarga umāmāheśvare pure  |
īśānamaparaṃ caiva sāmānyātkathitaṃ purā  || 49.18 ||
kapilāpūrvabhāge tu nātidūre vyavasthitam  |
arcanāttasya liṅgasya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt || 49.19 ||
śuklāṣṭaṃ yāṃ kārttike tu phalaṃ śatagaṇottaram  |
saṃkrame ca vyatīpāte tasya saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 49.20 ||
upahārapradānena kapileśvarapūjanāt |
varṣāṇāmayutaṃ sārddhaṃ loke krīḍati bhāskare  || 49.21 ||
mṛtavatsā tathā bandhyā garbhasrāvā ca yā bhavet |
raktavastraiḥ pañcaratnaiḥ snānaṃ sā ca samācaret || 49.22 ||
caturdaśyāṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ kapilāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
subhagā jīvaputrā ca satyametacchivoditam  || 49.23 ||
umayā ca varo datto nārībhyaśca prasādataḥ  |
kapilā nirgatā yasmātnarmadāyāṃ prasarpati  || 49.24 ||
tīrthānāmaṣṭasāhasraṃ kāmabhogaphalapradam  |
āste tatra mahārāja śivena kathitaṃ purā  || 49.25 ||
kapilā ca tato deyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā  |
brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ  || 49.26 ||
upavāsaparo nityaṃ tārpitāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ  |
hemajāleśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ tatraiva siddhidam  || 49.27 ||
arcanāttasya devasya yamalokaṃ na paśyati  |
vasudāno dhundhumāraścakravartī purā'bhavat || 49.28 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyāddivi devatvamāptavān  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  || 49.29 ||
kṣatriyāṇāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha koṭitīrthaprabhāvataḥ  |
ulūkaiḥ pātitān yatra koṭitīrthe śirāṃsyatha  || 49.30 ||
kākānāṃ jalamadhye tu śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehāstu te kākā yānamāśritāḥ  || 49.31 ||
vidyādharāṇāṃ rājāno mayā dṛṣṭāḥ purānagha  |
vṛndāśca jambukānāṃ tu vyāghrāṇāṃ ca bhayena vai  || 49.32 ||
tathā meghāvṛte kāle narmadājalamāviśan  |
yakṣalokaṃ tu te prāptāḥ sarvakāmaphalodayam  || 49.33 ||
jambukeśvaramityevaṃ tiryagyonivimokṣaṇam  |
pṛthivyāṃ naimiṣaṃ tīrthamantarikṣe ca puṣkaram  || 49.34 ||
vārāṇasīprayāgaṃ ca trailokye tvamareśvaram  |
trayastriṃśatkoṭibhistu surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 49.35 ||
tatra snātaśca rājendra hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
siddhā sarasvatī tatra tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 49.36 ||
yaḥ kaścitkurute śrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītivarddhanam  |
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha mānavaḥ  || 49.37 ||
liṅgaṃ sārasvataṃ nāma brahmahatyāvyāpohanam  |
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi itihāsaṃ purātanam  || 49.38 ||
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmāñchakratulyaparākramaḥ  || 49.39 ||
dhundhumāra iti khyātaḥ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ  |
yajñayājī dānaśīlo devatātithipūjakaḥ  || 49.40 ||
niravadyāḥ prajāstasya bhayadāridryavarjitāḥ  |
sapādalakṣavarṣāṇi prajā jīvanti bhārata  || 49.41 ||
yajñotsavavivāhaiśca vedamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ  |
svayaṃ kāmadughā dhenuḥ pṛthivī sasyaśālinī  || 49.42 ||
caturvarṣasahasrāṇi prākṛtā itare janāḥ  |
kauśeyapaṭṭaṃ vṛkṣeṣu baddhaṃ sarvatra bhārata  || 49.43 ||
yajñahomasahasraistu sadā dohamayīṃ nṛpa  |
evaṃ śaśāsa pṛthivīṃ yathā śakrastriviṣṭapam  || 49.44 ||
ekasmin samaye rājā pratīhāramuvāca ha  |
ādeśaya nṛpān sarvānnānādeśasamudbhavān  || 49.45 ||
pratīhārasamādiṣṭāḥ samāyātāstato nṛpāḥ  |
mṛgayāṃ tu sa taiḥ sarvaiḥ kartuṃ vindhyaṃ jagāma ha  || 49.46 ||
vedadhvanitanirghoṣairdvijānāmagnihotriṇām  |
nāditā triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātā saptakalpagā  || 49.47 ||
tatropaśobhitaṃ ramyaṃ vicitraṃ vanamaṇḍalam  |
hatvā jīvasahasrāṇi kṣatriyaiḥ saha bhārata  || 49.48 ||
viveśa ca vanaṃ sarvaṃ tataḥ paramadāruṇam  |
bhīmarūpaṃ mahāghoraṃ duṣprekṣyaṃ ca suduḥsaham  || 49.49 ||
meghanādena garjantaṃ sutarāṃ lomaharṣaṇam  |
vārāhaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ ca daṃṣṭrāyugalabhīṣaṇam  || 49.50 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ tatra vārāhaṃ nṛpasattamaḥ  |
uvāca kṣatriyān sarvānna dṛṣṭaṃ na mayā śrutam  || 49.51 ||
etādṛśaṃ varāhasya rūpaṃ vai lomaharṣaṇam  |
ityuktvā pāśamādāya yāvaddhantuṃ samudyataḥ  || 49.52 ||
tāvadvāyuvapurbhūtvā niryātaḥ prāṇapīḍitaḥ  |
viveśa jalamadhye ca koṭitīrthe narādhipa  || 49.53 ||
pṛṣṭhato'nujagāma'tha sa rājā hayavāhanaḥ  |
praviṣṭamātraḥ payasi varāhastu viśāmpate  || 49.54 ||
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehastu kāmikaṃ yānamāsthitaḥ  |
kimidaṃ prāha taṃ rājā vārāhaṃ devarūpaṇam  || 49.55 ||
hṛdi vismayamāpanno satyametacca brūhi me  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vārāho devarūpadhṛk || 49.56 ||
prahasannabravīdvākyaṃ śṛṇu rājanmahāmate  |
aṅgadaṃ nāma tu gaṇaṃ viddhi māṃ śaṅkarasya tu  || 49.57 ||
gaṇaiśca devamukhyaiśca umayā ca maheśvaraḥ  |
krīḍannāste kadācittu tasyāgre nṛpasattama  || 49.58 ||
tatra gītaṃ mayā gītaṃ ramyaṃ daṇḍakalakṣaṇam  |
dṛṣṭvorvaśīṃ tathā rambhāmabhūvaṃ kāmamohitaḥ  || 49.59 ||
vyāharaṃ chūkarīṃ vāṇīṃ visvaro vikṛtānanaḥ  |
vihvalena mayā tatra hyapsarobhistu krīḍitam  || 49.60 ||
tādṛśaṃ māṃ tudṛṣṭvāvaikāmakrīḍāvaśagatam  |
śaśāpa nandī kopātmā śukaro'medhyabhugbhava  || 49.61 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi bhramiṣyasi mahītale  |
brahmāpi naiva śaknoti śivasya tu prakīrtitam  || 49.62 ||
tvaṃ tu gāmaṭamāno'pi kiṅkarasyāpi kiṅkaraḥ  |
kupitaṃ nandinaṃ jñātvā bhayabhītāntarātmanā  || 49.63 ||
prasādito mayā nandī śāpāntaṃ varamādiśat |
darśanāddhundhumārasyakoṭitīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 49.64 ||
tyaktvā tu śūkarīṃ yoniṃ punaḥ pratyāgamiṣyasi  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājanvārāhīṃ yonimāśritaḥ  || 49.65 ||
yathā hi kilviṣātmuktastīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
oṃkāradarśanādrājan revātoyapariṣkṛtaḥ  || 49.66 ||
prāpto gandharvayoniṃ tu darśanāttava suvrata  |
viṣādaṃ tyaja rājendra gahanā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  || 49.67 ||
dharme buddhiṃ samādhāya sarvabhūtahito bhava  |
janmato maraṇaṃ rājanmaraṇājjanmasambhavaḥ  || 49.68 ||
jñātvā śubhāśubhaṃ karma tvamātmānaṃ samuddhara  |
svayamevārjitaṃ karma svayamevopabhujyate  || 49.69 ||
svayaṃ kartā ca bhoktā ca śubhasyāpyaśubhasya ca  |
svasti vo'sti gamiṣyāmi evamuktvā jagāma ha  || 49.70 ||
dhriyamāṇātapatrastu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
śivadhyānaparo bhūtvā kailāse nyavasatsukham  || 49.71 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vārāhasvargārohaṇaṃ nāmaikonapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 50

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sa tasminnṛpati śreṣṭhastṛṣitaḥ śrāntavāhanaḥ  |
hayaṃ mumoca rājā vai sarvopaskarameva ca  || 50.1 ||
smaranūrdhvagatiṃ tāvadupaviṣṭaḥ śilātale  |
reṇudhvastastato'śvo vai praviṣṭaḥ saptakalpagām  || 50.2 ||
pānasnānādikaṃ kṛtvā hyantarikṣasthito hayaḥ  |
brahmatejaḥ sthito bhūtvā brahmayānaṃ samāśrayat || 50.3 ||
atyadbhutaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā paraṃ vismayamāgata  |
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā turaṅgaṃ taṃ dvijarṣabham  || 50.4 ||
kimetatkāraṇaṃ brahmañcchaṃsa me'dyayathocitam  |
uvāca tadvacaḥ śrutvā hayarūpo dvijottamaḥ  || 50.5 ||
brahmarṣirgālavaścāhaṃ kurukṣetre purā sthitaḥ  |
aśvapratigrahāddagdhastvaśvayoniṃ samāśrita  || 50.6 ||
dāvāgninā ca yaddagdhamudakāttatprarohati  |
duṣṭapratigrahāddagdho na prarohetkadācana  || 50.7 ||
drumasenaḥ purā cāsīdrājā paramadhārmika  |
ayodhyādhipatiścāsau cakravartī mahābalaḥ  || 50.8 ||
rāhusūryasamāyoge kurukṣetraṃ jagāma ha  |
gajānaśvān samādāya hiraṇyaṃ gāstathaiva ca  || 50.9 ||
māṇikyavajravaidūryavāsāṃsi vividhāni ca  |
brāhmaṇārthe nṛpaśreṣṭha mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 50.10 ||
gṛhāṇi sāptabhaumāni dhūpitāni tu kāñcanaiḥ  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhāni brāhmaṇebhyo yathāvidhi  || 50.11 ||
dattvā sa yācayāmāsa saktuprasthavrate sthitam  |
rājottamakulaṃ vipramuñchavṛttiṃ samāśritam  || 50.12 ||
śrāddhakālaḥ pitṝṇāṃ me bhojanaṃ kriyatāmiti  |

ṛṣiruvāca - 
rājño hi darśanaṃ ghoraṃ medhāmathanamakṣamam  || 50.13 ||
dṛṣṭvā caiva mahīpālamādityaṃ cāvalokayet |
dvijātparataro nāsti pratigrahaparāṅmukhāt || 50.14 ||
asatpratigrahaṃ gṛhṇannarakaṃ yāti vai dhruvam  |
bhāryāpratigrahagrāhī yācasvā'nyaṃ dvijottamam  || 50.15 ||
ṛṣe rājā vacaḥ śrutvā pratīhāraṃ tathābravīt |
ghoṣaṇā kriyatāṃ śīghraṃ sthāneśvaranivāsinām  || 50.16 ||
pratigrahāya yaḥ kaścitsa cāyātu iha satvaram  |
kṛte tu ghoṣaṇe kaścitnāsītnṛpapratigrahī  || 50.17 ||
tatastu kupito rājā sthānaṃ tacca nininda ca  |
abrahmaṇyamidaṃ sthānaṃ na vedo na ca yājanam  || 50.18 ||
jugupsitvā tu tān sarvāṃstūṣṇīṃ caiva babhūva ha  |
tasya vākyaṃ tu tacchrutvā rājānaṃ cedamabruvam  || 50.19 ||
gālavo'haṃ dvijaśreṣṭhaścaturvedī mahātapāḥ  |
yajñayājī tapasvī ca sarvabhūtahite rataḥ  || 50.20 ||
anugrahamimaṃ viddhi uddhariṣye bhavārṇavāt |

rājovāca - 
dadāmi te na sandehaḥ tvameko munisattamaḥ  || 50.21 ||
mudgalādyairdvijaiḥ sarvairvāryamāṇo'pi cānagha  |
gṛhīto'śvarathastatra mayābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  || 50.22 ||
tataḥ sa māṃ namaskṛtya drumaseno yayau nṛpa  |
mayāpi cāgnihotrādikarma tyaktvā yathāsukham  || 50.23 ||
nānāvidhāni divyāni strībhiḥ sārddhaṃ sukhāni tu  |
krīḍato'pi tadarthaṃ vai yāvatme ca kṣayaṃ gatam  || 50.24 ||
evamuktvā yayau vipro brahmalokaṃ sanātanam  |
ekākī ca tato rājā cintayāmāsa bhārata  || 50.25 ||
ekākī yadi yāsyāmi gatāśvaścaraṇena tu  |
rājāno māṃ hasiṣyanti vacanaiḥ svaiḥ parasparam  || 50.26 ||
dasyubhirnihataścāsya haya ityevamādibhiḥ  |
aśvopaskaramādāya śirasā ca kathaṃ mayā  || 50.27 ||
praveṣṭavyaṃ puraṃ caitatmahālajjākaraṃ mama  |
adya yāvatmayā tāvadbrāhmaṇārohaṇaṃ kṛtam  || 50.28 ||
pāpasyāsya viśuddhyarthaṃ pravekṣyāmi hutāśanam  |
evaṃ vicintayābhāsa rājā tatraiva satvaram  || 50.29 ||
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāśritya śuṣkakāṣṭhāni cāharat |
tataḥ prajvālya kāṣṭhāni kṛtvā ca triḥpradakṣiṇam  || 50.30 ||
namaskṛtya hutāśaṃ ca viveśa svagṛhaṃ yathā  |
nirjitya tejasā tejaḥ pāvakasya tadā nṛpaḥ  || 50.31 ||
caturbhujā trinetrā tu muktābharaṇabhūṣitā  |
taṃ gṛhītvā kareṇaiva idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 50.32 ||
aprāptaṃ maraṇaṃ rājan kālo vihitastava  |
akasmātsāhasaṃ deva yuktaṃ na pratibhāti me  || 50.33 ||
kālaprāptaṃ pumāṃsaṃ tu na rakṣedīśvaraḥ svayam  |

rājovāca - 
kā'si tvaṃ ca varārohe hyumā gaṅgā'thavā ramā  || 50.34 ||
kathayasva mahābhāge mama tvaṃ bhaktidāyinī  |

stryuvāca - 
nāhaṃ gaṅgā na vāṇī vā kapilāṃ viddhi māṃ nṛpa  || 50.35 ||
enāṃ rudrādviniṣkrāntāṃ narmadātalavāhinīm  |
vasudānasya yajñe tu revākapilasaṅgamaḥ  || 50.36 ||
umā kātyāyanī gaṅgā yamunā gautamī tathā  |
sarasvatī tathā śiprā varaṇā ca śubhāpagā  || 50.37 ||
śatadrūścandrabhāgā ca sindhū revāmalā tathā  |
vitastā carmaṇā devī somāvabhṛthamadhyataḥ  || 50.38 ||
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ snānārthaṃ hṛtavāribhiḥ  |
tilodakairmunīnāṃ tu praṇītaḥ kalaśodakaiḥ  || 50.39 ||
tathā somarasaiścaiva ghṛtakhaṇḍādimiśritaiḥ  |
babhūvātipravāho vai ijyājanyo mahān purā  || 50.40 ||
etadbhūtaṃ mahatpuṇyamudayācalamāśritam  |
rudrāvartapadaṃ cātra vidyate nṛpasattama  || 50.41 ||
evamukto yayau rājā devī cāntaradhīyata  |
hṛṣṭastuṣṭaścakravartī mārkaṇḍeyāśramaṃ yayau  || 50.42 ||
gatvā praṇamya tamṛṣimupaviṣṭastathāgrataḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kuśalaṃ te nṛpaśreṣṭha dharmācāravidāṃ vara  || 50.43 ||
santyajya ca kathaṃ sainyamekākī tvamihāgataḥ  |

rājovāca - 
adya me saphalaṃ janma tvatpādāmbujadarśanāt || 50.44 ||
dhundhumārastathā rājā kathayāmāsa pūrvakam  |
mārkaṇḍeyastataḥ śrutvā vṛttāntaṃ pṛthivīpateḥ  || 50.45 ||
revākapilayoryoge snātvā stotraṃ cakāra ha  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti revākapilasaṅgame  || 50.46 ||
umā kātyāyanī gaṅgā yamunā gautamī tathā  |
sarasvatī tathā śiprā varaṇā ca śubhāpagā  || 50.47 ||
śatadrūścandrabhāgā ca sindhū revāmalā tathā  |
vitastā carmaṇādevī bāhudā vāruṇī tathā  || 50.48 ||
sarayūrgaṇḍakī caiva ghargharā badarī tathā  |
gomatī veṇukī caiva pārā vetravatī śubhā  || 50.49 ||
vipāśā ca tathā vāhā śaṅkhinī ca payoṣṇikā  |
godāvarī ca kāverī bhīmā kṛṣṇā tathā śubhā  || 50.50 ||
subhadrā ca tathā bhadrā karatoyātha mālinī  |
etāḥ sarvāstvamevāsi sarvage tvāṃ namāmyaham  || 50.51 ||
lokatrayaṃ tvayā vyāptamapāṃ rūpeṇa suvrate  |
prasīda tvaṃ mahābhāge lokatritayapāvanī  || 50.52 ||
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devī mārkaṇḍeyāttapodhanāt |
puṣpakaṃ yānamāruhya sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā  || 50.53 ||
caturbhujā trinetrā ca candrabimbanibhānanā  |
uvāca vacanaṃ devī mārkaṇḍeyaṃ mahāmunim  || 50.54 ||
stotreṇānena tuṣṭāhaṃ varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
parituṣṭāsi deveśi varaṃ dātuṃ tvamicchasi  || 50.55 ||
kaluṣaṃ hara kalyāṇi lokānāṃ harasaṃbhave  |
snānaṃ kṛtvā stuvan toye lokānāpuḥ śivājñayā  || 50.56 ||
varaṃ dadasva devi tvaṃ dhundhumārāya sāṃpratam  |
rājyaṃ kṛtvā divaṃ yātu sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  || 50.57 ||
yaṃ yaṃ cintayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnoti suvrate  |
evaṃ bhavatu viprendra matto yadvāñchitaṃ tvayā  || 50.58 ||
evamuktvā yayau devī kapilā lokapāvanī  |
mārkaṇḍeyaṃ muniṃ rājā munibhiḥ parivāritam  || 50.59 ||
praṇipatya yathā nyāyaṃ gataśca svapuraṃ tadā  |
tataḥ kālena mahatārājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 50.60 ||
rājyaṃ kṛtvā kratūniṣṭvā dhundhumāro divaṃ gataḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ mayā dṛṣṭaṃ purānagha  || 50.61 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 50.62 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kapilāmāhātmye dhundhumārasvargārohaṇaṃ nāma pañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 51

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
dvīpasaṃkhyā bhuvo mānaṃ sāgarāṇāṃ ca kīrtanam  |
pātālalokasaṃkhyānaṃ sarvato viditaṃ kuru  || 51.1 ||
narakaṃ svargamānaṃ ca yatkiṃcidanyadīdṛśam  |
uktānuktaṃ yatkiṃcitkarmākarmaśubhāvaham  || 51.2 ||
etatsarvaṃ samāsena skandapṛṣṭena śambhunā  |
kathitaṃ tu purāṇaṃ vai yathāvṛttaṃ purātanam  || 51.3 ||
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajñastrikālajñastrivedavit |
tvameva vetsi sarvaṃ ca prasādādvaktumarhasi  || 51.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi mayā dṛṣṭāni bhārata  || 51.5 ||
yuge yuge kṣatriyāṇāṃ dānayajñakriyāṇi ca  |
nānyastu tvādṛśo rājā dṛṣṭasteṣāṃ tu madhyataḥ  || 51.6 ||
etatsarvaṃ samāsena skandapṛṣṭena śambhunā  |
kathitaṃ tu purāṇaṃ vai sarvaṃ te kathayāmyaham  || 51.7 ||
candradvīpaḥ prabhāse tu tāmraparṇirgabhastimān  |
nāgadvīpaśca saumyaśca gandharvo varuṇastathā  || 51.8 ||
navamaḥ kumārikākhyastu iti dvīpāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
navakhaṇḍavatī caiṣā kathitā te samāsataḥ  || 51.9 ||
khaṇḍeṣveteṣu sarveṣu pravāho nārmadaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
jambūśākakuśakrauñcaśālmalyaśca yudhiṣṭhira  || 51.10 ||
plakṣaśca puṣkaraścaiva saptadvīpāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
kṣāraṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpistathaivekṣuraso'pi ca  || 51.11 ||
surodo madhurodaśca samudrāḥ saptakīrtitāḥ  |
bhūrlokaśca bhuvarlokaḥ svarlokaśca mahastathā  || 51.12 ||
janalokastapolokaḥ satyalokastathā paraḥ  |
bhūrlokādityayorviddhi tvantarālaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 51.13 ||
yojanānāṃ caturlakṣa pātālaṃ yatpramāṇataḥ  |
rudrāśca vasavaścāṣṭau nivasantyatra bhārata  || 51.14 ||
kathitāśca mayā lokāḥ pātālāni nibodha me  |
atalaṃ vitalaṃ caiva śarkaraṃ ca gabhastikam  || 51.15 ||
mahātalaṃ ca sutalaṃ rasātalamataḥ param  |
sauvarṇamaṣṭamaṃ viddhi sarvakāmasamanvitam  || 51.16 ||
vahnerdāho hyapāṃ śaityaṃ marutāṃ vahanaṃ tathā  |
kāṭhinyaṃ ca tathā dhātryā gagane śuṣiraṃ tathā  || 51.17 ||
svabhāva eva bhūtānāṃ svasvabhāvānusārataḥ  |
prakṛtiṃ yānti bhūtāni nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 51.18 ||
lakṣāṇi caturaśītiryonīnāṃ pāpakarmaṇām  |
narakeṣu ca ghoreṣu dāruṇā yamayātanāḥ  || 51.19 ||
niruddhāḥ prāṇinaḥ sarve nītāstu yamakiṅkaraiḥ  |
yātanā vividhā raudrāstatrasthairanubhūyate  || 51.20 ||
svakarmaphalayogena prāpnuvanti śubhāśūbham  |
etadarthaṃ tapo homaṃ dānaṃ dhyānaṃ ca pāvanam  || 51.21 ||
kāruṇyaṃ sarvabhūteṣu narmadāśrayaṇaṃ tathā  |
revāyāḥ stavanaṃ pūjā sūryasya prabhavo yathā  || 51.22 ||
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathāvṛttaṃ purātanam  |
mucukundasya saṃvādo dānavendrasya bhārata  || 51.23 ||
kuvalayāśvo'tha rājarṣiścakravartī mahāyaśāḥ  |
āsītkṛtayuge rājanantare cākṣuṣe kila  || 51.24 ||
śakrādaṣṭaguṇaṃ rājyaṃ rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi dānāni vividhāni ca  || 51.25 ||
dattāni tena rājñā vai sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  |
iṣṭāśca kratavaścāpi varjayitvā tu kalpagām  || 51.26 ||
dānavo mucukundaśca sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  |
brahmaṇyaḥ śivabhaktaśca viṣṇubhakto jitendriyaḥ  || 51.27 ||
rāhusomasamāyoge vaidūrye siddhaparvate  |
oṃkāranāthasahitā yatrāste kalpagā sarit || 51.28 ||
anyāni yāni liṅgāni loke caiva carācare  |
kalpānte tāni līyanta oṃkāre vai na saṃśayaḥ  || 51.29 ||
śivena kathitaṃ hyetadviṣṇoścaiva śatakratoḥ  |
pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasyāpi purāṇe skandakīrtite  || 51.30 ||
āgataḥ kalpagāṃ devīṃ koṭitīrthe narādhipaḥ  |
narmadākapilāyoge sarvasambhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  || 51.31 ||
lakṣamekaṃ tu dogdhrīṇāṃ samādāya gavāṃ nṛpa  |
ayutaṃ ca hayānāṃ ca sahasraṃ dantināṃ tathā  || 51.32 ||
kāmikānāṃ tu yānānāṃ sahasraṃ hemamālinām  |
dhanaṃ dhānya ca vāsāṃsi ratnāni vividhāni ca  || 51.33 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau tadā  |
mūrtau tu dakṣiṇāṃ cāpi oṃkārasya narādhipa  || 51.34 ||
yo yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ tasmai sa prayacchati  |
rājā kuvalayāśvastu dharmakarmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 51.35 ||
rāhusūryasamāyoge kurukṣetraṃ yayau kila  |
sāntaḥpuraparīvāro hyayodhyādhipatiḥ svayam  || 51.36 ||
rājaputrasahasraistu vṛtaḥ snānepsayā kila  |
lakṣamekaṃ hayānān ca dantināmayutaṃ tathā  || 51.37 ||
hemamāṇikyaratnāni vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca  |
śraddhayā parayā yukto brāhmaṇebhyo dadau nṛpa  || 51.38 ||
śeṣaṃ nirvāpitaṃ kṣetre sthāne vāyanapūrvakam  |
kālāntare tataḥ prāpte kurukṣetraprabhāvataḥ  || 51.39 ||
nānāyānasahasraistu sāntaḥ puraparigrahaḥ  |
dhriyamāṇā tapatrastu vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 51.40 ||
śaṅkhavāditraghoṣeṇa nānābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  |
vicacāra ca tatrastho vidyādhara ivāparaḥ  || 51.41 ||
mucukundo'pi daityendraḥ sarvakāmasamanvitaḥ  |
kāmikaiśca mahāyānairhemaratnavibhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 51.42 ||
śrutvā vādyasahasrāṇi dharmarājo viśāmpate  |
jagāma vismayaṃ ghoraṃ kimetaditi cābravīt || 51.43 ||
tataḥ kuvalayāśvo'pi tasminahani tatpuram  |
ubhau niveditau dūtairdharmarājasya dhīmataḥ  || 51.44 ||
kuvalayāśvo'tha rājarṣirmucukundo mahābalaḥ  |
lokāntaramubhāvetau vimānasthau samāgatau  || 51.45 ||
tāvadutpatitaṃ yānaṃ mucukundasya copari  |
yojanānāṃ sahasreṇa hyuparyupari saṃsthitam  || 51.46 ||
ayodhyādhipateryānamadhobhāge vyavasthitam  |
papraccha dharmarājo'pi citraguptaṃ tu lekhakam  || 51.47 ||
kiṃ tu yānaṃ samāsādya arghapādyena pūjaye  |
saptarṣīnṛṣimukhyāṃśca dharmādharmavicārakān  || 51.48 ||
citragupto'bravīdvākyaṃ tathā saptarṣayo'bruvan  |
mucukundaṃ samāsādya tvarghapādyena pūjaya  || 51.49 ||
dānena kāpilenejyo dānavendro na cāparaḥ  |
adhaḥ kuvalayāśvaśca mucukundastathopari  || 51.50 ||
evamukto dharmarājo dānavendramupāśrayat |
śvetavastraparīdhāno jvalatkuṇḍalabhūṣaṇaḥ  || 51.51 ||
añjaliṃ ca tato baddhvā yānasyāgre vyavasthitaḥ  |
kuśalaṃ te'dya daityendra sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ vara  || 51.52 ||
nirjitāste trayo lokā dānenā'nena suvrata  |
oṃkāradakṣiṇasyānte mūrtau kāpilasaṅgame  || 51.53 ||
saptakalpavahātīre dānasaṃkhyā na vidyate  |

mucukunda uvāca - 
dharmādharme tvamevādya svargadvārārgalo yataḥ  || 51.54 ||
evamukto yamastatra daityendreṇa mahātmanā  |
panthānaṃ darśayāmāsa daityendrasya yudhiṣṭhira  || 51.55 ||
tatastu preṣitastena mucukundo jagāma ha  |
mudā paramayā yukta umāmāheśvaraṃ puram  || 51.56 ||
saṃsmārayitvā vidhivaddaityendraṃ dharmarāṭtataḥ  |
āsādya kuvalayāśvaṃ dharmarājo'bravīdidam  || 51.57 ||
svāgataṃ te mahārāja kuśalaṃ tava sarvadā  |

kuvalayāśva uvāca - 
parasparavirodhatvaṃ devadānavayoḥ sadā  || 51.58 ||
māṃ tyaktvā dānavendrastu pādyārgheṇa tvayārcitaḥ  |
viparītaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ dharmarāja kṛtaṃ katham  || 51.59 ||

yama uvāca - 
viṣādaṃ tyaja rājendra gahanā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  |
nāhaṃ dātā ca hartā ca śubhāśubhaphalasya vai  || 51.60 ||
karmasākṣī ca sarveṣāṃ devāsuranṛṇāṃ nṛpa  |
sarasvatyāṃ kurukṣetre dānaṃ dattaṃ tvayānagha  || 51.61 ||
dvāparānte tu dānaṃ vai revādānaṃ samaṃ nahi  |
śivena kathitaṃ cāsīdbrahmaviṣṇumarudgāṇān  || 51.62 ||
kalāṃ nārhanti tīrthāni sārddhaṃ kalpagayā kvacit |
anṛtaṃ na mayā coktaṃ purāṇaṃ śrutisammatam  || 51.63 ||
etasminantare rājan dvayoḥ saṃvadatostayoḥ  |
uktaḥ kuvalayāśvastu tadākāśagirā svayam  || 51.64 ||
dharmaścetthaṃ mahārāja mā kṛthāstvaṃ kathaṃcana  |
kalpagā toyasaṃspṛṣṭo daityaḥ śivamavāptavān  || 51.65 ||
sa rājā vismayāpannaḥ punarvyāvṛtya cāgataḥ  |
narmadāṃ snātukāmo'pi kapilāsaṅgamaṃ prati  || 51.66 ||
tatra plutastato rājā śivalokaṃ jagāma ha  || 51.67 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mucukundakuvalayāśvasvargārohaṇaṃ nāmaikapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 52

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
ke vrajanti yamaṃ vipra kīdṛśā narakāstu te  |
etanme sarvamākhyāhi devalokaṃ vrajanti ke  || 52.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
yānti puṣpakayānena puṣparāgapradāyinaḥ  |
devāyatanakartāraḥ śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 52.2 ||
anāthamaṇḍapānāṃ tu te krīḍanti gṛhottamaiḥ  |
devāgniguruviprāṇāṃ mātāpitrośca pūjakāḥ  || 52.3 ||
pūjyamānā narā yānti kāmikaiśca yathā sukham  |
dyotayanto diśaḥ sarvā yānti dīpapradānataḥ  || 52.4 ||
pratiśrayapradānena sukhaṃ yānti yamālayam  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhena tathā gacchati toyadaḥ  || 52.5 ||
annaṃ pānaṃ prayacchanti sukhaṃ yānti nirākulāḥ  |
dīpamālāṃ hi yacchanti guruśuśrūṣaṇe ratāḥ  || 52.6 ||
pādābhyaṅgaṃ ca yaḥ kuryātso'śvapṛṣṭhena gacchati  |
hemaratnapradānena yānti ratnavibhūṣitāḥ  || 52.7 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā bhūmidānena gacchati  |
annapānapradānena piban khādaṃśca gacchati  || 52.8 ||
ityevamādibhirdānaiḥ sukhaṃ yānti śivālayam  |
svarge ca vipulān bhogān prāpnotyannapradānataḥ  || 52.9 ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāmannadānaparaṃ viduḥ  |
sarvaprītikaraṃ puṇyaṃ balapuṣṭivivarddhanam  || 52.10 ||
nānnadānasamaṃ dānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
annādānasamaṃ dānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
annādbhavanti bhūtāni mriyante tadabhāvataḥ  || 52.11 ||
raktaṃ māṃsaṃ majjaśukre kramādannātpravartate  |
śukrādbhavanti bhūtāni tasmādannamayaṃ jagat || 52.12 ||
hemaratnāśvanāgendranārīsrakcandanādibhiḥ  |
nāstyāhārasamaṃ saukhyaṃ nāsti krodhasamo ripuḥ  || 52.13 ||
ata eva mahāpuṇyamannadānaṃ ca bhārata  |
annadaḥ prāṇadaḥ proktastasmādannaprado bhavet || 52.14 ||
tasmādannapradānena dattānyanyāni sarvaśaḥ  |
trailokye yāni dānāni bhogastrīvāhanāni ca  || 52.15 ||
annapānādi yatkiṃcidaiśvaryaṃ ca yudhiṣṭhira  |
annadānasya tatsarvamihāmutra phalaṃ smṛtam  || 52.16 ||
yasmādannena puṣṭāṅgaḥ kurute dharmasaṃcayam  |
pavitramamṛtaṃ divyaṃ śuddhaṃ sattvaṃ rasāyanam  || 52.17 ||
annapānāśvagovatsaśayyāchatrāṇyupānahau  |
pretalokapraśastāni dānānyaṣṭau yudhiṣṭhira  || 52.18 ||
evaṃ dānaviśeṣeṇa dharmarājapure narāḥ  |
sukhenaiva tu gacchanti tasmāddharmaṃ samācaret || 52.19 ||
annadānaṃ mahārāja jalaṃ caiva tu puṣkalam  |
pitṛbhyaḥ prayato dadyāttīrthe caivāmareśvare  || 52.20 ||
tīrthāni puṇyakṣetrāṇi yāni vai kīrtitāni ca  |
kāmamokṣapradānīha sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca  || 52.21 ||
sarveṣāmeva teṣāṃ tu mātā vai parikīrtitā  |
narmadā saritā śreṣṭhā saptakalpavahā śubhā  || 52.22 ||
tapaso yajñadānānāṃ saṃkhyā tīrtheṣu vidyate  |
narmadāyāṃ na saṃkhyāsti teṣāmityāha śaṅkaraḥ  || 52.23 ||
yathā śivastathā revā pūjanīyā prayatnataḥ  |
eṣu ye tu kukarmāṇaḥ pāpā dānavivārjitāḥ  || 52.24 ||
pathā ghoreṇa te yānti dakṣiṇena yamālayam  |
ṣaḍaśītisahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ samantataḥ  || 52.25 ||
puraṃ jñeyaṃ mahāraudraṃ nānārūpairadhiṣṭhitam  |
samīpasthamivābhāti narāṇāṃ krūrakarmaṇām  || 52.26 ||
pāpināmapi dūrasthaṃ pathā raudreṇa yānti te  |
tīvrakaṇṭakayuktena śarkarābhūṣitena ca  || 52.27 ||
kṣuradhārāvṛtaistīkṣṇaiḥ pāṣāṇairnirmitena ca  |
kecittvanena mahatā dārudāraiśca ghātakaiḥ  || 52.28 ||
lohasūcībhirugrābhirgrastena ca kvacitkvacit |
latāpratānaviṣamaiḥ parvatairvṛkṣasaṃkulaiḥ  || 52.29 ||
sutaptāṅgārayuktena yānti mārgeṇa duḥkhitāḥ  |
kvacidviṣamagartābhistaptaloṣṭeṣṭakairapi  || 52.30 ||
sutaptabālukābhiśca tathā tīkṣṇaiśca śaṅkubhiḥ  |
anekabhagnaśākhābhirāvṛtena kvacitkvacit || 52.31 ||
kaṣṭena tamasā kecidgacchanti hi yamālayam  |
mārgasthāṅgārakaistaptairgrastā dāvāgnibhistathā  || 52.32 ||
kvacittaptaśilābhiśca paṅkena kaṭimānataḥ  |
kvacidduṣṭāmbunā vyāptaṃ duṣkarīṣāgninā kvacit || 52.33 ||
kvacidgṛdhrairbakairvyāghrairduṣṭaiḥ kīṭaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ  |
kvacinmahākulīrādyaiḥ kvacittvajagaraiḥ punaḥ  || 52.34 ||
makṣikābhiśca raudrābhiḥ kvacitsarpairviṣolvaṇaiḥ  |
mattamātaṅgayūthaiśca samantācca pramāthibhiḥ  || 52.35 ||
panthānamullikhadbhiśca tīkṣṇaśṛṅgairmahābalaiḥ  |
siṃhairviṣāṇamahiṣairraudrairmattaiśca śvāpadaiḥ  || 52.36 ||
ḍākinībhiśca raudrābhirvikarālaiśca rākṣasaiḥ  |
vyādhibhiśca mahāghoraiḥ pāvakaiśca durāsadaiḥ  || 52.37 ||
mahānalavimiśreṇa mahācaṇḍena vāyunā  |
mahāpāṣāṇavarṣeṇa bhidyamānā nirāśrayāḥ  || 52.38 ||
kvacitkvacitprataptena dīpyamānā vrajanti hi  |
mahatā va bāṇavarṣeṇa bhidyamānāḥ samantataḥ  || 52.39 ||
patadbhirvajrasaṅghātairulkāpātaiśca dāruṇaiḥ  |
pradīptāṅgāravarṣeṇa hanyamānā vrajanti hi  || 52.40 ||
mahāghoraravairghorairvitrasyanto muhurmuhuḥ  |
niśitāyudhavarṣeṇa pūryamāṇāśca sarvaśaḥ  || 52.41 ||
mahākṣarāmbudhārābhiḥ sicyamānā muhurmuhuḥ  |
mahāśītena raudreṇa kṣuradhārādibhistathā  || 52.42 ||
anyairbahuvidhākāraiḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  |
itthaṃ ca taptaraudreṇa mārgeṇa viṣameṇa ca  || 52.43 ||
aviśrāntena mahatā hyavidūreṇa bhārata  |
avidūreṇa kaṣṭena sarvaduḥkhāśrayeṇa ca  || 52.44 ||
nīyante dehinaḥ sarve mūḍhāḥ pāpaparāyaṇāḥ  |
yamadūtairmahāghorairyamājñākāribhirbalāt || 52.45 ||
ekākinaḥ parādhīnā mitrabandhuvivarjitāḥ  |
śocantaḥ svāni karmāṇi dahyante ca muhurmuhuḥ  || 52.46 ||
pretabhūtavimiśrāśca śuṣkakaṇṭhoṣṭhatālukāḥ  |
kṛśāṅgā bhītabhītāśca dahyamānā hutāgninā  || 52.47 ||
baddhāḥ śṛṅkhalayā kecitmajjantaḥ pāpino bhṛśam  |
kṛṣyante dahyamānāstu yamadūtairbalotkaṭaiḥ  || 52.48 ||
urasyadhomukhasthāne tathaiva khalu duḥkhitāḥ  |
keśapāśe vibaddhāśca kṛṣyante pāpinastathā  || 52.49 ||
lalāṭe cāśugairbiddhvā kṛṣyante dehinaḥ kvacit |
uttānā duṣṭapanthānaṃ nīyante pāpakarmaṇā  || 52.50 ||
pārśvabāhuvibaddhāśca jaṭhare paripīḍitāḥ  |
grīvāpāśavikṛṣyāśca ke'pi yānti suduḥkhitāḥ  || 52.51 ||
jihvāśaṅkupradānena samānīyakṛkāṭikāḥ  |
arddhacandreṇa gṛhyante kṣipyamāṇā itastataḥ  || 52.52 ||
śiśne ca vṛṣaṇe caiva rajjvā baddhāstathā paro  |
vicchinnahastapādāśca chinnakarṇoṣṭhanāsikāḥ  || 52.53 ||
vicchinnaśiśnavṛṣaṇāśchinnaśīrṣāṅgasaṃcayāḥ  |
aṅkuśairbhidyamānāstu khādyamānāḥ sarīsṛpaiḥ  || 52.54 ||
itaścetaśca dhāvanti krandamānā nirāśrayāḥ  |
mudgārairlohadaṇḍaiśca hanyamānā muhurmuhuḥ  || 52.55 ||
kaśābhirvividhābhiśca ghorābhiśca samantataḥ  |
bhindipālaiśca tudyante vamantaḥ śoṇitaṃ muhuḥ  || 52.56 ||
pātyamānāśca salile chāyāṃ vai prārthayanti ca  |
dānahīnāḥ prayāntyevaṃ prāyaścittakṛto narāḥ  || 52.57 ||
gṛhītvā caiva pātheyaṃ sukhaṃ yāti yamālayam  |
evaṃ pathā nikṛṣṭena prāptā yamapuraṃ narāḥ  || 52.58 ||
prājñāpitaistathā dūtaiḥ praveśyante yamāgrataḥ  |
tatra ye śubhakarmāṇastān vai saṃsmārayedyamaḥ  || 52.59 ||
svāgatāsanadānena pādyārgheṇa priyeṇa ca  |
dhanyā yūyaṃ mahātmānātmano hitakāriṇaḥ  || 52.60 ||
yaistu divyasukhārthaṃ hi bhavadbhiḥ sukṛtaṃ kṛtam  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya parvate'marakaṇṭake  || 52.61 ||
dānaṃ dattaṃ tapastaptaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ vidhānataḥ  |
vārāṇasyāṃ kurukṣetre prayāge puṣkare tathā  || 52.62 ||
gayāyāṃ naimiṣāraṇye gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  |
kedāre bharaive cāpi prabhāse śaśibhūṣaṇe  || 52.63 ||
mahākālavane ramye śrīśaile tripurāntake  |
traiyambake dhautapāpe nīlakaṇṭhe tathaiva ca  || 52.64 ||
gaṅgādvāre himadvāre tathā kālañjare girau  |
eteṣvanyeṣu tīrtheṣu kṣetreṣu ca yathākramam  || 52.65 ||
labdhaṃ janmaphalaṃ caiva bhavadbhirnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
idaṃ vimānamāruhya divyastrībhogabhūṣitam  || 52.66 ||
saṃgacchadhvaṃ śivaṃ svargaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam  |
tatra bhuktvā mahābhogānanantān puṇyasaṃkhyayā  || 52.67 ||
yatkiṃcidanyadaśubhaṃ svalpaṃ tadapi bhokṣyatha  |
ākhyātaṃ tu mayā tāvatkalpagātīravāsinaḥ  || 52.68 ||
ārohanti vimānāni sarveṣāmupari sthitāḥ  |
sarvatīrtheṣu saṃkhyāsti hyuktaṃ brahmādibhiḥ purā  || 52.69 ||
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ tena svarge mahīyate  |
mriyate tatra yaḥ kaścidvratenānaśanena ca  || 52.70 ||
divyayānaṃ samāśritya sa prayāti śivālayam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan kalpagāpuṇyamuttamam  || 52.71 ||
paśyanti puṇyakarmāṇo yamaṃ mitramivātmanaḥ  |
ye punaḥ krūrakarmāṇaste paśyanti bhayānakam  || 52.72 ||
daṃṣṭrā karālavadanaṃ bhrukuṭīkuṭilekṣaṇam  |
ūrddhvakeśaṃ mahāśmaśruṃ sphuradoṣṭhādharottaram  || 52.73 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ krūraṃ nīlāñjanacayopamam  |
sarvāyudhohyatakaraṃ garjantaṃ daṇḍapāṇinam  || 52.74 ||
mahāmahiṣamārūḍhaṃ taptāgnisamalocanam  |
raktamālyāmbaradharaṃ mahāmerumivotthitam  || 52.75 ||
pralayāmbudanirghoṣaṃ pibantamiva vāridhīn  |
grasantamiva trailokyamudgiratamivānalam  || 52.76 ||
mṛtyustasya samīpasthaḥ kālānalasamaprabhaḥ  |
kālaścāñjanasaṃkāśaḥ kṛtāntaśca bhayānakaḥ  || 52.77 ||
vividhā vyādhayastīkṣṇā nānārūpā bhayānakāḥ  |
śaktiśūlāṅkuśadharāḥ pāśacakrāsipāṇayaḥ  || 52.78 ||
vajradaṃṣṭrādharā raudrāḥ krūrāścāñjanasannibhāḥ  |
sarvāyudhodyatakarā yamadūtāśca ghātakāḥ  || 52.79 ||
evaṃ vidhaṃ yamaṃ tatra paśyanti pāpacāriṇaḥ  |
nirbhayo yāti cātyarthaṃ yamo vā pāpakāriṇam  || 52.80 ||
citraguptaśca bhagavān dharmaṃ teṣāṃ prabodhayan  |
bho bho duṣkṛtakarmāṇaḥ paradravyāpahārakāḥ  || 52.81 ||
gārjitā rūpavīryeṇa paradāropamardakāḥ  |
yastu yatkurute karma tena tadbhujyate punaḥ  || 52.82 ||
tatkimātmopaghātārthaṃ bhavadbhirduṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam  |
kimarthaṃ paritapyadhvaṃ pīḍyamānāḥ svakarmabhiḥ  || 52.83 ||
bhujyante svāni karmāṇi nāsti doṣo'tra kasyacit |
ete ca pṛthivīpālāḥ saṃprāptāśca mahīpate  || 52.84 ||
svakīyaiḥ karmabhirghorairduṣprajñābalagarvitāḥ  |
bho bho nṛpā durācārāḥ prajāvidhvaṃsakāriṇaḥ  || 52.85 ||
svalpakālasya rājyasya kiṃ vai tadduṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam  |
bhavadbhī rājyalobhena mohenānyāyavṛttibhiḥ  || 52.86 ||
yadgṛhītaṃ phalaṃ tasya yūyaṃ bhuṅgdhvaṃ yathātatham  |
kutra rājyaṃ kalatraṃ vā yadarthamaśubhaṃ kṛtam  || 52.87 ||
tatsarvasvaṃ parityajya yūyamekākinastathā  |
tvadbāndhavā na paśyanti yena vidhvaṃsitāḥ prajāḥ  || 52.88 ||
yamadūtaiḥ pātyamānā adhunā kīdṛśaṃ bhavet |
evaṃ bahuvidhairvākyairupalabdhā yamena te  || 52.89 ||
śocanti svāni karmāṇi tūṣṇīṃ bhūtāśca pārthiva  |
iti vākyaiḥ samādiśya nṛpāṃstān dharmarāṭtataḥ  || 52.90 ||
teṣāṃ pāpaviśuddhyarthaṃ yamo dūtānathābravīt |
bho bhoścaṇḍa mahācaṇḍa gṛhītvā nṛpatīnimān  || 52.91 ||
viśodhayadhvaṃ pāpebhyaḥ krameṇa narakāgninā  |
tataḥ śīghraṃ samāsthāya nṛpān saṃgṛhya pādayoḥ  || 52.92 ||
bhrāmayitvā tu vegena cikṣipuryamakiṅkarāḥ  |
sarve vegena mahatā supratapte mahītale  || 52.93 ||
āsphālayanti mahati cāśmasāramaye drume  |
tataste sarva evāśu prahārairjarjarīkṛtāḥ  || 52.94 ||
visaṃjñāśca tadā santi niśceṣṭāśca yudhiṣṭhira  |
tataste vāyunā spṛṣṭāḥ śanaistu jīvitāḥ punaḥ  || 52.95 ||
tānānīya viśuddhyarthaṃ kṣipanti narakārṇave  |
aṣṭāviṃśatirevādyāstīvrānarakakoṭayaḥ  || 52.96 ||
saptamasya talasyānte ghore tamasi saṃsthitāḥ  |
atighorā ca raudrā ca tathā ghoratamā sthitā  || 52.97 ||
atyantaduḥkhajananī ghorarūpā ca pañcamī  |
ṣaṣṭhī taraṇatārākhyā saptamī ca bhayānakā  || 52.98 ||
aṣṭamī kālarātriśca navamī ca ghaṭotkaṭā  |
daśamī caiva caṇḍā ca mahācaṇḍā tato'pyadhaḥ  || 52.99 ||
caṇḍakolāhalā caiva pracaṇḍā ca parāgnikā  |
jaghanyā hyavarā lomā bhīṣaṇī caiva nāyikā  || 52.100 ||
karālā vikarālā ca vajraviṃśatirāśritā  |
astā ca pañcakoṇā ca sudīrghā parivartulā  || 52.101 ||
saptabhaumāṣṭabhaumā ca dīrghamāyetihāparā  |
iti tā nāmataḥ proktā ghorā narakakoṭayaḥ  || 52.102 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatiretāstu bhūtānāṃ mānataḥ smṛtāḥ  |
tāsāṃ krameṇa vijñeyāḥ pañcapañcaiva nāyakā  || 52.103 ||
pratyekaṃ sarvakoṭīnāṃ nāmatastu viśāmpate  |
rauravaḥ prathamasteṣāṃ rudanti yatra dehinaḥ  || 52.104 ||
mahārauravapīḍābhirmahānto'pi rudanti hi  |
tamaḥ śītaṃ tathā coṣṇaṃ pañcaite nāyakāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 52.105 ||
aghoraḥ prathamastīkṣṇaḥ padmaḥ saṃjīvanaḥ śaṭhaḥ  |
mahāmāyo vilomaśca kaṇṭakaḥ kaṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 52.106 ||
tīvro vāmaḥ karālaśca kiṅkarālaḥ prakampanaḥ  |
mahācakraḥ supadmaśca kālasūtraḥ pragarjanaḥ  || 52.107 ||
sūcīmukhaḥ sunemiśca khādakaḥ suprapīḍitaḥ  |
kumbhīpākaḥ supākaśca krakacaśca sudāruṇaḥ  || 52.108 ||
aṅgārarātriḥ pacanaḥ asṛkpūyabhavastathā  |
sutīkṣṇāḥ śaṇḍaśakunī mahāsaṃvartakaḥ kratuḥ  || 52.109 ||
taptajantuḥ paṅkaleṣaḥ pūtimān ca hradastrapuḥ  |
ucchvāsaśca nirucchvāsaḥ sudīrghaḥ krūraśālmalī  || 52.110 ||
uṣṭritastu mahānādaḥ pravāhaḥ supravāhanaḥ  |
vṛṣāśrayo vṛṣāśvaśca siṃhavyāghragajānanāḥ  || 52.111 ||
śvaśūkarājamahiṣameṣamūṣakharānanāḥ  |
grāhakumbhīranakrāsyā mahāghorā bhayānakāḥ  || 52.112 ||
sarvabhakṣāḥ svabhakṣāśca sarvakarmā'śvavāyasāḥ  |
gṛdhrolūka ulūkaśca śārdūlakapikacchurāḥ  || 52.113 ||
gaṇḍakaḥ pūtivaktraśca raktāsyaḥ pūtimūtrikaḥ  |
kaṇadhūmrastuṣārāgniḥ kṛmimānnirayastathā  || 52.114 ||
ātodyaśca pratodyaśca rudhirodyaśca bhojanam  |
kālātmago'nubhakṣaśca sarvabhakṣaḥ sudāruṇaḥ  || 52.115 ||
karkaṭastu viśālaśca vikaṭaḥ kaṭapūtanaḥ  |
ambarīṣaḥ kaṭāhaśca kaṣṭā vaitaraṇī nadī  || 52.116 ||
sutapto lohaśaṅkuśca ekapādo'śrupūraṇaḥ  |
asipatravanaṃ ghoramasthiliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  || 52.117 ||
tilātasīkṣuyantrāṇi kūṭapāpapramardanāḥ  |
mahācullī vicullī ca taptalohamayī śilā  || 52.118 ||
parvataḥ kṣuradhārākhyo mayo yamalaparvataḥ  |
sūcīviṣṭhāndhakūpāśca patanaḥ pātanastathā  || 52.119 ||
muśalī vṛṣalī caivāśivā saṅkaṭalā tathā  |
tālapatrāsigahanaṃ mahāmohaka eva ca  || 52.120 ||
saṃmohano'sthibhaṅgaśca taptācalamayo'guṇaḥ  |
bahuduḥkho mahāduḥkhaḥ kaśmalo yamalastathā  || 52.121 ||
hālāhalo virūpaśca śvarūpaścyutamānasaḥ  |
ekapādastripādaśca tīvraśca viditastataḥ  || 52.122 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatirityete kramaśaḥ pañcakāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 52.123 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narakavarṇanaṃ nāma dvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 53

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
koṭī nāmanurūpāśca pañcapañcaiva nāyakāḥ  |
rauravādyaṃ marīcyantaṃ narakānāṃ śataṃ smṛtam  || 53.1 ||
catvāriṃśatsamadhikaṃ mahānarakamaṇḍalam  |
ekakramātprabhujyante naraiḥ karmānurūpataḥ  || 53.2 ||
kāmanābhirvirūpābhirakarmapracayāddrutam  |
sugūḍhayā tato dhvānte taptaśṛṅkhalayā narāḥ  || 53.3 ||
mahāvṛkṣasya śākhāyāṃ lambyante yamakiṅkaraiḥ  |
tatastān sarvataścaiva dolayanti hi kiṅkarāḥ  || 53.4 ||
dolitāścātivegena niḥsaṃjñāṃ yānti pāpinaḥ  |
antarikṣe sthitānāṃ ca lohabhāraśataṃ tadā  || 53.5 ||
pādayorbadhyate teṣāṃ yamadūtairbalotkaṭaiḥ  |
tena bhāreṇa mahatā bhṛśaṃ santāpitā narāḥ  || 53.6 ||
dhyāyanti svāni karmāṇi tūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭhanti niścalāḥ  |
tataḥ kramādagnivarṇairlohadaṇḍaiḥ sakaṇṭakaiḥ  || 53.7 ||
nihanyante prayatnena yamadūtaiśca mastake  |
viṣṭhāpūrṇe tataḥ kūpe kṛmīṇāṃ nilaye tataḥ  || 53.8 ||
samantātkiṅkarairghoraiḥ pacyante pāpakāriṇaḥ  |
tataḥ kṣāreṇa nīreṇa vahṇāvapi viśeṣataḥ  || 53.9 ||
vārtākavatprapacyante tapte lohakaṭāhake  |
amedhyakūpe prakṣipya jalajantu samākule  || 53.10 ||
medosṛkpūyapūrṇāyāṃ vāpyāṃ kṣiptāstu te tataḥ  |
bhakṣante kṛmibhistīkṣṇairlohatuṇḍaiśca vāyasaiḥ  || 53.11 ||
pacyante māṃsavaccāpi pradīptāṅgārarāśiṣu  |
protāḥ śūleṣu tīkṣṇeṣu narāḥ pāpasamanvitāḥ  || 53.12 ||
pacyante pāpinaste vai yamadūtairanekadhā  |
tailapūrṇakaṭāheṣu sutapteṣu tataḥ punaḥ  || 53.13 ||
teṣāṃ cotpāṭyate jihvā asatyā priyavādinām  |
sudṛḍhena sutaptena prapīḍyorasi pādataḥ  || 53.14 ||
mithyāgamaprayuktasya dvijasyāpi tathaivaca  |
yajñārthakośavistīrṇaṃ bhallaistīkṣṇaiḥ pratodyate  || 53.15 ||
nirbhatsayanti ye mūḍhā mātaraṃ pitaraṃ teṣāṃ  |
teṣāṃ vaktraṃ bālukābhirmuhurāpūrya sicyate  || 53.16 ||
tataḥ kṣāreṇa dīptena payasā tu punaḥ punaḥ  |
drutaṃ sampūryate'tyarthaṃ taptatailena tanmukham  || 53.17 ||
viṣṭhābhiḥ kṛmipūrṇābhiḥ śvānavaccaraṇairbhaṭaiḥ  |
paripīḍya viṣāṇena praviṣṭā lohaśālmalīm  || 53.18 ||
hanyante pṛṣṭhadeheṣu punarbhīmairmahābalaiḥ  |
dantureṇātikuṇṭhena krakacena balīyasā  || 53.19 ||
śiraḥ prabhṛti pāṭyante ghoraiḥ karmabhirātmajaiḥ  |
khādayanti svamāṃsāni pāyayanti svaśoṇitam  || 53.20 ||
annaṃ pānaṃ na dattaṃ yairmūḍhairnāpyanumoditam  |
ikṣuvatte prapīḍyante jarjarīkṛtya mudgaraiḥ  || 53.21 ||
asitālavane ghore chidyante khaṇḍakhaṇḍaśaḥ  |
sūcībhirbhinnasarvāṅgāstataḥ śūle praropitāḥ  || 53.22 ||
cācalyamānāḥ kṛṣyante na mriyante tathāpi ca  |
dehādutpāṭyate māṃsaṃ teṣāmasthīni mudgaraiḥ  || 53.23 ||
bahuśaḥ kṛṣyate tūrṇaṃ yamadūtairbalotkaṭaiḥ  |
te'nucchvāsenānuchvāsāstiṣṭhanti narake ciram  || 53.24 ||
ucchvāse ca sadocchvāsā bālukāvadanāvṛtāḥ  |
rauraveṣu tu dante vai pīḍyante vividhaiścaraiḥ  || 53.25 ||
mahārauravapīḍābhirmahānto'pi rudanti hi  |
upasthāsye gude pārśve pāde corasi mastake  || 53.26 ||
nihanyante bhaṭaistīkṣṇaiḥ sutaptairlohamudgaraiḥ  |
nindanti ye svarūpeṇa paradārān hasanti ca  || 53.27 ||
āliṅganti patīnanyānna vindanti svakān striyaḥ  |
kimu dhāvasi vegena na smareratiśāśvatīm  || 53.28 ||
vañcitaśca tvayā bhartā pāpāndhaśca yathā sukham  |
lohakumbhe vinikṣiptāḥ citāśca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 53.29 ||
samṛddhāgnau prapācyante praveśyante śilāsu ca  |
kṣipyante cāndhakūpeṣu daśyante'jagarairbhṛśam  || 53.30 ||
ye nindanti mahātmānamācāryaṃ dharmardarśinam  |
śivabhaktaṃ ca vipraṃ ca śivadharmaṃ ca śāśvatam  || 53.31 ||
teṣāmurasi kaṇṭhe ca jihvāyāṃ dehasandhiṣu  |
kīlakairoṣṭhapuṭake kīlyante yamakiṅkaraiḥ  || 53.32 ||
evamādimahāghorā yātanāḥ pāpakarmiṇām  |
ekaika narake jñeyāḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 53.33 ||
yātanā gahanā rājan sarveṣāṃ pāpakarmiṇām  |
ityeva yātanānantāḥ sarveṣu narakeṣu ca  || 53.34 ||
kastā varṣaśatenāpi vaktuṃ śaknoti mānavaḥ  |
ityevaṃ vividhairghoraiḥ pātyamānāḥ svakarmabhiḥ  || 53.35 ||
kramātsarveṣu pacyante narakeṣu na saṃśayaḥ  |
mahāpātakinaścāpi sarveṣu narakeṣu ca  || 53.36 ||
ācandratārakaṃ yāvatpīḍyante vividhaiścaraiḥ  |
tathā pātakinaḥ sarve nirayeṣveṣu sarvadā  || 53.37 ||
caturdikṣu supacyante narakeṣu na saṃśayaḥ  |
upapātakinaścāpi tadarddhaṃ yānti mānavāḥ  || 53.38 ||
mṛtyurna jñāyate tāta kadā kasya bhaviṣyati  |
prāpte cākasmike mṛtyau varṣaṃ vindati ko naraḥ  || 53.39 ||
parityajya yataḥ sarvamekākī yāsyasi dhruvam  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena satyadharmaparo bhava  || 53.40 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ narakāṇā tu lakṣaṇam  || 53.41 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narakāyātanānuvarṇano nāma tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 54

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
tīryate kena dharmeṇa saṃsārābdhiḥ sudustaraḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tena nirbhartsitaiḥ pāpaiḥ kathyamānāṃ kathāṃ śṛṇu  || 54.1 ||
rakto mūḍhaśca loko'yamakārye saṃpravartate  |
na cātmānaṃ vijānāti na paraṃ na ca daivatam  || 54.2 ||
na śṛṇoti paraṃ śreyaḥ sati cakṣuṣi nekṣate  |
same pathi śanairgacchan plavate sma pade pade  || 54.3 ||
evamukto dharmarājaḥ saṃkṣepātpāpadehinām  |
vistareṇa yadā cakhyau teṣāṃ tacchṛṇu sāmpratam  || 54.4 ||

yama uvāca - 
mayā mukto na jānāti bodhyamāno budhairapi  |
saṃsāre kliśyate nānā rāgalobhavaśātnaraḥ  || 54.5 ||
garbhapātena bhāvena śāstramuktaṃ na budhyate  |
narairna śrūyate karma svargamokṣaprasādhakam  || 54.6 ||
santapyati śivadhyāne sarvakāmārthasādhane  |
narakādātmanaḥ śreyo yadatra mahadadbhutam  || 54.7 ||
pretabhūtā narāḥ sarve yamalokaṃ samāgatāḥ  |
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathoddiṣṭaṃ purātanam  || 54.8 ||
sūryeṇa kathitaṃ tvāsīnnarmadākhyānamuttamam  |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca mama pitrānukampayā  || 54.9 ||
sapādalakṣamadhikaṃ brahmaṇā kathitaṃ raveḥ  |
tatra śrutaṃ mayā kṛtsnaṃ brahmaṇā tu śivācchrutam  || 54.10 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya tu  |
jambūdvīpaṃ samāsādya mānuṣīṃ yonimāśritaḥ  || 54.11 ||
nāśrayennarmadāṃ devīṃ saptakalpavahāṃ tu yaḥ  |
snānāvagāhanātpānāttathā dānakriyādibhiḥ  || 54.12 ||
loke'smin gatidā kānyā pāpopahatacetasām  |
ye dhyāyanti mahādevīṃ narmadāṃ pāpahāriṇīm  || 54.13 ||
aghāni teṣāṃ naśyanti tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  |
narmadāṃ saṃsmaredyastu kīrtayedyastu vā girā  || 54.14 ||
paralokaṃ samāyāto yamadūtairna bādhyate  |
narmadāṃ kīrtayedyastu pāpakarmā narādhamaḥ  || 54.15 ||
narakān samayoddiṣṭānna ca krāmati karhicit |
gaṅgādyāḥ saritastatra tīrthakoṭiranekadhā  || 54.16 ||
revātejaḥ pratāpena śuddhiṃ gacchanti tatkṣaṇāt |
narakasthaḥ smaredyastu mekalāṃ tu haraṃ harim  || 54.17 ||
mucyate yamadūtaiḥ sa tatkṣaṇānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
yadi tiṣṭhati vaidūryaparvate'marakaṇṭake  || 54.18 ||
oṃkāraḥ parameśāno bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ  |
kimarthaṃ tviha śocanti pāpopahatacetasaḥ  || 54.19 ||
siddheśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ lokānugrahakārakam  |
yajñeśvaraṃ ca madhye tu tatraiva śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 54.20 ||
narmadā dakṣiṇe bhāge liṅgaṃ caiva maheśvaram  |
caturthaṃ kapileśaṃ ca śivakṣetraṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 54.21 ||
ye'rcayanti sadā bhaktyā puṣpadhūpārtitarpaṇaiḥ  |
śivalokaṃ tu te yānti narakānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 54.22 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yathā pṛṣṭaṃ tvayānagha  |
pāpiṣṭhānnṝnadharmasthān kathayāmāsa bhārata (?)  || 54.23 || [stehtso dort]
godānaṃ hemadānaṃ ca tiladānaṃ tathaiva ca  |
annadānaṃ payodānaṃ sarvopaskarameva ca  || 54.24 ||
prāsādārāmadānaṃ ca ye kurvanti narottamāḥ  |
yamalokaṃ na te yānti narakaṃ ghorarūpiṇam  || 54.25 ||
mucyante sarvapāpebhyaḥ śivasya vacanaṃ yathā  |
sanmānaṃ cāpamānena viyogeneṣṭasaṅgamam  || 54.26 ||
yauvanaṃ jarayā grastaṃ kaṣṭātsaukhyamupadrutam  |
valibhiḥ palitaiścāpi jarjarīkṛtavigrahaḥ  || 54.27 ||
kiṃ karoti naraḥ prājño jarayā jarjarīkṛtaḥ  |
strīpuṃsoryauvanaṃ rūpaṃ yadanyo'nyaṃ priyaṃkaram  || 54.28 ||
tadeva jarayā grastamubhayorapi na priyam  |
apūrvavattathātmānaṃ śaithilyena samanvitam  || 54.29 ||
yaḥ paśyanna virajyeta ko'nyastasmādacetanaḥ  |
jarābhibhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ patnīputrādibāndhavaiḥ  || 54.30 ||
aśaktatvāddurācārairbhṛtyaiśca paribhūyate  |
dharmamarthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ na jarayā yutaḥ  || 54.31 ||
śaktaḥ sādhayituṃ tasmātpurā dharmaṃ samācaret |
vātapittakaphādīnāṃ vaiṣamyaṃ ca yudhiṣṭhira  || 54.32 ||
vātādīnāṃ samūhaśca dehajaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
tasmādvyādhiparaṃ jñeyaṃ śarīramidamātmanaḥ  || 54.33 ||
vātotpattyatirekeṇa bādhito vai jvareṇa ca  |
rogairnānāvidhibhavairbahuduḥkhānyanekadhā  || 54.34 ||
tāni ca svātmavedyāni kimanyatkathayāmyaham  |
ekottaraṃ mṛtyuśatamasmin dehe pratiṣṭhitam  || 54.35 ||
atraikaṃ kālarūpaṃ ca śeṣāstvāgantavaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
ye tvihāgantavaḥ proktāste praśāmyanti bhaiṣajaiḥ  || 54.36 ||
japahomapradānaiśca kālamṛtyurna śāmyati  |
apamṛtyuśca sarvasya viṣamadyādisambhavaḥ  || 54.37 ||
na cātipuruṣastasmādapamṛtyorbabheti vai  |
vividhā vyādhayaḥ kaṣṭāḥ savaidyāḥ prāṇinastathā  || 54.38 ||
viṣāṇi tvabhicārāśca mṛtyordvārāṇi dehinām  |
pīḍitaṃ rogasarpādyairapi dhanvantariḥ svayam  || 54.39 ||
svasthaṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti kālaprāptaṃ hi dehinam  |
naiṣadhaṃ na tapo dānaṃ na mitrāṇi na bāndhavāḥ  || 54.40 ||
paritrātuṃ no samarthāḥ kālena paripīḍitam  |
nāsti mṛtyusamaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāsti mṛtyusamo ripuḥ  || 54.41 ||
nāsti mṛtyusamaḥ kālaḥ sarveṣāmeva dehinām  |
sadbhāryā putramitrāṇi rājyaiśvaryasukhāni ca  || 54.42 ||
mṛtyuśchinatti sarvāṇi vividhānyapi bhārata  |
idaṃ te kathitaṃ rājañjñātisaṃsāradustaram  || 54.43 ||
pariṇāma iti jñātvā sarvaṅkālasya bhojanam  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena saṃsevyā saptakalpagā  || 54.44 ||
sarvaduḥkhāpahā nityaṃ sarvaśokavināśinī  |
yo yān kāmayate kāmāṃstāṃstān devī prayacchati  || 54.45 ||
idaṃ jñānamidaṃ dhyānaṃ pāṇḍityaṃ veda vedanam  |
nivāsaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sevyate saptakalpagā  || 54.46 ||
yajño dānaṃ tapaḥ satyaṃ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam  |
saphalaṃ labhate teṣāṃ yo revāmbu niṣevate  || 54.47 ||
brahmakūrcasahasrāṇi somapānāyutaṃ tathā  |
narmadātoyapānasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 54.48 ||
saṃyukto'pi mahāpāpairnānājanmakṛtairapi  |
oṃkāradakṣiṇe ghoraṃ mucyate tatkṣaṇājjapan  || 54.49 ||
godānānna paraṃ dānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
narmadāpayasi snātvā yo dadyādgāṃ dvijanmane  || 54.50 ||
saṃkhyāṃ kartuṃ yathāvacca na devairapi śakyate  || 54.51 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe karmagatiyamavākyaṃ nāma catuṣpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 55

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
dhenuḥ katividhā proktā kasmin kāle ca dīyate  |
sarvopaskarasaṃyuktā tvatta icchāmi veditum  || 55.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābāho kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
kathayāmi tavākhyānamādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 55.2 ||
cakravartī śaśāṅko'bhūtsarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  |
na ca varṇayituṃ śakyaḥ satyadharmavrate sthitaḥ  || 55.3 ||
bubhuje sa mahīmetāmekacchatrāṃ samāhitaḥ  |
navakhaṇḍāṃ saptadvīpā yathā śakro'marāvatīm  || 55.4 ||
hariścandrasya tasyāpi saṃvādaścakravartinaḥ  |
hariścandraḥ kurukṣetre gavāmayutamuttamam  || 55.5 ||
hemabhāramalaṅkārasarvaratnavibhūṣitam  |
brahmarṣirmudgalo nāma svayaṃ brahma pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 55.6 ||
mudgalāśca dvijāḥ sarve satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ  |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ sāgraṃ brāhmaṇā brahmavādinaḥ  || 55.7 ||
hariścandro dadau tebhyo rāhusūryasamāgame  |
dvijān sampūjya vidhicchraddhayā parayā yutaḥ  || 55.8 ||
arcayitvā maheśānaṃ sthānaṃ paramasiddhidam  |
cakrapāṇiṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 55.9 ||
sarasvatyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha tiladarbhānvitasya tu  |
dānasyāsya prabhāveṇa lokāstenākhilā jitāḥ  || 55.10 ||
antarikṣe purī prāptā sarvalokamanoharā  |
hariścandrapurī khyātā sā'smilloke carācare  || 55.11 ||
satyadānasarvatyāgairityādibhiralaṃkṛtaḥ  |
hariścandrasamo rājā na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 55.12 ||
evaṃ gāthā purā gītā śakrādyaiḥ surasattamaiḥ  |
śaśāṅko'pyakarotsarvaṃ narmadātīramāśritaḥ  || 55.13 ||
dānaṃ yajña tapaḥ satyaṃ parvate'marakaṇṭake  |
dadau cārdhaprasūtāṃ brāhmaṇāya mahātmane  || 55.14 ||
dānasyāsya prabhāveṇa hariścandrādhiko'bhavat |
anekabhāvikaṃ pāpaṃ dagdhvā tūlaughavacchikhī  || 55.15 ||
yāvadvatsasya pādau dvau mukhaṃ yonau pradṛśyate  |
tāvadgauḥ pṛthivī jñeyā saśailavanakānanā  || 55.16 ||
svarṇaśṛṅgī raupyakhurī savatsā kāṃsyadohanā  |
narmadāsnānayuktā tu sakuśā tilasaṃyutā  || 55.17 ||
oṃkārāmarayormadhye koṭitīrthe narādhipaḥ  |
etāḥ sahasrasakhyātā brāhmaṇebhyo nyavedayat || 55.18 ||
etasminnantare nedurdevadundubhayo divi  |
tatkṣaṇādyānamārūḍho jvalanmaṇigaṇairiva  || 55.19 ||
stūyamānaḥ samīpasthairasaṃkhyātairvimānibhiḥ  |
dhṛtasvarṇātapatrastu vījyamānastu cāmaraiḥ  || 55.20 ||
yojanānāṃ sahasreṇa hariścandro'pyadhaḥ sthitaḥ  |
taddṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ karma śaśāṅkasya viśāmpateḥ  || 55.21 ||
sa vinindya kurukṣetraṃ vismayāviṣṭacetanaḥ  |
mahānadyāṃ śaśāṅkena rāhusomasamāgame  || 55.22 ||
dattaṃ dānaṃ na sāmānyaṃ bhavediti samāsataḥ  |
viṣaṇṇavadano bhūtvā hariścandro nṛpottamaḥ  || 55.23 ||
brahmalokaṃ gataḥ kṣipraṃ yatra lokeśvaraḥ prabhuḥ  |
abhivādya yathā nyāyaṃ papraccha sa pitāmaham  || 55.24 ||
dānena nirjitā devāḥ śaśāṅkena mahātmanā  |
kiṃ ca puṇyamidaṃ brahman kurukṣetrādviśiṣyate  || 55.25 ||
amareśvaratīrthaṃ tu nāma revāsamudbhavam  |
kenāpi na samaṃ bhūtamuparyupari dīpyate  || 55.26 ||
iti śrutvā vacastasya hariścandrasya dhīmataḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā hariścandraṃ nṛpottamam  || 55.27 ||
viṣādaṃ tyaja rājendra gahanā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ  |
śaśāṅkasadṛśo rājā na dṛṣṭo na śruto mayā  || 55.28 ||
evaṃ vaktuṃ na yogyo'haṃ na devāpi savāsavāḥ  |
anekāni sahasrāṇi purā vai cakravartinā  || 55.29 ||
iṣṭāni ca vidhānena parvate'marakaṇṭake  |
rāhusūryasamāyoge tīrthalakṣāṇi bhārata  || 55.30 ||
sarasvatīṃ kurukṣetraṃ puṣkaraṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā  |
tīrthānyetāni cānyāni snānaṃ kartuṃ samāyayuḥ  || 55.31 ||
mekalāyāṃ hariścandra koṭitīrthe narādhipa  |
tīrthānāṃ tyaja rājendra sāmyaṃ mekalayā saha  || 55.32 ||
vārāṇasyā kurukṣetraṃ tolitaṃ ca mayā purā  |
tīrthāni na samaṃ yānti tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 55.33 ||
khyātamātraṃ kurukṣetraṃ lokayātrāpravartakam  |
purāṇaṃ na śrutaṃ yaistu mithyājñānasamanvitaiḥ  || 55.34 ||
sevyatāṃ kalpagā devī yadīcchetparamaṃ padam  |
namaskṛtya vidhātāramayodhyādhipatistadā  || 55.35 ||
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ sa yayāvamareśvaram  |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ yathāvattava suvrata  || 55.36 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro rājan gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 55.37 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe godānamahimānuvarṇano nāma pañcapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 56

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
māhiṣmatyāḥ paścime vai tīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ param  |
aśokavanikā nāma sarvaśokavināśanam  || 56.1 ||
snātvā tatrārcayedgaurīṃ yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ  |
mātaṅgasyāśrama tadvatsiddhagandharvasevitam  || 56.2 ||
śuklakṛṣṇātṛtīyāyāṃ gandhadhūpavilepanaiḥ  |
upahārairanekaiśca dīpamālāprabodhanaiḥ  || 56.3 ||
tatra yā pūjayennārī gaurīṃ bhaktisamanvitā  |
rūpasaubhāgyasampannaṃ labhate satpatiṃ tu sā  || 56.4 ||
kārttikyāṃ tu gataprāṇā modamānā tu saṃyatā  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyātprāptā māheśvaraṃ puram  || 56.5 ||
mātaṅgo nāma devarṣiḥ purākalpe yudhiṣṭhira  |
narmadātīramāśritya tapastepe suduṣkaram  || 56.6 ||
purā janmaniṣādaḥ sa jātiṃ smarati pūrvikām  |
aghamarṣaṇadeśasthaḥ sarvadharma bubodha ca  || 56.7 ||
maharṣīṇāṃ prasaṅgena narmadādarśanena ca  |
pāpabuddhiṃ parityajya dharmabuddhiṃ cakāra saḥ  || 56.8 ||
nirviṇṇo'haṃ ca bhikṣuścādhunā śvapacayoniṣu  |
evamuktvā tato rājannaśokavanikāṃ gataḥ  || 56.9 ||
jaṭāvalkaladhārī ca kandamūlaphalāśanaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu śivārādhanatatparaḥ  || 56.10 ||
śivadhyānaparaḥ so'bhūdugre tapasi saṃsthitaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ hi tathā tasya tapasyataḥ  || 56.11 ||
ekāśītisahasrāṇi jaṭāgrebhyo viniḥsmṛtāḥ  |
svayaṃ patanti vividhā narmadātoyamadhyataḥ  || 56.12 ||
tatkṣaṇādyakṣiṇīrūpā anantāścāpramāṇikāḥ  |
śyāmavarṇāḥ sutejaḥ kāḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ  || 56.13 ||
yakṣalokaṃ vrajantyāśu tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
mantrayantravihīno'pi śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ  || 56.14 ||
ṣaḍakṣaramimaṃ mantraṃ hṛdi cakre divāniśam  |
oṃnamaḥ śivāya iti sarvamantrottamottamam  || 56.15 ||
tasya bhaktiṃ parāṃ jñātvā devadeva umāpatiḥ  |
pratyakṣarūpo bhagavān chūlapāṇiḥ samāgataḥ  || 56.16 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devo mātaṅgaṃ prati bhārata  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te dhyānenānena suvrata  || 56.17 ||

mātaṅga uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varaṃ dātumihecchasi  |
mātaṅganāmnā vikhyātiṃ tīrthametatprayātu vai  || 56.18 ||
cāṇḍālāḥ śvapacāścaiva pāpayonigatā'pi  |
japādi rahitāścāpi mucyante'trāpi kilbiṣāt || 56.19 ||
mātaṅganāmaliṅgaṃ tu narmadātīramāśritam  |
snātvā yo'trārcayettasya bhavedbandhavimokṣaṇam  || 56.20 ||
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye tvatprasādātmaheśvara  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā pratyuvāca śivāpatiḥ  || 56.21 ||
evaṃ bhavatu tatsarvaṃ matprasādānna saṃśaya  |
evamuktvā yayau deva kailāsaṃ parvatottamam  || 56.22 ||
varaṃ samprāpya mātaṅga umāmāheśvaraṃ puram  |
kāmikaṃ yānamārūḍhaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣita  || 56.23 ||
jagāmāśu ciraṃ bhoktuṃ bhogān snānaprabhāvataḥ  |
yā caitramāse'māvāsyā kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  || 56.24 ||
tasyāṃ tatra hutaṃ dattamanantaphalamaśnute  |
tilodakapradānena pāpayonigatā'pi  || 56.25 ||
saktvāḍhyaguḍapiṇḍena pitṝnmodayate tu yaḥ  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 56.26 ||
tilataṇḍulamiśraṃ yaḥ kuryālliṅgasya pūjanam  |
so'pi varṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  || 56.27 ||
aśokavanikā nāma mātaṅgaṃ tīrthamucyate  |
revāyā uttare kūle kathitaṃ tava bhārata  || 56.28 ||
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi yāmyabhāge vyavasthitam  |
tīrthaṃ mṛgavanaṃ nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 56.29 ||
tatra snātvā'rcayedviṣṇuṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  |
ekādaśyāṃ mahārāja nirāhāro niśāṃ nayet || 56.30 ||
hariṃ mṛgavane tatra gandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet |
ekasmin bhojite vipre lakṣaṃ bhavatu bhojitam  || 56.31 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  |
tatraiva sanniviṣṭaṃ tu vārāhaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 56.32 ||
yatra vārāharūpeṇa dharā caiva samuddhṛtā  |
viśvarūpaṃ tathā cānyaddhariṇāmitatejasā  || 56.33 ||
pativratā ca nārī vai tathā māsopavāsinī  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena lokaṃ prāpnoti vaiṣṇavam  || 56.34 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mātaṅgāśramavarṇano nāma ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 57

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi khyātaṃ mṛgavanaṃ yathā  |
vyādha kaściddurācāraḥ sarvabhūteṣu nirdayaḥ  || 57.1 ||
pāśahasto dhanuṣpāṇirvicaran girikandare  |
ājaghāna mṛgān sarvān kuṭumbārthe nṛpottama  || 57.2 ||
jyeṣṭhe māsi tu saṃprāpte nidāghe jvalanaprabhe  |
bhramati sma tṛṣārtaśca vṛkṣamūle samāśritaḥ  || 57.3 ||
rātrau svapiti niśceṣṭo duḥkhārtaśca kṣudhānvitaḥ  |
vanasaṃgharṣajo vahnirutthito girikandarāt || 57.4 ||
pradagdhaṃ ca vana tena mṛgavyāghrasamāvṛtam  |
bhasmībhūtaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ reṇubhūtaṃ ca kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 57.5 ||
meghāgamoktakāle tu pravāhe nārmade śubhe  |
kanyārāśigate bhānau dvādaśyāṃ śravaṇena tu  || 57.6 ||
narmadātoya saṃsargādyakṣā jātāstu pannagāḥ  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehāstu vaiṣṇavaṃ yānamāsthitāḥ  || 57.7 ||
sa vyādhaścābhavadrājā tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi bhogān bhuṅkte manoharān  || 57.8 ||
ye'pi dagdhā mṛgāstatra te'pi gandharvatāṃ gatāḥ  |
vaiṣṇavenaiva yānena prāptāstu vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  || 57.9 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vāpi yastu prāṇān parityajet |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu viṣṇuloke sa modate  || 57.10 ||
tilodakapradānena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
manorathaṃ nāma tīrthamanyatparamasiddhidam  || 57.11 ||
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ revātīrasamudbhavam  |
yaṃ yaṃ prārthayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ snātvāpi mānavaḥ  || 57.12 ||
sarva ca samavāpnoti tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
aṅgarāvartasaṃbhedo gosahasraphalapradaḥ  || 57.13 ||
aṅgāreśvaradevaśca tatra tiṣṭhati saṅgame  |
snānamātro narastatra gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt || 57.14 ||
aṅgāraśca caturthyāṃ ca yadā bhavati bhārata  |
hiraṇyapuruṣaṃ kṛtvā raktavastreṇa veṣṭayet || 57.15 ||
ghṛtapūrṇaṃ tāmrapātraṃ guḍenāpi prapūritam  |
tatsarvaṃ vidhivaddadyācchrotriyāya viśeṣataḥ  || 57.16 ||
dānatīrthaprabhāveṇa śakrārddhāsanabhāgbhavet |
yasmātpāpāni duḥkhāni tīvrāṇyapi bahūnyapi  || 57.17 ||
tasmātpāpaṃ na kartavyamātmapīḍākaraṃ hi tat |
yasmin kāle ca deśe ca vayasā yādṛśena ca  || 57.18 ||
kṛtaṃ śubhāśūbhaṃ karma tattathā tena bhujyate  |
tasmātsadaiva dātavyamavicchinna tayārthine  || 57.19 ||
vicchidyante'nyathā bhogā grīṣme kusarito yathā  |
saṃsevyate yathā devī saptakalpavahā śubhā  || 57.20 ||
saṃsārasya samucchittyai jñānayogaṃ bravīmi te  |
śivaprakāśakaṃ jñānaṃ yogastatraiva cintitaḥ  || 57.21 ||
durvijñeya gatiryogo narmadāśivasannidhau  |
śivājñā vartate tatra snānapūjāvidhiryathā  || 57.22 ||
sa siddhāntā'virodhena pustakairna virodhayet |
dharmajñānāpavargārthaṃ sahitaṃ vindate naraḥ  || 57.23 ||
pūrvottaravirodhena kutrārtho'bhimato bhavet |
dṛṣṭvādyamūlatastarkaṃ śrutyā saha vivarjayet || 57.24 ||
tasmādāgamayuktena sadātmārthavicāraṇam  |
kartavyaṃ nānumānena kevalena vipaścitā  || 57.25 ||
hīnottamādyasya phalaṃ bahudhā svaṃ ca tatsmṛtam  |
tasmātparīkṣāṃ kurvīta puṇyaṃ sādhu mahatphalam  || 57.26 ||
pākhaṇḍino vikarmasthān vaiḍālavratikāṃ śaṭhān  |
varjayeddūrato dhīmān haitukyān tīrthanindakān  || 57.27 ||
digambarān śvetapaṭān ye cānye hetuvādinaḥ  |
etaiḥ saha na saṃvādaṃ saṃsargaṃ na kathaṃcana  || 57.28 ||
viparītaṃ kalau dharmaṃ nagnā muṇḍā malāśinaḥ  |
tasmāttaṃ ca parityajya tretādharmaṃ samācaret || 57.29 ||
pramāṇaṃ sarvadharmeṣu brahmaviṣṇuśivoditam  |
anyathā kurute yastu narake patati dhruvam  || 57.30 ||
sarveṣāmeva śāstrāṇāmevaṃ śāstraviniścayaḥ  |
sevyatāṃ kalpagā devī śivapūjārataiḥ sadā  || 57.31 ||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryādbhikṣāṃ dadyācca bhikṣave  |
kāruṇyaṃ sarvabhūteṣu narmadākhyānacintanam  || 57.32 ||
idaṃ jñānamaśeṣaṃ ca sarvakarmaviśodhanam  |
ādimadhyāntarahitaḥ svabhāvavimalaḥ prabhuḥ  || 57.33 ||
sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaśca śivo jñeyaḥ śivāgame  |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ jñānaṃ tatpraṇītamasaṃśayam  || 57.34 ||
yaḥ sarvajñaḥ susampūrṇaḥ svabhāvavimalaḥ śivaḥ  |
sarvadoṣavinirmuktaḥ sa brūyātkathamanyathā  || 57.35 ||
śivājñāmantareṇāpi jagatsṛṣṭiḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
acaitanyātpradhānena ajñatvātpuruṣasya ca  || 57.36 ||
pradhānaṃ paramāṇvādi yāvatkiṃcidacetanam  |
tanna kartṛ svayaṃ draṣṭṛ buddhimatkaraṇaṃ vinā  || 57.37 ||
na yathā ghaṭamānena mṛtpiṇḍaḥ svayamṛcchati  |
tathājñābuddhibhāvena na tiṣṭhetprakṛtiḥ svayam  || 57.38 ||
dharmādharmopadeśo na dharmādharmavicāraṇam  |
sarvajñena vinā jātu nādisarge prasiddhyati  || 57.39 ||
yathānādi pravṛtto'yaṃ ghoraḥ saṃsārasāgaraḥ  |
śivo'pi hi tathānādiḥ saṃsārānmocakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 57.40 ||
vyādhīnāṃ bhaiṣajaṃ yadvatpratipakṣaṃ svabhāvataḥ  |
tadvatsaṃsāraghorāṇāṃ pratipakṣaḥ śivaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 57.41 ||
vaidyaṃ vinā nirākrandāḥ kliśyante rogiṇo yathā  |
śivena tu vinā sarvaṃ nirākrandaṃ jagattathā  || 57.42 ||
tasmādanādiḥ sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ  |
asti nātaḥ paritrātā pumān saṃsārasāgare  || 57.43 ||
ye'bhyasanti śivajñānaṃ hṛdaye śivabhāvitāḥ  |
yadi vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ tu teṣāṃ jñānaṃ prajāyate  || 57.44 ||
iyaṃ ca sarvabhūtānāṃ śaraṇaṃ bhuvi narmadā  |
apāṃ rūpatayā devī lokānugrahakāriṇī  || 57.45 ||
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
bhagavatyuddharatyeṣāṃ patantaṃ narake dhruvam  || 57.46 ||
evaṃjñātvā naraśreṣṭha śivamanvīkṣya kalpagām  |
uccairgṛhāṇi divyāni dhanadhānyānvitāni ca  || 57.47 ||
sarvopaskaradivyāni brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet |
anāthāyātivṛddhāya vikalāya kuṭumbine  || 57.48 ||
kāṣṭhamṛnmayagehaṃ ca yo dvijāya prayacchati  |
evaṃ vidhān gṛhān ramyān sarvato'marakaṇṭake  || 57.49 ||
kārayedyaḥ pumān divyāṃstasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
kiṃ tasya bahubhirdattairdānairbhavati bhārata  || 57.50 ||
etadeva paraṃ dānaṃ sarvakāmārthasādhakam  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 57.51 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye saptapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 58

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
śrutvaitatparamaṃ guhyaṃ gavāṃ devasamudbhavam  |
brahmakūrcasya māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ  || 58.1 ||
ākhyāhi bhagavan sarvaṃ golokaḥ kīdṛśaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
prāpyate karmaṇā kena ke tasminnaniśaṃ sthitāḥ  || 58.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrūyatāmabhidhāsyāmi namaskṛtya maheśvaram  |
gomātṛlokaṃ paramaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam  || 58.3 ||
yathāvatsarvalokānāmuparyupari saṃsthitam  |
pātālāni tataḥ sapta pātālaṃ ca tatastathā  || 58.4 ||
yāvatpramāṇaṃ paritaḥ paricchinnaṃ mahītalam  |
tāvatpramāṇaṃ tasyāḥ samudrāstāni caiva tu  || 58.5 ||
teṣāṃ pratyekamutsedhapramāṇaṃ parikīrtitam  |
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi daśārdhāni tatastataḥ  || 58.6 ||
sahasrayojanotsedhastasyābhyantaratastathā  |
vivarāṇāṃ samastānāṃ sahasrāṇi nava smṛtam  || 58.7 ||
teṣāṃ ruciramāhātmyaṃ nāmatastu mahītale  |
divyadivyopasampannaḥ śrīmaccāmīkaradyutiḥ  || 58.8 ||
nāgarājaḥ sadaivāste tasmin kṛtaniketanaḥ  |
ananto'nantadhāmā ca mukundo nṛpaśaivalaḥ  || 58.9 ||
tato rasātalaṃ nāma śivasantoṣabhūmikam  |
vāsukernāgarājasya tatra cāru mahāpuram  || 58.10 ||
pura ca suralomnastu dānavādhipatermahat |
suparṇasya ca daityānāmaśeṣāṇāṃ mahātmanām  || 58.11 ||
tataḥ sutalanāmāsti śarkarāñcitabhūmikam  |
nāgādīnāṃ svastikānāṃ tatraiva vasatiḥ sadā  || 58.12 ||
dānavādhipatīnāṃ ca tatraiva nilayaḥ paraḥ  |
vairocanahiraṇyākhyaprabhṛtīnāṃ mahātmanām  || 58.13 ||
tataścātalamityuktaṃ pātālānāṃ tasya tu vai  |
teṣāmūrdhvavastusarveṣāṃ mṛnmayaṃ ca talaṃ kṣiteḥ  || 58.14 ||
asurādhipatestāvatkālanemermahāpuram  |
cārucāmīkarābhāsaṃ vainateyasya cāparam  || 58.15 ||
tataśca vitalaṃ nāma pātālaṃ raktabhūtalam  |
tasminmahāntako nāma dānavendrakṛtālayaḥ  || 58.16 ||
tālako'gnimukhastasminnalahrādaśca dānavāḥ  |
nivasanti kṛtāgārāstathā prahlādavarcasaḥ  || 58.17 ||
pātālaṃ vitalaṃ nāma śuklaṃ kṣititalaṃ tataḥ  |
kambalāśvatarau nāgau sahitau tatra tiṣṭhataḥ  || 58.18 ||
mahājambhahayagrīvaprabhṛtīnāṃ mahātmanām  |
vārāṇasyasurendrāṇāṃ nivāsastatrakalpitaḥ  || 58.19 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ kṣititalaṃ tasmātpātālatalasaṃjñakam  |
śaṅkukarṇamahānādanamucīnāṃ niketanam  || 58.20 ||
pātālātsaptamādūrdhvaṃ saptadvīpā mahī sthitā  |
samudraiḥ saptabhiryuktā parvataiḥ samalaṃkṛtā  || 58.21 ||
jambūdvīpaśca tanmadhye plakṣadvīpastataḥ paraḥ  || 58.22 ||
krauñcadvīpaśca parataḥ śākadvīpaśca tadbahiḥ  |
parataḥ puṣkaradvīpaḥ saptamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 58.23 ||
kṣārodakaścekṣurasaḥ surodaśca ghṛtodadhiḥ  |
dadhitoyaḥ kṣīrapūrṇaḥ svādūdaḥ saptamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 58.24 ||
saptadvīpasamudrāṇāṃ dviguṇadviguṇāntaraḥ  |
pramāṇavistaro jñeyo niyutaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 58.25 ||
himavān hemakūṭaśca niṣadhaśceti dakṣiṇe  |
nīlaśca śvetaḥ śṛṅgaśca meroruttarataḥ smṛtāḥ  || 58.26 ||
merurasti sthito madhye jambūdvīpasya bhārata  |
mālyavān pūrvato jñeyaḥ paścime gandhamādanaḥ  || 58.27 ||
ete parvatarājāno jambūdvīpe nava smṛtāḥ  |
plakṣadvīpādiṣu jñeyāḥ saptasaptaiva parvatāḥ  || 58.28 ||
puṣkaradvīpamadhye tu parvato valayākṛtiḥ  |
ekaḥ smṛtaḥ samantācca nāmato mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 58.29 ||
vindhyo nāma mahābhāgo jambūdvīpe vyavasthitaḥ  |
yatraiṣā narmadādevī plavantī lokatāriṇī  || 58.30 ||
vindhyasya cānujo bhrātā sahyo dakṣiṇataḥ smṛtaḥ  |
urvī kūrmatalākārā kāñcanī parimaṇḍalā  || 58.31 ||
aṇureva tathā sā tu nirdiṣṭātividaḥ kṣitiḥ  |
tasyāḥ pramāṇaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ daśayojanakoṭayaḥ  || 58.32 ||
lokāloka iti khyātastasyāḥ prānte samantataḥ  |
sphīto hemamayaḥ śrīmān saralaḥ parimaṇḍalaḥ  || 58.33 ||
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi cārdhamasya vyavasthitam  |
tāvadeva ca vistīrṇaṃ tadardhe bhānurāhitaḥ  || 58.34 ||
prakāśayati sa jyotiḥ parabhāge nihanyate  |
lokāloka iti proktastato'sāvacalo mahān  || 58.35 ||
lokālokāvasāno'yaṃ bhūrlokaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
gandharvayakṣarakṣobhiḥ piśācaiśca niṣevitaḥ  || 58.36 ||
mānuṣaiḥ paśubhiścaiva mṛgapakṣisarīsṛpaiḥ  |
sthāvarairvividhākārairbhūtairetaiśca ṣaḍvidhaiḥ  || 58.37 ||
bhūrlokaśca bhuvarloko yāvadādityamaṇḍalam  |
vasanti satataṃ rudrāssatataṃ vaktrabhāskarāḥ  || 58.38 ||
ādityamaṇḍalādūrdhvaṃ smṛtā svarlokasaṃsthitiḥ  |
vimānakoṭayastasminnaṣṭāviṃśatirāśayaḥ  || 58.39 ||
meḍhībhūto vimānānāṃ sarveṣāmupari dhruvaḥ  |
niyutā anilaskandhāḥ saptāsminnantare sthitāḥ  || 58.40 ||
pṛthivyāḥ prathamaḥ skandhaḥ sthitaścāmeghamaṇḍalam  |
āhavo nāma vai vāto vyūhānāṃ vyūhakṛttathā  || 58.41 ||
dvitīyaḥ pravaho nāma nibaddhaḥ sūryamaṇḍale  |
tṛtīyaḥ saṃvaho māma suskandho'sau pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 58.42 ||
caturthaḥ sodvahaḥ skandhaḥ sthito nakṣatramaṇḍale  |
tato dvayorvinirdiṣṭā vimānodvahanakriyā  || 58.43 ||
yojanānāṃ dhruvaḥ koṭirmaharlokaḥ samucchritaḥ  |
skandhaḥ parivaho nāma nibaddhaḥ saptamo dhruve  || 58.44 ||
annādīni karotyeṣa parvaṇāmupari sthitaḥ  |
vinirvṛttaṃ vikārāṇāmadhivāso mahātmanām  || 58.45 ||
tatrādhikāridevānāmaṣṭāviṃśati koṭayaḥ  |
janātsvarlokamāgatya niyogātpadmajanmanaḥ  || 58.46 ||
sthitā manvantaraṃ tatra svavyāpārāvasāyinaḥ  |
āruhya ca maharlokamāgacchanti tataḥ punaḥ  || 58.47 ||
brahmaṇo divasaikena devāḥ svarge caturdaśa  |
krameṇa kṛtvā karmāṇi maharloke vasanti te  || 58.48 ||
koṭidvayaṃ maharlokājjanalokaḥ samucchritaḥ  |
sādhyānāmasurāstatra vasanti sukhinaḥ sadā  || 58.49 ||
yojanānāṃ catuḥkoṭyaḥ janādapyucchritaṃ tapaḥ  |
prajānāṃ patayastatra sthitāstu brahmaṇaḥ sutāḥ  || 58.50 ||
satyalokastapolokātkoṭiṣaṭkaṃ samucchritam  |
āste parivṛtastatra devāsuragaṇairvirāṭ || 58.51 ||
brahmalokādviṣṇuloko dviguṇena samucchritaḥ  |
vistareṇa tadūrdhve ca divyalokaḥ samanvitaḥ  || 58.52 ||
viṣṇulokācca parataḥ śrīmacchivapuraṃ mahat |
dvāviṃśatkoṭivistīrṇaṃ tadūrdhve samupasthitam  || 58.53 ||
sūryāyutapratīkāśaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam  |
anekāraṇyavinyāsaṃ svarganadyupaśobhitam  || 58.54 ||
sarvaratnānvitairdivyaistaptajāmbūnadaprabhaiḥ  |
sahasrakhaṇḍabhaumaiśca sarvaśobhāsamanvitaiḥ  || 58.55 ||
vimānaiḥ sarvato vyāptaṃ candrairivanabhastalam  |
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇaṃ sarvavidyādharānvitam  || 58.56 ||
nṛtyagītaravopetairaprameyaguṇānvitaiḥ  |
manojavairasaṃkhyātaiḥ parivārasamanvitaiḥ  || 58.57 ||
kvaciddolāgṛhairramyaiḥ kiṅkiṇīravakānvitaiḥ  |
udgatairardhacandraiśca ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitaiḥ  || 58.58 ||
maṇimuktāvitānaiśca maṇiratnacayaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
sarvaratnārcitairdravyairmuktādāmasuśobhanaiḥ  || 58.59 ||
mahāsiṃhāsanairdivyaiḥ sarvaratnavibhūṣitaiḥ  |
kvacitpuṇyagṛhairvyāptamasaṃkhyaguṇānvitaiḥ  || 58.60 ||
sadā puṣpaphalairvṛkṣaiḥ kvacidvyāptaṃ manoramaiḥ  |
puṣpodyānairmahāramyaiḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 58.61 ||
saptakalpavahā puṇyā tatraivāste saridvarā  |
tatkalāyāḥ sahasrāṃśo jambūdvīpe pradṛśyate  || 58.62 ||
avatīrṇā mahīpṛṣṭhe lokānugrahakāmyayā  |
sarvātmanā'vatāraśca gaṅgādisaritāmiha  || 58.63 ||
amṛtasyandinībhiśca nadībhirupaśobhitam  |
hemaratnāñcitā vāpyaḥ sopānaiḥ sphāṭikairyutāḥ  || 58.64 ||
sitaraktāsitaiḥ pītaiḥ sarojairyāḥ sugandhibhiḥ  |
pañcavarṇaiśca gurubhiḥ śobhitāḥ kāñcanākulaiḥ  || 58.65 ||
mahāvikāśi saṃsnigdhaiḥ śrīmadbhiḥ pañcahastakaiḥ  |
daśa dvādaśahastaiśca tathā viṃśatihastakaiḥ  || 58.66 ||
nālairmarakataprakhyairmanoharadalānvitaiḥ  |
pūrṇanīlotpalaiścānyairdīrghikāśca kvacitkvacit || 58.67 ||
siṃhavyāghramukhairdivyairgajavājimṛgānanaiḥ  |
gomukhaiśchāgavadanaiḥ kapipakṣimukhaistathā  || 58.68 ||
ekavaktrairmahāvaktrairbahuvaktrairavaktrakaiḥ  |
ekapādaistripādaiśca bahupādairapādakaiḥ  || 58.69 ||
vāmanairjaṭilairmuṇḍairdīrghagrīvairmahodaraiḥ  |
mahākāyairmahānāsairmahākarṇairakarṇakaiḥ  || 58.70 ||
nānārūpākṛtidharairnānābharaṇabhūṣitaiḥ  |
nānāveṣadharairdivyaiḥ kāmarūpairmahābalaiḥ  || 58.71 ||
nānāprabhāvasaṃyuktairnānāśāstraviśāradaiḥ  |
asaṃkhyā jātayaścānyā nivasanti tathā vidhāḥ  || 58.72 ||
kubjāvāmanakādīrghā varadehā varānanāḥ  |
muṇḍāśca vikaṭā nīcā hrasvadīrghaśca tādṛśāḥ  || 58.73 ||
lambodarā hrasvabhujā vinatā hrasvajānukāḥ  |
mṛgendravadanāścānyā gajavājimukhāstathā  || 58.74 ||
hrasvakuñcitakeśāśca sundarapriyadarśanāḥ  |
pañcāśatkoṭayastatra śivasya paricārikāḥ  || 58.75 ||
maṇimāṇikyageheṣu ramante tā bahiḥ kvacit |
tatra geheṣu yaddvārisahasraśatabhūmiṣu  || 58.76 ||
vicitrabhūmayastatra vajravaidūryabhūṣitāḥ  |
iti sarvaguṇopetaiḥ strīsahasrairvarānanaiḥ  || 58.77 ||
asaṃkhyātaiḥ puraṃ vyāptamīśvarasya samantataḥ  |
tanmadhye sarvatobhadraṃ divyamāyatanaṃ mahat || 58.78 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ sthānamādyamumāpateḥ  |
tatrāste bhagavān somaḥ pūjyamāno gaṇeśvaraiḥ  || 58.79 ||
siddhaiḥ svasthānasaṃprāptairbrahmaviṣṇvādibhistathā  |
dharmastatra sthitaḥ śrīmānīśvarāyatane'nagha  || 58.80 ||
yatra vīravṛṣaḥ tatra nityaṃ gomātaraḥ sthitāḥ  |
tatra sā narmadādevī pūjyamānā surāsuraiḥ  || 58.81 ||
tenodakena tṛpyanti govatsāḥ sarvadevatāḥ  |
brahmāviṣṇuḥ sureśāna umayā sahito'nagha  || 58.82 ||
surāśca ṛṣayo bhūtāḥ pitaro mātarastathā  |
sa lokaḥ śivaloko'tra narmadāloka eva ca  || 58.83 ||
ye guṇā rudralokasya golokasya tathaiva ca  |
nandā bhadrā subhadrā ca suśīlā surabhistathā  || 58.84 ||
iti gomātaraḥ pañca śivalokavinirgatāḥ  |
ṣaṣṭhī tu narmadādevī lokānugrahakāmyayā  || 58.85 ||
etāḥ sarvā jagatsarvaṃ sarvalokasya mātaraḥ  |
tarpayanti mahārāja nityamatrātmikairguṇaiḥ  || 58.86 ||
kāraṇācca śivasthānādīśvarecchāvaśā'nugā  |
oṃkārātsarvalokānāmimaṃ lokaṃ samāśritāḥ  || 58.87 ||
tṛṇāni khādanti carantyaraṇye pibanti toyāni sunirmalāni  |
dugdhaṃ prayacchanti punanti dehaṃ gāvo yato jīvati jīvalokaḥ  || 58.88 ||
kutasteṣāṃ hi pāpāni yeṣāṃ gṛhamalaṃkṛtam  |
satataṃ bālavatsābhirgobhiḥ strībhiriva svayam  || 58.89 ||
ye smaranti sadoṃkāraṃ narmadāṃ ca śivātmanā  |
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  || 58.90 ||
ye kurvanti parāṃ bhaktiṃ tṛṇatoyapradānataḥ  |
prasādāttu gavāṃ tāsāṃ śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 58.91 ||
etāḥ sadānukūlena mātaraḥ sarvakāmadāḥ  |
ye rakṣanti śubhā gāśca śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 58.92 ||
ye'rcayanti śivaṃ bhaktyā sadvidhānaiḥ samāhitāḥ  |
te vindanti mahābhogān puraṃ yānti śivasya vai  || 58.93 ||
ye śivāśrayatīrthāni śraddhayā yānti mānavāḥ  |
kalpagāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa śailaṃ cāmarakaṇṭakam  || 58.94 ||
te krīḍanti mahābhogairbrahmaviṣṇuśivālaye  |
payo'mṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ madhudadhyādikaṃ tu yat || 58.95 ||
na paśyati mahābhāga kalpagāyāṃ vimohitaḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan revāvataraṇaṃ śubham  || 58.96 ||
asyākhyānena bhagavān  |॰
prīyatāṃ me śivaḥ svayam  || 58.97 ||॰

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śivalokavarṇano nāmāṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 59

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
dānadharmavidhānaṃ ca śrotumicchāmi kalpaga  |
daridrā bhikṣavo vāpi kathaṃ yānti śivālayam  || 59.1 ||
vidhinā kena dānena mucyate duṣkṛtaṃ tathā  |
lokānāṃ ca hitārthāya kathayasva mahāmune  || 59.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan yathā nyāyaṃ kathayāmi tavānagha  |
puṣkaraṃ bilvapatraṃ ca kuśāstoyaṃ ca nārmadam  || 59.3 ||
svayambhūrbhagavānāha sāmānyaṃ dharmakāraṇam  |
śraddhāpūtāḥ sarvadharmāḥ purāṇaṃ śrutayastathā  || 59.4 ||
tasyopadeśadharmeṇa narā yānti triviṣṭapam  |
yastūlapūrṇavistīrṇaṃ raktavastraṃ sasūtrakam  || 59.5 ||
vyāghracarmakṛtaṃ vāpi navavastrāvaguṇṭhitam  |
kṛṣṇājinopavītaṃ ca puṇyadhūpādhivāsitam  || 59.6 ||
śivadhyānābhiyuktāya śraddhayā vinivedayet |
tattūlavastratantūnāṃ romasaṃkhyāsti yāvatī  || 59.7 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  |
modate sarvalokeṣu bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ  || 59.8 ||
punaśca kṣitimāsādya siṃhāsanapatirbhavet |
tṛṇavalkalaparṇāni śayyā prāvaraṇādikam  || 59.9 ||
dattvā tadarthine bhūmau śivaloke mahīyate  |
śivamuddiśya naivedyaṃ yo dadyācchivadarśine  || 59.10 ||
śākaṃ mūlaṃ phalaṃ vāpi tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
yāvatsyāttaṇḍulādīnāṃ saṃkhyāphaladaleṣu ca  || 59.11 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  |
bhikṣāṃ savyañjanāṃ dattvā śivabhaktāya bhaktitaḥ  || 59.12 ||
varṣalakṣaṃ mahābhāga śivaloke mahīyate  |
dadhibhaktaṃ susampūrṇaṃ bhikṣāpātraṃ suśobhanam  || 59.13 ||
dadyādyaḥ śivabhaktāya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
varṣakoṭisamaṃ divyaṃ mahābhogaiḥ samanvitam  || 59.14 ||
sthitvā śivapure divye tasyānte ca mahīpatiḥ  |
suśītalena toyena śivabhaktaṃ sitāyujā  || 59.15 ||
tarpayitvā śambhuloke varṣalakṣaṃ ca modate  |
kalaśaṃ śarkaropetaṃ vastrapūtāmbupūritam  || 59.16 ||
dadyādyaḥ śivabhaktāya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ vimānaṃ sarvakāmikam  || 59.17 ||
saṃprāpya śivaloke tu varṣakoṭiṃ sa modate  |
palāśaparṇaiḥ patrairvā yaḥ kuryātpuṭakāni tu  || 59.18 ||
pradadyācchivayogibhyastāmrapātraprado hi saḥ  |
yastāmrapātraṃ sukṛtaṃ pradadyācchivayogine  || 59.19 ||
koṭiṣaṭkaṃ sa kalpānāṃ śivaloke mahīyate  |
śūlaṃ vahati yaḥ pāṇau śaktiṃ pṛṣṭhe kamaṇḍalum  || 59.20 ||
taṃ bhojayitvā yatnena śivalokamavāpnuyāt |
bhojayecca yathā śaktyā yaḥ śivavratacāriṇam  || 59.21 ||
bhogaiḥ sa krīḍati śreṣṭhaiḥ śivaloke vyavasthitaḥ  |
yaḥ śivāśramadharmaṃ ca gṛhasthaṃ bhojayedbudhaḥ  || 59.22 ||
vipulaiḥ sa mahābhogaiḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
śivāśramavratasthaṃ yaḥ kandādyairbhojayetnaraḥ  || 59.23 ||
sa divyānāpnuyādbhogānīśvarasya pure sthitaḥ  |
evaṃ pāśupataṃ bhaktaṃ bhojayitvā praṇamya ca  || 59.24 ||
nānāvidhairmahābhogaiḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
mahāvratadharāyaiva bhikṣāṃ yaḥ pratipādayet || 59.25 ||
sa divyaiḥ śobhanairbhogaiḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
sayamaniyamācāraṃ śivabhaktiparaṃ naram  || 59.26 ||
bhojayitvā yathāśaktyā śivaloke mahīyate  |
jñānayogabahiḥsthā ye lokasāmānyadharmiṇaḥ  || 59.27 ||
pūjayanti śivaṃ bhaktyā śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  |
anāśikenāpi karīṣavahṇinā payaḥ pradānena tapobhirugraiḥ  || 59.28 ||
prayānti yajñaiśca na tāṃ gatiṃ narā  |
nīco'pi yāṃ yāti hi rudrabhaktaḥ  || 59.29 ||
yathā revājalasparśāllabhante sadgatiṃ narāḥ  |
na tathā yajñadānādyairupāyairbharatarṣabha  || 59.30 ||
ityeṣa śivalokastu prasaṅgātsamudāhṛtaḥ  |
golokaḥ kalpagālokaḥ śivabhaktaiḥ samanvitaḥ  || 59.31 ||
jñānayogena ye śāntā japanti paramaṃ śivam  |
te sarvaduḥkhanirmuktā bhavanti sukhinaḥ sadā  || 59.32 ||
śivalokaśca vijñeyo maṇḍalāvaraṇātmakaḥ  |
pañcabhūtānyahaṃkāraḥ sattvaṃ prakṛtiraṣṭamī  || 59.33 ||
īdṛśānāṃ tu nāgānāṃ koṭyo jñeyāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
sarvāṅgatvātpradhānasya tiryagūrdhvamadhaḥ sthitam  || 59.34 ||
viṣṇulokātparaṃ sthānaṃ kumārasya mahātmanaḥ  |
svacchamauktikasaṃkāśaṃ paramā śrīsamanvitam  || 59.35 ||
skandalokātparaṃ sthānamumādevyāḥ prakīrtitam  |
taptacāmīkaraprakhyamaśeṣaguṇasaṃyutam  || 59.36 ||
umāsthānātparaṃ caiva harasthānaṃ taduttamam  |
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam  || 59.37 ||
gaṇairadhyuṣitaṃ sarvairasaṃkhyairyogatatparaiḥ  |
hiraṇyagarbhakūrmādyairvasurudradivākaraiḥ  || 59.38 ||
stūyate bhagavānnityaṃ tasyānti pratikāṅkṣibhiḥ  |
jñānadhyānaparaiḥ śāntairbhikṣāhārairjitendriyaiḥ  || 59.39 ||
prāpyaṃ taiśca paraṃ sthānaṃ sūryāyutasamaprabham  |
tatsatkarmakarairnityaṃ brāhmaṇairdagdhakalmaṣaiḥ  || 59.40 ||
vasanti yadṛtaṃ siddhāśayāstu kleśavarjitāḥ  |
narmadā sevyamānāśca labhante tatpadaṃ narāḥ  || 59.41 ||
etatte kathitaṃ pārtha yathoddiṣṭaṃ tu śambhunā  |
yanmayā kathitaṃ dānaṃ narmadātīramāśritam  || 59.42 ||
gacchanti ye'nyattīrthaṃ tu sahasrāṃśo viśiṣyate  |
sarvajñāḥ sarvagāḥ śuddhāḥ paripūrṇā bhavanti te  || 59.43 ||
śuddhakarmakarā ye tu paramaiśvaryasaṃyutāḥ  |
sadehāśca videhāśca bhavanti svecchayā punaḥ  || 59.44 ||
iti nityaṃ viśuddhaṃ ca sthānamādyamumāpateḥ  |
divyaṃ śrīkaṇṭhanāthasya jagadbhartuḥ samaṃsthitam  || 59.45 ||
sthānaṃ navakamityevaṃ nirgatā yatra kalpagā  |
paramāṣṭaguṇaiśvaryanityamakṣayamavyayam  || 59.46 ||
śaśvadgurupraṇītena dhyānayogena ye narāḥ  |
dhyāyanti devatāṃ nityaṃ te siddhā yānti tatpadam  || 59.47 ||
ye'bhyasanti śivajñānaṃ narmadātīramāśritāḥ  |
kāmatṛṣṇāvinirmuktāste'pi yānti ca tatpuram  || 59.48 ||
apyekadivasaṃ yāvacchivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  |
śivadharmaparastasya dharmasyānto na vidyate  || 59.49 ||
yogadharmasu sāratvādabhedyaṃ pāpamudgaraiḥ  |
vajrataṇḍulavajjñeyaṃ tasmāttasya phalaṃ mahat || 59.50 ||
dehāntenaiva dharmeṇa sthānamādyaṃ śivālayam  |
yatrāste vipulairbhogaiḥ krīḍan kalpāyutaṃ naraḥ  || 59.51 ||
tataḥ kalpāyutasyānte sthānaṃ kaumāramāpnuyāt |
tatrārdhasammitaṃ kālaṃ sa krīḍan sasukhaṃ vaset || 59.52 ||
tadante viṣṇulokaṃ ca saṃprāpya vasate punaḥ  |
brahmalokaṃ gataścānte tatrāpi vasate naraḥ  || 59.53 ||
brahmalokaparibhraṣṭo vasecchivapure sukham  |
tattasmādbrahmaviṣṇvādyāṃllokān prāpnotyanukramāt || 59.54 ||
ityevaṃ sarvalokeṣu ramitvā kramaśastataḥ  |
manuṣyalokamāsādya śivaṃ revāṃ samāśrayet || 59.55 ||
mayā te kathitā'nyatra yāni dānāni bhārata  |
tāni sarve praśaṃsanti parvate'marakaṇṭake  || 59.56 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye śivamahimānuvarṇano nāmaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 60

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
vaiṣṇavaṃ dānadharmaṃ ca kathyamānaṃ nibodha me  |
viṣṇuyogaṃ samabhyasya sarvakleśāpanuttaye  || 60.1 ||
viṣṇuṃ sampūjya vidhinā ghṛtasnānādibhiḥ stavaiḥ  |
dvādaśyāṃ viṣṇumuddiśya dadyādekāṃ payasvinīm  || 60.2 ||
narmadātīramāsādya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
pūjyate dharmarājena yathā viṣṇustathaiva saḥ  || 60.3 ||
śaivānāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca sahasraṃ bhojayettu yaḥ  |
garbhiṇī dhenusaṃmiśraṃ vṛṣabhairdaśabhiryutam  || 60.4 ||
arcitaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairhemavastrairalaṃkṛtam  |
pradakṣiṇamupākramya mantrarājaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ  || 60.5 ||
oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevayeti samuccaran  |
vedavidbhiḥ samākīrṇaṃ viṣṇorārādhanaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 60.6 ||
narmadātoyamāsādya dīpamālāṃ prabodhayet |
gāvo mamāgrato nityaṃ gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhata eva ca  || 60.7 ||
gāvo me hṛdaye vāpi gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham  |
imaṃ mantraṃ samutthāya japedāsāṃ puro gavām  || 60.8 ||
gandhato yākṣatairmiśrairgṛhītvā tāmrabhājanam  |
śṛṅgapuccha jalasnātaḥ śuklavastrasamanvitaḥ  || 60.9 ||
narmadāsnānapānena gavāṃ pucchāmbhasā tathā  |
sarvakalmaṣanirmuktaḥ susiddhaḥ suciravrataḥ  || 60.10 ||
snāpayitvā gatastatra sa vipro narmadātaṭe  |
paurṇamāsyāṃ pūrṇacandre rāhusomasamāgame  || 60.11 ||
taireva sārddhaṃ viprendraiḥ saṃprapūjya hariṃ smaret |
bhṛtyaputrakalatrādyairyuktaḥ svajanabāndhavaiḥ  || 60.12 ||
nivedayettu kṛṣṇāya mantreṇa śraddhayānvitaḥ  |
śrāddhe dāne ca home ca vivāhe maṅgale tathā  || 60.13 ||
gomātaraḥ sthitā nityaṃ viṣṇuloke śivātmikāḥ  |
