śivāyaitā mayā dattā viṣṇave ca mahātmane  || 60.14 ||
evaṃ viprāya yo dadyādyajñārthaṃ samalaṃkṛtāḥ  |
evaṃ nivedya puruṣo gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 60.15 ||
kulāni triṃśaduttārya narakādbhṛtyabāndhavān  |
sthāpayedvaiṣṇave loke śivasya ca mahātmanaḥ  || 60.16 ||
sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaśca viśuddhaḥ sarvagaḥ prabhuḥ  |
saṃsārasāgarānmukto haritulyaḥ prajāyate  || 60.17 ||
anenaiva vidhānena gṛhasthāḥ prāpnuyurdivam  |
vināpi jñānayogena gosahasrapradānataḥ  || 60.18 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi śūdro'thāpi ca bhaktitaḥ  |
narmadākapilāyoge yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ  || 60.19 ||
brāhmaṇāya daridrāya dīkṣitāyopaśobhine  |
candrasūryoparāge tu vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  || 60.20 ||
ṣaḍaśīti mukhe dadyādamāsomasamāgame  |
kārttikyā vā yugādau vā puṇye vāhani bhārata  || 60.21 ||
yaddhi gāyanti pitaro gāthāmetāṃ narādhipa  |
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  || 60.22 ||
narmadākapilāyoge koṭitīrthe ca muktide  |
narakāduddharedasmān dattvā gā yastu saṃyutāḥ  || 60.23 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi loke krīḍati vaiṣṇave  |
tasmāttvamapi rājendra gosahasraprado bhava  || 60.24 ||
devavaddivi modante yena te pitaraḥ sadā  |
kathayāmi tavāthāhamitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 60.25 ||
yuvanāśvaḥ purā rājā cakravartī mahāyaśāḥ  |
śakrācchatagaṇaṃ puṇyaṃ prajāpālanatatparaḥ  || 60.26 ||
ayodhyānagarī yasya brahmalokasamaprabhā  |
tasyā kṛtayuge cādau sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 60.27 ||
bṛhaspatibrahmasamaṃ vaśiṣṭhaṃ svapurohitam  |
abhivādya yathānyāyamuvāca munisattamam  || 60.28 ||
kasmin sthāne yajedyajñaṃ tīrthe deśe surālaye  |
vaśiṣṭhapramukhāḥ sarve munayaścedamabruvan  || 60.29 ||
pṛthivyāṃ naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthamayaṃ śubham  |
saphalo hayamedhastu koṭikoṭigaṇottaraḥ  || 60.30 ||
purāṇe kīrtitaṃ rājanmatsyarūpeṇa viṣṇunā  |
sūryeṇa kīrtitaṃ rājanmanuputrāya cātmanaḥ  || 60.31 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu purāṇānāṃ purāṇaṃ matsyakīrtitam  |
vedāścaiva purā naṣṭā matsyarūpeṇa coddhṛtāḥ  || 60.32 ||
vedahīnāśca vartante dvijā vai yajñakarmasu  |
evaṃ vidhaṃ tu tattīrthaṃ yuvanāśva tavoditam  || 60.33 ||
evaṃ śrutvā tato vākyaṃ vaśiṣṭhasya purodhasaḥ  |
ādideśa tato'mātyān dharmiṣṭhān satyavādinaḥ  || 60.34 ||
yajñopaskaramādāya samāgacchata satvaram  |
ghoṣaṇā kriyatāṃ rāṣṭre daṇḍahastaiśca kiṅkaraiḥ  || 60.35 ||
āhūtāstu tato devā nṛpateryajñakarmaṇi  |
brahmā viṣṇuḥ sureśaśca skando vaiśravaṇastathā  || 60.36 ||
śambhuścaiva viśeṣeṇa surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ  |
dhenūnāṃ daśalakṣāṇi hemaratnānvitāni ca  || 60.37 ||
lakṣamekaṃ hayānāṃ ca dantināmayutatrayam  |
maṇimāṇikyamuktāśca hiraṇyaṃ cāpyanantakam  || 60.38 ||
nānāvidhāni dravyāṇi bhakṣyabhojyamalaṃkṛtam  |
yajñadravyaṃ ca yaccānyattatsarvasahito nṛpaḥ  || 60.39 ||
nānāsahasrayānaistu nānādeśagatairnṛpaiḥ  |
nānāvādyasahasraistu nānāgītairmanoharaiḥ  || 60.40 ||
vedaghoṣeṇa mahatā divaṃ bhūmiṃ vinādayan  |
viveśa naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 60.41 ||
hariṃ sadyaḥ prabhuṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyate yatra kilbiṣāt |
svargasopānametattu pratyakṣaṃ devalokavat || 60.42 ||
tatra snātvā'bhyarcya hariṃ haraṃ svargamavāpnuyāt |
kīrtanānnaimiṣasyāsya naro dahati tatkṣaṇāt || 60.43 ||
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoraṃ tūlarāśimivānalaḥ  |
dīkṣitā brāhmaṇā devāḥ kutaścittu samāgatāḥ  || 60.44 ||
ārtānāmayutaṃ tebhyo dadau devāya cānagha  |
sahasramekaṃ nṛpatirbhūṣaṇānāṃ ca bhārata  || 60.45 ||
oṃ namaḥ śaṅkarāyeti mādhavāyeti cottamaḥ  |
jaladarbhau samādāya pātre rājā hiraṇmaye  || 60.46 ||
evaṃ saṃkalpya rājendra yajñavāṭamakārayat |
daśayojanaparyantaṃ yajñayūpāśca hemajān  || 60.47 ||
tato nirvartito yajño vaśiṣṭhapramukhairdvijaiḥ  |
muditā devatāḥ sarvā divyayānasamāśritāḥ  || 60.48 ||
jayaśabdaṃ pracakrustā rājā nānyo bhavatsamaḥ  |
nānyo mama samaḥ kaścidityahaṃkāravānnṛpaḥ  || 60.49 ||
yāvadyānaṃ samāruhya sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  |
niḥsṛto naimiṣāraṇyāttāvatpaśyati vānaram  || 60.50 ||
tiṣṭha tiṣṭhetyuvācātha śṛṇu rājan vaco mama  |
kiṃ te yajñavidhānena devatādānakarmaṇi  || 60.51 ||
ahaṃkāravimūḍhātmā kartāhamiti manyase  |
purāmareśvare yajñe satyadharmasya bhūpateḥ  || 60.52 ||
varjayitvā mukhaṃ me'bhūtkaṇṭhādho hemavarṇakam  |
ye gatāḥ śiśavasteṣāṃ sarvāṅgāśca hiraṇmayāḥ  || 60.53 ||
kapilā narmadāyoge yajñatoyapravāhataḥ  |
snānāvagāhanātpānālloḍanātkardame tathā  || 60.54 ||
gandharvalokaṃ samprāpto bhūtagrāmaścaturvidhaḥ  |
tvadīye lulitaṃ yajñe naimiṣāraṇyasambhave  || 60.55 ||
paṅkena liptaṃ gātraṃ me kṣālitaṃ cāmbunā tathā  |
na kiṃcitphalamāsīnme tava yajño nirarthakaḥ  || 60.56 ||
gavāṃ tvayāyute dattaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ tathā bahu  |
bhūbhujā satyadharmeṇa kiṃtu tāvannirarthakam  || 60.57 ||
dānena tapasā vāpi trayo lokāḥ samarjitāḥ  |
sarveṣāmeva tīrthānāṃ mātā vai mekalā smṛtā  || 60.58 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathābhūdamareśvare  |
svasti vo'stu gamiṣyāmi tvaṃ vāyodhyā prati vraja  || 60.59 ||
ahameva gamiṣyāmi narmadāṃ saptakalpagām  |
śrutvā tvadīyaṃ yajñaṃ hi naimiṣāraṇyamāgataḥ  || 60.60 ||
nirāśo'haṃ gamiṣyāmi nābhūnme kāṃcanaṃ mukham  |
vānarasya vacaḥ śrutvā yuvanāśvo'bravīdvacaḥ  || 60.61 ||
kastvaṃ vānararūpeṇa satyametadbravīṣi me  |

vānara uvāca - 
ahaṃ jābālinaḥ putraḥ kadambo nāma viśrutaḥ  || 60.62 ||
tiryagyonau praviṣṭaśca prākṛtaiḥ karmabhiḥ svakaiḥ  |
bhrāntāni sarvatīrthāni veṣeṇānena suvrata  || 60.63 ||
paritrāṇaṃ paraṃ nābhūtsatyadharmamakhottame  |
vapurhiraṇmayaṃ sarvaṃ mukhavarjaṃ mamābhavat || 60.64 ||
vānarasya vacaṃ śrutvā sannivṛtya nṛpottamaḥ  |
ārādhya devadeveśaṃ naimiṣe yajñapūruṣam  || 60.65 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ praṇipatya prasādya ca  |
madīyayajñe dānena tapasā niyamena ca  || 60.66 ||
śamicchaṃ chrāvayāmāsa eko vānararūpadhṛk |
hiraṇmayaṃ mukhaṃ cāsya yathā syāttvaṃ tathā kuru  || 60.67 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devo yuvanāśvaṃ mahīpatim  |
pratyakṣaṃ naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham  || 60.68 ||
pṛthivyāṃ naimiṣaṃ tīrthamantarikṣe ca puṣkaram  |
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyāto giriścāmarakaṇṭakaḥ  || 60.69 ||
na ca śrutaṃ tvayā tāta purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  |
mātā sā yatra saritāṃ tīrthānāṃ ca saridvarā  || 60.70 ||
nāmasaṃkīrtanādasyā mucyate bhavabandhanāt |
viṣādaṃ tyaja tīrthānāṃ pradhāno'marakaṇṭakaḥ  || 60.71 ||
satyadharmaḥ punastatra kariṣyati makhottamam  |
revākapilayoryoge mukhaṃ tatra hiraṇmayam  || 60.72 ||
bhaviṣyati na sandehastava vānarasattama  |
naimiṣaṃ sa namaskṛtya ādidevaṃ haraṃ harim  || 60.73 ||
sthānaṃ svaṃ ca jagāmātha mudā paramayā yutaḥ  |
naimiṣasya vacaḥ śrutvā ayodhyādhipatistathā  || 60.74 ||
viveśa nagarīṃ puṇyāṃ yathā śakro'marāvatīm  |
vānaro'pi gatastatra satyadharmo yataḥ svayam  || 60.75 ||
praṇamya satyadharmākhyamidaṃ vacanamabravīt |
revākapilayoryoge tvadīye ca mahāmakhe  || 60.76 ||
avabhṛthasnānajanite kardame luṭhanānmama  |
śarīraṃ kāñcanībhūtaṃ mukhamevāvaśiṣyate  || 60.77 ||
yajñamiṣṭvā punastatra mukhaṃ me kāñcanaṃ kuru  |
gandharvādhipatirbhūyo mukto vānarayonitaḥ  || 60.78 ||
hemībhūtavapustatra yadā vānarasattamaḥ  |
devandundubhinādena nānālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 60.79 ||
haṃsayuktena yānena vījyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
jagāma śāṅkaraṃ lokaṃ tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 60.80 ||
tatra ye śvāpadāssarve te'pi snātvā divaṅgatāḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ pārtha yathāvṛttaṃ purātanam  || 60.81 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanāccāsya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 60.82 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 61

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
śrutvā nānāvidhān dharmāstvatprasādānmahāmune  |
nāhaṃ tṛptiṃ tu gacchāmi narmadākhyānakīrtanāt || 61.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
gāvaḥ pavitramatulaṃ gāvaḥ sarvārthasādhakāḥ  |
tasmāddhi gopradānena śivabhaktyā pramucyate  || 61.2 ||
yasmin deśe bhavennityaṃ śivabhaktisamanvitaḥ  |
so'pi deśo bhavetpūtaḥ kiṃ punaśca sabāndhavaḥ  || 61.3 ||
uktāni ṣaṭsahasrāṇi purāṇe mekalātaṭe  |
ityāha jñānayogasya dharmayogasya tattvavit || 61.4 ||
dharmādharmagatīnāṃ ca svarūpamupavarṇitam  |
tīrthākhyānasamāyuktaṃ narmadākhyānamuttamam  || 61.5 ||
kīrtanācchravaṇāttasya mucyate bhavabandhanāt |
vidyāṃ siṃhāsane divye vastrapuṣpādhivāsitām  || 61.6 ||
pūjayitvā haraṃ viṣṇuṃ śṛṇuyādvācayettathā  |
śrīmatsiṃhāsanaṃ vāpi kḷptaṃ haimaṃ suśobhanam  || 61.7 ||
hemavastroparicchannaṃ nānāratnavibhūṣitam  |
rājataṃ tāmrakaṃ kāṃsyaṃ brahmacārivinirmitam  || 61.8 ||
tattu tārasamudbhūtaṃ śṛṅgavadratnabhūṣitam  |
divyaṃ siṃhāsanaṃ vāpi pūjāṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ  || 61.9 ||
gandhādhivasitakaraḥ śrīmadāsanasaṃsthitaḥ  |
śambhvāyatanatīrtheṣu narendra bhavaneṣu ca  || 61.10 ||
bodhayetparamaṃ dharmaṃ gṛhagrāmapureṣu ca  |
narmadākīrtanācchrotā śivaloke mahīyate  || 61.11 ||
idaṃ tīrthamidaṃ tīrthaṃ paryaṭanneti vai naraḥ  |
narmadaiva paraṃ tīrthamityāha bhagavān chivaḥ  || 61.12 ||
asmiṃstīrthe naraśreṣṭha śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ vidhānataḥ  |
svāgatena yamaḥ prītaścāsanena śatakratuḥ  || 61.13 ||
pitaraḥ pādaśaucena atrādyena prajāpatiḥ  |
viprapādodakaklinnā yāvattiṣṭhati medinī  || 61.14 ||
tāvatpuṣkarapātreṣu pibanti pitaro jalam  |
vidyāvate snātakāya bhikṣave śrotriyāya ca  || 61.15 ||
tathā paramahaṃsāya viṣṇuvratadharāya ca  |
sarvopaskaraṇaṃ dattvā śivaloke mahīyate  || 61.16 ||
anāhitāgniṃ yo vipramāhitāgniṃ karoti ca  |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ svavittenaiva kārayet || 61.17 ||
ardhārdhaṃ saphalaṃ tasya yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
viṣṇuloke'ntakāle ca bhogān bhuṅkte ca puṣkalān  || 61.18 ||
svadravyeṇa ca yo yajñaṃ karoti vidhivaddvijaḥ  |
narmadātīramāsādya brahmaloke sa modate  || 61.19 ||
dhātrīṃ hiraṇmayīṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇāya prakalpayet |
kalpagā tīramāśritya viṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 61.20 ||
tilataṇḍulakarpūrasusambhojyavimiśritaiḥ  |
kuṅkumairvastradhānyaiśca nirmitaṃ śivasannidhau  || 61.21 ||
parvakāle ca yo dadyātparvate mekalātaṭe  |
vasetviṣṇulokeṣu naraḥ svarge na saṃśayaḥ  || 61.22 ||
pratyakṣadhenuṃ yo dadyāthemaratnavibhūṣitām  |
ghṛtadhenuṃ guḍadhenuṃ śarkarādhenumeva ca  || 61.23 ||
revākapilayoryoge dattvaitānnarasattamaḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto loke krīḍati vaiṣṇave  || 61.24 ||
yadi tatra mahārāja bhikṣānnaṃ ca niveditam  |
tasya saṃkhyā na vidyeta sa yāvatsaṃgamo nṛpa  || 61.25 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrata  |
vaivasvate'ntare'thānyacchṛṇu tvaṃ nṛpasattama  || 61.26 ||
vīraṇasya tu rājarṣermaitreyo'bhūtpurohitaḥ  |
tena cāyatanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kāritaṃ narmadātaṭe  || 61.27 ||
puryāścaivāmarāvatyā diśi yāmyāṃ vyavasthitam  |
tadāyatanamāhātmyānnarmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  || 61.28 ||
modate vaiṣṇave loke yugasyārddhaṃ dvijottamaḥ  |
śṛṇu tvaṃ yāni tīrthāni revāyāḥ paścimottare  || 61.29 ||
vanaṃ meghavanaṃ nāma yajñaparvatamāśritam  |
rantidevaḥ purā tatra cakravartī yudhiṣṭhira  || 61.30 ||
gavi nītaṃ kulaṃ yena sa devāsuramānuṣam  |
pitaro mocitā yena gobhirvinihatāḥ purā  || 61.31 ||
cāṇḍālaiśca hatā ye ca prāpnuvanti parāṃ gatim  |
cāṇḍālādudakātsarpādvidyuto brāhmaṇādapi  || 61.32 ||
dantibhyaśca paśubhyaśca maraṇaṃ pāpaśālinām  |
viṣṇorbalipradānena kriyāṇāṃ karaṇena ca  || 61.33 ||
tīrthapiṇḍapradānena te yānti paramāṃ gatim  |
dadhīcirnāma rājarṣiravantyadhipatistathā  || 61.34 ||
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ śakratulyaparākramaḥ  |
purā devāsure yuddhe daityairdevā vinirjitāḥ  || 61.35 ||
devānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca hantāro daityakaṇṭakāḥ  |
naṣṭāḥ svapāpadoṣeṇa sabhṛtyakulabāndhavāḥ  || 61.36 ||
devāḥ samuditāḥ sarve lokapālāḥ savāsavāḥ  |
nirvighnaṃ pṛthivīṃ kṛtvā lokaṃ caiva carācaram  || 61.37 ||
vindhyaṃ giriṃ gatāste tu yasmin vahati kalpagā  |
samarthaṃ bhūpatiṃ jñātvā dadhīciṃ kurusattama  || 61.38 ||
dattānyastrāṇi rakṣārthaṃ tasya rājñaḥ surottamaiḥ  |
vajraṃ śaktiṃ tathā pāśaṃ daṇḍaṃ khaḍgaṃ dhvajaṃ gadām  || 61.39 ||
triśūlaṃ ceti devānāmāyudhāni pracakṣate  |
tāni dattvā yathā nyāyaṃ nākapṛṣṭhaṃ mudā yayuḥ  || 61.40 ||
purāṇamatamājñāya dadhīciḥ satyavikramaḥ  |
śāpasyaiva bhayādbhīto namaskṛtya pragṛhya ca  || 61.41 ||
prabhāvāttoyatāṃ nītvā śarīrāntarnyaveśayat |
tataḥ kālāntare prāpte dānavā baladarpitāḥ  || 61.42 ||
jambhakumbhahayagrīvapramukhāḥ punarutthitāḥ  |
dānavānāṃ balaṃ jñātvā trastā devāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 61.43 ||
kāryakāle samutpanne saṃsmṛtyāstrāyudhāni ca  |
nārada preṣayāmāsuḥ dadhīciṃ prati bhārata  || 61.44 ||
avantīṃ sa purīṃ prāpya devarṣirnāradastathā  |
viveśa bhavanaṃ rājño maṇikāñcanavedikam  || 61.45 ||
utthito nṛpaśārdūlo muniṃ dṛṣṭvā sutejasam  |
pūjayitvā yathā nyāyaṃ hemakāsanasaṃsthitam  || 61.46 ||
taṃ tu dṛṣṭvā sukhāsīnaṃ rājā vacanamabravīt |
kimarthaṃ mānuṣe loke devalokātsamāgataḥ  || 61.47 ||

nārada uvāca - 
yuddhaṃ mahatsamutpannaṃ devānāṃ dānavaiḥ saha  |
samarpaya tvaṃ śastrāṇi kṣīyante dānavā yathā  || 61.48 ||
kuru kāryaṃ ca devānāṃ satyadharmavrate sthitaḥ  |

dadhīciruvāca - 
śṛṇu kāryaṃ ca devarṣe devānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 61.49 ||
acireṇaiva kālena kṣayaṃ yāsyanti dānavāḥ  |
mayā tānyeva śastrāṇi rakṣaṇārthaṃ mahāmune  || 61.50 ||
āpobhūtāni pītāni śarīre santi tāni vai  |
upāyena hi gṛhṇantu dāsyāmyetāni vai punaḥ  || 61.51 ||
ityuktvā ca nṛpaśreṣṭha ājuhāva ca gāḥ purā  |
māṃsādibhakṣitaṃ gobhirasthivarjaṃ viśāmpate  || 61.52 ||
astragrāmastataḥ prāpto lokapālairyathā tathā  |
gonardaṃ nāma nagaraṃ tattu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 61.53 ||
dānavā nihatā devaiḥ punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ pravartitā  |
acintayattadā tatra rantidevo mahīpatiḥ  || 61.54 ||
govidyutpaśucāṇḍālasarpairvinihatā narāḥ  |
devalokaṃ na te yānti na teṣāmudakakriyā  || 61.55 ||
śocayitvā ciraṃ kālaṃ sāntaḥpuraparigrahaḥ  |
prakṣālya narmadātoye tadasthīni vyasarjayat || 61.56 ||
liṅgaṃ brahmeśvaraṃ tatra yajñaparvatasannidhau  |
dharmasaṃśayamāpanno rantidevo mahīpatiḥ  || 61.57 ||
papraccha muniśārdūlān vaśiṣṭhapramukhān dvijān  |
triḥpradakṣiṇamāvṛtya yathā nyāyamidaṃ vacaḥ  || 61.58 ||
ke deśāḥ parvatāḥ puṇyā nadyaḥ kāḥ kīrtitāḥ śubhāḥ  |
narakasthān pitṝn yatra tadvadeyuḥ samuddharet || 61.59 ||
akṣayaṃ ca pitṛśrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāmakṣayā gatiḥ  |

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
mārkaṇḍeyāśramaṃ gaccha asmābhiḥ saha bhūpate  || 61.60 ||
so'pi sarvaṃ vijānīyātkalpagā tīramāśritaḥ  |
tacchrutvā rantidevo'pi munibhiḥ paribhāṣitaḥ  || 61.61 ||
jagāma munibhiḥ sārddhaṃ kalpagātīravāsinam  |
sa rājā brāhmaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ praṇipatya tathā'rcayat || 61.62 ||
samutthāyābravīdvākyamupaviṣṭaḥ kuśāsane  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kānna mocayate ghorān pitṝn saṃsārasāgarāt || 61.63 ||
śṛṇvantu mama vākyāni munayo viditātmanaḥ  |
sarvatīrthamayī revā sarvārthātmamayī śubhā || 61.64 ||
śivenaitatnigaditaṃ purāṇe skandakīrtite  |
kubjārevāsamāyoge viśeṣātsurapūjite  || 61.65 ||
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
tatra śrāddhena yogena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  || 61.66 ||
idaṃ te kathitaṃ rājan kubjārevāsamāgame  |
arcayitvā maheśānaṃ tatra bilvāmrakāhvayam  || 61.67 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt |
sārdhakoṭistu kanyānāṃ tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ gatāḥ  || 61.68 ||
śaptāstāḥ pūrvamunibhiḥ kāmadoṣeṇa bhārata  |
vidyādharāśca yakṣāśca gandharvāḥ kinnarāstathā  || 61.69 ||
śaptāstenaiva doṣeṇa kuberapuravāsinaḥ  |
sarve te siddhimāpannāḥ kubjārevāsamāgame  || 61.70 ||
amāsomasamāyoge kārttikyāṃ caiva parvaṇi  |
vārāṇasī prayāgaśca puṣkaraṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā  || 61.71 ||
ete snātuṃ samāyānti kubjārevāsamāgame  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya śivaloke mahīyate  || 61.72 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe rantidevopākhyānaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

adhyāya 62

rantideva uvāca - 
yathā śaptāstu tāḥ kanyāstāsāṃ nāmāni kalpaga  |
śrotumicchāmi tattvena keṣu sthāneṣu pūjitā  || 62.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣī naimiṣe liṅgadhāriṇī  |
prayāge lalitādevī kāmukā gandhamādane  || 62.2 ||
mānase kumudā nāma viśvayonistathāmbare  |
gomante gomatī nāma mandare kāmacāriṇī  || 62.3 ||
madotkaṭā caitrarathe tapantī hastināpure  |
kānyakubje tathā gaurī prabhā kamalaparvate  || 62.4 ||
ekāgre kīrtimatyākhyā viśvā viśveśvare tathā  |
puṣkare puruhūteti kedāre mārgadāyinī  || 62.5 ||
nandā himavataḥ pṛṣṭhe gokarṇe bhadrakārṇikā  |
sthāneśvare bhavānīti bilvake bilvapatrikā  || 62.6 ||
śrīśaile mādhavī nāma bhadrā bhadreśvare tathā  |
jayā vārāhaśaile tu kamalā kamalālaye  || 62.7 ||
rudrakoṭyāṃ tu rudrāṇī koṭiḥ kālañjare tathā  |
mahāliṅge tu kapilā mākoṭe mukuṭeśvarī  || 62.8 ||
śālagrāme mahādevī śivaliṅge jalapriyā  |
māyāpuryāṃ kumārī tu santāne lalitā tathā  || 62.9 ||
utpalākhye sahasrākṣī hiraṇyākṣe mahotpalā  |
tīrthāyāṃ maṅgalā nāma vimalā puruṣottame  || 62.10 ||
vipāśāyāmamoghākṣī pāṭalā puṇḍravardhane  |
nārāyaṇī supārśve ca trikūṭe bhadrasundarī  || 62.11 ||
vipule vipulā nāma kalyāṇī pralayācale  |
koṭīvikoṭi tīrthe tu yamunāyāṃ mṛgāvatī  || 62.12 ||
karavīre mahālakṣmīrumādevī vināyake  |
ārogyā vaidyanāthe tu mahākāle maheśvarī  || 62.13 ||
abhayā kṛṣṇatīrthe tu amṛtā vindhyakandare  |
māṇḍavye māṇḍukā nāma svāhā māheśvare pure  || 62.14 ||
chāgalambā pracaṇḍe ca caṇḍikā'marakaṇṭake  |
someśvare varāhī tu prabhāse puṣkarāvatī  || 62.15 ||
devamātā sarasvatyāṃ pārā pārāvate tathā  |
mahālaye mahābhāgā payoṣṇyāṃ piṅgaleśvarī  || 62.16 ||
saṃhitā kṛtaśauce tu kārttikeye tu śāṅkarī  |
utpalā varṣake lolā subhadrā śoṇasaṅgame  || 62.17 ||
mālāsiddhatale lakṣmīranantā bhāratāśrame  |
jālandhare siddhamukhī tārā kiṣkindhaparvate  || 62.18 ||
devadāruvane puṣṭirmedhā kaśmīramaṇḍale  |
bhīmādevī himādrau tu tuṣṭirvastreśvare tathā  || 62.19 ||
kapālamocane siddhirmātā kāyāvarohaṇe  |
śaṅkhoddhāre dhṛtirnāma dhvaniḥ piṇḍārake tathā  || 62.20 ||
kalā tu candrabhāgāyāmakṣode śivadhāriṇī  |
vaijayantyamṛtā nāma badaryāmoṣadhī tathā  || 62.21 ||
oṣadhī cottarakurau kuśadvīpe kuśodakā  |
manmathā himakūṭe tu pramate satyavādinī  || 62.22 ||
aśvatthe vandinī nāma nidhirvaiśravaṇe tathā  |
gāyatrī vedavadane pārvatī śivasannidhau  || 62.23 ||
devaloke tathendrāṇī brahmaṇāsye sarasvatī  |
sūryabimbe prabhā nāma mātṛkā vaiṣṇavī tathā  || 62.24 ||
arundhatī satīnāṃ ca apsarassu tilottamā  |
citirbrahmakalā nāma śaktiḥ sarvaśarīriṇām  || 62.25 ||
etaduddeśataḥ proktaṃ nāmāṣṭaśatamuttamam  |
aṣṭottaraṃ tu tīrthānāṃ śatamekaṃ hyudāhṛtam  || 62.26 ||
yaḥ paṭhetprātarutthāya sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  |
eṣu tīrtheṣu ye snātvā etāḥ paśyānti mānavāḥ  || 62.27 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktāste yānti paramāṃ gatim  |
yaḥ karoti tanutyāgamumāsthāneṣu mānavaḥ  || 62.28 ||
sa bhittvā brahmasadanaṃ padamāpnoti śāṅkaram  |
nāmāṣṭakaśataṃ yastu śrāvayecchivasannidhau  || 62.29 ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ bahuputro bhavennaraḥ  |
godāne śrāddhakāle ca vivāhe maṅgale tathā  || 62.30 ||
devārcanavidhau vāpi paṭhan brahmatvamāpnuyāt |
śrutvaitatstotramatulaṃ namaskṛtya ca parvatam  || 62.31 ||
rājā svapitṛmokṣāya yajñārthaṃ prāha kalpagam  |
kasmiṃstīrthe bhavedyajñaḥ pitṝṇāṃ mokṣadāyakaḥ  || 62.32 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mātṛstutirnāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 63

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
revātaṭe mahāpuṇye pitṝṇāṃ mokṣaṇaṃ prati  |
kuru yajñaṃ mahābhāga mucyante pitaro yathā  || 63.1 ||
iti śrutvā mahārāja namaskṛtya ca kalpagām  |
vaśiṣṭhapramukhaiḥ sārddhaṃ jagāma svapuraṃ nṛpaḥ  || 63.2 ||
sa vatsānāṃ ca lakṣaikamaprasūtāyutaṃ tathā  |
viṃśatiḥ śyāmakarṇānāṃ hayānāṃ ca daśāyutam  || 63.3 ||
maṇimāṇikyamuktādi bhūṣitoccaiḥ śravastviṣām  |
ayutaṃ ca karīndrāṇāṃ ghaṇṭābharaṇaśobhinām  || 63.4 ||
maṇimāṇikyaratnānāṃ saṃkhyāṃ kartuṃ na śakyate  |
nānādeśanṛpaiḥ sārddhaṃ brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 63.5 ||
veṇuvīṇāninādena brahmaghoṣaraveṇa ca  |
āpūrayan diśaḥ sarvā divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca saṃspṛśan  || 63.6 ||
harṣeṇa mahatā yukto yajñasambhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  |
rantidevo mahīpālaḥ kalpagātīramāśritaḥ  || 63.7 ||
anekabhakṣabhojyānāṃ tatra saṃkhyā na vidyate  |
aṣṭayojanaparyantaṃ yajñayūpāśca maṇḍapāḥ  || 63.8 ||
hemaratnamayāḥ stambhā maṇimauktikabhūṣitāḥ  |
hiraṇmayāni kuṇḍāni vedikāśca sahasraśaḥ  || 63.9 ||
sruvaśca yajñapātrāṇi sarvaṃ svarṇamayaṃ tathā  |
samāhūtāstato devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 63.10 ||
candrādityau grahaiḥ sārddhaṃ nakṣatradhruvamaṇḍalam  |
siddhā vidyādharā yakṣāḥ surāsuramahoragāḥ  || 63.11 ||
devarājaśca devāśca bṛhaspatipurogamāḥ  |
tato yajñaḥ samārabdho brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 63.12 ||
homena tarpitā devāḥ sarvalokanivāsinaḥ  |
nirdhūmaśca jvaladvahṇiḥ saptajihvāsamanvitaḥ  || 63.13 ||
pratyakṣo havyavāhaśca svayaṃ yajñe narādhipa  |
tato nivartito yajño brāhmaṇairāptadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 63.14 ||
ghoṣaṇā bhrāmitā rāṣṭre pratīhāraiḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
yo yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ so'tra taṃ tvetyasaṃśayaḥ  || 63.15 ||
āhūtāḥ pūrvajāstatra mātṛkāḥ paitṛkāstathā  |
apamṛtyuvaśaṃ prāptāstiryagyonigatāśca ye  || 63.16 ||
te sarve śubhayonitvamāpannā yajñayogataḥ  |
arcitā narmadādevīṃ pratyakṣā rūpadhāriṇī  || 63.17 ||
arcito bhagavāṃstatra pārvatyā sahito haraḥ  |
śrīpatiśca śriyā sārddhaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  || 63.18 ||
śakrādayastathā devāḥ sapatnīkā alaṃkṛtāḥ  |
gāścāśvāṃśca karīndrāṃśca brāhmaṇebhyo nyavedayat || 63.19 ||
yaccānyadvidyate kiṃciddhanaṃ dhānyaṃ payo dadhi  |
agniśaucāni vastrāṇi sarvaṃ tebhyo nyavedayat || 63.20 ||
yugapatpūjitā devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
arcitā narmadādevī śailamūle vyavasthitā  || 63.21 ||
pravāho nirgato yatra kubjārevāsamāgame  |
pitarastarpitā devāḥ prāptāśca paramāṃ gatim  || 63.22 ||
divyayānasamārūḍho dadhīciśca nṛpottamaḥ  |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ pūrvaṃ paścimaṃ tadanantaram  || 63.23 ||
devayānapathe santaḥ śataśo'tha nṛpottama  |
ūcuśca devāste sarve brahmādyā rantidevakam  || 63.24 ||
vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te prītāḥ satyenānena bhūmipa  |
prāpto'si paramaṃ lokaṃ pitṛbhirmātṛbhiḥ saha  || 63.25 ||
rantidevo'bravīdvākyaṃ yūyaṃ me varadā yadi  |
kalaśaḥ sthāpito yatra brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 63.26 ||
caturvedadharairbhaktaiḥ pañcabrahmatanuḥ svayam  |
śivaliṅgaṃ bhavettatra jvālāmālāsamaprabham  || 63.27 ||
yajñaparvatamāsādya pravāho yajñanirgataḥ  |
snāne vinirgatā kubjā caruke carukā tathā  || 63.28 ||
carmilā cāṅghrimūle tu śilpe śilpā vinirgatā  |
dhanado devatāścānyāḥ sampūjya praṇipatya ca  || 63.29 ||
kalpagāṃ ca namaskṛtya kāmikaṃ yānamāśritāḥ  |
stotraṃ cakre mahābhāga liṅgarūpasya śūlinaḥ  || 63.30 ||
lokanātho jagatsraṣṭā praṇipatya yathāvidhi  |

brahmo vāca - 
nāsti rudrasamo devo nāsti rudrasamo guruḥ  || 63.31 ||
nityadāmalakāyāya svaprabhāmalamūrtaye  |
śivabhasmāṅgarāgāya deveśāya namo'stu te  || 63.32 ||
nīlakaṇṭhāya devāya sarvāyāmitamūrtaye  |
bahurūpāya kāntāya śāśvatāya namo'stu te  || 63.33 ||
parāya parameśāya sarvajñāya namo'stu te  |
sarvapraṇatadehāya svayamapraṇatāya ca  || 63.34 ||
pūjyānāmapi pūjyāya namastryakṣāya śūline  |
brahmendraviṣṇuvedyāya utpattisthitihetave  || 63.35 ||
devastuta namaste'stu bhuktimuktipradāya ca  |
vāmāya vāmarūpāya vāmomāropabhāsine  || 63.36 ||
vāmakāntārdhavehāya īśānāya namo'stu te  |
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devo brahmaṇaḥ so'mitadyutiḥ  || 63.37 ||
vṛṇīṣva vāñchitaṃ yajñe varamityāha śaṅkaraḥ  |
dadāmi te na sandeho yastvayā vara īpsitaḥ  || 63.38 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā śaṅkaraṃ sarvagaṃ prabhum  |
pañcavaktraṃ pañcaliṅgaṃ brahmapūjyaṃ prakīrtitam  || 63.39 ||
bilvā niveditā yasminnāmrāśca viniveditāḥ  |
bilvāmrakannāma liṅgaṃ saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam  || 63.40 ||
prasiddhiṃ paramāṃ yātu bhagavaṃstvatprasādataḥ  |
vāmanā mekalā yatra yatredaṃ liṅgamuttamam  || 63.41 ||
tatra snātvā naravyāghra śivalokamavāpyate  |
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye lokānugrahakārakam  || 63.42 ||
śaṅkarastu tathetyevaṃ prāha brahmāṇamavyayam  |
evamuktvā maheśāno gaṇakoṭisamāvṛtaḥ  || 63.43 ||
stūyamānaḥ suraiḥ sarvaiḥ jagāma bhavanaṃ svakam  |
brahmādyā devatāścaiva gatāḥ svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam  || 63.44 ||
rantidevaḥ pratuṣṭāva liṅgarūpadharaṃ śivam  |
niśamya rantidevasya stotraṃ prāha maheśvaraḥ  || 63.45 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te stotreṇānena suvrataṃ  |
rantidevo'bravīdvākyaṃ yadi me varadaḥ śivaḥ  || 63.46 ||
idaṃ tīrthaṃ na moktavyaṃ mahādeva sadā tvayā  |
aghaughasamaplutā ye tu tiryagyonigatā narāḥ  || 63.47 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyātte yāntu paramāṃ gatim  |
atra yaddīyate dānaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 63.48 ||
idaṃ varamahaṃ manye yadi tuṣṭo'si śaṅkara  |

śaṅkara uvāca - 
amāsomasamāyoge kārttikyāṃ caiva parvaṇi  || 63.49 ||
atra yaddīyate dānaṃ tadanantaṃ samaśnute  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃstīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  || 63.50 ||
viśvedevāḥ parāṃ siddhimasmiṃstīrthe samāgatāḥ  |
agastyaśaunakaścaiva parāśaro'ghamarṣaṇaḥ  || 63.51 ||
saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ prāptā nānā munigaṇāstathā  |
atrāyutaṃ munīnāṃ ca tapasā divamāruhat || 63.52 ||
saṃkrame ca vyatīpāte grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
amāsomasamāyoge ṣaḍaśītimukhe tathā  || 63.53 ||
puṇyaṃ daśaguṇaṃ vṛddhiṃ satyametacchivoditam  |
sapādakoṭistīrthānāṃ kubjārevāsamāgame  || 63.54 ||
dakṣiṇottarabhāge tu krośamātraṃ pratiṣṭhitam  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo vā'pi prāṇān yastu parityajet || 63.55 ||
rājā varṣasahasrāṇi vidyādharapure bhavet |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgādyāstīrthe'smin prāṇamokṣaṇe  || 63.56 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu rājā vidyādhare pure  |
bilvāmrakaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ kāmabhogaphalapradam  || 63.57 ||
kubjeśvaraṃ mahaccānyadbrahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  |
atrāntare mahārāja śivakṣetraṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 63.58 ||
revākubjāsamāyoge yavānāṃ saptatistathā  |
amāsomasamāyoge snānācchāntiḥ prakīrtitā  || 63.59 ||
vārāṇasī kurukṣetraṃ prayāgo naimiṣaṃ tathā  |
puṣkaraṃ ca gayā caiva kedāraṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 63.60 ||
phalameteṣu sāmānyamamāsomasamāgame  |
akṣayaṃ ca phalaṃ proktaṃ kubjārevāsamāgame  || 63.61 ||
tilodakapradānena mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ sutaḥ  |
narakāduddharetsarvān pūrvānapi parānapi  || 63.62 ||
so'pi rājā gṛhaṃ prāptaḥ sarvānuddhṛtya pūrvajān  |
ayaṃ te kathito rājan kubjārevāsamāgamaḥ  || 63.63 ||
rantidevo hariścandraḥ puruhūtaḥ purūravāḥ  |
atreṣṭvā vividhairyajñairdivyanti divi devavat || 63.64 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya tīrthasya narasattama  |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā śivaloke mahīyate  || 63.65 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kubjāmāhātmye triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 64

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthaṃ tīrthavaraṃ śubham  |
yāmyapradeśe revāyā āśramaḥ surapūjitaḥ  || 64.1 ||
suvarṇadvīpapavikhyāto devadroṇīsamāvṛtaḥ  |
hārīto gautamo viṣṇuḥ sāvarṇiḥ kauśikastathā  || 64.2 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
māsopavāsinaḥ kecidanye pakṣopavāsinaḥ  || 64.3 ||
cāndrāyaṇaparāścānye tathānye kṛcchracāriṇaḥ  |
phalamūlāśinaḥ kecittathānye vāyubhakṣakāḥ  || 64.4 ||
kaṇadhūmaṃ pibantyanye jalāhārāstathā pare  |
ekapādāḥ sthitāḥ kecidanye cārdhapadāḥ sthitāḥ  || 64.5 ||
dantolūkhalinaḥ kecidanye sūryāvalokinaḥ  |
brāhmaṇāśca brahmavidaḥ śrutismṛtiviśāradāḥ  || 64.6 ||
itihāsapurāṇāni mokṣopāyavicintakāḥ  |
agnihotraparā nityaṃ japayajñakriyāparāḥ  || 64.7 ||
vedadhvanitanirghoṣaistārayanti jagattrayam  |
na tasmin saṃcaretpāpaṃ tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  || 64.8 ||
mekalādakṣiṇe tīre brahmaloka iva sthitaḥ  |
āmrajambūkadambaiśca kapitthairbilvadāḍimaiḥ  || 64.9 ||
kadalībījapūrādyairjambīraiḥ panasaistathā  |
nyagrodhabadarairmukhyairbahuvṛkṣavibhūṣitam  || 64.10 ||
punnāgairnāgabakulairaśokaistilakaistathā  |
mandāraiścampakaiścāmrātakairnīlotpalotpalaiḥ  || 64.11 ||
patrapuṣpaphalopetairvṛkṣaiḥ sarvairalaṃkṛtam  |
nānāpakṣigaṇopetaṃ siddhagandharvasevitam  || 64.12 ||
vyāharatyaṇḍajāḥ sarve mānuṣāṇāṃ girā nṛpa  |
etadguṇasamāyuktaṃ suvarṇadvīpamuttamam  || 64.13 ||
svāyambhuve'ntare rājannādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
arcanāddevadevasya suvarṇadvīpavāsinaḥ  || 64.14 ||
apahāya tamaḥ kṛtsnaṃ loke krīḍanti śāṅkare  |
pitṝṇāmannadānena tilatoyapradānataḥ  || 64.15 ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā vasanti brahmaṇaḥ pure  |
puṇyāyāṃ bhūpakārtikyāṃ sarvatīrthaphalapradaḥ  || 64.16 ||
naitatpaśyanti manujāḥ kalau māyāvimohitāḥ  |
kalpagā yāmyabhāge tu tīrthakoṭiranekadhā  || 64.17 ||
prasiddhaṃ siddhamunibhirjñāyate kevalaṃ hi tat |
nāstikairbhinnamaryādaiḥ purāṇasmṛtinindakaiḥ  || 64.18 ||
tailābhyaktairna vedoktakarai revātaṭe tathā  |
kalimāyāvimūḍhaiśca sthānaṃ tanna pradṛśyate  || 64.19 ||
hiraṇyagarbhāsthāne tu yasmin vahati kalpagā  |
yajñagarbheśvaraṃ nāma śivaliṅgaṃ prakīrtitam  || 64.20 ||
pūjyate siddhagandharvaiḥ surāsuramahoragaiḥ  |
yatra vaivasvato rājā sūryaputro mahāyaśāḥ  || 64.21 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyāccandrabimbānano'bhavat |
caitrasyaiva tu māsasya śuklapakṣe narādhipa  || 64.22 ||
caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ yatra sannihito haraḥ  |
tilodakapradānena piṇḍadānena bhārata  || 64.23 ||
pitṝn samuddharettatra narakādbhūridakṣiṇaḥ  |
nivasedvaiṣṇave loke yāvaccandrārkadarśanam  || 64.24 ||
tatra yaddīyate dānaṃ kurukṣetrasamaṃ hi tat |
prāṇatyāge kṛte tatra na paśyanti yamālayam  || 64.25 ||
revāyā uttare kūle paryaṅko nāma parvataḥ  |
sa ca vindhyasutaḥ śrīmān sarvadevamayaḥ śubhaḥ  || 64.26 ||
tatra pāpaharo viṣṇuḥ svayaṃ tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ  |
narapāpaharo yastu narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 64.27 ||
tatra snātvā mahārāja gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |
tarpitāḥ pitarastasya tṛptā yānti hareḥ puram  || 64.28 ||
ekādaśīṃ dvādaśīṃ vā tatra yaḥ kurute naraḥ  |
na tasya punarāvṛttirmartyaloke durāsade  || 64.29 ||
krośamātrapramāṇaṃ ca harikṣetraṃ prakīrtitam  |
apamṛtyumṛtā ye ca te yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 64.30 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe viṣṇukīrtanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

adhyāya 65

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
narmadāyāmyabhāge tu tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
māṇḍavyasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ siddhagandharvasevitam  || 65.1 ||
vibhāṇḍakaśca gārgyaśca ṛṣyaśṛṅgādayastathā  |
tasmin sahasrasaṃkhyātā munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 65.2 ||
aśokavanikāṃ rājan chṛṇu sāmpratamuttamam  |
tatra sannihito deva umayā sahito haraḥ  || 65.3 ||
āviṣṭaścāśrame tatra viśoko vimalaḥ śivaḥ  |
viśokayā saricchreṣṭhā narmadā yatra saṅgatā  || 65.4 ||
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
aśokeśvaraliṅgaṃ ca pratyakṣaṃ siddhiśaṅkaram  || 65.5 ||
śāpabhraṣṭā dvijāstatra nāradena vimocitāḥ  |
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyātmodante divi devatāḥ  || 65.6 ||
nānāvṛkṣaphalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sarvakāmasamanvitaiḥ  |
nānā pakṣigaṇairjuṣṭaṃ nānāvṛkṣaniṣevitam  || 65.7 ||
siddhavidyādharairyakṣairgandharvaiḥ kinnaraistathā  |
veṇuvīṇāninādena śaṅkhavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ  || 65.8 ||
śobhate sarvadā rājannarmadā vindhyasaṅgamaḥ  |
aśokā devatā yatra brahmaśakrapurogamāḥ  || 65.9 ||
viśvedevāśramaṃ taddhi sarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
viśvāyāśca tathā putrā viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 65.10 ||
aśokavanikāyāṃ ca janayāmāsa kaśyapaḥ  |
vaivasvate'ntare prāpte tretāyāṃ narasattama  || 65.11 ||
pañcāyutāni tāni tīrthāni nivasanti śubhāni ca  |
tatra siddhā ca sāvitrī devamātāditistathā  || 65.12 ||
devayānī tathendrāṇī rohiṇī saṃbharāyaṇī  |
dākṣāyaṇī lokavandyā lopāmudrā mahāyaśā  || 65.13 ||
ratnāvalī sūryabhāryā dhruvā tārā gaṇeśvarī  |
aśokāstena vihitāstatrasthāḥ śatasaṃkhyakāḥ  || 65.14 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyānmucyate kilbiṣānnaraḥ  |
śuklapakṣe caturdaśyāmāśvine māsi bhārata  || 65.15 ||
aputriṇī tathā bandhyā durbhagā bhartṛvarjitā  |
pañcaratnaphalaiḥ snātā divyakumbhaiḥ samarcayet || 65.16 ||
sahasrajanma sā bhūyaḥ putriṇī subhagā bhavet |
aśokavanikā kṣetre tatra gauryā varaḥ kṛtaḥ  || 65.17 ||
yasmin vahati sā devī narmadā saptakalpagā  |
tatreṣṭaṃ dharmarājena varuṇena mahātmanā  || 65.18 ||
nairṛtyena tathānyaiśca lokapālairyathā vidhi  |
pratyakṣo havyavāhaśca lokapālānupāgataḥ  || 65.19 ||
atrirmarīciḥ kaśyapaścakrustatra makhottamam  |
anyakṣetrācchataguṇā tatra dānādikā kriyā  || 65.20 ||
vārāṇasī kurukṣetraṃ gayā vai naimiṣaṃ tathā  |
māyāpurī puṣkaraṃ ca prayāgaḥ śaśibhūṣaṇam  || 65.21 ||
kāśyapī sarvatīrthāni yatra tiṣṭhati kalpagā  |
aśokavanikāyāstu nānyattīrthaṃ samaṃ viduḥ  || 65.22 ||
iṣṭaṃ yatra purā rājan hayamedhaṃ makhottamam  |
brahmaṇā mocitāḥ pūrvaṃ viprāḥ kauleyayonitaḥ  || 65.23 ||
asya tīrthasya māhātmyātpāṭalīputravāsinaḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
hayamedhamakheneṣṭaṃ kathaṃ tāta svayambhuvā  || 65.24 ||
kathaṃ ca mocitā viprāḥ pūrvaṃ kauleyayonitaḥ  |
ko vā rājā purā brahman devarājasamo'bhavat || 65.25 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathā nyāyaṃ śaṃsa me munisattama  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga samākhyānaṃ purātanam  || 65.26 ||
aśokavanikā tīrthaṃ kalpagā taṭamāśritam  |
na jānanti mahāmūḍhā manujāḥ pāpamohitāḥ  || 65.27 ||
guptādguptataraṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthottamottamam  |
viśokeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu tasmin paramasiddhidam  || 65.28 ||
pūjyate siddhagandharvairna tatpaśyanti mānuṣāḥ  |
darśanātsparśanāttasya brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 65.29 ||
svāyambhuve'ntare prāpte ādikalpe kṛte yuge  |
raviścandro mahārāja cakravartī mahāyaśāḥ  || 65.30 ||
somavaṃśe janiṃ prāptaḥ kāñcīpurapatistathā  |
śaśāsa pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ yathā śakrastriviṣṭapam  || 65.31 ||
gatastu pṛthivīpālo nānāvṛkṣasamākulam  |
nānāpakṣigaṇairjuṣṭaṃ nānāmuniniṣevatam  || 65.32 ||
yatrāgastyeśvaraṃ nāma śambhorāyatanaṃ śubham  |
sevyate munibhiḥ sarvairagastyādyaistapodhanaiḥ  || 65.33 ||
rāhusūryasamāyoge raviścandro nṛpottamaḥ  |
saptakalpavahā yatra śailaścāmarakaṇṭakaḥ  || 65.34 ||
hastyaśvarathapādātaiḥ sakośabalavāhanaiḥ  |
tapasyantaṃ mahātmānaṃ munisaṅghaiḥ samāvṛtam  || 65.35 ||
maitrāvaruṇikaṃ nāma jvalantamiti pāvakam  |
teṣāṃ madhye samutthāya śāṇḍilyaśca mahātapāḥ  || 65.36 ||
urasā pṛthivīṃ gatvā so'gastiṃ paripṛcchati  |
raviścandro mahātejāḥ samāyātastavāśramam  || 65.37 ||
purohito'hamasyāsmi jānīhi tvaṃ taponidhe  |
tvatpādārcanamākāṅkṣī manyase cedanugrahaḥ  || 65.38 ||

agastya uvāca - 
āgacchatu nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ śīghraṃ siṃhāsane sthitaḥ  |
āgatastadanujñātaḥ pādau jagrāha tasya ca  || 65.39 ||
arghapādyaiśca sampūjya papraccha kuśalaṃ muniḥ  |
kuśalaṃ te mahābhāga sāntaḥ puraparicchadaḥ  || 65.40 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā munīndraṃ prati bhārata  |
adya me saphalaṃ janma rājyaṃ jīvanameva ca  || 65.41 ||
muktaśca kilbiṣādasmāttvātpādāmbujadarśanāt |
sarvatra kalpagā puṇyā sarvatīrthamayī śubhā  || 65.42 ||
kasmin sthāne yaje yajñaṃ śaṃsa me munisattama  |
yathā saṃsiddhyate yajñaḥ surāṇāṃ tṛptirakṣayā  || 65.43 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathā nyāyaṃ trikālajña nivedaya  |

agastya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kathyamānaṃ nibodha ca  || 65.44 ||
śivena kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  |
brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāmanyeṣāṃ ca divaukasām  || 65.45 ||
mayā tatra śrutaṃ rājanmārkaṇḍena cirāyuṣā  |
tatte'haṃ kathayiṣyāmi mekalātīrthasambhavam  || 65.46 ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ munayaḥ sarve yatpraṣṭavyāvatāraṇam  |
kasya śaktirmahārāja varjayitvā maheśvaram  || 65.47 ||
pramāṇaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ saṃkhyāṃ vā kartumāditaḥ  |
uddeśamātravaktā'haṃ mārkaṇḍasya mahāmuneḥ  || 65.48 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathāvṛttaṃ purātanam  || 65.49 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 66

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
evaṃ śrutaṃ purā rājñā raviścandreṇa dhīmatā  |
uvāca vacanaṃ śrīmānagastyo munisattama  || 66.1 ||
sarasvatī na gaṅgā ca yamunā vā na sāgarāḥ  |
na caivānyāni tīrthāni prayāgapramukhānyapi  || 66.2 ||
ekaiva narmadā puṇyā saptakalpavahā śubhā  |
lakṣayojanaparyantaṃ jambūdvīpaṃ prakīrtitam  || 66.3 ||
narmadātoyapānena lokāloke carācare  |
tapohīnā narāścaiva te'pi yānti śivālayam  || 66.4 ||
yo'yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ sa taṃ prāpnoti puṣkalam  |
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga pṛṣṭo'haṃ yattvayānagha  || 66.5 ||
narmadā kathitā divyā hṛdyā kasya na rocate  |
santi tīrthāni yāvanti dakṣiṇottarakūlayoḥ  || 66.6 ||
tvatprītidāni tāvanti kathayāmi nṛpottama  |
anyāni granthalakṣeṇa na ca kīrtayituṃ kṣamaḥ  || 66.7 ||
trayo vedāstrayo lokāstisraḥ sandhyāstrayo'gnayaḥ  |
siddhagandharvayakṣāśca sakinnaramahoragāḥ  || 66.8 ||
vidyādharaścāpsarasaḥ kalpagātaṭamāśritāḥ  |
oṃkārādīni liṅgāni vaidūryādinagāḥ purā  || 66.9 ||
dvāpare ca kaliṃ prāpya pāvanatvamavāpnuyuḥ  |
brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāṃ maryādā kathyate'dhunā  || 66.10 ||
prabhābhirdyotamānāyā revāyā dakṣiṇottare  |
yajñabhūmiriyaṃ khyātā surāsuranamaskṛtā  || 66.11 ||
aśokavanikā tatra tasyā devo maheśvaraḥ  |
tatra siddhyati nirvighno yajña ityāha śaṅkaraḥ  || 66.12 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā agastyaṃ munisattamam  |
svasti vo'stu gamiṣyāmi tvayā saha mahāmune  || 66.13 ||
aśokavanikāṃ prāptaḥ sa rājā munibhirvṛtaḥ  |
revāyā dakṣiṇe kūle puṇyatīrthesu śobhane  || 66.14 ||
daśayojanaparyantaṃ yajñayūpāśca maṇḍapam  |
maṇimāṇikyaratnaughaiḥ sarvadvāreṣu śobhitāḥ  || 66.15 ||
patākādhvajaśobhāḍyā nānāvastrāvaguṇṭhitāḥ  |
viśvāmitro bharadvājaḥ kaśyapo bhārgavastathā  || 66.16 ||
brahmadṛśyo lomaśaśca tathānye munisattamāḥ  |
vālakhilyā mahābhāgā mānasā brahmaṇaḥ sutāḥ  || 66.17 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
tataḥ pravartito yajño brāhmaṇairāptadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 66.18 ||
tṛptāśca devatāḥ sarvāḥ pratijagmustriviṣṭapam  |
jagmuḥ sarve ca muditā munayaḥ svāśramaṃ prati  || 66.19 ||
tato nivartito yajño durvāsāḥ kupito gataḥ  |
nātra vaivasvato nāhaṃ nāradaḥ parvatastathā  || 66.20 ||
kathaṃ nivartito yajñaḥ pāparkarminarādhamaiḥ  |
āgatastu yamastatra nāradaḥ parvatastathā  || 66.21 ||
lekhakaścitraguptaśca kālo mṛtyustathaiva ca  |
ete ca kupitāḥ sarve yajñabhāgaṃ vinā nṛpa  || 66.22 ||
tān sarvān kupitān dṛṣṭvā raviścandro'bravīdvacaḥ  |
aśokeśvaradevasya narmadāyāḥ prasādataḥ  || 66.23 ||
ko me samartho vighnāya surāsuragaṇeṣvapi  |
tathaiva ko'nyo jantūnāṃ yajñavighnasya hetave  || 66.24 ||
yajñakāle ca saṃprāpto ya kaścidapi mānavaḥ  |
pūjanīyastathārcyaśca yathā devaścaturbhujaḥ  || 66.25 ||
yathāyātā mahābhāgā brahmaputrā mahaujasaḥ  |
dadāmi vo na sandeho manasā yadabhīpsitam  || 66.26 ||

durvāsā uvāca - 
paripūjyaśca naḥ putro nāradaḥ parvatastathā  |
ekākī prārthaye nāhaṃ militvā prārthayāmahe  || 66.27 ||

raviścandra uvāca - 
yo yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ tasmai pradadāmyaham  |
iti sarve'pi tenaiva prastutā munipuṅgavāḥ  || 66.28 ||
suprītā vihitā rājannarghapādyapradānataḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
munayaḥ kena kāryeṇa pāṭalīputravāsinaḥ  || 66.29 ||
devyā śaptāḥ śvayoniṃ ca gatā muktāśca te katham  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
purā tapodhanāḥ sarve jaṭāvalkaladhāriṇaḥ  || 66.30 ||
naipāle vai paśupatiṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram  |
pūjayanti śivaṃ bhaktyā gauryā virahitaṃ haram  || 66.31 ||
ardhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
saṃśaptāstena kāryeṇa pārvatyā liṅgabhedinaḥ  || 66.32 ||
varṣaṃ sahasraṃ hi mitaṃ śvayoniṃ ca gamiṣyatha  |
nirmālyabhakṣakāḥ pāpāścaṇḍadravyasya bhakṣakāḥ  || 66.33 ||
teṣāṃ kṛte mahārāja durvāsā nṛpamabravīt |
śvayoniṃ samanuprāptāstatra kāle munīśvarāḥ  || 66.34 ||
mocaya tvaṃ tato rājannasmatpriyacikīrṣayā  |
pārvatyā te'bhiśaptāśca narake majjanti dāruṇe  || 66.35 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā muniṃ durvāsasaṃ tataḥ  |
mocayāmi na sandehastasmātpāpāddvijottamān  || 66.36 ||
preṣitāḥ kiṅkarāstena sīdanto yatra te vane  |
praṇipatya ca te sarve tānūcuśca vanecarān  || 66.37 ||
smārayanti pūrvajātimādiṣṭāḥ prabhuṇā yathā  |
tatastadvacanātprāptāste'śokavanikāṃ drutam  || 66.38 ||
raviścandraścakravartī tān vilokya tapodhanān  |
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ prāha tān prahasanniva  || 66.39 ||
aśokeśvaradevasya mekalāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
mama dānaprabhāveṇa maharṣīṇāṃ prasādataḥ  || 66.40 ||
tyaktvā śvayoniṃ munayaḥ śivalokaṃ prayāntu vai  |
etatpāpaṃ mahāghoraṃ mayi sarvaṃ niṣīdatu  || 66.41 ||
tatkṣaṇānmuktaśāpāste kāmikaṃ yānamāsthitāḥ  |
ūcurmaharṣayo vākyaṃ raviścandraṃ śatakratum  || 66.42 ||
tvaṃ mātā tvaṃ pitāsmākaṃ gururmokṣapradāyakaḥ  |
evamuktvā yayuste tu umāmāheśvaraṃ param  || 66.43 ||
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga tvaṃ tu yajñataponidhiḥ  |
nā'nyastvayā samaḥ kaścitsomavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ  || 66.44 ||
tvayā hi nirjitaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
evamuktvā suraśreṣṭhāḥ sādhuvādaistamārcayan  || 66.45 ||
devadundubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ca  |

durvāsā uvāca - 
kṣatriyeṣu tvayā tulyo na dṛṣṭo na śruto mayā  || 66.46 ||
prāṇatyāgo hi sukaro dharmatyāgo hi duṣkaraḥ  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  || 66.47 ||
prahasannabravīdvākyaṃ rājā durvāsasaṃ munim  |
mama dānaprabhāveṇa narā duṣkṛtabuddhayaḥ  || 66.48 ||
prāpnuvan tu paraṃ lokaṃ vara eṣa mama priyaḥ  |
evamastviti tasyāgre'bhidhāya munipuṅgavaḥ  || 66.49 ||
sa mudā parayā yuktastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
taddṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ karma rājñaścāmitatejasaḥ  || 66.50 ||
śaṅkayā parayā yukto dharmarājo'bravīdidam  |
varaṃ dadāmi bhadraṃ te yajñabhāgabahiṣkṛtaḥ  || 66.51 ||
śvayonitvaṃ gatā viprā mocitāḥ karmabandhanāt |
īdṛśaṃ tava sāmarthyaṃ jānāmi ca nṛpottama  || 66.52 ||
pṛthivyāṃ duṣkaraṃ karma yajñaścaiva viśeṣataḥ  |
yo dadāti mahābhāga svakīyaṃ puṇyamuttamam  || 66.53 ||
yamaloko jitastena devaloko jitastathā  |
varayogyo'si rājendra satyametatmayoditam  || 66.54 ||

raviścandra uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭaḥ sūryaputra varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  |
mama yajñaśatenaiva dānena tapasā tathā  || 66.55 ||
pāpayonigatā ye tu ye ca duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ  |
prayāntu tvatprasādena dharmarāja śivālayam  || 66.56 ||
imaṃ varamahaṃ manye prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  |

yama uvāca - 
evaṃ bhavatu rājendra satyadharmaparāyaṇa  || 66.57 ||
prāpnuhi tvaṃ paraṃ lokaṃ satyenānena suvrata  |
yataste mocitāḥ sarvāḥ kaśmalātpāpayonayaḥ  || 66.58 ||
kṣatriyāḥ śataśo rājannanye caiva sahasraśaḥ  |
pāpātsamuddhṛtā ye ca teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate  || 66.59 ||
evamuktvā nṛpaśreṣṭhaṃ dharmarājo mahābhujaḥ  |
kāmikaṃ yānamāruhya surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 66.60 ||
yayau svabhavanaṃ rājannāradaḥ parvatastathā  |
tasyāmaśītilakṣāṇi tīrthānāṃ narasattama  || 66.61 ||
aśokavanikāyāṃ tu kīrtitāni tavānagha  |
śravaṇātkīrtanātteṣāṃ gosahasraphalaṃ bhavet || 66.62 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'śokavanikāvarṇano nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 67

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athātaḥ kathayiṣyāmi tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
revāyā uttare kūle puraṃ vāgīśvarābhidham  || 67.1 ||
vāgurnāma nadī tatra revayā saha saṃgatā  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 67.2 ||
vāgīśā tatra cāmuṇḍā dānavakṣayakāriṇī  |
maṇibhadro vīrabhadrastathā'nye śataśo nṛpāḥ  || 67.3 ||
babhūvurmuktaśāpāste tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
tilapiṇḍapradānena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  || 67.4 ||
brahmadattaścakravartī sūryavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ  |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān cakratulyaparākramaḥ  || 67.5 ||
dhanadhānyasamāyukto bhayadāridryavarjitaḥ  |
prajāstasminmahīpāle sarvā'pi mudānvitāḥ  || 67.6 ||
iṣṭaḥ kratuvarastena narmadāvāgusaṅgame  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvāḥ śakraviṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 67.7 ||
pratyakṣaśca maheśāno gaṇeśvarasamanvitaḥ  |
lokapālāśca marutaścandrādityau dhruvastathā  || 67.8 ||
ṛkṣāṇi yogasiddhāśca somamukhyāśca sarvaśaḥ  |
vaśiṣṭho munibhiḥ sārddhaṃ videhādhipatistathā  || 67.9 ||
evamādyāḥ samāhūtā mitrāvaruṇa eva ca  |
sarve hiraṇmayāstatra yajñayūpāśca maṇḍapāḥ  || 67.10 ||
daśayojanaparyantaṃ yajñabhūmirmahībhṛtaḥ  |
svārociṣe'ntare rājannādikalpe kṛte yuge  || 67.11 ||
gavāṃ śatasahasrāṇi hemabhārānvitāni ca  |
hayānāṃ śyāmakarṇānāmayutaṃ sāgrameva ca  || 67.12 ||
dantināmayutaṃ caiva ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitam  |
maṇimāṇikyamuktāśca bhakṣyabhojyānyanekadhā  || 67.13 ||
evaṃ rājā brahmadattaḥ sarvabhūpālasattamaḥ  |
yajñaṃ pravartayāmāsa sarvasambhārasaṃbhṛtaḥ  || 67.14 ||
vedanirghoṣaśabdena gītavādyaraveṇa ca  |
nānāyānasamārūḍhaiḥ stūyamāno marudgaṇaiḥ  || 67.15 ||
brahmadattasya yajñena vāgīśasya prasādataḥ  |
narmadāyāḥ prasādena pretāstṛptiṃ parāṃ gatāḥ  || 67.16 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kathaṃ tu brahmadattasya kalpagātīrayājanam  |
kathaṃ pretā vinirmuktāḥ pretāste kena karmaṇā  || 67.17 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathā nyāyaṃ kathayasva tapodhana  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan yathā nyāyamitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 67.18 ||
kārttikyāmutsavaṃ prāpya puṣkare jyeṣṭhapuṣkare  |
ayogandhaḥ svayaṃbhūśca puṇḍarīkākṣa eva ca  || 67.19 ||
pitāmahaḥ svayaṃ tatra surāsuraguruḥ pitā  |
kāvyaśca hotṛsadanau vedagarbhaḥ kṛtadhvanaḥ  || 67.20 ||
svastikaścaiva sāvitro vāmadevo'ghamarṣaṇaḥ  |
ete cānye ca munayo brahmatejo'śasaṃbhavāḥ  || 67.21 ||
tathā te hi yathāśaktā ṛtukālābhigāminaḥ  |
gārhasthye ca sthitā bhāryā bhartṛśuśrūṣaṇe ratāḥ  || 67.22 ||
cīravalkaladhāriṇyaḥ śākasyāmākabhakṣikāḥ  |
viṣaṇṇāstena dharmeṇa satyastā apyagarhayan  || 67.23 ||
dvijasya ṣaṭca karmāṇi yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā  |
adhyāpanaṃ cādhyayanaṃ dānaṃ caiva pratigrahaḥ  || 67.24 ||
bhūṣaṇaṃ paridhānaṃ ca yoṣitāṃ bhartṛsevanam  |
evaṃ ca garhitā rājan yoṣidbhiḥ patayastathā  || 67.25 ||
bhītāste vismitāḥ sarve viṣaṇṇavadanāstathā  |
hariścandraḥ purā rājā na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 67.26 ||
dānena nirjitaṃ yena trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
rāhusūryasamāyoge kurukṣetraṃ jagāma ha  || 67.27 ||
tyaktvā te puṣkaraṃ tīrthaṃ dhanalobhena mohitāḥ  |
sahasrasaṃkhyāmunayaḥ sabhāryāḥ sasutāśca te  || 67.28 ||
yatra rājā hariścandraḥ pratigrahavilipsayā  |
munīnāha hariścandro mudā paramayā yutaḥ  || 67.29 ||
dhanyā me saphalā yātrā kurukṣetre ravigrahe  |
kṣudhārtā duḥkhitāścaiva bālā vṛddhāḥ kṛśāturāḥ  || 67.30 ||
valkalājinavastrāśca yauvane pretarūpiṇaḥ  |
yanno yūyamabhiprāptāḥ patnīputraiśca saṃyutāḥ  || 67.31 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
ādeśo dīyatāṃ mahyaṃ kiṃ karomi bhavatkṛte  || 67.32 ||
evamuktvā dadau śrīmānekaikasya pṛthakpṛthak |
lakṣaṃ lakṣaṃ hiraṇyasya tathā gāvaḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 67.33 ||
sahasraṃ turagāṇāṃ ca dantināṃ śatameva ca  |
sāptabhaumān gṛhān ramyān hemaprākāratoraṇān  || 67.34 ||
nānāvidhavilāsāṃśca yathā dhanapatiḥ svayam  |
kalpāntare mṛtā jātāḥ pretarūpā bhayaṅkarāḥ  || 67.35 ||
lamboṣṭhā lambavṛṣaṇā vikṛtānanasaṃyutāḥ  |
pratigrahaprabhāveṇa dvijasya patanaṃ dhruvam  || 67.36 ||
jātismarāḥ svaṃ śocanti ekākīnāstu te bahiḥ  |
na bhāryā na ca me putrā na bhṛtyā na ca bāndhavāḥ  || 67.37 ||
na te pratigrahairdagdhā yathā pūrvaṃ tathaiva ca  |
vayamekākino dagdhā vṛkṣā iva havirbhujā  || 67.38 ||
rājapratigrahairdagdhā na prarohanti mānavāḥ  |
vaiśvānareṇa dagdhānāṃ punarjanma prajāyate  || 67.39 ||
na mātā na pitā putro draviṇaṃ na ca bāndhavāḥ  |
yamadūtairgṛhītānāṃ dharma ekaḥ sahānugaḥ  || 67.40 ||
śocitvā suciraṃ kālaṃ bhāryāputravivarjitāḥ  |
bhramitvā ca mahīṃ sarvāṃ puṣkaraṃ tīrthamāgatāḥ  || 67.41 ||
pretarūpānmunīn dṛṣṭvā viṣādaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  |
tānuvāca muniśreṣṭhaḥ kathaṃ pretatvamāgatāḥ  || 67.42 ||

pretā ūcu̱ḥ
hariścandraḥ satyadharma sūryavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ  |
ayodhyādhipatiḥ śrīmān devatulyaparākramaḥ  || 67.43 ||
tasya pratigraho'smābhirāptaḥ sūryagrahe sthite  |
tena pretatvamāpannāḥ sarve brahmarṣayo mune  || 67.44 ||
etatte kathitaṃ brahmanmokṣo'smākaṃ vidhīyatām  |
bhaviṣyabhūtatattvajño brahmaputrastaponidhiḥ  || 67.45 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ śrīmānnāradastāṃstapodhanān  |
kasminnavasare puṇye kārttikyāṃ divyaparvaṇi  || 67.46 ||
śivena kīrtitaṃ pūrvaṃ pārvatyāḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca  |
śrutaṃ mayāpi tatraiva purāṇaṃ skandakīrtitam  || 67.47 ||
kānyā pāpakṣayaṃ kartuṃ śaktā revāṃ vinā nṛpa  |
gaṅgādyāḥ sarito viprāḥ puṇyatīrthāstathāpi ca  || 67.48 ||
vāgīśaṃ ca puraṃ tatra narmadātaṭamāśritam  |
adhvare brahmadattasya mokṣaṇaṃ tu bhaviṣyati  || 67.49 ||
uddeśaṃ tu tato dattvā nāradastridivaṃ gataḥ  |
te'pi pretā mahābhāga dhyātvā śivamumāpatim  || 67.50 ||
abhijagmustamuddeśaṃ vāgīśapuramuttamam  |
tatra snātvābhyarcya śivaṃ hariṃ bhāskarameva ca  || 67.51 ||
adhvare brahmadattasya muktāḥ sarve'pi kilbiṣāt |
brahmayānaṃ samāruhya brahmalokaṃ samāgatāḥ  || 67.52 ||
pratapanti yathādityā brahmatejovapurdharāḥ  |
tasyopari nareśasya puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta vai  || 67.53 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathāvṛttaṃ purātanam  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 67.54 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vāgīśvarākhyāna nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 68

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
pratigrahagrahagrastā lobhamohavimohitāḥ  |
majjanti narake ghore yatra gatvā na nirgatāḥ  || 68.1 ||
saphalā vedayajñāśca tīrthayātrā ca bhārata  |
tathā kliśyanti cātmānaṃ pratigrahaparā narāḥ  || 68.2 ||
dātā ca yācakaścaiva karābhyāmeva sūcitau  |
atho gacchedgrahītā tu dātā gacchati corddhvataḥ  || 68.3 ||
sahasrāvartakaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
tatra snātasya vidhivadvṛṣotsargaphalaṃ bhavet || 68.4 ||
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ caiva punīte nā'tra saṃśayaḥ  |
revāyā uttare kūle sahasrāyudhasaṃkhyayā  || 68.5 ||
tataścānte mahābhāga kārāyā vanamuttamam  |
agniṣṭomaphalaṃ yatra snātvā svargaṃ ca gacchati  || 68.6 ||
revāyā uttare bhāge tīrtha paramaśobhanam  |
saugandhikaṃ vanaṃ nāma brahmacāriśucivratāḥ  || 68.7 ||
siṣicuḥ pitarastattu brahmādyāḥ sutapodhanāḥ  |
siddhacāraṇagandharvāḥ sakinnaramahoragāḥ  || 68.8 ||
praviśya tadvanaṃ martyaḥ sarvapāpātpramucyate  |
tataḥ sarasvatī cāsti nadīnāmuttamā nadī  || 68.9 ||
lakṣyā devasutā rājanmahāpuṇyā prakīrtitā  |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta mānavo nṛpate jale  || 68.10 ||
tarpayitvā pitṝn devānaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
īśānādhyuṣitaṃ nāma tatra tīrthaṃ sudurlabham  || 68.11 ||
tatra snātvā vyatīpāte saṃkrāntau grahaṇe naraḥ  |
sahasrakapilādāne vājimedhe ca yatphalam  || 68.12 ||
sugandhāṃ chātakumbhāśca pañcayajñāṃśca bhārata  |
abhigamya naraśreṣṭha svargaloke mahīyate  || 68.13 ||
triśūlākhyaṃ tu tatraiva tīrthamāsādya bhārata  |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryādarcayetpitṛdaivatam  || 68.14 ||
gaṇeśatvaṃ sa labhate tyaktvā dehaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
tato gacchenmahārāja brahmasthānamanuttamam  || 68.15 ||
revāyā uttare kūle kāmabhogaphalapradam  |
brahmodamiti vikhyātaṃ prakāśyaṃ bhuvi bhārata  || 68.16 ||
tatra saptarṣayaḥ prāptāḥ snānārthaṃ bharatarṣabha  |
kapiñjalo muniśreṣṭho havyavāhaśca bhārata  || 68.17 ||
bhagavān devayānaśca viśvāvasumahāmuniḥ  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyādbrahmalokamavāpnuyuḥ  || 68.18 ||
pitaraḥ śrāddhadānena prayātā brahmaṇaḥ puram  |
udumbarasya kṛtvā tu vidhivaddarśanaṃ tataḥ  || 68.19 ||
antarddhānamavāpnoti tapasā dagdhakilbiṣaḥ  |
tato gacchenmahārāja śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram  || 68.20 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāmabhigamya vṛṣadhvajam  |
labhate sarvakāmāṃśca svargalokaṃ hi gacchati  || 68.21 ||
narmadāyāmyabhāge tu gopyādgopyatara mahat |
siddhaliṅgaṃ maṇimayaṃ na tatpaśyanti mānavāḥ  || 68.22 ||
nāgendrasurasiddhaiśca nāgakanyābhirarcyate  |
sapādakoṭistīrthānāṃ śaṅkare kurunandana  || 68.23 ||
vasūnāmāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ munīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām  |
śivabhaktiparāṇāṃca kandamūlaphalāśinām  || 68.24 ||
pitṝṇāmakṣayā tṛptistilatoyapradānataḥ  |
mudā paramayā yukto dātā yāti śivālayam  || 68.25 ||
dhruvo dharaśca somaśca sāvitraścānalo'nilaḥ  |
pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca vasavo'ṣṭau prakīrtitā  || 68.26 ||
śaṅkarasya prasādena divi devatvamāgatāḥ  |
kalpagāsaumyabhāge tu somatīrthamanuttamam  || 68.27 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājan svargaloke mahīyate  |
saptasārasvataṃ tīrthaṃ tato gacchennṛpottama  || 68.28 ||
brahmaṇā ca kṛtaṃ stotraṃ puṇyakīrte niśāmaya  |

brahmovācā̱
vākpatirvacasāṃ nityaṃ vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.29 ||
haṃsaḥ sureśo vaktā vā vasūnāmantarā vasan  |
hotā diviṣadīśāno vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.30 ||
svāhākāraḥ svadhākāro vaṣaṭkāro haviṣyabhuk |
ṛṅmūrtiryajuṣāṃ mūrtirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.31 ||
kṣetrajñaḥ paramaḥ sūkṣmo jagatāṃ tārako hariḥ  |
īśvaro hṛdayāvāso vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.32 ||
śravaṇyaḥ śravaṇopāyaḥ puṇyaślokaḥ śuciśravāḥ  |
varado vāsudevo'gnirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.33 ||
puruṣaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ purāṇo bhuvaneśvaraḥ  |
ādityāntargato vahṇirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.34 ||
kaṃsakāliyahantā ca subalo balamardanaḥ  |
śiśupālanihantā'gnirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.35 ||
kālaneminihantā'gniryaḥ kāle niyatāntakaḥ  |
śatāsuraśarīraghno vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.36 ||
kaṅkāsuranihantā ca madhukaiṭabhanāśanaḥ  |
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.37 ||
śuklaḥ salilaśāyī ca viṣṇuḥ pāpakṣayāhvayaḥ  |
indro vacanasatpālo vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.38 ||
hṛṣīkeśaścendrapālopendro garuḍāsanaḥ  |
sahasranāmā dharmajño vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.39 ||
śaṅkhī ca nandakī cakrī śārṅgadhanvā gadādharaḥ  |
dhīro vapuṣmānmedhāvī vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.40 ||
bṛhatsaṃkarṣaṇaḥ śambhuḥ svayambhūrbhūtabhāvanaḥ  |
nipuṇo lakṣaṇaḥ śuddho vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.41 ||
traikālikastrikālajñastrayī kartā trilocanaḥ  |
trisāmā devakīsūnurvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.42 ||
avyaktātmā mahātmā ca antarātmā janārdanaḥ  |
prāṇaścendriyabhūtātmā vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.43 ||
paramātmā paraṃ brahma parameśaḥ parā gatiḥ  |
parameṣṭhī paraṃ jyotirvāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.44 ||
viśvātmā viśvakartā ca viśvasya patirātmavān  |
dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ kartā ca vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.45 ||
sahasraśīrṣā bhagavān sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt |
sahasrakoṭidhārī vā vāsudevo'stu me gatiḥ  || 68.46 ||
iti vāgīśvaro vāgmī pūjitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
bhaktasya bhagavān viṣṇuḥ prīyatāṃ me janārdanaḥ  || 68.47 ||
janmaprabhṛti yatkiṃcinmayā sukṛtamarjitam  |
tatsamagraṃ phalaṃ cāstu śāśvataṃ puruṣottama  || 68.48 ||
idamabhyasyato nityaṃ pūjitaḥ syātsakeśavaḥ  |
vināśayati pāpāni prakāśayati tatphalam  || 68.49 ||
eṣa niṣkaṇṭakaḥ panthā yatra sampūjyate hariḥ  |
kupathaṃ taṃ vijānīyādyatra nārādhyate hariḥ  || 68.50 ||
vāsudevaparā vedā sudevaparākriyā  |
vāsudevātmakā viprā vāsudevaparāśrayaḥ  || 68.51 ||
sarve devā vāsudevaṃ yajante sarve devā vāsudevātprasūtāḥ  |
sarveṣāṃ vā vāsudevo'pi devo nānyatkiṃcidavāsudevātiriktam  || 68.52 ||
nānyaḥ puṇyataro devo nāsti viṣṇuparaṃ tapaḥ  |
nāsti viṣṇuparaṃ jñānaṃ sarvaṃ viṣṇumayaṃ jagat || 68.53 ||
ye paṭhanti narā bhaktyā viṣṇunāmāṅkitastavam  |
te yānti vaiṣṇavaṃ lokaṃ paraṃ brahma sanātanam  || 68.54 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devo brahmaṇaḥ samahātmanaḥ  |
śriyā prabodhitaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śayāno yoganidrayā  || 68.55 ||
dṛṣṭvā'bravītsurān sarvānnānārūpān bhayānakān  |
kimasti vaḥ samutpannaṃ māṃ didṛkṣurihāgatāḥ  || 68.56 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā keśavaṃ prati bhārata  |
tvāṃ vinātra jagannātha kastrātā devakaṇṭakaiḥ  || 68.57 ||
dānavairveṣṭitā dhātrī svargaścaiva vināśitaḥ  |
dharmakāmādikā yajñā vedā viplāvitāstathā  || 68.58 ||
danubhārabharākrāntā rasātatatalaṃ gatā  |
jaṭāsuraśca jābālirdaityo mayasutastathā  || 68.59 ||
daśakoṭyastu daityānāṃ samagrabalaśālinām  |
śivaprasādayuktānāṃ svargaviplavakāriṇām  || 68.60 ||
tasmātpravartitaṃ cakramuddhara svavasundharām  |
śrutvā vākyamidaṃ devo bhayārtaṃ prāṇapīḍitam  || 68.61 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devo bhayaṃ tyajata daityajam  |
acireṇaiva kālena haniṣyāmi mahāsurān  || 68.62 ||
vārāharūpamāsthāya preṣitaṃ kalpagājale  |
daṃṣṭrāgreṇa dhṛtā dhātrī dānavānāṃ kṣayaḥ kṛtaḥ  || 68.63 ||
punaḥ pravartitā sṛṣṭiryathāpūrvaṃ tathaiva ca  |
brahmādyā muditā devāḥ pratijagmustriviṣṭapam  || 68.64 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan vārāhaṃ kalpagātaṭe  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 68.65 ||
iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe vārāhamahimānuvarṇano nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 69

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato devapathaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubham  |
tatra snātaśca vidhivatsarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 69.1 ||
māse māse kuśāgreṇa somayāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
sa revājalapūtasya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm  || 69.2 ||
liṅgaṃ devapathaṃ nāma surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
śraddhayā taddarśanena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  || 69.3 ||
caitre māse caturdaśyā pakṣayorubhayorapi  |
snānārthaṃ sarvatīrthāni jagmuḥ kartuṃ ca satkriyām  || 69.4 ||
yaddevaloke devānāmīpsitaṃ ca nṛpadhvaja  |
sahasrāṇi munīndrāṇāṃ tasmiṃśśivamupāsate  || 69.5 ||
cāndrāyaṇaparāḥ kecidbrahmakūrcaparāstathā  |
kandamūlaphalāhārā jalāhārā jalapriyāḥ  || 69.6 ||
agnihotraparā nityaṃ tathā hutahutāśanāḥ  |
upāsate devapathaṃ saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ  || 69.7 ||
athānyatsaṃpravakṣyāmi tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
sahasrayajñaṃ paramaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 69.8 ||
ekādaśyāṃ mārgaśīrṣe pūjayitvā janārdanam  |
sahasrayajñasyeṣṭasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 69.9 ||
na paśyedyamalokaṃ ca tiryagyoniṃ na gacchati  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa naro vigatakalmaṣaḥ  || 69.10 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan puṇyākhyānamanuttamam  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya viṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 69.11 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthamayaṃ śubham  |
yatra snāto'pi labhate daśadhenuphalaṃ naraḥ  || 69.12 ||
śuklīkṛtāstena devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
sapādakoṭistīrthānāṃ śuklatīrthe vyavasthitā  || 69.13 ||
aṣṭahastapramāṇaṃ ca śuklatīrthaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
tatra kālāgnirudraśca śrīkaṇṭhaśca tathā paraḥ  || 69.14 ||
taistaistapobhirugraiśca tatra siddhiṃ parā gatāḥ  |
śuklatīrthaprabhāveṇa modante divi devatāḥ  || 69.15 ||
śakro'pi ca purādhyakṣaṃ devadevamumāpatim  |
revātoyena saṃsnāpya bilvapatraiḥ samārcayat || 69.16 ||
paurṇamāsyāmamāvasyāṃ somaḥ sūryaḥ prabhāvatīm  |
tatra snāto grahaiḥ sārddhaṃ nakṣatradhruvamaṇḍalaiḥ  || 69.17 ||
tena devāśca dīvyante śuklatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  |
kaśyapasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ surasiddhaniṣevitam  || 69.18 ||
munīnāmayutaṃ tatra svayaṃ tiṣṭhati bhārata  |
kandamūlaphalāhārā jalāhārāstathā pare  || 69.19 ||
śākāhārā nirāhārā brahmakūrcāstathā pare  |
cāndrāyaṇaparāḥ kecidanye māsopavāsinaḥ  || 69.20 ||
ṛṣikoṭyastrayastriṃśacchukleśvaramupāsate  |
rāhugraste tathā candre paurṇamāsyāṃ tithau tathā  || 69.21 ||
āyānti sarvatīrthāni snātumetacchivoditam  |
sthāneśvare yatphalaṃ syādrāhusūryasamāgame  || 69.22 ||
tatphalaṃ prāpnuyātsarvaṃ śuklatīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  |
hemadhenudharādīni rūpyadā gajadāstathā  || 69.23 ||
etaddattvā mahārāja puṇyasaṃkhyā na vidyate  |
dhanadena kubereṇa devagandharvadānavaiḥ  || 69.24 ||
rāhusūryasamāyoge śuklatīrthe maheśvaraḥ  |
candanāgurukarpūrapuṣpamālābhirarcitaḥ  || 69.25 ||
vitānadhvajamukhyaiśca dīpamālāprabodhanaiḥ  |
asya tīrthaprabhāveṇa yakṣarājo dhaneśvaraḥ  || 69.26 ||
bhogā nānāvidhāstena samprāptā divi devatāḥ  |
sarvatīrthamayaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ ca yat || 69.27 ||
api varṣasahasreṇa śuklatīrthasya varṇanam  |
na śakyate suraiḥ kartuṃ purāṇe skandakīrtite  || 69.28 ||
pāpayonigato yaśca tiryagyonigataśca yaḥ  |
brahmahā ca surāpaśca śivanirmālyabhakṣakaḥ  || 69.29 ||
mucyate tena pāpena tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
tatra snānaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā pūjayitvā vṛṣadhvajam  || 69.30 ||
surāsuragaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ pūjyate narasattama  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājanmahāpātakanāśanam  || 69.31 ||
pitāmahena yatreṣṭo yajñe yajñeśvaraḥ purā  |
stotraṃ kṛtvā yathā nyāyaṃ devadevasya śūlinaḥ  || 69.32 ||
pūjayitvā tu śukleśaṃ brahmā stotramudāharat |
namaḥ śivāya śāntāya jñānavijñānarūpiṇe  || 69.33 ||
sūkṣmāya sūkṣmarūpāya sarvasūkṣmāya hetave  |
sūkṣmāṇāmapi sūkṣmāya namaḥ sūkṣmatamāya ca  || 69.34 ||
divyāya divyarūpāya divyadehāya setave  |
divyānāmapi divyāya namo divyatamāya ca  || 69.35 ||
vyomaprabhāya bhāvāya aghorāya namo namaḥ  |
vyomapramāṇadhāmāya vāmeśāya namo namaḥ  || 69.36 ||
parāya parameśāya pāramārthikahetave  |
parāya paramuktāya namaḥ paratarāya ca  || 69.37 ||
ekajihvadvijahvāya bahujihvāya te namaḥ  |
tathaivāsaṃkhyajihvāya triṇetrāya namo namaḥ  || 69.38 ||
pūjyāya pūjyapūjyāya sarvapūjyaikahetave  |
nityāyānityarūpāya nityanityaikahetave  || 69.39 ||
nityānāmapi nityāya namo nityatamāya ca  |
śaktāya śaktirūpāya sarvaśaktyekahetave  || 69.40 ||
śaktānāmapi śaktāya namaḥ śaktatamāya ca  |
śuddhāya sarvaśuddhāya sarvaśuddhyekahetave  || 69.41 ||
kālāya kālarūpāya sarvakālaikahetave  |
kālānāmapi kālāya namaḥ kālatamāya te  || 69.42 ||
sarvamantraśarīrāya sarvamantraikahetave  |
mantrāṇāmapi mantrāya namo mantratamāya ca  || 69.43 ||
aprameyamaheśāya īśānāya namo namaḥ  |
yogāya yogarūpāya yogapuruṣa te namaḥ  || 69.44 ||
ekakaṇṭhadvikaṇṭhāya bahukaṇṭhāya te namaḥ  |
asaṃkhyakaṇṭhayuktāya nīlakaṇṭhāya te namaḥ  || 69.45 ||
anantāya maheśāya hartre kartre namo'stu te  |
namaste'stu mahādeva namaste'stu sadāśiva  || 69.46 ||
namaste'stu mahāśuddha namastubhyaṃ namo namaḥ  |
namo bhasmāṅgarāgāya namaḥ khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇe  || 69.47 ||
sarvātmane namastubhyaṃ viśveśāya namo namaḥ  |
sarvajñāya namastubhyaṃ sanāthāya namo namaḥ  || 69.48 ||
avyaktāya namastubhyaṃ śāśvatāya namo namaḥ  |
kailāsavāsine tubhyaṃ namaḥ pātālavāsine  || 69.49 ||
tvayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ lokālokaṃ carācaram  |
api varṣasahasreṇa kaḥ stotuṃ śaktimān bhavet || 69.50 ||
iti stavena divyena yaḥ stauti parameśvaram  |
vidhūya sarvapāpāni rudraloke mahīyate  || 69.51 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śambhustutirnāmaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

adhyāya 70

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kimarthaṃ saṃstuto devo brahmaṇā tena tatra vai  |
śuklatīrthamidaṃ kasmādāste yatra maheśvaraḥ  || 70.1 ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyāhi pṛcchato me mahāmune  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājan kathāṃ divyāṃ svargārhaṇyāmanuttamām  || 70.2 ||
yāṃ śrutvā sarvapāpebhyastīrthasnānena mucyate  |
yayātirnāma dharmātmā satyadharmaparāyaṇaḥ  || 70.3 ||
cakravartī nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  |
iyāja sa mahāyajñaiḥ śatakraturivāparaḥ  || 70.4 ||
nadī madhumatī puṇyā revayā yatra saṅgatā  |
yatra yajñaḥ samārabdha ṛtvigbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha  || 70.5 ||
madhyeśvaraṃ yatra liṅgaṃ svayaṃdevo maheśvaraḥ  |
tatra snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  || 70.6 ||
cakreṇa viṣṇunā tatra ghātitau madhukaiṭabhau  |
arcanāttasya devasya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 70.7 ||
tilatoyapradānena piṇḍadānena bhārata  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 70.8 ||
tatra yajñaḥ samārabdho hariśaṅkaravarjitaḥ  |
jaṭāsurastatra daityaśchidraṃ dṛṣṭvā samāgataḥ  || 70.9 ||
tatovidhvaṃsito yajño dānavairbaladarpitaiḥ  |
yajñayūpā yajñapātraṃ daśa dikṣu nipātitāḥ  || 70.10 ||
bhukto hutapuroḍāśaḥ somapānaṃ ca taiḥ kṛtam  |
praṇaṣṭā devatāḥ sarvā dānavānāṃ bhayena ca  || 70.11 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ devā mṛgarūpeṇa nirgatāḥ  |
dhanado yakṣarūpeṇa praṇaṣṭaḥ svapurīṃ gataḥ  || 70.12 ||
mahiṣārūḍho dharmarājo gajārūḍhaśśatakratuḥ  |
meṣārūḍho havyavāho nirgatā vratamāsthitāḥ  || 70.13 ||
varuṇaśca samāyātaḥ praṇaṣṭaḥ svapurīṃ gataḥ  |
makarāsanamārūḍho vāyuśca mṛgamāśritaḥ  || 70.14 ||
īśāna īśarūpeṇa vṛṣārūḍhaḥ palāyitaḥ  |
astrāṇi lokapālānāṃ hṛtāni danusambhavaiḥ  || 70.15 ||
ekākī yānamāruhya kathaṃ yāmi striyā saha  |
cintayitvā nṛpaśreṣṭhaścāstraṃ jagrāha bhārata  || 70.16 ||
tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti coktvā vai daityasiṃha durāsadam  |
na kṣatrakulasaṃjātā jātu dṛṣṭvā palāyitāḥ  || 70.17 ||
daśa dvādaśavarṣāṇi vimukhāstava pūrvajāḥ  |
na cātrāhvānito rudro rudra bhāgo na kalpitaḥ  || 70.18 ||
yajñe'smin yajñapuruṣo nāhūto bhagavān hariḥ  |
tena doṣeṇa me yajño dānavaiśca vināśitaḥ  || 70.19 ||
evamuktvā nṛpaśreṣṭho rudraṃ dhyātvā maheśvaram  |
raudrarūpaṃ samāsthāya jyāghoṣaṃ ghoṣarūpiṇam  || 70.20 ||
jagrāha kopānnistriṃśaṃ nijaghāna ca dānavān  |
āhūtāśca punardevāḥ sarve brahmapurogamāḥ  || 70.21 ||
ūcuste vacanaṃ devā rājānaṃ prati bhārata  |
tvayā samo'tra rājarṣe na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 70.22 ||
devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā yayātirvākyamabravīt |
punaḥ pravartito yajño haraviṣṇuprasādataḥ  || 70.23 ||
yuktaṃ palāyanaṃ cātra kṣatriyasya na vidyate  |
stutastu tena kāryeṇa śūlapāṇiḥ pinākadhṛk || 70.24 ||
pātālādutthitaṃ tatra liṅgaṃ kālānalaprabham  |
śuklīkṛtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ prabhayā tasya bhārata  || 70.25 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te tamuvāca vṛṣadhvajaḥ  |

yayātiruvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ mamecchasi  || 70.26 ||
idaṃ sthānaṃ na moktavyamumayā saha śaṅkara  |
yajñadānādikaṃ sarvamakṣayaṃ cātra sarvadā  || 70.27 ||
tapohīnā narā ye ca dānahīnāḥ sakilbiṣāḥ  |
te sarve tvatpuraṃ yāntu śuklatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 70.28 ||
tamuvāca mahādevaḥ satyametattavoditam  |
yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 70.29 ||
asya tīrthasya mahātmyālliṅgasyāsya samarcanāt |
narakaṃ naiva paśyanti janmajanmani bhārata  || 70.30 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājan yathā skandaśivoditam  |
tatra ye nihatā daityāḥ prāptāste'pi śivālayam  || 70.31 ||
svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ samārūḍhā yayurdevāstriviṣṭapam  |
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ stūyamāno nṛpottamaḥ  || 70.32 ||
yayātirnāma rājarṣī rājyaṃ kṛtvā divaṃ gataḥ  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya śivaloke mahīyate  || 70.33 ||


adhyāya 71

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dīptakeśvaradeveśaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ prakīrtitam  |
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiṃcittriṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 71.1 ||
darśanāddīptadevasya sparśanādarcanāttathā  |
anekabhāvikaṃ ghoraṃ kṣaṇamātreṇa naśyati  || 71.2 ||
arcayeddinamekaṃ tu yo muhūrtaṃ tu mānavaḥ  |
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  || 71.3 ||
mokṣadā nāma cāmuṇḍā viddhi gaurīṃ sarasvatīm  |
stutaḥ sahasranāmnā vai viṣṇunā brahmaṇā svayam  || 71.4 ||
stutāni tāni liṅgāni revāyā uttare taṭe  |
oṃkāraścādhidevaśca bilvāmrakamaheśvaraḥ  || 71.5 ||
śukleśvaro bhṛguśceti dvīpeśvaratrilocanau  |
vaivasvate'ntare prāpte ādikalpekṛteyuge  || 71.6 ||
śrīpatiḥ paramādyaśca dvitīyaśca pitāmahaḥ  |
tṛtīyo devarājaśca caturthaḥ sūrya eva ca  || 71.7 ||
pañcamaḥ kathitaḥ somaḥ ṣaṣṭho rāhuḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
saptamaśca śaniścaiva tvaṣṭamaḥ ketukaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 71.8 ||
vaiśvānaraśca navamo daśamaśca digīśvaraḥ  |
ekādaśo vaikramaśca dvādaśo vāruṇastathā  || 71.9 ||
trayodaśaśca vāyurvai dhanadaśca caturdaśaḥ  |
nānā padaprakāreṇa stuto deva umāpatiḥ  || 71.10 ||
viṣṇunā devanāthena brahmaṇā ca surāsuraiḥ  |
sthiraḥ sthāṇuḥ prabhābhānuḥ pravaro varado varaḥ  || 71.11 ||
hariśca hariṇākhyaśca sarvabhūtaharaḥ prabhuḥ  |
pravṛttiśca nivṛttiśca niyamaḥ śāśvato dhruvaḥ  || 71.12 ||
śmaśānavāsī bhagavān khecaro gocarastathā  |
abhivandyo mahākarmā tapasvī bhūtabhāvanaḥ  || 71.13 ||
unmattaveṣapracchannaḥ sarvalokaprajāpatiḥ  |
mahārūpo mahākāyaḥ sarvalokaprajāpatiḥ  || 71.14 ||
parātmā sarvabhūtānāṃ virūpo vāmano manuḥ  |
lokapālo pihitātmā prasanno bhavanāśanaḥ  || 71.15 ||
pravṛttaśca mahāṅgaśca nicayo niyatāśrayaḥ  |
sarvakāmaḥ svayambhūśca ādinādikaro nidhiḥ  || 71.16 ||
sahasrākṣo virūpākṣaḥ somo nakṣatrasādhakaḥ  |
candraḥ sūryaḥ śaniḥ keturgraho grahapatirvaraḥ  || 71.17 ||
tapodraṣṭā balaḥ sthāturmṛgabāṇārpaṇo'naghaḥ  |
mahātapā dīrghatapā ādirdīnānukampanaḥ  || 71.18 ||
savatsarakaro mantraḥ pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ  |
yogī yogamahāvīryo mahāretā haro haraḥ  || 71.19 ||
mahācetāśca sarvajñaḥ sabījo'paharo haraḥ  |
kamaṇḍaludharo dhanvī prāṇahastaḥ pratāpavān  || 71.20 ||
aṃśo'nīśastathā śūlī khaṭvāṅgī paṭṭiśī tathā  |
śuciśca śucirūpaśca tejastejaskaro nidhiḥ  || 71.21 ||
uṣṇīṣī ca suvaktrāśca udakyo vitanastathā  |
hariśca harinetraśca sutīrthaḥ kṛṣṇa eva ca  || 71.22 ||
śṛgālarūpī sarvārthaḥ śuṇḍīśuddhaḥ kamaṇḍaluḥ  |
ajaśca gandhamālī ca mṛgarūpī kapālabhṛt || 71.23 ||
ūrdhvaretā ūrdhvasākṣī ūrdhvabāhurnabhaḥ sthalaḥ  |
trijaṭī ca nivāsaśca rudraḥ senāpatirvibhuḥ  || 71.24 ||
ahaścaro rātricaraḥ suvāsaśca diśāmpatiḥ  |
rājahā daityahā caiva dhātārūpaguṇātmakaḥ  || 71.25 ||
siṃhaśārdūlarūpaśca ārdracarmadharo haraḥ  |
kālayogī mahānādaḥ sarvavāsaścatuṣpathaḥ  || 71.26 ||
durvārapretacārī ca bhūtacārī maheśvaraḥ  |
bahubhūto bahudhanaḥ sarvārtho rucirā gatiḥ  || 71.27 ||
nṛtyapriyo nṛtyakartā nartakaśca balāhakaḥ  |
ghoro mahātapā vāso nityo giridharo nabhaḥ  || 71.28 ||
sahasrabhūto vijñeyo vyavasāyaśca niścayaḥ  |
amarṣo marṣaṇo dakṣo dakṣakratuvināśanaḥ  || 71.29 ||
dakṣayajñāpahārī ca sumaho madhyamastathā  |
tejo'pahārī balihā muditaścārcito bhavaḥ  || 71.30 ||
gambhīraghoṣo gambhīro gabhīro havyavāhanaḥ  |
nyagrodharūpo nyagrodhaṛkṣavarṇaḥ prabhurvibhuḥ  || 71.31 ||
tīkṣṇabāṇaśca haryakṣo maheśaḥ karmakālavit |
dīkṣaḥ prasādito yajñaḥ samudro vaḍavānalaḥ  || 71.32 ||
hutāśaśca hṛtāśāsyaḥ prasannātmā hṛtāśanaḥ  |
mahātejāḥ sutejāśca vijayo jaya eva ca  || 71.33 ||
jyotiṣāmayanaṃ siddhiḥ sandhirvigraha eva ca  |
śikhī daṇḍī jaṭī jvālī mūrtodo durbalo bahiḥ  || 71.34 ||
vaiṇavī pāpavetālaḥ kālāgniḥ kāladaṇḍakaḥ  |
nakṣatravigraho vṛddhirajo gandhavaho'grajaḥ  || 71.35 ||
prajāpatirharirbāhurvibhāgaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ  |
vimocanaḥ suragaṇo hiraṇyakavaco bhavaḥ  || 71.36 ||
arajo dhūlidhārī ca mahācārī śrutaśravāḥ  |
anādiḥ sarvabhūtādiḥ sarvasyādyaḥ pitā guruḥ  || 71.37 ||
vyālarūpo mahāvāsī hīnamālī taraṅgavit |
tripadastryambako'vyaktaḥ sarvabandhavimocakaḥ  || 71.38 ||
sāṃkhyaprasādo durvāsāḥ sarvasādhuniṣevitaḥ  |
praskandano vibhāgaśca tulyo yajñavibhāgavit || 71.39 ||
sarvavāsī sarvacārī durvāsā bhairavo yamaḥ  |
himo himakaro yajñaḥ sarvadhātā budhottamaḥ  || 71.40 ||
lohitākṣo mahākṣaśca vijayākhyo viśāradaḥ  |
saṃgraho vigrahaḥ karma sarpa rājavibhūṣaṇaḥ  || 71.41 ||
mukhyo vimuktadehaśca dehacārī ca kardamaḥ  |
sarvācāraḥ prasādaśca khecaro balarūpadhṛk || 71.42 ||
ākāśavṛttirūpaśca nipāta uragaḥ khalaḥ  |
raudrarūpaḥ surādityo vasuraśmiḥ suvarcasaḥ  || 71.43 ||
vasuvego mahāvego manovego niśācaraḥ  |
sarvāvāsaḥ śriyāvāsa āpadīśakalo haraḥ  || 71.44 ||
munirātmagatirlokaḥ sahasravadano vibhuḥ  |
yakṣī ca yakṣarājaśca śyeno dīptirviśāmpatiḥ  || 71.45 ||
unmado madanākāro'pyarthānarthakaro mahān  |
siddhayogo'pahārī ca siddhaḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ  || 71.46 ||
bhikṣuśca bhikṣurūpaśca vibhuḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ mṛdutvacaḥ  |
mahāseno viśākhaśca yaṣṭibhāgo gavāmpatiḥ  || 71.47 ||
vajrahastaśca viṣṭambhirviṣṭhaḥ stambhana eva ca  |
ṛkṣo ripukaraḥ kālo madhurmadhukalocanaḥ  || 71.48 ||
vācaspatyo vājaseno naiṣṭhaścāśramasūcakaḥ  |
brahmacārī lokacārī sarvacārī suratnavit || 71.49 ||
īśāna īśvaraḥ kālo niśācārī tvamekadhṛk |
amitaścāprameyaśca nadīnadakaro'vyayaḥ  || 71.50 ||
nandīśvaraḥ sunandī ca nandano nandavarddhanaḥ  |
nāgahāro vihārī ca kālo brahmavidāṃ varaḥ  || 71.51 ||
caturmukho mahāliṅgaścaturliṅgastathaiva ca  |
liṅgādhyakṣaḥ suradhyakṣo kālādhyakṣo yugāvahaḥ  || 71.52 ||
umāpatirumākānto jāhnavī dhṛtimān varaḥ  |
sarvārthaḥ sarvabhūtārtho nityaḥ sarvavrataḥ śuciḥ  || 71.53 ||
yo na brahmādibhirdevo jāyate na maharṣibhiḥ  |
stotavyaḥ sa kathaṃ nātha paramātmā parātparaḥ  || 71.54 ||
jihvācāpalyamasmākaṃ kṣamasva parameśvara  |
śivaṃ kuruṣva devānāṃ svargyāṇāṃ puṣṭivardhanam  || 71.55 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śrutvā stotramidaṃ devaḥ śrīmān dvīpeśvaraḥ śivaḥ  |
prasannabravīddevān prārthayadhvaṃ varaṃ surāḥ  || 71.56 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
yadi tuṣṭo maheśāno devānāṃ varadaḥ prabhuḥ  |
tadvināśāya daityānāṃ trātā bhava maheśvara  || 71.57 ||
pāpakarmādhamaścaiva pañcaliṅgāni yo'rcayet |
so'pi tāṃ gatimāpnoti durlabhā yā mahāmakhaiḥ  || 71.58 ||
śakreṇābhivṛtastatra devadeva umāpatiḥ  |
purā nāmnāṃ sahasreṇa surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ  || 71.59 ||
śivaprasādasampanno devarājastato'bhavat |
dhanadena stutastatra devo lakṣeśvaraḥ prabhuḥ  || 71.60 ||
mokṣadā nāma gaurīṃ ca tāṃ devīṃ viddhi bhārata  |
mokṣeśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 71.61 ||
siddhairvidyādharairyakṣairgandharvaiḥ kinnarairnaraiḥ  |
devatvaṃ samanuprāptaṃ pañcaliṅgasamarcanāt || 71.62 ||
kubero mārutaścaiva varuṇo nirṛtistathā  |
vaivasvato yamaścaiva tataśca narakeśvaraḥ  || 71.63 ||
tasya liṅgasya māhātmyātsūryaputro mahāyaśāḥ  |
anyairabhiṣṭutastatra pūrvaṃ dvīpeśvaraḥ prabhuḥ  || 71.64 ||
bhaktyā nāmasahasreṇa stutaḥ pūjyatamaḥ śivaḥ  |
somenāto'bhavattatra śambhoḥ śirasi bhūṣaṇam  || 71.65 ||
rohiṇyā'bhyarcitā gaurī subhagā tena sābhavat |
ṛkṣairyogataraistadvatstuto devaḥ pinākadhṛk || 71.66 ||
tatastairbhāskareṇaiva tabhaḥ sthalamalaṃ kṛtam  |
vyādhayaḥ kālamṛtyuśca citraguptaśca lekhakaḥ  || 71.67 ||
tathā śakraḥ suragaṇairetaiḥ parivṛtaḥ prabhuḥ  |
pāpiṣṭhānāṃ mahāraudro dharmiṣṭhānāṃ prasādavān  || 71.68 ||
koṭyo'ṣṭau cordhvakeśā raudrāśca vikṛtānanāḥ  |
pativratāsahasraiśca tathā māsopavāsibhiḥ  || 71.69 ||
kilkilāravaśabdaiśca dharmarājapurottamam  |
vyāptaṃ tu paritaḥ śrīmadasaṃkhyātairmanoramaiḥ  || 71.70 ||
śrutvā teṣāṃ ravaṃ sārddhaṃ dharmarājaḥ sabhāsadaiḥ  |
śvetavastraparīdhānaḥ śvetamālyānulepanaḥ  || 71.71 ||
pādacārī gataḥ kṣipraṃ yatra te yānasaṃsthitāḥ  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā papraccha śubhakarmaṇaḥ  || 71.72 ||
yathā śaktena yogena dharmaṃ dharmottaraṃ mahat |
kasmāddeśātsamāyātāḥ kathaṃ puṇyamupārjitam  || 71.73 ||

vimānārūḍhā ūcu̱ḥ
kurukṣetre tapastaptaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
sarveṣāmeva lokānāṃ dvāraṃ taddhi pratiṣṭhitam  || 71.74 ||
dharmādharmaṃ tava balaṃ kāraṇaṃ ceti tattvataḥ  |
vārāṇasī prayāgaśca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ  || 71.75 ||
pitṛtīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ puṣkaraṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā  |
kedāraṃ bhairavaṃ caiva tathā rudramahālayam  || 71.76 ||
sarasvatīrudrakoṭiḥ prabhāsaṃ śaśibhūṣaṇam  |
nānātīrthasahasreṣu dānayajñatapaḥ kṛtam  || 71.77 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ sūryaputramahāyaśaḥ  |
anye dṛṣṭvā yathā nyāyaṃ dharmarājaṃ tatastathā  || 71.78 ||
ūcuḥ sarve vacaḥ ślakṣṇaṃ dharmarājaṃ yathoditam  |
na tvaṃ prabhuḥ sukṛtināṃ brahmā viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ prabhuḥ  || 71.79 ||
pāpakarmaratā ye tu teṣāṃ śāstā yamaḥ svayam  |

yama uvāca - 
gatvā kailāsamāyāmi yāvattāvatpratīkṣatām  || 71.80 ||
evamuktvā yayau rājan kailāsaṃ sa nagottamam  |
yasmin śivādyāste sarve pārvatī ṣaṇmukhastathā  || 71.81 ||
stuvanti devatāḥ sarvādevadevamumāpatim  |
nṛtyanti cāgrataḥ kecidutpatya nipatanti ca  || 71.82 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśaṃ śambhuṃ stuvantaṃ dīptatejasam  |
stuvannāmasahasreṇa devadevaṃ pinākinam  || 71.83 ||
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ ca namaskṛtya dharmarājo'bravīdidam  |
ye'smatpurīṃ samāyātāsteṣāṃ kā gatirucyate  || 71.84 ||
prahasannabravīddevo dharmarājaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
atra prayāntu te sarve ye revātīravāsinaḥ  || 71.85 ||
anyatīrthanivāsā ye bhogān bhuñjantu te divi  |
śivavākyaṃ tataḥ śrutvā brahmāviṣṇuryathātatham  || 71.86 ||
tuṣṭā devasya vākyena sarvadevagaṇeśvaruḥ  |
āgataḥ kṣaṇamātreṇa dharmarājaḥ purottamam  || 71.87 ||
śivoktāḥ preṣitāḥ sarve śivalokaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
yathā yathā samādiṣṭāstato'nye'pi śūbhānvitāḥ  || 71.88 ||
purākalpe mayā dṛṣṭaṃ kārttikyāṃ devatāgame  |
tato gacchenmahārāja vaiṣṇavaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 71.89 ||
kokilā nāma vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpavimokṣaṇam  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ kṣetramityāha devadevo janārdanaḥ  || 71.90 ||
sapādakoṭistīrthānāṃ tatrāste caiva bhārata  |
upoṣyaikādaśīṃ puṇyāṃ dīpamālāṃ prabodhayet || 71.91 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirmartyaloke durāsade  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhena vimānāgreṇa bhārata  || 71.92 ||
asaṃkhyakālikā tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
vipre ca toṣite tatra dānasaṅkhyā na vidyate  || 71.93 ||
atrāntare tyajetprāṇānavaśaḥ svavaśo'pi vā  |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rājā vaidyādhare pure  || 71.94 ||
dhruvo dhruvatvaṃ svarge tu tārātejaḥ samujjvalan  |
martyayoniṣu sambhūtā bhūtagrāmā tathāpare  || 71.95 ||
arcanrāḍdevadevasya divi devatvamāpnuvan  |
devapuṇyakṣaye martyā bhaktyā puṇyaiśca devatāḥ  || 71.96 ||
svargamartyaprabhedo'yaṃ dharmādharmaprabhedataḥ  |
kenāpi tatprakāreṇa pūjanīyo maheśvaraḥ  || 71.97 ||
yadvā tadvā śive deyaṃ bhaktiyuktena cetasā  |
arundhatyā sā bharaṇyā sāvitryā ca tathā tathā  || 71.98 ||
ahalyayā menakayā marutvatyā ca rambhayā  |
apsarogaṇasaṅghaiśca surasiddhagaṇaistathā  || 71.99 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya pūjito yena śaṅkaraḥ  |
tena vai vipulā bhogāḥ prāptā mokṣaśca bhārata  || 71.100 ||
na pūjayeddharaṃ yastu śivamāyāvimohitaḥ  |
na tasya svargamokṣau ca kailāsaṃ prati kā kathā  || 71.101 ||
na ca svargasya rājyasya bhājanaṃ ca narādhipa  |
sarvatīrthamayī revā sarvadevamayo haraḥ  || 71.102 ||
sarvadharmamayī buddhiḥ kṣamāsatyamayaṃ tapaḥ  |
brahmacaryaṃ tapomūlaṃ pañcendriyavinigrahaḥ  || 71.103 ||
kṣamā satyaṃ japo'dhītaṃ tapaḥ saṃyamalakṣaṇam  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃ śivena kathitaṃ purā  || 71.104 ||
mayā ca tava rājendra bhrātṝṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
na sāmānyatarā devī kathitā yā mayā tava  || 71.105 ||
dvīpeśvaraḥ kapileśvarastathā vai narakeśvaraḥ  |
etān devān samutthāya yathāvatparikīrtayet || 71.106 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ prāpya śivaloke mahīyate  |
aghaughe ca parikṣīṇe prāpyate saptakalpagā  || 71.107 ||
śivaḥ saṃnihito yasyāṃ śivakṣetraṃ tataḥ param  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya śivaloke mahīyate  || 71.108 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dvīpeśvaravarṇano nāmaikasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 72

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
narmadāsaṅgamaṃ puṇyaṃ surasiddhaniṣevitam  |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 72.1 ||
āgacchantī purā loke narmadā bharatarṣabha  |
stutā pūrvaṃ namaskṛtya devairbrahmarṣibhistathā  || 72.2 ||
tvayā pavitritaṃ puṇyaṃ martyalokaṃ carācaram  |
apāṃ rūpagatā revā harasya paramā kalā  || 72.3 ||
umā kātyāyanī gaṅgā yamunā ca sarasvatī  |
cāmuṇḍā carcikā devī revā tvaṃ saptakalpagā  || 72.4 ||
śivajā pravahā puṇyā mekalādrisutā stutā  |
yajñayūpā ca mūrdhā ca svargamokṣapradā tathā  || 72.5 ||
tāriṇī sarvabhūtānāṃ pāpaghnī ca taraṅgiṇī  |
lakṣmīḥ svāhā svadhā caiva puruhūtā yaśasvinī  || 72.6 ||
tvayā vyāptaṃ jagatkṛtsnamapāṃ rūpeṇa suvrate  |
saṅgamaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ ca surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 72.7 ||
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ sarvametadbhavati cākṣayam  |
atyadbhutaṃ mahārāja narmadāsnānamarcanam  || 72.8 ||
anekāni sahasrāṇi vimānāni yudhiṣṭhira  |
nānāratnaprabhājālaiḥ sūryakoṭisamāni ca  || 72.9 ||
gatāni dharmarājasya purīṃ vīṇādiniḥ svanaiḥ  |
nādayanti divaṃ bhūmiṃ vedanirghoṣaṇādibhiḥ  || 72.10 ||
ekasmin samaye dṛṣṭvāścaryaṃ vaivasvato nṛpaḥ  |
atriścaiva vaśiṣṭhaśca pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  || 72.11 ||
ityādyāḥ sapta munayo dharmādharmavicārakāḥ  |
śivena sthāpitāḥ pūrvaṃ hariṇā brahmaṇā tathā  || 72.12 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
akṣīṇakarmabandhustu puruṣo munisattama  |
paraṃ padamavāpnoti tanme kathaya kalpaga  || 72.13 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
viṣṇunā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ brahmaṇe ca mahātmane  |
prapadye puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ harim  || 72.14 ||
lokanāthaṃ sahasrākṣamakṣaraṃ paramaṃ padam  |
bhagavantaṃ prapadye'haṃ bhūtabhavyabhavatprabhum  || 72.15 ||
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāmanantabalapauruṣam  |
padmanābhaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ prapadye satyamavyayam  || 72.16 ||
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ bhūgarbhamamṛtaṃ viśvatomukham  |
anaśvaramanāthaṃ ca prapadye bhāskaradyutim  || 72.17 ||
sahasraśirasaṃ devaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ tārkṣyavāhanam  |
prapadye sūkṣmamacalaṃ vareṇyamabhayapradam  || 72.18 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ hariṃ caiva yogātmānaṃ sanātanam  |
śaraṇyaṃ sarvalokānāṃ prapadye dhruvamīśvaram  || 72.19 ||
yaḥ prabhuḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ yena sarvamidaṃ tatam  |
yaḥ saṃhārakaro devaḥ sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  || 72.20 ||
yasmājjātaḥ purā brahmā padmayoniḥ prajāpatiḥ  |
prasīdatu sa me viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaparaḥ prabhuḥ  || 72.21 ||
purā laye tu saṃprāpte naṣṭe loke carācare  |
ekastiṣṭhati yogātmā sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  || 72.22 ||
jayedyaḥ pṛthivīṃ satyaṃ kālo dharmaḥ kriyāphalam  |
gaṇākāraḥ sa tāṃ vāco vāsudevaḥ prasīdatu  || 72.23 ||
yogāvāsa namastubhyaṃ sarvāvāsa varaprada  |
yajñabhogin pañcabhoginnārāyaṇa namo'stu te  || 72.24 ||
caturmūrte jagaddhāma lakṣmīvāsa varaprada  |
viśvāvāsa namaste'stu sākṣībhūta jagatpate  || 72.25 ||
ajeyaḥ ṣaḍvibhāgaikaviśvamūrtirvṛṣākapiḥ  |
mṛgādhipaśca kālaśca namaste jñānasāgara  || 72.26 ||
avyaktādaṇḍamutpannamavyaktādaparaḥ prabhuḥ  |
yasmātparataraṃ nāsti tamasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 72.27 ||
cintayanto hi yaṃ nityaṃ brahmeśānādayaḥ prabhum  |
ekāṃśena jagatsarvaṃ yo viṣṭabhya vibhuḥ sthitaḥ  || 72.28 ||
agrāhyo nirguṇaḥ śāstā tamasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
divākarasya somasya madhye jyotiriva sthitam  || 72.29 ||
kṣetrajña iti yaṃ prāhuḥ sa mahātmā prasīdatu  |
sāṃkhyayogena ye cānye siddhāścaiva maharṣayaḥ  || 72.30 ||
yaṃ viditvā vimucyante sa mahātmā prasīdatu  |
namaste sarvatobhadra sarvato'kṣiśiromukha  || 72.31 ||
nirvikāra namaste'stu ādikalpa hṛdi sthita  |
atīndriya namastubhyaṃ paramātmannamo'stu te  || 72.32 ||
ye ca tvāmabhijānanti saṃsāre na vasanti te  |
rāgadveṣavinirmuktā lobhamohavivārjitāḥ  || 72.33 ||
aśarīraḥ suguptaḥ san sarvadeheṣu tanmayaḥ  |
avyaktabuddhyahaṃkāramahābhūtendriyāṇi ca  || 72.34 ||
tvayi tāni na teṣu tvaṃ te ca tāni na tu svayam  |
avyakto nātikūṭastho guṇānāṃ prabhurīśvaraḥ  || 72.35 ||
āvarto heturahitaḥ prabhuḥ svātmavyavasthitaḥ  |
namaste puṇḍarīkākṣa vāsudeva namo'stu te  || 72.36 ||
īśvaro'si jagannātha kimataḥ paramucyate  |
bhaktānāṃ muktidastvaṃ ca guruśca tridaśeśvaraḥ  || 72.37 ||
same bhūtapatistvaṃ hi prabhurjanmani janmani  |
ahaṃkāreṇa baddho vā tathā sattvādibhirgaṇaiḥ  || 72.38 ||
pṛthivīṃ yātu me ghrāṇaṃ yātu me rasanājalam  |
cakṣurhutāśanaṃ yātu sparśo me yātu mārutam  || 72.39 ||
śabdaścākāśamāyātu mano vai kāraṇaṃ tathā  |
ahaṃkāraśca me buddhiṃ tvayi buddhirmamāstviti  || 72.40 ||
viyogaḥ sarvakaraṇairguṇairbhūtaistathā'stu me  |
sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva prakṛtiṃ svāṃ viśantu me  || 72.41 ||
prabhoḥ prabhumanavadyaṃ prapadye'haṃ naraḥ prabhum  |
sahasraśirasaṃ devaṃ maharṣiṃ bhūtabhāvanam  || 72.42 ||
brahmayoniśca viśvasya sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  |
brahmapatnyāṃ pralīyante naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame  || 72.43 ||
āhūtasaṃplave caiva līyate prakṛtau mahat |
hūyate ca punastābhyāṃ sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  || 72.44 ||
agnisomārkadevānāṃ brahmarudrendrayoginām  |
yaste jayati tejāṃsi sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  || 72.45 ||
ajastvaṃ jagataḥ panthā amūrtirvīśvamūrtijit |
navaṃ pradhānaṃ ca mahān puruṣaścetano'lasaḥ  || 72.46 ||
agopyo yaḥ paratarastameva śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
somasūryopamastejo yo'vatārayati svayam  || 72.47 ||
vijāyante diśo yasmātsa mahātmā prasīdatu  |
guṇavānnirguṇaścaiva cetano'cetano svagaḥ  || 72.48 ||
sūkṣmaḥ sarvagato dehaḥ sa mahātmā prasīdatu  |
sūryamadhye sthitaḥ somastasya madhye tu saṃsmṛtaḥ  || 72.49 ||
bhūtatvādyo'calo dīptaḥ sa mahātmā prasīdatu  |
ekatvāttava nānātvaṃ ye viduryānti te param  || 72.50 ||
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu pradveṣyātmajanapriyaḥ  |
samaṃ bhajatyanākāṅkṣī bhajate nānyacetasaḥ  || 72.51 ||
yo'yaṃ sarvātmanā jñeyaḥ sa me viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu  |
carācaramidaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  || 72.52 ||
tvayi taṃ tantuvatprotaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva  |
na te dharmo hyadharmo'sti na garbho janma vā punaḥ  || 72.53 ||
jarājanmavimokṣārthaṃ tameva śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
indriyāṇi guṇaścaiva śvāsocchvāsaśca yoniṣu  || 72.54 ||
kevalaṃ dāruvaddehaṃ naśyaṃ yatparamāpadam  |
svayamekākibhāvo me janmato'tra punarbhavaḥ  || 72.55 ||
tvadbuddhistvadgataprāṇā tvadbhaktastvatparāyaṇaḥ  |
tvāmevāhaṃ smariṣyāmi maraṇe paryupasthite  || 72.56 ||
pūrvadehe kṛtā ye tu vyādhayaḥ praviśantu mām  |
vātādayaśca duḥkhāni ṛṇaṃ tanmuñcatātprabho  || 72.57 ||
śreyasāṃ ca paraṃ śreyastvanyeṣāṃ ca yaśasvinām  |
sarvapāpaviśuddhyarthaṃ puṇyaṃ yatparamaṃ padam  || 72.58 ||
prātarutthāya satataṃ madhyāhne ca dinakṣaye  |
ajasraṃ ca tathā japyaṃ sarvapāpopaśāntidam  || 72.59 ||
hariṃ kṛṣṇaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ vāsudevaṃ janārdanam  |
praṇato'smi jagannāthaṃ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu  || 72.60 ||
govardhanadharaṃ devaṃ gobrāhmaṇahite ratam  |
praṇato'smi gadāpāṇiṃ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu  || 72.61 ||
śaṅkhinaṃ cakriṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ śārṅgiṇaṃ madhusūdanam  |
praṇato'smi patiṃ lakṣmyāḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu  || 72.62 ||
dāmodaraṃ mudā yuktaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣamavyayam  |
praṇato'smi sthitaṃ sthityai sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu  || 72.63 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ naraṃ saumyaṃ mādhavaṃ ca janārdanam  |
śrīvatsaṃ śrīvapuḥ śrīmacchrīdharaṃ śrīniketanam  || 72.64 ||
praṇato'smi śriyaḥ kāntaṃ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu  |
