yamīśaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ dhyāyanti ca tamakṣaram  || 72.65 ||
vāsudevamanirdeśyaṃ tamasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
sarvabandhavinirmukto yaṃ praviśya punarbhavam  || 72.66 ||
puruṣo naiva prāpnoti tamasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
kṛtvā brahmavapuḥ sarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam  || 72.67 ||
yaḥ karoti punaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ tamasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  |
brahmarūpadharaṃ devaṃ yonirūpaṃ janārdanam  || 72.68 ||
sṛṣṭitve saṃsthitaṃ nityaṃ praṇato'smi janārdanam  |
yasmānnānyatparaṃ kiṃcidyasmin sarvamidaṃ jagat || 72.69 ||
yaḥ sarvamadhyago'nantaḥ sarvagaṃ taṃ namāmyaham  |
yo'sti bhūteṣu sarveṣu sthāvare jaṅgameṣu ca  || 72.70 ||
viṣṇureva sa vai pāpaṃ mamāśeṣaṃ praṇaśyatu  |
na vṛttaṃ nirvṛtaṃ karma viṣṇoryatkarma vā kṛtam  || 72.71 ||
anekajanmakarmotthaṃ pāpaṃ naśyati me tathā  |
niśāyāṃ ca tathā prātarmadhyāhṇe cāparāhṇayoḥ  || 72.72 ||
ajñānācca kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā  |
yatkṛtaṃ cāśubhaṃ kiṃcittatsarvaṃ naśyatu kṣaṇāt || 72.73 ||
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yātu toyeṣu lavaṇaṃ yathā  |
parapīḍāṃ ca nindāṃ ca kurvato janma nārjitam  || 72.74 ||
paradravyaparakṣetravāñchā krodhodbhavaṃ ca yat |
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yātu toyeṣu lavaṇaṃ yathā  || 72.75 ||
viṣṇave vāsudevāya haraye keśavāya ca  |
janārdanāya kṛṣṇāya namo bhūyo namo namaḥ  || 72.76 ||
nābhāgo nāma rājarṣirnarmadātīrasaṅgame  |
cakāra stotramatulaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tu prajāpatiḥ  || 72.77 ||
brahmaṇo'ṅgirasā prāptaṃ tasmādindreṇa bhārata  |
vaśiṣṭhaḥ śrāvayāmāsa nābhāgaṃ rājasattamam  || 72.78 ||
snātvā ca narmadātoye dattvā dānānyanekaśaḥ  |
kāmikaṃ yānamāruhya nābhāgaḥ svapurīṃ yayau  || 72.79 ||
stauti nāmasahasreṇa yaḥ stavena janārdanam  |
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  || 72.80 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe viṣṇustutirnāma dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 73

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra meghanādamiti smṛtam  |
jalamadhye mahādevo yatra tiṣṭhatyadarśitaḥ  || 73.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
jalamadhye mahādevastiṣṭhate kena hetunā  |
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kūlaṃ varjayitvā dvijottama  || 73.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
etadākhyānamatulaṃ puṇyaṃ śrutisukhāvaham  |
purāṇe yacchrutaṃ tāta tatte vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 73.3 ||
purā tretāyuge tāta paulastyo devakaṇṭakaḥ  |
trilokavijayī raudraḥ surāsurabhayaṃkaraḥ  || 73.4 ||
devadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām  |
avadhyo varadānena yatra paryaṭate mahīm  || 73.5 ||
tatra devagirau ramye dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
mayo nāmeti vikhyāto mahānāsīnnṛpeśvara  || 73.6 ||
rāvaṇastaṃ tato gatvā vinayāvanataḥ sthitaḥ  |
pūjito dānasaṃmānairmayaṃ vacanamabravīt || 73.7 ||
kasyeyaṃ padmapatrākṣī pūrṇacandranibhānanā  |
kiṃ nāmadheyā tapate tapa ugraṃ kathaṃ vibho  || 73.8 ||

maya uvāca - 
dānavānāṃ patiḥ śreṣṭho mayo'haṃ nāmanāmataḥ  |
bhāryā tejavatī nāma mameyaṃ tanayā śubhā  || 73.9 ||
mandodarīti vikhyātā tapate patikāraṇāt |
śrutvā tu vacanaṃ tasya rāvaṇo madadarpitaḥ  || 73.10 ||
praśritaḥ praṇato bhūtvā mayaṃ vacanamabravīt |
paulastyo nāma rājāhaṃ devadānavadarpahā  || 73.11 ||
prārthayāmi mahābhāga sutāṃ tvaṃ dātumarhasi  |
jñātvā paitāmahaṃ vaṃśaṃ mayenāpi mahātmanā  || 73.12 ||
sutā dattā rāvaṇāya kṛtvā vidhividhānataḥ  |
gṛhītvā tāṃ tadā rakṣaḥ pūjyamāno niśācaraiḥ  || 73.13 ||
divyairyānairvimānaiśca krīḍate tu tayā saha  |
putraṃ putravatāṃ śreṣṭho janayāmāsa bhārata  || 73.14 ||
tenaiva jātamātreṇa ravo mukto mahātmanā  |
saṃvartakasya meghasya yena loko jaḍīkṛtaḥ  || 73.15 ||
śrutvā tanninadaṃ ghoraṃ trasto lokapitāmahaḥ  |
nāma cakre tadā tasya meghanādo bhaviṣyati  || 73.16 ||
etannāmakṛtaṃ so'pi paramaṃ vratamāsthitaḥ  |
bhāvayāmāsa deveśamumayā saha śaṅkaram  || 73.17 ||
vratairniyamadānaiśca homairjāpyairvidhānataḥ  |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇairdivyaiḥ kliśyate ca kalevaram  || 73.18 ||
evamanyaddine tāta kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  |
gatvā liṅgamayaṃ prāpya prasthito dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 73.19 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya snātukāmo mahābalaḥ  |
nikṣipyāpūjayaddevaṃ kṛtvā jāpyaṃ janeśvaraḥ  || 73.20 ||
gantukāmaḥ paraṃ mārgaṃ laṅkāyāṃ nṛpasattama  |
ekaṃ samuddhṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ gṛhitaṃ savyapāṇinā  || 73.21 ||
prathamaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ ca bhaktyā paulastyanandanaḥ  |
tadā devamahāliṅgaṃ patitaṃ narmadāmbhasi  || 73.22 ||
pāhi pāhīti tenokto liṅgena parameṣṭhinā  |
dvitīyaṃ patitaṃ tāvaduttare narmadātaṭe  || 73.23 ||
meghanādeti vikhyātaṃ liṅgaṃ tatra suśobhanam  |
madhyameśvaranāmeti jalamadhye vyavasthitam  || 73.24 ||
yāvaduddhartukāmo'sau saptapātālamāgamat |
devayorniścayaṃ jñātvā nivṛtto'sau niśācaraḥ  || 73.25 ||
jagāmākāśamāviśya pūjyamāno niśācaraiḥ  |
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ meghanādeti viśrutam  || 73.26 ||
meghāraveti vikhyātamuttare kheṭakaḥ śubhaḥ  |
pūrve tu garjanaṃ nāma sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 73.27 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra yastu snānaṃ samācaret |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā sa labhecchāśvataṃ śubham  || 73.28 ||
piṇḍadānaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
tena dvādaśa varṣāṇi pitarastarpitā divi  || 73.29 ||
yastu bhojayate viprāṃstasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
yatphalaṃ yogināṃ tatra labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 73.30 ||
agniveśaṃ jale vāpi athavāpi hyanāśakam  |
anivartikā gatistasya syādidaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 73.31 ||
evaṃ te naraśārdūla garjiteśvaramuttamam  |
kathitaṃ smaraṇādeva sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 73.32 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe meghanādeśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāma trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 74

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra dārutīrthamanuttamam  |
dāruko yatra saṃsiddhimindrasya dayitaḥ sakhā  || 74.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
dārukeṇa kathaṃ tāta tapaścīrṇaṃ purā'nagha  |
vidhānaṃ śrotumicchāmi sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 74.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ahaṃ te kathayiṣyāmi vicitraṃ yatpurātanam  |
vṛttaṃ samabhavattatra ṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 74.3 ||
sūto vajradharasyāsītmātalirnāmanāmataḥ  |
sa putraṃ śaptavān pūrvaṃ kathaṃcitkāraṇāntare  || 74.4 ||
śāpahetorvepamāna indrasya caraṇau śubhau  |
prapīḍya tatra devendraṃ vijñāpayati bhārata  || 74.5 ||
mama tātābhiśaptasya anāthasya sureśvara  |
karmaṇā kena śāpasya ghorasyānto bhaviṣyati  || 74.6 ||

śakra uvāca - 
narmadātaṭamāśritya toṣaya tvaṃ maheśvaram  |
tiṣṭha yāvadyugasyāntaṃ punarjananamāpsyasi  || 74.7 ||
punarbhūtvā yadukule dāruko nāma nāmataḥ  |
ārohayitvā deveśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 74.8 ||
mānuṣaṃ tatra sampannaṃ tataḥ siddhimavāpsyasi  |
evamuktastu devena sahasrākṣeṇa bhārata  || 74.9 ||
praṇamya śirasā bhūmimāgato'sau hataprabhaḥ  |
narmadā taṭamāśritya karśitasvakalevaraḥ  || 74.10 ||
vratopavārsairvividhairjapahomaparāyaṇaḥ  |
mahādevaṃ mahātmānaṃ varadaṃ śūlapāṇinam  || 74.11 ||
abhajatparayā bhaktyā yāvadāhūtasamplavam  |
aṃśāvataraṇe viṣṇostato bhūtvā mahāmatiḥ  || 74.12 ||
toṣayitvā jagannāthaṃ tato yātaḥ sa sadgatim  |
eṣa te sambhavastāta dārutīrthasya suvrata  || 74.13 ||
kathitastu mayā pūrvaṃ yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt |
tato yudhiṣṭhiro rājā vismayāviṣṭacetanaḥ  || 74.14 ||
bhrānto'valokayāmāsa stabdharomā muhurmuhuḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā vidhipūrvaṃ nareśvara  || 74.15 ||
upāsya sabhyāṃ tatraiva saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
dehatyāgaṃ ca tatraiva yaḥ karoti samāhitaḥ  || 74.16 ||
so'śvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya ramate śivasannidhau  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo bhaktyā bhojayedbrāhmaṇaṃ śuciḥ  || 74.17 ||
sa tu viprasahasrasya labhate phalamuttamam  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tapo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam  || 74.18 ||
yatkṛtaṃ tu śubhaṃ tatra tatsarvaṃ labhate'kṣayam  || 74.19 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dārutīrthatamahimānuvarṇano nāma catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 75

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra devatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra devāstrayastriṃśattaptvā siddhiṃ parāṃ gatāḥ  || 75.1 ||
purā devāsure yuddhe dānavairbaladarpitaiḥ  |
indro devagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ svarājyāccyāvito bhṛśam  || 75.2 ||
hastyaśvarathayānaughairmardayitvā ca vāhinīm  |
viśaktā bhejire mārgaṃ prahārairjarjarīkṛtāḥ  || 75.3 ||
jambhaśumbhaniśumbhādyaistu huṇḍagrahakaiḥ saha  |
balibhirbādhitāḥ sarve brahmāṇamupatasthire  || 75.4 ||
praṇamya śirasā devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam  |
vyajñāpayanta deveśamindrāgnikapurogamāḥ  || 75.5 ||
paśya paśya mahābhāga dānavairākulīkṛtāḥ  |
bādhitāḥ putradārābhyāṃ tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 75.6 ||
paritrāyasva deveśa sarvalokapitāmaha  |
nānyā gatiḥ sureśāna muktvā tvāṃ parameṣṭhinam  || 75.7 ||

brahmovāca - 
dānavānāṃ vighātārthaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritāḥ  |
tapaḥ kuruta bho devāstapo hi paramaṃ balam  || 75.8 ||
nānyopāyo na vai mantro na vidyā na ca vikramaḥ  |
vinā revājalaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 75.9 ||
dāridryavyādhimaraṇabandhanavyasanāni ca  |
etāni caiva pāpasya phalānīti matirmama  || 75.10 ||
evaṃ jñātvā vidhānena tapaḥ kuruta duṣkaram  |
pūjyate śāmbhavaṃ sarvaiḥ prāpnuyātābhayaṃ tataḥ  || 75.11 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ devā brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
narmadāmāgatāḥ sarve tadendrāgnipurogamāḥ  || 75.12 ||
vicerustatra vipulaṃ tapaḥ paramaduḥsaham  |
sakalpaiḥ paramāṃ rājaṃstatte siddhimavāpnuvan  || 75.13 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ devatīrthamiti śrutam  |
gīyate sarvalokeṣu sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 75.14 ||
tatra śraddhātmanā yo'pi vidhināpi samanvitaḥ  |
snānaṃ samācaredbhaktyā sa labhenmauktikaṃ phalam  || 75.15 ||
yastamarcayate devaṃ sarvadevaistu pūjitam  |
labhate cāśvamedhasya phalaṃ yāgasya cottamam  || 75.16 ||
yastu bhojayate viprāṃstasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
tatra devaśilā ramyā mahāpuṇyādrirvadhinī  || 75.17 ||
saṃnyāsena mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāmakṣayā gatiḥ  |
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāddevatīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  || 75.18 ||
rudraloke vasettāvadyāvadāhūtasamplavam  |
evaṃ snānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  || 75.19 ||
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vāpi tatra tīrthe'kṣayaṃ bhavet |
etāvadvidhiruddiṣṭā utpattiścaiva bhārata  || 75.20 ||
devatīrthasya caritaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  || 75.21 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe devatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 76

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tatogacchecca rājendra guhāvāsīti cottamam  |
yatra siddho mahādevo guhāvāsīti śaṅkaraḥ  || 76.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kena kāryeṇa viprendra guhāvāsīti śaṅkaraḥ  |
etadvistarataḥ sarva kathayasva mamānagha  || 76.2 ||
śrotumicchāmyahaṃ deva sarvaṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
mahāpraśnaḥ kṛto māṃ yo mahāprājña nareśvara  || 76.3 ||
purāṇe vistaro'pyasya na śakyo hi mayādhunā  |
vṛddhabhāvātkathayitumahaṃ ca bahukālikaḥ  || 76.4 ||
pūrva dāruvane viprā vasanti sma suraiḥ samāḥ  |
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprastho yatistathā  || 76.5 ||
svadharmaniratānāṃ ca kathitaṃ paramaṃ padam  |
tāvadvasantasamaye kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  || 76.6 ||
vimānastho mahādevo gamyamānomayā saha  |
dadarśa ca janāvāsaṃ vedadhvaninināditam  || 76.7 ||
agatāgatasaṃvāsaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  |
taddṛṣṭvā muditāṃ devīṃ harṣagadgadayā girā  || 76.8 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ devo dṛṣṭvā tāpasayoṣitaḥ  |
nānyaṃ devaṃ na vai dharmaṃ dhyāyanti himanandini  || 76.9 ||
etacchrutvā paraṃ vākyaṃ devadevena bhāṣitam  |
kautūhalasamāviṣṭā śaṅkaraṃ punarabravīt || 76.10 ||
yattvayoktaṃ mahādeva patidharmaparāstriyaḥ  |
tāsāmanaṅgo bhūtvā tvaṃ caritraṃ kṣobhaya prabho  || 76.11 ||

mahādeva uvāca - 
tvayoktaṃ vacanaṃ devi na caitadrocate priye  |
brāhmaṇā hi mahābhāgā na teṣāṃ vipriyaṃ caret || 76.12 ||
manyupraharaṇā viprāścakrapraharaṇo hariḥ  |
cakrāttīkṣṇataro manyustasmādvipraṃ na kopayet || 76.13 ||
na te devā na te lokāste nāgā nāsurāstathā  |
dṛśyante ca tribhirlokairetairuṣṭairna vañcitāḥ  || 76.14 ||
teṣāṃ kṣobhakaraḥ prāyaḥ svargabhogaphalacyutaḥ  |
yeṣāṃ tuṣṭā mahābhāgā brāhmaṇāḥ kṣitidevatāḥ  || 76.15 ||
teṣāṃ dharmastathārthaśca kāmo mokṣo na saṃśayaḥ  |
evaṃ jñātvā mahābhāge āgrahastyajyatāmayam  || 76.16 ||
etallokaviruddhaṃ hi yadīcchasi vaśe sukham  |

devyuvāca - 
nāhaṃ te dayitā deva nāhaṃ te vaśavartinī  || 76.17 ||
anyāyadharṣaṇāṃ cātra sarvāsāṃ kuru suvrata  |
lokāloke mahādeva aśakyaṃ nāsti te vibho  || 76.18 ||
kriyatāṃ mama devaitatparaṃ kautūhalaṃ prabho  |
evamukto mahādevo devyāḥ priyahite rataḥ  || 76.19 ||
kṛtvā kāpālikaṃ rūpaṃ yayau dāruvanaṃ prati  |
mahāhināṃ jaṭājūṭaṃ niyamya śaśibhūṣaṇaḥ  || 76.20 ||
kaṅkatrāṇaṃ paraṃ kṛtvā tathā sauvarṇakuṇḍale  |
vyāghracarmaparīdhāno hārakeyūrabhūṣitaḥ  || 76.21 ||
nūpurārāvanirghoṣaiḥ kampayaṃśca vasundharām  |
mahāḍamarughoṣeṇa vīraghaṇṭāninādinā  || 76.22 ||
prabhātasamaye prāpte tatra dāruvanaṃ gata  |
tāvadviprajanaḥ sarvaḥ puṣpamūlaphalāśanaḥ  || 76.23 ||
nirgato bahubhiḥ sārdhaṃ paṭhyamāna itastataḥ  |
taddṛṣṭvā mahadāścaryarūpaṃ devasya bhārata  || 76.24 ||
yuvatījanaḥ pramattaśca kāmena kaluṣīkṛtaḥ  |
surūpaṃ paramaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvāstāśca varānanāḥ  || 76.25 ||
kleśabhāvaṃ tadāgacchan yāśca dāruvane striyaḥ  |
vikārā bahavastāsāṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā manojavam  || 76.26 ||
saṃjātā viprapatnīnāṃ tāṃ śṛṇuṣva nṛpottama  |
paridhānaṃ na jānanti paribhraṣṭaṃ karodyatāḥ  || 76.27 ||
dātukāmā tathā bhaikṣyaṃ ceṣṭituṃ naiva śakyate  |
kāciddṛṣṭvā mahādevaṃ rūpayauvanagarvitā  || 76.28 ||
utsaṅge saṃsthitaṃ vālaṃ patitaṃ vyasmarattataḥ  |
kāmabāṇahatā cānyā bāhubhyāṃ pīḍati stanau  || 76.29 ||
niḥśvasantī tathā cānyā na kiṃcitparijalpate  |
evamakṣobhayatsarvā maheśaḥ patidevatāḥ  || 76.30 ||
cālitāstā viditvā tu nirjagmurdvārisatvarāḥ  |
saṃcitāḥ paramaṃ kṛtvā sarvā devo maheśvaraḥ  || 76.31 ||
kṣobhayitvā gaṇaṃ strīṇāṃ gataścādarśanaṃ ca tam  |
tato vipragaṇaḥ sarvaḥ samāyāto dadarśa ha  || 76.32 ||
kṣobhitaṃ strījanaṃ sarvaṃ hareṇa krodhamāviśat |
krodhāviṣṭo dvijaḥ kaściddaṇḍamudyamya dhāvati  || 76.33 ||
kuṭhārahasto hyaparastathānyo darbhamuṣṭimān  |
itaścetaśca te sarve bhramitvā tacca kānanam  || 76.34 ||
ekībhūtā mahātmāno vyājahuśca ruṣā giram  |
yadi dattaṃ hutaṃ kiṃcidguravastoṣitā yadi  || 76.35 ||
tena satyena devasya liṅgaṃ patatu bhūtale  |
evaṃ satyaprabhāveṇa vacanena dvijanmanām  || 76.36 ||
devasya paśyato liṅgaṃ patitaṃ dharaṇītale  |
hāhākāro mahānāsīllokāloke tu bhārata  || 76.37 ||
devasya patitaṃ liṅgaṃ jagatyeva mahākṣayam  |
patamānasya liṅgasya śabdo'bhūttatra dāruṇaḥ  || 76.38 ||
patanti parvatāgrāṇi śoṣamāyānti sāgarāḥ  |
devasya patite liṅge vimānairdevadānavāḥ  || 76.39 ||
sametya sahitāḥ sarve brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā devaṃ stuvanti vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ  || 76.40 ||
tatastuṣṭo jagannātho devānāṃ varado'bhavat |
devo'pi patite liṅge śāpena dharaṇītale  || 76.41 ||
gatvā dāruvanaṃ viprāṃstoṣayāmāsa bhārata  |
viśvāmitro vaśiṣṭhādyā jābāliratha kāśyapaḥ  || 76.42 ||
sametya sahitāḥ sarve tamūcustripurāntakam  |
mahājano'pi balavānnāvajñeyaḥ sureśvaraḥ  || 76.43 ||
kṣāntiyukto'pi bhūtvā ca bhaviṣyasi gataklamaḥ  |
vidhvaṃsitā vipradārā yadetalliṅgamuttamam  || 76.44 ||
patitaṃ te mahādeva etatpuṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
tatpuṇyaṃ nāgnihotreṇa agniṣṭomairna labhyate  || 76.45 ||
yaccharma rudra prāpsyanti mānavā liṅgapūjayā  |
devānāṃ caiva yakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām  || 76.46 ||
vacanena tu viprāṇāmetatpuṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
brahmaviṣṇvindrarudrāṇāṃ darśanena ca yatphalam  || 76.47 ||
tatphalaṃ tasya liṅgasya darśane sakalaṃ labhet |
evamukto jagannāthaḥ praṇipatya dvijottamān  || 76.48 ||
mudā paramayā yuktastāṃścaiva pratyabhāṣata  |
brāhmaṇā jaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ nirjalaṃ sarvakāmikam  || 76.49 ||
yeṣāṃ vākyodayenaiva śuddhyanti malanā janāḥ  |
kiṃcittīrthaṃ na hi kṣetramūṣaraṃ gahvarāṇi ca  || 76.50 ||
brāhmaṇācchreṣṭhamuddiṣṭaṃ yatra gatvā viśuddhyati  |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni gaṅgādyāḥ saritastathā  || 76.51 ||
ekasyā vipravākyasya kalā nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
abhinandya dvijān sarvānanujñāto mahānṛṣiḥ  || 76.52 ||
tato'gācca mahādevo narmadātaṭamuttamam  |
parama vratamāsthāya guhāvāsaṃ mahārbudam  || 76.53 ||
maunaṃ cacāra bhagavāñjapadhyānarataḥ sadā  |
samāpte niyame tāta sthāpayitvā maheśvaram  || 76.54 ||
vandyamānaḥ suraiḥ sārdhaṃ kailāsamagamatprabhuḥ  |
narmadāyāstaṭe yena sthāpitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 76.55 ||
tenaiva kāraṇenāsau narmadeśvaramuttamam  |
yadyadācarati śreṣṭhastattadevetaro janaḥ  || 76.56 ||
sa yatpramāṇaṃ kurute lokastadanuvartate  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā vidhinā sujitendriyaḥ  || 76.57 ||
arcayitvā mahādevamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
pitṛbhyo dadate yastu tilapuṣpakuśodakam  || 76.58 ||
trisaptapuruṣāstasya svarge pāṇḍava modante  |
yastu bhojayate viprān yathāśaktyā narottamaḥ  || 76.59 ||
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi brāhmaṇebhyo yudhiṣṭhira  || 76.60 ||
dadate toyamāspṛśya so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pūjayecca viśeṣataḥ  || 76.61 ||
narmadeśvaramāsādya avāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam  |
agniveśe jale vāpi anāśakamṛtaśca yaḥ  || 76.62 ||
anivartikā gatistasya yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt |
etatkīrtayate yastu narmadeśvaramuttamam  || 76.63 ||
bhaktyā śṛṇoti yo rājannaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 76.64 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dāruvanaprasaṅge narmadeśvarakīrtanaṃ nāma ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 77
tato gacchecca rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam  |
snānamātraḥ prasaṅgena narmadeśvarakīrtanam  || 77.1 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
yāvanti romakūpāni tatprasūtikuleṣu ca  || 77.2 ||
tāvantyabdasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate  |
jale vāgnipraveśe vā athavānaśake naraḥ  || 77.3 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛto yastu na garbhe jāyate kvacit |
eva hi vidhiruddiṣṭaḥ sakalo nṛpasattama  || 77.4 ||
tīrthasya ca phalaṃ puṇyaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi  |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  || 77.5 ||
etacchrutvā naro rājan sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
tato gacchettu rājendra karañjeśvaramuttamam  || 77.6 ||
tatra siddho mahābhāga daityo vai lokaviśrutaḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
ko'sau siddho mahābhāga tatra tīrthe mahātapāḥ  || 77.7 ||
kasya putro'tha kiṃkāryaṃ kālaḥ kaḥ kathaya dvija  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
purā cādiyuge rājanmānaso brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ  || 77.8 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajño marīcirnāma nāmataḥ  |
tasyā'pi kaśyapo rājan kālena mahatānagha  || 77.9 ||
putro'tha mānaso jātaḥ sākṣādbrahmeva cāparaḥ  |
kṣamā damā dayā dānaṃ satyaṃ śaucamathārjavam  || 77.10 ||
marīcestu guṇāstvete santi bhānti ca bhārata  |
evaṃ guṇagaṇāḥ kīrtiḥ kaśyape dvijasattame  || 77.11 ||
jñātvā prajāpatirdakṣo bhāryārthe svasutāṃ dadau  |
aditirvai danuścaiva tathā hyekādaśā parāḥ  || 77.12 ||
yāsāṃ putrāśca pautrāśca jātā bhrātarasaṃkhyakāḥ  |
aditirjanayāmāsa putrānindrapurogamān  || 77.13 ||
nāgān pretapiśācāṃśca patatrīn yakṣarākṣasān  |
siṃhavyāghravarāhāṃśca anyā ityevamādayaḥ  || 77.14 ||
jātāstasya mahābāho kaśyapasya prajāpateḥ  |
yaistu lokatrayaṃ vyāptaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ mahat || 77.15 ||
tathānyaśca mahābhāgo danoḥ putro vyajāyata  |
sarvalakṣaṇasampannaḥ karañjo nāma nāmataḥ  || 77.16 ||
baleriva mahābhāga cacāra sumahattapaḥ  |
vareṇa chandayāmāsa tripurāntakaraḥ prabhuḥ  || 77.17 ||
bhoḥ karañja mahāsattva parituṣṭo'smi te'nagha  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  || 77.18 ||

karañja uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva tripurāntakara prabho  |
tataḥ putraiśca pautraiśca sahitaṃ me dhanaṃ kuru  || 77.19 ||
tathetyuktvā mahādeva umayā sahitastadā  |
vṛṣārūḍho gaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 77.20 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve so'pi daityo mudānvitaḥ  |
svanāmnā ca mahādevaṃ sthāpayitvā gṛhaṃ yayau  || 77.21 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ karañjeśvaramuttamam  |
snānamātro naro rājanmucyate brahmahatyayā  || 77.22 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe mahārāja tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
so'gniṣṭomasya yatpuṇyaṃ phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 77.23 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narottama  || 77.24 ||
athāgnau ca jale prāṇān bhūtale vāpi yastyajet |
svarge sa tu vasennūnaṃ varṣāṇāmayutadvayam  || 77.25 ||
dhanavān sukule cānte jāyate vipule kule  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 77.26 ||
rājā vā rājatulyo vā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
putrapautrasamāyuktaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 77.27 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yattvaṃ māṃ paripṛcchasi  |
tīrthasya tu phalaṃ yuktyā sarvadāneṣu bhārata  || 77.28 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥkhapraṇāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  || 77.29 ||
yastu śrāvayate śrāddhe sa tatpitṛparāyaṇaḥ  |
akṣayaṃ tasya sarvaṃ syācchaṅkarastvidamabravīt || 77.30 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe karañjeśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāma saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 78
atha aṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ


mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddhiṃ gato devaḥ kuṇḍadhāro nṛpottama  || 78.1 ||
tapaḥ kṛtvā tu vipulaṃ surāsurabhayaṃ karam  |
kuṇḍadhāro mandarasthaḥ krīḍate sa nṛpottama  || 78.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kasyānvaye samutpannaḥ kasya putro mahāmatiḥ  |
tapastaptvā suvipulaṃ toṣito yena śaṅkaraḥ  || 78.3 ||
etadvistaratastāta kathayasva mamānagha  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
purā tretāyuge rājan paulastyo nāma viśravāḥ  || 78.4 ||
upaye me mahābhāga bharadvājasutāṃ nṛpa  |
putraḥ putraguṇairyuktastasyāṃ jāto dhanaṃ jayaḥ  || 78.5 ||
jātamātraṃ sutaṃ jñātvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
cakāra nāma suprīta ṛṣidevasamanvitaḥ  || 78.6 ||
yasmādviśravaso jāto mama pautratvamāgataḥ  |
tasmādvaiśravaṇo nāma mayā dattaṃ tavānagha  || 78.7 ||
yaḥ svayaṃ sarvadevānāṃ dhanagoptā bhaviṣyati  |
caturtho lokapālānāmakṣayo yakṣapo'pi vā  || 78.8 ||
yakṣo yakṣādhipaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kuṇḍadhāro'bhavatsutaḥ  |
susvaṃrūpavayaḥ prāpya mātāpitroranujñayā  || 78.9 ||
tapaścakāra vipulaṃ narmadātīramāśritaḥ  |
yatra vyāghreśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ vyāghrakheṭakamuttamam  || 78.10 ||
kuṇḍadhāreṇa tatraiva tapastaptaṃ sudāruṇam  |
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsvāsārathāraṇaḥ  || 78.11 ||
śiśire jalamadhyastho vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ samāḥ  |
evaṃ varṣaśate pūrṇe ekāṅguṣṭho'bhavattataḥ  || 78.12 ||
cakravadbhramate sūryamabhito bharatarṣabha  |
caturthe pañcame tāvattutoṣa vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 78.13 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva he vatsa yatte manasi rocate  |
taddadāmi na sandehastapasā toṣito hyaham  || 78.14 ||

kuṇḍadhāra uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaditsurihāgataḥ  |
tato mannāmakaṃ liṅgaṃ tīrthaṃ caitadbhavatviti  || 78.15 ||
tathetyuktvā mahādevaḥ somo'ntardhānamāgamat |
jagāmā kāśamāviśya kailāsaṃ dharaṇidharam  || 78.16 ||
gate cādarśana deva so'pi yakṣo mudānvitaḥ  |
sthāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam  || 78.17 ||
alaṃkṛtvā gajaṃ dhenuṃ dhūpapuṣpavilepanaiḥ  |
vitānaiścāmaraiśchatraistathaiva liṅgapūjanaiḥ  || 78.18 ||
tarpayitvā dvijān samyagannapānādi bhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
prīṇayitvā mahādevaṃ tataḥ svabhavanaṃ yayau  || 78.19 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
yudhiṣṭhira paraṃ puṇyaṃ kuṇḍaleśvarasaṃjñakam  || 78.20 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścidupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
arcayeddevamīśānaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 78.21 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi maṇiṃ mauktikameva ca  |
brāhmaṇebhyo dadātyatra sa mukhyo modate divi  || 78.22 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā ṛgyajuḥ sāmasu dvijaḥ  |
ṛcamekāṃ japitvā ca caturvedaphalaṃ labhet || 78.23 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu godānamannadānamathāpi vā  |
yaḥ prayacchati viprebhyastatphalaṃ śṛṇu pāṇḍava  || 78.24 ||
yāvanti tasya romāṇi tatprasūtikuleṣu ca  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  || 78.25 ||
svargavāso bhavettasya putrapautrasamanvitaḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe mahābhāga vyāghraścaiva pipāsitaḥ  || 78.26 ||
niṣādānāṃ bhayenaiva aṭavyāmaṭati svayam  |
niṣādānāṃ bharyairnaṣṭaḥ patito narmadājale  || 78.27 ||
jalapluto mahābhāga liṅgarūpadharo'bhavat |
uktaścākāśavāṇyā vai vyāghreśvaramanuttamam  || 78.28 ||
pūjyaṃ vai triṣu lokeṣu khyātiṃ yāsyatyasaṃśayam  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā talliṅgamarcayettu yaḥ  || 78.29 ||
brahmahatyādipāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam  || 78.30 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 78.31 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kuṇḍaleśvaramahimā anuvarṇanonāma aṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 79

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra pippaleśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahāyogī pippalādo mahātapāḥ  || 79.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
pippalādasya caritaṃ śrotumicchāmyahaṃ prabho  |
māhātmyaṃ tasya tīrthasya yatra siddho mahātapāḥ  || 79.2 ||
kasya putro mahābhāga kimarthaṃ taptavāṃstapaḥ  |
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  || 79.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
mithilāstho mahābhāga vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
yājñavalkyaśca purataścacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 79.4 ||
tāpasī tasya bhaginī yājñavalkyasya dhīmataḥ  |
cacāra sāpi tatrasthā śuśrūṣantī mahattapaḥ  || 79.5 ||
tatastvekasmin samaye snātāhani rajasvalā  |
antarvāsaṃ kṛtavatī dṛṣṭvā karkaṭakaṃ rahaḥ  || 79.6 ||
yājñavalkyo'pi tadrātrau paridhānena tena vai  |
svapnaṃ dṛṣṭvātyajacchukraṃ prabhāte'nvaiṣayatpunaḥ  || 79.7 ||
tata sā brāhmaṇī tāta kimanveṣyasi bhārata  |
kena kāryaṃ tava vibho vadasva mama tattvataḥ  || 79.8 ||

yājñavalkya uvāca - 
apavitro mayā bhadre svapno dṛṣṭo'dya vai niśi  |
śuklaṃ me cātra vastraṃ svaṃ nikṣiptaṃ tanna dṛśyate  || 79.9 ||
tacchrutvā brāhmaṇī vākyaṃ bhītabhītābhavannṛpa  |
tadvastraṃ tu mayā brahman snātvāntardhānakaṃ kṛtam  || 79.10 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā hāhetyuktvā mahātapāḥ  |
papāta sahasā bhūmau chinnamūla iva drumaḥ  || 79.11 ||
kimetaditi jalpantamākāśādvāgvinirgatā  |
toṣayantī ca taṃ vipraṃ provāca nṛpate tadā  || 79.12 ||
nāsya doṣo mayā dṛṣṭastava caiva śubhavrate  |
tava garbhodayo yena tatra daivaṃ parāyaṇam  || 79.13 ||
na vināśo'sya kartavyo yāvatkālasya paryayaḥ  |
tatheti vrīḍitā sā ca durmaneti vimānataḥ  || 79.14 ||
pālayāmāsa taṃ garbhaṃ yāvatputro vyajāyata  |
jātamātraṃ tu taṃ garbhaṃ kathayitvā na kaṃcana  || 79.15 ||
aśvasthavṛkṣamāsādya sotsasarja mahītale  |
yāni sattvāni lokeṣu sthāvarāṇi carāṇi vai  || 79.16 ||
tāni vai pālayantvenaṃ bālakaṃ tyajati sma sā  |
evamuktvā tataḥ sādhvī brāhmaṇī nṛpasattama  || 79.17 ||
yathāgataṃ jagāmātha sāvasthāya muhūrtakam  |
pādau pāṇī vinikṣipya vimṛjya nayane śūbhe  || 79.18 ||
āsyaṃ ca vikṛtaṃ kṛtvā rurodoccairanāthavat |
tena śabdena vitrastāḥ sthāvarā jaṅgamāśca ye  || 79.19 ||
akampayatmahīṃ tāta saśailavanakandarām  |
tato jñātvā mahadbhūtaṃ kṣudhāviṣṭaṃ dvijarṣabham  || 79.20 ||
na jahāti nagaśchāyāmārpayacca tataḥ payaḥ  |
āpyāyitastatastena amṛtenaiva bhārata  |
tataḥ sa cintayāviṣṭo nirmame grahagocaram  || 79.21 ||
tena krūrasamācāraḥ krūradṛṣṭyā nirīkṣitaḥ  |
papāta sahasā bhūmau śanaiścārī śanaiścaraḥ  || 79.22 ||
śanaiścaraṃ bālako'pi pādenaiva parāmṛśat |
pīḍitaḥ so'pi bālena uvāca vacanaṃ tadā  || 79.23 ||
kiṃ mayā'pakṛtaṃ vipra pippalāda mahāmune  |
niṣkrāman gagane caiva pātito dharaṇītale  || 79.24 ||
sauriṇāpyevamuktastu pippalādo mahāmuniḥ  |
krodharūpo'bravīdvākyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 79.25 ||
pitṛmātṛvihīnasya bālabhāvasya durmate  |
pīḍāṃ karoṣi kasmāttvaṃ saure tvamavaśeṣitaḥ  || 79.26 ||

śanaiścara uvāca - 
krūrasvabhāvasaṃjātā mama dṛṣṭi dvijottama  |
muñca tvaṃ māṃ ca kartāhaṃ yadbravīṣi na saṃśayaḥ  || 79.27 ||

pippalāda uvāca - 
adya prabhṛtibālānāṃ janmataḥ ṣoḍaśīḥ samāḥ  |
pīḍā tvayā na kartavyā eṣa te samayaḥ paraḥ  || 79.28 ||
evamastviti taṃ coktvā prajagāma yathāgataḥ  |
devamārgaṃ śanaiścārī praṇamya ṛṣisattamam  || 79.29 ||
tataścādarśanaṃ tatra gatavān sa mahāgrahaḥ  |
vicintayānaścaikākī krodhena kaluṣīkṛtaḥ  || 79.30 ||
āgneyīṃ hi diśaṃ dhyātvā janayāmāsa pāvakam  |
kṛttvā māṃsaṃ juhāvāgnau kriyāsambhavatattvataḥ  || 79.31 ||
tāvacca janitā kṛtyā jvālāmālāvibhūṣitā  |
hutabhuksadṛśākārā kiṃkaromīti cābravīt || 79.32 ||
śoṣayāmi samudraṃ kiṃ cūrṇayāmi ca parvatam  |
bhūmiṃ ca veṣṭayāmīha pātayitvā nabhastalam  || 79.33 ||
kasya mūrdhni patiṣyāmi ghātayāmi ca kaṃ dvija  |
śīghramādiśa me kāryaṃ na kālātikramo bhavet || 79.34 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā pippalādo mahātapāḥ  |
krodharaktāntanayana idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 79.35 ||
mahatā krodhavegena mayā tvaṃ cintitā śubhe  |
pitā me yājñavalkyastu taṃ tvaṃ ghātaya māciram  || 79.36 ||
evamuktā tu sā śīghraṃ sphuṭantīva nabhastalam  |
mithilāstho mahāprājño yatra tepe mahātapāḥ  || 79.37 ||
yāvatpaśyati diṅmārgaṃ jvalanārkasamaprabham  |
yājñavalkyo mahātejāstadbhūtaṃ samupasthitam  || 79.38 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasāyāntīṃ bhītabhīto mahāmuniḥ  |
bhūtenākramito vipro janakaṃ nṛpatiṃ yayau  || 79.39 ||
śaraṇārthamanuprāptaṃ viddhi māṃ nṛpasattama  |
mahābhūtācca māṃ rakṣa yadi śaknoṣi mānada  || 79.40 ||

rājovāca - 
brahmatejobhavaṃ bhūtamanivāryaṃ durāsadam  |
prabhurnaivādya śaknomi anyaṃ gaccha mahāmate  || 79.41 ||
tataścānyaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭhaṃ śaraṇārthī mahātapāḥ  |
jagāma tena caivokta indrasya śaraṇaṃ yayau  || 79.42 ||
devarāja namaste'stu mahābhūtācca rakṣa mām  |
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvābravīdindrastadā vacaḥ  || 79.43 ||
na ca śaktaḥ paritrātuṃ brahmatejo hi duḥsaham  |
tataśca brahmabhavanaṃ brāhmaṇo brahmavittamaḥ  || 79.44 ||
jagāma viṣṇubhavanaṃ śakto'pi tyaktavān bhayāt |
tataḥ sa paramodvigno nirāśo jīvite nṛpa  || 79.45 ||
anugamyamāno bhūtena agacchacca maheśvaram  |
tasya yogabalopeto mahādevasya pāṇḍava  || 79.46 ||
nakhamāṃsāntare lupto yathā devo na paśyati  |
adṛṣṭamagamadbhūtaṃ jvalanārkasamaprabham  || 79.47 ||
muñca muñceti puruṣa muñceśvaramuvāca ha  |
evamukto mahādevastena bhūtena bhārata  || 79.48 ||
yogīndraṃ darśayāmāsa nakhamāṃsāntare sthitam  |
saṃsthāpya kṛtyāṃ bhūteśo jvalatkālānalaprabhām  || 79.49 ||
uvāca mā bhaistvaṃ vipra mā ca gaccha mahāmune  |
tatastaṃ sūkṣmadehasthaṃ mahādevo'bravīdidam  || 79.50 ||
kimasya tvaṃ mahābhūta kartā kṛtya vadasva me  |

kṛtyovāca - 
krodhadīptena deveśa pippalādena cintitā  || 79.51 ||
asya dehe patiṣyāmi ahiṃsyāṃ viddhi māṃ prabho  |
etacchrutvā mahādevo bhūtasya vadanāccyutam  || 79.52 ||
variṣṭhaṃ bandhayāmāsa yājñavalkyasya ghātakam  |
yogīśvaraṃ taṃ viprendraṃ dattvābhītiṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 79.53 ||
visarjayitvā devastaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata  |
preṣayitvā tu taṃ bhūtaṃ pippalādo'pi durmanāḥ  || 79.54 ||
mātāpitṛvihīnastu narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
ekaniṣṭho nirāhāro varṣāṇi ṣoḍaśaiva tu  || 79.55 ||
toṣayāmāsa deveśamumayā saha śaṅkaram  |

hara uvāca - 
parituṣṭo'smi te vipra tapasānena suvrata  || 79.56 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva te dadyāṃ manasābhīpsitaṃ śubham  |

pippalāda uvāca - 
yadi me bhagavāṃstuṣṭo yadi deyo varo mama  || 79.57 ||
atra sannihito deva mama nāmnā ca śaṅkara  |
evamuktastathetyuktvā pippalādaṃ mahāmunim  || 79.58 ||
jagāmādarśanaṃ devo bhūtasaṅghairniṣevitaḥ  |
pippalādo gate deve snātvā tatra mahāmbhasi  || 79.59 ||
sthāpayitvā mahadevaṃ jagāmottaraparvatam  |
tatra tīrthe naro bhaktyā snātvā mantrayuto nṛpa  || 79.60 ||
tarpayitvā pitṝn devān pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam  || 79.61 ||
mṛto rudrapuraṃ yāti pippaleśvarasannidhau  |
athavā bhojayedviprān pitṝnuddiśya bhaktitaḥ  || 79.62 ||
dvādaśābdasahasrāṇi tṛptā gacchanti sadgatim  |
saṃnyāsena tu yaḥ kaścittatra tīrthe tanuṃ tyajet || 79.63 ||
anivartikā gatistasya yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ yattvaṃ māṃ paripṛṣṭavān  || 79.64 ||
māhātmyaṃ pippalādasya pippaleśvaramuttamam  |
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥkhapraṇāśanam  || 79.65 ||
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvapāpapramocanam  || 79.66 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pippaleśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāmaikonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 80

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra vimaleśvaramuttamam  |
tatra devaśilā ramyā mahādevena bhāṣitā  || 80.1 ||
garjanaṃ kheṭakaṃ nāma tatra devaśilā śubhā  |
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 80.2 ||
tasya te dvādaśābdāni sutṛptā divi moditāḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo bhaktyā brāhmaṇān pūjayennṛpa  || 80.3 ||
svalpenāpi hi dānena tasya cānto na vidyate  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kāni dānāni viprendra śastāni dharaṇītale  || 80.4 ||
yāni dattvā naro bhaktyā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ maṇiṃ mauktikameva ca  || 80.5 ||
bhūmidānaṃ tathā gāvo mocayantyaṃśumānnaram  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitkurute prāṇasaṃkṣayam  || 80.6 ||
rudraloke vasettāvadyāvadāhūtasaṃplavam  |
tataḥ puṣkariṇīṃ gacchetkurukṣetrasamāṃ nṛpa  || 80.7 ||
pūrvaṃ puṣkariṇī nāma kurukṣetraṃ kalau smṛtam  |
tatra snātvā yajeddevaṃ tejorāśiṃ divākaram  || 80.8 ||
ṛcamekāṃ japetsaumyaḥ sāmavedaphalaṃ labhet |
yajurvedasya japanaṃ ṛgvedasya tathaiva ca  || 80.9 ||
tryakṣaraṃ vā japenmantraṃ dhyāyamāno divākaram  |
ādityahṛdayaṃ japtvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 80.10 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vidhinā pūjayeddvijān  |
tasya koṭiguṇaṃ dānaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 80.11 ||
kārtikyāṃ ca tathā mādhyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ  |
amāvāsyāṃ vyatīpāte saṃkrame vaidhṛtau ravau  || 80.12 ||
kurukṣetre naraḥ snātvā rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
anāśake jale hyagnau pañcāgnau vā tathāpi vā  || 80.13 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe mṛto yastu sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā nṛpasattama  || 80.14 ||
vihitaṃ karmakurvāṇaḥ sa gacchati satāṃ gatim  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kiṃ japanmucyate vyādherjñātvā varṇaṃ dvijottama  || 80.15 ||
kiṃ kurvanmucyate prāṇī yāti lokamanāmayam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu rājannavahita itihāsaṃ purātanam  || 80.16 ||
guhyatīrthe samāsādya brāhmaṇo muktavān yathā  |
purā dvijavaraścāsīdgovindo nāma nāmataḥ  || 80.17 ||
tasya bhāryā susampannā brāhmaṇī ca pativratā  |
tasyāṃ saṃjanayāmāsa putramekaṃ ca sundaram  || 80.18 ||
sa bāla eva bhavane krīḍate śiśulīlayā  |
kadācidbrāhmaṇaśreṣṭhaḥ kāṣṭhamānayituṃ gataḥ  || 80.19 ||
vanānnītvā kāṣṭhabhāraṃ gṛhe paścācca kṣiptavān  |
krīḍannāste śiśustatra kāṣṭhabhāreṇa pīḍitaḥ  || 80.20 ||
mamāra bālakastatra dvijo na jñātavāṃstadā  |
brāhmaṇyapi tadā tasmai na śaśaṃsa bhayāttathā  || 80.21 ||
purnadvijaḥ sa govindo vipinaṃ saṃjagāma ha  || 80.22 ||

brāhmaṇyuvāca - 
rāvaṇo brahmaṇaḥ pautrastrailokyaṃ yasya śaṅkate  |
sa hato rāmacandreṇa saputrāmātyabāndhavaḥ  || 80.23 ||
evaṃ putraṃ vinā saukhyaṃ martye nāke na vidyate  |
yaśa ākhyāyitaṃ yasya svargārthaṃ yasya bhāratī  || 80.24 ||
miṣṭānnaṃ brāhmaṇasyārthe svargavāso'pi vidyate  |
putrotpattivināśābhyāṃ nāparaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ  || 80.25 ||
brahmahatyāśvamedhābhyāṃ nāparaṃ pāpapuṇyayoḥ  |
kiṃ bravīmīti he vatsa nānusaukhyaṃ sutaṃ vinā  || 80.26 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ duḥkhaṃ pralapitvā punaḥ punaḥ  |
bālaṃ gṛhagate vipre saṃgopya brāhmaṇī tathā  || 80.27 ||
evaṃ tasyāṃ vilapantyāṃ gatā rātriryudhiṣṭhira  |
bhūmyāṃ prasuptaṃ govindaṃ putraśokena pīḍitā  || 80.28 ||
yāvannirīkṣate bhāryā bhartāraṃ duḥkhapīḍitam  |
kṛmirāśimayaṃ tāvadgovindaṃ nṛpasattama  || 80.29 ||
duḥkhādduḥkhatare magnā dṛṣṭvā taṃ pātakānvitam  |
evaṃ duḥkhanimagnāyāḥ śarvarī vigatā tadā  || 80.30 ||
punaḥ prātastu govindo darbhāya ca vanaṃ gataḥ  |
evaṃ na jñātavān vipraḥ kāṣṭhena ca hataṃ sutam  || 80.31 ||
gatāśca divasāḥ pañcabrāhmaṇyā gopitaṃ ca yat |
paśupālaḥ pañcame'hni mahiṣīruttamāśca gāḥ  || 80.32 ||
araṇye mahiṣīrmuktvā gāśca bhoktuṃ gṛhaṃ gataḥ  |
vijñaptaḥ paśupālena govindo brāhmaṇottamaḥ  || 80.33 ||
yāvadbhakṣāmyahaṃ svāminmahiṣīrgāśca rakṣaya  |
tataḥ sa tvarito gāśca brāhmaṇo mahiṣīḥ prati  || 80.34 ||
jagāma mahiṣīrgāśca viprasya tasya rakṣataḥ  |
dhāvamānasya gāvaśca mahiṣyaḥ saṅgamaṃ gatāḥ  || 80.35 ||
tatra praviṣṭāstu jale nadyā revāsusaṅgame  |
tajjalaṃ pītamātraṃ tu tvarayā tena vāritāḥ  || 80.36 ||
akāmātsalilaṃ pītvā prakṣālya nayane śubhe  |
ājagāma tataḥ paścādbhavanaṃ dinasaṃkṣaye  || 80.37 ||
bhuktvā duḥkhānvito rātrau govindaḥ śayanaṃ yayau  |
nidrābhibhūto duḥkhena śrameṇaiva tu kheditaḥ  || 80.38 ||
punastaṃ cārdharātre tu tasya bhāryā nirīkṣate  |
kṛmibhirveṣṭitaṃ gātraṃ kvacitpaśyatyaveṣṭitam  || 80.39 ||
punaḥ sā vismayāviṣṭā tasya bhāryā guṇānvitā  |
uvāca duṣkṛtaṃ tasya sādhvasāviṣṭacetanā  || 80.40 ||

bhāryovāca - 
atīte pañcame cāhṇi indhanaṃ kṣipatā tu te  |
gṛhe paścātsthito bālastvajñāto ghātitastvayā  || 80.41 ||
mayā tatpātakaṃ ghoraṃ tvatkṛtaṃ na prakāśitam  |
tena pracchannapāpena dahyamānā divāniśam  || 80.42 ||
na sukhaṃ tava gātrasya na ca paśyāmi cātmanaḥ  |
nidrā praṇaṣṭā me nātha ratiścaiva tvayā saha  || 80.43 ||
śrūyate mānave śāstre śloko gīto maharṣibhiḥ  |
smṛtvā smṛtvā ca taṃ rātrau paritāpo na śāmyati  || 80.44 ||
kīrtanānnaśyate'dharmo vardhate'sau ca gūhanāt |
iha loke pare caiva pāpasyānto na vidyate  || 80.45 ||
evaṃ saṃcintyamānāhaṃ sthitā rātrau bhayāturā  |
kṛmirāśimayaṃ tvāṃ tu paśyāmi kathayāmi kim  || 80.46 ||
punaśca kānta tvaddehaṃ bhrūṇahatyākṛmiplutam  |
kvacittudanti te caiva kvacinnaṣṭāḥ samantataḥ  || 80.47 ||
etatsaṃsmṛtya saṃsmṛtya vimṛśantī punaḥ punaḥ  |
na jāne kāraṇaṃ kiṃcitpṛcchāmi kathayasva me  || 80.48 ||
taḍāgaṃ vāpi saritaṃ tīrthaṃ vā devatālayam  |
yaṃ gato'si prabhāvo'yaṃ tasya nānyasya me matiḥ  || 80.49 ||
evamuktastvasau vipraḥ kathayāmāsa bhārata  |
bhāryayā pūrvavṛttāntaṃ ramamāṇo nṛpottama  || 80.50 ||
golulāyī nivṛttyarthaṃ narmadā saṅgamaṃ gataḥ  |
nābhimātre jale magnastoyaṃ pītaṃ yatheṣṭataḥ  || 80.51 ||
nānyattīrthaṃ vijānāmi narmadā ca saridvarā  |
evaṃ śrutvā ca tatsarvamupavāsaḥ kṛtaḥ kṣaṇāt || 80.52 ||
bhartrā saha gatā tatra saṅgame varavarṇinī  |
snātvā vidhiprayuktena saṅgame surapūjite  || 80.53 ||
tarpayāmāsa deveśaṃ śaṅkaraṃ ca sahomayā  |
pañcāmṛtaiḥ snāpayitvā brāhmaṇyā sahito dvija  || 80.54 ||
gandhamālyādidhūpaiśca naivedyaiśca suśobhanaiḥ  |
apūjayattatra liṅgaṃ devīṃ kātyāyanīṃ śubhām  || 80.55 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhartrā tena sahaiva sā  |
tataḥ prabhāte vimale dvijaṃ sampūjya yatnataḥ  || 80.56 ||
godānena hiraṇyena vastreṇānnena bhārata  |
govindaḥ pūjayāmāsa svaśaktyā brāhmaṇaṃ śubham  || 80.57 ||
uktaścākāśavāṇyā tu tīrthaṃ guhyāvatī tvidam  |
guhyeśvaraṃ tatra liṅgaṃ pātālādutthitaṃ tadā  || 80.58 ||
guhyāvatīnarmadayoḥ saṅgamo guṇavānabhūt |
muktapāpo gṛhaṃ yātaḥ svabhāryāsahito dvijaḥ  || 80.59 ||
etattīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ bālahatyāpraṇāśanam  |
tatra snātvā ca japtvā ca dattvā brāhmaṇabhojanam  || 80.60 ||
upāsya śrāddhakaraṇāttilodakapradānataḥ  |
nivasecchivaloke hi yāvadāhūta saṃplavam  || 80.61 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe guhyāvatītīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāmāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 81

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra uttare narmadātaṭe  |
meghanādasamīpe tu viśvarūpā saridvarā  || 81.1 ||
nirgatā viśvarūpasya śarīrādupakurvataḥ  |
purā dāruvane devo liṅgahīnaḥ kṛto dvijaiḥ  || 81.2 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya tapaḥ kurvaṃstadā nṛpa  |
viśvarūpo'bhavaddevo nirgatā saritāṃ varā  || 81.3 ||
gatā sā narmadātoyaṃ saṅgamo guṇavānabhūt |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā sa bhave na punarbhavet || 81.4 ||
tatra yatkriyate karma sarvaṃ tadakṣayaṃ bhavet |
sārikā siddhimāyātā patitā tīrthasaṅgame  || 81.5 ||
pūrvamapsarasāṃ śreṣṭhā śakraśāpādakāmataḥ  |
citrāṅgadena ramitā kācitkaṣṭamavāpa ha  || 81.6 ||
sārikā bhava kalyāṇi varṣāṇāṃ sā'graviṃśatim  |
mṛtvā tvaṃ narmadātoye viśvarūpā susaṅgame  || 81.7 ||
vicitrā bahucārvaṅgī sañjātā sārikā nṛpa  |
jātismarā surābhāvā narmadātaṭamāśritā  || 81.8 ||
tataḥ kāle ca saṃprāpte prajvālya pāvakaṃ śubham  |
praviṣṭā sā śubhācārā viśvarūpā susaṅgame  || 81.9 ||
divyadehadharī rājan prāptā śakrasya mandiram  |
etadantaramāsādya sārikātīrthamucyate  || 81.10 ||
tatra yatkriyate karma śrāddhaṃ yajñaḥ śivārcanam  |
sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ vidyānmeghanādasya darśanāt || 81.11 ||
avaśaḥ svavaśo vāpi yastu prāṇān parityajet |
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  || 81.12 ||
khyātāni pañcaliṅgāni yāni dṛṣṭvā śivaṃ vrajet |
mānavo manujaśreṣṭha śṛṇu tāni yudhiṣṭhira  || 81.13 ||
meghanādaṃ ca goṣṭheśaṃ vāgīśaṃ kākaḍeśvaram  |
lakṣeśvaraṃ pañcaliṅgānyekāhe yastu pūjayet || 81.14 ||
anenaiva śarīreṇa sa naro hi śivaṃ vrajet |
koṭiyajñaphalaṃ prāpya paścānmokṣamavāpnuyāt || 81.15 ||
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi purāvṛttaṃ tavānagha  |
dharmasenaḥ purā rājā ayodhyādhipatirbalī  || 81.16 ||
dharmeṇa rājyaṃ kṛtavān yajñāṃśca bahudakṣiṇān  |
śṛṇvan sa dharmaśāstrāṇi narmadācaritaṃ tathā  || 81.17 ||
śrutvā vinirgato rājā revāyā uttare taṭe  |
meghanādaṃ samabhyarcya snātvā vai narmadājale  || 81.18 ||
udgacchati dinakare aśvārūḍho nareśvaraḥ  |
uttarāṃ diśamāśritya gato goṣṭheśvaraṃ śivam  || 81.19 ||
yathāvidhānaṃ sampūjya vāgīśvaraṃ gatastataḥ  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena pūjayitvā śivaṃ nṛpaḥ  || 81.20 ||
candanāgurukarpūrairdhūpairdīpairvidhānakaiḥ  |
aśvārūḍho nṛpaśreṣṭhaḥ kākaḍeśvaramāgataḥ  || 81.21 ||
taṃ prapūjya tato rājā gatvā vai nārmade jale  |
lakṣeśvaraṃ pūjayitvā sthitaṃ vai vidhipūrvakam  || 81.22 ||
meghanādaṃ tato gatvā sūryaścāstamupāgamat |
dhyātvā svayaṃ kālarūpaṃ yāvattiṣṭhati vai nṛpaḥ  || 81.23 ||
tāvadghoro'pi turago hyantarikṣacarastadā  |
divyadehadharaḥ saścāpyapsarobhiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  || 81.24 ||
vimāne devarājasya yayāvindrapurīṃ sthitaḥ  |
śunī pṛṣṭhe tu yā rājñastīrthayātrāṃ prakurvatī  || 81.25 ||
divyadehadharā sāpi vimānena gatā divi  |
dharmaseno'pi tāṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayāviṣṭacetanaḥ  || 81.26 ||
aśvarūpaṃ jagādātha kimetaditi bhārata  |
uvācākāśago vācaṃ kathaṃ tvaṃ khidyase nṛpa  || 81.27 ||
śarīrajena kaṣṭena tapaḥ sādhyā vibhūtayaḥ  |
pādacārī hi gaccha tvaṃ parapādairgato hyasi  || 81.28 ||
bhūyo yātrāṃ prakuruṣe tadā siddhimavāpsyasi  |
tato rājā ca tasyātha śrutvā tadvacanaṃ tadā  || 81.29 ||
punardvitīyadivase prasthito liṅgapūjanam  |
pañcaliṅgān samabhyarcya samāyātastu narmadām  || 81.30 ||
meghanādaṃ yadāpaśyaddvāre devaṃ ca dṛṣṭavān  |
pañcavaktraṃ daśabhujaṃ trinetraṃ śūlapāṇinam  || 81.31 ||
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ jagadgarbhaṃ śaśāṅkakṛtaśekharam  |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ devadeveśaṃ tuṣṭāva parameśvaram  || 81.32 ||
jaya deva mahādeva mahāpātakanāśana  |
saṃsārasāgare magnaṃ māṃ samuddhara sāmpratam  || 81.33 ||

hara uvāca - 
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |
taddadāmi na sandehaśśvibhakto hi putraka  || 81.34 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva tanmāṃ sahacaraṃ kuru  |
ekāhe pañcaliṅgāni pūjayiṣyati yo naraḥ  || 81.35 ||
sa tavānucaro deva bhavatveṣa varo mama  |
dharmasenavacaḥ śrutvā bhavatvevaṃ haro'bravīt || 81.36 ||
taṃ gṛhītvā tu rājānaṃ kailāsaṃ sa jagāma ha  |
svadehasthaṃ cakārāsau dharmasenaṃ nṛpaṃ nṛpa  || 81.37 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
śravaṇātkīrtanādasya aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 81.38 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe pañcaliṅgamahimānuvarṇano nāmaikāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 82

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānyatkathayiṣyāmi tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
mayūrakukkuṭaṃ nāma brahmahatyā vyapohanam  || 82.1 ||
mṛkaṇḍasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
mṛkaṇḍo nāma bhūpāla ṛṣiḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  || 82.2 ||
tapastepe mahābhāga divyairvarṣasahasrakaiḥ  |
tasyāśramapade ramye munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 82.3 ||
vasanti sma jalāhārāḥ śuṣkapatrakṛtāśanāḥ  |
kecittanna nirāhārā mokṣopāyavicintakāḥ  || 82.4 ||
etasminnantare rājan gandharvau śakragāyanau  |
hetiprahetināmānau gatau śakrasabhāṃ nṛpa  || 82.5 ||
vadhūrapsarasāṃ śreṣṭhā dṛṣṭā tābhyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
dṛṣṭamātrau tu gandharvau kāmabāṇaprapīḍitau  || 82.6 ||
hetiḥ kukkuṭaśabdena prahetirbarhiṇastathā  |
ghoṣyamāṇau sumadhuraṃ sādayāmāsa tu ca tām  || 82.7 ||
vṛtrahā tadabhiprāyaṃ jñātvā śāpaṃ dadau tavā  || 82.8 ||
pūrṇe divyaśate varṣe paścādatrāgamiṣyathaḥ  |
tiryagyonau tu saṃprāptau gandharvau hi yudhiṣṭhira  || 82.9 ||
jātismarau durācārau pakṣiṇau priyadarśīnau  |
sarvatīrthānyuttaran tau nāradaṃ ca dadarśatuḥ  || 82.10 ||

gandharvāvūcatu̱ḥ
bhaviṣyāvaḥ śubhācāra brahmaputra tapodhana  |
karmaṇā kena cāvāṃ hi muktāvetau vadasva tat || 82.11 ||

nārada uvāca - 
narmadā dakṣiṇe tīre mṛkaṇḍasyāśramaṃ śubham  |
tiryagyonivimokṣaṃ ca tīrthaṃ hi paramaṃ matam  || 82.12 ||
jalāplutau narmadāyāḥ sarvaṃ tatra bhaviṣyati  |
tato hetiḥ prahetiśca susnātau divyarūpiṇau  || 82.13 ||
ekena snānamātreṇa pakṣiṇau divyatāṃ gatau  |
snātvā tu vidhinā'nena dhyātvā devaṃ sadāśivam  || 82.14 ||
uccāryyāghoramantraṃ tau sadā dhyānasthitau nṛpa  |
etasminnantare rājan pātālādutthitaṃ śubham  || 82.15 ||
śatasūryaprakāśaṃ hi liṅgaṃ tatra yudhiṣṭhira  |
kukkuṭeśvaramekaṃ tu mayūreśvarameva ca  || 82.16 ||
gandharvau tu vimānasthau gatau śakrasya mandiram  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā bhave naiva punarbhavet || 82.17 ||
snātvā tilodakaṃ dattvā pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo'vāpi yastu prāṇān parityajet || 82.18 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  |
tatra kīṭāḥ pataṅgāśca pakṣiṇo'tha sarīsṛpāḥ  || 82.19 ||
maṇḍūkāḥ pāpavṛkṣāśca mṛtā yānti śivaṃ padam  || 82.20 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mṛkaṇḍāśramakīrtano nāma dvyaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 83

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato'nyatparamaṃ tīrthaṃ candramatyāstu saṅgame  |
candreśvaraṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ tathā siddheśvaraṃ punaḥ  || 83.1 ||
ghaṇṭeśvaraṃ mahiṣeśamaśvatīrthamataḥ param  |
vṛṣasenaṃ hayagrīvaṃ śukatīrthamataḥ param  || 83.2 ||
rameśvaraṃ tato gacchettīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
mekalāyāstaṭe rājanmahāpātakanāśanam  || 83.3 ||
yadā dāruvane pūrvaṃ mahādevena mohitāḥ  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ striyastatra ramamāṇāḥ samāgatāḥ  || 83.4 ||
cintayantyaśca tā mokṣaṃ mekalātīramāśritāḥ  |
tābhiśca ramamāṇābhirāvṛtaṃ śivapūjanam  || 83.5 ||
nīlotpaladalairbilvairmallikājātikundakaiḥ  |
śūnyaṃ prapūjitaṃ yāvattāvalliṅgaṃ samutthitam  || 83.6 ||
pātālādāgataṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
rameśvareti vikhyātaṃ ramamāṇātsamutthitam  || 83.7 ||
strīṇāṃ uvāca deveśaḥ śāpamokṣo bhavatviti  |
gacchantu sarvāḥ svagṛhaṃ sāmprataṃ gatakalmaṣāḥ  || 83.8 ||
ityuktvā devadeveśastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā sa bhave na punarbhavet || 83.9 ||
anāśakena cāgnau hi ye mṛtā na punarbhavāḥ  |
tilodakaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu piṇḍadānaṃ yathāvidhi  || 83.10 ||
śrāddhenaiva ca dānena pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
indreṇa brahmaṇā pūrvaṃ viṣṇunā dhanadena ca  || 83.11 ||
rakṣasā rāvaṇenātha tathā cendrajitā nṛpa  |
japo japtastapastaptaṃ yajñāni vividhāni ca  || 83.12 ||
kṛtāni nṛpaśārdūla gatā hi paramāṃ gatim  |
anyacca kathayiṣyāmi hāriṇaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 83.13 ||
hariṇeśaṃ siddhaliṅgaṃ tathā vai dhanurīśvaram  |
bāṇeśvaraṃ paraviddhi tathā vai lubdhakeśvaram  || 83.14 ||
liṅgarūpāṇi pūjayitvā śivaṃ vrajet |
ākhyānaṃ kathayiṣyāmi purāvṛttaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 83.15 ||
arjuno lubdhako nāma mandajātisamudbhavaḥ  |
paryaṭanmṛgayāṃ rājannarmadātīramāgataḥ  || 83.16 ||
dṛṣṭvā yūthaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ tu dhāvamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
palāyamānāḥ sarve te ekaḥ paścātsthito mṛgaḥ  || 83.17 ||
hato madhyadine so'dya kuraṅgo narmadātaṭe  |
patito'sau gataprāṇo divyadehadharaḥ punaḥ  || 83.18 ||
vimāne haṃsayukte vai brahmalokaṃ jagāma ha  |
gate tu hariṇe so'tha lubdhakaścintayānvitaḥ  || 83.19 ||
mahāpāpānyanekāni kṛtāni tu mayā punaḥ  |
kā gatiṃ yāmyahaṃ cātha śreyase maraṇaṃ mama  || 83.20 ||
cintayitvā tato rājan patito narmadājale  |
tatkṣaṇāddivyadeho'sau gandharvapuramāyayau  || 83.21 ||
gate tasmin devaloke dhanurbāṇau jale sthitau  |
catvāryetāni liṅgāni khyātāni bhuvanatraye  || 83.22 ||
hariṇeśvaraṃ ca bāṇeśaṃ lubdheśaṃ dhanurīśvaram  |
rameśvaraṃ pañcamaṃ tu pañcaliṅgāni kīrtayet || 83.23 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirghore saṃsārasāgare  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naro rājan snātvā śivapuraṃ vrajet || 83.24 ||
brahmahatyādi pāpāni vilayaṃ yānti pārthiva  |
anāśake cārdhajale mṛtaḥ śivamavāpnuyāt || 83.25 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe rameśvarahariṇeśvaralubdhakeśvaradhanurīśvarabāṇeśvarakathano nāma tryaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 84

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tatra snātvā tu bhaktyā ya upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ kuryāddadyāddānaṃ ca yatnataḥ  || 84.1 ||
devasya snapanaṃ kuryādamṛtaiḥ pañcabhistathā  |
samālabhedyathāśaktyā pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  || 84.2 ||
pātraṃ parīkṣya dātavyamātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti dvādaśābdaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 84.3 ||
gacchati tatra dātā ca yatra devo nirañjanaḥ  |
gṛhamadhye praviṣṭastu smarannāmāsya śaktitaḥ  || 84.4 ||
nīlādrau tu ca yatpuṇyaṃ tatsamastaṃ labheta saḥ  |
śūlabhede ca yaḥ kuryācchrāddhaṃ parvaṇi parvaṇi  || 84.5 ||
viśeṣāccaiva māsānte tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
kedāre caiva yatpuṇyaṃ kubjāyāṃ ca tathā nṛpa  || 84.6 ||
kanakhale caiva yatpuṇyaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  |
sitāsite tu yatpuṇyamanyatīrthe viśeṣataḥ  || 84.7 ||
arbude caiva yatpuṇyaṃ puṇyaṃ cāmaraparvate  |
gaṅgādyaiḥ sarvatīrthaiśca phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 84.8 ||
asmiṃstīrthe tathā puṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
vidhimantrasamāyuktaṃ tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 84.9 ||
kulāni tārayatyeva daśa pūrvāparāṇi saḥ  |
dakṣiṇāṃ caiva martyaśca śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 84.10 ||
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvoktaṃ pradadyādaṣṭapuṣpakam  |
śāstroktairaṣṭabhirmantrairmānasaiḥ śṛṇu tāṃstathā  || 84.11 ||
vārijaṃ saumyamāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ pārthivaṃ punaḥ  |
vānaspatyaṃ bhavetpuṣpaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu saptamam  || 84.12 ||
aṣṭamaṃ śivapuṣpaṃ ca śṛṇveteṣāṃ vinirṇayam  |
vārijaṃ salilaṃ jñeyaṃ saumyaṃ madhuyutaṃ payaḥ  || 84.13 ||
āgneyaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ ca vāyavyaṃ candanādikam  |
pārthivaṃ kandamūlādyaṃ vānaspatyaphalātmakam  || 84.14 ||
prājāpatyamannādyaṃ ca śivapuṣpamupāsanam  |
ahiṃsā prathamaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigrahaḥ  || 84.15 ||
tṛtīyaṃ ca dayāpuṣpamebhistuṣyanti devatāḥ  |
tapasā cārcayedbhaktyā atra tīrthe narādhipa  || 84.16 ||
chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ dadyācchayyāṃ copānahau tathā  |
tena pūjanamātreṇa pūjitāḥ puruṣāstrayaḥ  || 84.17 ||
svargaloke vasettāvadyāvadāhuta saṃplavam  |
śūlapāṇestu yo bhaktyā snapanaṃ caiva kārayet || 84.18 ||
pañcāmṛtena yaścaiva yakṣakardamakuṃkumaiḥ  |
samālabhecca deveśaṃ śrīkhaṇḍairagarādibhiḥ  || 84.19 ||
nānāvidhaiśca puṣpaiścārcāṃ kurvanti ye dvijāḥ  |
rudraṃ puruṣasūktaṃ ca loke yaḥ svasvasūtrakam  || 84.20 ||
iṣe tvādikamantrādi jyotirbrāhmaṇameva ca  |
gāyatrī ca madhuścaiva maṇḍalabrāhmaṇameva ca  || 84.21 ||
etajjapaṃ tu ye bhaktyā yajurvedasamudbhavam  |
devavrataṃ nāma daivyaṃ puruṣāstatpuraṃ yayuḥ  || 84.22 ||
āsītpurā mahārāja andhako nāma durjaya  |
ārādhayāmāsa śivaṃ cirakālamupasthitaḥ  || 84.23 ||
prasanno bhagavān devo varaṃ yācasva suvrata  |
varaṃ labdhvā tadā daityo'dhāvatsaharṣato'ndhakaḥ  || 84.24 ||
pure janāśca dṛśyante bhājanairratnapūritaiḥ  |
sākṣatairbhājanaistasya śatasāhasrayoṣitaḥ  || 84.25 ||
mantrān paṭhanti viprāśca māṅgalyanisvanena ca  |
bhūpaṃ cāmātyabhṛtyaiśca rājyāśvarathadantibhiḥ  || 84.26 ||
vardhāpayanti te sarve ye kecitpuravāsinaḥ  |
hṛṣṭaḥ puṣṭo vasaṃstatra sa surairnābhibhūyate  || 84.27 ||
varalabdhaṃ tu taṃ jñātvā gīrvāṇāḥ śaṅkitāstadā  |
ekībhūtāśca te sarve śakrasya śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ  || 84.28 ||
samāgatān surān dṛṣṭvā śakro vacanamabravīt |
kathaṃ samāgatāssarve yūyaṃ ca tridivaukasaḥ  || 84.29 ||
kathaṃ ca bhayamutpannaṃ kathayadhvaṃ mahāsurāḥ  || 84.30 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
mṛtyuloke'bhavatpāpastvandhako nāma durmadaḥ  || 84.31 ||
tasmācca bhayamāpannā bhavaccharaṇamāgatāḥ  |
etasminnantare raudro dānavo baladārpitaḥ  || 84.32 ||
ekākī syandanārūḍha āyudhairvividhairyutaḥ  |
andhako rājaśārdūla yayau śakrapurīṃ tataḥ  || 84.33 ||
svarṇaprākārasaṃyuktāṃ śobhitāṃ vividhairgṛhaiḥ  |
durgamāṃ śatruvargasya sadā pārthivasattama  || 84.34 ||
praviveśāsurastatra līlayā svagṛhaṃ yathā  |
samutthāya tataḥ śakraḥ svakīyaṃcāsanaṃ dadau  || 84.35 ||
upaviṣṭo'ndhakastatra śakrasyaivāsane śubhe  |

śakra uvāca - 
kiṃ vo hyāgamanaṃ cātra kiṃ kāryaṃ kathayasva me  || 84.36 ||
yadasmadīyaṃ vittaṃ ca tatte dāsyāmi dānava  |

andhaka uvāca - 
na cāhaṃ kāmaye vittaṃ na gajānna turaṃgamān  || 84.37 ||
svakīyaṃ darśayasvādya svargaśṛṅgārabhūmikam  |
airāvataṃ mahānāgaṃ saindhavoccaiḥ śravohayam  || 84.38 ||
urvaśyādīni sarvāṇi vāditratritayāni ca  |
anyāḥ svīyā vibhūtīśca darśayasva śacīpate  || 84.39 ||
tasyaitadvacanaṃ śrutvā śakro'pi bhayavihvalaḥ  |
sarvāṇi ca padārthāni darśayāmāsa cāndhakam  || 84.40 ||
tadāgatya suraiḥ sārdhaṃ yakṣagandharvakinnaraiḥ  |
nṛtyantyapsarasastatra vāditrairvividhairnṛpa  || 84.41 ||
tattasya vibhramaccittaṃ dṛṣṭvāpyapsarasastadā  |
tena devagaṇāssarve trastāḥ pārthivasattama  || 84.42 ||
saṃgrāmairvividhaistatra cakravajrāribhīṣaṇaiḥ  |
santāpitāḥ surāssarve kṣayaṃ nītā hyanekaśaḥ  || 84.43 ||
āditya marutādyāśca bhagnāssaṃgrāmamaṇḍale  |
yathā siṃhakarākrāntāḥ śvāpadā vyacaran vane  || 84.44 ||
tadvadekena te devāḥ kṛtāssarve parāṅmukhāḥ  |
balāddeśeṣu grāmeṣu prajāḥ pīḍayate'niśam  || 84.45 ||
ākampya gṛhyate kṣīraṃ śākaṃ vāsastathaiva ca  |
na sammāne vacasteṣāṃ prajāsantāpane rataḥ  || 84.46 ||
gṛhītvā śakrabhāryāṃ ca dānavo'pi gṛhaṃ gataḥ  |
tataḥ surāśca śakraśca brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ  || 84.47 ||
gajaiśca parvatākārairaśvaiścaiva gajopamaiḥ  |
syandanairgaganākāraiḥ siṃhaśārdūlayojitaiḥ  || 84.48 ||
kacchapairmakaraiścāpi mṛgameṣaistathoragaiḥ  |
brahmalokamanuprāptā devāścendrapurogamāḥ  || 84.49 ||
dṛṣṭvā padmodbhavaṃ devaṃ praṇamyeśaṃ pratuṣṭuvuḥ  |
jayadeva jagannātha jayasaṃbhūti kāraka  || 84.50 ||
padmayone suraśreṣṭha tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  |
sodvegaṃ bhāṣitaṃ śrutvā devānāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 84.51 ||
meghagambhīrayā vācā brahmā provāca vāsavam  |
kiṃ vo hyāgamanaṃ devāssarveṣāṃ vai vivarṇatā  || 84.52 ||
kenāvamānitāssarve tatsarvaṃ me nivedyatām  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
anyako nāma nāmneti dānavo baladarpitaḥ  || 84.53 ||
tena devagaṇāssarve dhanaratnairvivarjitāḥ  |
hatvā devagaṇānnātha parśucakrāsitomaraiḥ  || 84.54 ||
gṛhītvā śakrabhāryāṃ vai dānavo vigato balāt |
tataḥ śrutvā vacasteṣāṃ brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  || 84.55 ||
cintayāmāsa bhagavān vadhaṃ tasya tu rakṣasaḥ  |
avadhyo dānavaḥ pāpaḥ sarvairapi surāsuraiḥ  || 84.56 ||
tataḥ pratuṣṭuvuḥ sarve devāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ  |
jaya tvaṃ deva deveśa lakṣmyā cārdhaśarīravān  || 84.57 ||
āśu rakṣaya deveśa tasmātte śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  |

janārdana uvāca - 
svāgataṃ vo mahābhāgā bruvatāṃ caiva svāgatam  || 84.58 ||
kiṃ kāryaṃ procyatāṃ sarvaṃ kāraṇaṃ yanmayepsitam  |
parābhavaḥ kṛto yena sa gacchatu yamālayam  || 84.59 ||
evamuktāssurāssarve kathayanti sma tattvataḥ  |
pradarśayanti cāṅgāni vepamānāstvadhomukhāḥ  || 84.60 ||
hṛtarājyāḥ kṛtā nātha andhakena parājitāḥ  || 84.61 ||
piteva putrān parirakṣa deva jahīha śatruṃ saha putragotraiḥ  |
tatheti coktvā kamalāsanaṃ prabhuḥ surāsurairvanditapādapīṭhaḥ  || 84.62 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'ndhakopākhyāne caturaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 85

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ pāśaṃ saṃgṛhya parameśvaraḥ  |
utthitaḥ śayanāttūrṇaṃ devānāṃ ca jayapradaḥ  || 85.1 ||

keśava uvāca - 
pātāle yadi vā svarge martye vā yadi vā surāḥ  |
andhakaṃ taṃ vadhiṣyāmi yena santāpitāssurāḥ  || 85.2 ||
gacchantu svagṛhaṃ devāssantoṣe bhāvitātmanaḥ  |
viṣṇostu vacanaṃ śrutvā brahmādyāstu savāsavāḥ  || 85.3 ||
svaṃ svaṃ yānaṃ samāruhya hṛdi tuṣṭā divaṃ yayuḥ  |
tato devo mādhavastu yatra tiṣṭhati cāndhakaḥ  || 85.4 ||
tatra gatvā hṛṣīkeśa āgneyāstraṃ mumoca ha  |
dṛṣṭvā jvalantaṃ cāgneyaṃ keśavena visarjitam  || 85.5 ||
visarjayāmāsa tadā vāruṇaṃ cāstramuttamam  |
vāruṇāstreṇa bāṇena āgneya śoṣitaṃ tadā  || 85.6 ||
andhakaścintayāmāsa kena bāṇo visarjitaḥ  |
kasyeyaṃ pauruṣī śaktiḥ ko yāsyati yamālayam  || 85.7 ||
tato'ndhakaḥ kopayukto bāṇamārgasya saṃcaran  |
dṛṣṭvā yuddhapathe prāptaṃ devaṃ taṃ cāndhako'bravīt || 85.8 ||
na śarma prāpnuṣe cātra mama dṛṣṭyā nirīkṣitaḥ  |
tathā na śaknuṣe tvaṃ tu śārdūlādgavayo hare  || 85.9 ||
āgataṃ ca yathābhakṣaṃ mārjārasya ca mūṣakam  |
tathā na śaknuṣe tvaṃ tu saṃsthito'pi mamāgrataḥ  || 85.10 ||
tatastu deva deveśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  |
caturbhujāvadāttaṃ ca dvandvayuddhapradāyinam  || 85.11 ||
dṛṣṭvā gadādharaṃ devaṃ sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato bhuvi  |

andhaka uvāca - 
jaya kṛṣṇa parastvaṃ hi viṣṇo jiṣṇo namo namaḥ  || 85.12 ||
hṛṣīkeśāya keśāya jagaddhātre'cyutāya ca  |
namaḥ paṅkajanābhāya namaḥ paṅkajamāline  || 85.13 ||
janārdanāya devāya pītāmbaradharāya ca  |
govindāya namo nityaṃ namaścodadhiśāyine  || 85.14 ||
namaḥ karālavaktrāya nṛsiṃhāya ninādine  |
śārṅgiṇe smitavaktrāya śaṅkhacakragadābhṛte  || 85.15 ||
namo vāmanarūpāya krāntalokatrayāya ca  |
namo varāharūpāya yajñarūpāya te namaḥ  || 85.16 ||
vāsudeva namastubhyaṃ namaḥ kaiṭabhanāśine  |
vasudevasutaśceśa namaste suranāyaka  || 85.17 ||
viṣṇo devādhideveśa jagaddhātaḥ prajāpate  |
praṇāmaṃ ye'pi kurvanti tebhyaścāpi namo namaḥ  || 85.18 ||
samastabhūtadevāya vāsudevāya dhīmate  |
tasmai yajñavarāhāya viṣṇave'mitatejase  || 85.19 ||
guṇānāṃ hi vidhānāya namaste'stu punaḥ punaḥ  |

deva uvāca - 
tuṣṭo hyahaṃ dānavendra varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 85.20 ||
dadāmi te varaṃ cādya yācamānasya sāmpratam  |

andhaka uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātumihecchasi  || 85.21 ||
tadā dadasva me deva yuddhaṃ paramaśobhanam  |

śrībhagavānuvāca - 
kathaṃ dadāmi te yuddhaṃ toṣito'haṃ tvayā punaḥ  || 85.22 ||
na tvāṃ prati bhavetkopaḥ kathaṃ yudhye'hamandhaka  |
yadi te vartate buddhiryuddhaṃ prati na saṃśayaḥ  || 85.23 ||
tarhi tvaṃ gaccha śīghraṃ vai devaṃ prati maheśvaram  |

andhaka uvāca - 
prasādāttasya devasya vijayī bhuvanatraye  || 85.24 ||
kathaṃ yuddhaṃ care tena śaṅkareṇa vadasva naḥ  |
etacchrutvā dānavasya bhagavānabravīdidam  || 85.25 ||
ahaṃ te kathayiṣyāmi yena yuddhaṃ tvayā saha  |
kailāsaśikharaṃ gatvā dhunu taṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 85.26 ||
dhunite parvate tasmin kampitaṃ bhuvanatrayam  |
patanti śikharāgrāṇi śīryamāṇānyanekaśaḥ  || 85.27 ||
catvāraḥ sāgarā rājannekībhūtāḥ samantataḥ  |
umayā sahito rudro viṣayāsaktacetanaḥ  || 85.28 ||
kampamānaśca patitaḥ pārvatyā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
gāḍhamāliṅgya deveśamumā vacanamabravīt || 85.29 ||
kimarthaṃ kampate śailaḥ kathaṃ vai kampate dharā  |
pātālāni tu saptaiva kampate svargasaptakam  || 85.30 ||
kiṃ vā yugakṣayo deva tanmamākhyātumarhasi  |

maheśvara uvāca - 
kasyaiṣā durmatirjātā'pi pārśvacarasya nu  || 85.31 ||
lalāṭe cedayaṃ bhagnaḥ prayāsyati yamālayam  |
kailāse saṃsthito dhyāne supto'haṃ pratibodhitaḥ  || 85.32 ||
vadhiṣye taṃ na sandehaḥ ṣaṇmukho vā bhavedyadi  |
tataḥ sa cintayāmāsa jānātītyandhako'pyayam  || 85.33 ||
upāyaṃ cintayāmāsa yenāsau vadhyate kṣaṇāt |
tatassamāgatā devā indrabrahmapurogamāḥ  || 85.34 ||
rathaṃ devamayaṃ kṛtvā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam  |
keciddevāḥ sthitāścakre kecittuṇḍāgrasaṃsthitāḥ  || 85.35 ||
kecidakṣe sthitā rājan yugaraśmiṣu saṃsthitāḥ  |
rathastambhe dhvajāgre tu kecidanyatra saṃsthitāḥ  || 85.36 ||
evaṃ devamayaṃ kṛtvā samārūḍho jagatprabhuḥ  |
niryayau dānavo yatra krodhenā'pi maheśvaraḥ  || 85.37 ||
dānavānardayāmāsa ākāśaṃ cāśumāniva  |
na tatra dṛśyate sūryo na kāṣṭhā na ca candramāḥ  || 85.38 ||
tato dānavarājena āgneyāstraṃ suyojitam  |
dahyamānaṃ śaraistatra sarvaṃ gīrvāṇamaṇḍalam  || 85.39 ||
dahyamānāḥ śaraiścaivaṃ devaṃ śaraṇamāyayuḥ  |
tato devādhidevena vāruṇāstraṃ visarjitam  || 85.40 ||
vāruṇāstreṇa tenaiva āgneyāstraṃ praśāmitam  |
dānavena tato muktaṃ vāyavyāstraṃ nṛpottama  || 85.41 ||
pannagāstraṃ ca devo'pi kopāviṣṭaḥ pramuktavān  |
māruto bhakṣitaḥ sarpaiḥ krodhāviṣṭairna saṃśayaḥ  || 85.42 ||
dānavena tadā muktaṃ garuḍāstraṃ balīyasā  |
tena tacchatadhā nītaṃ pannagāstraṃ na dṛśyate  || 85.43 ||
tato devādhidevena nārasiṃhaṃ visarjitam  |
astrairastrāṇi saṃvārya yudhyete ca parasparam  || 85.44 ||
samaṃ yuddhamabhūttāta surāsurabhayaṅkaram  |
cakreṇālīkanārācaistomaraiḥ khaḍgamudgaraiḥ  || 85.45 ||
vatsadantaistathā bhallaiḥ karṇikāraiśca śobhanaiḥ  |
evaṃ na śakyate hantuṃ dānavairvividhāyudhaiḥ  || 85.46 ||
tato daṃṣṭrākarālena khaḍganārācatomaraiḥ  |
śvaśrūn dṛṣṭvā yathā yāti lajjamānā hyadhomukhī  || 85.47 ||
na saṃspṛśanti gātrāṇi śastā gauḍavadhūryathā  |
āyudhāni tatastyaktvā bāhuyuddhamupasthitau  || 85.48 ||
karaiḥ karāṃstu saṃgṛhya praharantau hi muṣṭibhiḥ  |
bandhaiḥ karaprahārādyairyudhyete sma parasparam  || 85.49 ||
dānavo'pi ca taṃ devaṃ kakṣāntaramapīḍayat |
niśceṣṭaśca tadā devo mūrchitastu maheśvaraḥ  || 85.50 ||
mūrcchāgataṃ tu taṃ jñātvā cintayāmāsa dānavaḥ  |
hā hā kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ vādya pāpena ca durātmanā  || 85.51 ||
kiṃ nu kāryaṃ mayā cātra kathaṃ vāpi vrajāmyaham  |
taṃ gṛhītvātha deveśaṃ gataḥ kailāsaparvatam  || 85.52 ||
muktvā śayānammuccetamandhako'pi yayau kṣaṇāt |
tatassa cetano bhūtvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 85.53 ||
yāvatpaśyati cātmānaṃ svakīye bhavane sthitam  |
tāvatsa cintayāmāsa parābhūto durātmanā  || 85.54 ||
krodhavegasamāviṣṭo niryayau dānavaṃ prati  |
āyasaṃ laguḍaṃ dhṛtvā prabhurbhārasahasrakam  || 85.55 ||
dānavaṃ dṛṣṭavān devaḥ prākṣipattasya mūrdhani  |
khaḍgena tāḍayāmāsa dānavaḥ prahasan raṇe  || 85.56 ||
gṛhītvā deva deveśaḥ kauveraṃ bāṇamuttamam  |
hṛdaye tāḍayāmāsa jvalitena ca tatkṣaṇāt || 85.57 ||
tatassa dānavastatra rudhirodgāramudgiran  |
adhomukhastato bhūtvā śūlena vidalīkṛtaḥ  || 85.58 ||
śūlāgravikṣataḥ pāpaścakravadbhramate tadā  |
ye tu bhūmau patanti sma dehato raktabindavaḥ  || 85.59 ||
tebhya udabhavan pāpā dānavāḥ śastrapāṇayaḥ  |
vyākulaśca tato devo dānavaiśca punaḥ punaḥ  || 85.60 ||
devena saṃsmṛtā durgā cāmuṇḍā bhīṣaṇā tadā  |
āgatā bhīṣaṇā devī āyudhāyutasaṃyutā  || 85.61 ||
mahādaṃṣṭrā mahākāyā piṅgākṣī lambakarṇikā  |
uvāca devī deveśaṃ samādiśa maheśvara  || 85.62 ||

deva uvāca - 
piba tvaṃ rudhiraṃ bhadre yatheṣṭaṃ dānavasya ca  |
patitaṃ ca pṛthivyāṃ tu durge yatnādgṛhāṇa tat || 85.63 ||
dānavasya vadhe cādya sahāyaṃ kartumarhasi  |
tato hatāśca te sarve khaḍgenāpi sahasraśaḥ  || 85.64 ||
andhako'pi ca tān dṛṣṭvā dānavānnidhanaṃ gatān  |
tato vāgbhiḥ supuṣṭābhiḥ stuvan devaṃ maheśvaram  || 85.65 ||
tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti deveśaṃ caṇḍīṃ prati mahābalaḥ  |
śūlavikṣatarandhreṇa raktaṃ vai srāvayan bahu  || 85.66 ||
pṛthivīṃ pūrayāmāsa catuḥ sāgaramekhalām  |
antarikṣe sthitenāpi śūlāgre saṃsthitena ca  || 85.67 ||
skandhe dhṛtena devena rudhiraughapravarṣiṇā  |
pṛthivī pūritā tena saśailavanakānanā  || 85.68 ||
rudhireṇa kaṭiṃ yāvadvārito'pi maheśvaraḥ  |
tato hṛdayaparyantaṃ devasya ca samāgamat || 85.69 ||
vyākulāśca tato devāḥ praṇaṣṭāśca diśaṃ gatāḥ  |
sa tu svasya śarīrasya aṅgānyaṣṭau vyamardayat || 85.70 ||
aṣṭau bhairavarūpāśca samutpannā maheśvarāt |
daṃṣṭrākarālinaḥ sarve hāhākāraṃ prakurvataḥ  || 85.71 ||
kharparāgrakarāssarve khaḍginaḥ kartinastathā  |
pibantu rudhiraṃ sarvamityāha parameśvara  || 85.72 ||
pītaṃ tu taiśca rudhiraṃ kṣīṇaṃ raktaṃ sthitaṃ sthalam  |
śarīraṃ śoṣitaṃ tasya asthicarmāvaśeṣitam  || 85.73 ||
dānavaścāndhakaḥ prāha antarikṣacarastathā  |

andhaka uvāca - 
jayadeva jagannātha umāṅgārdhaśarīrabhṛt || 85.74 ||
vṛṣabhāsanamārūḍha śaśāṅkakṛtaśekhara  |
jayakhaṭvāṅgahastāya gaṅgāṃ śirasi dhāriṇe  || 85.75 ||
smarapramathanāyeha īśvarāya namo'stu te  |
pūṣṇodantavināśāya gaṇanātha namo namaḥ  || 85.76 ||
jaya surūpadehāya arūpāya namo namaḥ  |
brahmottamāṅganāśāya viśveśvara sanātana  || 85.77 ||
śmaśānavāsine nityaṃ nityaṃ bhairavarūpiṇe  |
tvaṃ sarvagaśca kartā tvaṃ tvaṃ hartā nānya eva ca  || 85.78 ||
tvaṃ bhūmistvaṃ diśaścaiva jyotistvaṃ tejasastathā  |
tvaṃ vāyuḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ janturūpo maheśvaraḥ  || 85.79 ||
tvaṃ somastvaṃ budhaścaiva tvaṃ gururbhārgavastathā  |
sauristvaṃ devadeveśa bhūmiputrastathaiva ca  || 85.80 ||
ṛkṣāṇi yāni dṛśyante gagane śaśibhāskarau  |
etānyeva ca sarvāṇi tvatprasādānmaheśvara  || 85.81 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ stutvā devadevaṃ sa dānavaḥ  |
saṃhatābhyāṃ ca hastābhyāṃ taṃ praṇamya maheśvaram  || 85.82 ||

śaṅkara uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahāsattva varaṃ yācasva dānava  |
dātāhaṃ yācakastvaṃ tu dadāmīti yathepsitam  || 85.83 ||

andhaka uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
tadātmanaḥ samīpe'haṃ sthāpitavyo hi nānyathā  || 85.84 ||
bhasmī jaṭī triśūlī ca trinetrī ca caturbhujaḥ  |
vyāghracarmottarīyaśca nāgayajñopavītakaḥ  || 85.85 ||
etadicchāmyahaṃ sarvaṃ yadi dāsyasi śaṅkara  |
śūlāgrastho vadadyāvattāvattuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 85.86 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
dadāmi te varaṃ cādya yastvayā paribhāṣitaḥ  |
mayā tvamuditaḥ pūrvaṃ bhṛṅgirīṭirbhaviṣyasi  || 85.87 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'ndhakavarapradāno nāma pañcāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 86

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
andhakasya varaṃ dattvā tenaiva saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
umayā sahitaścāpi kailāsaṃ parvataṃ gataḥ  || 86.1 ||
tatassamāgatā devā brahmādyāssaha vāsavāḥ  |
hṛṣṭapuṣṭāśca te sarve maheśa taṃ praṇemire  || 86.2 ||

deva uvāca - 
svāgataṃ vo mahābhāgā ye kecittviha cāgatāḥ  |
nihato dānavastatra bhavadarthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 86.3 ||
raktena tasya me śūlaṃ nirmalaṃ ca na dṛśyate  |
kartavyaṃ kiṃ mayā cādya kathyatāṃ hi pitāmaha  || 86.4 ||
sutastu bhavato brahman yaścā'sau nihato mayā  |
kartumicchāmyahaṃ samyaktīrthayātrāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 86.5 ||
utthāya gamyatāṃ sarve ye kecittviha cāgatāḥ  |
tatassarvaiḥ suraiḥ sārdhaṃ prabhāsaṃ prati niryayau  || 86.6 ||
prabhāsādyāni tīrthāni gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  |
avagāhya tu sarvāṇi nirmalatvaṃ na vidyate  || 86.7 ||
nīlībhūtaṃ yathāvastraṃ sitatvaṃ naiva gacchati  |
tathā kṛṣṇatriśūlasya nirmalatvaṃ na jāyate  || 86.8 ||
narmadā tu tato gatvā devo devaiḥ samanvitaḥ  |
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kūlamavagāhya prayatnataḥ  || 86.9 ||
gatvā tu dakṣiṇe kūle parvate bhṛgusaṃjñite  |
tatra sthitvā mahādevo devaiḥ saha dharāpate  || 86.10 ||
manoharaṃ tu tatsthānaṃ sarveṣāṃ hi divaukasām  |
jñātvā tīrthaviśeṣaṃ tu sthito devo maheśvaraḥ  || 86.11 ||
giriṃ bibheda śūlena tena bhinnaṃ rasātalam  |
nirmalaṃ cābhavacchūlaṃ na lepo dṛśyate kvacit || 86.12 ||
pātālānniḥsṛtā gaṅgā nāmnā bhogavatīti sā  |
tatra tīrthaṃ samutpannaṃ śūlabhedeti viśrutam  || 86.13 ||
sūrye rāhugate tatra mahāpuṇyā sarasvatī  |
dvitīyaṃ saṅgamaṃ tatra yathā veṇī sitāsitam  || 86.14 ||
tatra brahmā svayaṃ devo brahmeśaṃ liṅgamuttamam  |
yasya yāmyadiśābhāge svayaṃ devo janārdanaḥ  || 86.15 ||
vidyate ca svayaṃ tatra viṣṇuḥ pīṭheṣu saṃsthitaḥ  |
śūlena ca kṛtā rekhā tatra toyavahā nṛpa  || 86.16 ||
tattoyaṃ ca gataṃ tatra yatra revā nadījalam  |
tatra liṅgaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ cakratīrtheti viśrutam  || 86.17 ||
śūlabhede ca deveśaḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā yathā vidhi  |
ātmānaṃ manyate śuddhaṃ na kiṃcitkaluṣaṃ tanau  || 86.18 ||
tasya caivottare bhāge deva devaṃ jagadgurum  |
śūlapāṇiṃ tataḥ prāpya pūjayāmāsa yatnataḥ  || 86.19 ||
sarvatīrthamayaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthādhikaṃ param  |
sarvapuṇyādhikaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvadruḥkhaghnamuttamam  || 86.20 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya devadevo jagadguruḥ  |
rakṣakāṃstu tato muktvā tatra sthāpya vināyakam  || 86.21 ||
kṣetrapālaśataṃ cāṣṭau tīrthaṃ rakṣanti yatnataḥ  |
vighnāni tasya kurvanti yastatra sthātumicchati  || 86.22 ||
kecitkuṭumbacintāṃ tu keciccintāṃ kṛṣīṣu ca  |
sevāṃ ca kurvate keciddravyārjanaparāyaṇāḥ  || 86.23 ||
parokṣavādaṃ kurvanti anye hisāratā janāḥ  |
paradārān prasarpanti anye ca vittacintakāḥ  || 86.24 ||
anye'pi ca vadantyevaṃ kathaṃ tīrtheṣu gamyate  |
kṣudhayā pīḍyate bhāryā hyapatyāni tathaiva ca  || 86.25 ||
mohajāle nipatitāḥ pāpācārāśca ye narāḥ  |
tebhyo rakṣanti tattīrthaṃ devasya ca gaṇāḥ śubham  || 86.26 ||
puṇyā janāḥ sthirā ye ca snānaṃ teṣāṃ ca jāyate  |
payoṣṇyāṃ devanadyāṃ ca bhogavatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 86.27 ||
etacca saṅgamaṃ puṇyaṃ yathā veṇyāṃ sitāsite  |
dṛṣṭvā tīrthaṃ tu te sarve gīrvāṇā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ  || 86.28 ||
devasya sannidho bhūtvā varṇayanti parasparam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ ca deveśa gayātīrthasamaṃ viduḥ  || 86.29 ||
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
śūlapāṇiṃ samabhyarcya brahmādyāśca suraiḥ saha  || 86.30 ||
ye gaṇāścaiva gandharvā yamo varuṇavāsavau  |
nṛtyagītaistathā stotraiḥ sarve caiva surāsurāḥ  || 86.31 ||
devena bhodito yatra śūlāgreṇa nṛpottama  |
trayo gartāstu saṃjātāstoyapūrṇā narādhipa  || 86.32 ||
āryāvartā naraśreṣṭha mahākuliśalāñchitāḥ  |
sarvapāpakṣayakarāḥ sarvaduḥkhāpahārakāḥ  || 86.33 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
dīkṣāmantravihīno'pi mucyate bhavabandhanāt || 86.34 ||
yaḥ punarvidhivatsnātvā mantraiḥ pañcabhireva ca  |
vedoktaiḥ pañcabhirmantraiḥ sahiraṇyairghaṭaistathā  || 86.35 ||
akṣarairdaśabhiścaiva pañcākṣaraistribhistathā  |
pṛthagbhūtairdvijātīnāṃ tīrthaṃ śastaṃ narādhipa  || 86.36 ||
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ vāpi śūdrasyātha striyāstathā  |
dhyātvā devatrayaṃ rājan snānaṃ caiva yathāvidhi  || 86.37 ||
daśākṣareṇa mantreṇa toyaṃ pibati yo naraḥ  |
kedāre ca yathā pītaṃ tathā kuṇḍe na saṃśayaḥ  || 86.38 ||
pañcarephasamāyuktaṃ kṣakārākṣarabhūṣitam  |
oṃkāradvayasaṃyuktametadatrānukīrtanam  || 86.39 ||
yastatra kurute snānaṃ vidhiyukto jitendriya  |
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 86.40 ||
kulaṃ tārayate viṃśaddaśapūrvān daśāparān  |
gaṅgāyāṃ pañcatīrthe ca śrāddhaṃ vai kurute tu yaḥ  || 86.41 ||
sa tatra phalamāpnoti śūlabhede na saṃśayaḥ  |
yastatra vidhinā yukto dānaṃ dadyācca bhaktitaḥ  || 86.42 ||
tadakṣayaṃ phalaṃ tatra kṛtasya sukṛto'thavā  |
gayākṣetre yathāpuṇyaṃ sarvakāryeṣu caiva hi  || 86.43 ||
śūlabhede tathā puṇyaṃ snānadānāditarpaṇaiḥ  |
bhaktyā ca yo dadātyatra kāṃcanaṃ gāṃ mahīṃ jalam  || 86.44 ||
annaṃ kṛṣībhavaṃ śayyāṃ vāsāṃsi bhūṣaṇāni ca  |
annādibhirdhanaiścaiva gṛhaṃ pūrṇaṃ ca sarvataḥ  || 86.45 ||
yugyayuglāṅgalaṃ mukhyaṃ navaṃ caiva dhurandharau  |
dānānyetāni yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage  || 86.46 ||
śrotriyaṃ ca kulīnaṃ ca śuciṃ ca vijitendriyam  |
jñātvā dānaṃ ca yo dadyāttasyānto naiva vidyate  || 86.47 ||
trayodaśadineṣvekaṃ trayodaśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 86.48 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedotpattirnāma ṣaḍaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 87

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
uttānapādo rājendra pṛcchati sma maheśvaram  |
siddhāśca kīdṛśā deva apūjyāścaiva kīdṛśāḥ  || 87.1 ||
śrāddhe caivāhnike yajñe dāne caiva viśeṣataḥ  |
etadākhyāhi me deva kasya dānaṃ na dīyate  || 87.2 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ  |
brāhmaṇaścānadhīyānastrayaste nāmadhārakāḥ  || 87.3 ||
rogī hīnātiriktāṅgaḥ kāṇaḥ paunarbhavastathā  |
avakīrṇaḥ kuṇḍagolau śrāddhe dāne na śuddhyati  || 87.4 ||
māhiṣyo vṛṣalaḥ steno vārdhakyo'tha viśeṣataḥ  |
ete viprāḥ sadā tyājyāḥ paścānmānaṃ praśaṃsati  || 87.5 ||
pratigrahaṃ tu gṛhṇāti kālajñānaṃ vinā dvijaḥ  |
tasya dānaṃ na dātavyaṃ vṛthā bhavati niṣphalam  || 87.6 ||
daridrān dehi rājaṃstvaṃ mā samṛddhān kadācana  |
vyādhitasyauṣadhaṃ pathyaṃ nīrujasya kimauṣadham  || 87.7 ||

uttānapāda uvāca - 
vidhiśca kīdṛśī deva kathaṃ śrāddhasya ca kriyā  |
dānaṃ ca dīyate yena tanmamākhyātumarhasi  || 87.8 ||

deva uvāca - 
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā gṛhe bhaktyā susnāto vijitendriyaḥ  |
vāgyatastarpayettāvadyāvatsaṃkhyāmalaṅghayan  || 87.9 ||
śūlabhedaṃ tato gatvā snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
pañcasthāneṣu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ havyakavyādibhiścaret || 87.10 ||
piṇḍadānaṃ ca ya kuryātpāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  |
tasya tatphalamāpnoti tasmiṃstīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 87.11 ||
upānahau ca yo dadyādbrāhmaṇebhyo viśeṣataḥ  |
gacchedvimānamārūḍhastvamaraiḥ parivāritaḥ  || 87.12 ||
uttamaṃ ca gṛhaṃ dadyātsaptadhānyaiśca pūritam  |
sa svargaloke vasati kāñcane bhavanottame  || 87.13 ||
tiladhenuṃ ca yo dadyātsavatsāṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
nākapṛṣṭhe vasettāvadyāvadāhūtasaṃplavam  || 87.14 ||
gṛhe vā yadi vāraṇye tīrthe vā kupatheṣu ca  |
toyamannaṃ ca yo dadyādyamalokaṃ na paśyati  || 87.15 ||
akṣayaṃ cānnadānaṃ ca toyabhūmistathaiva ca  |
annadānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 87.16 ||

uttānapāda uvāca - 
kanyādānaṃ kathaṃ deva kartavyaṃ kathayasva tat |
pratigrahaṃ tathā toṣyaṃ kanyodvāhamupaskaram  || 87.17 ||
dātavyaṃ kasya vai dānaṃ dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  |
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ vāpi kanīyāṃsaṃ kathaṃcana  || 87.18 ||
rājasaṃ tāmasaṃ vāpi niḥśreyasamathāpi vā  |

īśvara uvāca - 
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ kanyādānaṃ viśiṣyate  || 87.19 ||
yo dadyātparayā bhaktyā abhigamya ca yatnataḥ  |
kulīnasya svarūpasya guṇajñasya viśeṣataḥ  || 87.20 ||
sulagne ca muhūrte ca dadyātkanyāmalaṃkṛtām  |
aśvānnāgāṃśca vāsāṃsi yo dadyāccaiva bhaktitaḥ  || 87.21 ||
tasya vāso bhavettatra padaṃ yatra nirāmayam  |
yena sā duhitā dattā prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasī  || 87.22 ||
tena sarvamidaṃ dattaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
dhanaṃ kanyārthataḥ kalpyo na rocayati durmatiḥ  || 87.23 ||
sa bhavetkarmacāṇḍālaḥ kośakāro bhavenmṛtaḥ  |
kanyārthaṃ yācate yastu sa dhanī nirdhano'pi vā  || 87.24 ||
abhojyo bhavate martyaḥ sarvavastuṣu varjitaḥ  |
gṛhe tasya ca yo'śnīyājjihvālampaṭako nṛpa  || 87.25 ||
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhaḥ syāttaptakṛcchramathāpi vā  |

rājovāca - 
vittaṃ na vidyate yasya kanyodvāhe'vatiṣṭhati  || 87.26 ||
kathaṃ codvāhanaṃ kuryādetadācakṣva me prabho  |

deva uvāca - 
svavittenānukartavyaṃ kanyodvāhanameva ca  || 87.27 ||
kanyā nāma samuccārya na doṣo yācakasya ca  |
abhigamyottamaṃ dānamāhūtaṃ caiva madhyamam  || 87.28 ||
adhamaṃ procyamānaṃ tu sevādānaṃ ca niṣphalam  |
asamarthe na dātavyaṃ kanyādānaṃ tathaiva ca  || 87.29 ||
samarthastārayedvidvān kāṣṭhaṃ kṣiptaṃ yathā jale  |
yathā naukā tathā vidvāṃstārayetparamaṃ taṭam  || 87.30 ||
āhitāgnistu yo bhūtvā gṛhnañśūdrapratigraham  |
iha janmani śūdratvaṃ mṛtaḥ śvā copajāyate  || 87.31 ||
vṛthā kleśāśca jāyante brāhmaṇasyāgnihotriṇaḥ  |
asatpratigrahaṃ gṛhṇannāpadaṃ ca vinā dvijaḥ  || 87.32 ||
tatsarvaṃ nāśayettasya bhinnā naukā yathāmbhasi  |
atikleśavaśārjitaṃ vināśayati tatkṣaṇāt || 87.33 ||
evaṃ duḥkhārjitaṃ puṇyaṃ śūdre gacchati nānyathā  |
lakṣadākṣiṇyalābhāya pradānaṃ cāparādhakam  || 87.34 ||
kīrtipātreṣu yaddattaṃ vṛthā bhavati pārthiva  || 87.35 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dānamahimānuvarṇanaṃ nāma saptāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 88

uttānapāda uvāca - 
kasmin kāle ca kriyate śrāddhaṃ dānaṃ ca śaṅkara  |
tīrthayātrā kathaṃ kāryā ātithyaṃ kathayasva naḥ  || 88.1 ||

śaṅkara uvāca - 
piturarthaṃ yathā puṇyaṃ sārvakālikamuttamam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ snānadānāditarpaṇaiḥ  || 88.2 ||
viśeṣeṇa ca kurvanti śrāddhaṃ caturyugādiṣu  |
manvantarādayo vatsa śrūyatāṃ ca caturdaśa  || 88.3 ||
āśvine navamī śuklā dvādaśī kārtikasya ca  |
tṛtīyā caitramāse tu tathā bhādrapadasya ca  || 88.4 ||
āṣāḍhadaśamī tathā māghasyaiva casaptamī  |
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhī tu pūrṇimā  || 88.5 ||
phālgunasya amāvāsyā pauṣasyaikādaśī śubhā  |
kārttikī phālgunī caitrī jyaiṣṭhī pañcadaśī sitā  || 88.6 ||
manvantarādayaścaiva hyanantaphaladāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
ayane tūttare caiva dakṣiṇe ca tathaiva hi  || 88.7 ||
kārttikyāṃ ca tathā mādhyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ ca tṛtīyayā  |
caitryāṃ caiva tathā ṣaṣṭhyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ tathaiva ca  || 88.8 ||
śrāddhakālāśca te sarve dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  |
madhumāse site pakṣa ekādaśyāmupoṣitaḥ  || 88.9 ||
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ kuryādviṣṇoḥ padasamīpataḥ  |
dadyāddānaṃ tathā śaktyā hiraṇyaṃ go'mbarāṇi ca  || 88.10 ||
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ srakpuṣpacandanāni ca  |
arcāṃ karoti yo viṣṇoḥ kathāṃ paurāṇakīrtanam  || 88.11 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvāṇāṃ sūktaṃ tajjapati dvijaḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  || 88.12 ||
prabhāte kurute śrāddhaṃ dvijān bhojya prayatnataḥ  |
dadeddānaṃ yathā śaktyā hiraṇyaṃ go'mbarāṇi ca  || 88.13 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadāhūtasaṃplavam  |
śrāddhadaśca vasettatra yatra devo janārdanaḥ  || 88.14 ||
trayodaśyāṃ tato gacchedguhāvāsīti tiṣṭhati  |
dṛṣṭvā mārkaṇḍamīśānaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 88.15 ||

uttānapāda uvāca - 
guhāmadhye yathā deva liṅgaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
pratiṣṭhā yena devasya tanmamākhyātumarhasi  || 88.16 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ mārkaṇḍeśvarasaṃjñikam  |
bṛhadrathāntaraṃ yacca sāmavedaṃ dvijottamaḥ  || 88.17 ||
atharvātharvaśīrṣāṇi tathā hṛcca vṛṣākapim  |
śivasaṃkalpitaṃ japtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 88.18 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
pādaśaucaṃ tathā tasya kurvate ye ca bhaktitaḥ  || 88.19 ||
godānenaiva yatpuṇyaṃ labhante nā'tra saṃśayaḥ  |
brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra pāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  || 88.20 ||
ekena bhojitenāpi sahasraṃ tena bhojitam  |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ dadyādbhaktyā dvijātiṣu  || 88.21 ||
tena tṛpyanti te devā manuṣyāḥ pitarastathā  |
candrasūryagrahe bhaktyā snānaṃ kurvanti ye narāḥ  || 88.22 ||
devārcanaṃ ca yaḥ kuryājjapaṃ homaṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
dahyāddānaṃ yathāśaktyā brāhmaṇe vedapārage  || 88.23 ||
aśvaratnaṃ gajaratnaṃ tūlāpuruṣameva ca  |
yo dadyācchakaṭaṃ tatra saptadhānyaprapūritam  || 88.24 ||
yuktaṃ ca lāṅgalaṃ dadyādyuvānau tu dhurandharau  |
gobhūtilahiraṇyaṃ ca pātre dātavyamīpsitam  || 88.25 ||
apātre viduṣā kiṃcitna deyaṃ śreya icchatā  |
sarvabhūtāni cātmaiva yato dhārayate mahī  || 88.26 ||
tato viprāya sā deyā sarvasasyānuśālinī  |
anyacca śṛṇu rājendra godānasya ca yatphalam  || 88.27 ||
yāvadvatsasya pādau dvau mukhaṃ yonyāṃ ca dṛśyate  |
tāvadgauḥ pṛthivī jñeyā yāvadgarbhaṃ na muñcati  || 88.28 ||
yena kenāpyupāyena brāhmaṇāya samarpayet |
pṛthvī dattā bhavettena saśailavanakānanā  || 88.29 ||
tārayantī ca sā dattā kulānāmekaviṃśatim  |
raupyakhurīṃ kāṃsyadohāṃ savatsāṃ ca payasvinīm  || 88.30 ||
prayacchanti janāḥ puṇyā rāhugraste niśākare  |
sarvasyaiva tu dānasya saṃkhyā cāsti narādhipa  || 88.31 ||
candrasūryoparāge ca dānasaṃkhyā na vidyate  |
yatra gāvaḥ pradṛśyante sarvatīrthāni tatra vai  || 88.32 ||
tatra yajñaṃ vijānīyātnātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
punaḥ smṛtvā tu tattīrthaṃ gamanaṃ kurute naraḥ  || 88.33 ||
athavā śrūyate yastu rudrasyānucaro bhavet || 88.34 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamahimānukathanaṃ nāmāṣṭāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 89

īśvara uvāca - 
anyaccākhyānakaṃ vakṣye purāvṛttaṃ narādhipa  |
sakuṭumbo gataḥ svargamṛṣirdīrghatapā mahān  || 89.1 ||

śaṅkara uvāca - 
kāśirājeti vikhyātaścitraseno mahābalaḥ  |
tasya puryāṃ sa vasate sarvakāmasamanvitaḥ  || 89.2 ||
sā purī janasampūrṇā nānāratnopaśobhitā  |
vārāṇāsīti vikhyātā gaṅgātīre samāśritā  || 89.3 ||
indraprasthasamaprakhyā gaurīgokulasaṃyutā  |
bahudvijasamākīrṇā vedadhvanitaniḥsvanā  || 89.4 ||
vaṇigjanairbahuvidhaiḥ krayavikrayasaṃyutaiḥ  |
aṭṭāṭṭālaiḥ pratolībhirutsavādyaistu maṇḍitā  || 89.5 ||
devatāyatanairdivyairārāmairupaśobhitā  |
nānāpuṣpaphalairramyaiḥ kadalīṣaṇḍamaṇḍitā  || 89.6 ||
tasyā uttaradigbhāge ārāmaścottamaḥ śubhaḥ  |
samandāravanaṃ nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 89.7 ||
nānādrumalatākīrṇaṃ nānāpuṣpopaśobhitam  |
bahumandārasaṃyuktaṃ tena mandārakaṃ vanam  || 89.8 ||
vipro dīrghatapā nāma sarvadā tatra tiṣṭhati  |
tapastapati so'tyarthaṃ tena dīrghatapāḥ smṛtaḥ  || 89.9 ||
sa tiṣṭhate sapatnī kastiṣṭhate putrasaṃyutaḥ  |
śuśrūṣayanti taṃ sarve sutāḥ pañca samīpagāḥ  || 89.10 ||
tasya putraḥ kanīyāṃstu ṛṣyaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  |
vedādhyayanasaṃyukto brahmacārī guṇānvitaḥ  || 89.11 ||
yogābhyāsarato nityaṃ kandamūlaphalāśanaḥ  |
tiṣṭhate mṛgarūpeṇa mṛgamadhye vasan sadā  || 89.12 ||
dinārambhe dinānte ca mātāpitragrataḥ sthitaḥ  |
abhivādayate nityaṃ bhāktamānṛṣiputrakaḥ  || 89.13 ||
punarjagāma tatraiva kānane girigahvare  |
krīḍan bālamṛgaiḥ sārdhaṃ rājabāṇamṛtastu saḥ  || 89.14 ||

rājovāca - 
āśrame vasatastatra sudīrghatapasastadā  |
sūnustasya kanīyāṃstu kathaṃ mṛtyuvaśaṃ gataḥ  || 89.15 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca - 
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā kathāṃ citrāṃ mahīpate  |
śravaṇādeva tasyā hi sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 89.16 ||
kāśirājo mahārājā mahābalaparākramaḥ  |
citrasena iti khyāto vārāṇasyāṃ vasatyasau  || 89.17 ||
evaṃ vasaṃstatra rājye mantriṇo vākyamabravīt |
mṛgayā ca gamiṣyāmi yūyaṃ rājye pratiṣṭhitāḥ  || 89.18 ||
gamyatāṃ mantribhiḥ prokto gato'sau vasudhādhipaḥ  || 89.19 ||
chatraiśchatrāṇi dṛśyante gacchantaṃ kānanaṃ prati  |
rajastatrotthitaṃ bhūri gajavājipadāhatam  || 89.20 ||
tenaivācchāditaṃ sarvaṃ sādityaṃ bhūmimaṇḍalam  |
na tatra dṛśyate sūryo na kāṣṭhā na ca candramāḥ  || 89.21 ||
pādapāśca na dṛśyante girisātūni sarvaśaḥ  |
tatrāpi ca mahārāja mṛgayūthamadṛśyata  || 89.22 ||
adhāvan puruṣāḥ sarve sarājārājaputrakāḥ  |
vṛndalopo'bhavatteṣāṃ śīghraṃ jagmurdiśo daśa  || 89.23 ||
ekamārgaṃ gato rājā citraseno mahīpatiḥ  |
ekākī sa gatastatra yatra yatra ca te mṛgāḥ  || 89.24 ||
praviṣṭastu tato durge kānane pakṣivarjite  |
valmīgulmalatākīrṇe praviṣṭo naiva dṛśyate  || 89.25 ||
ekākyapaśyadātmānaṃ na cāśvaṃ na padātikān  |
na ko'pi cātra jānāti nāhaṃ vedmi diśo daśa  || 89.26 ||
evaṃ kaṣṭaṃ gato rājā citraseno narādhipaḥ  |
chāyāṃ samāśritastatra viśramya ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 89.27 ||
kṣuttṛṣārto bhraman durge kānane girigahvare  |
tato'paśyatsaro divyaṃ padminīṣaṇḍamaṇḍitam  || 89.28 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ cakravākopakūjitam  |
saro dṛṣṭvā tu rājendraḥ saṃprahṛṣṭatanūruhaḥ  || 89.29 ||
kumudāni gṛhītvā tu tatra snānaṃ samācarat |
tarpayitvā pitṝn devānmanuṣyāṃśca yathā vidhi  || 89.30 ||
papau pānīyamamalaṃ yathāvatsamabhīpsitam  |
uttīrya sa jalāttīre dṛṣṭvā vṛkṣaṃ samīpataḥ  || 89.31 ||
cintayā'nupaviṣṭo'sau kiṃ tu karma karomyaham  |
tataśchāyāśritān paśyan vanoddeśe mṛgān bahūn  || 89.32 ||
kecitpūrvamukhāstatra apare dakṣiṇāmukhā  |
vāruṇyabhimukhāḥ kecitkecitkauberamāśritāḥ  || 89.33 ||
kecinnidrāṃ prakurvanti ūrdhvakarṇāḥ sthitāḥ pare  |
mṛgamadhye sthito yogī ṛṣyaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 89.34 ||
mṛgān dṛṣṭvā tato rājā prahārārthamacintayat |
vadhitvā ca mṛgaṃ caikaṃ bhakṣayāmi yadṛcchayā  || 89.35 ||
svasthāvastho bhaviṣyāmi mṛgamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt |
kāśīṃ prati gamiṣyāmi mārgamanveṣayaṃstataḥ  || 89.36 ||
vicintyaivaṃ tato rājā vṛkṣamūlaṃ samāśritaḥ  |
cāpaṃ dhṛtvā karāgreṇa prākṣipattaccharaṃ vibhuḥ  || 89.37 ||
kṣiptamātre śare tasmin sarve naṣṭā mṛgāstataḥ  |
teṣāṃ madhye sa caiveka ṛṣyaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 89.38 ||
śareṇa viddhaḥ patitaḥ kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti cābravīt |
hāhā śabdaṃ kṛtaṃ tena kenāhaṃ pātito'dhunā  || 89.39 ||
kasyaiṣā durmatirjātā yayā buddhirmamopari  |
mṛgamadhye sthitaścāhaṃ na kiṃcadaparāddhavān  || 89.40 ||
vācāṃ tāṃ mānuṣīṃ śrutvā sa rājā vismayānvitaḥ  |
śīghraṃ gatvā tato'paśyadbrāhmaṇaṃ brahmavarcasam  || 89.41 ||
hā hā kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ me'dya yenāsau ghātito mayā  |

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
na te siddhirbhavetkiṃcinmayi pañcatvamāgate  || 89.42 ||
tavaiva vihitā hatyā mayi pañcatvamāgate  |
jananī me pitā vṛddhau bhrātaro hi tapasvinaḥ  || 89.43 ||
bhrātṛjāyā mariṣyanti mayi pañcatvamāgate  |
etā hatyā bhaviṣyanti tava śuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 89.44 ||
upāyaṃ kathayiṣyāmi kartuṃ tvaṃ yadi manyase  || 89.45 ||

rājovāca - 
upāyaḥ kathyatāṃ me'dya yaste manasi vartate  |
kariṣye tadahaṃ sarvaṃ prayatnena mahāmune  || 89.46 ||

śṛṅgyuvāca - 
pṛcchāmi tvāṃ kutaḥ ko vā kathaṃ tvamiha cāgataḥ  |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ madhye'ntyajaḥ śūdro'thavā punaḥ  || 89.47 ||

rājovāca - 
nāhaṃ vipro na vaiśyo'haṃ na śūdraḥ kṣatriyo hyaham  |

śṛṅgyuvāca - 
māṃ gṛhītvāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ yatra tau pitarau mama  || 89.48 ||
āvedayasva dayasva cātmānaṃ putrapāpinamāgatam  |
tau dṛṣṭvā māṃ kariṣyete kāruṇyaṃ ca tavopari  || 89.49 ||
upāyaṃ vā kariṣyete yena śāntirbhaviṣyati  |
