tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā citraseno nṛpottamaḥ  || 89.50 ||
skandhe kṛtvā ca taṃ vipraṃ jagāmāśramakaṃ prati  |
na śaknoti ca taṃ voḍhuṃ viśramya ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 89.51 ||
tāvatpaśyati taṃ vipraṃ mūrchitaṃ vikalendriyam  |
mumoca citrasenastu chāyāṃ nyagrodhakasya ca  || 89.52 ||
viśrāmaṃ ca tataḥ kṛtvā vācaṃ kurvanmuhurmuhuḥ  |
paśyatastasya rājendra ṛṣyaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 89.53 ||
pañcatvamagamacchīghraṃ dhyānayogena yogavit |
dāhayāmāsa taṃ vipraṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  || 89.54 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā tu śokārto ruroda ca mumoha ca  |
tataścānantaraṃ rājā udvegaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  || 89.55 ||
kathaṃ yāsye gṛhānadya vārāṇasyāṃ hato hyaham  |
brahmahatyā samāviṣṭo juhomyagnau kalevaram  || 89.56 ||
athavā ṛṣivākyena gacchāmyevāśramaṃ prati  |
kathayāmi yathāvṛttaṃ gatvā tasya mahā ṛṣeḥ  || 89.57 ||
evaṃ vicintya rājāsau jagāmāśramasannidhau  |
ṛṣyaśṛṅgasya cāsthīni gṛhītvā sa nṛpottamaḥ  || 89.58 ||
dṛṣṭimārge sthitastasya brahmarṣerbhāvitātmanaḥ  |

dīrghatapā uvāca - 
āgaccha svāgataṃ te'dya āsane upaviśyatām  || 89.59 ||
dīrghatapāsmyahaṃ te'dya madhuparkaḥ saviṣṭaraḥ  |

rājovācā̱
arghasyaiva na yogyo'haṃ maharṣerbhāvitātmanaḥ  || 89.60 ||
mṛgamadhye sthito vipra tava putro mayā hataḥ  |
putraghnaṃ śādhi māṃ vipra tīvradaṇḍena daṇḍaya  || 89.61 ||
mṛgabhrāntyā hato vipra ṛṣyaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  |
iti jñātvā ca māṃ vipra kuruṣva ca yathocitam  || 89.62 ||
mātā tasya vacaḥ śrutvā gṛhānnirgatya vihvalā  |
hā hatāsmītyuvācātha patitā ca mahītale  || 89.63 ||
vilalāpa suduḥkhārtā putraśokena pīḍitā  |
hā putra putreti vadan karuṇaṃ kurarī yathā  || 89.64 ||
śrutyadhyayanasampūrṇo japahomaparāyaṇaḥ  |
āgataṃ tvāṃ gṛhadvāre kadā pṛcchāmi putraka  || 89.65 ||
trilokyāmapi śrūyate candanaṃ kila śītalam  |
putragātapariṣvaṅgaścandanādapi śītalaḥ  || 89.66 ||
pariṣvajitumicchāmi tvāmahaṃ putrasupriyam  |
pañcatvaṃ ca gamiṣyāmi tvadvihīnā suduḥkhitā  || 89.67 ||
evaṃ vilapatī dīnā putraśokena pīḍitā  |
mūrchitā vihvalā dīnā nipapāta mahītale  || 89.68 ||
bhāryāṃ ca patitāṃ dṛṣṭvā putraśokena pīḍitaḥ  |
cukopa muniśreṣṭhaścitrasenaṃ nṛpaṃ tadā  || 89.69 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca - 
yāhi yāhi mahāpāpa mā mukhaṃ darśayasva me  |
kiṃ tvayā ghātito vipra hyakāmācca suto mama  || 89.70 ||
brahmahatyā bhaviṣyanti bahavaste narādhipa  |
sakuṭumbasya me tvaṃ hi mṛtyurevamupāgataḥ  || 89.71 ||
evamuktvā tato vipro vicintya ca punaḥ punaḥ  |
krodhaṃ parityajya tato munimārgaṃ jagāma ha  || 89.72 ||

ṛṣiruvāca - 
udvegaṃ tyaja bho rājan duruktaṃ gaditaṃ mayā  |
putraśokābhibhūtena duḥkhamāptena mānada  || 89.73 ||
kiṃ karoti naraḥ prājñaḥ preryamāṇaḥ svakarmabhiḥ  |
prāyeṇa hi manuṣyāṇāṃ buddhiḥ karmānusāriṇī  || 89.74 ||
anenaiva prakāreṇa yattvayā likhitaṃ mama  |
paraṃ tava bhaviṣyanti viprahatyā na saṃśayaḥ  || 89.75 ||
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ madhye śūdro vā cāntyajādiṣu  |
kastvaṃ kathaya satyaṃ me kasmācca nihataḥ sutaḥ  || 89.76 ||

citrasena uvāca - 
vijñāpayāmi viprarṣe kṣantavyaṃ ca mamopari  |
nāhaṃ vipro'smi bho tāta na śūdro naiva vaiśyajaḥ  || 89.77 ||
nacāpi cāntyajātīyaḥ kṣatriyo'haṃ dvijottama  |
kāśirājo mṛgān hantumāgato vanamuttamam  || 89.78 ||
bhramatā pātitastatra mṛgarūpadharo muniḥ  |
kiṃ kartavyaṃ mayā vipra upāyaṃ kathayasva me  || 89.79 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca - 
brahmahatyā na śakyeta ekena tarituṃ vibho  |
deśe kāle yathāśaktyā tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 89.80 ||
catvāro me sutā rājan sabhāryā mātṛpūrvakāḥ  |
mayā saha na jīvanti ṛṣyaśṛṅgasya kāraṇe  || 89.81 ||
upāyaṃ śobhanaṃ tāta kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva bhoḥ  |
śakyate yadi cetkartuṃ sukhopāyaṃ nareśvara  || 89.82 ||
sakuṭumbasamastānno dāhayasvānale nṛpa  |
asthīni narmadātoye śūlabhede vinikṣipeḥ  || 89.83 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle śūlabhedeti viśrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthottamottamam  || 89.84 ||
śucirbhūtvā mamāsthīni kṣipa tvaṃ śūlabhedake  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mama vākyātna saṃśayaḥ  || 89.85 ||

rājovāca - 
ādeśo dīyatāṃ tāta kariṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
sarvasvamapi yatkiṃcidrājyaṃ kośaḥ striyaḥ sutāḥ  || 89.86 ||
tava dānaṃ prayacchāmi vipra māṃ tvaṃ prasīda ca  |
parasparaṃ vivadatormunirājñostadā nṛpa  || 89.87 ||
sphuṭitvā hṛdayaṃ śīghraṃ munerbhāryā mṛtā tadā  |
putraśokasamākrāntā nirjīvā patitā kṣitau  || 89.88 ||
putrāśca mātṛśokena sarve pañcatvamāgatāḥ  |
snuṣāścaiva tu tāḥ sarvā mṛtāśca saha bhartṛbhiḥ  || 89.89 ||
pañcatvaṃ tu gatān sarvānmunimukhyānnirīkṣya tān  |
viprāścāhvānitāstena te tatrāśramavāsinaḥ  || 89.90 ||
tebhyo nivedayāmāsa yathā vṛttaṃ narottamaḥ  |
saṃhataistairanujñātaḥ kathaṃciddahya yatnataḥ  || 89.91 ||
dehaṃ svaṃ pāvanaṃ kṛtvāsthīni pragṛhya yatnataḥ  |
yāmyāṃ hi prasthito rājā pādacārī mahīpatiḥ  || 89.92 ||
na śaknoti yadā gantuṃ chāyāmāśritya tiṣṭhati  |
viśramya ca punargacchan viśramya ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 89.93 ||
sacailaṃ kurute snānamasthīn voḍhā pade pade  |
vinā jalaṃ nirāhāraḥ so'gacchaddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 89.94 ||
acireṇaiva kālena sa gato narmadātaṭe  |
āśramasthān dvijān sarvān papraccha rājasattamaḥ  || 89.95 ||
kathyatāṃ me dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ śūlabhedasya mārgakaḥ  || 89.96 ||

viprā ūcu̱ḥ
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle gato drakṣyasi nānyathā  || 89.97 ||
ṛṣivākyena vai rājā gato'tha hi nareśvaraḥ  |
sa dadarśa tatastīrthaṃ bahudvijasamākulam  || 89.98 ||
bahudrumalatākīrṇaṃ bahupuṣpopaśobhitam  |
bahumūlaphalopetaṃ bahuśvāpadaśobhitam  || 89.99 ||
ṛṣisaṅghaiḥ samākīrṇaṃ nānāvratadharaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
ekapādasthitāḥ kecidapare sūryavarcasaḥ  || 89.100 ||
ekadṛṣṭisthitāḥ kecidūrdhvabāhusthitāḥ pare  |
cāndrāyaṇaparā kecitkecitpakṣopavāsinaḥ  || 89.101 ||
māsopavāsinaḥ kecitkecidṛtumupoṣitāḥ  |
śīrṇaparṇāśinaḥ kecitkecinmārutabhojanāḥ  || 89.102 ||
yogābhyāsaratāḥ keciddhyāyantaḥ paramaṃ padam  |
gārhasthamāsthitāḥ kecitkeciccaivāgnihotriṇaḥ  || 89.103 ||
evaṃ vidhān dvijān dṛṣṭvā jānubhyāmavanīṃ gataḥ  |
praṇamya śirasā rājan rājā vacanamabravīt || 89.104 ||
kasmin deśe tu tattīrthaṃ kathayadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
sarveṣāṃ vāñchitāṃ siddhiṃ dadyācca phalamīpsitam  || 89.105 ||

ṛṣiruvāca - 
dhanvantaraśataṃ gaccha bhṛgutuṅgasya mūrdhani  |
kuṇḍaṃ drakṣyasi vistīrṇaṃ toyapūrṇaṃ suśobhanam  || 89.106 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā gataḥ kuṇḍasya mūrdhani  |
dṛṣṭvā hi caiva tattīrthaṃ bhrāntirjātā nṛpasya hi  || 89.107 ||
vīkṣya kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāgaṃ gaṅgāṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ  |
prācīṃ sarasvatīṃ dṛṣṭvā bhrāntirjātā nṛpasya hi  || 89.108 ||
tato vismayamāpannaścintayāno muhurmuhuḥ  |
ākāśasaṃsthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sāmiṣaṃ kuraraṃ tathā  || 89.109 ||
bhramamāṇaṃ gṛhītvā taṃ vadhyamānaṃ nirāmiṣaiḥ  |
parasparaṃ hi yudhyante sarve cāmiṣabhakṣakāḥ  || 89.110 ||
hataścañcuprahāraistu kuraraḥ patito'mbhasi  |
śūlena śūlinā yatra bhūbhāgaṃ bheditaṃ purā  || 89.111 ||
tattīrthasya prabhāveṇa sa sadyaḥ puruṣo'bhavat |
vimānasthaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā krauṃca vai divyarūpiṇam  || 89.112 ||
apsarobhirgīyamānaṃ nṛpastattīrthamāgataḥ  |
asthīni bhūmau nikṣipya snānaṃ kṛtvā yathā vidhi  || 89.113 ||
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayitveṣṭadevatāḥ  |
dhṛtvāsthīni tato rājā nikṣipyāntarjale tathā  || 89.114 ||
kṣaṇamekaṃ tato vīkṣya rājā ūrdhvamukhaḥ sthitaḥ  |
tāṃ dadarśa tataḥ sarvān devamūrtidharān śubhān  || 89.115 ||
divyavastraiśca saṃvītān divyābharaṇabhūṣitān  |
vimānaiḥ kāñcanairdivyairapsarogaṇasevitaiḥ  || 89.116 ||
pṛthagbhūtāśca tān sarvān vimāneṣu vyavasthitān  |
utpatataḥ samālokya sa rājā harṣito'bhavat || 89.117 ||
ṛṣirvimānamārūḍhaścitrasenamathābravīt |
bho bhoḥ sādhu mahārāja citrasena mahāmate  || 89.118 ||
tvatprasādānnṛpaśreṣṭha gatirdivyā mamādya vai  |
idaṃ ca yattvayā kiṃcitkṛtaṃ paramaduṣkaram  || 89.119 ||
svasuto'pi na śaknoti pitṝṇāṃ kartumīdṛśam  |
madīyavacanādrājanniṣpāpastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 89.120 ||
yattvaṃ drakṣyasi rājendra kāmikaṃ manasepsitam  |
āśīrvādaṃ tato dattvā citrasenāya dhīmate  || 89.121 ||
svargaṃ jagāma svasutaistato dīrghatapā muniḥ  || 89.122 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dīrghatapākhyāno nāmaikonanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 90

uttānapāda uvāca - 
dṛṣṭvā tattīrthamāhātmyaṃ citraseno nareśvaraḥ  |
vipulaṃ tīkṣṇadhāraṃ ca kaṇṭhe cāsi nṛpottama  || 90.1 ||
devān sarvān hṛdi dhyāyan brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān  |
vinikṣipannathātmānau pratyakṣau viṣṇuśaṅkarau  || 90.2 ||
kare dhṛtvā tu rājānaṃ rudro vacanamabravīt |

hara uvāca - 
prāṇatyāgaṃ mahārāja akāle mā kuruṣva ha  || 90.3 ||
adyāpi tu yuvāsi tvaṃ na yuktaṃ maraṇaṃ tava  |
svasthānaṃ gaccha vai śīghraṃ bhogān bhuṅkṣva yathepsitān  || 90.4 ||
bhuṅkṣva niṣkaṇṭakaṃ rājyaṃ nākaṃ śakra ivāparaḥ  |

citrasena uvāca - 
na rājyaṃ kāmaye deva na putrānna ca bāndhavān  || 90.5 ||
na bhāryāṃ na ca kośaṃ ca na gāśca na turaṅgamān  |
muñcasva māṃ mahādeva avighnaṃ kriyatāṃ mama  || 90.6 ||
svargaprāptirmamādyaiva tvatprasādānmaheśvara  |

deva uvāca - 
yasyāgrato bhavedviṣṇurbrahmā rudrastathaiva ca  || 90.7 ||
svargeṇa tasya kiṃ kāryaṃ gato'sau kiṃ kariṣyati  |
tuṣṭāva tvāṃ trayo devā vṛṇīṣva varamuttamam  || 90.8 ||
yathepsitaṃ mahārāja satyametanna saṃśayaḥ  |

citrasena uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭāstrayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 90.9 ||
adya prabhṛti yuṣmābhiḥ sthātavyamiha sarvadā  |
gayāśiraṃ yathā puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ yuṣmābhireva ca  || 90.10 ||
tathaivedaṃ tu kartavyaṃ śūlabhedaṃ ca pāvanam  |
yatra yatra sthitā yūyaṃ tatra tatra vasāmyaham  || 90.11 ||
gaṇānāmiha sarveṣāmavadhyo'haṃ sureśvara  |

īśvara uvāca - 
adya prabhṛti tiṣṭhāma śūlabhede nareśvara  || 90.12 ||
kalāṃśena trayo devāstrikālaṃ nivasāmahe  |
nandisaṃjño gaṇaśca tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi na saṃśaya  || 90.13 ||
bhaviṣyatyagrapūjā te matsamīpe sadā nṛpa  |
prakṣipya ca nijāsthīni yathā dīrghatapā yayau  || 90.14 ||
sakuṭumbo vimānasthaḥ svarge tiṣṭhati tatkuru  |
evaṃ devā varaṃ dattvā citrasenāya pārthiva  || 90.15 ||
kuṇḍamūrdhani yāsyāmastrayo devāstadā sthitāḥ  |
parasparaṃ vadantyevamidaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā śubham || 90.16 ||
yathā gayāśiraṃ puṇyaṃ sarvamāsi ca paṭhyate  |
tathā revātaṭe puṇyaṃ śūlabhedaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 90.17 ||

brahmovāca - 
idaṃ tīrthaṃ mahārāja yathāpuṇyaṃ gayāśiraḥ  |
snātvā caivodake tasminnaro nirmalatāṃ vrajet || 90.18 ||
ekaṃ gayāśiraṃ muktvā sarvatīrthāni śaṅkara  |
śūlabhedasya tīrthasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 90.19 ||
kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇe bhāge daśahastapramāṇataḥ  |
aindravāruṇavāyavyāṃ pramāṇantvekaviṃśatiḥ  || 90.20 ||
etatpramāṇaṃ tīrthasya piṇḍadānādikarmasu  |
narāḥ puṇyāśca te sarve atra dānaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yaiḥ  || 90.21 ||
viṣṇustrinetrarūpeṇa brahmarūpī pitāmahaḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe sthitā nityaṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇanti bhaktitaḥ  || 90.22 ||
jātaṃ jātaṃ nirīkṣyante svaputraṃ hi pitāmahāḥ  |
kadā yāsyati putro'sau kadā dātā bhaviṣyati  || 90.23 ||
pañcasthāneṣu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute bhaktimānnaraḥ  |
svakulāni tu sarvāṇi pretabhūtāni tārayet || 90.24 ||
ekaviṃśatpitṛpakṣe mātṛpakṣaikaviṃśatim  |
bhāryāyā ekādaśaiveti sarvāṇyetāni tārayet || 90.25 ||
dvijadevaprasādena pitṝṇāṃ ca tathaiva hi  |
śrāddhado vasate tatra yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 90.26 ||
ātmano ghātakā ye ca goghnāḥ straiṇahatāśca ye  |
daṃṣṭribhirjalapātena vidyutpātena ye hatāḥ  || 90.27 ||
na teṣāmagnisaṃskāro na śaucaṃ nodakakriyā  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ teṣāṃ kuryātsvabhaktitaḥ  || 90.28 ||
mokṣaprāptirbhavetteṣāṃ tristhāneṣu na saṃśayaḥ  |
tṛptistu jāyate teṣāṃ varṣamekaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 90.29 ||
ajānatā kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bālabhāveṣu yatkṛtam  |
tatsarvaṃ naśyati kṣipraṃ sakṛtsnānena bhūpate  || 90.30 ||
rajakena yathā dhautavastraṃ nirmalatāṃ vrajet |
pāpopaliptastīrthe'smin snāto nirmalatāṃ vrajet || 90.31 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yastu tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
dhyāyamāno mahādevaṃ sa gacchetparamaṃ padam  || 90.32 ||
krīḍitvā ca yathā kāmaṃ svecchayā śivamandire  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajño jāyate vipule kule  || 90.33 ||
rūpavān subhagaścaiva sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
sarvadharmasamopetaḥ sarvācārasamanvitaḥ  || 90.34 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃstīrthasya phalamuttamam  |
tacchrutvā mānavo nityaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 90.35 ||
yaścainaṃ śrāvayedbhaktyā ākhyānaṃ dvijasannidhau  |
śrāddhe devagṛhe caiva paṭhetparvaṇi parvaṇi  || 90.36 ||
gīrvāṇāstasya tuṣyanti manuṣyāḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva naśyedvai pāpasaṃcayaḥ  || 90.37 ||
likhitvā tīrthamāhātmyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadāti yaḥ  |
jātismaraṃ sa labhate prāpnotyabhimataṃ phalam  || 90.38 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe citrasenakathāvarṇano nāma navatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 91

rājovāca - 
anyacca śrotumicchāmi kena gaṅgāvatāritā  |
rudraśīrṣasthitā puṇyā devī kathamihāgatā  || 91.1 ||
puṇyā devaśilānāma tasyā māhātmyamuttamam  |
etadākhyāhi me sarvaṃ prasādātpuruṣottama  || 91.2 ||
rudra uvāca - 
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā yathā gaṅgāvatāritā  |
purā devī mahābhāga brahmādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ  || 91.3 ||
abhyarthayajjagannāthaṃ devadevaṃ jagadgurum  |
ghaṭamadhye sthitā gaṅgā mocitā ca subhūtale  || 91.4 ||
bhāratī ca tato muktā rudreṇa śiraso bhuvi  |
narāstīrthe taṭe tasyāḥ snānaṃ kurvanti bhaktitaḥ  || 91.5 ||
pibanti ca jalaṃ nityaṃ na te yānti yamālayam  |
yatra sā patitā kuṇḍe śūlabhede narādhipa  || 91.6 ||
devanadyāḥ pratīcyāṃ ca yatra prācī sarasvatī  |
yāmyāṃ ca śūlabhedākhyamasti tīrthamanuttamam  || 91.7 ||
tatradevaśilā ramyā svayaṃ devena nirmitā  |
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayennṛpa  || 91.8 ||
alpasyaiva tu dānasya tasya cānto na vidyate  |

uttānapāda uvāca - 
kāni dānāni śastāni deveśa dharaṇītale  || 91.9 ||
yāni dattvā naro bhaktyā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  |
devaśilāyā māhātmyaṃ snānadānāddhi yatphalam  || 91.10 ||
vratopavāsani yamairyatprāpyaṃ tadvadasva me  |

śrībhagavānuvāca - 
āsītpurā mahāvīryaścedinātho mahābalaḥ  || 91.11 ||
vīrasena iti khyāto maṇḍalādhipatīśvaraḥ  |
tasya rājye ripurnāsti na vyādhirna ca taskaraḥ  || 91.12 ||
na cādharmo'bhavattatra dharma eva hi sarvadā  |
sadā mudānvito rājā sabhāryo bahuputrakaḥ  || 91.13 ||
ekā ca duhitā tasya surūpā girijā iva  |
dṛṣṭā sā pitṛmātṛbhyāṃ bandhuvargajanaiḥ saha  || 91.14 ||
kṛtvā vaivāhikaṃ kāryaṃ kāle prāpte yathā vidhi  |
anantaraṃ cedipatirdvādaśābde maheśvaraḥ  || 91.15 ||
tatastasyāstu yo bhartā sa mṛtyuvaśamāgataḥ  |
vidhavāṃ tāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā rājā śokasamanvitaḥ  || 91.16 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ rājā svabhāryāṃ duḥkhapīḍitaḥ  |
bhadre duḥkhamidaṃ jātaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ suduḥsaham  || 91.17 ||
naiṣā rakṣayituṃ śakyā rūpayauvanadarpitā  |
nopāyo vidyate bhāryye bhānumatyāśca rakṣaṇe  || 91.18 ||
parasparaṃ vivadatostacchrutvā kanyakā'bravīt |

bhānumatyuvāca - 
na vrīḍāmi tavāgre'haṃ jvalantī dāhakenaca  || 91.19 ||
satyaṃ notpadyate doṣo madarthe ca narādhipa  |
adya prabhṛtyahaṃ tāta na veṣaṃ dhāraye kvacit || 91.20 ||
sthūlavastrairnijāṅgāni paridhāsyāmi saṃyatā  |
cariṣyāmi vratān sarvān purāṇavihitānapi  || 91.21 ||
ātmānaṃ śoṣayiṣyāmi toṣayantī janārdanam  |
mamaiṣā vartate buddhiryadi tvaṃ tāta manyase  || 91.22 ||
bhānumatyā vacaḥ śrutvā rājā snehārdito'bhavat |
tīrthayātrāṃ samuddiśya kośaṃ dattvā ca puṣkalam  || 91.23 ||
visṛjya rājā svasutāṃ vṛddhān kṛtvā tu rakṣaṇe  |
puruṣaṃ sāyudhaṃ cānyaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca purohitam  || 91.24 ||
avagāhya taṭe dhyātuṃ gaṅgāyāṃ sā narādhipa  |
nityamāpūjayadviprān gandhamālyādibhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 91.25 ||
dāsīdāsaprabhṛtayastasyā ye rakṣaṇe kṣamāḥ  |
tataḥ piturmatenaiva gaṅgātīre samāsthitāḥ  || 91.26 ||
dvādaśābdāni sā tīre gaṅgāyāḥ samavasthitā  |
tyaktvā gaṅgāṃ kvacidrājaputrī kāṣṭhāṃ tu dakṣiṇām  || 91.27 ||
prāptā sā sacivaiḥ sārddhaṃ yatra revā mahānadī  |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ ca sthitā tatra oṃkāre'marakaṇṭake  || 91.28 ||
nānāvidheṣu tīrtheṣu tīrthāttīrthaṃ jagāma ha  |
snātvā snātvā dvijān pūjyabhaktiyuktā hyadhiṣṭhitā  || 91.29 ||
vāruṇīṃ ca diśaṃ gatvā devanadyāśca saṅgame  |
dadarśa cāśramaṃ puṇyamṛṣisaṅghairniṣevitam  || 91.30 ||
dṛṣṭvā ṛṣisamūhaṃ sā praṇipatyedamabravīt |
māhātmyaṃ cāsya tīrthasya nāma caivāsya kīrtaya  || 91.31 ||

ṛṣiruvāca - 
cakratīrthaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ cakraṃ dattaṃ purā hareḥ  |
maheśvareṇa tuṣṭena devadevena śūlinā  || 91.32 ||
atra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
anivartikā gatistasya bhavitā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 91.33 ||
dvitīye'hni tato gacchecchūlabhedaṃ tapasvini  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā paṭhetpaurāṇikīṃ kathām  || 91.34 ||
viṣṇupūjāṃ prakurvīta puṣpadīpanivedanaiḥ  |
prabhāte bhojayedviprān dānaṃ dadyātsvabhaktitaḥ  || 91.35 ||
caturthe'hṇi tathā gacchedyatra prācī sarasvatī  |
brahmadevādviniṣkrāntā pāvanārthaṃ narādhipa  || 91.36 ||
tatra snātvā naro gatvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
śrāddhadastu vasettatra yatra devaḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 91.37 ||
pañcame'hṇi tato gacchelliṅgaṃ mārkaṇḍasaṃjñitam  |
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā arcayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 91.38 ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathā nyāyamanindyān pūjayeddvijān  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti dvādaśābdaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 91.39 ||
sarvadevamayaṃ sthānaṃ sarvatīrthamanuttamam  |
koṭitīrthasamaṃ sthānaṃ koṭiliṅgottamottamam  || 91.40 ||
trirātraṃ kurute yastu śuciḥ snānaṃ jitendriyaḥ  |
pakṣaṃ māsaṃ ca ṣaṇmāsamabdamekaṃ kadācana  || 91.41 ||
na tasya vasatirmartye nāke vāsaḥ sadākṣayaḥ  |
niyamasthastu mucyeta trijanmajanitādaghāt || 91.42 ||
vinā pumāṃsaṃ yā nārī dvādaśābda tu suvratā  |
tiṣṭhate sākṣayaṃ kālaṃ rudraloke mahīyate  || 91.43 ||
muneśca vacanaṃ śrutvā mudāṃ paramikāṃ yayau  |
tato'vagāhate tīrthamaharniśamatandritam  || 91.44 ||
dṛṣṭvā tīrthaprabhāvaṃ tu rājñī vacanamabravīt |
śrūyatāṃ vacanaṃ me'dya brāhmaṇāḥ sapurohitāḥ  || 91.45 ||
na tyajāmīdṛśaṃ sthānaṃ yāvajjīvāmyaharniśam  |
mātre pitre tathā bhrātre sadbhirvācyamidaṃ vacaḥ  || 91.46 ||
vartate śūlabhede sā niyatā vratacāriṇī  |
ekāntaropavāsena śanairmāsamupoṣitā  || 91.47 ||
devaśilāsthitā nityaṃ dhyāyamānā tu keśavam  |
aharniśaṃ sthitā bhūmau dṛṣṭā rājñī śubhānanā  || 91.48 ||
vratasthā niyatāhārā nāmnā bhānumatī śubhā  |
gateṣu dvijamukhyeṣvāyayau śabarayugmakam  || 91.49 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ tatra tāṃ dṛṣṭvā śabarāṅganā  |
naivā'syāḥ sadṛśī kācittriṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  || 91.50 ||
sākṣādasau devakanyā hyavatīrṇā mahītale  |
bhāryāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā śabarastāmuvāca ha  || 91.51 ||
kamalāni yathā lābhaṃ dattvā tvaṃ bhuṅkṣva satvaram  |
mama caivārcane buddhirna bhoktavyaṃ mayā'dya vai  || 91.52 ||
na mayā varjitaṃ bhadre pāpavṛddhyā'śubhaṃ kṛtam  |

śabaryuvāca - 
na pūrvaṃ tu mayā svāmin bhuktaṃ tasmiṃstu vāsare  || 91.53 ||
bhuktaśeṣaṃ mayā bhuktaṃ yāvatkālaṃ smarāmyaham  |
bhāryāyā niścayaṃ jñātvā snānaṃ kartuṃ jagāma ha  || 91.54 ||
ardhottarīyavastreṇa snānaṃ kṛtvā tu bhaktitaḥ  |
sarvadevaṃ namaskṛtya gato devaśilāṃ prati  || 91.55 ||
tasthau sa śaṅkamāno'pi dhyāyamāno janārdanam  |
kumudadvayaṃ śabaryā tu dāsīhaste niveditam  || 91.56 ||
dṛṣṭvā rājñī tathā puṣpe dāsīṃ caiva tadābravīt |
tvedaṃ puṣpadvayaṃ labdhaṃ kathyatāṃ tacca sāmpratam  || 91.57 ||
śīghraṃ gacchāvagaccha tvaṃ puṣpaṃ caivānayāparam  |
anena vasunā caiva kamalāni samānaya  || 91.58 ||
bhānumatyā vacaḥ śrutvā gatā sā śabarīṃ prati  |
śrīphalāni ca puṣpāṇi bahūnyanyāni dehi me  || 91.59 ||

śabaryuvāca - 
śrīphalāni ca dāsyāmi puṣpāṇi ca viśeṣataḥ  |
mūlyena me spṛhā nāsti gatvā rājñīṃ nivedaya  || 91.60 ||
gatā dāsī nivedyātha rājñī ca svayamāgatā  |
uvāca śabaraṃ rājñī puṣpaṃ mūlyena dehi me  || 91.61 ||

śabara uvāca - 
na mūlyaṃ kāmaye devi phalapuṣpasamudbhavam  |
śrīphalāni ca puṣpāṇi yatheṣṭaṃ mama gṛhyatām  || 91.62 ||
arcāṃ kuru yathānyāyaṃ vāsudevaṃ jagatpatim  |

rājñī uvāca - 
vinā mūlyaṃ na gṛhṇāmi kamalāni tavādhunā  || 91.63 ||
dhānyasya khaṇḍikāmekāmetāṃ prati nigṛhyatām  || 91.64 ||

śabara uvāca - 
nāhāraṃ cintayāmyadya muktvā devaṃ varānane  || 91.65 ||
devakāryaṃ vinā bhadre nānyābuddhiḥ pravartate  |

rājñī uvāca - 
na tvayānnaṃ parityājyaṃ sarvamanne pratiṣṭhitam  || 91.66 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena mamānnaṃ pratigṛhyatām  |
tapasvino mahābhāgā ye cāraṇyanivāsinaḥ  || 91.67 ||
te maddvāre sthitāḥ sarve yācante te'nnakāṅkṣiṇaḥ  |

śabara uvāca - 
niṣedho'dhikṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ mayā satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 91.68 ||
satyamūlaṃ jagatsarvaṃ satye caiva pratiṣṭhitam  |
satyena tapate sūryaḥ satyena dyotate śaśī  || 91.69 ||
satyena vāyavovānti dharā satye pratiṣṭhitā  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena satyaṃ satyaṃ na lopayet || 91.70 ||

rājñī uvāca - 
ārāmopahṛtaṃ puṣpamāraṇyaṃ puṣpameva ca  |
krītaṃ prahigrahāllabdhaṃ puṣpamevaṃ caturvidham  || 91.71 ||
uttamaṃ phalamāraṇyaṃ gṛhītvā svayameva hi  |
madhyamaṃ phalamārāmyamadhamaṃ krītameva ca  || 91.72 ||
pratigraheṇa yallabdhaṃ niṣphalaṃ tadvidurbudhāḥ  |

purohita uvāca - 
gṛhāṇa rājñi puṣpāṇi pūjāṃ kuru janārdane  || 91.73 ||
upakāraṃ prakurvantī pūjāṃ cakre yathāvidhi  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kathā paurāṇikī śrutā  || 91.74 ||
śabarastu tato bhāryāmidaṃ vacanamabravīt |
dīpārthaṃ gṛhyatāṃ sneho yathālābhena sundari  || 91.75 ||
dattvā dīpaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhūpaṃ pūjāṃ janārdane  |
kṛtvā jāgaraṇaṃ rātrau dhyāyamānastu keśavam  || 91.76 ||
tataḥ prabhātasamaye dṛṣṭvā snānotsukaṃjanam  |
kecicca śūlabhede tu devanadyāṃ tathaiva ca  || 91.77 ||
sarasvatyāṃ tathā kecinmārkaṇḍeye tathā hrade  |
cakratīrthe tathā kecitsnānaṃ kurvanti bhaktitaḥ  || 91.78 ||
śucibhūtāstu te sarve janā devaśilopari  |
śrāddhaṃ kurvanti vai tatra prayatnena dvijarṣabhāḥ  || 91.79 ||
tān dṛṣṭvā śabaro bilvaiḥ piṇḍaṃ nirvartayettataḥ  |
bhānumatyā tathā saktupiṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtam  || 91.80 ||
anindya bhojayedvipraṃ dambhadoṣavivarjitam  |
haviṣyeṇa tathā dadhnā śarkarāmadhusarpiṣā  || 91.81 ||
pāyasena ca gavyena kṛśareṇa viśeṣataḥ  |
bhojayitvā tathā rājñī dānaṃ dattvā yathāvidhi  || 91.82 ||
pādukopānahau chatraṃ śayyā govṛṣameva ca  |
vividhāni ca dānāni hemaratnamayāni ca  || 91.83 ||
tatra tīrthe mahārāja kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
tena dattā mahī rājan saśailavanakānanā  || 91.84 ||

uttānapāda uvāca - 
tilapradaḥ prajā iṣṭā dīpadaścakṣuruttamam  |
bhūmidaḥ svargamāpnoti dīrghāyuśca hiraṇyadaḥ  || 91.85 ||
gṛhado rogarahito raupyado rūpamuttamam  |
vāsodaścandralokaṃ tu aśvadaḥ sūryalokabhāk || 91.86 ||
vṛṣadastu śriyaṃ puṇyāṃ godānāttu triviṣṭapam  |
śayyādānaṃ ca yo dadyātsa svargamabhayapradaḥ  || 91.87 ||
dhānyadaḥ śāśvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahma śāśvatam  |
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ brahmadānaṃ viśiṣyate  || 91.88 ||
bhāryāmaśvaṃ mahīṃ vāsastilakāṃ ca na sarpiṣam  |
ye nayena hi bhāvena dānaṃ viprāya yacchati  || 91.89 ||
tena tena hi bhāvena prāpnoti padapūjitam  |
dṛṣṭvā dānāni sarvāṇi rājñyā dattāni yāni ca  || 91.90 ||
uvāca śabaro bhāryāṃ yacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |

śabara uvāca - 
purāṇaṃ paṭhitaṃ bhadre brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 91.91 ||
śrutaṃ ca tanmayā sarvaṃ dānadharmaparaṃ śubham  |
pūrvajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ snānadānavratena ca  || 91.92 ||
tatsarvaṃ ca kṣayaṃ jātaṃ madīyena priye śṛṇu  |
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ taptaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 91.93 ||
te dvijā bhānumatyā ca śūlabhedaṃ gatāstataḥ  |
dadṛśuḥ śabaraṃ kuṇḍe śabaryā saha saṃsthitam  || 91.94 ||
īśānyāṃ ca tato gatvā bhṛguparvatamūrdhani  |
martukāmastathārūḍho bhāryayā saha pārthiva  || 91.95 ||

rājaputryuvāca - 
tiṣṭha tiṣṭha mahāsattva śṛṇuṣva vacanaṃ mama  |
kimarthaṃ tyajasi prāṇānadyāpi ca yuvā bhavān  || 91.96 ||
kiṃ santāpaḥ samudvegaḥ kiṃ duḥkhaṃ vyādhireva ca  |
śiśuśca dṛśyate'dyāpi kāraṇaṃ kathayasva me  || 91.97 ||

śabara uvāca - 
kāraṇaṃ nāsti me kiṃcinna duḥkhaṃ kiṃcideva hi  |
saṃsārasārabhūtatve nānyā buddhiḥ pravartate  || 91.98 ||
duḥkhena labhate yasmānmanuṣyatvaṃ varānane  |
mānuṣyaṃ janma cāsādya yo na dharmaṃ samācaret || 91.99 ||
sa gacchennarakaṃ ghoramalpadoṣeṇa sundari  |
tasmātpatitumicchāmi asmiṃstīrthe tapasvini  || 91.100 ||

rājaputryuvāca - 
adyāpi vartate kālaḥ svadharmā vividhāḥ kriyāḥ  |
kṛtvā prakṛtadharmāṇi tatra dānena śuddhyati  || 91.101 ||
ahaṃ dāsyāmi te dhānyaṃ vāsāṃsi draviṇāni ca  |
nityaṃ tvamācarerdharmaṃ dhyāyamāno janārdanam  || 91.102 ||

śabara uvāca - 
na cāhaṃ kāmaye devi dhānyaṃ vastrāṇi caiva hi  |
yaḥ parasyānnamaśnāti sa naro'śnāti kilbiṣam  || 91.103 ||

rājaputryuvāca - 
kandamūlaphalāhāro bhuktvā vai bhakṣyamuttamam  |
avagāhya ca tīrthāni sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyase  || 91.104 ||
tato vimuktapāpastu yaḥ kaścitpuruṣaḥ śuciḥ  |
karmaṇā tena caiva tvaṃ gatiṃ samprāpsyase śubhām  || 91.105 ||

śabara uvāca - 
atra madhye mayā tyaktāḥ prāṇā dṛṣṭvā hitaṃ ca yat |
satyanna lopaye devi iti me niścitā matiḥ  || 91.106 ||
prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ devi kṣamasva tvaṃ janaiḥ saha  |
baddhottarīyavastreṇa ātmānaṃ ca prayatnataḥ  || 91.107 ||
bhāryayā sahitastatra hariṃ dhyātvā papāta ha  |
nagārdhaṃ patito yāvadgatajīvo narādhipa  || 91.108 ||
tūṣṇīṃ bhūtaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā kuṇḍasyopari bhūmipa  |
trimūrtigarte tatkāle śabaro bhāryayā saha  || 91.109 ||
divyaṃ vimānamārūḍho gataśca gatimuttamām || 91.110 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śabarasvargārohaṇaṃ nāmaikanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 92

rājovāca - 
tatastayāpi deveśa bhānumatyā hi kiṃkṛtam  |
etanme saṃśayaṃ deva kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 92.1 ||

hara uvāca - 
cintayitvā tu sā rājñī gatā kuṇḍasya sannidhau  |
dṛṣṭvā tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ rājñī harṣeṇa pūritā  || 92.2 ||
viprān bahūn samāhūya pūjayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt |
dadau ca vividhaṃ dānaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo narādhipa  || 92.3 ||
dattvā ca dakṣiṇāmevaṃ madhumāse ca bhūmipa  |
amāyāṃ ca tato rājñī gatā parvatamūrdhani  || 92.4 ||
nagaśṛṅgaṃ samāruhya kṛtvā tu karasampuṭam  |
vijñāpya brāhmaṇān sarvānidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 92.5 ||
mama mātā pitā bhrātā tathā'nye caiva bāndhavāḥ  |
sarve kṣamantu te sarvairidaṃ vācyaṃ tadā vacaḥ  || 92.6 ||
ityuktvā śūlabhede tu tapaḥ kṛtvā sudāruṇam  |
visṛjya caivamātmānaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe divaṃgatā  || 92.7 ||

brāhmaṇā ūcu̱ḥ
sandeśaṃ kathayiṣyāmastvayoktaṃ śobhanavrate  |
mātāpitrośca suśroṇi mā te'bhūdatra saṃśayaḥ  || 92.8 ||
tato visṛjya lokaṃ tu sthitā parvatasannidhau  |
ardhottarīyavastraṃ tu gāḍhaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ punaḥ  || 92.9 ||
tato visṛjya cātmānamekacittā narādhipa  |
nagārddhaṃ patitā yāvattāvaddṛṣṭā surāsuraiḥ  || 92.10 ||
divyaṃ vimānamāruhya kailāsaṃ sā jagāma ha  |
tataḥ sā paśyatāṃ teṣāṃ janānāṃ tridivaṃ gatā  || 92.11 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe bhānumatīsvargārohaṇaṃ nāma dvinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 93

deva uvāca - 
tataḥ puṣkariṇīṃ gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  |
śrutvā tasyāḥ prabhāvaṃ tu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 93.1 ||
revāyā uttare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
yatrāste sarvadā devo divyamūrtirdivākaraḥ  || 93.2 ||
kurukṣetraṃ yathāpuṇyaṃ sarvakāmikamuttamam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 93.3 ||
kurukṣetre yathā vṛddhirdānasya jagatīpate  |
puṣkariṇyāṃ tathā vṛddhirdānasyāpi na saṃśayaḥ  || 93.4 ||
yavamekaṃ tu yo dadyātsauvarṇaṃ cātra vai nṛpa  |
puṣkariṇyāṃ tathā snānaṃ sarvaṃ sthāneśvare smṛtam  || 93.5 ||
sūryagrahe yathā śaktyā dattvā dānaṃ yathā vidhi  |
hastyaśvaratharatnāni gṛhaṃ gāśca dhurandharān  || 93.6 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi brāhmaṇebhyo dadāti yaḥ  |
trayodaśadinaṃ yāvattrayodaśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 93.7 ||
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
dvādaśābdaṃ bhavettṛptistatra tīrthe mahīpate  || 93.8 ||
yastatra kurute śrāddhaṃ pāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  |
śrāddhaṃ maghādi ṛkṣeṣu pitṝṇāṃ dattamakṣayam  || 93.9 ||
akṣatairbadarairbilvairiṅgudairvai tilaiḥ saha  |
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti tasmiṃstīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 93.10 ||
tatra snātvā tu yo devaṃ pūjayecca divākaram  |
sa gacchetparamaṃ lokaṃ tridaśairapi vanditaḥ  || 93.11 ||
ṛcamekāṃ paṭhedyastu yajuṣaḥ sāmna eva ca  |
samagrasya sa vedasya phalamāpnoti vai dvijaḥ  || 93.12 ||
tripuṣkaraṃ japenmantraṃ dhyāyamāno divākaram  |
sa gacchetparamaṃ lokaṃ tridaśairapi vanditam  || 93.13 ||
yastatra vidhivatprāṇāṃstyajate nṛpasattama  |
sa gacchetparamaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo divākaraḥ  || 93.14 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
bhūyo'pyanyatpravakṣyāmi ādityeśvaramuttamam  |
sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ pārtha sarvavighnavināśanam  || 93.15 ||
asya tīrthasya cānyāni tīrthāni kurunandana  |
na labhante śriyaṃ nāke martye pātālagocare  || 93.16 ||
kurukṣetraṃ yathā gaṅgā naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  |
vārāṇasī ca kedāraṃ prayāgo nṛpanandana  || 93.17 ||
ravitīrthasya sarvāṇi kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
ravitīrthe ca yaddattaṃ śṛṇuṣva kurunandana  || 93.18 ||
snehārthe kathayiṣyāmi vārdhakye nāsti paṇḍitaḥ  |
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve taponiṣṭhā mahātmānaḥ  || 93.19 ||
śrutaṃ me rudrasānnidhye skandarudragaṇaiḥ saha  |
pārvatyā prārthitaḥ śambhūravitīrthasya yatphalam  || 93.20 ||
śambhunāpi tadākhyātaṃ girijāyāḥ purastadā  |
tatsarvamekacittena rudrodgītaṃ śrutaṃ mayā  || 93.21 ||
durbhikṣopahatā viprā narmadā taṭamāśritāḥ  |
uddālako vaśiṣṭhaśca māṇḍavyo gautamastathā  || 93.22 ||
yājñavalkyo'tha śāṇḍilyaścyavano bhārgavastathā  |
nāśake turvibhāṇḍaśca bālakhilyādayastathā  || 93.23 ||
śātātapo'pi śaṅkhaśca jaiminirgobhilastathā  |
jaigīṣavyaḥ śatānīka ṛṣisaṅghāḥ samāgatāḥ  || 93.24 ||
tīrthayātrā kṛtā taistu narmadāyā samantataḥ  |
ādityeśaṃ samāyātāḥ prasaṅgādṛṣisattamāḥ  || 93.25 ||
vṛkṣaissaṃchāditaṃ sarvaṃ dhavaistindukapāṭalaiḥ  |
jambīrairarjunaiḥ kundairjaṭākesarakiṃśukaiḥ  || 93.26 ||
punnāganārikeraistu khadiraiḥ kalpapādapaiḥ  |
anekaśvāpadākīrṇaṃ mṛgamālāsamākulam  || 93.27 ||
ṛkṣahastisamāyuktaṃ citrakaiśca suśobhitam  |
praviśya ṛṣayaḥ sarve vane puṣpaphalākule  || 93.28 ||
vanānte ca nārī śubhrā dṛṣṭā raktāmbarānvitā  |
raktamālyā suśobhāḍhyā rastacandanacarcitā  || 93.29 ||
raktābharaṇasaṃyuktā śaśihastā bhayāvahā  |
tasyāḥ samīpago dṛṣṭaḥ kṛṣṇajīmūtasannibhaḥ  || 93.30 ||
mahākāyo bhīmavaktraḥ pāśahasto bhayāvahaḥ  |
anādhṛṣyo vayovṛddha āturaḥ piṅgalocanaḥ  || 93.31 ||
dīrghajihvaḥ karālāsyastīkṣṇādaṃṣṭro durāsadaḥ  |
vṛddhāṃ striyaṃ kuruśreṣṭha te paśyan viprapuṃgavāḥ  || 93.32 ||
tataḥ samīpagā vṛddhā sa ca vṛddhaśca bhārata  |
svādhyāyaniratairvipraistau pṛṣṭau pāpakarmiṇau  || 93.33 ||

vṛddhāvūcatu̱ḥ
yuṣmākaṃ yaminaḥ sarve tiṣṭhadhvaṃ tīrthamadhyataḥ  |
śīghraṃ praviśyatāṃ sarve narmadā caiva sevyatām  || 93.34 ||
tayoḥ śrutvā tu vacanaṃ brāhmaṇāḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
jagmuste narmadākacchaṃ dṛṣṭvā revāṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 93.35 ||
natāḥ kecitstuvantyanye jayadevi namo'stu te  || 93.36 ||

ṛṣaya ūcu̱ḥ
namo'stu te siddhagaṇairniṣevite namo'stu te sarvapavitramaṅgale  |
namo'stu te viprasahasrapūjite  |
namo'stu te rudrasamudbhave pare  || 93.37 ||
namo'stu te sarvapavitrapāvane namo'stu te devi vare prasīda naḥ  |
namo'stu te śītajale sukhaprade saridvare pāpahare dayānvite  || 93.38 ||
anekabhūtāṅgasuśobhitāṅge gandharvayakṣoragapāvitāṅge  |
mahāgajaughā mahiṣā varāhāḥ krīḍanti toye sumahormimālaiḥ  || 93.39 ||
namāmaḥ sarve varade sukhaprade'smān paśupāśabaddhān  |
pāpairanekaiḥ paśupāśabaddhā bhramanti tāvannarakeṣu nityam  || 93.40 ||
yāvattavāmbho nahi saṃspṛśanti spṛṣṭaṃ karaiścandramaso raveśca  |
anekasaṃsārabhayārditānāṃ pāpairanekaiḥ pariveṣṭitānām  || 93.41 ||
gatistvamambhojasamānavaktre dvandvairanekairabhisaṃvṛtānām  |
nadyastu pūjyā vimalā bhavanti  |
tvāṃ devi cāsādya na saṃśayo'tra  || 93.42 ||
dukhāturāṇāmabhayaṃ dadāsi devairanekairabhipūjitāsi  |
viṇmūtradehārṇavamagnadehā bhavanti tāvannarakeṣu martyāḥ  || 93.43 ||
mahāniloddhūtataraṅgabhaṅgaṃ jalaṃ na yāvattava saṃspṛśanti  |
mlecchāḥ pulindāstvatha yātudhānāḥ pibanti cāmbhastava devi puṇyam  || 93.44 ||
te'pi pramuñcanti bhayāttu ghorātkimatra viprā bhayapāpabhītāḥ  |
ghore yuge'smin kalināmnyapuṇye tvaṃ bhrājase kālajalaughapūrṇe  || 93.45 ||
devyatra nakṣatrapathe'pi gaṅgā tava prasādāddivi devyatiṣṭhat |
kāle yatheṣṭaṃ paripālaya tvaṃ yāsyāma lokaṃ tava suprasādāt || 93.46 ||
vayaṃ tathā tvaṃ kuru naḥ prasādaṃ tvāmāśritāstvāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā vai  |
gatistvamevātra piteva putraṃ tvamādideva prabhave vicitre  || 93.47 ||
kāle'pyanāvṛṣṭibhavaṃ kṣayaṃ ca rakṣasva (?)  |
sarvaṃ jagataḥ svarūpam .... ....  || 93.48 ||
evaṃ stutā mahādevī narmadā saritāṃ varā  |
pratyakṣā sā parābhūtā brāhmaṇānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 93.49 ||

narmadovāca - 
tuṣṭāhaṃ varadā viprā dāsye vo vāñchitaṃ phalam  |
tato'varṣanmahāmeghā dhānyaṃ ca pracuraṃ tathā  || 93.50 ||
kandamūlaphalaṃ śākaṃ sukhaṃ sarvasaṃśritam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
paṭhanti ye stotramidaṃ narendra śṛṇvanti bhaktyā parayaā prapannāḥ  || 93.51 ||
tebhyo'ntakāle sariduttameyaṃ gatiṃ viśaddhāṃ nitarāṃ dadāti  |
prātaḥ samutthāya samāna evaṃ saṃkīrtayedrudramumāṃ ca devīm  || 93.52 ||
pāpāni sarvāṇi layaṃ grayānti samāśrayante ca mahānubhāvāḥ  |
pāpaistu muktā divi modayante śambhorgirā caiva tu nānyathā ca  || 93.53 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dṛṣṭāste puruṣā nānyā narmadātaṭamāśritāḥ  |
snānadevārccanairyuktāḥ pañcaiva tu mahābalāḥ  || 93.54 ||
te dṛṣṭā brāhmaṇaiḥ sarvairvedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ  |

viprā ūcu̱ḥ
dinānte ca striyoryugmaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ raudraṃ bhayāvaham  || 93.55 ||
trayo vṛddhāśca puruṣāḥ pāśahastā bhayāvahāḥ  |
durddharā durnisaṃkāśā itaścetaśca cañcalāḥ  || 93.56 ||
vyāharanti bhiyā vācā ākāṅkṣā darśanasya ca  |
aparaspariṇassarve nirīkṣante parasparam  || 93.57 ||
teṣu saṅgheṣu yatproktaṃ tatsarvaṃ kathayāmi te  |

puruṣā ūcu̱ḥ
tīrthāvagāhanaṃ sarvaiḥ pūrvapaścimadakṣiṇe  || 93.58 ||
uttare ca kṛtaṃ bhaktyā na pāpaṃ tadvyapohitam  |
niṣpāpāścātra sañjātāstīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 93.59 ||
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve agnijvālopamā dvijāḥ  |
pātakāni ca ghorāṇi yānyacintyāni dehinām  || 93.60 ||
pāpiṣṭhena tu cānena garordārā vidūṣitāḥ  |
hṛtaṃ cānyena mitrasya suvarṇaṃ ca tathā ca vai  || 93.61 ||
brahmahatyā kṛtā raudrā kṛtaṃ cānyena pātakam  |
surāpānaṃ tu cāpyasya saṃjātaṃ cānyakāmataḥ  || 93.62 ||
govadhaṃ pāpametena kṛtamekena pāpinā  |
akāmato'pi sarveṣāṃ pātakāni narādhipa  || 93.63 ||
brāhmaṇāstāṃstu te dṛṣṭvā pāpiṣṭhā gatakalmaṣāḥ  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāveṇa narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  || 93.64 ||
na kvācitpātakānāṃ tu praveśaścātra jāyate  |
evaṃ saṃcintya te sarve pāpiṣṭhāśca parasparam  || 93.65 ||
kṣiprameva samuddhṛtya vicintya hṛdaye harim  |
snātvā revājale puṇye tārpitvā pitṛdevatāḥ  || 93.66 ||
natvā tu bhāskaraṃ devaṃ hṛdi dhyātvā janārdanam  |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ bhaktyā jvalite jātavedasi  || 93.67 ||
patitāḥ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha pāpodvignāśca pāpinaḥ  |
sāttvikīṃ kāmanāṃ kṛtvā tyaktvā prāṇān divaṅgatāḥ  || 93.68 ||
niṣpāpāste mahābhāgairnarmadāyottare taṭe  |
vimānasthāstadā dṛṣṭā brāhmaṇaiste yudhiṣṭhira  || 93.69 ||
āścaryamatulaṃ dṛṣṭamṛṣibhirnarmadātaṭe  |
tadā prabhṛti te sarve rāgadveṣavivarjitāḥ  || 93.70 ||
ravitīrthaṃ dvijā hṛṣṭāḥ sevante mokṣakāṅkṣayā  || 93.71 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'rkatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma trinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 94

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tīrthasyāsya ca yatpuṇyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |
paṇḍito vṛddhabhāvena bhaktyā trāto nareśvara  || 94.1 ||
uddeśaṃ kathayiṣyāmi dṛṣṭvā vāntarameva ca  |
kurukṣetraṃ yathāpūtaṃ ravitīrthaṃ śrutaṃ tathā  || 94.2 ||
īśvareṇa purākhyātaṃ ṣaṇmukhasya yudhiṣṭhira  |
śrutaṃ rudragaṇaiḥ sarvairahaṃ tatra samīpagaḥ  || 94.3 ||
mārtaṇḍagrahaṇe prāpte ye vrajanti ṣaḍānana  |
ravitīrthe kurukṣetre tulyameva phalaṃ bhavet || 94.4 ||
snāne dāne tathā jāpye home caiva viśeṣataḥ  |
kurukṣetre tathā puṇyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 94.5 ||
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  |
ravitīrthe viśeṣeṇa raviparvaṇi bhūmipa  || 94.6 ||
tatra sūryadine bhaktyā vyatīpāte ca vaidhṛtau  |
saṃkramaṇe grahaṇe vā'pi ye vrajanti jitendriyāḥ  || 94.7 ||
kāmakrodhavinirmuktā rāgadveṣaistathaiva ca  |
kathāṃ ca vaiṣṇavīṃ pārtha vedādhyayanameva ca  || 94.8 ||
ṛgvedaṃ vā yajurvedaṃ sāmavedamatharvaṇam  |
ṛcamekāṃ tu japtvaiva samastaphalamāpnuyuḥ  || 94.9 ||
gāyatryā ca caturvedaphalamāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
prabhāte pūjayeddevamannadānahiraṇmayaiḥ  || 94.10 ||
tatra snātvā dvije yogye kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
pṛthivī tena vai dattā saśailavanakānanā  || 94.11 ||
bhūrlokaśca bhuvarloko maharloko janastathā  |
tapaḥ satya tathā lokaṃ pātālānyekaviṃśatiḥ  || 94.12 ||
tena dattaṃ bhavetsarvaṃ godānaṃ yena vai kṛtam  |
teṣāmabdakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ naśyedvai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 94.13 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
puṇyā gatiḥ kathaṃ tāta etatkathaya tattvataḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
purā kṛtayugasyādau brahmālokapitāmahaḥ  || 94.14 ||
utpādya tathā sakalaṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
ākulā pṛthivī tena saṃjātā pāṇḍunandana  || 94.15 ||
tataḥ paścādvicintyedaṃ kathaṃ loko bhaviṣyati  |
kathaṃ svargaṃ prayāsyanti mānavā bhaktisaṃyutāḥ  || 94.16 ||
bhānuścaiva kathaṃ prīto lokānāṃ jāyate bhṛśam  |
virañceścintyamānasya agnikuṇḍātsamutthitā  || 94.17 ||
jvalantī tejasā pūrṇā ghaṇṭā lulitaniḥsvanā  |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ tu mahābhāgāṃ kapilāṃ kuṇḍamadhyagām  || 94.18 ||
brahmālokagurustāṃ tu praṇamyedamuvāca ha  |
namaste kāpile puṇye sarvalokeṣvanuttame  || 94.19 ||
māṅgalye maṅgale devi triṣu lokeṣu vandite  |
tvaṃ lakṣmīstvaṃ dhṛtirmedhā pavitrā tu varānane  || 94.20 ||
umādevīti vikhyātā tvaṃ śacī nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
vaiṣṇavī tvaṃ mahādevī brahmāṇī tvaṃ varānane  || 94.21 ||
kumārī tvaṃ mahābhāge bhaktiḥ śraddhā tathaiva ca  |
kālarātrī tu bhūtānāṃ kumārī parameśvarī  || 94.22 ||
tvaṃ truṭistvaṃ ghaṭī caiva muhūrtakṣaṇameva ca  |
saṃvatsarartavo māsāstvaṃ kālaḥ puruṣaḥ sadā  || 94.23 ||
nāsti kiṃcittvayā hīnaṃ trailokye sacarācare  |
evaṃ stutā tu sā tena kapilā parameṣṭhinā  || 94.24 ||
tamuvāca mahābhāgā prahṛṣṭā parameṣṭhinam  |
prasannā tava vākyena devadeva jagadguro  || 94.25 ||

brahmovāca - 
jagaddhitāya janitā mayā tvaṃ parameśvari  |
svargānmartyamito yāhi lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 94.26 ||
sarvadevamayīṃ tvāṃ tu sarvalokamayīṃ tathā  |
vidhinā ye pradāsyanti teṣāṃ vāsastriviṣṭape  || 94.27 ||
evamuktā tataḥ puṇyā kapilā parameṣṭhinā  |
ājagāma bhuvaḥ pṛṣṭhe vandyamānā surottamaiḥ  || 94.28 ||
pavitrā vasudhā tena saṃjātā pāṇḍunandana  |
tasyā aṅgeṣu ye devāstānme nigadataḥ śṛṇu  || 94.29 ||
mukhe hyagniḥ sthito devo danteṣu ca bhujaṅgamāḥ  |
dhātā vidhātā coṣṭhau ca aśvinau karṇasaṃsthitau  || 94.30 ||
vajrapāṇiḥ sthitaḥ śṛṅge śṛṅgamadhye pitāmahaḥ  |
kālo madhyagatastāta pāśabhṛdvaruṇastathā  || 94.31 ||
yamaśca bhagavān deva āsyasyopari saṃsthitaḥ  |
nābhimadhye sthitaśchando devā jaṅghāsu bhārata  || 94.32 ||
vasundharā sthitā nābhyāṃ parvatāḥ sandhiṣu sthitāḥ  |
vṛkṣā gulmāni vallyaśca sandhimārge vyavasthitāḥ  || 94.33 ||
ṛṣayo romakūpeṣu saṃsthitāḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
snāyusthāḥ pitaraḥ sarve prasravaṃ sarvatīrthajam  || 94.34 ||
sarveṣāṃ gomayaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam  |
khureṣu pannagāḥ sarve pucchāgre sūryaraśmayaḥ  || 94.35 ||
evaṃ bhūtā tu kapilā sarvadevamayī kila  |
ye dhyāyanti gṛhe bhaktyā te muktā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 94.36 ||
prātarutthāya yo bhaktyā nityaṃ kuryātpradakṣiṇam  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasundharā  || 94.37 ||
kapilāpañcagavyena yaḥ snāpayati śaṅkaram  |
viṣṇuṃ vā jagadādhāraṃ sūryaṃ vā tvanyadaivatam  || 94.38 ||
pañcāmṛtena saṃsnāpya bhaktyā gavyena pāṇḍava  |
abdaṃ vā śrotriye nityaṃ kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  || 94.39 ||
tulyametatphalaṃ proktaṃ śaṅkareṇa yudhiṣṭhira  |
ya pradāsyati viprāya ravitīrthe suyantritaḥ  || 94.40 ||
kapilāṃ vātha kṛṣṇāṃ vā śvetāṃ raktāṃ ca pāṭalām  |
kṣīriṇīṃ taruṇīṃ śubhrāṃ savatsāṃ vastrasaṃyutām  || 94.41 ||
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurīṃ viṣṇurūpaṃ dvijaṃ smaran  |
ātmānaṃ viṣṇurūpaṃ ca dhenumādityarūpiṇīm  || 94.42 ||
yo dadāti mahābāho tasya vāsastriviṣṭapo  |
brahmahatyā vinirmuktaḥ surāpānaṃ ca dāruṇam  || 94.43 ||
gurvaṅganāgamaḥ steyo svāmidroho gavāṃ vadhaḥ  |
mitraviśvāsaghātaṃ ca gurunindāsamudbhavam  || 94.44 ||
sthitirnaṣṭe ca vaṃśe ca nirmālyasyāvalaṅghanam  |
kanyāgamāgamaścaiva abhakṣasya tu bhakṣaṇam  || 94.45 ||
vṛṣalī gamanaṃ raudraṃ kurūpāgamanodbhavam  |
agnidaṃ garadaṃ caiva kūṭasākṣyasamudbhavam  || 94.46 ||
tatsarvaṃ nāśayetpāpaṃ dhenurdānena pāṇḍava  |
surabhisaṃgame puṇye niṣṭhute pāpanāśane  || 94.47 ||
śrāddhaṃ pretasya yo bhaktyā dāpayetkuntinandana  |
tasya prīto bhavetsūryaḥ suprīto bhava eva ca  || 94.48 ||
dānaṃ yaddīyate tatra sūryamuddiśya bhaktitaḥ  |
mitraloke sukhaṃ yāti narmadāyāḥ prasādataḥ  || 94.49 ||
dadhicchande madhucchande devayāne sukhaprade  |
bhīmeśvare kuruśreṣṭha samaṃ puṇyaṃ praśasyate  || 94.50 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ prakhyātaṃ tīrthapañcakam  |
ye na jānanti bhūmisthā te mṛtā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 94.51 ||
snānaṃ devārcanaṃ jāpyaṃ homaṃ brāhmaṇapūjanam  |
bhūmidānena vastreṇa annadānena bhaktitaḥ  || 94.52 ||
upānacchatraśayānā gṛhadānena pāṇḍava  |
grāmakanyāpradānena gajadānahayena ca  || 94.53 ||
vidyāśakaṭadānena sarveṣāmabhayapradaḥ  |
sa yāti sarvatīrthāni ravitīrthaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 94.54 ||
tīrthayātrāprabhāveṇa vyādhayo yānti saṃkṣayam  |
śatravo mitratāṃ yānti viṣaṃ vā hyamṛtāyate  || 94.55 ||
grahāḥ sarve'bhavan prītāḥ prītastasya divākaraḥ  |
tīrthasyāsya payaḥ pītvā yatpuṇyaṃ jāyate nṛṇām  || 94.56 ||
abdamaśvatthasevāyāṃ kapilāyāstu dānataḥ  |
tatphalaṃ kathayiṣyāmi bhaktyā tava mahīpate  || 94.57 ||
pāpāḥ sarve vilīyante bhinnapātre jalaṃ yathā  |
tīrthasyābhimukhaṃ vṛttaṃ gacchatāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 94.58 ||
iha bāhyāntaraṃ tīrthaṃ kathitaṃ tava pārthiva  |
pāpiṣṭhānāṃ kṛtaghnānāṃ svāmimitravirodhinām  || 94.59 ||
tīrthākhyānaṃ śubhaṃ teṣāṃ gopitavyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ  || 94.60 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe ādityeśvaratīrthakīrtano nāma caturnavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 95

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra karañjeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra te nihatāstāta dānavāstatpadānugaiḥ  || 95.1 ||
indrādyaiścaiva saṃhṛṣṭaiḥ stuto yajñaḥ subuddhibhiḥ  |
teṣāṃ ye putrapautrāśca pūrvavairamanusmaran  || 95.2 ||
tatra sthāstu surāḥ sarve sthāpayitvā hyumāpatim  |
indracandrayamāḥ sūryaḥ sthāpayitveṣṭasiddhaye  || 95.3 ||
hṛṣṭapuṣṭāḥ surāḥ sarve jagmurākāśasasthitāḥ  |
dānavānāṃ mahābhāga karoṭyaḥ patitā yataḥ  || 95.4 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ karoṭīti mahīpate  |
vikhyātaṃ bhārate loke bhūpṛṣṭhe pāṇḍunandana  || 95.5 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ śubhe pakṣe tu bhaktitaḥ  |
upoṣya śūlinaścāgre rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 95.6 ||
tatkathālāpasaṃyuktaṃ vedodgītaṃ tathaiva ca  |
prabhāte vimale prāpte sthāṇuṃ sampūjya yatnataḥ  || 95.7 ||
pañcāmṛtena saṃsnāpya śrīkhaṇḍenaiva cārcayet |
śatapallavapuṣpaiśca pūjayecca prayatnataḥ  || 95.8 ||
bahurūpaṃ japenmantraṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tu pradāya ca  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti ādityeśvara nārmade  || 95.9 ||
śrutatīrthaprabhāvaṃ vai yaḥ paṭhecca narādhipa  |
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi bhaktyā tava mahīpate  || 95.10 ||
yathoktena vidhānena nābhimātre jale sthitaḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva pretāya kārayeta jitendriyaḥ  || 95.11 ||
vividhairagrapāṭhaiśca vedādhyayanatatparaiḥ  |
gohiraṇyena sampūjya vastratāmbūlabhojanaiḥ  || 95.12 ||
bhūṣaṇaiḥ paṭṭadānaiśca brāhmaṇaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu sampūjya kāmikaṃ bhojanaṃ dadet || 95.13 ||
bhavetkoṭigaṇaṃ tasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
tatra tīrthe yathā bhaktyā tyajeddehaṃ ca mānada  || 95.14 ||
tasya tīrthe bhavetpuṇyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |
yāvadasthimanuṣyasya tiṣṭhate narmadāmbhasi  || 95.15 ||
tāvadvasati dharmātmā śivaloke sudurlabhe  |
tataḥ kālātpracyutaśca devo mānuṣyatāṃ gataḥ  || 95.16 ||
koṭidhvajapatiḥ śrīmān jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
sarvadharmasamāyukto medhāvī jīvaputrakaḥ  || 95.17 ||
vikhyātaśca dharāpṛṣṭhe dīrghāyurmānavo bhavet |
indracandrayamaiḥ rudrairādityairvasubhistathā  || 95.18 ||
viśvedevaistathā sarvaiḥ sthāpitāstridaśeśvaraḥ  |
narmadottarakūle tu lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 95.19 ||
mānavaḥ pretamuddiśya prāsādaṃ kārayettu yaḥ  |
tasminnaravaraśreṣṭhaḥ sa sadgatimavāpnuyāt || 95.20 ||
nyāyopārjitadravyeṇa yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute'tra vai  |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ striyaḥ śūdrāśca satkṛtāḥ  || 95.21 ||
te'pi yānti pare loke śāṅkare surapūjite  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā māhātmyaṃ tīrthajaṃ nṛpa  || 95.22 ||
tasya pāpaṃ praṇaśyeta ṣaṇmāsena tu yatkṛtam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra kumāreśvaramuttamam  || 95.23 ||
prasiddhaṃ sarvatīrthānāmagastyeśvaramuttamam  |
ṣaṇmukhena tapastaptaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  || 95.24 ||
snānaṃ ca parayā bhuktyā siddhiḥ prāptā narādhipa  |
devasainyādhipo rājan sarvaśatruvimardanaḥ  || 95.25 ||
ugratejā mahātmā ca saṃjātastīrthasevanāt |
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ vikhyātaṃ narmadātaṭe  || 95.26 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo bhaktyā ekacitto jitendriyaḥ  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ kārttikasya viśeṣataḥ  || 95.27 ||
snāpayedgirijānāthaṃ dadhidugdhena sarpiṣā  |
gītaṃ tatra prakartavyaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ yathāvidhi  || 95.28 ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ śrotriyaiḥ pārtha ṣaṭkarmanirataiḥ sadā  |
yatkiṃciddīyate tatra akṣayaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 95.29 ||
sarvatīrthātparaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ śaśinā nṛpa  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kumāreśvarajaṃ phalam  || 95.30 ||
kumāradarśanātpuṇyaṃ prāpyate pāṇḍunandana  |
mṛtaḥ svargamavāpnoti satyamīśvarabhāṣitam  || 95.31 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra agastyeśvaramuttamam  |
tatra siddho mahābhāga agastyo munipuṅgavaḥ  || 95.32 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kathaṃ siddho mahābhāga agastyo munipuṅgavaḥ  |
kumbhodbhavo mahābhāga mitrāvaruṇasambhavaḥ  || 95.33 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya tatsarvaṃ kathayasva me  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
mahāpraśno mahārāja paripṛṣṭaśca yastvayā  || 95.34 ||
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ sadā  |
purākṛtayuge tāta bhārārtā jagatī sthitā  || 95.35 ||
vijñaptukāmā deveśaṃ nākapṛṣṭhaṃ gatā nṛpa  |
indrāya kathayāmāsa daityabhārārditaṃ jagat || 95.36 ||

indra uvāca - 
brahmā ca jagataḥ kartā tavaiva mama sundari  |
brahmalokaṃ gamiṣyāmi mantribhirdaivataiḥ saha  || 95.37 ||
tataḥ sarve gatāstatra yatrāsau kamalāsanaḥ  |

bṛhaspatiruvāca - 
brahmannirlambanā jātā daityabhārādvasundharā  || 95.38 ||
asahantī tu taṃ bhāraṃ yāti devī rasātalam  |
pratīkāraṃ pṛthivyāśca kuruṣva jagatīpate  || 95.39 ||
sarvasattvopakārāya sṛṣṭistvayi jagatpate  |

pitāmaha uvāca - 
kartā'smi sarvajagatāmayonikalaśodbhavaḥ  || 95.40 ||
agastyastapasāṃ rāśiḥ śakto daityanivāraṇe  |
ekataḥ sarvadevānāṃ balaṃ tejaśca jāyate  || 95.41 ||
ekato ṛṣimukhyasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 95.42 ||
tathaiva kāraṇaṃ cānyatkathayanti sma bhārata  |
vijñātaṃ devadeveśa viśvāmitracikīrṣitam  || 95.43 ||
triśaṅkvarthe ca yajño'yaṃ viśvāmitreṇa sādhitaḥ  |
spardhayā ca vaśiṣṭhasya yajñāṅgāni samāsṛjat || 95.44 ||
spardhayā sṛjatākāśaṃ bhūmiṃ cānyāṃ samāsṛjat |
yathā tu himavacchailaḥ pūrvāparamahodadhim  || 95.45 ||
vyāpyaiva saṃsthito bhūmyāṃ devakāryārthasādhakaḥ  |
tathāsau spardhate vindhyaḥ spardhayā kauśikasya ca  || 95.46 ||
tiṣṭhanti devakāryāṇi kauśikena sureśvara  |
kāryadvayapratīkāraṃ cintaya svajagadguro  || 95.47 ||

brahmovāca - 
ekastvasya gururvipro hyagastyo munipuṅgavaḥ  |
utpathe vartamānasya kauśikasya durāsadaḥ  || 95.48 ||
agastyo mārgabhettā vai bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ  |
gururātmavatāṃ śāstā sarveṣāṃ vai na saṃśayaḥ  || 95.49 ||
vardhanaṃ parvatasyāsya devamārgapravartanam  |
śāsaḥ kauśikaviprasya vasudhāyāṃ samantataḥ  || 95.50 ||
kṣamaḥ samastakāryāṇāṃ mitrāvaruṇanandanaḥ  |
evaṃ tu niścayaṃ kṛtvā devāḥ sendrapitāmahāḥ  || 95.51 ||
yayurvasundharāsārdhaṃ himavantaṃ nageśvaram  |
dadṛśuste sthitaṃ vipraṃ dhyāyamānaṃ ca yoginam  || 95.52 ||
sudṛḍhaṃ niścaladhyānaṃ mokṣamārganiyāmakam  |
taṃdṛṣṭvā stotumārabdhāḥ sendracandrāḥ savāruṇāḥ  || 95.53 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
jayamicchasva devānāṃ bhagavan kalaśodbhava  |
prasādasumukho bhūtvā devānāṃ bhayamāgatam  || 95.54 ||

agastya uvāca - 
kiṃ kāryaṃ tu samutpannaṃ yena dūraṃ samāgatāḥ  |
ekāntavāsinaṃ nityaṃ taṃ māṃ yūyaṃ surāśca bhoḥ  || 95.55 ||
ucyatāṃ yanmayā kāryaṃ tatsarvaṃ karavāṇyaham  |
tato mandāniloddhūtakamalākaraśobhinā  || 95.56 ||
guruṃ netrasahasreṇa prerayāmāsa vṛtrahā  |

vākpatiruvāca - 
tvayā pūrvaṃ mahābhāga devakāryārthasiddhaye  || 95.57 ||
samudrāḥ karṣitāḥ sarve īśvareṇa yathā jagat |
vidhvastāstridaśāḥ sarve dānavairbaladarpitaiḥ  || 95.58 ||
jitā devāstu te sarve dānavebhyaḥ parāṅmukhāḥ  |
teṣāṃ varāpahārāya samudrāḥ śoṣitāḥ purā  || 95.59 ||
sāmprataṃ duḥkhitā dhātrī paśyemāṃ bhūtadhāriṇīm  |
daityabhāreṇa duḥkhārtā bhūmirjātā rasātalam  || 95.60 ||
gantavyaṃ dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ taporāśe dvijottama  |
narmadodadhimaryādāṃ kuru puṇyāṃ mahādvija  || 95.61 ||
vṛddhiṃ vindhyanagasyāpi devakāryaṃ samuddhara  |
kauśiko'tha kanīyāṃste ya unmārgapravartakaḥ  || 95.62 ||

agastya uvāca - 
devakāryaṃ kariṣyāmi sukhaṃ tiṣṭha vasundhare  |
gacchāmi dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ śaktaḥ śiṣyasya vāraṇe  || 95.63 ||
vardhanaṃ parvatasyāsya vārayāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
yāmyāṃ gatvā suraiḥ sārdhaṃ draṣṭavyo yaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 95.64 ||
siṃhasthe bhāskare vipra yena yāsyanti bhaktitaḥ  |
naśyate ca dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ teṣāṃ saukhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 95.65 ||
adhikārāya devānāṃ sa ca dṛṣṭo bhaviṣyati  |
tatpratijñāya gīrvāṇāḥ samaṃ vasudhayā gatāḥ  || 95.66 ||
agastyaṃ tapasāṃ rāśiṃ nirgacchantaḥ samantataḥ  |
agastyapadavikṣepāccalitā ca vasundharā  || 95.67 ||
manovegena samprāptaḥ kauśiko yatra tāpasaḥ  |
kauśiko'pi guruṃ dṛṣṭvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca  || 95.68 ||
dhanye'haṃ muniśārdūlaprīto'haṃ tava darśanāt |
arghapātraṃ samādāya dadhyakṣatasamanvitam  || 95.69 ||
dūrvā ca candanaṃ pūjyabhaktyā pātre samāhitam  |
gurupādaparikṣipta uvāca madhuraṃ tadā  || 95.70 ||
ādeśo dīyatāṃ tāta tava preṣyo dvijottama  |

agastya uvāca - 
devakāryavighātaṃ ca kauśika tvaṃ visarjaya  || 95.71 ||
devakāryavisṛṣṭena karmaṇā na pravartase  |
yadi te niścalā bhaktirviśvāmitra mamopari  || 95.72 ||
tadā tvaṃ varjayeḥ sarvamunmārgasya pravartanam  |

viśvāmitra uvāca - 
gururātmavatāṃ śāstā rājā śāstā durātmanām  || 95.73 ||
iha pracchannapāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ  |
spardhayā ca vaśiṣṭhasya triśaṅkurme suyācitaḥ  || 95.74 ||
adya prabhṛti tatsarvaṃ tyaktameva dvijottama  |
ityuktaḥ prayayau śīghraṃ revātīraṃ sudurlabham  || 95.75 ||
uttaraṃ taṭamāsādya tapastatra samārabhat |
narmadā triṣu lokeṣu pavitrā pāpanāśinī  || 95.76 ||
niścayaṃ paramaṃ kṛtvā mitrāvaruṇanandanaḥ  |
śilātale niviṣṭastu cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 95.77 ||
vāyubhakṣaḥ sadā kālaṃ kumbhayonirmahātapāḥ  |
jñāto bhaktiyutaḥ śreṣṭha īśvareṇa yudhiṣṭhira  || 95.78 ||
pratyakṣo dvādaśe varṣe saṅgataḥ pārvatīpatiḥ  |

īśvara uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu muniśreṣṭha tapasā dyotitaṃ nabhaḥ  || 95.79 ||
niścayaṃ tava tuṣṭo'smi mitrāvaruṇanandana  |
varṣāyutasahasreṇa nānyeṣāṃ varado hyaham  || 95.80 ||

agastya uvāca - 
saṃsārapalvalātītasṛṣṭijanmavivarjita  |
durlakṣyāsurasaṅghānāṃ pramatheśa namo'stu te  || 95.81 ||
nandiskandagaṇā devā vṛthā kliśyanti mohitāḥ  |
sanatkumāramukhyāśca ṛṣayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  || 95.82 ||
tvadrūpaṃ te na jānanti śambho nātha namo'stu te  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarve dhyāyanti tvāmaharniśam  || 95.83 ||
naite paśyanti tvadrūpaṃ dhātardeva namo'stu te  |

īśvara uvāca - 
prasannastava viprendra ūrdhvaretastvayonija  || 95.84 ||
tava bhaktigṛhīto'haṃ prasanna umayā saha  |

agastya uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  || 95.85 ||
pratyakṣo bhava tīrthe'smin yadi satyaṃ varapradaḥ  |
antarjale sadākālaṃ dharmādhyakṣo maheśvara  || 95.86 ||
śilāyāṃ bhava nityaṃ ca narmadāyottare taṭe  |
devakāryasya kartāhaṃ tvatprasādājjagatpate  || 95.87 ||
tatheti coktvā vṛṣavāhano'pi jagāma kailāsanagaṃ nageśaḥ  |
ayonijo yogabalena yuktaḥ pravidyayā liṅgabalācchivasya  || 95.88 ||
jagāma dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ surasaṅghairabhiṣṭutaḥ  |
tapovanaṃ yathāpuṇyaṃ devadānavasevitam  || 95.89 ||
praviṣṭo muniśārdūlaḥ pavitraṃ devakambalam  |
niścalā suṣamādevī saṃsthitā dharaṇī tathā  || 95.90 ||
puṣpāṇi vavṛṣurdevā jayaśabdaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
tasya tīrthasya yatpuṇyaṃ kathyatāṃ munisuvrata  || 95.91 ||
ādimadhyāvasāne ca brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bāndhavaiḥ  |
pitṝṇāṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ sarvasattvopakārakam  || 95.92 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
pitṝnmokṣadaṃ proktaṃ sarvakāle janādhipa  |
śivākhyāṃ kārttike māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  || 95.93 ||
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
śamītaruṃ samāsthāya rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 95.94 ||
tatkathālāpasaṃyukto dharmākhyānairdvijaiḥ saha  |
gavāṃ ghṛtena deveśaṃ rātrau ca snāpayetpunaḥ  || 95.95 ||
ghaṭenaiva ghaṭārdhena tadardhena svaśaktitaḥ  |
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpaṃ ghṛtaṃ viprāya dāpayet || 95.96 ||
pañcāmṛtena gavyena snāpayetparameśvaram  |
prabhāte pūjayedviprān svadāraniratān sadā  || 95.97 ||
vedābhyasanaśīlāṃśca paradāravivarjitān  |
śūdrasevāratā nityaṃ dhūrtakarmaratā janāḥ  || 95.98 ||
patitāḥ kūṭasākṣyeṇa pratigraharatāḥ sadā  |
vedadveṣaṇaśīlāśca kubjāśca vikalāḥ sadā  || 95.99 ||
hīnātiriktagātrā ye dvijāḥ śrāddhe vivarjitāḥ  |
vedoktena viśuddhāṅgāḥ pūjyā nityaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 95.100 ||
bhūmidānena vastreṇa kanyādānairviśeṣataḥ  |
śrāddhakāleṣu yogeṣu bhartavyā bhaktitatparaiḥ  || 95.101 ||
godānaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ śreyo'rthamātmanastathā  |
savatsāṃ kṣīriṇīṃ śubhrāṃ puṣṭāṃ vai śīlasaṃyutām  || 95.102 ||
kambalaṃ parayā bhaktyā pādukopānahau tathā  |
hiraṇyarukmiṇīṃ kanthāṃ tāmbūlaṃ bhojanaṃ tathā  || 95.103 ||
ghaṇṭābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāṃ vastrayugmāvaguṇṭhitām  |
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurīṃ kāṃsyadohena saṃyutām  || 95.104 ||
uccārya parayā bhaktyā yāvadāhūtasaṃplavam  |
sarva koṭiguṇaṃ pārtha śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  || 95.105 ||
tīrthākhyānaṃ ca yo bhaktyā paṭhate śṛṇute'thavā  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ śivaloke vasatyapi  || 95.106 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe'gastyatīrthavarṇano nāma pañcanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 96

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
athānandeśvaraṃ gacchetsarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
rudrasya paramānando yatra jāto yudhiṣṭhira  || 96.1 ||
tattīrthaṃ kathayiṣyāmi sūryapāpakṣayaṃ karam  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
ānandaścaiva saṃjāto rudrasya dvijasattama  || 96.2 ||
kathayasva mahābhāga saṃkṣepānmunisattama  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kathayāmi nṛpaśreṣṭha ānandeśvaramuttamam  || 96.3 ||
dānavānāṃ vadhaṃ kṛtvā devadevaśca śaṅkaraḥ  |
pūjito daivataiḥ sarvaiḥ kinnarairyakṣapannagaiḥ  || 96.4 ||
ānandaṃ paramaṃ prāpya nanarta vṛṣavāhanaḥ  |
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsādya gaurī cārddhāṅgadhāritā  || 96.5 ||
bhūtavetālakaṅkālairbhairavairbhairavo vṛtaḥ  |
narmadāyottare tīre dakṣiṇe pāṇḍunandana  || 96.6 ||
tuṣṭairmarudgaṇaistatra sthāpitaḥ kamalāsanaḥ  |
tadā prabhṛti vai deva ānandeśvara ucyate  || 96.7 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ narādhipa  |
vidhiṃ snātvārcayeddevāṃ sugandhena vilepayet || 96.8 ||
brāhmaṇān pūjayettatra yathāśaktyā yudhiṣṭhira  |
godānaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vastradānaṃ tathaiva ca  || 96.9 ||
vasantasya trayodaśyāṃ śrāddhaṃ tatraiva kārayet |
iṅgudairbadarairbilvairakṣatena jalena vā  || 96.10 ||
pretānāṃ kārayecchrāddhamānandeśvaratīrthake  |
pretā ānanditāḥ syuste yāvadāhūta samplavam  || 96.11 ||
santatirdhanasaukhyaṃ ca saptajanmani jāyate  |
ānandaśca bhavetteṣāṃ janma janma yudhiṣṭhira  || 96.12 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra mātṛtīrthamanuttamam  |
saṅgamasya samīpasthaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 96.13 ||
mātarastatra rājendra saṃjātā narmadātaṭe  |
umayā yācitastatra vyālayajñopavītakaḥ  || 96.14 ||
uvāca yoginīvṛndaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ na śobhanam  |
uvāca varadaścāsmi yogivṛndavarapradaḥ  || 96.15 ||

yoginya ūcu̱ḥ
ajeyāḥ sarvadevānāṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara  |
tīrthānāmabhisaṃkhyāne prakhyātā vasudhātale  || 96.16 ||
evaṃ bhavatu yoginyastatraivāntaradhīyata  || 96.17 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo martyo navamyāṃ vijitendriyaḥ  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā pūjayenmātṛmaṇḍalam  || 96.18 ||
tasya tā mātaraḥ prītāḥ prīto'yaṃ vṛṣavāhanaḥ  |
bandhyāyā mṛtavatsāyā aputrāyā yudhiṣṭhira  || 96.19 ||
snapanaṃ cārabhettatra mantrajñairbrāhmaṇottamaiḥ  |
sahiraṇyena kumbhena pañcaratnaphalānvitam  || 96.20 ||
snāpayetputrakāmā ca kāṃsyapātreṇa mantrataḥ  |
putrān sā labhate nārī vīryayuktān guṇānvitān  || 96.21 ||
yaṃ yaṃ kāmamabhidhyāyettaṃ taṃ sā labhate nṛpa  |
mātṛtīrthātparaṃ tīrthaṃ nāstyanyatpāṇḍunandana  || 96.22 ||
tasyaivānantaraṃ tāta jalamadhyeśvaraṃ param  |
liṅgeśvaramiti khyātaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 96.23 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
atyaktā sā tu revā yā kathaṃ tyaktā ca śambhunā  |
jalamadhye hi tiṣṭheta śūlapāṇiḥ pinākadhṛk || 96.24 ||
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi tava vākyāddvijottama  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
āścaryabhūtā loke'smin pratiṣṭhā pāṇḍunandana  || 96.25 ||
paṇḍito vṛddhabhāvena kathayāmi nṛpottama  |
ādau kṛtayuge tāta dānavo baladarpitaḥ  || 96.26 ||
kālabāṣpa iti prokto durjayo devadānavaiḥ  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ narmadāyā jale śubhe  || 96.27 ||
ārādhayanmahādevamugreṇa tapasā bhṛśam  |
tatastutoṣa bhagavān sapatnīko maheśvaraḥ  || 96.28 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
bho bho vatsa varaṃ brūhi tuṣṭo'haṃ tava bhaktitaḥ  |
devasya vacanaṃ śrutvā kālabāṣpo'bravīdvacaḥ  || 96.29 ||
devāścaiva mayā bhagnāḥ prasādāttava śūlinaḥ  |
saṃgrāme ca viṣaṇṇo'haṃ tasmādārādhanaṃ kṛtam  || 96.30 ||
hastaṃ śirasi yasyaiva dāsyāmi ca maheśvara  |
na sa jīvetpumāṃlloke varametaṃ dadasva me  || 96.31 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
yatte'bhilaṣitaṃ daitya tattathaiva bhaviṣyati  |
iti śrutvā vaco daityaḥ śambhumevābhidudruve  || 96.32 ||
hastaṃ te mūrdhni dāsyāmi na tatsatyaṃ vacastava  |
rudraḥ palāyitastena keśavaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 96.33 ||
nivedya keśavaṃ sarvaṃ tasminneva nyalīyata  |

keśava uvāca - 
hanmyahaṃ taṃ mahādeva duṣṭaṃ daityajaneśvaram  || 96.34 ||
hastaṃ śirasi tasyaiva dāpayāmi maheśvara  |
tatastvaritamāpannaḥ praviṣṭo namadātaṭe  || 96.35 ||
kṛṣṇaḥ strīveṣadhārī ca daityasaṃmukhamāgataḥ  |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇopetā niyuktā kāmasāyakaiḥ  || 96.36 ||
madhumāthavake śambhuṃ dhyātvā sarvatra kaiśavī  |
vanaṃ babhrāma sarvatra suśīlā vaṭapādapam  || 96.37 ||
kṣobhayantīva cittāni sā reme dharmanandana  |
riṅgamāṇaśca daityo'sau kālabāṣpaḥ sudurjanaḥ  || 96.38 ||
praviṣṭaḥ sa vane ramye yatra sā śubhalocanā  |
ahaṃ bhavāmi te bhartā durjayo devadānavaiḥ  || 96.39 ||
trailokyasvāminī tvaṃ ca prasīda mama sundari  |

śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca - 
yadi māṃ manyase bhāryāṃ pratyayaśca bhavenmama  || 96.40 ||

dānava uvāca - 
svayaṃ bhavāmi tanvaṅgi śapathaṃ mama sādhanam  |
tadahaṃ ca kariṣyāmi iti me satyabhāṣitam  || 96.41 ||

stryuvāca - 
kuruṣva tvaṃ mahābhāga śiro haste pradīyatām  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kāmāndhenaiva rājendra nikṣipto mastake karaḥ  || 96.42 ||
tatkṣaṇādabhavadbhasma dagdhastṛṇacayo yathā  |
keśavasyopari tadā puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ha  || 96.43 ||
gatāḥ sarve divaṃ devāḥ svasthānaṃ vigatajvarāḥ  |
kṣaurābdhimagamadviṣṇuḥ kālabāṣpe nipātite  || 96.44 ||
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā caritaṃ dānavasya ca  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yaḥ kuryātkāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  || 96.45 ||
uddhṛtāstena sarve vai narakācca pitāmahāḥ  |
kṣetre tasmiṃstu yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage  || 96.46 ||
tasya dānaphalaṃ sarvaṃ kurukṣetrādviśiṣyate  |
sparśate ya idaṃ liṅgaṃ śaṅkareṇa ca nirmitam  || 96.47 ||
sparśamātro manuṣyastu rudravāso'bhijāyate  |
etasmātkāraṇādrājaṃllokapālāśca devatāḥ  || 96.48 ||
durgādevī tathā caiva madhuhantā caturbhujaḥ  |
dānavādyāśca sarve'pi rakṣaṇe ceśvarasya ca  || 96.49 ||
rakṣante ca sadākālaṃ gṛhavyāpārarūpataḥ  |
putrabhrātṛsamā bhūtvā svāmisambandharūpiṇaḥ  || 96.50 ||
liṅgeśvaraṃ tu rājendra devairadyā'pi rakṣyate  || 96.51 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe bhasmāsuravadho nāma ṣaṇṇavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 97

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra dhanadaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 97.1 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ tatra prāpyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
caitramāse trayodaśyāṃ śuklapakṣe jitendriyaḥ  || 97.2 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  |
pañcāmṛtena rājendra snāpayedvaradaṃ vibhum  || 97.3 ||
pūjayedbhaktiyuktena gītavādyaṃ pradāpayet |
prabhāte pūjayedviprānātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  || 97.4 ||
pratigrahāvimuktāśca vidyāsiddhāntavādinaḥ  |
bhartavyā hi priyairbhaktyā parivādavivarjitāḥ  || 97.5 ||
pūjayedgohiraṇyena vastrālaṃkaraṇena ca  |
hastyaśvarathadānena sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 97.6 ||
trijanmajanitaṃ pāpaṃ dhanadasya prabhāvataḥ  |
svargadaṃ durvinītānāṃ vinītānāṃ ca muktidam  || 97.7 ||
dhanavān sa naravyāghra bhavejjanmani janmani  |
kulīnatvaṃ svarūpatvaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāsti nirantaram  || 97.8 ||
vyādhestu na bhayaṃ teṣāṃ nandeddhanadasevanāt |
dhanasya ca yastīrthe vidyāṃ vai pradadāti hi  || 97.9 ||
sa yāti bhāskaraṃ lokaṃ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
ahaṃ te kathayiṣyāmi cetihāsaṃ purātanam  || 97.10 ||
dve bhāryye kaśyapasyāstāṃ sarvalokeṣvanuttame  |
garutmān vinatāputraḥ kadrūputroragāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 97.11 ||
santoṣeṇa dvayaṃ tāta tiṣṭhataḥ kāśyape gṛhe  |
kadrvāstu bhaginī tatra iṣṭā ca vinatā tathā  || 97.12 ||
krīḍedvinatayā sārdhaṃ kaśyapo'pi prajāpatiḥ  |
tatastvekadine pārtha āśramasthā suśobhanā  || 97.13 ||
uccaiḥśravohayaṃ dṛṣṭvā ativegaṃ nabhaḥsthitam  |
paśya paśya ca tanvaṅgi aśvaṃ sarvatra pāṇḍuram  || 97.14 ||
dhāvamānamaviśrāntaṃ javena mānasopamam  |

kadrūruvāca - 
kathametattu tanvaṅgi kṛṣṇaṃ jalpasi pāṇḍuram  || 97.15 ||
asatyaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhadre yamalokaṃ gamiṣyasi  |

vinatovāca - 
satyānṛte tu vacane paṇo'yaṃ me'stu te'dhunā  || 97.16 ||
sahasraṃ caiva varṣāṇāmajñātvā dāsyatāṃ vrajet |
asatyā yadi me vāṇī kṛṣṇa uccaiḥśravāhayaḥ  || 97.17 ||
tadāhaṃ tvadgṛhe dāsī sarvadaiva bhavāmi hi  |
yadi tūccaiḥśravāḥ śveto dāsī tvaṃ madgṛhe punaḥ  || 97.18 ||
evaṃ parasparaṃ dvābhyāṃ dāsīyamabravīditi  |
svāśramaṃ hi gatā kadrū rātrau cintāturā sthitā  || 97.19 ||
śvetavarṇaṃ tu kathitaṃ śyāmaṃ tamaśvakaṃ tadā  |
putrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ pārtha paṇaścaiva kṛto mayā  || 97.20 ||
śrutaṃ sarvaistathā vākyaṃ sarpairmātṛpaṇastadā  |
jātā dāsī na sandehaḥ śveto bhāskaravāhanaḥ  || 97.21 ||

kadrūruvāca - 
yathāhaṃ na bhave dāsī tatkāryaṃ ca vicintyatām  |
uccaiḥśravo romakūpe viśadhvaṃ yūyameva ca  || 97.22 ||
ekaṃ muhūrtaṃ tiṣṭhadhvaṃ yāvatkṛṣṇaḥ pradṛśyate  |
kṣaṇenaikena bhavatāṃ dāsī sā bhavate mama  || 97.23 ||
dāsītve yā tu tanvaṅgī vinatā satyagārvitā  |
tataḥ svasthānagāḥ sarve bhavantu sukhinaḥ sadā  || 97.24 ||

sarpā ūcu̱ḥ
yathā tvaṃ jananī caiva sarveṣāṃ bhuvi pannagī  |
tathā sāpi viśeṣaṇa vañcitavyā na mātṛvat || 97.25 ||
tataḥ sā tena vākyena kruddhā kālānalopamā  |
mama vākyamakurvāṇā ye kecidbhuvi pannagāḥ  || 97.26 ||
havyavāhamukhaṃ sarve yāsyantītyavicāriṇaḥ  |
tena vākyena bhītāste hayaromasuveṣṭitāḥ  || 97.27 ||
naṣṭāḥ keciddiśo divyāḥ kadrūśāpabhayānvitāḥ  |
kecidgaṅgājale naṣṭāḥ kecinnaṣṭāḥ sarasvatīm  || 97.28 ||
kecinmahodadhiṃ nītāḥ praviṣṭā vindhyakandare  |
āśritya narmadātoyaṃ maṇināgottamo nṛpa  || 97.29 ||
cacāra vipulaṃ bhaktyā uttare narmadātaṭe  |
mātṛśāpadharo nāgaḥ praviṣṭo narmadājale  || 97.30 ||
tvatprasādena bho nātha mātṛśāpaṃ tarāmyaham  |
havyavāhamukhaṃ yasmātprayāmi na jagatpate  || 97.31 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
havyavāhamukhaṃ vatsa na yāsyasi mamājñayā  |
mama lokanivāso'pi tava putra bhaviṣyati  || 97.32 ||

maṇināga uvāca - 
asmin sthāne mahādeva sthīyatāmaṃśabhāgataḥ  |
sahasrāṃśena bhāgena sthīyatāṃ narmadājale  || 97.33 ||
upakārāya lokānāṃ mama nāmnā ca śaṅkara  |

īśvara uvāca - 
sthāpaya svaparaṃ liṅgamājñayā mama pannaga  || 97.34 ||
ityuktvāntardadhe devastadaiva śivayā saha  |
tatra tīrthe tu ye bhaktyā śucayo yatamānasāḥ  || 97.35 ||
pañcamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ śuklapakṣake  |
arcayanti sadā pārtha nopasarpanti te yamam  || 97.36 ||
dadhnā ca madhunā caiva ghṛtena kṣīrato janāḥ  |
snāpayanti virūpākṣamumādehārdhadhāriṇam  || 97.37 ||
kāmāṅgadahanaṃ devaṃ mahāsuraniṣūdanam  |
saṃsnāpayanti ye bhaktyā paśyanti paramaṃ padam  || 97.38 ||
ṣaṭkarmaniratāstāta śūdrapraṇayavarjitāḥ  |
te'pi yānti paraṃ lokaṃ sarvapāpavivarjitāḥ  || 97.39 ||
vrātyāṃśca durdharān ṣaṇḍhān vārdhakyāṃśca kṛṣīvalān  |
bhinnadṛṣṭikarān viprān kaścinnaiva ca pūjayet || 97.40 ||
vṛṣalī mandire yasya mahiṣaṃ yastu vāhayet |
te viprā dūratastyājyā vrate śrāddhe nṛpeśvara  || 97.41 ||
kāṇāḥ kuṇḍāśca golāśca vaidyāścaiva vivarjitāḥ  |
naite pūjyā dvijāḥ pārtha maṇināgeśvare śubhe  || 97.42 ||
yadīcchedūrdhvagamanāṃ pitṛṇāmātmanastathā  |
sarvāṅgarucirāṅgāśca sadā pūjyā dvijāstu vai  || 97.43 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ lokaṃ yāvadāhūtasamplavam  |
tataḥ svargāccyutaḥ so'pi jāyate vipule kule  || 97.44 ||
maṇināgeśvaraṃ devaṃ yaḥ paśyati narādhipa  |
dhenuṃ śayyāṃ tathā chatraṃ kanyāṃ dāsīṃ subhaktitaḥ  || 97.45 ||
pātre dadyāttu rājendra yadīcchecchreya ātmanaḥ  |
surabhīṇi ca puṣpāṇi gandhavastrāṇi dāpayet || 97.46 ||
dīpaṃ dhānyaṃ gṛhaṃ śubhraṃ sarvopaskarasaṃyutam  |
dadate ye narā bhaktyā te vrajanti triviṣṭapam  || 97.47 ||
maṇināge nṛpa svarṇapannago yaḥ pradīyate  || 97.48 ||
pātakāni pralīyanta āmapātre jalaṃ yathā  |
narmadātoyasaṃsiddhaṃ bhojyaṃ viprāya dīyate  || 97.49 ||
te'pi pāpairvinirmuktāḥ krīḍante daivataiḥ saha  |
tyāgino bhogasaṃyuktā dharmākhyānaratāḥ sadā  || 97.50 ||
devadvijagurorbhaktāstīrthasevāparāyaṇāḥ  |
mātāpitṛsvāmibhaktāḥ krodhadrohavivarjitāḥ  || 97.51 ||
etaiḥ sarvairguṇairyuktā ye narāḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
jāyante svargakāmāśca svarge vāso bhaviṣyati  || 97.52 ||
sarvatīrthavaraṃ tīrthaṃ maṇināgaṃ nṛpottama  |
tīrthākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi  || 97.53 ||
so'pi pāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
na viṣaṃ kramate teṣāṃ vicaranti yathecchayā  || 97.54 ||
bhādrapadyāṃ ca yaḥ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ bhādre snāyācca darśake  |
tasya puṇyaphalāvāptirākhyānakathanena tu  || 97.55 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe maṇināgatīrthavarṇano nāma saptanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 98

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dakṣiṇe narmadātīre tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ gopāleśvaramuttamam  || 98.1 ||
godehānniḥsṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ puṇyaṃ bhūmitale nṛpa  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
godehātniḥsṛtaṃ kasmālliṅgaṃ pāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 98.2 ||
dakṣiṇe narmadā tīre maṇināgasamīpataḥ  |
saṃkṣepātkathyatāṃ vipra gopāleśvaramuttamam  || 98.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kāmadhenustapastatra putrārthaṃ ca cakāra ha  |
dhyāyatī parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 98.4 ||
tuṣṭastasyā jagannāthaḥ kapilāyā maheśvaraḥ  |
niḥsṛto dehamadhyāttu akṣayaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 98.5 ||

maheśvara uvāca - 
tuṣṭo devi jaganmātaḥ kapile parameśvari  |
ārādhanaṃ kṛtaṃ kasmādvada devi varānane  || 98.6 ||

surabhiruvāca - 
lokānāmupakārāya sṛṣṭāhaṃ parameṣṭhinā  |
loke kāryaṃ hi sarvaṃ vai matprasādātprasidhyati  || 98.7 ||
lokāḥ sarve prapaśyanti tvatpasādāttriśūlinam  |
tīrthe tvaṃ bhava bhoḥ śambho lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 98.8 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tadā prabhṛtitattīrthaṃ vikhyātaṃ vasudhātale  |
snānenaikena rājendra sarvapāpaṃ vyapohati  || 98.9 ||
gopāleśe tu godānaṃ yastu bhaktyā pradāpayet |
yogye dvijottame deyā yogyā dhenuḥ sakāñcanī  || 98.10 ||
savastrā taruṇī śubhrā kṣīriṇī vṛṣasaṃyutā  |
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ vā yudhiṣṭhira  || 98.11 ||
sarveṣu caiva māseṣu kārttike ca viśeṣataḥ  |
dāpayetparayā bhaktyā dvije svādhyāyatatpare  || 98.12 ||
vidhinā ca pradāsyanti vidhinā pratigṛhṇate  |
ubhayoḥ puṇyakarmāṇi prekṣakāḥ puṇyabhājanāḥ  || 98.13 ||
piṇḍadānaṃ prakartavyaṃ pretānāṃ bhāvasaṃyutaiḥ  |
piṇḍenaikena rājendra pretā yānti parāṃ gatim  || 98.14 ||
bhaktyā praṇāmaṃ rudrasya ye kurvanti dine dine  |
teṣāṃ pāpaṃ pralīyeta bhinnapātre jalaṃ yathā  || 98.15 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo rājan vṛṣaṃ caiva samutsṛjet |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
vṛṣotsarge kṛte tāta yatphalaṃ bhavate nṛṇām  || 98.16 ||
tatsarvaṃ kathayasvādya prayatnena dvijottama  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sarvalakṣaṇasampanne vṛṣe caiva tu yatphalam  || 98.17 ||
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvaṃ dharmanandana  |
kārttike caiva vaiśākhe paurṇamāsyāṃ narādhipa  || 98.18 ||
rudrasya sannidhau bhūtvā śuciḥ snātvā jitendriyaḥ  |
vṛṣotsargaṃ tathā rājan kārayeddharaprītaye  || 98.19 ||
sthāne sthitvā pavitre tu catasro vatsikāḥ śubhāḥ  |
vṛṣabhāya ca muñceta sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ  || 98.20 ||
prīyatāṃ ca mahādevo brahmāviṣṇustathā pare  |
vṛṣabhe romasaṃkhyā tu sarvāṅgeṣu narādhipa  || 98.21 ||
tāvadvarṣapramāṇaṃ tu śivaloke mahīyate  |
śivaloke uṣitvā tu paścānmartye ca jāyate  || 98.22 ||
kule mahati sambhūto dhanadhānyasamākule  |
surūpe rūpavāṃścaiva vidyāḍhye satyavādinām  || 98.23 ||
gopāleśvarakaṃ puṇyaṃ mayākhyātaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
godehātniḥsṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 98.24 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe gopāleśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāmāṣṭanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 99

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
narmadāyottare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ nāmnā vai gautameśvaram  || 99.1 ||
sthāpitaṃ gautamenaiva lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
svargasopānarūpeṇa tīrthaṃ puṃsāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 99.2 ||
gaccha tvaṃ parayā bhaktyā yatra devo jagadguruḥ  |
pātakānāṃ vināśāya svargavāsāptaye nṛpa  || 99.3 ||
saukhyasya vardhanaṃ liṅgaṃ jayadaṃ duḥkhanāśanam  |
piṇḍadānena caikena kulānāṃ uddharettrayam  || 99.4 ||
yatkiṃciddīyate bhaktyā svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu  |
tatsarvaṃ śatasāhasramājñayā gautamasya ca  || 99.5 ||
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ svayaṃ rudreṇa bhāṣitam  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dakṣiṇe narmadākūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  || 99.6 ||
śaṅkhacūḍeśvaraṃ tatra prasiddhaṃ bhūmimaṇḍale  |
śaṅkhacūḍeśvarastatra saṃsthitaḥ pāṇḍunandana  || 99.7 ||
vainateyabhayātpārtha saṃsthito narmadātaṭe  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 99.8 ||
snāpayecchaṅkhacūḍaṃ tu kṣaudreṇa dadhisarpiṣā  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā devasyāgre narādhipa  || 99.9 ||
dadhibhaktena sampūjya brāhmaṇān chaṃsitavratān  |
godānaṃ ca tathā deyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 99.10 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ pārtha sarpadaṣṭo'pi naśyati  |
so'pi yāti paraṃ lokaṃ śaṅkhacūḍasya cājñayā  || 99.11 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe śaṅkhacūḍatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma navanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 100

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra parāśaravarottamam  |
parāśaro mahātmā ca narmadāyāstaṭe śubhe  || 100.1 ||
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ putrārthaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
himācalasutā gaurī lakṣmīnārāyaṇānvitā  || 100.2 ||
toṣitā parayā bhaktyā narmadāyottare taṭe  |
parāśareṇa ṛṣiṇā tasya tuṣṭā varaṃ dadau  || 100.3 ||

devyuvāca - 
bho bho ṛṣivaraśreṣṭha tuṣṭāhaṃ tava bhaktitaḥ  |
varaṃ yācasva viprendra parāśaramahāmate  || 100.4 ||

parāśara uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭāsi me devi yadi deyo varo mama  |
putro me dīyatāṃ śīghraṃ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 100.5 ||
tīrthaṃ cātra bhaveddevi sannidhānaṃ vareṇa tu  |
lokopakārahetvarthaṃ sthīyatāṃ girinandini  || 100.6 ||
parāśarābhidhānena narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
parāśaravacaḥ śrutvā devī himavataḥ sutā  || 100.7 ||
evaṃ bhavatu te vipra ityuktvāntaradhīyata  |
parāśaro mahātmā ca sthāpayāmāsa pārvatīm  || 100.8 ||
śaṅkaraṃ sthāpayāmāsa surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
arcitaṃ sarvadevānāṃ dānavānāṃ durāsadam  || 100.9 ||
parāśaro mahātmā ca kṛtārtho vigatajvaraḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo bhaktyā śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ  || 100.10 ||
māse caitre ca vikhyāte śrāvaṇe nṛpanandana  |
māsi mārgaśire caiva śuklapakṣe tu sarvadā  || 100.11 ||
śaṅkaraṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha girijāṃ pūjayettathā  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ sūryaparvaṇi sarvadā  || 100.12 ||
striyo vā puruṣā vāpi kāmakrodhavivarjitāḥ  |
tatra gatvā śūcau sthāne narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 100.13 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā vrataṃ kuryurmahāmune  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpadānaṃ svaśaktitaḥ  || 100.14 ||
sapatnīkānuttamāṃśca śīlaśraddhāsamanvitān  |
pūjayedbrāhmaṇān pārtha annadānahiraṇmayaiḥ  || 100.15 ||
vastreṇa cchatradānena śayyā tāmbūlabhojanaiḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha āmaśrāddhaṃ praśasyate  || 100.16 ||
āmaṃ caturguṇaṃ proktaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
vedoktena vidhānena dvijāḥ pūjyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 100.17 ||
hastamātrakuśaiścaiva tilaiśca vāñcchitairnṛpa  |
vipraṃ codaṅmukhaṃ caiva ātmānaṃ dakṣiṇāmukham  || 100.18 ||
āmaṃ darbheṣu niḥkṣipya ityuccārya dvijāgrataḥ  |
pretā yāntu paraṃ lokaṃ tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 100.19 ||
pāpaṃ me praśamaṃ yātu yātu vṛddhiḥ sadā śubham  |
vṛddhiṃ yātu sadā vaṃśo jñātivargo dvijottama  || 100.20 ||
evamuccārya viprendraṃ deyaṃ parāśarāśrame  |
gobhūnīlahiraṇyāni annaṃ vastraṃ ca śaktitaḥ  || 100.21 ||
dātavyaṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha parāśaravarāśrame  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 100.22 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe parāśaratīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 101

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
bhīmeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
sevyate ṛṣisaṅghaiśca bhīmavratadharairapi  || 101.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
japaṃścaikākṣaraṃ mantramūrdhvabāhurdivākaram  || 101.2 ||
tasya janmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  |
saptajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ gāyatryā naśyate dhruvam  || 101.3 ||
daśabhirjanmajanitaṃ śatena ca purākṛtam  |
trijanmanā sahasreṇa gāyatrī hanti kilbiṣam  || 101.4 ||
vaidikaṃ laukikaṃ cāpi jāpyaṃ japtaṃ janeśvara  |
tatkṣaṇāddahate pāpaṃ tṛṇaṃ ca jvalano yathā  || 101.5 ||
tadeva balamāśritya kadācitpāpamācaret |
ajñānāttasya tatkṣipraṃ na phalaṃ hi kadācana  || 101.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu godānaṃ śaktimātreṇa dāpayet |
tadakṣayaṃ phalaṃ sarvaṃ jāyate pāṇḍunandana  || 101.7 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra nāradeśvaramuttamam  |
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ nāradena tu  || 101.8 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
nāradena muniśreṣṭha kasya tīrthaṃ vinirmitam  |
etadākhyāhi me sarva prasanno yadi sattama  || 101.9 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
parameṣṭhisutaścāpi nārado bhagavānṛṣiḥ  |
narmadāyotare kūle tapastepe purākṛte  || 101.10 ||
navanāḍīnirodhena kāṣṭhāvasthāṃ gatena ca  |
toṣitaḥ śrīmahādevo nāradena yudhiṣṭhira  || 101.11 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
tuṣṭo'haṃ tava viprendra yogīśvara ayonija  |
varaṃ prārthaya he deva yatte manasi vartate  || 101.12 ||

nārada uvāca - 
tvatprasādena bho deva yogaścaiva prasidhyatu  |

īśvara uvāca - 
yogo bhavatu bhaktiste sarvakālaṃ mamaiva tu  || 101.13 ||
svecchācāro bhave gaccha svargapātālagocare  |
martye ca bhramase yoginna kenāpi nivāryase  || 101.14 ||
saptasvarāstrayo grāmā mūrchanāstvekaviṃśatiḥ  |
mama priyakaraṃ divyaṃ nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ ca yoginā  || 101.15 ||
kaliṃ ca paśyase nityaṃ devadānavakinnaraiḥ  |
tvattīrthaṃ bhūtale puṇyaṃ matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 101.16 ||
ityuktvāntardadhe devo nāradastatra liṅginam  |
sthāpayāmāsa rājendra sarvasattvopakārakam  || 101.17 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
pṛthivyāmuttamaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ nāradena tu  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraśreṣṭha nāgacchedvijitendriyaḥ  || 101.18 ||
māsi bhādrapade ramye kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 101.19 ||
chatraṃ tatra pradātavyaṃ brāhmaṇe śubhalakṣaṇam  |
śastreṇa nihatā ye tu teṣāṃ śrāddhaṃ pradāpayet || 101.20 ||
yānti te paramaṃ lokaṃ piṇḍadānaprabhāvataḥ  |
kapilā caiva dātavyā tatra deśe narādhipa  || 101.21 ||
asya śrāddhaprabhāveṇa brāhmaṇānāṃ narādhipa  |
narmadātoyapānasya nyāyārjitadhanasya ca  || 101.22 ||
eteṣāṃ ca prabhāveṇa pretā yāntu parāṃ gatim  |
ityuccārya dvije deyā dakṣiṇā ca svaśaktitaḥ  || 101.23 ||
haviṣyānnaṃ viśālākṣa dvijānāṃ caiva dāpayet |
vidyādānena caikena akṣayā gatirāpyate  || 101.24 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra yo dadyādagrajanmane  |
kāṃcanaṃ sa tilaṃ caiva sa gacchecca triviṣṭapam  || 101.25 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthadvayamanuttamam  |
dadhichandaṃ madhuchandaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 101.26 ||
dadhicchande naraḥ snātvā yo dadyācca dvije dadhi  |
upatiṣṭhati tasyaitatsaptajanmasu bhārata  || 101.27 ||
na vyādhirna jarā tasya na śoko na ca matsaraḥ  |
daśacandraśataṃ yāvajjāyate vipule kule  || 101.28 ||
madhucchande tu madhunā miśritaṃ ca tilodakam  |
na ca vaivasvataṃ devaṃ paśyate saptajanmasu  || 101.29 ||
madhunā saha miśraṃ ca tilaṃ yastu prayacchati  |
tasya putrasya pautrasya dāridryaṃ naiva jāyate  || 101.30 ||
madhunā saha saṃmiśraṃ tilaṃ yastu prayacchati  |
madhunā saha saṃmiśraṃ yastu piṇḍaṃ pradāpayet || 101.31 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vidhivaddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  || 101.32 ||
ṣoḍaśābdāni tuṣyanti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra nanditīrthamanuttamam  || 101.33 ||
yatra siddhaśca vai nandī tatsarvaṃ kathayāmyaham  |
narmadāṃ purataḥ kṛtvā purā nandī maheśvaram  || 101.34 ||
tapastaptaṃ japaṃścaiva tīrthāttīrthaṃ jagāma ha  |
dadhicchandaṃ madhucchandaṃ yāvattyaktvā ca gacchati  || 101.35 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo nandinaṃ tamuvāca ha  |

maheśvara uvāca - 
bho bhoḥ prasanno nandīśa varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 101.36 ||
tapasā tena tuṣṭo'haṃ tīrthayātrākṛtena ca  |

nandyuvāca - 
na cāhaṃ kāmaye vittaṃ na cāhaṃ kulasantatim  || 101.37 ||
muktiṃ na kāmaye cānyaddeveśa caraṇāmbujam  |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgeṣu tiryagyonigateṣu ca  || 101.38 ||
janmajanmani yāsyāmi tvadbhaktiracalā ca me  |
tatheti cokto devena parameśena nandikaḥ  || 101.39 ||
gṛhītvā taṃ kare śīghraṃ jagāma nilayaṃ haraḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhaktyā tryakṣaṃ prapūjayet || 101.40 ||
agniṣṭome ca yatpuṇyaṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe mahāpuṇye prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  || 101.41 ||
śivasyānucaro bhūtvā modate kalpamakṣayam  |
tataḥ kālena mahatā jāyate vipule kule  || 101.42 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajñe jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  |
etatte kathitaṃ pārtha sarvatuṣṭipradaṃ śubham  || 101.43 ||
durlabhaṃ satyasaṃjñasya sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 101.44 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe nanditīrthavarṇano nāmaikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 102

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra varuṇeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddhaḥ purā devo varuṇo nṛpasattama  || 102.1 ||
piṇyākaśākaparṇaiśca kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ  |
ārādhya girijānāthaṃ tataḥ siddhiṃ gatā janāḥ  || 102.2 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjayecchaṅkaraṃ bhaktyā sa gacchetparamaṃ padam  || 102.3 ||
kuṇḍikāṃ vardhanīṃ vāpi mahadvā jalabhājanam  |
annena sahitaṃ pārtha tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 102.4 ||
yatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ satre dvādaśavārṣike  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 102.5 ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāmannadānamanuttamam  |
yadyatprītikaraṃ caiva toyaṃ ca nṛpasattama  || 102.6 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ ca narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
vāruṇe ca pure vāso yāvadāhūta samplavam  || 102.7 ||
bhuktvā tatra bahuṃ kālaṃ martyaloke'bhijāyate  |
annadānaprado nityaṃ jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 102.8 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra agnitīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahātejāstapaḥ kṛtvā hutāśanaḥ  || 102.9 ||
sarvabhakṣīkṛto yaśca daṇḍake muninā purā  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya pūto jāto hutāśanaḥ  || 102.10 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā samabhyarcya jagadgurum  |
umayā sahita bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 102.11 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā datte vai kāṃcanaṃ nṛpa  |
brāhmaṇebhyo jalaṃ dattvā labhate vārbudaṃ phalam  || 102.12 ||
dadhicchande madhucchande nandīśe vāruṇe tathā  |
āgneye tatphalaṃ tāta snātvā mucyeta kilbiṣaiḥ  || 102.13 ||
te vandyā mānuṣe loke dhanyāścāptamanorathāḥ  |
yairhi dṛṣṭaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ narmadātīrthapañcakam  || 102.14 ||
svargalokamavāpuste yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  |
tataḥ svargāccyutāścāpi rājānaḥ santi dhārmikāḥ  || 102.15 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktā bhuñjate te'calāṃ mahīm  |
ākhaṇḍalapratāpo'yaṃ narmadātaṭasevane  || 102.16 ||
gaṅgākanakhale puṇyā kurukṣetre sarasvatī  |
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  || 102.17 ||
revātīraṃ vasennityaṃ toyaṃ yastu sadā pibet |
snāto'sau sarvatīrtheṣu somapānaṃ dine dine  || 102.18 ||
gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāśca sarāṃsi ca  |
kalpānte saṃkṣayaṃ yānti na mṛtaikā ca narmadā  || 102.19 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe tīrthapañcakavarṇano nāma dvyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 103

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
hanūmadīśvarannāma kathaṃ jātaṃ mahāmate  |
brahmahatyāharaṃ tīrthaṃ revāyādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 103.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahābāho somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇa  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 103.2 ||
tava snehātpravakṣyāmi pīḍito vārdhakena tu  |
jātaṃ pūrvaṃ mahāyuddhaṃ rāmarāvaṇayorapi  || 103.3 ||
pulastyo brahmaṇaḥ putrastasya vai viśravāḥ sutaḥ  |
rāvaṇastasya saṃjāto daśagrīvo'pi rākṣasaḥ  || 103.4 ||
trailokyavijayī jātaḥ prasādācchūlinastathā  |
gīrvāṇā nirjitāḥ sarve rāmasya gṛhiṇī hṛtā  || 103.5 ||
yadbhrātā kumbhakarṇo vai sītā sā vanamāśritā  |
vibhīṣaṇena pāpo'yaṃ mandastyakto vicārya ca  || 103.6 ||
sa jitaḥ kārtavīryeṇa sa jito jāmadagninā  |
sa hato rāmacandreṇa tasya rājyaṃ hṛtaṃ tathā  || 103.7 ||
tato rāmeṇa rakṣo'pi jitaḥ saṃkhye mahābalaḥ  |
vanaṃ bhagnaṃ hato rakṣo gatvā vāyusutena vai  || 103.8 ||
rāvaṇasya sutaḥ saṃkhye hataścākṣakumārakaḥ  |
evaṃ rāmāyaṇe jāte sītā mokṣe kṛte tataḥ  || 103.9 ||
ayodhyāyāṃ gate rāme hanūmāśca mahābalaḥ  |
kailāsaṃ hi gataḥ pārtha praṇāmārthaṃ maheśvare  || 103.10 ||
tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti cokto vai nandinā vānarottamaḥ  |
brahmahatyāyutastvaṃ hi rākṣasānāṃ vadhena hi  || 103.11 ||
bhairavasyāsanaṃ puṇyaṃ na gantā'si mahābala  |

hanumānuvāca - 
nandiṃstvaṃ hi varaṃ yaccha pātakasyopaśāntaye  || 103.12 ||
bhūtvā niṣpātako'haṃ vai praṇamāmi maheśvaram  |

nandyuvāca - 
rudradehodbhavā puṇyā narmadā saritāṃ varā  || 103.13 ||
śravaṇājjanmacaritaṃ kīrtanāddviguṇaṃ vrajet |
saptajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ naśyedrevāvagāhanāt || 103.14 ||
tasmāttīre vasa tvaṃ ca revāsaṅgamadakṣiṇe  |
dhyāyamāno virūpākṣaṃ triśūlakarasaṃsthitam  || 103.15 ||
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃkāśaṃ vyālayajñopavītakam  |
umārdhāṅgadharaṃ devaṃ gorājāsanasaṃsthitam  || 103.16 ||
vatsarān subahūn yāvaddhyāyatastasya tatra vai  |
tatra tuṣṭo mahādeva āgataḥ saha bhāryayā  || 103.17 ||
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ meghagambhīrayā girā  |
sādhu vatsa tvayā cātra kaṣṭaṃ tapasi saṃsthitam  || 103.18 ||
hanūmāṃśca haraṃ dṛṣṭvā umārdhāṅgadharaṃ sthitam  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato bhūtvā jayadeva namo'stu te  || 103.19 ||
jaya cāndhakaghātāya bāṇāsuravimardine  |
jaya bhūtapanāthāya jaya bhairavabhūṣaṇa  || 103.20 ||
jaya kāmavināśāya gaṅgāṃ śirasi dhāriṇe  |
evaṃ stuto mahādevo varado vānarasya ca  || 103.21 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
varaṃ prārthaya tvaṃ vatsa prārthitaṃ rabhasaṃ vada  |

hanumānuvāca - 
brahmarakṣovadhājjātā brahmahatyā maheśvara  || 103.22 ||
niṣpāpo'haṃ bhaveyaṃ vai yuṣmatsambhāṣaṇena ca  |

īśvara uvāca - 
narmadātīrthaṃ māhātmyadhyānayogaprabhāvataḥ  || 103.23 ||
manmūrtidarśanātsadyo niṣpāpo nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
ityuktvāntardadhe deva umāsārdhaṃ trilocanaḥ  || 103.24 ||
hanumadīśvaraṃ tatra sthāpayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ  |
ātmayogabalenaiva brahmacaryaprabhāvataḥ  || 103.25 ||
īśvarasya prabhāveṇa kāmadaṃ sthāpitaṃ śivam  |
acchedyamapratarkyaṃ ca vināśotpattivarjitam  || 103.26 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
hanumadīśvare tatra pratyayaṃ yatpurābhavat |
yadvṛttaṃ dvāparasyādau tretānte ca nareśvarā  || 103.27 ||
