suparṇo nāma rājarṣirbabhūva vasudhātale  |
tasya rājye sadā saukhyaṃ dīrghāyurmānavaḥ sadā  || 103.28 ||
śatabāhurbabhūvāsya putro bhīmaparākramaḥ  |
āsaktaḥ sa sadākālaṃ japadhyāne nareśvara  || 103.29 ||
krīḍate pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ parvatāṃśca vanāni ca  |
vadhārthaṃ mṛgayūthānāmāgato vindhyaparvate  || 103.30 ||
mṛgajātisamākīrṇe hastijātisamāśrite  |
hasticitrakaśobhāḍhye mṛgavārāhasaṃkule  || 103.31 ||
krīḍitvā ca tato rājā cāsane saṃsthitaḥ sa ca  |
vanamadhye tadā dṛṣṭvā bhramantaṃ piṅgalaṃ dvijam  || 103.32 ||

rājovāca - 
eko vane vane kasmādbhramase pustikākaraḥ  |
itaśceto nirīkṣaṃstvaṃ kathayasva dvijottama  || 103.33 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
kānyakubjātsamāyātaḥ preṣito rājakanyayā  |

rājovāca - 
kathayasva prasādena kasmātkāryādvada prabho  || 103.34 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
śikhaṇḍī caiva rājā vai kānyakubjaṃ bubhukṣate  |
aputraḥ sa mahīpālaḥ kanyā jātā manorathaiḥ  || 103.35 ||
he nṛpa
jātismarā śubhācārā narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
pitroktā sā ca kanyā vai vivāhāya prakalpitā  || 103.36 ||
asāre cādya saṃsāre kanyādānaṃ dadāmyaham  |

kanyovācā̱
yasmin kāle hyahaṃ lipse tasmin kāle pradīyatām  || 103.37 ||
putrīvākyena rājāsau vismayāviṣṭacetanaḥ  |

śikhaṇḍyuvāca - 
kathyatāṃ me mahābhāge bhāṣitaṃ hi tvayā katham  || 103.38 ||
pitṛvākyena sā vālā śirasāvanatā bhuvi  |
kathayāmāsa yadvṛttaṃ hanumadīśvare nṛpa  || 103.39 ||
kalāpinyasmyahaṃ tāta sthitā bhartṛsahānugā  |
uraṅgameśa sānnidhye revāyā uttare taṭe  || 103.40 ||
hanumato vane puṇye krīḍāmi sma yadṛcchayā  |
bhartṛyuktā tatra guhye vañjule sarale drume  || 103.41 ||
āgatā lubdhakāstatra kṣudhārtā vanamuttamam  |
bhartṛkopayutaiḥ pāpairhatāhaṃ patinā saha  || 103.42 ||
grīvāṃ nimoṭayāmāsurbhakṣaṇotpāṭanaṃ kṛtam  |
hutāśanamukhe te tu hasantaścāśu lubdhakāḥ  || 103.43 ||
bharjayitvā tato māṃsaṃ bhakṣayitvā yathecchayā  |
suptāḥ svasthendriyā rātrau vigatā śarvarī kṣayam  || 103.44 ||
tanmāṃsaśeṣaṃ juṣṭaṃ vai jambukairgṛdhravāyasaiḥ  |
maccharīrodbhavaṃ cāsthi snāyumāṃsena saṃyutam  || 103.45 ||
gṛhītvā patriṇaikena ākāśātpatatā tadā  |
sāmiṣaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā pakṣiṇo'nye samāgatāḥ  || 103.46 ||
dṛṣṭvā pakṣisamūhaṃ tu asthikhaṇḍaṃ vyasarjayat |
vihagānāṃ samastānāṃ dhāvatāṃ cāpi paśyatām  || 103.47 ||
patitaṃ narmadātoye hanumadīśvare nṛpa  |
madīyamasthikhaṇḍaṃ ca patitaṃ narmadājale  || 103.48 ||
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa jātāhaṃ kṣatriye kule  |
bhūpa kanyāpyahaṃ jātā sampūrṇaśaśivanmukhī  || 103.49 ||
jātismarā narendrāsmi jātāhaṃ kṣatriye kule  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kāraṇaṃ nṛpasattama  || 103.50 ||
madarthaṃ viṣamasthāne śakuntamṛgajātiṣu  |
yadi preṣayase tāta kamapi narmadājale  || 103.51 ||
tasyāhaṃ kathayiṣyāmi sthānacihnaṃ samagrakam  |
kanyāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā śikhaṇḍī hyāha māṃ nṛpa  || 103.52 ||
grāmaviṃśaṃ ca dāsyāmi gaccha tvaṃ narmadātaṭe  |
prekṣaṇaṃ me pratijñātamalakṣyā'pīḍitena tu  || 103.53 ||
gaccha tvaṃ narmadāṃ puṇyāṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarīm  |
agrajāṃ somanāthasya hanumadīśvare śubhe  || 103.54 ||
ardhakrośe tu revāyā vistīrṇe vaṭapādapaiḥ  |
kadambakavanaiścaiva saṃpradhāne vanasya ca  || 103.55 ||
nyagrodhavaṭasānnidhye asthilakṣyaṃ pradṛśyate  |
mṛttikāmasthisaṃgṛhya gaccha revān dvijottama  || 103.56 ||
āśvinasya site pakṣe tripurāritithau sthite  |
snāpaya śūlinaṃ bhaktyā rātrau kuru ca jāgaram  || 103.57 ||
prabhāte kṣipyatāṃ śīghraṃ nābhimātre jale sthitaḥ  |
ityuccārya dvijaśreṣṭha sugatistasya jāyate  || 103.58 ||
asthi kṣiptvā punaḥ snānaṃ kartavyamaghanāśanam  |
kathitaṃ kanyayā yacca tatsarvaṃ pustake kṛtam  || 103.59 ||
āgato'haṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha tasmiṃstīrthe mahālaye  |
sābhijñānaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā gṛhītvāsthi nṛpottama  || 103.60 ||
pūrvoktena vidhānena nikṣiptaṃ narmadājale  |
puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papātātha sādhu sādhviti brāhmaṇa  || 103.61 ||
vimānaṃ tu tato divyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ hanumadīśvare  |
tato brāhmaṇarājānau gṛhītvānaśanaṃ sthitau  || 103.62 ||
ātmānaṃ śoṣayitvā ca īśvarārādhane ratau  |
evaṃ sandhyāyato devaṃ śatabāhurdvijottamaḥ  || 103.63 ||
māsārdhāttu mṛto rājā śatabāhurmahāmatiḥ  |
kiṅkiṇījālaśobhāḍhyaṃ vimānaṃ tatra cāgatam  || 103.64 ||
sādhu sādhu nṛpaśreṣṭha vimānārohaṇaṃ kuru  |

rājovāca - 
ūrdhvamārgaṃ na gacchāmi vipro yāvanna saṃsthitaḥ  || 103.65 ||
upadeśaprado mahyaṃ garurūpo dvijottamaḥ  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
hanumadīśvare rājanye mṛtāḥ santi mānavāḥ  || 103.66 ||
te yānti śivalokaṃ vai sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  |
naiva pāpakṣayaścāsya brāhmaṇasya nareśvara  || 103.67 ||
gṛhaṃ ca gṛhiṇī vittaṃ brāhmaṇasya pravartate  |
śatabāhustato vipraṃ bhāṣayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ  || 103.68 ||
tyaja mūlamadharmasya lobhamekaṃ dvijottama  |
ityuktvā prayayau rājā svargaṃ svargijanaiḥ saha  || 103.69 ||
dinaiḥ kaiścidgato vipraḥ svargaṃ sukṛtibhiḥ saha  |
vāhinyāḥ kāśirājasya putryāḥ tīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 103.70 ||
ātmanaḥ kanyayā datte pūrvajanmārjitaṃ tapaḥ  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ sarvakālaṃ munīśvara  || 103.71 ||
viśeṣādāśvine māse kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  |
snāpayedīśvaraṃ bhaktyā kṣaudrakṣīreṇa sarpiṣā  || 103.72 ||
dadhnā ca khaṇḍayuktena tilatoyena vā punaḥ  |
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena cārcayettaṃ maheśvaram  || 103.73 ||
tataḥ sugandhapuṣpaiśca bilvapatraiśca pūtanam  |
śrāddhaṃ yaḥ kārayettatra brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 103.74 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaiḥ kulīnairgṛhapālakaiḥ  |
tarpayedbrāhmaṇaṃ bhaktyā annavastrahiraṇyakaiḥ  || 103.75 ||
narakasthā divaṃ yānti ityuccārya dvijātayaḥ  |
svargasthāḥ paramaṃ lokamityuktvā praṇameddvijān  || 103.76 ||
patitān varjayedviprān vṛṣalī yasya mandire  |
svavṛṣaṃ tu parityajya vṛṣairanyairvṛṣāyate  || 103.77 ||
vṛṣalīṃ tāṃ vidurdevā na śūdrā vṛṣalī bhavet |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ gurudāraniṣevaṇam  || 103.78 ||
suvarṇaharaṇaṃ tasya mitradrohabhavaṃ tathā  |
naśyanti pātakāḥ sarve ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 103.79 ||
vākpralāpena kiṃ vatsa bahunoktena kiṃ tu vā  |
sarvapāpasamopeto dadyāddānaṃ dvijottame  || 103.80 ||
sarvadevamayī dhenuḥ sarvadevātmikā sthitā  |
śṛṅgāgreṣu mahīpāla śakro vasati nityaśaḥ  || 103.81 ||
hari skandhe śiro brahmā lalāṭe vṛṣavāhanaḥ  |
candrārkau locane jñeyau jihvāyāṃ tu sarasvatī  || 103.82 ||
marudgaṇāḥ sadā sādhyāstasyāṅgāni nareśvara  |
oṃkāraścaturo vedāḥ saṣaḍaṅgapadakramāḥ  || 103.83 ||
ṛṣayo romakūpeṣu asthikhyātā mahānagāḥ  |
daṇḍahasto mahākāyaḥ kṛṣṇo mahiṣavāhanaḥ  || 103.84 ||
pṛṣṭhabhāgasthito nityaṃ śubhāśubhanirīkṣakaḥ  |
catvāraḥ sāgarāḥ puṇyāḥ kṣīradhārāḥ staneṣu ca  || 103.85 ||
viṣṇudehodbhavā gaṅgā darśanātpāpahāriṇī  |
evaṃ yā saṃsthitā yasmāttasmādeṣā sadā budhaiḥ  || 103.86 ||
lakṣmīśca gomaye yasyāḥ pavitrā sarvamaṅgalā  |
gomayāllepanaṃ tasmātkartavyaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 103.87 ||
gandharvāpsaraso gaṅgā gokhureṣu ca saṃsthitāḥ  |
aśvinau karṇayornityaṃ vartete raviputrakau  || 103.88 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ yāni tīrthāni pāṇḍava  |
tāni sarvāṇi prāptāni gavāṃ pādeṣu nityaśaḥ  || 103.89 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
sarvatīrthasamā gāvo gīrvāṇaiḥ samalaṃkṛtāḥ  |
etatkathaya me tāta kasmādgoṣu samāśritāḥ  || 103.90 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sarvadevamayo viṣṇurgāvo viṣṇuśarīrajāḥ  |
deyāstasmātsadā vandyāḥ kalpitā vibudhairjanaiḥ  || 103.91 ||
śvetā vā kapilā vāpi kṣīriṇī pāṇḍunandana  |
sarvāsāṃ kṣīriṇīrgāvaḥ śvetavastrāvaguṇṭhitāḥ  || 103.92 ||
kāṃsyadohanikā deyāḥ svarṇaśṛṅgīrvibhūṣitāḥ  |
hanūmadīśvarasyāgre bhaktyā vipreṣu dāpayet || 103.93 ||
nisargasthena sā deyā svargamātmana icchatā  |
asamartho yamasteṣāṃ viṣṇulokaṃ prayānti te  || 103.94 ||
viṣṇulokacyutaḥ so'pi prayāti dvijamandiram  |
tatraiva jāyate putro vidvān vanasamanvitaḥ  || 103.95 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ hanumadīśvaraṃ śubham  |
śṛṇoti mucyate pāpādvarṇasaṅkarasambhavāt || 103.96 ||
darśe saṃcintayedyastu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 103.97 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe hanumadīśvaravarṇano nāma tryadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 104

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
āścaryaṃ kathitaṃ tāta yadabhūnnarmadātaṭe  |
somanāthasyatīrthaṃ hi vārāṇasyā samaṃ nṛpa  || 104.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
nimagno duḥkhasaṃsāre hṛtarājyo dvijottama  |
yuṣmadvāṇī jalaiḥ snāto nirduḥkho'haṃ sabāndhavaḥ  || 104.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahābāho somavaṃśa vibhūṣaṇa  |
pṛṣṭaṃ te durlabhaṃ tīrthaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ yathā  || 104.3 ||
ādau pitāmahastāta samastasya janasya ca  |
manasā tasya saṃjātā ṛṣayo daśa puṅgavāḥ  || 104.4 ||
marīciratryaṅgirasau pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  |
pracetāśca vaśiṣṭhaśca bhṛgurnārada eva ca  || 104.5 ||
jajñe pracetaso dakṣo mahātejāḥ prajāpatiḥ  |
dakṣasyāpi sutā jātāḥ pañcāśatkanyakāḥ kila  || 104.6 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa  |
tathaiva ca mahābhāga saptaviṃśatimindave  || 104.7 ||
tāsāṃ hi rohiṇī candrasyābhīṣṭā sābhavattadā  |
tasyāśca kāraṇaṃ kṛtvā śapto dakṣeṇa candramāḥ  || 104.8 ||
kṣayarogyabhavaccandro vivakṣāyāḥ prajāpateḥ  |
dakṣaśāpaprabhāveṇa nistejāḥ śarvarīpatiḥ  || 104.9 ||
gataḥ pitāmahaṃ somo vepamānaḥ praṇamya ca  |
brahmayone namastubhyaṃ vedagarbha namo'stu te  || 104.10 ||

brahmovāca - 
sarvatra durlabhā revā triṣu sthāneṣu bhārata  |
oṃkāre ca bhṛgukṣetre narmadā hyurageśvare  || 104.11 ||
kāṣṭhavatsaṃsthitaḥ somo dhyāyate parameśvaram  |
yāvadvarṣaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ tāvattuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 104.12 ||
pretyakṣaḥ somanāthasya vṛṣāsana umārdhagaḥ  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato bhūtvā jaya deva namo'stu te  || 104.13 ||
jaya śaṅkara pāpakṛtānta namo jaya īśvara nātha namo'stu namaḥ  |
jaya vāsukibhūṣaṇa bhūtapate jaya śūlakapāladharāya namaḥ  || 104.14 ||
jaya andhakadehavināśa namo jaya dānavadehavadhāya namaḥ  |
jaya niṣkalasakalakalāya namo jaya kālakalādamanāya namaḥ  || 104.15 ||
jaya nīlakaṇṭha umāpate jaya sūkṣmanirañjanaśabda namaḥ  |
jaya ādya anādya ananta namo jaya pāṇipinākatriśūla namaḥ  || 104.16 ||
evaṃ stuto mahādevaḥ somanāthena pāṇḍava  |
tuṣṭastasya nṛpaśreṣṭha umayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  || 104.17 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
varaṃ varaya bhadraṃ te yatte manasi vartate  |

soma uvāca - 
dakṣaśāpena dagdho'haṃ kṣīṇadeho maheśvara  || 104.18 ||
pāpapraśamanaṃ deva kuru sarvaṃ mamaiva tu  |

maheśvara uvāca - 
bhavadbhaktigṛhīto'haṃ tūṣṭaścaivomayā saha  || 104.19 ||
niṣpāpaḥ somanāthasya saṃjātastīrthasevanāt |
ityuktvāntardadhe devaḥ somo dhyātvā kṣaṇaṃ nṛpa  || 104.20 ||
sthāpayāmāsa liṅgaṃ tu siddhidaṃ prāṇināṃ bhuvi  |
sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ devaṃ brahmahatyāvināśanam  || 104.21 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
somanāthaprabhāvaṃ ca saṃkṣepātkathayāmi te  |
śambaro nāma rājābhūttasya putrastrilocanaḥ  || 104.22 ||
trilocanasutaḥ kaṇṭhaḥ pāpaniṣṭho mahādhamaḥ  |
vane vibhramatastasya mṛgayūthaṃ tvadṛśyata  || 104.23 ||
mṛgayūthaṃ hataṃ sarvaṃ trilocanasutena ca  |
mṛgarūpī dvijo madhye vicarannirjane vane  || 104.24 ||
tadā hatastu śastreṇa kaṇṭhena ṛṣisattamaḥ  |
brahmahatyāyutaḥ kaṇṭho nistejā vyacaranmahīm  || 104.25 ||
vicarannapi saṃprāpto narmadānāgasaṃgame  |
kadambapāṭalākīrṇe bilvanāraṅgaśobhite  || 104.26 ||
ciñcinī campakopete agastitaruśobhite  |
unmattabhṛṅgasaṃyukte tathā sarvatra śobhite  || 104.27 ||
citrakairmṛgamārjāraiḥ siṃhaiḥ sarvatra śūkaraiḥ  |
śaśakairgavayairyukte śikhaṇḍiravanādite  || 104.28 ||
praviṣṭastadvane kaṇṭhastṛṣārtaḥ śramakarṣitaḥ  |
snāto revājale puṇye saṅgame pāpanāśane  || 104.29 ||
patrāṇi ca vicitrāṇi bhakṣayan saha kiṅkaraiḥ  |
suptaḥ pādapachāyāyāṃ śrānto mṛgavadhena ca  || 104.30 ||
ānarca parayā bhaktyā somanāthaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
pītvā toyaṃ kaṇṭhamātraṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 104.31 ||
tāvattīrthavare vipraḥ snānārthaṃ saṃgamaṃ prati  |
mārgago brāhmaṇo bhūyastatastadgatamānasaḥ  || 104.32 ||
mārge vṛkṣe samārūḍhā strī caikā ca bhayaṅkarī  |
uvāca brāhmaṇaṃ sā hi tiṣṭha tiṣṭha dvijottama  || 104.33 ||
trasto nirīkṣate yāvaddiśaḥ sarvā nareśvara  |
tāvadvṛkṣa samārūḍhāṃ striyaṃ raktāmbarāvṛtām  || 104.34 ||
raktapuṣpadharāṃ vālāṃ raktacandanacarcitām  |
raktābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāṃ pāśahastāṃ dadarśa ha  || 104.35 ||

stryuvāca - 
sandeśaṃ śṛṇu me vipra yadi gacchasi saṃgamam  |
madbhartā tiṣṭhate tatra śīghrameva visarjaya  || 104.36 ||
ekākinī ca te bhāryā tiṣṭhate vanamadhyagā  |
ityākarṇya gato vipraḥ saṅgamaṃ suradurlabham  || 104.37 ||
vṛkṣachāyāsthitaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ brāhmaṇo hi dadarśa ha  |

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
vanānte ca mayā dṛṣṭā bālā kamalalocanā  || 104.38 ||
raktāmbaradharā tanvī raktacandanarcarcitā  |
raktamālyā suśobhāḍhyā pāśahastā mṛgekṣaṇā  || 104.39 ||
vṛkṣārūḍhāvadadvākyaṃ bhartāraṃ preṣayasva mām  |

kaṇṭha uvāca - 
kasmin sthāne tu viprendra tiṣṭhate mṛgalocanā  || 104.40 ||
kasya sā kena kāryeṇa etatsarvaṃ vada hi me  |

brāhmaṇa uvāca - 
saṃgamādardhakrośe ca udyānānte praśobhite  || 104.41 ||
tatra tiṣṭhati sā nārī sotkaṇṭhitamanā vibho  |
tato bhṛtyamuvācedaṃ kaṇṭho rājā yudhiṣṭhira  || 104.42 ||
pṛccha tvaṃ gaccha kā cāsi āgatā kva gamiṣyasi  |
tataḥ kṣipraṃ gatastatra yatra nārī sthitābhavat || 104.43 ||
vṛkṣasthāṃ dadṛśe bālāmuvāca nṛpasattama  |
tvāṃ rājā pṛcchate bāle kāsi tvaṃ kva gamiṣyasi  || 104.44 ||

stryuvāca - 
gururātmavatāṃ śāstā rājā śāstā durātmanām  |
iha pracchannapāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ  || 104.45 ||
brahmahatyāsya saṃjātā mṛgarūpadvijodvadhāt |
mayā yukto'pi rājāsau muktastīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 104.46 ||
atrārdhakrośamātraṃ vai brahmahatyā na saṃviśet |
somanāthaprabhāvācca tīrthaṃ vārāṇasīsamam  || 104.47 ||
gaccha tvaṃ preṣayeḥ kaṇṭhaṃ śīghrameva na saṃśayaḥ  |
samastaṃ kathayāmāsa tadvṛttāntaṃ nṛpaṃ prati  || 104.48 ||
tasya vākyena rājāsau papāta dharaṇītale  |

bhṛtya uvāca - 
kasmāttvaṃ śocase nātha pūrvajātaṃ śūbhāśubham  || 104.49 ||
ityākarṇya vacastasya sa rājā tvidamabravīt |
prāṇatyāgaṃ karomyatra somanātha samīpataḥ  || 104.50 ||
śīghramānīyatāṃ vahṇirindhanāni bahūnyapi  |
ānītaṃ tatkṣaṇātsarvaṃ bhṛtyaiḥ svairvaśavartibhiḥ  || 104.51 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā śubhe toye saṃgame pāpanāśane  |
arcitvā parayā bhaktyā somanāthaṃ nareśvara  || 104.52 ||
triḥpradakṣiṇakaṃ kṛtvā jvalite jātavedasi  |
praviṣṭaḥ kaṇṭharājastu hṛdi kṛtvā janārdanam  || 104.53 ||
pītāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ mahāmukuṭadhāriṇam  |
viṣṇordhyānena cātraiva sugatirme bhavatviti  || 104.54 ||
papāta puṣpavṛṣṭiśca sādhu sādhu nṛpātmaja  |
āścaryamatulaṃ dṛṣṭvā nirīkṣa ca parasparam  || 104.55 ||
hutaṃ taiḥ pāvake bhṛtyairhṛdi dhyātvā gadādharam  |
vimānasthā divaṃ sarve saṅgatāḥ pāṇḍunandana  || 104.56 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
somanāthaprabhāvo'yaṃ śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ sarvakāle śubhe dine  || 104.57 ||
viśeṣācchuklapakṣe ca sūryavāreṇa saptamī  |
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 104.58 ||
pañcāmṛtena gavyena snāpayetparameśvaram  |
śrīkhaṇḍalepanaṃ kuryātpuṣpadhūpādikaṃ tathā  || 104.59 ||
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpaṃ gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ ca kārayet |
somavāreṇa cāṣṭamyāṃ prabhāte pūjayeddvijam  || 104.60 ||
ātmavantaṃ jitakrodhaṃ dvijanindāvivarjitam  |
sarvāṅgaruciraṃ śāntaṃ svadāraparipālakam  || 104.61 ||
gāyatrīṃ paṭhamānaṃ ca vikarmarahitaṃ sadā  |
punarbhūrvṛṣalī śūdrī vartate yasya mandire  || 104.62 ||
hīnāṅgāstvatiriktāṅgā yeṣāṃ pūrvāpare na hi  |
vrate śrāddhe tathā dāne dvijā varjyāḥ sadā budhaiḥ  || 104.63 ||
puṃścalī taruṇī bhāryā dvijaḥ svādhyāyavarjitaḥ  |
ātmanā saha dātāramadho nayati pāṇḍava  || 104.64 ||
śālmalī naukayā tulyāḥ svadharmaniratā dvijāḥ  |
dātāraṃ caivamātmānaṃ tārayanti taranti ca  || 104.65 ||
śrāddhaṃ someśvare pārtha yaḥ kuryāduta mānavaḥ  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 104.66 ||
annaṃ vastraṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca yo dadyādagrajanmane  |
sa yāti śāṅkaraṃ lokamiti me satyabhāṣitam  || 104.67 ||
hayaṃ yo vai dadātyatra sampūrṇābharaṇānvitam  |
raktaṃ vā pītavarṇaṃ vā sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam  || 104.68 ||
kuṅkumena viliptāṅgamaprajaṃ ca dadediti  |
sragdāmabhūṣitaṃ kaṇṭhe sitavastrāvaguṇṭhitam  || 104.69 ||
aṅghrirādhīyatāṃ skandhe madīyaṃ hayamāruha  |
ārūḍhe brāhmaṇe bhūyo bhāskaraḥ prīyatāmiti  || 104.70 ||
sa yāti śāṅkaraṃ lokaṃ sarvapāpavivarjitaḥ  |
tasmāllokāccyutaścāpi rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 104.71 ||
tasya vaṃśe sadā rājyaṃ na naśyati kadācana  |
dīrghāyurjāyate putro bhāryā ca vaśavartinī  || 104.72 ||
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ  |
somasya coparāge tu yo gacchedvijitendriyaḥ  || 104.73 ||
sopavāso jitakrodho gāṃ tu dadyāddvijanmane  |
savatsāṃ kṣīrasaṃyuktāṃ śvetavarṇāṃ balānvitām  || 104.74 ||
śabalīṃ pītavarṇāṃ vā dhūmrāṃ vā nīlakandharām  |
kapilāṃ vā savastrāṃ vā ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitām  || 104.75 ||
raupyakhurāṃ kāṃsyadohāṃ svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ nareśvara  |
śvetayā vardhate vaṃśo raktā saubhāgyavardhinī  || 104.76 ||
śabalī tāmravarṇā ca duḥkhaghnā ca prakīrtitā  |
kapilā tu haretpāpaṃ trijanmabhirupārjitam  || 104.77 ||
tasya lokamavāpnoti māndhātuśca janeśvara  |
pakṣānte ca vyatīpāte vaidhṛtau ravisaṃkrame  || 104.78 ||
dinakṣaye gajacchāyā grahaṇe bhāskarasya ca  |
ye vrajanti viśuddhāṅgā vairañcye suradurlabhe  || 104.79 ||
mātṛhā guruhā yo hi ātmahā tu viśeṣataḥ  |
drupadādyaṃ japennityaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ tathā nṛpa  || 104.80 ||
gāyatrīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ caiva saurīṃ śaivīṃ yadṛcchayā  |
so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyeta ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 104.81 ||
yaḥ kuryātsomanāthasya tatra kartā pradakṣiṇam  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtaṃ tena jambūdvīpaṃ nareśvara  || 104.82 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ gurudāraniṣevaṇam  |
bhrūṇahā śudhyate tatra evameva na saṃśayaḥ  || 104.83 ||
tīrthākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti jitendriyaḥ  |
vyādhito rājarogena atulāṃ śriyamāpnuyāt || 104.84 ||
putrārthī labhate putraṃ niṣkāmaḥ svargamāpnuyāt |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyastīrthaṃ śrutvā varaṃ nṛpa  || 104.85 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ somanāthasya yatphalam  || 104.86 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe somanāthatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma caturadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 105

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra piṅgalāvartakaṃ nṛpa  |
saṅgamasya samīpasthaṃ revāyā uttare taṭe  || 105.1 ||
havyavāhena rājendra sthāpitaḥ piṅgaleśvaraḥ  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
havyavāhena viprendra sthāpitaśceśvaraḥ katham  || 105.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
retasā yadi rudreṇa tarpito havyavāhanaḥ  |
prāpto rudreṇa saumyena dehaṃ prāpya jagāma saḥ  || 105.3 ||
havyavāhamukhe kṣipte rudreṇāmitatejasā  |
rudrasya tejasā dagdhaḥ tīrthayātrāṃ karoti saḥ  || 105.4 ||
cacāra parayā bhaktyā dhyānamugraṃ hutāśanaḥ  |
vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ sāgraṃ yāvadāsīthutāśanaḥ  || 105.5 ||
tāvattuṣṭo mahādevo hutāśanamuvāca ha  |
havyavāha varaṃ brūhi yatte manasi vartate  || 105.6 ||

hutāśana uvāca - 
namaste sarvalokeśa ugrarūpa namo'stu te  |
yuṣmadretena saṃpluṣṭaḥ kubjo jāto maheśvara  || 105.7 ||
śarīrārto hyahaṃ kṛṣṇaḥ saṃsthito narmadātaṭe  |
ityuktvāntardadhe devo nīrujastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 105.8 ||
havyavāhena rājendra sthāpitaḥ piṅgaleśvaraḥ  |
jitakrodho'pi yastatra upavāsaṃ samācaret || 105.9 ||
atirātraphalaṃ tatra ante rudramavāpnuyāt |
guṇānvitāya dīnāya kapilāṃ tatra bhārata  || 105.10 ||
alaṃkṛtvā savastrāṃ ca savatsāṃ rūpasaṃyutām  |
yaḥ prayacchati viprāya sa gacchetparamaṃ padam  || 105.11 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sthāpitaṃ hyṛṣisaṅghaiśca brahmavaṃśodbhavairdvijaiḥ  || 105.12 ||
ṛṇamocanavikhyātaṃ revātaṭasamāśritam  |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ manujo bhaktyā tatra yastarpayetpitṝn  || 105.13 ||
divyaiḥ pitryairmanuṣyaiśca ṛṇairātmakṛtaiḥ saha  |
mucyate tatkṣaṇātso'tha snātvā vai narmadājale  || 105.14 ||
pratyakṣaṃ pātakaṃ tatra dṛśyate caiva rūpi ca  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājannekacitto jitendriyaḥ  || 105.15 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ naro dhīmān kārayedbhaktitatparaḥ  |
ṛṇatrayavimuktastu vīryavānmodate divi  || 105.16 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tasyaivānantaraṃ pārtha kapilātīrthamuttamam  |
sthāpitaṃ kapilenaiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 105.17 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca site pakṣe caturdaśyāṃ nareśvara  |
snāpayetparayā bhaktyā kapilākṣīrasarpiṣā  || 105.18 ||
madhunā khaṇḍayuktena dadhyakṣataphalena ca  |
kapileśaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha niśīthe taṃ jagatprabhum  || 105.19 ||
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena guṇṭhayecca maheśvaram  |
tataḥ sugandhapuṣpaiśca śvetaiśca nṛpanandana  || 105.20 ||
arcayanti jitakrodhā na te yānti yamālayam  |
asipatravanaṃ ghoraṃ yamavallīṃ sudāruṇām  || 105.21 ||
te vrajanti sukhaṃ pārtha kapileśe supūjite  |
snātvā revājale puṇye pūjayedbrāhmaṇaṃ śubham  || 105.22 ||
gopradānena vastreṇa annena kila bhāratā  |
chatraśayyāpradānena bhūmau rājā bhavettu saḥ  || 105.23 ||
nīrogastīvratejāśca jīvatputraḥ priyaṃvadaḥ  |
śatravo mitratāṃ yānti jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 105.24 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe kapileśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāma pañcādhikaśatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 106

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra pūtakeśvaramuttamam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 106.1 ||
susthāpitaḥ śivastatra lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
yastatra manujaḥ śambhuṃ pūjayetpāṇḍunandana  || 106.2 ||
sarvān kāmānavāpnoti na sa yāti yamālayam  |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ sarvakāmā narādhipa  || 106.3 ||
ye'rcayanti mahākālaṃ na te yānti yamālayam  || 106.4 ||
jalaśāyīti nāmnā vai vikhyātaṃ vasudhātale  |
dānavānāṃ vadhaṃ kṛtvā suptastatra janārdanaḥ  || 106.5 ||
cakraṃ ca kṣālitaṃ tatra devadevena śauriṇā  |
sudarśanaṃ ca niṣpāpaṃ revātoyaprabhāvataḥ  || 106.6 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
cakratīrthaṃ samācakṣva ṛṣisaṅghairniṣevitam  |
viṣṇoḥ prabhāvamatulaṃ revāyāścaiva yatphalam  || 106.7 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga viṣṇunā ca yudhiṣṭhira  |
gṛhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ cakriṇā svayam  || 106.8 ||
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm  |
āsītpurā mahādaityo nalamegha iti śrutaḥ  || 106.9 ||
tena devā jitāḥ sarve hṛtarājyā narādhipa  |
nalameghabhayātpārtha viṣṇurudrāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 106.10 ||
yamaskandajaleśāgnivāyavo vai dhaneśvaraḥ  |
vasuvākpatisiddhāśca pracetāśca pitāmahaḥ  || 106.11 ||
gatā devāḥ paraṃ lokaṃ viṣṇurudranamaskṛtam  |
stuvanti vividhaiḥ stotrairvāgīśapramukhā surāḥ  || 106.12 ||
namaḥ śivamūrtaye tubhyaṃ prāksṛṣṭeḥ kevalātmane  |
guṇatrayavibhāgāya paścādbhedamupeyuṣe  || 106.13 ||
indrādipramukhān devān vivarṇānavanīpate  |
prasādābhimukho devaḥ pratyuvāca divaukasaḥ  || 106.14 ||
svāgataṃ surasaṅghāśca kāntirnaṣṭā purātanī  |
himapluṣṭaprabhāṇīva jyotiṣāṃ ca mukhāni vai  || 106.15 ||
prasabhādarciṣāmetadanudgīrṇaṃ surāyudham  |
vṛtrasya hantuḥ kuliśaṃ kuṇṭhitaśrīva lakṣate  || 106.16 ||
kiṃcāyamaridurvāraḥ pāṇau pāśaḥ pracetasaḥ  |
mantropahṛtavīryasya phaṇino dainyamāgataḥ  || 106.17 ||
kuberasya manaḥ śalyaṃ śaṃsatīva parābhavam  |
apaviddhāṅgado bāhurbhagnaśākha iva drumaḥ  || 106.18 ||
yamo'pi vyalikhadbhūmiṃ daṇḍenāpi hatatviṣā  |
kuru tasmai namo dehanirviṇṇo yāti lāghavam  || 106.19 ||
amī ca dvādaśādityāḥ pratāpakṣayaśītalāḥ  |
citranyastā iva gatāḥ prakāmālokanīyatām  || 106.20 ||
mayi sṛṣṭiśca lokānāṃ rakṣā yuṣmāsvavasthitā  |
tato mandānilodbhūtakamalākaraśobhinā  || 106.21 ||
gurunetrasahasreṇa prerayāmāsa vṛtrahā  |
sadvinetro harastryakṣaḥ sahasranayanādhikau  || 106.22 ||
vācaspatiruvācedaṃ prāñjalirhaṃsavāhanam  |
yuṣmadvaṃśodbhavastāta nalamegho mahābalaḥ  || 106.23 ||
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve nirastā dānavena ca  |
nalamegho na vadhyo'taścalitena mayā suraiḥ  || 106.24 ||
vinā mādhavadevena sādhyo bhavati naiva hi  |
śaṅkhapadmagadāpāṇe jaya cakradhara prabho  || 106.25 ||
iti devastutiṃ śrutvā prabuddho jalaśāyikaḥ  |
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ meghagambhīrayā girā  || 106.26 ||
kimarthaṃ bodhito brahman samastaiśca surāsuraiḥ  |

brahmovāca - 
nalameghabhayeneha samprāptāstava mandiram  || 106.27 ||
na vadhyaḥ kasyacitpāpo nalamegho janārdana  |
tava hastena duṣṭātmā mṛtyuṃ prāpsyati nānyathā  || 106.28 ||

janārdana uvāca - 
svasthānaṃ yāntu gīrvāṇā vadhiṣyāmi mahābalam  |
sthānaṃ śaṃsantu me devā vasate yatra durmatiḥ  || 106.29 ||

devā ūcu̱ḥ
himālayaguhāṃ kṛṣṇa vasate dānaveśvaraḥ  |
caturviṃśatsahasraistu kanyābhistu samāvṛtaḥ  || 106.30 ||
turaṅgaiḥ syandanaiścaiva saṃkhyā teṣāṃ na vidyate  |
bhavanāni vicitrāṇi asaṃkhyāni bahūnyapi  || 106.31 ||
dviradāḥ parvatākārā hayāśca dviradopamāḥ  |
mahābalo'vasattatra gīrvāṇabhayadāyakaḥ  || 106.32 ||
śrutvā devo vacasteṣāṃ devānāmāturātmanām  |
garuḍaṃ cintayāmāsa śatrusaṅghavidāraṇam  || 106.33 ||
cakraṃ kareṇa saṃgṛhya gadāṃ śaṅkhaṃ tataḥ prabhuḥ  |
śārṅgaṃ ca muśalaṃ sīraṃ gṛhītvā hi janārdanaḥ  || 106.34 ||
ārūḍhaḥ pakṣirājaṃ tu vadhārthaṃ dānavasya ca  |
dānavasya gṛhe pārtha utpātā ghoradarśanāḥ  || 106.35 ||
gomāyurgṛhamadhye tu kapoto gṛhamāviśat |
vinā vātena tasyaiva dhvajadaṇḍaṃ papāta ha  || 106.36 ||
sarpamūṣakayorbuddhaṃ tathā keśarināgayoḥ  |
unmārgāḥ saritastatra vahante cakramāśritāḥ  || 106.37 ||
akāle tarupuṣpāṇi dṛśyante tatra parvate  |
tataḥ prāpto jagannātho himavantaṃ nageśvaram  || 106.38 ||
pāñcajanyaṃ ca kṛṣṇena pūritaṃ purasannidhau  |
pāñcajanyasya śabdena ārūḍho dānaveśvaraḥ  || 106.39 ||

nalamegha uvāca - 
ko'yaṃ mṛtyuvaśaṃ prāptastvajñānena samāvṛtaḥ  |
dhundhumāra vraja śīghraṃ svasainyaparivāritaḥ  || 106.40 ||
balādānaya taṃ baddhvā mamāgre balaśālinam  |

dhundhumāra uvāca - 
ānayāmi na sandehaḥ surapakṣāṃśca sāmpratam  || 106.41 ||
syandanaiśca samāyukto gajavājibhaṭaiḥ saha  |
dṛṣṭastāto jagadyoniḥ suparṇastho mahābalaḥ  || 106.42 ||
gṛhyatāṃ gṛhyatāmeṣa ityuktāste ca kiṅkarāḥ  |
caturdikṣu ca vartante kiṅkarāḥ keśavasya ca  || 106.43 ||
suparṇenāgnibāṇena hanyante śalabhā iva  |
dhundhumāro'pi kṛṣṇena śaraghātena tāḍitaḥ  || 106.44 ||
hato vakṣasthalopānte patitaḥ syandanopari  |
hāhākāraṃ tataḥ sarve dānavāścakrurudyatāḥ  || 106.45 ||
nalameghastataḥ kruddho rathārūḍho vinirgataḥ  |
dadarśa keśavaṃ pārtha śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 106.46 ||

nalamegha uvāca - 
hanyatāṃ dānavāḥ kṛṣṇo nihato yena dānavaḥ  |
hatvā ca me camūmukhyamadhunā ca kva yāsyati  || 106.47 ||
ityuktvā dānavaḥ pārtha dharṣayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ  |
dānavasya śarāṃstatra chedayāmāsa keśavaḥ  || 106.48 ||
garutmān bhakṣayāmāsa tatsainyamatibhīṣaṇam  |
kṛṣṇena dviguṇāstatra preṣitā hi śilīmukhāḥ  || 106.49 ||
dviguṇāddviguṇīkṛtya preṣayāmāsa dānavaḥ  |
te'pi cāṣṭaguṇāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ chādayāmāsurojasā  || 106.50 ||
tataḥ kruddhena devena āgneyaṃ preṣitaṃ tadā  |
vāruṇaṃ prativāyavyaṃ nalamegho vyasarjayat || 106.51 ||
nārasiṃhaṃ nṛsiṃho'yaṃ preṣayāmāsa pāṇḍava  |
nārasiṃhaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā nalamegho mahābalaḥ  || 106.52 ||
uttīrṇaḥ syandanācchīghraṃ khaḍgaṃ gṛhya kareṇa tu  |
preṣayāmāsa kṛṣṇāya taṃ hantuṃ balavattaraḥ  || 106.53 ||
kruddho'tha dānavaḥ pārtha āgataḥ keśavaṃ prati  |
khaḍgena ghātayāmāsa gadāpāṇiṃ janārdanam  || 106.54 ||
maṇḍalāgraṃ tato gṛhya keśavo hṛṣṭamānasaḥ  |
hato vakṣasthale daityo nalamegho mahābalaḥ  || 106.55 ||
janārdanaṃ tadā daityo nārācena jaghāna ha  |
janārdanastataḥ kruddho nalameghaṃ mṛdhe nṛpa  || 106.56 ||
amoghaṃ cakramādāya śirastasya nyapātayat |
patatā śirasā tasya vasudhā ca prakampitā  || 106.57 ||
samudrāḥ kṣubhitāḥ pārtha bhayādunmārgagāminaḥ  |
puṣpavaṣṭiṃ tato devā vavṛṣuḥ keśavopari  || 106.58 ||
avadhyaḥ surasaṅghānāṃ sa hataḥ keśavena tu  |
svasthānaśca gato devo nalameghe nipātite  || 106.59 ||
janārdano'pi kaunteya narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
lakṣmīsamanvitaḥ kṛṣṇo vilīno narmadātaṭe  || 106.60 ||
cakraṃ vimocitaṃ pāpakṣālanāya malānvitam  |
patitaṃ narmadātoye jalaśāyi samanvitam  || 106.61 ||
nirdhūtakalmaṣaṃ jātaṃ narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
nalameghavadhotpannaṃ yatpāpaṃ manujādhipa  || 106.62 ||
tatsarvaṃ kṣālitaṃ śīghraṃ revāyāmbhasi bhārata  |
tadā prabhṛti loke'smin jalaśāyī mahītale  || 106.63 ||
cakratīrthaṃ vadantyanye anekāghaughanāśanam  || 106.64 ||
tattīrthasya prabhāvaṃ vai śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  |
nāgānāṃ ca yathānanto gīrvāṇānāṃ janārdana  || 106.65 ||
māsānāṃ mārgaśīrṣo'pi nadīpuṇyā hi narmadā  |
māsi mārge site pakṣe ekādaśyāṃ śubhe dine  || 106.66 ||
gatvā ye manujāstatra kāmakrodhavivarjitāḥ  |
snāpayanti śriyaḥ kāntaṃ nāgaparyaṅkaśāyinam  || 106.67 ||
rājendra parayā bhaktyā kṣaudrasāgarasarpiṣā  |
guḍena toyamiśreṇa jagadyoniṃ janārdanam  || 106.68 ||
snāpyamānāśca paśyanti ye lokāgatapātakāḥ  |
te yānti paramaṃ lokaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 106.69 ||
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpamathavā tailamiśritam  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā devaḥ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 106.70 ||
kathāṃ ca vaiṣṇavīṃ bhaktyā ye śṛṇvanti narottamāḥ  |
brahmahatyādipāpebhyo mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 106.71 ||
ye kurvanti pradakṣiṇaṃ jalaśāyi jagadgurum  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtaṃ tena jambūdvīpaṃ nareśvara  || 106.72 ||
tataḥ prabhāte vimale pitṝn saṃtarpya yatnataḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ vai brāhmaṇaistatra pūjyaiḥ pāṇḍavasattama  || 106.73 ||
svadāranirataiḥ śāntaiḥ paradāravivarjitaiḥ  |
vedābhyasanaśīlaiśca svakarmanirataiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 106.74 ||
nityaṃ sajjanaśīlaiśca trisandhyāparipālakaiḥ  |
tādṛśaiḥ kārayecchrāddhamiccheyuḥ śreya ātmanām  || 106.75 ||
te dhanyā mānuṣe loke puṇyāścaivātra mānuṣāḥ  |
ye vasanti sadā kālaṃ pade brahmāśraye hrade  || 106.76 ||
jalaśāyinaṃ ca paśyanti pratyakṣaṃ suranāyakam  |
pakṣopavāsaṃ ye kecidvrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ śubham  || 106.77 ||
māsopavāsamugraṃ ca tathānyatparamaṃ vratam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ pārtha kuryātsvargamavāpnuyāt || 106.78 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi tiladhenostu yatphalam  |
tathā yasmin yathā deyaṃ dānaṃ tasya śrutaṃ phalam  || 106.79 ||
etatkathāntare puṇye munīndraiḥ pāpanāśanam  |
śrutaṃ hi naimiṣāraṇye nāradādyairanekadhā  || 106.80 ||
idamākhyānamāyuṣyaṃ puṇyaṃ kīrtivivardhanam  |
viprāṇāṃ śrāvayedyastu sarvaṃ tatphalamāpnuyāt || 106.81 ||
bahūnāṃ na pradeyāni gogṛhaṃ śayanaṃ kila  |
vibhaktadakṣiṇā hyeṣā dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhati  || 106.82 ||
ekasmai sā pradātavyā bahūnāṃ na yudhiṣṭhira  |
sā ca vikrayamāpannā dahedāsaptamaṃ kulam  || 106.83 ||
tilāḥ śvetāstathā kṛṣṇāstilāḥ proktāśca varṇataḥ  |
tilānāṃ ca pramāṇāni dhenorvatsasya kārayet || 106.84 ||
dātavyā vatsakenātha bahūnāṃ kāmināṃ na tu  |
yasmin deśe ca yanmānaṃ tilānāṃ ca vicārataḥ  || 106.85 ||
tena mānena sā kāryā akṣayaṃ phalamicchatā  |
arcanīyā prayatnena gandhapuṣpākṣatairvibho  || 106.86 ||
nāsāyāṃ sarvagandhāśca jihvāyāṃ ṣaḍrasā dhṛtāḥ  |
muktāphalāni vā dantajaṅghāpuccheṣu yojayet || 106.87 ||
kukṣau kārpāsakaṃ deyaṃ nābhyāṃ padmaṃ sakāñcanam  |
oṣṭhe madhughṛtaṃ dadyātkuryātsarpiśca romake  || 106.88 ||
kambale kambalaṃ dadyāllalāṭe tāmrabhājanam  |
skandhe tu śakalā deyā lohadaṇḍaṃ ca saṅkaṭe  || 106.89 ||
guḍaṃ caiva gude dadyācchroṇyāṃ madhughṛte tathā  |
yavase pāyasaṃ dadyādghṛtakṣaudrasamanvitam  || 106.90 ||
svarṇaśṛṅgī raupyakhurī muktālāṅgulabhūṣaṇā  |
vastraṃ sadannaṃ dātavyaṃ kāṃsyapātrasudohanā  || 106.91 ||
yattu bālakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yadvā kṛtamajānatā  |
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā yacca cintitam  || 106.92 ||
jalamātre ṣṭhīvane ca muśale vā vilaṅghite  |
vṛṣalīgamane caiva gurudāraniṣevaṇe  || 106.93 ||
kanyāyāṃ gamane caiva suvrarṇasteya eva ca  |
surāpānaṃ ca yaccāpi tiladhenuḥ punāti hi  || 106.94 ||
ahorātropavāsena vidhivatsā mayoditā  |
yāsau yamapure caiva nadī vaitaraṇī smṛtā  || 106.95 ||
bālukāyāḥ sthale caiva pacyate yatra duṣkṛtī  |
avīcinarakopetau yau vai yugalaparvatau  || 106.96 ||
yatra lohamukhāḥ kākā yatra sthānaṃ bhayānakam  |
asipatravanaṃ yatra taptaṃ ca bālukam  || 106.97 ||
tatsukhena vyatikramya dharmarājā'śramaṃ vrajet |
dharmarājastu taṃ dṛṣṭvā sūnṛtaṃ vākyamabravīt || 106.98 ||
vitānaṃ vitataṃ yogyaṃ maṇiralavibhūṣitam  |
atrāgaccha nṛpaśreṣṭha gaccha tvaṃ paramāṃ gatim  || 106.99 ||
mā ca pāparate dānaṃ na taddānaṃ paraṃ hitam  |
mā vikāle virūpe ca na vyaṅge tu tathaiva ca  || 106.100 ||
avedaviduṣe caiva brāhmaṇe madaviklave  |
mitraghne ca kṛtaghne ca vratahīne tathaiva ca  || 106.101 ||
vedāntagāya dātavyā tasya tattvaṃ vijānate  |
vedāntage tu sā deyā śrotriye'bhūtabālakā  || 106.102 ||
sarvāṅgarucire deyā pavitre ca priyaṃvade  |
paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāṃ kārtikyāṃ cāpi bhārata  || 106.103 ||
vaiśākhyāṃ mārgaśīrṣe ca grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
ayane viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte ca sarvathā  || 106.104 ||
ṣaḍaśītimukhe caiva gajachāyāsu sarvadā  |
eṣa te kathitaḥ kalpastiladhenormayānagha  || 106.105 ||
bhittvā ca bhāskaraṃ lokaṃ harilokaṃ vrajanti te  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ cakratīrthaphalaṃ nṛpa  || 106.106 ||
śravaṇātkīrtanādvā'pi gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 106.107 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe cakratīrthamāhātmyavarṇano nāma ṣaḍadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 107

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
candrādityaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha sthāpitaṃ caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥ  || 107.1 ||
āsītpurā mahābhāgau caṇḍamuṇḍau tu dānavau  |
tapaśca ceratustatra narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 107.2 ||
dhyāyato bhāskaraṃ devaṃ tamo nāśaṃ jagadgurum  |
tābhyāṃ ca toṣitaḥ so'pi sahasrāṃśuruvāca ha  || 107.3 ||
sādhusādhviti tau pārtha narmadāyāstaṭe śūbhe  |
caṇḍamuṇḍau varaṃ brūtaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ manasepsitam  || 107.4 ||

caṇḍamuṇḍāvūcatu̱ḥ
ajeyau caiva deveśa sarveṣāṃ devatānṛṇām  |
rogaiśceva parityaktau savikāśaṃ divākara  || 107.5 ||
tattatheti śubhaṃ vākyaṃ bhāskarastāvavīdat |
tataścāntardadhe bhānurdānavābhyāṃ ca bhāskaram  || 107.6 ||
sthāpitaṃ parayā bhaktyā pūjayedyo jitendriyaḥ  |
gīrvāṇatā manuṣyasya prāptā tatraiva vartate  || 107.7 ||
vasate bhāskare loke vairañce devasannidhau  |
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpaṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ nareśvara  || 107.8 ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mṛto yāti puraṃ raveḥ  |
candrādityamiti khyātaṃ tatra tīrthaṃ narottama  || 107.9 ||
vijayante sadā kālaṃ candrādityaṃ vrajanti ye  || 107.10 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe candrādityeśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāma saptādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 108

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra yamahāsamanuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ pārtha revāyāstaṭamāśritam  || 108.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
yamahāsaṃ kathaṃ jātaṃ pṛthivyāṃ munipuṅgava  |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyāhi mama kautūhalaṃ param  || 108.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga pṛṣṭaṃ te dharmanandana  |
snānārthaṃ narmadāyāṃ tu āgataste pitā purā  || 108.3 ||
rajakena yathā dhautaṃ nirmalaṃ vasanaṃ bhavet |
tathaiva paśyatā rājan revājalamanuttamam  || 108.4 ||
hāsyaṃ kṛtaṃ yamenātha utthitaṃ liṅgamuttamam  |
tatastadā hi rājendra vāguvācā'śarīriṇī  || 108.5 ||
yamahāsamidaṃ tīrthaṃ khyātiṃ yāsyati sarvadā  |
snāpayitvā śivaṃ tatra yamaḥ svargaṃ jagāma ha  || 108.6 ||
yamahāse tu rājendra jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  |
viśeṣādāśvine māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  || 108.7 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpaṃ devasya bodhayet || 108.8 ||
ghṛtena caiva rājendra śṛṇu tasyaiva yatphalam  |
mucyate sarvapāpaistu agamyāgamanodbhavaiḥ  || 108.9 ||
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaiḥ pāpaiḥ pāpairvā peyasambhavaiḥ  |
avāhyavāhaneyacca adrohya drohaṇe tathā  || 108.10 ||
snānamātreṇa taccaiva naśyetpāpamanekadhā  |
yamalokaṃ na paśyecca na tyajetpāṇḍunandana  || 108.11 ||
bahūnāṃ paramaṃ guptaṃ tīrthaṃ bhūmyāṃ nṛpātmaja  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ teṣāṃ yamahāse pradāyinām  || 108.12 ||
amāvāsyāṃ jitakrodho yastu pūjayate dvijān  |
bhūmidānena yo bhaktyā tiladānena bhārata  || 108.13 ||
kṛṣṇājinapradānena tiladhenupradānataḥ  |
vasubhe vṛddhiyoge ca ye pradāsyanti bhaktitaḥ  || 108.14 ||
odanaṃ vāri dhūrvāhaṃ hayaṃ cāpi mahābalam  |
kanyāṃ vastramajāṃ gāṃ vai mahiṣīmathavāśvinīm  || 108.15 ||
ye yacchanti nṛpaśreṣṭha nopasarpanti te yamam  |
yamo'pi bhavati prīto janmajanma yudhiṣṭhira  || 108.16 ||
yamasya vāhanaṃ strīṃ ca mahiṣīṃ tatra bhārata  |
tasya dānena satataṃ yamaḥ prīto bhavennṛpa  || 108.17 ||
na sa yāti yame loke yadi pāpaiḥ samāśritaḥ  |
etasmātkāraṇātpārtha mahiṣīdānaṃ uttamam  || 108.18 ||
aurṇavastradvayaṃ kāryaṃ lohavarṇaṃ ca veṣṭitam  |
dāpayeddharmarājāya prīyatāṃ me dvijottama  || 108.19 ||
anenaiva tu dānena yamaḥ prīto'stu me sadā  |
ityuccārya dvijasyāgre yamalokaṃ bhayāvaham  || 108.20 ||
asipatravane ghore yamavallī sudāruṇā  |
raudrā vaitaraṇī ceti kumbhīpākaḥ sudāruṇaḥ  || 108.21 ||
kālasūtraṃ mahābhīmaṃ tathā yamalaparvatau  |
krakacaṃ tailayantraṃ ca sthāne gṛdhrāḥ sudāruṇāḥ  || 108.22 ||
aniśvāso mahāraudro bhīṣaṇo rauravastathā  |
ete ghorāśca narakāḥ śrūyante dvijasattama  || 108.23 ||
tatprasādena te saumyāstīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
dānasyāsya prabhāveṇa yamahāsaprabhāvataḥ  || 108.24 ||
yamalokaṃ na vai yāti narakaṃ naiva paśyati  || 108.25 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe yamahāsamahimānuvarṇano nāmāṣṭādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 109

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra koṭīśvaramanuttamam  |
ṛṣikoṭiḥ samāyātā tatra vai kurunandana  || 109.1 ||
kṛṣṇadvaipāyanastatra mokṣārthaṃ munipuṅgavaḥ  |
mantrayitvā dvijaiḥ sarvairvedamaṇḍalapāragaiḥ  || 109.2 ||
pañcāmṛtena gavyena tilamiśreṇa tatpara  |
pitṛṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍadānaṃ yathāvidhi  || 109.3 ||
śrāvaṇasya tu māsasya pūrṇimāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
prāpyate cākṣayā tṛptiryāvadāhūtasamplavam  || 109.4 ||
pitṝṇāṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ revājalamupāśritam  |
mokṣadaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ nirmitaṃ munipuṅgavaiḥ  || 109.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra vyāsatīrthamanuttamam  |
durlabhaṃ manujaiḥ pārtha antarikṣe vyavasthitam  || 109.6 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kasmādvai vyāsatīrthaṃ tu antarikṣe vyavasthitam  |
etadācakṣva saṃkṣepānna ca granthasya vistaraḥ  || 109.7 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahābāho dharmavān guruvatsalaḥ  |
svadharmanirataḥ pārtha tīrthayātrākṛtādaraḥ  || 109.8 ||
durlabhaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ vyāsatīrthaṃ nareśvara  |
dharṣito vṛddhabhāvena vaikalyena narādhipa  || 109.9 ||
gatacetāstu sañjātastathā bhoḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 109.10 ||
kālastatraiva rājendra na vasedvāsavājñayā  |
antarikṣe ca saṃjātaṃ revāyāśceṣṭitaṃ yataḥ  || 109.11 ||
virañcirnaiva śaknoti revāyā guṇakīrtanam  |
vyāsatīrthaṃ viśeṣeṇa śrutamātraṃ vadāmyaham  || 109.12 ||
pratyakṣaḥ pratyayo yatra dṛśyate hi kalau yuge  |
vihaṅgo gacchate naiva bhittvā śūlaṃ sudāruṇam  || 109.13 ||
tasyotpattiṃ samāsena kathayāmi nṛpātmaja  |
āsītpūrvaṃ mahārāja ṛṣiścaiva parāśaraḥ  || 109.14 ||
tena cograṃ tapastaptaṃ gaṅgāmbhasi tu bhārata  |
prāṇāyāmena cātiṣṭhatpraviṣṭo jāhnavījale  || 109.15 ||
pūrṇe ca dvādaśe varṣe niṣkrānto jalamadhyataḥ  |
bhikṣārthe cāgato grāmaṃ nāvi tatraiva tiṣṭhatī  || 109.16 ||
tatra dṛṣṭā parotsṛṣṭā bālā tena manoramā  |
tāṃ ca dṛṣṭvā sa kāmārta uvāca madhurākṣaram  || 109.17 ||
māṃ ramasvādya tvaṃ kāsi kāmukaṃ mṛgalocane  |
nāvārūḍhe nadītīre mama cittapramāthinī  || 109.18 ||
evamuktā tu sā tena praṇamya ṛṣisattamam  |
nāvaṃ rakṣāmyahaṃ vipra jānāmi svāminaṃ na tu  || 109.19 ||
mamedaṃ ca vayo brahmañccheṣaṃ tvaṃ jñātumarhasi  |
evamuktastayā so'pi kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvābravīdidam  || 109.20 ||
ahaṃ jñānabalādbhadre jānāmi tava sambhavam  |
kaivartaputrikā na tvaṃ rājaputrī hi sundarī  || 109.21 ||

kanyovāca - 
kaḥ pitā kathyatāṃ brahman kasyāhamudarodbhavā  |
kasmin vaṃśe prajātāhaṃ kaivartatanayā katham  || 109.22 ||

parāśara uvāca - 
kathayāmi ca te tātaṃ yaṃ tvaṃ māṃ paripṛcchasi  |
vasu nāmā ca rājābhūtsomavaṃśe pratāpavān  || 109.23 ||
jambūdvīpādhipo bhadre śatrusaṃtrāsanastathā  |
śatāni sapta bhāryāṇāṃ putrāṇāṃ tu daśaiva hi  || 109.24 ||
dharmeṇa pālitā lokāḥ śivapūjārataḥ sadā  |
mlecchāstasya virodhena śākadvīpanivāsinaḥ  || 109.25 ||
teṣāṃ ca sādhanārthāya gatollaṅghya mahodadhim  |
saṃyuktaḥ putrabhṛtyaiśca pauruṣe mahati sthitaḥ  || 109.26 ||
sagrāmastaiḥ samārabdhaścārvaṅgi vasunā saha  |
jitā mlecchāḥ samastāśca vasunā hyavanībhṛtā  || 109.27 ||
karadāste kṛtāstena sabhṛtyabalavāhanāḥ  |
pradhānā tasya mahiṣī tava mātā mṛgekṣaṇā  || 109.28 ||
pravāsasthe ca bhūpāle saṃjātā ca rajasvalā  |
nārīṇāṃ tu sadā kāle manmatho hyadhiko bhavet || 109.29 ||
viśeṣeṇa ṛtau kāle bhidyate kāmasāyakaiḥ  |
manmathena tu saṃtaptācintayatsā śubhekṣaṇā  || 109.30 ||
dūtaṃ sampreṣayāmyadya vasurājasamīpataḥ  |
vyāhṛtaḥ satvaro dūto gaccha tvaṃ nṛpasannidhau  || 109.31 ||

dūta uvāca - 
pararājye vasurdevi gato rājā dviḍantakṛt |
tatra gantuṃ na śakyeta jalayantrairvinā śubhe  || 109.32 ||
jalayānāni sarvāṇi neyāni ca pare taṭe  |
tasya vākyena sā nārī viṣaṇṇā madapīḍitā  || 109.33 ||
rājñīṃ dṛṣṭvā sakhī brūte kasmāttvaṃ parikhidyase  |
lekho'yaṃ preṣyatāṃ devi śukahaste yathā tathā  || 109.34 ||
samudraṃ laṅghayitvā tu śakunto yāti sundari  |
vyāhṛto lekhakastatra likha lekhaṃ'mamājñayā  || 109.35 ||
tvāṃ vinā paṭṭarājñī sā vasurāja na jīvati  |
ṛtukālaśca samprāptaḥ samastaṃ cāvadhāryatām  || 109.36 ||
likhito bhūrjapatre ca vṛttaḥ sa lekhakena tu  |
śūkaḥ pañjaramadhyastha ānītastatra sannidhau  || 109.37 ||
rājñī uvāca taṃ tatra gṛhyalekhaṃ śuka vraja  |
gataḥ pakṣī tataḥ śīghraṃ vasurājasamīpataḥ  || 109.38 ||
kṣipto lekhaḥ śukenaiva satyabhāmā visarjitaḥ  |
lekhārthaṃ cintayāmāsa vīryaṃ gṛhya nareśvaraḥ  || 109.39 ||
amoghapuṭikāṃ kṛtvā pratilokena viśrutaḥ  |
śūkasya cārpayāmāsa gaccha rājñī samīpataḥ  || 109.40 ||
vasurājaṃ praṇamyātha bījaṃ gṛhya papāta ha  |
samudropari saṃprāptaḥ śuko yāti yathecchayā  || 109.41 ||
sāmiṣaṃ ca śukaṃ jñātvā śyenastatrāpi dhāvitaḥ  |
hataścañcuprahāreṇa śukaḥ śyenena bhārata  || 109.42 ||
mūrchāpannasya tadvīrya patitaṃ jalamadhyataḥ  |
matsyena gilitaṃ tatra tadvīryaṃ pārthivasya ca  || 109.43 ||
dāśairmatsyo gahītaśca ānītaḥ svagṛhaṃ prati  |
yāvattaṃ pāṭayāmāsūryamalaṃ dadṛśe tadā  || 109.44 ||
śaśimaṇḍalasaṅkāśaṃ sūryatejaḥ samaprabham  |
dṛṣṭvā te dharṣitāḥ sarve kaivartā jāhnavītaṭe  || 109.45 ||
harṣitāste gatāḥ sarve pradhānasya ca mandiram  |
putraṃ rājñe pradāyaiva putrīṃ ca pratyapālayat || 109.46 ||
tattvaṃ devi varārohe kaivartakanyakā nahi  |
tataḥ sā cintayāmāsa parāśaravacastadā  || 109.47 ||
evamuktā tu sā tena dattvātmānaṃ nareśvara  |
uvāca sādhvī bho brahmanmatsyagandho nivartyatām  || 109.48 ||
tatastena tu sā bālā divyagandhābhivāsitā  |
kṛtā yogabalenaiva jvālayitvā vibhāvasum  || 109.49 ||
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ vahneruddhṛtā tena sā tadā  |
jvalamānasya madhye tu kāmasthānāni so'spṛśat || 109.50 ||
jñātvā kāmotsukaṃ vipraṃ bhītā sā nṛpanandana  |
uvāca sahasā bālā divā ca lokasannidhau  || 109.51 ||
tatastena kṣaṇaṃ dhyātā pūrayantī digambaram  |
āgatā tāmasī tāta yayā vyāptaṃ samantataḥ  || 109.52 ||
tataḥ savismayaṃ tena rahobālā mṛgekṣaṇā  |
kāmenaiva hi taptena strīsaukhyaṃ krīḍatā tadā  || 109.53 ||
tatastena muhūrtenāpatyabhāreṇa pīḍitā  |
bālakaṃ tatra jaṭilaṃ subhagaṃ daṇḍadhārakam  || 109.54 ||
kamaṇḍaludharaṃ śāntaṃ mekhalākaṭibhūṣaṇam  |
dhṛtopavītakaṃ skandhe viṣṇumāyāvivārjitam  || 109.55 ||
mātā hi śaṅkitā tatra dṛṣṭvā putrasya cāpalam  |
vepamānā tato bālā gatā sā śaraṇaṃ muneḥ  || 109.56 ||
rakṣa rakṣa muniśreṣṭha parāśara mahāmune  |
jātamatyadbhutaṃ vipraṃ kaupīnāmbaramekhalam  || 109.57 ||
daṇḍahastaṃ jaṭāyuktamuttarīyavibhūṣitam  |

parāśara uvāca - 
mā bhaiṣīstvaṃ suto jātaḥ kanyaiva tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 109.58 ||
nāmnā satyavatī ceti dvitīyā gandhayojanā  |
śantanurnāma rājā vai sa te bhartā bhaviṣyati  || 109.59 ||
prathamā mahiṣī tasya somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇā  |
gaccha tvaṃ svāśramaṃ bhadre pūrvarūpeṇa saṃsthitā  || 109.60 ||
mā vaikalyaṃ kuruṣvedaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ jñānasya me balam  |
ityuktvā prayayau vipraḥ sā bālā sthalamāśritā  || 109.61 ||
natvā putraṃ parābhaktyā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇayena ca  |
taṃ prayāntamathālokya satyavatyabravītsutam  || 109.62 ||
kāmo deyastvayābhīṣṭaḥ sneho vai yadi mātari  |
kimapyupādiśa tvaṃ māṃ yena siddhimavāpnuyām  || 109.63 ||

vyāsa uvāca - 
īśvarārādhane yatnaṃ kuruṣva tvaṃ sadāmbike  |
tataḥ sā putravākyena viṣaṇṇā pāṇḍunandana  || 109.64 ||

yojanagandhovāca - 
tvadviyogādahaṃ putra pañcatvaṃ yāmi nānyathā  |
nāsti putrasamaḥ sneho nāsti bhrātṛsamaṃ balam  || 109.65 ||

putra uvāca - 
mā viṣādaṃ kuruṃ tvaṃ hi satyaṃ vai pitṛbhāṣitam  |
āpatkāle tvayāhaṃ vai smartavyaḥ kāryasiddhaye  || 109.66 ||
āpadastārayiṣyāmi kṣamyatāṃ mātarātmaje  |
ityuktvā tu yayau vyāsaḥ kanyā sā'pi piturgṛham  || 109.67 ||
parāśarasutastatra niṣaṇṇo vanamadhyataḥ  |
tretāyugāvasāne tu dvāparādau nareśvara  || 109.68 ||
vyāso virañcaye jāta ākhyāto nāradena ca  |
ṛṣirmahānubhāvastu putraḥ pārāśarasya ca  || 109.69 ||
kaivartakanyakājāto jānīhi jāhnavī taṭe  |
vākyoktyā nāradasyaiva āgatāḥ surasattamāḥ  || 109.70 ||
bhānuḥ pitāmahaḥ śakra ṛṣisaṅghaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  |
āśīrvādaṃ pṛthagdattvā sādhu sādhviti bhāṣitaḥ  || 109.71 ||
pitāmahena bālo'sau jātamakarmādi saṃskṛtaḥ  |
dvaipāyano dvīpajanmā pārāśaryaḥ parāśarāt || 109.72 ||
kṛṣṇagātrātkṛṣṇanāmā havyādāturviśiṣyati  |
virañcinābhiṣikto'sau ṛṣisaṅghaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 109.73 ||
vyāsastvaṃ sarvalokānāmityuktvā prayayuḥ punaḥ  |
tīrthayātrā samārabdhā kṛṣṇadvaipāyanena tu  || 109.74 ||
gaṅgāvagāhitā tena kedāraṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  |
gayā ca naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ kurukṣetre sarasvatī  || 109.75 ||
ujjayinyāṃ mahākālaṃ somanāthaṃ yayau tataḥ  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ snāto vyāso mahāmuniḥ  || 109.76 ||
aṭan vai narmadāṃ prāpto rudradehodbhavāṃ nadīm  |
sāhlādaṃ narmadāṃ dṛṣṭvā cittaṃ viśrāmyapārthiva  || 109.77 ||
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ  || 109.78 ||
sārdravāsāstu hemante dhyāyamāno maheśvaram  |
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakartāramacchedyaṃ ca sadāśivam  || 109.79 ||
nityaṃ siddheśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ pūjayan pāṇḍunandana  |
arcanātsiddhaliṅgasya dhyānayogaprabhāvataḥ  || 109.80 ||
pratyakṣa īśvaro jātaḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasya tu  |
toṣito'haṃ tvayā vipra varaṃ prārthaya śobhanam  || 109.81 ||

vyāsa uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva yadi deyo varo mama  |
pratyakṣo narmadātīre svayaṃ bhavādya me vibho  || 109.82 ||
atītānāgatajñānaṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara  |

īśvara uvāca - 
evaṃ bhavatu te vipra matpasādātna saṃśayaḥ  || 109.83 ||
tvayā bhaktigṛhīto'haṃ pratyakṣo narmadātaṭe  |
ityuktvā prayayau devaḥ kailāsaṃ nagamuttamam  || 109.84 ||
patnīsaṃgrahaṇaṃ jātaṃ putro jāto yadāsya ca  |
devairadhyāsitaḥ sarvaiḥ sendrabrahmapurogamaiḥ  || 109.85 ||
putrajanmatatojñātvā āgatā ṛṣisattamāḥ  |
tīrthayātrāprasaṅgena parāśarapurogamāḥ  || 109.86 ||
manvatriviṣṇuhārītayājñavalkyośano'ṅgirāḥ  |
yamāpastambasaṃvartā kātyāyanabṛhaspatī  || 109.87 ||
evamādi sahasrāṇi lakṣakoṭiranekadhā  |
vyāsāśrame śubhe puṇye prāptāḥ sarve dvijottamāḥ  || 109.88 ||
dṛṣṭvā vyāsastu viprendrānabhyutthāya kṛtodyamaḥ  |
pitṛpūrvaṃ praṇamyādau teṣāṃ vārtāṃ pradāpayan  || 109.89 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā idaṃ vākyamuvāca ha  |
uddhṛtaṃ tu mamānandaṃ yuṣmatsambhāṣadarśanāt || 109.90 ||
āraṇyāni ca śākāni phalānyāraṇyakāni ca  |
tāni dāsyāmi sarveṣāṃ yuṣmākaṃ pitṛpūrvakam  || 109.91 ||
niyamaiḥ saṃyutān sarvān pratyekaṃ praṇanāma ca  |
tatastaṃ praṇataṃ dṛṣṭvā tatra dvaipāyanaṃ dvijam  || 109.92 ||
vardhayitvā jayāśīrbhistato vīkṣa parasparam  |
parāśaraḥ samastaiśca vīkṣito munipuṅgavaiḥ  || 109.93 ||
uttaraṃ dīyatāṃ tāta kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasya ca  |
evamuktastu taiḥ sarvairbhagavāṃśca parāśaraḥ  || 109.94 ||
uvāca svātmajaṃ vyāsamṛṣibhiryaccikīrṣitam  |
necchanti dakṣiṇe kūle vratabhaṅgabhayātsuta  || 109.95 ||
paraṃ vai bhoktukāmāśca tava śraddhāviśeṣataḥ  |

vyāsa uvāca - 
karomi bhavatāṃ yuktamatraiva sthīyatāṃ kṣaṇam  || 109.96 ||
yāvadāsādya saritaṃ karomi vidhipūrvakam  |
evamuktvā śucirbhūtvā narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 109.97 ||
stotraṃ jagāda sahasā taṃ nibodha janeśvara  || 109.98 ||

vyāsa uvāca - 
jaya devi namo varade śivade jaya pāpavimardini śūlakare  |
jaya bhairavadehavilīnakare jaya devi pitāmahasannamite  || 109.99 ||
jaya bhāskaraśakrasadānamite jaya ṣaṇmukhatātasute varade  |
jaya devaśarīrasamūhamaye jaya sāgaragāmini bhūmisute  || 109.100 ||
jaya jaya lokasamastakṛtābharaṇe jaya duḥkhadaridravināśakare  |
jaya putra kalatravivṛddhikare jaya devi sudarśanapāpahare  || 109.101 ||
etadvyāsakṛta stotraṃ yaḥ paṭhecchivasannidhau  |
gṛhe vā śuddhabhāvena kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
vyāsastasya bhavetprītaḥ prīto'yaṃ vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 109.102 ||
stutā ca narmadādevī tato vacanamabravīt |

narmadovāca - 
stutivādena tuṣṭāsmi bho bho vyāsamahāmune  || 109.103 ||
yamicchasi varaṃ samyaktaṃ te sarvaṃ dadāmyaham  |

vyāsa uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭāsi me devi yadi deyo varo mama  || 109.104 ||
ātithyamuttare kūle mama dātuṃ tvamarhasi  |

narmadovāca - 
ayuktaṃ cintitaṃ vyāsa amārge tvaṃ pravartase  || 109.105 ||
indracandrayamāḥ śaktā unmārgeṇa na vāhitum  |
anyaṃ yācasva he vatsa yatkiṃcidbhuvi durlabham  || 109.106 ||
eva śrutvā vaco devyā vyāso mūrcchāṃ gatastadā  |
vṛthā kleśaśca saṃjātaḥ patito dharaṇītale  || 109.107 ||
dharaṇī kampitā sarvā saśailavanakānanā  |
mūrchāpannaṃ tato vyāsaṃ jñātvā devāḥ parāśaram  || 109.108 ||
āyātā devatāḥ sarve hāhākāraṃ prakurvataḥ  |
utthāpayantaste vyāsamūcuśca saritāṃ varām  || 109.109 ||
brāhmaṇārthaṃ tu saṃkliṣṭā nāmahetoḥ saridvare  |
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe ca prāṇān parityajet || 109.110 ||
evaṃ sā narmadā proktā brahmādyaiśca surairdrutam  |
vikūlatāṃ vai pradadau samantādrevābhiṣiktaḥ sa jalena pūtaḥ  || 109.111 ||
sacetanaḥ satyavatī suto'yaṃ nanāma devaiḥ saha narmadāntaiḥ  |

vyāsa uvāca - 
tīrthaṃ samastaṃ tvayi devatānāṃ phalaṃ pradiṣṭaṃ mama mandabhāgyam  || 109.112 ||

narmadovāca - 
yato yato vyāsamahānubhāva sumerunāmno dharaṇīdharasya  |
vindhyasya cānyasya ca te hi mārgaṃ yāsyāmi vai dharmadharasya dhanyā  || 109.113 ||
evamukto mahātejā vyāsaḥ satyavatīyutaḥ  |
dakṣiṇe cālayāmāsa svāśramānmunipuṅgavān  || 109.114 ||
daṇḍahasto mahātejā huṅkārairnṛpanandana  |
vyāsahuṅkārabhītā ca calitā rudranandinī  || 109.115 ||
daṇḍena darśayanmārgaṃ devī tatra pravartitā  |
vyāsamārgagatāṃ devīṃ dṛṣṭvā cendrapurogamāḥ  || 109.116 ||
puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ dadurvyāse stutiṃ kurvanti kinnarāḥ  |
praphullanayanā jātāḥ parāśaramukhā dvijāḥ  || 109.117 ||
kiṃ kurmo'tra mahimnā te karmaṇā te varaṃ jitāḥ  |

vyāsa uvāca - 
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ dānaṃ dattvā mahatphalam  || 109.118 ||
etadeva naraiḥ kāryaṃ sādhūnāṃ paritoṣaṇam  |
suvibhaktā mahābhāgā anugrāhyasya samprati  || 109.119 ||
tasmānmamāśrame puṇye sthīyatāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
ātithyaṃ śākaparṇaiśca udakena vimiśritaiḥ  || 109.120 ||
pratipannaṃ samastaiśca parāśaramukhairdvijaiḥ  |
śrayadhvamāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadāyottare taṭe  || 109.121 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
snānatarpaṇakṛtyāni kṛtāni ca dvijottamaiḥ  |
vyāsakuṇḍaṃ tato gatvā homaṃ samyagakārayan  || 109.122 ||
śrīphalairbilvapatraiśca dhyānayuktāśca juhvati  |
gautamo bhṛgumāṇḍavyau nārado lomaśastathā  || 109.123 ||
parāśarastathā śaṅkhaḥ kauśikaścyavano muniḥ  |
pippalādo vaśiṣṭhaśca nāśiketurmahātapāḥ  || 109.124 ||
viśvāmitro hyagastyaśca uddālakayamau tathā  |
śāṇḍilyo jaiminiḥ kāvyo yājñavalkyośanāṅgirāḥ  || 109.125 ||
atriḥ śātātapaścaiva bharato mudgalastathā  |
vātsyāyano mahātejāḥ saṃvartaḥ śaktireva ca  || 109.126 ||
jātūkarṇo bharadvājo bālakhilyādayastathā  |
ekacittā dvijā rājanmantratantraṃ prakurvataḥ  || 109.127 ||
tataḥ samutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ jvalatkālānalaprabham  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato vyāso devaṃ dṛṣṭvā trilocanam  || 109.128 ||
āśīrvādaṃ punarviprā dattvā vyāsaṃ tadā yayuḥ  |
tataḥ prabhṛti tattvajña tīrthaṃ khyātaṃ tu pāṇḍava  || 109.129 ||
snānadānavidhānaṃ ca yasmin kāle pratiṣṭhitam  |
kathayāmi samastaṃ te bhrātṝṇāṃ caiva pāṇḍava  || 109.130 ||
kārttikasyāsite pakṣe caturdaśyāṃ nṛpottama  |
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 109.131 ||
snāpayedīśvaraṃ bhaktyā kṣaudreṇa kṣīrasarpiṣā  || 109.132 ||
dadhnā ca khaṇḍayuktena kuśatoyena vā punaḥ  |
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena pūjayeta maheśvaram  || 109.133 ||
tataḥ sugandhapuṣpaiśca bilvapatraiśca pūjayet |
kumudena ca kundena kuśapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ  || 109.134 ||
unmattapuṣpaiśca rasaiḥ saumyaiścaivāpyanuttamaiḥ  |
arcayetparayā bhaktyā dvīpeśvaramanuttamam  || 109.135 ||
mandārādikapuṣpaiśca pūjayetparameśvaram  |
guḍamaṇḍapradānena pātakaṃ divasārjitam  || 109.136 ||
māsārjitaṃ ca naśyeta guḍamaṇḍaśatena ca  |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ ca sahasreṇa arkābdaṃ dviguṇena tu  || 109.137 ||
ājanmajanitaṃ pāpamayutena praṇaśyati  |
paurṇamāsyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha snānaṃ kurvīta bhaktitaḥ  || 109.138 ||
mantroktena vidhānena kṛtvā pāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
vāruṇaṃ ca tathā jñeyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 109.139 ||
devān pitṝnmanuṣyāṃśca vidhivattarpayennṛpa  |
ṛcamekāṃ japetsnātaḥ sāmavedaphalaṃ labhet || 109.140 ||
yajurvedamatharvāṇaṃ gāyatryā sarvamāpnuyāt |
japedaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ sauraṃ vā śaivameva ca  || 109.141 ||
athavā vaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ dvādaśākṣarameva ca  |
pūjayedbrāhmaṇān bhaktyā sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitān  || 109.142 ||
svadharmaniratān viprān dambhalobhavivarjitān  |
hīnāṅgānadhikāṅgāṃśca patitāñchūdrasevitān  || 109.143 ||
śūdrānnena ca saṃyuktā vṛṣalī yasya mandire  |
paunarbhavāstathā duṣṭā gurunindāparāyaṇāḥ  || 109.144 ||
vedādhyayanahīnāśca hetukāḥ kākavṛttayaḥ  |
īdṛśān varjayecchrāddhe dāne caiva vrate tathā  || 109.145 ||
gāyatrī pāṭhamātreṇa varaṃ vipraḥ supaṇḍitaḥ  |
nāyaṃ bhṛtacaturvidyaḥ sarvāśīsarvavikrayī  || 109.146 ||
īdṛśān varjayecchrāddhe vrate dāne hiraṇmaye  |
upānahau ca vastraṃ ca śayyāṃ vā chatramāsanam  || 109.147 ||
yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe bhaktyā so'pi svarge mahīyate  |
pratyakṣā surabhī tatra tiladhenustathā matā  || 109.148 ||
tiladhenupradātāraḥ svasvadātāra eva ca  |
kṛṣṇājinapradātāro dātāraḥ kuñjarasya ca  || 109.149 ||
kanyāvidyāpradātāro'kṣayaṃ lokamavāpnuyuḥ  |
dhūrvahau dakṣiṇāyuktau dhānyopaskarasaṃyutau  || 109.150 ||
dāpayetsarvakāmāya iti me satyabhāṣitam  |
sūtreṇa veṣṭayedīśamathavā jagatīruham  || 109.151 ||
mandiraṃ parayā bhaktyā athavā parameśvaram  |
atha pradakṣiṇā kāryā vinā śūdreṇa mānavaiḥ  || 109.152 ||
jambūplakṣāhvayau dvīpau śālmaliśca bhavennṛpa  |
kuśaḥ krauñcastathā śākaḥ puṣkaraśceti saptamaḥ  || 109.153 ||
saptasāgaraparyantā veṣṭitā tena bhārata  |
dvīpeśvare tu rājendra vṛṣotsargo jitendriyaiḥ  || 109.154 ||
vṛṣasya mokṣaṇe naiva aiśvaraṃ lokamāpnuyāt |
yastu vai pāṇḍuro vaktre lalāṭe caraṇe tathā  || 109.155 ||
lāṅgūle ca mukhe śubhraḥ sa vai nākasya darśanaḥ  |
nīlo'yamīdṛśaḥ prokto dadyāddvīpeśvare śūbhe  || 109.156 ||
pāmarāste'pyasaṃkhyātā nāke gacchanti pārthiva  |
saure caṇḍeśvare loke pure vai cakrapāṇinaḥ  || 109.157 ||
sa bhuṅkte svecchayā lokaṃ vyāsatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  |
sapatnīkāṃstato viprān pūjayettatra bhaktitaḥ  || 109.158 ||
sitaratnāni vastrāṇi pradadyādagrajanmane  |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāyugmaṃ prīyatāṃ me jagadguruḥ  || 109.159 ||
nāsti viprasamo bandhuriha loke paratra ca  |
yamaloke mahāghore patitaṃ yo'bhirakṣati  || 109.160 ||
puruṣāḥ parayā bhaktyā vedaśāstrārthacintakāḥ  |
dvīpeśvaraṃ mahādevaṃ saṃsmaranti gṛhe sthitāḥ  || 109.161 ||
teṣāṃ na jāyate śoko na hānirna ca duṣkṛtam  |
prathamaṃ pūjayettatra liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ nṛpa  || 109.162 ||
yatra siddho mahābhāgaḥ satyavatyāśca nandanaḥ  |
asyaivārcanataḥ siddhaḥ pārāśaryo munistataḥ  || 109.163 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu ye rājan prāṇatyāgaṃ prakurvate  |
te yānti paramaṃ lokaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 109.164 ||
samā sahasrāṇi mṛto jale hi yo vai nimagnaḥ patane ca ṣoḍaśa  |
mahāhave ṣaṣṭiraśītigogṛhe tvanāśake bhārata cākṣayā gatiḥ  || 109.165 ||
atha yogena tenaiva prāpyate cākṣayā gatiḥ  |
sūryalokaṃ tato gatvā śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 109.166 ||
pitāpitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  |
anubhūtā nirīkṣante āgacchantaṃ svagotrajam  || 109.167 ||
tiṣṭhate caiva gotreṣu yo dadyācca tilodakam  |
kārtikyāṃ ca tathā māghyāṃ vaiśākhyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 109.168 ||
svargaṃ ca te prayāntyante svasvaputraprabhāvataḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ dvīpeśvaraphalaṃ śubham  || 109.169 ||
yaḥ paṭhetprātarutthāya yaḥ śṛṇoti naro nṛpa  |
so'pi pāpairvinirmukto modate śivamandire  || 109.170 ||
īśvaraṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ nirmitaṃ ṛṣipuṅgavaiḥ  |
kāmadaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ revāyāṃ ca nṛpottama  || 109.171 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe dvīpeśvaravyāsatīrthavarṇano nāma navādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 110

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra prabhāseśvaramuttamam  |
vikhyātāṃ triṣu lokeṣu svargasopānamuttamam  || 110.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
prabhāsaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu yathājātaṃ mahāphalam  |
svargasopānamārgaṃ ca saṃkṣepātkathayasva me  || 110.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
durbhagā ravipatnī ca prabhā nāmeti viśrutā  |
tayā cārādhitaḥ śambhurugreṇa tapasā purā  || 110.3 ||
vāyubhakṣā sthitā varṣaṃ śivadhyānaparāyaṇā  |
tatastuṣṭo mahādevaḥ prabhāṃ tāṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 110.4 ||
uvāca kliśyate kasmādbāle tvaṃ brūhi ceṣṭitam  |
ahaṃ ca bhāskaropetaścāntaraṃ naiva vidyate  || 110.5 ||

prabhovāca - 
nānyo devastathā śambho bhartā puṣyati na kvacit |
saguṇo vāpi cākhyāto nirguṇo dravyavarjitaḥ  || 110.6 ||
priyo vā yadi vā dveṣyaḥ strīṇāṃ bhartā hi devatā  |
durbhagā tvena dagdhāhaṃ lokamadhye maheśvara  || 110.7 ||
alabdhasaukhyā bhartuśca tena kliśye maheśvara  |

deva uvāca - 
vallabhā bhāskarasya tvaṃ matprabhāvādbhaviṣyasi  || 110.8 ||

pārvatyuvāca - 
vallabhā tava vākyena bhāskare na bhaviṣyati  |
vṛthā kleśo bhavedeva prabhāyāstatra kā kathā  || 110.9 ||
devīvākyena rudreṇa dhyātastimiranāśanaḥ  |
āgato gaganādbhānurnarmadāyottare taṭe  || 110.10 ||

bhānuruvāca - 
kasmādāhvānito deva andhakāsuranāśana  |

deva uvāca - 
prabhāṃ pālaya he bhāno santoṣeṇa pareṇa ca  || 110.11 ||
prabhāyā mandire nityaṃ sthīyatāṃ himanāśana  |
evaṃ labdhavarā devātprabhā sthāpyāha śaṅkaram  || 110.12 ||
svāṃśena sthīyatāmatra tīrthamunmīlayānagha  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
sarvadevamayaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 110.13 ||
prabhāseśvaranāmedaṃ sarvaloke ca durlabham  |
anyāni yāni tīrthāni kāle te'pi phalanti vai  || 110.14 ||
prabhāsaṃcāpi rājendra sadyaḥ puṇyaphalapradam  |
māghamāse ca saptamyāṃ viśeṣaphaladaṃ bhavet || 110.15 ||
aśvaṃ yo dāpayettatra yathoktaṃ brāhmaṇe nṛpa  |
indrasya prāpyate lokamathavā bhāskaraṃ vrajet || 110.16 ||
daurbhāgyaṃ naśyate tatra snānamātreṇa pāṇḍava  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā kanyādānaṃ prayacchati  || 110.17 ||
brāhmaṇāya vivāhārthe dāpeyatpāṇḍunandana  |
samānavayase vipre kulīne dhanine tathā  || 110.18 ||
yo dadāti mahārāja mahāpātakasaṃyutaḥ  |
tasya pāpaṃ ca naśyeta udake lavaṇaṃ yathā  || 110.19 ||
svāmidrohodbhavaṃ pāpaṃ yatpāpaṃ steyasambhavam  |
kūṭasākṣyapradaṃ pāpaṃ cāṇḍālavratacāriṇām  || 110.20 ||
dāmbhikaṃ vṛkṣakacchedamagamyāgamanodbhavam  |
grāmakūṭodbhavaṃ yacca garadaṃ vā pravārakam  || 110.21 ||
vidyāvikrayaṇe yacca saṃsargodbhavapātakam  |
patnīdrohodbhavaṃ ghoraṃ sarvadrohodbhavaṃ nṛpa  || 110.22 ||
brahmahatyā ca yatpāpaṃ yatpāpaṃ bhūmihāriṇaḥ  |
govadhe caiva yatpāpaṃ gurvagnibrāhmaṇeṣu ca  || 110.23 ||
jambūplakṣāhvayau dvīpau śālmaliśca bhavennṛpa  |
kuśakrauñcastathā śākaḥ puṣkaraścaiva saptamaḥ  || 110.24 ||
tatpāpaṃ vilayaṃ yāti kanyādānena pāṇḍava  |
bhittvātha bhāskaraṃ lokaṃ śubhaṃ vrajet || 110.25 ||
krīḍate rudralokastho yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  |
sarvapāpakṣaye jāte śivo bhavati bhāvataḥ  || 110.26 ||
tāvadbhramati tattīrthaṃ prabhāsaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
so'śvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya satyamīśvarabhāṣitam  || 110.27 ||
godānaṃ ca mahatpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
pratyakṣaṃ surabhīṃ tatra jaladhenuṃ tathādṛtaḥ  || 110.28 ||
tiladhenupradātā ca aśvadātā tathaiva ca  |
kanyāvidyāpradātā ca akṣayaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 110.29 ||
bhūrivastrāṃ kṣīrayuktāṃ dhānyopaskarasaṃyutām  |
dāpayetsarvakāmo'tha surabhīṃ nṛpasattama  || 110.30 ||
saptasāgaraparyantā veṣṭitā tena bhārata  |
dvīpeśvare tu rājendra vṛṣotsargaṃ jitendriyaiḥ  || 110.31 ||
sarvakālaṃ tu kartavyaṃ caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 110.32 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe prabhāsatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma daśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 111

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
sthāpitaṃ vāsukernāmnā aśeṣāghaughanāśanam  || 111.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
āḥ kasmātkāraṇāttāta sthāpitaṃ dakṣiṇe taṭe  |
tattvaṃ sarvaṃ mamākhyāhi tvaśeṣaṃ dharmakāraṇam  || 111.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
śṛṇu tvaṃ kuruśārdūla yaḥ praśnaḥ kriyate tvayā  |
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsthāya nṛtyaṃ śambhuścakāra ha  || 111.3 ||
tacchramājjāyate svedo gaṅgātoyavimiśritaḥ  |
tatraiva pannagaḥ snāto haratoyavimiśrite  || 111.4 ||
mandākinī tataḥ kruddhā vyālasyopari bhārata  |
ajagaratvamāpnoṣi uragaṃ cābravīttadā  || 111.5 ||

vāsukiruvāca - 
anugrāhyo'smyahaṃ pāpo bhavatyā harasambhṛte  |
trailokyapāvanī puṇyā sarittvaṃ śubhalakṣaṇe  || 111.6 ||
saṃsāracchedanakarī ārtā nāmārtināśinī  |
svargadvārasthite devi dayāṃ kuru mamopari  || 111.7 ||

gaṅgovāca - 
cara tvaṃ vipulaṃ nāga tapo vai śaṅkaraṃ prati  |
tatastapaścacārāsāvīśvarārādhanaṃ param  || 111.8 ||
tataśca dhyāyato devaṃ damayukto'bhavatsa ca  |
tato varṣaśate pūrṇe uparuddho jagadguruḥ  || 111.9 ||
āgatya tatsamīpasthaḥ ślakṣṇāṃ vāṇīmudāharat |
varaṃ varayatu śreṣṭhaṃ pannaga tvaṃ mahābala  || 111.10 ||

pannaga uvāca - 
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dātuṃ triśūlabhṛt |
tadā me dīyatāṃ sthānaṃ svakīyaṃ vṛṣavāhana  || 111.11 ||

īśvara uvāca - 
prasanno'haṃ mahābāho revāṃ gaccha śubhāṃ tvaram  |
yāmye caiva taṭe puṇye snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  || 111.12 ||
ityuktvā'ntardadhe devo vāsukistvaritānvitaḥ  |
rūpeṇājagareṇātha viveśa narmadājale  || 111.13 ||
mārgeṇa tasya tajjātaṃ jāhnavyāḥ srota uttamam  |
nirdhūtakalmaṣaḥ sarpaḥ sa jāto narmadājale  || 111.14 ||
sthāpitaśceśvarastatra narmadāyā yudhiṣṭhira  |
tena nāgeśvaro bhūmyāṃ sarvapāpavināśanaḥ  || 111.15 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ madhunā snāpayecchivam  |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo jāyate hyanalo yathā  || 111.16 ||
aputrā ye narāḥ pārtha snānaṃ kurvanti saṃgame  |
te labhante śubhān putrān kārtavīryopamānapi  || 111.17 ||
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva ye bhaktyā upavāsaparāyaṇāḥ  |
kurvanti tārayanti svānnarakānnṛpanandana  || 111.18 ||
evamākhyātavānasmi tava snehādviśeṣataḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchecca rājendra mārkaṇḍeśvaramuttamam  || 111.19 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle gīrvāṇairvanditaṃ śubham  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 111.20 ||
sthāpitaṃ ca mayā puṇyaṃ svargabhogaṃ ca muktidam  |
jñānaṃ tatraiva me jātaṃ prasādācchaṅkarasya ca  || 111.21 ||
anyasūktaṃ ca yo dhyāyeddrupadaṃ ca jale japet |
so'pi ghorādaghaughācca mucyate pāṇḍunandana  || 111.22 ||
vācikairmānasaiścāpi karmajairapi pāṇḍava  |
pañcendriyāṇyavaṣṭabhya yāmyāmāśāṃ ca saṃsthitaḥ  || 111.23 ||
yo japetsalile bhaktyā ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yo bhaktyā kurute nṛpanandana  || 111.24 ||
pitarastasya vai tṛptā yāvadāhūtasamplavam  |
āmalairbadarairbilvairakṣatairvā jalena vā  || 111.25 ||
tarpayettatra yaḥ pretān pretā yānti śubhāṃ gatim  || 111.26 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeśvaramahimānuvarṇano nāmaikādaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 112

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
uttare narmadākūle yajñavāṭasya madhyataḥ  || 112.1 ||
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pāpanāśanam  |
tapaścīrṇaṃ purā rājannarmadāyāstaṭe śubhe  || 112.2 ||
balabhadreṇa rājendra prāṇināmupakārakam  |
gīrvāṇaiścaiva tatraiva sannidhau nṛpanandana  || 112.3 ||
umayā sahitaḥ śambhuḥ sthitastatraiva keśavaḥ  |
yastatra snāpayedbhaktyā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  || 112.4 ||
ekādaśyāṃ site pakṣe mantreṇa snāpayecchivam  |
śrāddhaṃ tatra ca yo bhaktyā pretānāṃ vai pradāpayet || 112.5 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ balabhadravaco yathā  |
tato gacchecca rājendra manmatheśvaramuttamam  || 112.6 ||
snānamātro naro rājan yamalokaṃ na paśyati  |
anapatyā tu yā nārī snāpayetpāṇḍunandana  || 112.7 ||
putraṃ sā labhate pārtha satyavantaṃ dṛḍhavratam  |
tatra snātvā naro rājanmuniḥ prayatamānasaḥ  || 112.8 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |
tatra nṛtyaṃ prakurvanti ye narā bhaktimānasāḥ  || 112.9 ||
gītavāditrasaṃyuktaṃ rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ śubham  |
sahāmbiko mahādevastuṣṭo vai manmatheśvaraḥ  || 112.10 ||
kiṃ kariṣyati saṃruṣṭo yamastaṃ na ca paśyati  |
kāmena sthāpitaṃ tatra etasmātkāraṇātnṛpa  || 112.11 ||
annadānena bho rājan kīrtitaṃ phalamuttamam  |
sopānaṃ svargamārgasya pṛthivyāṃ manmatheśvaraḥ  || 112.12 ||
viśeṣāttatra saṃkhyātaṃ śrāddhadānena bhārata  |
annadānena bho rājan kīrtitaṃ phalamuttamam  || 112.13 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ tava bhaktyā tu bhārata  || 112.14 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe manmatheśvaramahimānuvarṇanonāma dvādaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 113

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra eraṇḍīsaṅgameśvaram  |
prakhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu brahmahatyāpraṇāśanam  || 113.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
kāraṇaṃ naiva jāne'haṃ tatsarvaṃ kathayasva me  |
evamuktastu dharmātmā dharmaputreṇa dhīmatā  || 113.2 ||
kathayāmāsa tatsarvamṛṣisaṅghaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
kathitaṃ comayā pūrvaṃ śambhunā parameṣṭhinā  || 113.3 ||
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śrūyatāṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha  |

maheśvara uvāca - 
atrirnāmāhvayo deva mānaso brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ  || 113.4 ||
agnihotrarato nityaṃ devatā'tithipūjakaḥ  |
somaḥ saṃsthāpito'traiva kṛto vipreṇa parvate  || 113.5 ||
anasūyeti nāmnā vai tasya bhāryā guṇānvitā  |
pativratā patiprāṇā patyuḥ kāryahite ratā  || 113.6 ||
evaṃ jātaḥ sadā kālo na putro na ca putrikā  |
aparāhṇe mahābāho sukhāsīnau tu tau kvacit || 113.7 ||
vadataḥ sukhaduḥkhāni daivadattāni yāni ca  |

atriruvāca - 
saumye śubhe priye kānte surūpe priyabhāṣiṇi  || 113.8 ||
pūrṇacandranibhākāre priyakāme nirālase  |
na tvayā sadṛśī loke trailokye sacarācare  || 113.9 ||
patiputrapriyā nārī suhṛjjanahite ratā  |
putreṇa lokājjayati putreṇa paramā gatiḥ  || 113.10 ||
nāsti putrasamo bandhuḥ pṛthivyāṃ caiva dṛśyate  |
asipatravane ghore patantaṃ yo'bhirakṣati  || 113.11 ||
durbhikṣeṣvapi dainyādau vṛddhakāle'pi putrakaḥ  |
putraṃ vinā ca kiṃ bhadre jīvitaiḥ sadhanairapi  || 113.12 ||
vyādhibhiḥ paribhūto'pi nirviṇṇo'pi yadā sutaḥ  |
lokalajjānayatrastaḥ pavitraṃ kartumarhati  || 113.13 ||
etadguṇasamāyukto nirguṇaḥ saguṇaḥ sutaḥ  |
putrahīne kutaḥ saukhyamiha loke paratra ca  || 113.14 ||
ahaśca madhyarātre ca cintyamānaśca sarvadā  |
śuṣyanti mama gātrāṇi grīṣme kusarito yathā  || 113.15 ||

anasūyovāca - 
yattvayā sūcitaṃ vipra tatsarvaṃ śocayāmyaham  |
tavodvegakaraṃ kāryaṃ tanme dahati cetasi  || 113.16 ||
ye ca putrā bhaviṣyanti dīrghāyurguṇasaṃyutāḥ  |
tatkāryaṃ ca samīkṣe'haṃ yena tuṣṭaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 113.17 ||

atriruvāca - 
tapastaptaṃ mayā bhadre janmaprabhṛtiduṣkaram  |
vratopavāsairniyamaiḥ śākāhāreṇa sundari  || 113.18 ||
kṣīṇaṃ dehaṃ tu paśyāmi aśakto'haṃ śubhānane  |
sthātuṃ śocāmi cātmānaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ mayā  || 113.19 ||

anasūyovāca - 
bhartaḥ pativratā nārī patiputravivarddhinī  |
trivargasādhanā sā ca sevyā sā vipule jane  || 113.20 ||
japastapastīrthayātrā putrejyāmantrasādhanam  |
vadanti guravaḥ sarve yathoktaṃ gurubhāṣitam  || 113.21 ||
anujñātā ca duḥkhe'haṃ tapastapsyāmi duṣkaram  |
putrārthinī bahudinānyahaṃ yāmi surottamam  || 113.22 ||

atriruvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe mama santoṣakāriṇi  |
anujñātā mayā bhadre putrārthaṃ tapa ācara  || 113.23 ||
devānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ pitṝṇāmanṛṇaṃ kuru  |
na bhāryā sadṛśo bandhustriṣu lokeṣu vidyate  || 113.24 ||
nahi devāḥ praśaṃsanti bhāryayā rahitaṃ sukham  |
sammukhe sammukhā yāti vilome ca parāṅmukhī  || 113.25 ||
tena bhāryā praśaṃsanti sadevāsuramānuṣāḥ  |
mahāvrate mahāprajñe satyarūpe śubhe kṣaṇe  || 113.26 ||
tapaścarasva śīghraṃ tvaṃ putrārthaṃ ca mamājñayā  |
etadvākyāvasāne sā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇatā'bravīt || 113.27 ||
tvatprasādena viprendra sarvametadavāpnuyām  |
haṃsalīlāgatiryāntī lolākṣī varavarṇinī  || 113.28 ||
viṣamasthānasūyā tu prāptāsau narmadāṃ nadīm  |
somanāthena tattulyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 113.29 ||
ye smaranti divā rātrau yojanānāṃ śatairapi  |
mucyante sarvapāpebhyo rudraloke vasanti te  || 113.30 ||
narmadāyāḥ samīpe tu dve taṭe dve ca yojane  |
praviśantī tapastatra revāyāṃ varavarṇinī  || 113.31 ||
yasyā darśanamātreṇa naśyate pāpasaṃcayam  |
tataḥ tasyottare tīre pare parṇāśanā śubhā  || 113.32 ||
niyamasthā viśālākṣī śākāhāreṇa sundarī  |
stuvantī tu tato devāñchubhaiḥ stotraiśca saṃyatā  || 113.33 ||
grīṣmeṣu ca mahādevī pañcāgniṃ sādhayettataḥ  |
varṣākāle sārdravāsācaraccāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam  || 113.34 ||
hemante ca tataḥ prāpte toyavāsābhavattataḥ  |
prātaḥ snānaṃ tataḥ sāndhyaṃ kuryāddevāditarpaṇam  || 113.35 ||
devānāmarcanaṃ kṛtvā homaṃ kṛtvā yathā vidhi  |
evaṃ varṣaśate prāpte viṣṇurudrapitāmahāḥ  || 113.36 ||
samprāptā dvijarūpeṇa eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgamaṃ prati  |
saṃsthitā agratastasyā vedamabhyuccaranti te  || 113.37 ||
anasūyā japaṃ tyaktvā nirīkṣantī muhurmuhuḥ  |
utthitā sā viśālākṣī arghaṃ dattvā yathāvidhi  || 113.38 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ  |
darśanena tu viprāṇāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 113.39 ||
pradakṣiṇāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇatā'bravīt |
kandamūlaphalairdivyairadyāhaṃ tarpayāmi vaḥ  || 113.40 ||

viprā ūcu̱ḥ
tapasā tu vicitreṇa tava satyena suvrate  |
tṛptā vai sarvakāmaistu tapasvinyāśca darśanāt || 113.41 ||
asmākaṃ kautukaṃ jātaṃ kimarthaṃ tapyate tvayā  |
svargamokṣaṃ tu rakṣārthaṃ tapastapyasi duṣkaram  || 113.42 ||

anasūyovāca - 
tapasā sidhyate svargastapasā paramā gatiḥ  |
tapasā caiva bho viprāḥ sarvakāmamavāpnuyāt || 113.43 ||

brāhmaṇā ūcu̱ḥ
tanvī śyāmā viśālākṣī snigdhāṅgī rūpasaṃyutā  |
haṃsalīlāgatistvaṃ hi kiṃ cātmānaṃ viśoṣasi  || 113.44 ||

anasūyovāca - 
yuvatve ca tapaḥ kāryaṃ yuvatve paramā gati  |
yuvatve ca sutotpattirvṛddhatve sarvamapriyam  || 113.45 ||

viprā ūcu̱ḥ
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe varaṃ prārthaya suvrate  |
yattvayā cābhilaṣitaṃ tatsarvaṃ pradadāmyaham  || 113.46 ||
ahaṃ viṣṇurahaṃ rudro hyahaṃ sākṣātpitāmahaḥ  |
gūḍharūpadharā loke svacihnairupalakṣitāḥ  || 113.47 ||
tasyā vākyāvasāne tu svarūpaṃ darśayanti te  |
svaiḥ svai rūpaiḥ sthitā devāḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhāḥ  || 113.48 ||
caturbhujo vāsudevaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
atasīpuṣpavarṇastu pītavāsā janārdanaḥ  || 113.49 ||
garutmān vāhanaṃ yasya śriyā ca sahito hariḥ  |
prasannavadanaḥ śrīmāñchivarūpo vyavasthitaḥ  || 113.50 ||
sitavāsā mahābhāgaścaturvadanasaṃyutaḥ  |
haṃsoparisamārūḍho hyakṣamālākaro'nagha  || 113.51 ||
āgato narmadātīre brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
vṛṣabhaṃ tu samārūḍho daśabāhusamanvitaḥ  || 113.52 ||
bhasmoddhūlitagātrastu pañcavaktrastrilocanaḥ  |
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃyuktaścandrārdhakṛtaśekharaḥ  || 113.53 ||
etadrūpadharo devaḥ sarvavyāpī maheśvaraḥ  |
anasūyā tu tatraiva devānāṃ darśanātparam  || 113.54 ||
vepamānā rahasye tu tān paśyantī muhurmuhuḥ  |
ahaṃ brahmā hyahaṃ viṣṇurahaṃ rudraḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 113.55 ||
ānanditā tu sā devī dṛṣṭvaitānmahāvrata  |

anasūyovāca - 
kiṃ vyāpārāśca ke yūyaṃ viṣṇurudrapitāmahāḥ  || 113.56 ||
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmīmaṃ praśnaṃ kathayantu te  |

brahmovāca - 
prāvṛṭkālo hyahaṃ prokta āpaścaiva prakīrtitaḥ  || 113.57 ||
megharūpo hyahaṃ prokto varṣāmi vasudhātale  |
ahaṃ sarvāṇi bhūtāni prāksandhyā hyudite ravau  || 113.58 ||
etasmātkāraṇādbhāvarahasyaṃ kathitaṃ mayā  |

viṣṇuruvāca - 
hemantatvācca vihitaṃ viṣṇurūpaṃ carācaram  || 113.59 ||
pālanīyaṃ jagatsarvaṃ viṣṇormāhātmyamuttamam  |

rudra uvāca - 
grīṣmakālo hyahaṃ proktaḥ sarvabhūtakṣayaṃkaraḥ  || 113.60 ||
śoṣayāmi jagatsarvaṃ rudrarūpastapasvini  |
evaṃ brahmā ca viṣṇuśca rudraścaiva mahīpate  || 113.61 ||
tisraḥ sandhyāstrayo devāstrayaḥ kālāstrayo'gnayaḥ  |
tathā brahmā ca viṣṇuśca rudraścaikatvamāgatāḥ  || 113.62 ||
varaṃ dadāmi te bhadre yatte manasi vartate  |

anasūyovāca - 
vandhyā lokairahaṃ loke khyāpyamānā ca sarvadā  || 113.63 ||
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca prasādātsumukhā yataḥ  |
parituṣṭāstrayo devā durdharṣā'pi mamopari  || 113.64 ||
asmiṃstīrthe tu sānnidhyaṃ varaṃ dadatu me'dhunā  |

devā ūcu̱ḥ
evaṃ bhavatu te vākyaṃ yattvayā prārthitaṃ śubhe  || 113.65 ||
pratyakṣā vaiṣṇavī māyā eraṇḍī caiva nāmataḥ  |

anasūyovāca - 
yadi tuṣṭāstrayo devā mama bhaktiprabodhitāḥ  || 113.66 ||
mama putrā bhavantvatra harirudrapitāmahāḥ  |

viṣṇuruvāca - 
arthadāḥ putratāṃ yānti na kadācicchrutaṃ mayā  || 113.67 ||
bhadre dadāmi tān putrān devatulyaparākramān  |
pitṛtulyaguṇopetān somayāji bahuśrutān  || 113.68 ||

anasūyovāca - 
īpsitaṃ tu pradātavyaṃ yanmayā prārthitaṃ hare  |
nānyathā tacca kartavyaṃ nivasantu mamodare  || 113.69 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca - 
pūrvaṃ tu bhṛgusaṃvāde garbhavāsa upārjitaḥ  |
tasyāhaṃ caiva pāraṃ tu na ca paśyāmi śobhane  || 113.70 ||
smaramāṇaḥ purāvṛttaṃ cintayāmi punaḥ punaḥ  |
evaṃ saṃcintyamānau hi pitāmahamaheśvarau  || 113.71 ||
ayonijā bhaviṣyāmastava putrāḥ suśobhane  |
yonivāsaṃ ca vai devā naiva yānti varānane  || 113.72 ||
ityuktvā ca tayā sārdhaṃ pratyakṣāste'bhavaṃstadā  |
trayo devā gatāḥ pārtha narmadāyottare taṭe  || 113.73 ||
prāptā varaṃ tu sā devī priyaṃ māhendraparvate  |
kṣīṇadehā ca sā nārī śuṣkadehā sudāruṇā  || 113.74 ||
kṛtayajñopavītā sā taponiṣṭhā śubhekṣaṇā  |
śilātale niṣaṇṇā sā'paśyatkāntā mahāvratam  || 113.75 ||
hṛṣṭā tuṣṭā mahādevī tiṣṭha kānteti cābravīt |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa munirdhīmān punaḥ kāntāmuvāca ha  || 113.76 ||

atriruvāca - 
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe anasūye mahāvrate  |
asādhyaṃ sarvanārīṇāṃ varaṃ prāptāsi durlabham  || 113.77 ||

anasūyovāca - 
tvatprasādānmaharṣe'haṃ varaṃ prāptā ca durlabham  |
tenāhaṃ te prayacchāmi putrānṛṣitapodhanān  || 113.78 ||
evamuktvā tato devī harṣeṇa mahatā yutā  |
alokayattadā kāntaṃ tenāpi śubhadarśanā  || 113.79 ||
darśanādeva saṃjātaṃ lalāṭe maṇḍalaṃ śubham  |
navayojanasāhasraraśmi jālasamāvṛtam  || 113.80 ||
kadambagolakākāraṃ trigaṇaṃ parimaṇḍalam  |
tasya madhye tu deveśaḥ puruṣo divyarūpadhṛk || 113.81 ||
hemavarṇaḥ sa vai devaḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ  |
pūrvaputro'nasūyāyāḥ sākṣāddevaḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 113.82 ||
candramā iti vikhyātaḥ somaḥ putro nṛpātmaja  |
iṣṭaḥ putro varīyāṃstu kalāṣoḍaśasaṃyutaḥ  || 113.83 ||
pratipacca dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā ca tathā nṛpa  |
caturthī pañcamī ṣaṣṭhī saptamī cāṣṭamī tathā  || 113.84 ||
navamī daśamī caiva tathā caikādaśī parā  |
dvādaśī ca trayodaśī caturdaśī tataḥ param  || 113.85 ||
tataḥ pañcadaśī devī pūrṇamāsī prakīrtitā  |
amāvāsyā tu vikhyātā atha sā ṣoḍaśī kalā  || 113.86 ||
caturvidhasya lokasya sūkṣmo bhūtvā varānane  |
āpyāyate jagatsarvaṃ somo'yaṃ sacarācaram  || 113.87 ||
surāsurāśca gandharvā rākṣasāḥ pannagāstathā  |
piśācāśca tathādityāḥ pitaraśca pitāmahaḥ  || 113.88 ||
sarve tamupajīvanti hutaṃ dravyaṃ tu tatsthitam  |
vanaspatigate some yacchindyācca vanaspatim  || 113.89 ||
bhuṅkte duḥkhaṃ ca vai mūḍho dahatyabdakṛtaṃ śubham  |
vanaspatigate some yo bhakṣeddantadhāvanam  || 113.90 ||
candramā bhakṣitastena pitṛvaṃśastu ghātitaḥ  |
amāvāsyāṃ tu rājendra snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 113.91 ||
abdamekaṃ viśālākṣi pitṝṇāṃ paramā gatiḥ  |
hiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ yo dadati dvijātiṣu  || 113.92 ||
sarvaṃ lakṣaguṇaṃ rājaṃllabhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
etadguṇaviśiṣṭo'sau somarūpaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 113.93 ||
saṃjātaḥ prathamaḥ putro'nasūyāyāstu nandanaḥ  |
dvitīyastu mahābhāga durvāsā nāma nāmataḥ  || 113.94 ||
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakartā ca svayaṃ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ  |
indro'pi śāpitastena dvitīyena varānane  || 113.95 ||
dvitīyasya tu putrasya sambhavaḥ kathito mayā  |
dattātreyastu nāmnā vai tṛtīyo madhusūdanaḥ  || 113.96 ||
jagadvyāpī jagannāthaḥ svayaṃ devo janārdanaḥ  |
avatīrṇo mahābhāgabrahmaśambhusamanvitaḥ  || 113.97 ||
putraprāptipadaṃ tīrthaṃ narmadāyottare taṭe  |
anasūyākṛtaṃ pārtha sarvapāpakṣayaṃ karam  || 113.98 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe eraṇḍītīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma trayodaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 114

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
etasyānantaraṃ pārtha sauvarṇaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 114.1 ||
revāyāṃ durlabhaṃ snānaṃ saṅgamasya samīpataḥ  |
vibhaktaṃ hastamātraṃ ca puṇyakṣetre narādhipa  || 114.2 ||
suvarṇaśilake snātvā śāntiṃ yāti parāṃ śubhām  |
nirmitvā bhāskaraṃ devaṃ hotavyaṃ tu hutāśane  || 114.3 ||
bilvena ghṛtamiśreṇa bilvapatreṇa bhūriṇā  |
prīyatāṃ hi jagannātho vyādhirnaśyatu me sadā  || 114.4 ||
dvijebhyaścetprayuktaṃ syādyāgasya phalamāpnuyāt |
tatra dānena prītātmā mṛtaḥ svargamavāpnuyāt || 114.5 ||
śuklapakṣe tathāṣṭamyāṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
yastatra kurute śrāddhaṃ nṛdevabhaktito naraḥ  || 114.6 ||
samuddharetkule tatra daśapūrvān daśāparān  |
kāṃcanaṃ vāpi yo dadyāddhenuṃ caiva suśobhanām  || 114.7 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
pūjayitvā śivaṃ tatra śatrūṇāṃ vijayo bhavet || 114.8 ||
putravān guṇavāṃścaiva sarvavyādhi vivarjitaḥ  |
ityevaṃ kathitaṃ rājan sauvarṇaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 114.9 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
etasminnantare tīrthaṃ karaṇḍeśvaramuttamam  |
prakhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu narmadāyottare taṭe  || 114.10 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ proktaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
tato gacchecca rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  || 114.11 ||
saubhāgyakaraṇaṃ divyaṃ narāṇāṃ pāpanāśanam  |
tatra yā durbhagā nārī naro vā nṛpanandana  || 114.12 ||
snātvā'rcayedumāṃ rudraṃ saubhāgyaṃ tasya jāyate  |
tṛtīyāyāmahorātraṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  || 114.13 ||
nimantrayeddvijaṃ tatra sapatnīkaṃ surūpiṇam  |
gandhamālyairalaṃkṛtya puṣpadhūpādhivāsitam  || 114.14 ||
bhojayetpāyasānnena kṛśareṇātha bhaktitaḥ  |
bhojayitvā yathā nyāyaṃ pradakṣiṇamathācaret || 114.15 ||
tvaṃ tu devo mahādeva sapatnīko vṛṣadhvaja  |
yathā te deva deveśa na viyogaḥ kadācana  || 114.16 ||
somanāthākhyakārpaṇyāttaṃ dhyāyāmīha cintayan  |
jyeṣṭhe śukle tṛtīyāyāṃ saubhāgye narmadājale  || 114.17 ||
snātvā dattvā ca subhagā na priyeṇa viyujyate  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
na daurbhāgyaṃ na dāridryaṃ na śoko na ca durgatiḥ  || 114.18 ||
etatsarvaṃ bhavedyena tatsarvaṃ kathayasva me  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
daurbhāgyaṃ durgatiṃ caiva dāridryaṃ śokavardhanam  || 114.19 ||
vaidhavyaṃ saptajanmani jāyate na yudhiṣṭhira  |
karmaṇā yena pāpānāṃ kṣayastacca vadāmi te  || 114.20 ||
jyeṣṭhe māse site pakṣe tatīyāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā pañcāgniṃ sādhayettapaḥ  || 114.21 ||
so'pi pāpairaśeṣaistu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
gugguluṃ dāhayedyastu gaurī śivasamīpataḥ  || 114.22 ||
tasmin karmaṇi viprasya uktāni bhavate tataḥ  |
dehapāte kṛte svargamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 114.23 ||
śvetairaktaistathā pītairvastraiśca vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
brāhmaṇīrbrāhmaṇāṃścaiva pūjayitvā yathāvidhi  || 114.24 ||
puṣpairnānāvidhaiścaiva gandhadhūpaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  |
kaṇṭhe sūtraṃ samādhāya kuṅkumena vilepayet || 114.25 ||
kalpayitvā striyaṃ gaurīṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śivarūpiṇam  |
tābhyāṃ dadyātsamādṛtya dānamutsṛjya vāriṇā  || 114.26 ||
karṇaveṣṭaṃ tvaṅgadaṃ ca kāñcanīṃ mudrikāṃ tathā  |
saptadhānyaṃ tathā deyaṃ bhojanaṃ nṛpasattama  || 114.27 ||
anyāni caiva dānāni tasmiṃstīrthe narottama  |
sarvadānaiśca yatpuṇyaṃ tatpuṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ bhavet || 114.28 ||
tatra sāhasraguṇitaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
śaṅkareṇa samaṃ tatra bhuṅkte bhogānanuttamān  || 114.29 ||
saubhāgyaṃ tasya vipulaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
aputro labhate putraṃ nirdhano dhanamāpnuyāt || 114.30 ||
kāmadaṃ tīrtharājaṃ tu narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 114.31 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe saubhāgyatīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma caturdaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 115

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra bhāṇḍāraṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
dāridryabhedakaraṇaṃ puruṣāṃścaikaviṃśatim  || 115.1 ||
dhanadena tapastaptaṃ prasannaḥ padmasambhavaḥ  |
tatraiva svasvadānena prāptaṃ vittamanantakam  || 115.2 ||
tatra gatvā tu yo bhaktyā snātvā vittaṃ prayacchati  |
tasya vittaparicchedo na bhavecca kadācana  || 115.3 ||
tasyaivānantaraṃ rājan rohiṇītīrthamuttamam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu sarvapāpaharaṃ param  || 115.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca - 
rohiṇītīrthamāhātmyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
śrotumicchāmi tattvena tanme tvaṃ vaktumarhasi  || 115.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tasminnekārṇave ghore naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame  |
tasyodare śayānasya devadevasya pāṇḍava  || 115.6 ||
nābhyāmabhūnmahatpadmaṃ ravimaṇḍalasannibham  |
kīrṇakākesarayutaṃ patraiśca samalaṃkṛtam  || 115.7 ||
tatra brahmā samutpannaścaturvadanapaṅkajaḥ  |
kiṃ karomīti deveśa cintyamānaḥ svadehataḥ  || 115.8 ||
bhagavānabhavattatra marīcirbharatādhipa  |
marīceḥ kaśyapo jātaḥ sarvasṛṣṭikarastataḥ  || 115.9 ||
dakṣasyāpi tadā jātāḥ pañcāśatkanyakāstu vai  |
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa  || 115.10 ||
tathaiva ca parāḥ kanyāḥ saptaviṃśatimindave  |
rohiṇī nāma yā tāsāṃ madhye tārādhipānanā  || 115.11 ||
abhīṣṭā sarvanārīṇāṃ bhartuścāpi viśeṣataḥ  |
punaḥ sā niścayībhūtā tapase bho narādhipa  || 115.12 ||
tataḥ sā narmadātīre cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ ca ṣaḍdvādaśa tathāparaiḥ  || 115.13 ||
pakṣamāsopavāsaiśca karṣayantī kalevaram  |
ārādhayantī satataṃ mahiṣāsuramārdinīm  || 115.14 ||
snātvā snātvā jale nityaṃ narmadāyāḥ śuciḥ smitā  |
tatastuṣṭā mahābhāgā devī nārāyaṇī nṛpa  || 115.15 ||
prasannā te mahābhāge vratena niyamena ca  |
dadāmi te na sandeho varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 115.16 ||
evaṃ śrutvā tu vacanaṃ rohiṇī śaśinaḥ priyā  |
varaṃ vavre tato devīmidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 115.17 ||
sarvāsāṃ ca sapatnīnāmadhikā śaśinaḥ priyā  |
yathā bhavāni hyacirāttvatprasādāttathā kuru  || 115.18 ||
evamastviti sā proktā bhavānyā bhaktitatparaiḥ  |
stūyamānā suragaṇaistatraivāntaradhīyata  || 115.19 ||
tadā prabhṛti sā devī rohiṇī śaśinaḥ priyā  |
saṃjātā sarvalokasya vallabhā nṛpasattama  || 115.20 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yā nārī naro vā snāti bhaktitaḥ  |
vallabhā bhavate sā tu bharturvai rohiṇī yathā  || 115.21 ||
tatra tīrtheṣu yaḥ kaścitprāṇatyāgaṃ karoti ca  |
saptajanmani tasyaiva viyogo naiva jāyate  || 115.22 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
tato gacchettu rājendra cakratīrthamanuttamam  |
senāpureti vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpakṣayakaram  || 115.23 ||
saināpatye'bhiṣiktena devadevena cakriṇā  |
abhiṣikto mahāsenaḥ sa devendrapurogamaiḥ  || 115.24 ||
dānavasya vadhārthāya vijayāya divaukasām  |
bhūmidānena viprendrāṃstarpayitvā yathā vidhi  || 115.25 ||
śaṅkhabherīninādena paṭahānāṃ ca niḥsvanaiḥ  |
vīṇābhiśca mṛdaṇgaiśca jhallarīkāṃsyatālakaiḥ  || 115.26 ||
tacchrutvā ninadaṃ ghoraṃ dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
surāṇāmāvighātārthamabhiṣekasya cāgrataḥ  || 115.27 ||
hastyaśvarathapattyādyaiḥ paripūrṇo ravākulaiḥ  || 115.28 ||
tatastu tāṃ raudravasya vāhinīṃ śaraiḥ suśārṅgojjhitakaiḥ sutīkṣṇaiḥ  |
vidhvasya hastyaśvarathānmahātmā cakraṃ vimuktaṃ madhughātinā ca  || 115.29 ||
dṛṣṭvā tu bhīṣaṇaṃ cakramabhivyāptaṃ ṣaḍānanaḥ  |
tyaktvā tatrāpyavasthānaṃ cakāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 115.30 ||
cakraṃ muktaṃ vināśāya hariṇā lokadhāriṇā  |
vihvalāṃ dāhayāmāsa papāta vimale jale  || 115.31 ||
niṣpāpaṃ tacca saṃjātaṃ narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
prāvṛṭkāle śubhe pakṣe dvādaśyāṃ caiva bhārata  || 115.32 ||
yaśca yāti jitakrodhaścakratīrthaṃ haripriyam  |
so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyeta yamaṃ ghoraṃ na paśyati  || 115.33 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpaṃ devasya dāpayet |
kathāṃ ca vaiṣṇavīṃ tatra devadevaṃ samāhitaḥ  || 115.34 ||
bhīmavrataṃ ca pārākaṃ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ tathā  |
vrataṃ sāntapanaṃ deva trirātravratakaṃ bhṛśam  || 115.35 ||
taredvaitaraṇīmante bhīmaṃ cakramaharniśam  |
kūṭaśālmalivṛkṣāṃśca kadācinnaiva paśyati  || 115.36 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ cakratīrthasya yatphalam  || 115.37 ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe cakratīrthamahimānuvarṇano nāma pañcadaśādhika śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


adhyāya 116

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
dhūmapātaṃ tato gacchenmahāpātakanāśanam  |
samīpe cakratīrthasyā viṣṇunā nirmitaṃ purā  || 116.1 ||
mekalāṃ paramāṃ devīmimāṃ trailokyapāvanīm  |
kadācitpayasāṃ rājā sāntaḥpuraparicchadaḥ  || 116.2 ||
śiṣṭairiṣṭairbandhubhiśca arghapātreṇa saṃyutaiḥ  |
hastābharaṇasaṃyuktaiḥ puṇyairamalakāntibhiḥ  || 116.3 ||
candramaṇḍalamānaiśca yuktairmuktāphalaistathā  |
pravālalatikābhiśca indranīlasamanvitaiḥ  || 116.4 ||
arghaṃ dadau tadā tasyai varuṇaḥ saritāṃ patiḥ  |
gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāstāpī cāpi payoṣṇikā  || 116.5 ||
nandinī nalinī puṇyā sarvamarghaṃ dadustadā  |

narmadovāca - 
madīye saṅgame divye snātvā santarpayanti ye  || 116.6 ||
tasya saptakulotpannāṃstārayāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
jalāñjaliṃ tato dattvā samudro vākyamabravīt || 116.7 ||
dhanyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ tvayā devi varānane  |
samāyātāsi bhadraṃ te māṃ cātra pāvanaṃ kuru  || 116.8 ||

narmadovāca - 
pavitro'si mahābhāga ekākī tvaṃ mahodadhe  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca - 
evaṃ bhagavatī rājannarmadā mekalā śubhā  || 116.9 ||
pūjitā sāgareṇāpi śubhe siṃhāsane sthitā  |
pāṇigrahaṃ gṛhītā sā purukutsena bhārata  || 116.10 ||
purukutsasya bhāryā sā gṛhadharmeṇa saṃyutā  |
sadā vai vartate rājaṃstatraiva saṅgame śubhe  || 116.11 ||
puṣpavṛṣṭistadā hyāsīttridaśānāṃ mahotsave  |
tatra svayaṃvaraścāsītsaritaḥ pṛthivīpate  || 116.12 ||
tatra yaḥ kurute śrāddhaṃ sthānaṃ ca pitṛtarpaṇam  |
lakṣayajñaphalaṃ prāpya sa mukto nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 116.13 ||
evaṃ trailokyapūjyā te narmadā lokapāvanī  |
tasyā māhātmyamatulaṃ kīrtitaṃ hi mahābhuja  || 116.14 ||
bhaktyā śrutvā mahābhāga rudraloke mahīyate  |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu revāmāhātmyamuttamam  || 116.15 ||
yaḥ kaścicchṛṇuyādbhaktyā tasya syādvāñchitaṃ phalam  |
śrutvā māhātmyamatulaṃ yo naro hi jitendriyaḥ  || 116.16 ||
dānaṃ kuryāttadā tasya sarvakāmārthasiddhayaḥ  |
pustakaṃ pūjayitvā tu dhūpadīpakacandanaiḥ  || 116.17 ||
dānaṃ tatra prakartavyaṃ brāhmaṇāṃścāpi pūjayet |
śravaṇena tu dānena suprītā narmadā bhavet || 116.18 ||
tīrthe tīrthe ca kathitaṃ tatpūrvaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
puṇyaṃ śrutvā tu māhātmyaṃ taddānenaiva pāṇḍava  || 116.19 ||
etasmātkāraṇāddānaṃ śrutvā dānaṃ hi kāraṇam  |
tacchrutvā rājaśārdūlo mārkaṇḍeyasya bhāṣitam  || 116.20 ||
arghaṃ dattvā yathānyāyaṃ pūjayitvā ṛṣīn sadā  |
aśvairgajaistathā rathairbhrātṛbhiḥ saha dharmarāṭ || 116.21 ||
tīrthayātrāṃ cakārāśu narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
uttare dakṣiṇe tīre snānapānāvagāhanam  || 116.22 ||
iti śrīskandapurāṇe revākhaṇḍe yudhiṣṭhiramārkaṇḍeyasaṃvāde narmadācaritravarṇano nāma ṣoḍaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

iti śrīskandapurāṇasya revākhaṇḍaḥ ||
